[No.1] Spectral Perturbation Bounds for Low-Rank Approximation with Applications to Privacy
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 低秩逼近的谱扰动界及其在隐私保护中的应用
类别: theory
作者: Phuc Tran;Van Vu;Nisheeth K. Vishnoi;
机构: Yale University;;
类型: Oral
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=F0JzotXYgC
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.25670
英摘: A central challenge in machine learning is to understand how noise or measurement errors affect low-rank approximations, particularly in the spectral norm. This question is especially important in differentially private low-rank approximation, where one aims to preserve the top-$p$ structure of a data-derived matrix while ensuring privacy. Prior work often analyzes Frobenius norm error or changes in reconstruction quality, but these metrics can over- or under-estimate true subspace distortion. The spectral norm, by contrast, captures worst-case directional error and provides the strongest utility guarantees. We establish new high-probability spectral-norm perturbation bounds for symmetric matrices that refine the classical Eckart--Young--Mirsky theorem and explicitly capture interactions between a matrix $A \in \mathbb{R}^{n \times n}$ and an arbitrary symmetric perturbation $E$. Under mild eigengap and norm conditions, our bounds yield sharp estimates for $\|(A + E)_p - A_p\|$, where $A_p$ is the best rank-$p$ approximation of $A$, with improvements of up to a factor of $\sqrt{n}$. As an application, we derive improved utility guarantees for differentially private PCA, resolving an open problem in the literature. Our analysis relies on a novel contour bootstrapping method from complex analysis and extends it to a broad class of spectral functionals, including polynomials and matrix exponentials. Empirical results on real-world datasets confirm that our bounds closely track the actual spectral error under diverse perturbation regimes.
中摘: 机器学习中的核心挑战之一是理解噪声或测量误差如何影响低秩近似，特别是在谱范数下。这个问题在差分隐私下的低秩近似中尤为重要，其中目标是在保证隐私的同时保持数据衍生矩阵的前$p$结构。以往的工作通常分析弗罗贝尼乌斯范数误差或重建质量的变化，但这些指标可能过高或过低估计了真实的子空间扭曲。相比之下，谱范数捕捉了最坏情况下的方向误差，并提供了最强的效用保证。我们为对称矩阵建立了新的高概率谱范数扰动界，这改进了经典的埃克特-杨-米尔斯基定理，并明确捕捉了一个矩阵$A \in \mathbb{R}^{n \times n}$与任意对称扰动$E$之间的相互作用。在温和的特征值间隙和范数条件下，我们的界给出了$\|(A + E)_p - A_p\|$的精确估计，其中$A_p$是$A$的最佳秩$p$近似，其改进可高达$\sqrt{n}$倍。作为应用，我们推导出了差分隐私PCA改进的效用保证，解决了文献中的一个开放问题。我们的分析依赖于复分析中的一种新颖的轮廓引导方法，并将其扩展到广泛的谱函数类，包括多项式和矩阵指数。在真实世界数据集上的实证结果证实，在各种扰动制度下，我们的界紧密跟踪实际的谱误差。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.2] FlyLoRA: Boosting Task Decoupling and Parameter Efficiency via Implicit Rank-Wise Mixture-of-Experts
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: FlyLoRA：通过隐式秩别混合专家提升任务解耦和参数效率
类别: deep learning
作者: Heming Zou;Yunliang Zang;Wutong Xu;Yao Zhu;Xiangyang Ji;
机构: Tsinghua University;Tianjin University;Zhejiang University;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=nGQLYn13Xf
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08396
英摘: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is a widely used parameter-efficient fine-tuning method for foundation models, but it suffers from parameter interference, resulting in suboptimal performance. Although Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)-based LoRA variants show promise in mitigating intra-task correlations in single-task instruction tuning, they introduce additional router parameters and remain ineffective in multi-task model merging where inter-task interference arises. Inspired by the fly olfactory circuit, we propose FlyLoRA, an implicit MoE-based LoRA variant that introduces: (1) rank-wise expert activation in the up-projection matrix, and (2) an implicit router that unifies expert routing and down-projection, where a frozen sparse random projection matrix replaces the traditional dense trainable version. This design resolves the trade-off between intra-task decorrelation and computational efficiency by eliminating the need for an explicit router, while inherently mitigating inter-task interference due to the orthogonality property of random matrices. Extensive experiments across four domains -- general knowledge understanding, scientific question answering, mathematical reasoning, and code generation -- demonstrate consistent performance improvements over existing methods. Beyond empirical gains, FlyLoRA highlights how biological structures can inspire innovations in AI technologies. Code is available at https://github.com/gfyddha/FlyLoRA.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）是一种广泛用于基础模型参数高效微调的方法，但它受到参数干扰的影响，导致性能次优。尽管基于专家混合（MoE）的LoRA变体在单一任务指令微调中显示出减轻任务内相关性的潜力，但它们引入了额外的路由参数，并且在多任务模型融合时依然无效，因为此时会出现任务间干扰。受苍蝇嗅觉回路的启发，我们提出了FlyLoRA，这是一种隐式MoE基LoRA变体，它引入了：(1) 在上投影矩阵中的按秩专家激活，以及 (2) 一个隐式路由器，它统一了专家路由和下投影，其中使用冻结的稀疏随机投影矩阵替换了传统的密集可训练版本。这种设计通过消除对显式路由器的需求解决了任务内去相关性和计算效率之间的权衡，同时由于随机矩阵的正交性特性，内在地缓解了任务间干扰。在四个领域——通用知识理解、科学问题回答、数学推理和代码生成——进行的大量实验表明，与现有方法相比，FlyLoRA表现出了持续的性能提升。除了实证收益外，FlyLoRA还突显了生物结构如何能激发人工智能技术的创新。代码可在https://github.com/gfyddha/FlyLoRA获取。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.3] COALA: Numerically Stable and Efficient Framework for Context-Aware Low-Rank Approximation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: COALA：一种数值稳定且高效的上下文感知低秩逼近框架
类别: deep learning
作者: Uliana Parkina;Maxim Rakhuba;
机构: Higher School of Economics;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=z1wIUZtBmK
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.07580
英摘: Recent studies suggest that context-aware low-rank approximation is a useful tool for compression and fine-tuning of modern large-scale neural networks. In this type of approximation, a norm is weighted by a matrix of input activations, significantly improving metrics over the unweighted case. Nevertheless, existing methods for neural networks suffer from numerical instabilities due to their reliance on classical formulas involving explicit Gram matrix computation and their subsequent inversion. We demonstrate that this can degrade the approximation quality or cause numerically singular matrices.
  To address these limitations, we propose a novel inversion-free regularized framework that is based entirely on stable decompositions and overcomes the numerical pitfalls of prior art. Our method can handle possible challenging scenarios: (1) when calibration matrices exceed GPU memory capacity, (2) when input activation matrices are nearly singular, and even (3) when insufficient data prevents unique approximation. For the latter, we prove that our solution converges to a desired approximation and derive explicit error bounds.
中摘: 最近的研究表明，情境感知的低秩近似是一种有用的工具，用于压缩和微调现代大规模神经网络。在这种类型的近似中，范数由输入激活的矩阵加权，与未加权的情况相比，显著提高了指标。然而，现有的神经网络方法由于依赖于涉及显式Gram矩阵计算及其后续求逆的经典公式，因此遭受数值不稳定的影响。我们证明了这可能会降低近似质量或导致数值上奇异的矩阵。

为了解决这些限制，我们提出了一种新颖的无需求逆的正则化框架，该框架完全基于稳定的分解，克服了先前技术的数值陷阱。我们的方法可以处理可能的挑战性场景：(1)当校准矩阵超过GPU内存容量时，(2)当输入激活矩阵接近奇异时，甚至(3)当数据不足无法进行唯一近似时。对于最后一种情况，我们证明了我们的解决方案收敛到期望的近似，并推导出了明确的误差界限。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.4] 3BASiL: An Algorithmic Framework for Sparse plus Low-Rank Compression of LLMs
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 3BASiL：一种用于稀疏加低秩压缩大型语言模型的算法框架
类别: optimization
作者: Mehdi Makni;Xiang Meng;Rahul Mazumder;
机构: Massachusetts Institute of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=byNNv5Et10
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01376
英摘: Sparse plus Low-Rank $(\mathbf{S} + \mathbf{LR})$ decomposition of Large Language Models (LLMs) has emerged as a promising direction in model compression, aiming to decompose pre-trained model weights into a sum of sparse and low-rank matrices $(\mathbf{W} \approx \mathbf{S} + \mathbf{LR})$. Despite recent progress, existing methods often suffer from substantial performance degradation compared to dense models. In this work, we introduce 3BASiL-TM, an efficient one-shot post-training method for $(\mathbf{S} + \mathbf{LR})$ decomposition of LLMs that addresses this gap. Our approach first introduces a novel 3-Block Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) method, termed 3BASiL, to minimize the layer-wise reconstruction error with convergence guarantees. We then design an efficient transformer-matching (TM) refinement step that jointly optimizes the sparse and low-rank components across transformer layers. This step minimizes a novel memory-efficient loss that aligns outputs at the transformer level. Notably, the TM procedure is universal as it can enhance any $(\mathbf{S} + \mathbf{LR})$ decomposition, including pure sparsity. Our numerical experiments show that 3BASiL-TM reduces the WikiText2 perplexity gap relative to dense LLaMA-8B model by over 30% under a (2:4 Sparse + 64 LR) configuration, compared to prior methods. Moreover, our method achieves over 2.5x faster compression runtime on an A100 GPU compared to SOTA $(\mathbf{S} + \mathbf{LR})$ method. Our code is available at https://github.com/mazumder-lab/3BASiL.
中摘: 大型语言模型(LLMs)的稀疏加低秩(\(\mathbf{S} + \mathbf{LR}\))分解作为模型压缩的一个有前景的方向已经崭露头角，其目标是将预训练模型权重分解为稀疏矩阵和低秩矩阵之和(\(\mathbf{W} \approx \mathbf{S} + \mathbf{LR}\))。尽管近期取得了进展，现有方法通常在性能上相比密集模型存在显著下降。在此工作中，我们引入了3BASiL-TM，这是一种针对LLMs的\(\mathbf{S} + \mathbf{LR}\)分解的有效的一次性后训练方法，旨在弥补这一差距。我们的方法首先引入了一种新颖的三块交替方向乘子法(ADMM)，称为3BASiL，以最小化层间重建误差并保证收敛性。然后，我们设计了一个高效的Transformer匹配(TM)优化步骤，该步骤跨Transformer层联合优化稀疏和低秩组件。这一步骤最小化了一个新颖的记忆效率损失，使输出在Transformer层面保持一致。值得注意的是，TM过程具有普遍性，因为它可以增强任何\(\mathbf{S} + \mathbf{LR}\)分解，包括纯稀疏情况。我们的数值实验表明，在(2:4 稀疏 + 64 LR)配置下，与先前方法相比，3BASiL-TM相对于密集型LLaMA-8B模型减少了超过30%的WikiText2困惑度差距。此外，我们的方法在A100 GPU上的压缩运行时间比最先进的\(\mathbf{S} + \mathbf{LR}\)方法快2.5倍以上。我们的代码可在https://github.com/mazumder-lab/3BASiL获取。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.5] Generalized Top-k Mallows Model for Ranked Choices
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 广义Top-k Mallows模型用于排序选择
类别: theory
作者: Shahrzad Haddadan;Sara Ahmadian;
机构: Rutgers University;Google;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=jVwIfsJLvh
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22040
英摘: The classic Mallows model is a foundational tool for modeling user preferences. However, it has limitations in capturing real-world scenarios, where users often focus only on a limited set of preferred items and are indifferent to the rest. To address this, extensions such as the top-k Mallows model have been proposed, aligning better with practical applications. In this paper, we address several challenges related to the generalized top-k Mallows model, with a focus on analyzing buyer choices. Our key contributions are: (1) a novel sampling scheme tailored to generalized top-k Mallows models, (2) an efficient algorithm for computing choice probabilities under this model, and (3) an active learning algorithm for estimating the model parameters from observed choice data. These contributions provide new tools for analysis and prediction in critical decision-making scenarios. We present a rigorous mathematical analysis for the performance of our algorithms. Furthermore, through extensive experiments on synthetic data and real-world data, we demonstrate the scalability and accuracy of our proposed methods, and we compare the predictive power of Mallows model for top-k lists compared to the simpler Multinomial Logit model.
中摘: 经典的马洛斯模型是模拟用户偏好的基础工具。然而，它在捕捉现实场景方面存在局限性，因为在这些场景中，用户通常只关注有限的偏好项目集，对其他项目则无差别。为了解决这一问题，提出了诸如前k项马洛斯模型等扩展，更好地与实际应用相吻合。在这篇论文中，我们针对广义前k项马洛斯模型相关的几个挑战进行了探讨，重点分析了买家的选择行为。我们的主要贡献包括：(1)一种针对广义前k项马洛斯模型的新型抽样方案，(2)在此模型下计算选择概率的有效算法，以及(3)一种主动学习算法，用于从观察到的选择数据中估计模型参数。这些贡献为关键决策场景下的分析和预测提供了新工具。我们对算法性能进行了严谨的数学分析。此外，通过在合成数据和真实世界数据上的广泛实验，我们展示了所提出方法的可扩展性和准确性，并比较了前k项列表的马洛斯模型与更简单的多项式逻辑模型在预测能力上的差异。

[No.6] GaRA-SAM: Robustifying Segment Anything Model with Gated-Rank Adaptation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: GaRA-SAM: 基于门控秩适应性增强的Segment Anything模型
类别: applications
作者: Sohyun Lee;Yeho Gwon;Lukas Hoyer;Suha Kwak;
机构: Pohang University of Science and Technology;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=VXygIRRHxz
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.02882
英摘: Improving robustness of the Segment Anything Model (SAM) to input degradations is critical for its deployment in high-stakes applications such as autonomous driving and robotics. Our approach to this challenge prioritizes three key aspects: first, parameter efficiency to maintain the inherent generalization capability of SAM; second, fine-grained and input-aware robustification to precisely address the input corruption; and third, adherence to standard training protocols for ease of training. To this end, we propose gated-rank adaptation (GaRA). GaRA introduces lightweight adapters into intermediate layers of the frozen SAM, where each adapter dynamically adjusts the effective rank of its weight matrix based on the input by selectively activating (rank-1) components of the matrix using a learned gating module. This adjustment enables fine-grained and input-aware robustification without compromising the generalization capability of SAM. Our model, GaRA-SAM, significantly outperforms prior work on all robust segmentation benchmarks. In particular, it surpasses the previous best IoU score by up to 21.3\%p on ACDC, a challenging real corrupted image dataset.
中摘: 提高Segment Anything Model（SAM）在输入降质情况下的鲁棒性对于其在高风险应用（如自动驾驶和机器人技术）中的部署至关重要。我们应对这一挑战的方法优先考虑三个方面：首先，参数效率，以保持SAM固有的泛化能力；其次，细粒度和输入感知的鲁棒化，以精确解决输入污染；第三，遵循标准训练协议，便于训练。为此，我们提出了门控等级适应（GaRA）。GaRA在冻结的SAM中间层中引入轻量级适配器，每个适配器根据输入动态调整其权重矩阵的有效秩，通过学习的门控模块有选择地激活矩阵的（秩1）组件来实现。这种调整使细粒度和输入感知的鲁棒性得以实现，而不损害SAM的泛化能力。我们的模型，GaRA-SAM，在所有鲁棒分割基准测试中显著优于先前的工作。特别是，在ACDC上，一个具有挑战性的现实降质图像数据集，它超过了之前最好的IoU分数高达21.3个百分点。

[No.7] Towards a Pairwise Ranking Model with Orderliness and Monotonicity for Label Enhancement

[No.8] Fira: Can We Achieve Full-rank Training of LLMs Under Low-rank Constraint?
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: Fira: 在低秩约束下，我们能否实现LLMs的满秩训练？
类别: deep learning
作者: Xi Chen;Kaituo Feng;Changsheng Li;Xunhao Lai;Xiangyu Yue;Ye Yuan;Guoren Wang;
机构: Beijing Institute of Technology;Chinese University of Hong Kong;Peking University;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=7aSBAw7tJf
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01623
英摘: Low-rank training has emerged as a promising approach for reducing memory usage in training Large Language Models (LLMs). Previous methods either rely on decomposing weight matrices (e.g., LoRA), or seek to decompose gradient matrices (e.g., GaLore) to ensure reduced memory consumption. However, both of them constrain the training in a low-rank subspace, thus inevitably leading to sub-optimal performance. This raises a question: whether it is possible to consistently preserve the low-rank constraint for memory efficiency, while achieving full-rank training (i.e., training with full-rank gradients of full-rank weights) to avoid inferior outcomes? In this paper, we propose a new plug-and-play training framework for LLMs called Fira, as the first attempt to achieve this goal. First, we observe an interesting phenomenon during LLM training: the scaling impact of adaptive optimizers (e.g., Adam) on the gradient norm remains similar from low-rank to full-rank training. Based on this observation, we propose a norm-based scaling method, which utilizes the scaling impact of low-rank optimizers as substitutes for that of original full-rank optimizers to enable full-rank training. In this way, we can preserve the low-rank constraint in the optimizer while achieving full-rank training for better performance. Moreover, we find that there are sudden gradient rises during the optimization process, potentially causing loss spikes. To address this, we further put forward a norm-growth limiter to smooth the gradient via regulating the relative increase of gradient norms. Extensive experiments on the pre-training and fine-tuning of LLMs show that Fira outperforms both LoRA and GaLore, achieving performance that is comparable to or even better than full-rank training.
中摘: 低秩训练作为一种有前景的方法，已经浮出水面，旨在减少在训练大型语言模型（LLM）时的内存使用。先前的方法要么依赖于分解权重矩阵（例如，LoRA），要么试图分解梯度矩阵（例如，GaLore），以确保降低内存消耗。然而，这两种方法都将训练限制在一个低秩子空间内，从而不可避免地导致次优性能。这就引出了一个问题：是否有可能在保持低秩约束以提高内存效率的同时，实现全秩训练（即使用全秩权重的全秩梯度进行训练），以避免较差的结果？在这篇论文中，我们提出了一种名为Fira的新插件式训练框架，这是首次尝试达到这一目标，专为LLM设计。首先，我们在LLM训练过程中观察到了一个有趣的现象：自适应优化器（如Adam）对梯度范数的缩放影响从低秩到全秩训练保持相似。基于这一观察，我们提出了一种基于范数的缩放方法，利用低秩优化器的缩放影响作为原始全秩优化器的替代品，从而实现全秩训练。这样，我们可以在优化器中保留低秩约束，同时实现全秩训练以获得更佳性能。此外，我们发现优化过程中存在梯度突然增加的情况，这可能导致损失值激增。为了解决这个问题，我们进一步提出了一种范数增长限制器来通过调节梯度范数的相对增加来平滑梯度。在LLM预训练和微调上的广泛实验表明，Fira超越了LoRA和GaLore，其性能与全秩训练相当甚至更好。

[No.9] RankMatch: A Novel Approach to Semi-Supervised Label Distribution Learning Leveraging Rank Correlation between Labels
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: RankMatch：一种利用标签间秩相关性进行半监督标签分布学习的新方法
类别: general machine learning
作者: Zhiqiang Kou;Yucheng Xie;Hailin Wang;Junyang Chen;Jing Wang;Ming-Kun Xie;Shuo Chen;Yuheng Jia;Tongliang Liu;Xin Geng;
机构: RIKEN;Southeast University;Xi'an Jiao Tong University;;Nanjing University;University of Sydney;Mohamed bin Zayed University of Artificial Intelligence;

[No.10] MokA: Multimodal Low-Rank Adaptation for MLLMs
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: MokA: 多模态低秩适应性技术用于元大型语言模型
类别: deep learning
作者: Yake Wei;Yu Miao;Dongzhan Zhou;Di Hu;
机构: Renmin University of China;Beijing University of Posts and Telecommunications;Shanghai AI Laboratory;
类型: Oral
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=oJ84bedrtM
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05191
英摘: In this paper, we reveal that most current efficient multimodal fine-tuning methods are hindered by a key limitation: they are directly borrowed from LLMs, often neglecting the intrinsic differences of multimodal scenarios and even affecting the full utilization of all modalities. Inspired by our empirical observation, we argue that unimodal adaptation and cross-modal adaptation are two essential parts for the effective fine-tuning of MLLMs. From this perspective, we propose Multimodal low-rank Adaptation (MokA), a multimodal-aware efficient fine-tuning strategy that takes multimodal characteristics into consideration. It compresses unimodal information by modality-specific parameters while explicitly enhancing cross-modal interaction, ensuring both unimodal and cross-modal adaptation. Extensive experiments cover three representative multimodal scenarios (audio-visual-text, visual-text, and speech-text), and multiple LLM backbones (LLaMA2/3, Qwen2, Qwen2.5-VL, etc). Consistent improvements indicate the efficacy and versatility of the proposed method. Ablation studies and efficiency evaluation are also conducted to fully asses our method. Overall, we think MokA provides a more targeted solution for efficient adaptation of MLLMs, paving the way for further exploration. The project page is at https://gewu-lab.github.io/MokA.
中摘: 在本文中，我们揭示了当前大多数高效的多模态微调方法受到一个关键限制：它们直接借鉴自大型语言模型（LLMs），常常忽视了多模态场景的内在差异，甚至影响了所有模态的充分利用。受我们的实证观察启发，我们认为单模态适应和跨模态适应是有效微调多模态大型语言模型（MLLMs）的两个基本组成部分。基于这一视角，我们提出了多模态低秩适应（MokA），这是一种考虑到多模态特性的高效微调策略。它通过模态特定参数压缩单模态信息，同时明确增强跨模态交互，确保了单模态和跨模态的适应性。广泛的实验涵盖了三种代表性的多模态场景（视听文本、视觉文本和语音文本），以及多个LLM骨干网络（如LLaMA2/3、Qwen2、Qwen2.5-VL等）。一致的改进表明了所提出方法的有效性和通用性。我们也进行了消融研究和效率评估，以全面评估我们的方法。总体而言，我们认为MokA为MLLMs的高效适应提供了一个更具针对性的解决方案，为未来的探索铺平了道路。项目页面位于https://gewu-lab.github.io/MokA。

[No.11] On the rankability of visual embeddings
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 视觉嵌入的可排序性
类别: deep learning
作者: Ankit Sonthalia;Arnas Uselis;Seong Joon Oh;
机构: ;Parameter Lab;Eberhard Karls University of Tübingen;

链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=sPbsnUCyNt
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.03683
英摘: We study whether visual embedding models capture continuous, ordinal attributes along linear directions, which we term _rank axes_. We define a model as _rankable_ for an attribute if projecting embeddings onto such an axis preserves the attribute's order. Across 7 popular encoders and 9 datasets with attributes like age, crowd count, head pose, aesthetics, and recency, we find that many embeddings are inherently rankable. Surprisingly, a small number of samples, or even just two extreme examples, often suffice to recover meaningful rank axes, without full-scale supervision. These findings open up new use cases for image ranking in vector databases and motivate further study into the structure and learning of rankable embeddings. Our code is available at https://github.com/aktsonthalia/rankable-vision-embeddings.
中摘: 我们研究视觉嵌入模型是否能在线性方向上捕捉连续的、序数属性，我们将这些方向称为“排名轴”。如果将嵌入投影到这样的轴上能保持属性的顺序，我们就定义该模型对某个属性是“可排名”的。在7种流行的编码器和9个带有如年龄、人群数量、头部姿态、美学和新旧程度等属性的数据集上，我们发现许多嵌入本质上是可排名的。令人惊讶的是，少量样本，甚至仅仅两个极端例子，通常就足以恢复有意义的排名轴，而无需全面的监督。这些发现为向量数据库中的图像排序开辟了新的应用场景，并激发了进一步研究可排名嵌入的结构和学习的动力。我们的代码可在https://github.com/aktsonthalia/rankable-vision-embeddings获取。

[No.12] PoLAR: Polar-Decomposed Low-Rank Adapter Representation
来源: NeurIPS 2025

标题: PoLAR：极分解低秩适配器表示
类别: deep learning
作者: Kai Lion;Liang Zhang;Bingcong Li;Niao He;

链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=jDxFD45kkc
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.03133
英摘: We show that low-rank adaptation of large-scale models suffers from a low stable rank that is well below the linear algebraic rank of the subspace, degrading fine-tuning performance. To mitigate the underutilization of the allocated subspace, we propose PoLAR, a parameterization inspired by the polar decomposition that factorizes the low-rank update into two direction matrices constrained to Stiefel manifolds and an unconstrained scale matrix. Our theory shows that PoLAR yields an exponentially faster convergence rate on a canonical low-rank adaptation problem. 中摘: 我们发现大规模模型的低秩适应面临着稳定秩过低的问题，远低于子空间的线性代数秩，从而降低了微调性能。为了缓解分配的子空间未充分利用的问题，我们提出了PoLAR，这是一种受极分解启发的参数化方法，它将低秩更新分解为两个受限于Stiefel流形的方向矩阵和一个无约束的尺度矩阵。我们的理论表明，PoLAR在标准的低秩适应问题上能带来指数级更快的收敛速度。将该参数化方法与黎曼优化相结合，在测试通用语言理解、常识推理和数学问题解决的三个不同基准上，对于基础模型大小从3.5亿到270亿范围内的模型，均取得了持续的提升。

[No.19] Ravan: Multi-Head Low-Rank Adaptation for Federated Fine-Tuning
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 拉万：多头低秩适应性 federated 微调
类别: deep learning
作者: Arian Raje;Baris Askin;Divyansh Jhunjhunwala;Gauri Joshi;
机构: Carnegie Mellon University;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=gyn4n8oC9B
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05568
英摘: Large language models (LLMs) have not yet effectively leveraged the vast amounts of edge-device data, and federated learning (FL) offers a promising paradigm to collaboratively fine-tune LLMs without transferring private edge data to the cloud. To operate within the computation and communication constraints of edge devices, recent literature on federated fine-tuning of LLMs proposes the use of low-rank adaptation (LoRA) and similar parameter-efficient methods. However, LoRA-based methods suffer from accuracy degradation in FL settings, primarily because of data and computational heterogeneity across clients. We propose Ravan, an adaptive multi-head LoRA method that balances parameter efficiency and model expressivity by reparameterizing the weight updates as the sum of multiple LoRA heads $s_i\textbf{B}_i\textbf{H}_i\textbf{A}_i$ in which only the core matrices $\textbf{H}_i$ and their lightweight scaling factors $s_i$ are trained. These trainable scaling factors let the optimization focus on the most useful heads, recovering a higher-rank approximation of the full update without increasing the number of communicated parameters since clients upload $s_i\textbf{H}_i$ directly. Experiments on vision and language benchmarks show that Ravan improves test accuracy by $2-8\%$ over prior parameter-efficient baselines, making it a robust and scalable solution for federated fine-tuning of LLMs.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLM）尚未有效利用边缘设备上的海量数据，而联邦学习（FL）提供了一个有前景的范式，可以在不将私有边缘数据传输到云端的情况下协作微调LLM。为了在边缘设备的计算和通信限制下运行，最近关于LLM联邦微调的文献提出了使用低秩适应（LoRA）和其他类似的参数高效方法。然而，在FL设置中，基于LoRA的方法会遭受准确度下降的问题，主要是因为客户间的数据和计算异质性。我们提出了Ravan，一种自适应多头LoRA方法，通过将权重更新重新参数化为多个LoRA头部$s_i\textbf{B}_i\textbf{H}_i\textbf{A}_i$之和，平衡了参数效率和模型表达力，其中仅训练核心矩阵$\textbf{H}_i$及其轻量级缩放因子$s_i$。这些可训练的缩放因子使优化专注于最有用的头部，恢复了完整更新的更高秩近似，而不会增加通信参数的数量，因为客户端直接上传$s_i\textbf{H}_i$。在视觉和语言基准测试上的实验表明，Ravan将测试准确度提高了2%-8%，超过了先前的参数高效基线，使其成为LLM联邦微调的稳健且可扩展的解决方案。

[No.20] Revisiting Frank-Wolfe for Structured Nonconvex Optimization
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 重新审视弗兰克-沃尔夫算法在结构化非凸优化中的应用
类别: optimization
作者: Hoomaan Maskan;Yikun Hou;Suvrit Sra;Alp Yurtsever;
机构: Umeå University;;Technische Universität München;Massachusetts Institute of Technology;

链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=hJRklWq4Ah
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08921
英摘: We introduce a new projection-free (Frank-Wolfe) method for optimizing structured nonconvex functions that are expressed as a difference of two convex functions. This problem class subsumes smooth nonconvex minimization, positioning our method as a promising alternative to the classical Frank-Wolfe algorithm. DC decompositions are not unique; by carefully selecting a decomposition, we can better exploit the problem structure, improve computational efficiency, and adapt to the underlying problem geometry to find better local solutions. We prove that the proposed method achieves a first-order stationary point in $O(1/ε^2)$ iterations, matching the complexity of the standard Frank-Wolfe algorithm for smooth nonconvex minimization in general. Specific decompositions can, for instance, yield a gradient-efficient variant that requires only $O(1/ε)$ calls to the gradient oracle. Finally, we present numerical experiments demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed method compared to other projection-free algorithms.
中摘: 我们介绍了一种新的无投影（弗兰克-沃尔夫）方法，用于优化表示为两个凸函数之差的结构化非凸函数。这一问题类别涵盖了平滑非凸最小化问题，使我们的方法成为传统弗兰克-沃尔夫算法的一个有前景的替代方案。DC分解不是唯一的；通过仔细选择分解方式，我们可以更好地利用问题结构，提高计算效率，并适应底层问题几何形状，以找到更好的局部解。我们证明了所提出的方法在$O(1/ε^2)$次迭代内达到一阶驻点，与一般平滑非凸最小化问题中标准弗兰克-沃尔夫算法的复杂度相匹配。特定的分解，例如，可以产生一个梯度高效的变体，只需要$O(1/ε)$次调用梯度算子。最后，我们展示了数值实验，证明了所提方法与其他无投影算法相比的有效性。

[No.21] RankSEG-RMA: An Efficient Segmentation Algorithm via Reciprocal Moment Approximation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: RankSEG-RMA：一种通过互反矩近似实现的高效分割算法
类别: general machine learning
作者: Zixun Wang;Ben Dai;
机构: Chinese University of Hong Kong;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=4tRMm1JJhw
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.15362
英摘: Semantic segmentation labels each pixel in an image with its corresponding class, and is typically evaluated using the Intersection over Union (IoU) and Dice metrics to quantify the overlap between predicted and ground-truth segmentation masks. In the literature, most existing methods estimate pixel-wise class probabilities, then apply argmax or thresholding to obtain the final prediction. These methods have been shown to generally lead to inconsistent or suboptimal results, as they do not directly maximize segmentation metrics. To address this issue, a novel consistent segmentation framework, RankSEG, has been proposed, which includes RankDice and RankIoU specifically designed to optimize the Dice and IoU metrics, respectively. Although RankSEG almost guarantees improved performance, it suffers from two major drawbacks. First, it is its computational expense-RankDice has a complexity of O(d log d) with a substantial constant factor (where d represents the number of pixels), while RankIoU exhibits even higher complexity O(d^2), thus limiting its practical application. For instance, in LiTS, prediction with RankSEG takes 16.33 seconds compared to just 0.01 seconds with the argmax rule. Second, RankSEG is only applicable to overlapping segmentation settings, where multiple classes can occupy the same pixel, which contrasts with standard benchmarks that typically assume non-overlapping segmentation. In this paper, we overcome these two drawbacks via a reciprocal moment approximation (RMA) of RankSEG with the following contributions: (i) we improve RankSEG using RMA, namely RankSEG-RMA, reduces the complexity of both algorithms to O(d) while maintaining comparable performance; (ii) inspired by RMA, we develop a pixel-wise score function that allows efficient implementation for non-overlapping segmentation settings.
中摘: 语义分割为图像中的每个像素标记其对应的类别，并通常使用交并比（IoU）和Dice指标来量化预测分割掩模与真实值分割掩模之间的重叠。在文献中，大多数现有方法估计像素级的类别概率，然后应用argmax或阈值处理以获得最终预测。这些方法已经被证明通常会导致不一致或次优的结果，因为它们没有直接最大化分割指标。为了解决这个问题，提出了一种新颖的一致性分割框架，RankSEG，其中包括专门设计用于优化Dice和IoU指标的RankDice和RankIoU。尽管RankSEG几乎保证了性能提升，但它存在两个主要缺点。首先，计算成本高-RankDice的复杂度为O(d log d)，其中包含一个相当大的常数因子（d代表像素数量），而RankIoU的复杂度更高，达到O(d^2)，这限制了它的实际应用。例如，在LiTS数据集上，使用RankSEG进行预测需要16.33秒，而使用argmax规则只需0.01秒。其次，RankSEG仅适用于重叠分割设置，即多个类别可以占据同一像素，这与标准基准测试通常假设的非重叠分割形成对比。在这篇论文中，我们通过RankSEG的互倒矩近似（RMA）克服了这两个缺点，具体贡献如下：(i) 我们使用RMA改进了RankSEG，即RankSEG-RMA，将两种算法的复杂度降低至O(d)，同时保持相似的性能；(ii) 受RMA启发，我们开发了一个像素级的评分函数，使非重叠分割设置下的高效实现成为可能。

[No.22] VisualQuality-R1: Reasoning-Induced Image Quality Assessment via Reinforcement Learning to Rank
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 视觉质量-R1：通过强化学习排序进行的推理诱导图像质量评估
类别: applications
作者: Tianhe Wu;Jian Zou;Jie Liang;Lei Zhang;Kede Ma;
机构: City University of Hong Kong;Tsinghua University;Harbin Institute of Technology;OPPO Mobile Telecommunications Corp.,Ltd.;Hong Kong Polytechnic University;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.14460
英摘: DeepSeek-R1 has demonstrated remarkable effectiveness in incentivizing reasoning and generalization capabilities of large language models (LLMs) through reinforcement learning. Nevertheless, the potential of reasoning-induced computation has not been thoroughly explored in the context of image quality assessment (IQA), a task depending critically on visual reasoning. In this paper, we introduce VisualQuality-R1, a reasoning-induced no-reference IQA (NR-IQA) model, and we train it with reinforcement learning to rank, a learning algorithm tailored to the intrinsically relative nature of visual quality. Specifically, for a pair of images, we employ group relative policy optimization to generate multiple quality scores for each image. These estimates are used to compute comparative probabilities of one image having higher quality than the other under the Thurstone model. Rewards for each quality estimate are defined using continuous fidelity measures rather than discretized binary labels. Extensive experiments show that the proposed VisualQuality-R1 consistently outperforms discriminative deep learning-based NR-IQA models as well as a recent reasoning-induced quality regression method. Moreover, VisualQuality-R1 is capable of generating contextually rich, human-aligned quality descriptions, and supports multi-dataset training without requiring perceptual scale realignment. These features make VisualQuality-R1 especially well-suited for reliably measuring progress in a wide range of image processing tasks like super-resolution and image generation.
中摘: DeepSeek-R1已经证明了其在通过强化学习激励大型语言模型(LLM)推理和泛化能力方面的显著效果。然而，在图像质量评估(IQA)这一关键依赖于视觉推理的任务中，推理诱导计算的潜力尚未得到充分探索。在本文中，我们引入了VisualQuality-R1，这是一种基于推理诱导的无参考IQA（NR-IQA）模型，并使用针对视觉质量固有相对性的强化学习算法进行训练，即排名强化学习。具体而言，对于一对图像，我们采用组相对策略优化来为每张图像生成多个质量评分。这些估计值用于根据瑟斯顿模型计算一个图像比另一个图像质量更高的比较概率。每个质量估计的奖励定义使用连续保真度度量，而非离散化的二进制标签。广泛的实验表明，所提出的VisualQuality-R1始终超越了基于判别深度学习的NR-IQA模型以及最近的推理诱导质量回归方法。此外，VisualQuality-R1能够生成与人类对齐的、上下文丰富的质量描述，并且支持无需感知尺度重新调整的多数据集训练。这些特点使得VisualQuality-R1特别适合于可靠地衡量超分辨率和图像生成等广泛图像处理任务中的进展。

[No.23] Beyond Higher Rank: Token-wise Input-Output Projections for Efficient Low-Rank Adaptation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 超越高秩：基于令牌的输入输出投影以实现高效的低秩适应
类别: deep learning
作者: Shiwei Li;Xiandi Luo;Haozhao Wang;Xing Tang;Ziqiang Cui;Dugang Liu;Yuhua Li;xiuqiang He;Ruixuan Li;
机构: Huazhong University of Science and Technology;Beijing University of Posts and Telecommunications;Shenzhen Technology University;Tencent;;Shenzhen University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=w2xl15ZbD3
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.23123
英摘: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is a parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) method widely used in large language models (LLMs). LoRA essentially describes the projection of an input space into a low-dimensional output space, with the dimensionality determined by the LoRA rank. In standard LoRA, all input tokens share the same weights and undergo an identical input-output projection. This limits LoRA's ability to capture token-specific information due to the inherent semantic differences among tokens. To address this limitation, we propose Token-wise Projected Low-Rank Adaptation (TopLoRA), which dynamically adjusts LoRA weights according to the input token, thereby learning token-wise input-output projections in an end-to-end manner. Formally, the weights of TopLoRA can be expressed as $BΣ_X A$, where $A$ and $B$ are low-rank matrices (as in standard LoRA), and $Σ_X$ is a diagonal matrix generated from each input token $X$. Notably, TopLoRA does not increase the rank of LoRA weights but achieves more granular adaptation by learning token-wise LoRA weights (i.e., token-wise input-output projections). Extensive experiments across multiple models and datasets demonstrate that TopLoRA consistently outperforms LoRA and its variants. The code is available at https://github.com/Leopold1423/toplora-neurips25.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）是一种参数高效的微调（PEFT）方法，在大型语言模型（LLM）中广泛应用。LoRA本质上描述了将输入空间投影到低维输出空间的过程，其维度由LoRA的秩决定。在标准LoRA中，所有输入标记共享相同的权重，并经历相同的输入-输出投影。这限制了LoRA捕获标记特定信息的能力，因为标记之间存在固有的语义差异。为了解决这一局限性，我们提出了基于标记的投影低秩适应（TopLoRA），它根据输入标记动态调整LoRA权重，从而以端到端的方式学习基于标记的输入-输出投影。形式上，TopLoRA的权重可以表示为$BΣ_X A$，其中$A$和$B$是低秩矩阵（与标准LoRA相同），而$Σ_X$是从每个输入标记$X$生成的对角矩阵。值得注意的是，TopLoRA并没有增加LoRA权重的秩，而是通过学习基于标记的LoRA权重（即，基于标记的输入-输出投影）实现了更精细的适应。在多个模型和数据集上的广泛实验表明，TopLoRA始终优于LoRA及其变体。代码可在https://github.com/Leopold1423/toplora-neurips25获取。

[No.24] Demystifying Language Model Forgetting with Low-rank Example Associations
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 解密低秩示例关联下的语言模型遗忘现象
类别: deep learning
作者: Xisen Jin;Xiang Ren;
机构: University of Southern California;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=u4j0LtCYid
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.14026
英摘: Large language models (LLMs) suffer from forgetting of upstream knowledge when fine-tuned. Despite efforts on mitigating forgetting, few have investigated how forgotten upstream examples are dependent on newly learned tasks. Insights on such dependencies enable efficient and targeted mitigation of forgetting. In this paper, we empirically analyze forgetting that occurs in $N$ upstream examples of language modeling or instruction-tuning after fine-tuning LLMs on one of $M$ new tasks, visualized in $M\times N$ matrices. We show that the matrices are often well-approximated with low-rank matrices, indicating the dominance of simple associations between the learned tasks and forgotten upstream examples. Leveraging the analysis, we predict forgetting of upstream examples when fine-tuning LLMs on unseen tasks with matrix completion over the empirical associations. This enables fast identification of most forgotten examples without expensive inference on the entire upstream data. Despite simplicity, the approach outperforms prior approaches that learn semantic relationships of learned tasks and upstream examples with LMs. We demonstrate the practical utility of our analysis by showing statistically significantly reduced forgetting as we upweight predicted examples for replay during fine-tuning. Code, data, and statistics collected: https://github.com/AuCson/low-rank-forgetting
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLM）在微调时会遗忘上游知识。尽管有努力减少遗忘，但很少有人研究遗忘的上游示例如何依赖于新学习的任务。对这种依赖性的洞察能够实现遗忘的有效和针对性缓解。在这篇论文中，我们实证分析了在微调LLM以适应$M$个新任务之一后，发生在$N$个上游语言建模或指令微调示例中的遗忘现象，并将结果可视化为$M \times N$矩阵。我们发现这些矩阵通常可以用低秩矩阵很好地近似，这表明学习任务与遗忘的上游示例之间存在简单的关联主导。利用这一分析，我们通过矩阵补全在经验关联上预测当微调LLM以适应未见任务时上游示例的遗忘。这使得无需对整个上游数据进行昂贵的推理就能快速识别最被遗忘的示例。尽管方法简单，但该方法在预测任务与上游示例之间的语义关系方面超越了之前使用语言模型（LM）学习的方法。我们通过展示在微调过程中对预测示例加权重播时统计显著减少的遗忘，证明了我们分析的实际效用。收集的代码、数据和统计信息：https://github.com/AuCson/low-rank-forgetting

[No.25] C-LoRA: Contextual Low-Rank Adaptation for Uncertainty Estimation in Large Language Models
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: C-LoRA：上下文低秩适应性调整在大型语言模型中的不确定性估计
类别: probabilistic methods
作者: Amir Hossein Rahmati;Sanket Jantre;Weifeng Zhang;Yucheng Wang;Byung-Jun Yoon;Nathan Urban;Xiaoning Qian;
机构: Texas A&M University;;Brookhaven National Laboratory;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17773
英摘: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) offers a cost-effective solution for fine-tuning large language models (LLMs), but it often produces overconfident predictions in data-scarce few-shot settings. To address this issue, several classical statistical learning approaches have been repurposed for scalable uncertainty-aware LoRA fine-tuning. However, these approaches neglect how input characteristics affect the predictive uncertainty estimates. To address this limitation, we propose Contextual Low-Rank Adaptation (C-LoRA) as a novel uncertainty-aware and parameter efficient fine-tuning approach, by developing new lightweight LoRA modules contextualized to each input data sample to dynamically adapt uncertainty estimates. Incorporating data-driven contexts into the parameter posteriors, C-LoRA mitigates overfitting, achieves well-calibrated uncertainties, and yields robust predictions. Extensive experiments on LLaMA2-7B models demonstrate that C-LoRA consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art uncertainty-aware LoRA methods in both uncertainty quantification and model generalization. Ablation studies further confirm the critical role of our contextual modules in capturing sample-specific uncertainties. C-LoRA sets a new standard for robust, uncertainty-aware LLM fine-tuning in few-shot regimes. Although our experiments are limited to 7B models, our method is architecture-agnostic and, in principle, applies beyond this scale; studying its scaling to larger models remains an open problem. Our code is available at https://github.com/ahra99/c_lora.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）为微调大型语言模型（LLM）提供了一种经济高效的方法，但在数据稀缺的少量样本设置中，它往往会产生过度自信的预测。为了解决这个问题，一些经典统计学习方法已经被重新用于可扩展的不确定性感知LoRA微调。然而，这些方法忽略了输入特征如何影响预测不确定性的估计。为了解决这一限制，我们提出了情境低秩适应（C-LoRA）作为一种新颖的不确定性感知和参数高效的微调方法，通过为每个输入数据样本开发新的轻量级LoRA模块来动态调整不确定性估计。将数据驱动的上下文融入参数后验分布中，C-LoRA减轻了过拟合问题，实现了良好的不确定性校准，并产生了稳健的预测。在LLaMA2-7B模型上的大量实验表明，C-LoRA在不确定性量化和模型泛化方面始终优于最先进的不确定性感知LoRA方法。消融研究进一步确认了我们的情境模块在捕捉特定样本不确定性中的关键作用。C-LoRA为在少量样本环境下进行鲁棒、不确定性感知的LLM微调树立了新的标准。尽管我们的实验仅限于7B模型，但我们的方法与架构无关，在原则上适用于更大规模的模型；将其扩展到更大模型的研究仍然是一个开放性问题。我们的代码可在https://github.com/ahra99/c_lora上获取。

[No.26] AcuRank: Uncertainty-Aware Adaptive Computation for Listwise Reranking
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: AcuRank：面向列表重排的不确定性意识自适应计算
类别: deep learning
作者: Soyoung Yoon;Gyuwan Kim;GYU-HWUNG CHO;seung-won hwang;
机构: Seoul National University;University of California, Santa Barbara;NAVER Corporation;

链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=H918WyPf0s
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.18512
英摘: Listwise reranking with large language models (LLMs) enhances top-ranked results in retrieval-based applications. Due to the limit in context size and high inference cost of long context, reranking is typically performed over a fixed size of small subsets, with the final ranking aggregated from these partial results. This fixed computation disregards query difficulty and document distribution, leading to inefficiencies. We propose AcuRank, an adaptive reranking framework that dynamically adjusts both the amount and target of computation based on uncertainty estimates over document relevance. Using a Bayesian TrueSkill model, we iteratively refine relevance estimates until reaching sufficient confidence levels, and our explicit modeling of ranking uncertainty enables principled control over reranking behavior and avoids unnecessary updates to confident predictions. Results on the TREC-DL and BEIR benchmarks show that our method consistently achieves a superior accuracy-efficiency trade-off and scales better with compute than fixed-computation baselines. These results highlight the effectiveness and generalizability of our method across diverse retrieval tasks and LLM-based reranking models.
中摘: 利用大型语言模型（LLMs）进行列表级重新排序，在基于检索的应用中提升了顶级结果的质量。由于上下文大小的限制和长上下文推理成本高，重新排序通常在固定大小的小子集上执行，最终排名由这些部分结果聚合而成。这种固定的计算方式忽略了查询难度和文档分布，导致效率低下。我们提出了AcuRank，一个自适应重新排序框架，根据对文档相关性的不确定性估计动态调整计算量和目标。通过使用贝叶斯TrueSkill模型，我们迭代地细化相关性估计直到达到足够的置信水平，我们对排名不确定性的明确建模使我们能够有原则地控制重新排序行为，并避免对已经置信的预测进行不必要的更新。在TREC-DL和BEIR基准测试上的结果显示，我们的方法在准确性和效率之间的权衡上始终优于固定计算基线，并且随着计算资源的增加，性能提升更好。这些结果凸显了我们的方法在不同检索任务和基于LLM的重新排序模型中的有效性和泛化能力。

[No.27] AuroRA: Breaking Low-Rank Bottleneck of LoRA with Nonlinear Mapping
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: AuroRA：打破LoRA低秩瓶颈的非线性映射
类别: deep learning
作者: Haonan Dong;Wenhao Zhu;Guojie Song;Liang Wang;
机构: Peking University;Tongji University;Alibaba Group;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=2hgHyoyVWj
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.18738
英摘: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is a widely adopted parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) method validated across NLP and CV domains. However, LoRA faces an inherent low-rank bottleneck: narrowing its performance gap with full finetuning requires increasing the rank of its parameter matrix, resulting in significant parameter overhead. Recent linear LoRA variants have attempted to enhance expressiveness by introducing additional linear mappings; however, their composition remains inherently linear and fails to fundamentally improve LoRA's representational capacity. To address this limitation, we propose AuroRA, which incorporates an Adaptive Nonlinear Layer (ANL) between two linear projectors to capture fixed and learnable nonlinearities. This combination forms an MLP-like structure with a compressed rank, enabling flexible and precise approximation of diverse target functions while theoretically guaranteeing lower approximation errors and bounded gradients. Extensive experiments on 22 datasets and 6 pretrained models demonstrate that AuroRA: (I) not only matches or surpasses full fine-tuning performance with only 6.18% ~ 25% of LoRA's parameters but also (II) outperforms competitive PEFT methods by up to 10.88% in both NLP and CV tasks, and (III) exhibits robust performance across various rank configurations.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）是一种在自然语言处理（NLP）和计算机视觉（CV）领域广泛采用的参数高效微调（PEFT）方法。然而，LoRA面临着固有的低秩瓶颈：要缩小与全量微调性能差距，需要增加其参数矩阵的秩，这会导致显著的参数开销增加。近期的线性LoRA变体试图通过引入额外的线性映射来增强表达能力；然而，这些组合本质上仍然是线性的，并未能从根本上提升LoRA的表现力。为了解决这一局限性，我们提出了AuroRA，它在两个线性投影器之间加入了一个自适应非线性层（ANL），用于捕捉固定的和可学习的非线性关系。这种结合形成了一个类似多层感知机（MLP）的结构，但具有压缩的秩，能够在理论上保证较低的逼近误差和有界的梯度的同时，灵活而精确地逼近各种目标函数。在22个数据集和6个预训练模型上的大量实验表明，AuroRA：(I)仅使用LoRA参数数量的6.18%至25%，就能达到或超过全量微调的性能；(II)在NLP和CV任务上，相对于竞争性的PEFT方法，最多能提高10.88%的性能；(III)在不同秩配置下展现出稳健的性能表现。

[No.28] Efficient Low Rank Attention for Long-Context Inference in Large Language Models
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 高效低秩注意力机制在大型语言模型长期上下文推理中的应用
类别: deep learning
作者: Li Tenghui;Guoxu Zhou;Xuyang ZHAO;Yuning Qiu;Qibin Zhao;
机构: RIKEN;Guangdong University of Technology;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=Mc0eJHZhW5
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.23649
英摘: As the length of input text increases, the key-value (KV) cache in LLMs imposes prohibitive GPU memory costs and limits long-context inference on resource constrained devices. Existing approaches, such as KV quantization and pruning, reduce memory usage but suffer from numerical precision loss or suboptimal retention of key-value pairs. In this work, Low Rank Query and Key attention (LRQK) is introduced, a two-stage framework that jointly decomposes full-precision query and key matrices into compact rank-\(r\) factors during the prefill stage, and then employs these low-dimensional projections to compute proxy attention scores in \(\mathcal{O}(lr)\) time at each decode step. By selecting only the top-\(k\) tokens and a small fixed set of recent tokens, LRQK employs a mixed GPU-CPU cache with a hit-and-miss mechanism where only missing full-precision KV pairs are transferred, thereby preserving exact attention outputs while reducing CPU-GPU data movement. Extensive experiments on the RULER and LongBench benchmarks with LLaMA-3-8B and Qwen2.5-7B demonstrate that LRQK matches or surpasses leading sparse-attention methods in long context settings, while delivering significant memory savings with minimal accuracy loss. Our code is available at https://github.com/tenghuilee/LRQK.
中摘: 随着输入文本长度的增加，大型语言模型（LLMs）中的键值（KV）缓存对GPU内存成本造成了巨大的负担，并在资源受限的设备上限制了长上下文推理。现有的方法，如KV量化和剪枝，虽然减少了内存使用，但遭受了数值精度损失或保留键值对的次优效果。在这项工作中，我们引入了一种名为低秩查询与键注意力（LRQK）的两阶段框架，它在预填充阶段将全精度查询和键矩阵联合分解为紧凑的秩-r因子，然后在每个解码步骤中利用这些低维投影以\(\mathcal{O}(lr)\)的时间复杂度计算代理注意力得分。通过仅选择前-k个令牌和一小套最近的固定令牌，LRQK采用混合的GPU-CPU缓存，其中包含命中和未命中的机制，只有缺失的全精度KV对被传输，从而在减少CPU-GPU数据移动的同时保持精确的注意力输出。在RULER和LongBench基准测试上，使用LLaMA-3-8B和Qwen2.5-7B进行的广泛实验表明，LRQK在长上下文设置下匹配甚至超越了领先的稀疏注意力方法，同时提供了显著的内存节省，而准确性损失极小。我们的代码可在https://github.com/tenghuilee/LRQK获取。

[No.29] GoRA: Gradient-driven Adaptive Low Rank Adaptation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: GoRA：梯度驱动自适应低秩适应
类别: deep learning
作者: haonan he;Peng Ye;Yuchen Ren;yuan yuan;LuyangZhou;ShucunJu;lei chen;

英摘: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is a crucial method for efficiently fine-tuning large language models (LLMs), with its effectiveness influenced by two key factors: rank selection and weight initialization. While numerous LoRA variants have been proposed to improve performance by addressing one of these aspects, they often compromise usability or computational efficiency. In this paper, we analyze and identify the core limitations of existing approaches and propose a novel framework--GoRA (Gradient-driven Adaptive Low Rank Adaptation)--that simultaneously adapts both the rank and initialization strategy within a unified framework. GoRA leverages gradient information during training to dynamically assign optimal ranks and initialize low-rank adapter weights in an adaptive manner. To our knowledge, GoRA is the first method that not only addresses the limitations of prior approaches--which often focus on either rank selection or initialization in isolation--but also unifies both aspects within a single framework, enabling more effective and efficient adaptation. Code is available at: https://github.com/hhnqqq/MyTransformers.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）是高效微调大型语言模型（LLM）的关键方法，其有效性受到两个关键因素的影响：秩选择和权重初始化。尽管已经提出了许多LoRA变体，通过解决其中一个方面来提高性能，但它们往往牺牲了可用性或计算效率。在这篇论文中，我们分析并识别了现有方法的核心局限性，并提出了一种新的框架——GoRA（梯度驱动自适应低秩适应），它在一个统一的框架内同时调整秩和初始化策略。GoRA在训练过程中利用梯度信息，以动态分配最优秩并以自适应方式初始化低秩适配器权重。据我们所知，GoRA是第一个不仅解决了先前方法的局限性——这些方法通常单独关注秩选择或初始化——而且在一个单一框架内统一这两个方面的方法，从而实现更有效和高效的适应。在各种架构和模态上的广泛实验表明，GoRA在保持vanilla LoRA效率的同时，始终优于现有的基于LoRA的方法。例如，在对Llama3.1-8B-Base进行数学推理的微调时，GoRA在标准LoRA的基础上提高了5.13分，在高秩设置下甚至比全量微调高出2.05分。代码可在以下链接获取：https://github.com/hhnqqq/MyTransformers。

[No.30] The Persistence of Neural Collapse Despite Low-Rank Bias
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 尽管存在低秩偏差，神经网络塌缩的持久性
类别: theory
作者: Connall Garrod;Jonathan P. Keating;
机构: University of Oxford;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=AumtzgHji1
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.23169
英摘: Neural collapse (NC) and its multi-layer variant, deep neural collapse (DNC), describe a structured geometry that occurs in the features and weights of trained deep networks. Recent theoretical work by Sukenik et al. using a deep unconstrained feature model (UFM) suggests that DNC is suboptimal under mean squared error (MSE) loss. They heuristically argue that this is due to low-rank bias induced by L2 regularization. In this work, we extend this result to deep UFMs trained with cross-entropy loss, showing that high-rank structures, including DNC, are not generally optimal. We characterize the associated low-rank bias, proving a fixed bound on the number of non-negligible singular values at global minima as network depth increases. We further analyze the loss surface, demonstrating that DNC is more prevalent in the landscape than other critical configurations, which we argue explains its frequent empirical appearance. Our results are validated through experiments in deep UFMs and deep neural networks.
中摘: 神经崩溃（NC）及其多层变体，深度神经崩溃（DNC），描述了在训练有素的深度网络的特征和权重中出现的结构化几何。Sukenik等人最近使用无约束特征模型（UFM）进行的理论工作表明，在均方误差（MSE）损失下，DNC是次优的。他们通过启发式论证指出，这是由于L2正则化引起的低秩偏置导致的。在这项工作中，我们将这一结果扩展到了使用交叉熵损失训练的深层UFM，证明了包括DNC在内的高秩结构通常不是最优的。我们表征了相关的低秩偏置，证明了随着网络深度增加，全局最小值处非微不足道的奇异值数量有一个固定界限。我们进一步分析了损失表面，证明了DNC在景观中的出现频率高于其他临界配置，我们认为这解释了其在经验上频繁出现的原因。我们的结果通过在深层UFM和深度神经网络中的实验得到了验证。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.31] Accurate and Efficient Low-Rank Model Merging in Core Space
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 核心空间中的准确高效低秩模型融合
类别: deep learning
作者: Aniello Panariello;Daniel Marczak;Simone Magistri;Angelo Porrello;Bartłomiej Twardowski;Andrew D. Bagdanov;Simone Calderara;Joost van de Weijer;

类型: Poster

英摘: In this paper, we address the challenges associated with merging low-rank adaptations of large neural networks. With the rise of parameter-efficient adaptation techniques, such as Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), model fine-tuning has become more accessible. While fine-tuning models with LoRA is highly efficient, existing merging methods often sacrifice this efficiency by merging fully-sized weight matrices. We propose the Core Space merging framework, which enables the merging of LoRA-adapted models within a common alignment basis, thereby preserving the efficiency of low-rank adaptation while substantially improving accuracy across tasks. We further provide a formal proof that projection into Core Space ensures no loss of information and provide a complexity analysis showing the efficiency gains. Extensive empirical results demonstrate that Core Space significantly improves existing merging techniques and achieves state-of-the-art results on both vision and language tasks while utilizing a fraction of the computational resources. Codebase is available at https://github.com/apanariello4/core-space-merging.
中摘: 在本文中，我们探讨了合并大型神经网络低秩适应所带来的挑战。随着参数高效适应技术的兴起，如低秩适应（LoRA），模型微调变得更加容易获取。虽然使用LoRA进行模型微调极为高效，但现有的合并方法往往通过合并全尺寸权重矩阵而牺牲了这种效率。我们提出了Core Space合并框架，它能够在共同的对齐基底内合并LoRA适应的模型，从而在保持低秩适应效率的同时，显著提高跨任务的准确性。我们进一步提供了正式证明，表明投影到Core Space可以确保信息无损，并提供复杂性分析，展示了效率提升。广泛的实证结果表明，Core Space大幅改进了现有的合并技术，在视觉和语言任务上均取得了最先进的成果，同时仅消耗了一小部分计算资源。代码库可在https://github.com/apanariello4/core-space-merging获取。

[No.32] From Condensation to Rank Collapse: A Two-Stage Analysis of Transformer Training Dynamics
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 从凝缩到秩崩溃：Transformer训练动力学的两阶段分析
类别: deep learning
作者: Zheng-An Chen;Tao Luo;
机构: Shanghai Jiao Tong University;;
类型: Oral
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=gm5mkiTGOy
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.06954
英摘: Although transformer-based models have shown exceptional empirical performance, the fundamental principles governing their training dynamics are inadequately characterized beyond configuration-specific studies. Inspired by empirical evidence showing improved reasoning capabilities under small initialization scales in language models, we employ the gradient flow analytical framework established in [Zhou et al. NeurIPS 2022] to systematically investigate linearized Transformer training dynamics. Our theoretical analysis dissects the dynamics of attention modules into two distinct stages. In the first stage, asymmetric weight perturbations from random initialization sustain non-degenerate gradient dynamics in parameter matrices, facilitating systematic escape from small initialization regimes. Subsequently, these matrices undergo condensation, progressively aligning toward the target orientation. In the second stage, the previously static key-query matrices actively participate in training, driving the normalized matrices toward asymptotic rank collapse. This two-stage framework generalizes classical directional convergence results.
中摘: 尽管基于变压器（Transformer）的模型展现出了卓越的经验性能，但其训练动态背后的基本原理在超出特定配置的研究之外仍描述不足。受语言模型在小规模初始化下推理能力增强的实证证据启发，我们运用了在[Zhou等人，NeurIPS 2022]中建立的梯度流分析框架，系统地探究线性化Transformer的训练动态。我们的理论分析将注意力模块的动力学分解为两个不同的阶段。在第一阶段，从随机初始化产生的不对称权重扰动维持参数矩阵中的非退化梯度动力学，有助于系统地摆脱小初始化状态。随后，这些矩阵经历凝聚过程，逐渐向目标方向对齐。在第二阶段，原本静态的关键-查询矩阵开始参与训练，驱动归一化矩阵趋向于渐近秩崩溃。这一两阶段框架推广了经典的方向收敛结果。

[No.33] Gated Integration of Low-Rank Adaptation for Continual Learning of Large Language Models
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 面向持续学习的大语言模型的门控低秩适应集成
类别: deep learning
作者: Yan-Shuo Liang;Jiarui Chen;Wu-Jun Li;
机构: Nanjing University;Wuhan University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=lVV7F0piDK
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.15424
英摘: Continual learning (CL), which requires the model to learn multiple tasks sequentially, is crucial for large language models (LLMs). Recently, low-rank adaptation~(LoRA), one of the most representative parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, has gained increasing attention in CL of LLMs. However, most existing CL methods based on LoRA typically expand a new LoRA branch to learn each new task and force the new and old LoRA branches to influence old tasks equally, potentially leading to forgetting. In this work, we propose a new method, called gated integration of low-rank adaptation (GainLoRA), for CL of LLMs. GainLoRA expands a new LoRA branch for each new task and introduces gating modules to integrate the new and old LoRA branches. Furthermore, GainLoRA leverages the new gating module to minimize the influence from the new LoRA branch to old tasks, effectively mitigating forgetting and improving the model's overall performance. Experimental results on CL benchmarks demonstrate that GainLoRA outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods.
中摘: 持续学习（CL），要求模型顺序地学习多个任务，对于大型语言模型（LLM）至关重要。最近，低秩适应（LoRA）作为最具代表性的参数高效微调（PEFT）方法之一，在LLM的CL领域获得了越来越多的关注。然而，大多数基于LoRA的现有CL方法通常为每个新任务扩展一个新的LoRA分支，并强制新旧LoRA分支对旧任务产生同等影响，这可能会导致遗忘现象。在本文中，我们提出了一种新的方法，称为低秩适应的门控集成（GainLoRA），用于LLM的CL。GainLoRA为每个新任务扩展一个新的LoRA分支，并引入门控模块来整合新旧LoRA分支。此外，GainLoRA利用新的门控模块最小化新LoRA分支对旧任务的影响，有效地减轻了遗忘并提高了模型的整体性能。在CL基准上的实验结果表明，GainLoRA超越了现有的最先进方法。

[No.34] The Primacy of Magnitude in Low-Rank Adaptation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 低秩适应中的幅度优先原则
类别: deep learning
作者: Zicheng Zhang;Haoran Li;Yifeng Zhang;Guoqiang Gong;Jiaxing Wang;pengzhang liu;Qixia Jiang;Junxing Hu;
机构: ;University of Chinese Academy of Sciences;JD.com;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=s4LnWgjacg
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.06558
英摘: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) offers a parameter-efficient paradigm for tuning large models. While recent spectral initialization methods improve convergence and performance over the naive "Noise & Zeros" scheme, their extra computational and storage overhead undermines efficiency. In this paper, we establish update magnitude as the fundamental driver of LoRA performance and propose LoRAM, a magnitude-driven "Basis & Basis" initialization scheme that matches spectral methods without their inefficiencies. Our key contributions are threefold: (i) Magnitude of weight updates determines convergence. We prove low-rank structures intrinsically bound update magnitudes, unifying hyperparameter tuning in learning rate, scaling factor, and initialization as mechanisms to optimize magnitude regulation. (ii) Spectral initialization succeeds via magnitude amplification. We demystify that the presumed knowledge-driven benefit of the spectral component essentially arises from the boost in the weight update magnitude. (iii) A novel and compact initialization strategy, LoRAM, scales deterministic orthogonal bases using pretrained weight magnitudes to simulate spectral gains. Extensive experiments show that LoRAM serves as a strong baseline, retaining the full efficiency of LoRA while matching or outperforming spectral initialization across benchmarks.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）为调整大型模型提供了一种参数高效的范式。尽管最近的谱初始化方法在收敛性和性能上优于简单的“噪声与零”方案，但它们额外的计算和存储开销削弱了效率。在这篇论文中，我们将更新幅度确立为驱动LoRA性能的根本因素，并提出LoRAM，这是一种基于幅度驱动的“基底与基底”初始化方案，它在没有引入额外不必要开销的情况下，可以媲美谱方法。我们的主要贡献有三点：(i) 权重更新的幅度决定了收敛性。我们证明了低秩结构本质上限制了更新幅度，将学习率、缩放因子和初始化的超参数调优统一为优化幅度调节的机制。(ii) 谱初始化通过幅度放大取得成功。我们揭开了谱成分所假设的知识驱动优势的神秘面纱，实际上，这种优势主要来源于权重更新幅度的提升。(iii) 我们提出了一种新颖且紧凑的初始化策略LoRAM，利用预训练权重的幅度来缩放确定性的正交基底，以模拟谱增益。广泛的实验表明，LoRAM作为一个强大的基准，在保持LoRA全部效率的同时，在各项基准测试中匹配或超越了谱初始化的表现。

[No.35] Accelerated Evolving Set Processes for Local PageRank Computation

类别: general machine learning
作者: BinbinHuang;Luo Luo;Yanghua Xiao;Deqing Yang;Baojian Zhou;

英摘: This work proposes a novel framework based on nested evolving set processes to accelerate Personalized PageRank (PPR) computation. At each stage of the process, we employ a localized inexact proximal point iteration to solve a simplified linear system. We show that the time complexity of such localized methods is upper bounded by $\min\{\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(R^2/ε^2), \tilde{\mathcal{O}}(m)\}$ to obtain an $ε$-approximation of the PPR vector, where $m$ denotes the number of edges in the graph and $R$ is a constant defined via nested evolving set processes. Furthermore, the algorithms induced by our framework require solving only $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrtα)$ such linear systems, where $α$ is the damping factor. When $1/ε^2\ll m$, this implies the existence of an algorithm that computes an $\ epsilon $-approximation of the PPR vector with an overall time complexity of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(R^2 / (\sqrtαε^2)\right)$, independent of the underlying graph size. Our result resolves an open conjecture from existing literature. Experimental results on real-world graphs validate the efficiency of our methods, demonstrating significant convergence in the early stages.
中摘: 本工作提出了一种基于嵌套演化集合过程的创新框架，以加速个性化PageRank（PPR）计算。在过程的每个阶段，我们使用局部近似乘子点迭代来求解简化的线性系统。我们证明了这种局部方法的时间复杂度上限为 $\min\{\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(R^2/ε^2), \tilde{\mathcal{O}}(m)\}$，以获得PPR向量的$ε$-逼近值，其中$m$表示图中的边数，而$R$是通过嵌套演化集合过程定义的一个常数。此外，由我们的框架诱导的算法仅需求解$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrtα)$个这样的线性系统，其中$α$是阻尼因子。当$1/ε^2\ll m$时，这意味着存在一个算法，其计算PPR向量的$\epsilon$-逼近值的总时间复杂度为$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(R^2 / (\sqrtαε^2)\right)$，且与底层图的大小无关。我们的结果解决了现有文献中的一个公开猜想。在真实世界图上的实验结果验证了我们方法的效率，在早期阶段就显示出显著的收敛性。

英摘: Counterfactual inference aims to estimate the counterfactual outcome at the individual level given knowledge of an observed treatment and the factual outcome, with broad applications in fields such as epidemiology, econometrics, and management science. Previous methods rely on a known structural causal model (SCM) or assume the homogeneity of the exogenous variable and strict monotonicity between the outcome and exogenous variable. In this paper, we propose a principled approach for identifying and estimating the counterfactual outcome. We first introduce a simple and intuitive rank preservation assumption to identify the counterfactual outcome without relying on a known structural causal model. Building on this, we propose a novel ideal loss for theoretically unbiased learning of the counterfactual outcome and further develop a kernel-based estimator for its empirical estimation. Our theoretical analysis shows that the rank preservation assumption is not stronger than the homogeneity and strict monotonicity assumptions, and shows that the proposed ideal loss is convex, and the proposed estimator is unbiased. Extensive semi-synthetic and real-world experiments are conducted to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

[No.40] GeoRanker: Distance-Aware Ranking for Worldwide Image Geolocalization
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: GeoRanker：全球图像地理定位的距离感知排名
类别: machine learning for sciences

英摘: Worldwide image geolocalization-the task of predicting GPS coordinates from images taken anywhere on Earth-poses a fundamental challenge due to the vast diversity in visual content across regions. While recent approaches adopt a two-stage pipeline of retrieving candidates and selecting the best match, they typically rely on simplistic similarity heuristics and point-wise supervision, failing to model spatial relationships among candidates. In this paper, we propose GeoRanker, a distance-aware ranking framework that leverages large vision-language models to jointly encode query-candidate interactions and predict geographic proximity. To support this, we curate GeoRanking, the first dataset explicitly designed for geographic ranking tasks with multimodal candidate information. GeoRanker achieves state-of-the-art results on two well-established benchmarks (IM2GPS3K and YFCC4K), significantly outperforming current best methods.
中摘: 全球图像地理定位，即从地球上任意地方拍摄的图像预测GPS坐标，由于各地区视觉内容的极大多样性而构成基本挑战。虽然近期的方法采用两阶段流程，包括检索候选位置和选择最佳匹配，但它们通常依赖于简单的相似性启发式算法和点对点监督，未能建模候选位置之间的空间关系。在这篇论文中，我们提出GeoRanker，一个基于距离的排名框架，利用大型视觉语言模型来联合编码查询与候选位置的交互，并预测地理接近度。此外，我们引入了多阶距离损失，它能够对绝对距离和相对距离进行排名，使模型能够推断结构化的空间关系。为此，我们整理了GeoRanking数据集，这是第一个专门为地理排名任务设计、包含多模式候选信息的数据集。GeoRanker在两个公认的基准测试（IM2GPS3K和YFCC4K）上实现了最先进的成果，显著超越当前最佳方法。

[No.42] RefLoRA: Refactored Low-Rank Adaptation for Efficient Fine-Tuning of Large Models
来源: NeurIPS 2025

标题: RefLoRA：重构低秩适应性调整以提高大型模型高效微调

英摘: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) lowers the computational and memory overhead of fine-tuning large models by updating a low-dimensional subspace of the pre-trained weight matrix. Albeit efficient, LoRA exhibits suboptimal convergence and noticeable performance degradation, due to inconsistent and imbalanced weight updates induced by its nonunique low-rank factorizations. To overcome these limitations, this article identifies the optimal low-rank factorization per step that minimizes an upper bound on the loss. The resultant refactored low-rank adaptation (RefLoRA) method promotes a flatter loss landscape, along with consistent and balanced weight updates, thus speeding up stable convergence. Extensive experiments evaluate RefLoRA on natural language understanding, and commonsense reasoning tasks with popular large language models including DeBERTaV3, LLaMA-7B, LLaMA2-7B and LLaMA3-8B. [No.43] GraLoRA: Granular Low-Rank Adaptation for Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 粒度低秩适应：参数高效微调
类别: deep learning
作者: Yeonjoon Jung;Daehyun Ahn;Hyungjun Kim;Taesu Kim;Eunhyeok Park;
机构: SqueezeBits Inc.;Pohang University of Science and Technology;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=8wvOMQ2Olw
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20355
英摘: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is a popular method for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) of generative models, valued for its simplicity and effectiveness. Despite recent enhancements, LoRA still suffers from a fundamental limitation: overfitting when the bottleneck is widened. It performs best at ranks 32-64, yet its accuracy stagnates or declines at higher ranks, still falling short of full fine-tuning (FFT) performance. We identify the root cause as LoRA's structural bottleneck, which introduces gradient entanglement to the unrelated input channels and distorts gradient propagation. To address this, we introduce a novel structure, Granular Low-Rank Adaptation (GraLoRA) that partitions weight matrices into sub-blocks, each with its own low-rank adapter. With negligible computational or storage cost, GraLoRA overcomes LoRA's limitations, effectively increases the representational capacity, and more closely approximates FFT behavior. Experiments on code generation and commonsense reasoning benchmarks show that GraLoRA consistently outperforms LoRA and other baselines, achieving up to +8.5% absolute gain in Pass@1 on HumanEval+. These improvements hold across model sizes and rank settings, making GraLoRA a scalable and robust solution for PEFT. Code, data, and scripts are available at https://github.com/SqueezeBits/GraLoRA.git
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）是一种流行的参数高效微调（PEFT）方法，用于生成模型，因其简单性和有效性而受到重视。尽管最近有所改进，LoRA仍然存在一个根本性的局限：当瓶颈扩大时，会出现过拟合现象。它在秩为32-64时表现最佳，但在更高秩时准确度停滞或下降，仍然无法达到完全微调（FFT）的性能水平。我们发现其根源在于LoRA的结构性瓶颈，这会将无关输入通道的梯度纠缠在一起，扭曲梯度传播。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了一种新的结构，称为粒度低秩适应（GraLoRA），它将权重矩阵分割成子块，每个子块都有自己的低秩适配器。在几乎不增加计算或存储成本的情况下，GraLoRA克服了LoRA的局限性，有效地提高了表示能力，并更接近FFT的行为。在代码生成和常识推理基准测试上的实验表明，GraLoRA始终优于LoRA和其他基线，在HumanEval+上的Pass@1指标上实现了高达+8.5%的绝对增益。这些改进在不同模型大小和秩设置下均有效，使得GraLoRA成为PEFT的一种可扩展且稳健的解决方案。代码、数据和脚本可在https://github.com/SqueezeBits/GraLoRA.git获取。

[No.44] LaX: Boosting Low-Rank Training of Foundation Models via Latent Crossing
来源: NeurIPS 2025

英摘: Training foundation models such as ViTs and LLMs requires tremendous computing cost. [No.45] Low Rank Gradients and Where to Find Them
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 低秩梯度与何处寻得它们
类别: theory
作者: Rishi Sonthalia;Michael Murray;Guido Montufar;
机构: Boston College;University of Bath;University of California, Los Angeles;Max Planck Institute for Mathematics in the Sciences;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=de3kwOXQ9e
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01303
英摘: This paper investigates low-rank structure in the gradients of the training loss for two-layer neural networks while relaxing the usual isotropy assumptions on the training data and parameters. We consider a spiked data model in which the bulk can be anisotropic and ill-conditioned, we do not require independent data and weight matrices and we also analyze both the mean-field and neural-tangent-kernel scalings. We show that the gradient with respect to the input weights is approximately low rank and is dominated by two rank-one terms: one aligned with the bulk data-residue , and another aligned with the rank one spike in the input data. We characterize how properties of the training data, the scaling regime and the activation function govern the balance between these two components. Additionally, we also demonstrate that standard regularizers, such as weight decay, input noise and Jacobian penalties, also selectively modulate these components. Experiments on synthetic and real data corroborate our theoretical predictions.
中摘: 本文研究了两层神经网络训练损失梯度中的低秩结构，同时放宽了对训练数据和参数通常的各向同性假设。我们考虑了一种尖峰数据模型，在该模型中，主体可以是各向异性的且条件数不良，我们不需要数据和权重矩阵独立，并且我们也分析了平均场和神经切线核缩放两种情况。我们证明了相对于输入权重的梯度近似为低秩，并且主要由两个秩一的项主导：一个与主体数据残差对齐，另一个与输入数据中的秩一尖峰对齐。我们描述了训练数据的属性、缩放领域以及激活函数如何控制这两个组成部分之间的平衡。此外，我们还展示了标准正则化器，如权重衰减、输入噪声和雅可比矩阵惩罚，也选择性地调节这些组成部分。在合成数据和真实数据上的实验验证了我们的理论预测。

[No.47] DynamicRAG: Leveraging Outputs of Large Language Model as Feedback for Dynamic Reranking in Retrieval-Augmented Generation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 动态RAG：将大型语言模型的输出作为反馈用于检索增强生成中的动态重排序

作者: Jiashuo Sun;Xianrui Zhong;Sizhe Zhou;Jiawei Han;

英摘: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems combine large language models (LLMs) with external knowledge retrieval, making them highly effective for knowledge-intensive tasks. A crucial but often under-explored component of these systems is the reranker. Since irrelevant documents in RAG systems can mislead the generator, the reranker plays a vital role in refining retrieved documents to enhance generation quality and explainability. However, it is challenging to determine the appropriate number of documents ($k$) that the reranker should select: too few may result in missing critical information, while too many introduce noise and inefficiencies. Although recent studies have explored LLM-based rerankers, they primarily leverage internal model knowledge and overlook the rich supervisory signals that LLMs can provide, such as using response quality as feedback for optimizing reranking decisions. In this paper, we propose DynamicRAG, a novel RAG framework where the reranker dynamically adjusts both the order and number of retrieved documents based on the query. We model the reranker as an agent optimized through reinforcement learning (RL), using rewards derived from LLM output quality. Across seven knowledge-intensive datasets, DynamicRAG demonstrates superior performance, achieving state-of-the-art results among models of same parameter sizes. The model, data and code are available at https://github.com/GasolSun36/DynamicRAG.
中摘: 检索增强生成（RAG）系统结合了大型语言模型（LLM）与外部知识检索，使其在知识密集型任务中表现出极高的效率。这些系统中一个关键但往往被忽视的组成部分是重排序器（reranker）。由于RAG系统中的不相关文档可能会误导生成器，因此重排序器在精炼检索到的文档方面扮演着至关重要的角色，以提高生成质量与可解释性。然而，确定重排序器应选择的文档数量（$k$）是一个挑战：过少的选择可能导致关键信息的缺失，而过多的选择则会引入噪音并降低效率。尽管近期的研究探讨了基于LLM的重排序器，但它们主要依赖于模型内部的知识，忽略了LLM可以提供的丰富监督信号，比如将响应质量作为优化重排序决策的反馈。在本文中，我们提出了DynamicRAG，这是一种新颖的RAG框架，其中的重排序器能够根据查询动态调整检索到的文档顺序和数量。我们将重排序器建模为一个通过强化学习（RL）进行优化的代理，利用源自LLM输出质量的奖励。在七个知识密集型数据集上，DynamicRAG展现出了卓越的性能，在相同参数规模的模型中达到了最先进的成果。该模型、数据和代码可在https://github.com/GasolSun36/DynamicRAG获取。

[No.48] AltLoRA: Towards Better Gradient Approximation in Low-Rank Adaptation with Alternating Projections
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: AltLoRA：在交替投影低秩适应中的更好梯度近似
类别: deep learning
作者: Xin Yu;Yujia Wang;Jinghui Chen;Lingzhou Xue;
机构: Pennsylvania State University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=9YNJ03jYsU
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12455
英摘: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as an effective technique for reducing memory overhead in fine-tuning large language models. However, it often suffers from sub-optimal performance compared with full fine-tuning since the update is constrained in the low-rank space. Recent variants such as LoRA-Pro attempt to mitigate this by adjusting the gradients of the low-rank matrices to approximate the full gradient. However, LoRA-Pro's solution is not unique, and different solutions can lead to significantly varying performance in ablation studies. Besides, to incorporate momentum or adaptive optimization design, approaches like LoRA-Pro must first compute the equivalent gradient, causing a higher memory cost close to full fine-tuning. A key challenge remains in integrating momentum properly into the low-rank space with lower memory cost. In this work, we propose AltLoRA, an alternating projection method that avoids the difficulties in gradient approximation brought by the joint update design, meanwhile integrating momentum without higher memory complexity. Our theoretical analysis provides convergence guarantees and further shows that AltLoRA enables stable feature learning and robustness to transformation invariance. Extensive experiments across multiple tasks demonstrate that AltLoRA outperforms LoRA and its variants, narrowing the gap toward full fine-tuning while preserving superior memory efficiency.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）作为一种有效技术，已经崭露头角，用于减少在微调大型语言模型时的记忆负担。然而，与完全微调相比，它通常性能欠佳，因为更新被限制在低秩空间内。最近的一些变体，如LoRA-Pro，试图通过调整低秩矩阵的梯度来近似完整的梯度，以缓解这一问题。但是，LoRA-Pro的解决方案并不唯一，不同的解决方案在消融研究中会导致显著不同的性能表现。此外，为了整合动量或自适应优化设计，像LoRA-Pro这样的方法必须首先计算等效梯度，这导致了接近完全微调的更高记忆成本。一个关键挑战在于如何在较低的记忆成本下，将动量正确地融入低秩空间。在这项工作中，我们提出了AltLoRA，一种交替投影方法，避免了联合更新设计带来的梯度近似困难，同时在不增加更高记忆复杂性的情况下融合了动量。我们的理论分析提供了收敛保证，并进一步表明AltLoRA能够实现稳定的特征学习和对变换不变性的鲁棒性。在多个任务上的广泛实验表明，AltLoRA超越了LoRA及其变体，在保留卓越记忆效率的同时，缩小了与完全微调之间的差距。

[No.49] Training-Free Bayesianization for Low-Rank Adapters of Large Language Models
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 无训练贝叶斯化在大规模语言模型中的低秩适配器
类别: probabilistic methods
作者: Haizhou Shi;Yibin Wang;Ligong Han;Huan Zhang;Hao Wang;
机构: Rutgers University;;International Business Machines Corporation;University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=itXwzGwGFC
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.05723
英摘: Estimating the uncertainty of responses from Large Language Models (LLMs) remains a critical challenge. While recent Bayesian methods have demonstrated effectiveness in quantifying uncertainty through low-rank weight updates, they typically require complex fine-tuning or post-training procedures. In this paper, we propose Training-Free Bayesianization (TFB), a simple yet theoretically grounded framework that efficiently transforms trained low-rank adapters into Bayesian ones without additional training. TFB systematically searches for the maximally acceptable level of variance in the weight posterior, constrained within a family of low-rank isotropic Gaussian distributions. Our theoretical analysis shows that under mild conditions, this search process is equivalent to KL-regularized variational optimization, a generalized form of variational inference. Through comprehensive experiments, we show that TFB achieves superior uncertainty estimation and generalization compared to existing methods while eliminating the need for complex Bayesianization training procedures. Code will be available at https://github.com/Wang-ML-Lab/bayesian-peft.

[No.50] Provable Meta-Learning with Low-Rank Adaptations
来源: NeurIPS 2025

标题: 可验证的元学习与低秩适应

作者: Jacob L. Block;Sundararajan Srinivasan;Liam Collins;Aryan Mokhtari;Sanjay Shakkottai;

英摘: The power of foundation models (FMs) lies in their capacity to learn highly expressive representations that can be adapted to a broad spectrum of tasks. However, these pretrained models require additional training stages to become effective for downstream applications. In the multi-task setting, prior works have shown empirically that specific meta-learning approaches for preparing a model for future adaptation through parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) can outperform standard retraining methods, but the mechanism of the benefits of meta-learning has been largely unexplored. We introduce a framework for generic PEFT-based meta-learning to learn a model that can easily adapt to unseen tasks. For linear models using LoRA, we show that standard retraining is provably suboptimal for finding an adaptable set of parameters and provide strict performance guarantees for our proposed method. We verify these theoretical insights through experiments on synthetic data as well as real-data vision and language tasks. We observe significant performance benefits using a simple implementation of our proposed meta-learning scheme during retraining relative to the conventional approach.
中摘: 基础模型（FMs）的强大之处在于其能够学习高度表现力的表示，这些表示可以适应广泛的任务。然而，这些预训练模型需要额外的训练阶段才能在下游应用中发挥作用。在多任务设置下，先前的研究已经通过实证表明，特定的元学习方法通过参数高效的微调（PEFT）来准备模型以适应未来的调整，可以胜过标准的重新训练方法，但元学习带来的好处机制在很大程度上未被探索。我们引入了一个通用的基于PEFT的元学习框架，用于学习一个能够轻松适应未知任务的模型。对于使用LoRA的线性模型，我们证明了标准的重新训练在寻找可适应的参数集方面是次优的，并为我们的提议方法提供了严格的性能保证。我们通过合成数据以及真实数据的视觉和语言任务的实验验证了这些理论洞察。我们观察到，在重新训练期间，与传统方法相比，使用我们提出的元学习方案的简单实现可以获得显著的性能优势。

[No.51] Robust Distributed Estimation: Extending Gossip Algorithms to Ranking and Trimmed Means
来源: NeurIPS 2025

标题:  robust分布式估计：将闲聊算法扩展到排名和修剪平均值

英摘: This paper addresses the problem of robust estimation in gossip algorithms over arbitrary communication graphs. Gossip algorithms are fully decentralized, relying only on local neighbor-to-neighbor communication, making them well-suited for situations where communication is constrained. A fundamental challenge in existing mean-based gossip algorithms is their vulnerability to malicious or corrupted nodes. In this paper, we show that an outlier-robust mean can be computed by globally estimating a robust statistic. More specifically, we propose a novel gossip algorithm for rank estimation, referred to as \textsc{GoRank}, and leverage it to design a gossip procedure dedicated to trimmed mean estimation, coined \textsc{GoTrim}. In addition to a detailed description of the proposed methods, a key contribution of our work is a precise convergence analysis: we establish an $\mathcal{O}(1/t)$ rate for rank estimation and an $\mathcal{O}(1 / {t})$ rate for trimmed mean estimation, where by $t$ is meant the number of iterations. Moreover, we provide a breakdown point analysis of \textsc{GoTrim}. We empirically validate our theoretical results through experiments on diverse network topologies, data distributions and contamination schemes.
中摘: 本文针对任意通信图上的流言算法中的鲁棒估计问题进行了探讨。流言算法完全分散化，仅依赖于邻近节点之间的本地通信，这使其非常适合通信受限的情况。现有基于平均值的流言算法面临的一个基本挑战是它们对恶意或损坏节点的脆弱性。在本文中，我们证明了可以通过全局估计一个鲁棒统计量来计算抗异常值的平均值。更具体地说，我们提出了一种新的用于秩估计的流言算法，称为\textsc{GoRank}，并利用它设计了一个专门用于修剪均值估计的流言过程，命名为\textsc{GoTrim}。除了对所提方法的详细描述外，我们工作的关键贡献之一是对收敛性的精确分析：我们为秩估计建立了$\mathcal{O}(1/t)$的收敛率，为修剪均值估计也建立了$\mathcal{O}(1/t)$的收敛率，其中$t$表示迭代次数。此外，我们还提供了\textsc{GoTrim}的崩溃点分析。我们通过在不同网络拓扑、数据分布和污染方案下的实验验证了理论结果。

[No.52] StelLA: Subspace Learning in Low-rank Adaptation using Stiefel Manifold
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: StelLA：低秩适应性Stiefel流形子空间学习
类别: deep learning
作者: Zhizhong Li;Sina Sajadmanesh;Jingtao Li;Lingjuan Lyu;
机构: Sony Europe;Sony;Sony Corporation;

英摘: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has been widely adopted as a parameter-efficient technique for fine-tuning large-scale pre-trained models. However, it still lags behind full fine-tuning in performance, partly due to its insufficient exploitation of the geometric structure underlying low-rank manifolds. In this paper, we propose a geometry-aware extension of LoRA that uses a three-factor decomposition $U\!SV^\top$. Analogous to the structure of singular value decomposition (SVD), it separates the adapter's input and output subspaces, $V$ and $U$, from the scaling factor $S$. Our method constrains $U$ and $V$ to lie on the Stiefel manifold, ensuring their orthonormality throughout the training. To optimize on the Stiefel manifold, we employ a flexible and modular geometric optimization design that converts any Euclidean optimizer to a Riemannian one. It enables efficient subspace learning while remaining compatible with existing fine-tuning pipelines. Empirical results across a wide range of downstream tasks, including commonsense reasoning, math and code generation, image classification, and image generation, demonstrate the superior performance of our approach against the recent state-of-the-art variants of LoRA. Code is available at https://github.com/SonyResearch/stella.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）作为一种参数高效的技术，已被广泛用于大规模预训练模型的微调。然而，在性能上，它仍然落后于全量微调，部分原因是未能充分挖掘低秩流形下潜在的几何结构。在这篇论文中，我们提出了一种基于几何意识的LoRA扩展，使用了三因子分解$U\!SV^\top$。类似于奇异值分解（SVD）的结构，它将适配器的输入和输出子空间，即$V$和$U$，与缩放因子$S$分开。我们的方法约束$U$和$V$位于Stiefel流形上，确保它们在整个训练过程中的正交性。为了在Stiefel流形上进行优化，我们采用了一种灵活且模块化的几何优化设计，可以将任何欧几里得优化器转换为黎曼优化器。这使得子空间学习更加高效，同时保持与现有微调管道的兼容性。在一系列下游任务上的实证结果，包括常识推理、数学和代码生成、图像分类以及图像生成，证明了我们的方法相较于近期LoRA的最新变体具有优越的性能。代码可在https://github.com/SonyResearch/stella获取。

[No.53] ResponseRank: Data-Efficient Reward Modeling through Preference Strength Learning
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 数据高效奖励建模通过偏好强度学习
类别: general machine learning
作者: Timo Kaufmann;Yannick Metz;Daniel A. Keim;Eyke Hüllermeier;

类型: Poster

英摘: Binary choices, as often used for reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), convey only the direction of a preference. A person may choose apples over oranges and bananas over grapes, but which preference is stronger? Strength is crucial for decision-making under uncertainty and generalization of preference models, but hard to measure reliably. Metadata such as response times and inter-annotator agreement can serve as proxies for strength, but are often noisy and confounded. We propose ResponseRank to address the challenge of learning from noisy strength signals. Our method uses relative differences in proxy signals to rank responses to pairwise comparisons by their inferred preference strength. To control for systemic variation, we compare signals only locally within carefully constructed strata. This enables robust learning of utility differences consistent with strength-derived rankings while making minimal assumptions about the strength signal. Our contributions are threefold: (1) ResponseRank, a novel method that robustly learns preference strength by leveraging locally valid relative strength signals; (2) empirical evidence of improved sample efficiency and robustness across diverse tasks: synthetic preference learning (with simulated response times), language modeling (with annotator agreement), and RL control tasks (with simulated episode returns); and (3) the Pearson Distance Correlation (PDC), a novel metric that isolates cardinal utility learning from ordinal accuracy.
中摘: 二元选择，常用于从人类反馈中进行强化学习（RLHF），仅传达了偏好的方向。一个人可能选择苹果而非橙子，香蕉而非葡萄，但哪种偏好更强呢？强度对于在不确定性下的决策制定和偏好模型的泛化至关重要，但难以可靠地测量。诸如响应时间和注释者间的一致性等元数据可以作为强度的代理指标，但往往带有噪音且易受混淆因素影响。我们提出ResponseRank来应对从嘈杂的强度信号中学习的挑战。我们的方法利用代理信号之间的相对差异，根据推断出的偏好强度对成对比较的响应进行排序。为了控制系统性变化，我们仅在精心构建的层内局部比较信号。这使得在对强度信号做出最小假设的情况下，能够稳健地学习与强度衍生排名一致的效用差异。我们的贡献有三：(1) ResponseRank，一种新颖的方法，通过利用局部有效的相对强度信号稳健地学习偏好强度；(2) 在各种任务中的实证证据，包括改进的样本效率和鲁棒性：合成偏好学习（带有模拟响应时间）、语言建模（带有注释者一致性）以及RL控制任务（带有模拟的回合回报）；以及(3) Pearson距离相关性（PDC），一个新颖的指标，将基数效用学习与序数准确性隔离。
