[No.56] Shift Before You Learn: Enabling Low-Rank Representations in Reinforcement Learning
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 学习之前移位：启用强化学习中的低秩表示
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Bastien Dubail;Stefan Stojanovic;Alexandre Proutiere;
机构: KTH Royal Institute of Technology;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=loVvFhfsDf
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.05193
英摘: Low-rank structure is a common implicit assumption in many modern reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms. For instance, reward-free and goal-conditioned RL methods often presume that the successor measure admits a low-rank representation. In this work, we challenge this assumption by first remarking that the successor measure itself is not approximately low-rank. Instead, we demonstrate that a low-rank structure naturally emerges in the shifted successor measure, which captures the system dynamics after bypassing a few initial transitions. We provide finite-sample performance guarantees for the entry-wise estimation of a low-rank approximation of the shifted successor measure from sampled entries. Our analysis reveals that both the approximation and estimation errors are primarily governed by a newly introduced quantitity: the spectral recoverability of the corresponding matrix. To bound this parameter, we derive a new class of functional inequalities for Markov chains that we call Type II Poincaré inequalities and from which we can quantify the amount of shift needed for effective low-rank approximation and estimation. This analysis shows in particular that the required shift depends on decay of the high-order singular values of the shifted successor measure and is hence typically small in practice. Additionally, we establish a connection between the necessary shift and the local mixing properties of the underlying dynamical system, which provides a natural way of selecting the shift. Finally, we validate our theoretical findings with experiments, and demonstrate that shifting the successor measure indeed leads to improved performance in goal-conditioned RL.
中摘: 低秩结构是许多现代强化学习（RL）算法中常见的隐含假设。例如，无奖励和目标条件下的RL方法通常假定后继测量具有低秩表示。在本工作中，我们首先指出后继测量本身并非近似低秩，以此挑战这一假设。相反，我们证明了在跳过一些初始转换后，系统动态在移位后的后继测量中自然呈现出低秩结构。我们为从采样条目估计移位后继测量的低秩逼近提供了有限样本性能保证。我们的分析揭示，逼近误差和估计误差主要由一个新引入的量：对应矩阵的谱恢复性所主导。为了限定这个参数，我们推导出一类针对马尔可夫链的新功能不等式，我们称之为II型Poincaré不等式，从中我们可以量化有效低秩逼近和估计所需的移位量。特别地，这种分析表明所需的移位取决于移位后继测量的高阶奇异值衰减，因此在实践中通常很小。此外，我们建立了必要移位与底层动力系统局部混合性质之间的联系，这提供了一种自然选择移位的方法。最后，我们通过实验验证了理论发现，并展示了对后继测量进行移位确实能提高目标条件下RL的表现。

[No.57] Generalization Bounds for Rank-sparse Neural Networks
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 秩稀疏神经网络的一般化界
类别: theory
作者: Antoine Ledent;Rodrigo Alves;Yunwen Lei;
机构: ;Czech Technical University;University of Hong Kong;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=n3M8h9mqDm
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.21945
英摘: It has been recently observed in much of the literature that neural networks exhibit a bottleneck rank property: for larger depths, the activation and weights of neural networks trained with gradient-based methods tend to be of approximately low rank. In fact, the rank of the activations of each layer converges to a fixed value referred to as the ``bottleneck rank'', which is the minimum rank required to represent the training data. This perspective is in line with the observation that regularizing linear networks (without activations) with weight decay is equivalent to minimizing the Schatten $p$ quasi norm of the neural network. In this paper we investigate the implications of this phenomenon for generalization. More specifically, we prove generalization bounds for neural networks which exploit the approximate low rank structure of the weight matrices if present. The final results rely on the Schatten $p$ quasi norms of the weight matrices: for small $p$, the bounds exhibit a sample complexity $ \widetilde{O}(WrL^2)$ where $W$ and $L$ are the width and depth of the neural network respectively and where $r$ is the rank of the weight matrices. As $p$ increases, the bound behaves more like a norm-based bound instead.
中摘: 最近在大量文献中观察到，神经网络展现了一种瓶颈秩属性：对于较大的深度，使用基于梯度的方法训练的神经网络的激活和权重倾向于大约为低秩。实际上，每一层激活的秩会收敛到一个固定值，被称为“瓶颈秩”，这是表示训练数据所需的最小秩。这一观点与观察一致，即对线性网络（没有激活函数）进行权重衰减正则化等价于最小化神经网络的Schatten $p$准范数。在这篇论文中，我们探讨了这种现象对泛化的影响。更具体地说，如果存在近似低秩结构，我们证明了针对神经网络的泛化界。最终结果依赖于权重矩阵的Schatten $p$准范数：对于较小的$p$，边界显示出样本复杂度$\widetilde{O}(WrL^2)$，其中$W$和$L$分别是神经网络的宽度和深度，而$r$是权重矩阵的秩。随着$p$的增加，边界的行为更像是基于范数的边界。

这段文字描述了神经网络中的一种现象，即“瓶颈秩”属性，并讨论了它对泛化能力的影响。作者指出，当神经网络的深度增大时，其激活和权重趋于低秩，这与使用基于梯度方法训练的网络相关。他们还提到了线性网络中的权重衰减正则化与神经网络Schatten $p$准范数之间的关系。在论文中，作者证明了利用权重矩阵的近似低秩结构可以得到神经网络的泛化界，并详细说明了这个泛化界的依赖关系和行为特征。

[No.59] Language Ranker: A Lightweight Ranking framework for LLM Decoding
来源: NeurIPS 2025

类别: deep learning
作者: Chenheng Zhang;Tianqi Du;Jizhe Zhang;Mingqing Xiao;Yifei Wang;Yisen Wang;Zhouchen Lin;

英摘: Conventional research on large language models (LLMs) has primarily focused on refining output distributions, while paying less attention to the decoding process that transforms these distributions into final responses. Recent advances, such as scaling the computation of inference time with reward models, have underscored the importance of decoding, but these methods often suffer from high computational costs and limited applicability. In this paper, we revisit LLM generation through the lens of recommender systems, conceptualizing the decoding process as analogous to the ranking stage in recommendation pipelines. From this perspective, we observe that both traditional decoding methods and reward models exhibit clear limitations such as redundancy. Motivated by this insight, we propose Language Ranker, a novel framework that introduces a lightweight module to rerank candidate responses using features extracted by the base model. Experiments across a wide range of tasks show that Language Ranker achieves performance comparable to large-scale reward models, while requiring only <0.5M additional parameters, significantly reducing the computational overhead during both training and inference stages. This highlights the efficiency and effectiveness of our method, showcasing its potential to fully unlock the capabilities of LLMs.
中摘: 传统的大型语言模型（LLMs）研究主要集中在优化输出分布，而对将这些分布转化为最终响应的解码过程关注较少。最近的研究进展，比如在推理时间中利用奖励模型进行计算规模的扩展，强调了解码的重要性，但这些方法常常伴随着高昂的计算成本和有限的应用范围。在这篇论文中，我们从推荐系统的角度重新审视了LLM生成，将解码过程视为与推荐管道中的排序阶段类似。从这一视角出发，我们发现传统解码方法和奖励模型都存在明显的局限性，如重复性问题。基于这一洞察，我们提出了Language Ranker，一种创新框架，它引入了一个轻量级模块，使用基础模型提取的特征对候选响应进行重新排序。跨多种任务的实验表明，Language Ranker能够在仅增加不到0.5M额外参数的情况下，实现与大规模奖励模型相当的性能，显著降低了训练和推理阶段的计算开销。这凸显了我们方法的高效性和有效性，并展示了其全面释放LLMs潜力的可能。

[No.63] MINGLE: Mixture of Null-Space Gated Low-Rank Experts for Test-Time Continual Model Merging
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: MINGLE：混合空域门控低秩专家用于测试时持续模型合并
类别: general machine learning
作者: Zihuan Qiu;Yi Xu;Chiyuan He;Fanman Meng;Linfeng Xu;Qingbo Wu;Hongliang Li;
机构: University of Electronic Science and Technology of China;;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11883
英摘: Continual model merging integrates independently fine-tuned models sequentially without access to the original training data, offering a scalable and efficient solution for continual learning. However, existing methods face two critical challenges: parameter interference among tasks, which leads to catastrophic forgetting, and limited adaptability to evolving test distributions. To address these issues, we introduce the task of Test-Time Continual Model Merging (TTCMM), which leverages a small set of unlabeled test samples during inference to alleviate parameter conflicts and handle distribution shifts. We propose MINGLE, a novel framework for TTCMM. MINGLE employs a mixture-of-experts architecture with parameter-efficient, low-rank experts, which enhances adaptability to evolving test distributions while dynamically merging models to mitigate conflicts. To further reduce forgetting, we propose Null-Space Constrained Gating, which restricts gating updates to subspaces orthogonal to prior task representations, thereby suppressing activations on old tasks and preserving past knowledge. We further introduce an Adaptive Relaxation Strategy that adjusts constraint strength dynamically based on interference signals observed during test-time adaptation, striking a balance between stability and adaptability. Extensive experiments on standard continual merging benchmarks demonstrate that MINGLE achieves robust generalization, significantly reduces forgetting, and consistently surpasses previous state-of-the-art methods by 7-9% on average across diverse task orders. Our code is available at: https://github.com/zihuanqiu/MINGLE
中摘: 持续模型融合通过顺序整合独立微调的模型，无需访问原始训练数据，为持续学习提供了一种可扩展且高效的解决方案。然而，现有方法面临着两个关键挑战：任务之间的参数干扰导致灾难性遗忘，以及对演化的测试分布适应性有限。为了解决这些问题，我们引入了“测试时持续模型融合”（TTCMM）任务，该任务在推理过程中利用一小部分无标签测试样本来缓解参数冲突并处理分布偏移。我们提出了MINGLE，一种用于TTCMM的创新框架。MINGLE采用专家混合架构，配备参数高效、低秩的专家，这不仅增强了对演化的测试分布的适应性，同时动态地合并模型以减轻冲突。为了进一步减少遗忘，我们提出了空空间约束门控，它将门控更新限制在与先前任务表示正交的子空间内，从而抑制旧任务的激活并保留过去的知识。我们还引入了一种自适应松弛策略，根据测试时适应期间观察到的干扰信号动态调整约束强度，在稳定性和适应性之间取得平衡。在标准持续融合基准上的广泛实验表明，MINGLE实现了稳健的泛化能力，显著减少了遗忘，并且在不同任务顺序下平均比之前的最先进方法高出7-9%，表现一贯超越。我们的代码可在以下网址获取：https://github.com/zihuanqiu/MINGLE

[No.65] Q3R: Quadratic Reweighted Rank Regularizer for Effective Low-Rank Training
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: Q3R：二次加权秩正则化器，用于有效的低秩训练

英摘: Parameter-efficient training based on low-rank optimization has become a highly successful tool for fine-tuning large deep learning models. However, these methods often fail for low-rank pre-training, where simultaneously maintaining low-rank weight structure and optimizing the task objective remains challenging. We propose the $\textit{Quadratic Reweighted Rank Regularizer}$ ($\texttt{Q3R}$), which leads to a novel low-rank-inducing training strategy inspired by the Iteratively Reweighted Least Squares (IRLS) framework. $\texttt{Q3R}$ is based on a quadratic regularizer term that majorizes a smoothed log-determinant rank surrogate. Unlike other low-rank training techniques, $\texttt{Q3R}$ can train weight matrices to prescribed low target ranks while achieving predictive performance comparable to dense models, with small computational overhead and full compatibility with existing architectures. For example, we demonstrate a $\texttt{Q3R}$-regularized ViT-Tiny experiment where truncating the model to $60\%$ and $80\%$ of its parameters results in only minor absolute accuracy drops of $1.3\%$ and $4\%$, respectively, on CIFAR-10. We confirm the efficacy of $\texttt{Q3R}$ on Transformers across both vision and language tasks, including low-rank fine-tuning.
中摘: 基于低秩优化的参数高效训练已成为微调大型深度学习模型的高度成功工具。然而，这些方法在低秩预训练时常常失败，因为同时保持低秩权重结构和优化任务目标仍然具有挑战性。我们提出了$\textit{二次重加权秩正则器}$($\texttt{Q3R}$)，它基于迭代重加权最小二乘(IRLS)框架，导致了一种新颖的诱导低秩的训练策略。$\texttt{Q3R}$基于一个二次正则化项，该正则化项大于平滑对数行列式秩替代。与其它低秩训练技术不同，$\texttt{Q3R}$可以在实现与密集模型相当的预测性能的同时，将权重矩阵训练到预定的低目标秩，计算开销小，并且与现有架构完全兼容。例如，我们在ViT-Tiny实验中展示了$\texttt{Q3R}$正则化的例子，其中将模型裁剪至其参数量的$60\%$和$80\%$时，在CIFAR-10上分别只会造成绝对准确度的小幅下降$1.3\%$和$4\%$。我们在视觉和语言任务上的Transformer上验证了$\texttt{Q3R}$的有效性，包括低秩微调。

[No.66] Transductive Conformal Inference for Full Ranking
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 全传递性区间预测全面排名
类别: general machine learning
作者: Jean-Baptiste Fermanian;Pierre Humbert;Gilles Blanchard;
机构: University of Montpelier;Sorbonne Université;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=3Bxn5XSgrO
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.11384
英摘: We introduce a method based on Conformal Prediction (CP) to quantify the uncertainty of full ranking algorithms. We focus on a specific scenario where $n+m$ items are to be ranked by some ``black box'' algorithm. It is assumed that the relative (ground truth) ranking of $n$ of them is known. The objective is then to quantify the error made by the algorithm on the ranks of the $m$ new items among the total $(n+m)$. In such a setting, the true ranks of the $n$ original items in the total $(n+m)$ depend on the (unknown) true ranks of the $m$ new ones. Consequently, we have no direct access to a calibration set to apply a classical CP method. To address this challenge, we propose to construct distribution-free bounds of the unknown conformity scores using recent results on the distribution of conformal p-values. Using these scores upper bounds, we provide valid prediction sets for the rank of any item. We also control the false coverage proportion, a crucial quantity when dealing with multiple prediction sets. Finally, we empirically show on both synthetic and real data the efficiency of our CP method for state-of-the-art algorithms such as RankNet or LambdaMart.
中摘: 我们介绍了一种基于一致预测（Conformal Prediction, CP）的方法来量化全排序算法的不确定性。我们专注于一个特定场景，即需要通过某种“黑盒”算法对$n+m$个项进行排序。假设已知其中$n$个项的相对排名（实际排名）。目标是量化算法在对剩下的$m$个新项进行排序时，在总$(n+m)$个项中的误差。在这种情况下，$n$个原始项在总$(n+m)$个项中的真实排名取决于$m$个新项的未知真实排名。因此，我们无法直接获得校准集以应用传统的CP方法。为了解决这一挑战，我们提议使用近期关于一致p值分布的结果来构建未知一致性得分的无分布限制。利用这些得分上限，我们为任何项的排名提供了有效的预测集。我们还控制了错误覆盖比例，这是处理多个预测集时的关键量。最后，我们在合成数据和真实数据上都展示了我们的CP方法对于如RankNet或LambdaMart等先进算法的有效性。

[No.67] Differentially Private Federated Low Rank Adaptation Beyond Fixed-Matrix
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 差分隐私联邦低秩适应超越固定矩阵
类别: deep learning
作者: Ming Wen;Jiaqi Zhu;Yuedong Xu;Yipeng Zhou;DINGDING HAN;
机构: ;Fudan University;Macquarie University;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.09990
英摘: Large language models (LLMs) typically require fine-tuning for domain-specific tasks, and LoRA offers a computationally efficient approach by training low-rank adapters. LoRA is also communication-efficient for federated LLMs when multiple users collaboratively fine-tune a global LLM model without sharing their proprietary raw data. However, even the transmission of local adapters between a server and clients risks serious privacy leakage. Applying differential privacy (DP) to federated LoRA encounters a dilemma: adding noise to both adapters amplifies synthetic noise on the model, while fixing one adapter impairs the learnability of fine-tuning. In this paper, we propose FedASK (Differentially Private Federated Low Rank Adaptation with Double Sketching) , a novel federated LoRA framework to enable effective updating of both low-rank adapters with robust differential privacy. Inspired by randomized SVD, our key idea is a two-stage sketching pipeline. This pipeline first aggregates carefully sketched, privacy-preserving local updates, and then reconstructs the global matrices on the server to facilitate effective updating of both adapters. We theoretically prove FedASK's differential privacy guarantee and its exact aggregation property. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that FedASK consistently outperforms baseline methods across a variety of privacy settings and data distributions.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLM）通常需要为特定领域的任务进行微调，LoRA通过训练低秩适配器提供了一种计算高效的方法。在多个用户协作微调全局LLM模型而不共享他们的专有原始数据时，LoRA对于联邦LLM也具有通信效率。然而，即使是在服务器和客户端之间传输本地适配器也会带来严重的隐私泄露风险。将差分隐私（DP）应用于联邦LoRA会遇到一个困境：对两个适配器都添加噪声会放大模型上的合成噪声，而固定一个适配器则损害了微调的学习能力。在这篇论文中，我们提出了FedASK（基于双草图的差分隐私联邦低秩适应），这是一种新颖的联邦LoRA框架，能够以强大的差分隐私有效地更新两个低秩适配器。受随机SVD的启发，我们的核心思想是一个两阶段的草图管道。该管道首先聚合精心草图化、保护隐私的本地更新，然后在服务器上重建全局矩阵，以促进两个适配器的有效更新。我们在理论上证明了FedASK的差分隐私保证及其精确聚合属性。全面的实验表明，在各种隐私设置和数据分布下，FedASK始终优于基线方法。

[No.69] Ranking-based Preference Optimization for Diffusion Models from Implicit User Feedback
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 基于排名的偏好优化：从隐式用户反馈改进扩散模型
类别: deep learning
作者: Yi-Lun Wu;Bo-Kai Ruan;Chiang Tseng;Hong-Han Shuai;
机构: National Yang Ming Chiao Tung University;;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.18353
英摘: Direct preference optimization (DPO) methods have shown strong potential in aligning text-to-image diffusion models with human preferences by training on paired comparisons. These methods improve training stability by avoiding the REINFORCE algorithm but still struggle with challenges such as accurately estimating image probabilities due to the non-linear nature of the sigmoid function and the limited diversity of offline datasets. In this paper, we introduce Diffusion Denoising Ranking Optimization (Diffusion-DRO), a new preference learning framework grounded in inverse reinforcement learning. Diffusion-DRO removes the dependency on a reward model by casting preference learning as a ranking problem, thereby simplifying the training objective into a denoising formulation and overcoming the non-linear estimation issues found in prior methods. Moreover, Diffusion-DRO uniquely integrates offline expert demonstrations with online policy-generated negative samples, enabling it to effectively capture human preferences while addressing the limitations of offline data. Comprehensive experiments show that Diffusion-DRO delivers improved generation quality across a range of challenging and unseen prompts, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines in both both quantitative metrics and user studies. Our source code and pre-trained models are available at https://github.com/basiclab/DiffusionDRO.
中摘: 直接偏好优化（DPO）方法在通过配对比较训练来使文本到图像的扩散模型与人类偏好保持一致方面展现出强大潜力。这些方法通过避免使用REINFORCE算法来提高训练稳定性，但仍面临诸如由于sigmoid函数的非线性本质和离线数据集多样性有限导致准确估计图像概率的挑战。在本文中，我们引入了一种新的偏好学习框架——扩散去噪排序优化（Diffusion-DRO），该框架基于逆强化学习。Diffusion-DRO通过将偏好学习转化为排名问题，消除了对奖励模型的依赖，从而将训练目标简化为去噪公式，并克服了先前方法中的非线性估计问题。此外，Diffusion-DRO独特地结合了离线专家演示与在线策略生成的负样本，使其能够有效捕捉人类偏好，同时解决离线数据的局限性。全面的实验表明，Diffusion-DRO在一系列具有挑战性和未见过的提示下提高了生成质量，在定量指标和用户研究中均超越了最先进的基线。我们的源代码和预训练模型可在https://github.com/basiclab/DiffusionDRO获取。

[No.70] Perturbation Bounds for Low-Rank Inverse Approximations under Noise
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 噪声条件下低秩逆近似扰动界
类别: optimization
作者: Phuc Tran;Nisheeth K. Vishnoi;
机构: Yale University;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=bPNzBXl1n7
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.25571
英摘: Low-rank pseudoinverses are widely used to approximate matrix inverses in scalable machine learning, optimization, and scientific computing. However, real-world matrices are often observed with noise, arising from sampling, sketching, and quantization. The spectral-norm robustness of low-rank inverse approximations remains poorly understood. We systematically study the spectral-norm error $\| (\tilde{A}^{-1})_p - A_p^{-1} \|$ for an $n\times n$ symmetric matrix $A$, where $A_p^{-1}$ denotes the best rank-\(p\) approximation of $A^{-1}$, and $\tilde{A} = A + E$ is a noisy observation. Under mild assumptions on the noise, we derive sharp non-asymptotic perturbation bounds that reveal how the error scales with the eigengap, spectral decay, and noise alignment with low-curvature directions of $A$. Our analysis introduces a novel application of contour integral techniques to the \emph{non-entire} function $f(z) = 1/z$, yielding bounds that improve over naive adaptations of classical full-inverse bounds by up to a factor of $\sqrt{n}$. Empirically, our bounds closely track the true perturbation error across a variety of real-world and synthetic matrices, while estimates based on classical results tend to significantly overpredict. These findings offer practical, spectrum-aware guarantees for low-rank inverse approximations in noisy computational environments.
中摘: 低秩伪逆在可扩展机器学习、优化和科学计算中被广泛用于近似矩阵逆。然而，现实世界的矩阵通常伴随着由采样、概要描绘和量化产生的噪声。低秩逆近似的谱范数鲁棒性仍然理解不足。我们系统地研究了$n\times n$对称矩阵$A$的谱范数误差$\| (\tilde{A}^{-1})_p - A_p^{-1} \|$，其中$A_p^{-1}$表示$A^{-1}$的最佳秩$p$近似，而$\tilde{A} = A + E$是一个有噪声的观测值。在对噪声的温和假设下，我们推导出了尖锐的非渐近扰动界，揭示了误差如何随特征值间隙、谱衰减以及噪声与$A$的低曲率方向的对齐程度而变化。我们的分析引入了一种新颖的轮廓积分技术应用于非全纯函数$f(z) = 1/z$，所得界比直接改编经典满秩逆界提高了最多$\sqrt{n}$倍。实证上，我们的界在多种真实世界和合成矩阵中紧密追踪真实的扰动误差，而基于经典结果的估计往往显著高估。这些发现为在嘈杂的计算环境中低秩逆近似提供了实际的、谱感知的保证。

[No.71] Cooperative Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Question Answering: Mutual Information Exchange and Ranking by Contrasting Layers
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 协作检索增强生成问答：对比层间的互信息交换与排名
类别: applications
作者: Youmin Ko;Sung Jong Seo;Hyunjoon Kim;
机构: Hanyang University;
类型: Poster

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.10422
英摘: Since large language models (LLMs) have a tendency to generate factually inaccurate output, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has gained significant attention as a key means to mitigate this downside of harnessing only LLMs. However, existing RAG methods for simple and multi-hop question answering (QA) are still prone to incorrect retrievals and hallucinations. To address these limitations, we propose CoopRAG, a novel RAG framework for the question answering task in which a retriever and an LLM work cooperatively with each other by exchanging informative knowledge, and the earlier and later layers of the retriever model work cooperatively with each other to accurately rank the retrieved documents relevant to a given query. In this framework, we (i) unroll a question into sub-questions and a reasoning chain in which uncertain positions are masked, (ii) retrieve the documents relevant to the question augmented with the sub-questions and the reasoning chain, (iii) rerank the documents by contrasting layers of the retriever, and (iv) reconstruct the reasoning chain by filling the masked positions via the LLM. Our experiments demonstrate that CoopRAG consistently outperforms state-of-the-art QA methods on three multi-hop QA datasets as well as a simple QA dataset in terms of both the retrieval and QA performances. Our code is available.
中摘: 由于大型语言模型（LLM）倾向于生成事实不准确的输出，增强检索的生成（RAG）方法已受到广泛关注，被视为缓解仅依赖LLM所带来的缺点的关键手段。然而，现有的用于简单和多跳问答（QA）的RAG方法仍然容易出现错误检索和幻觉。为了解决这些限制，我们提出了CoopRAG，这是一种新颖的RAG框架，其中检索器和LLM通过交换信息知识相互协作，并且检索器模型的前层和后层也相互协作，以准确地对与给定查询相关的检索文档进行排名。在这个框架中，我们（i）将问题展开成子问题和推理链，其中不确定的位置被屏蔽，（ii）检索与问题、子问题和推理链相关联的文档，（iii）通过对比检索器的各层来重新排名文档，以及（iv）通过LLM填充屏蔽位置来重建推理链。我们的实验表明，在三个多跳QA数据集以及一个简单QA数据集上，CoopRAG在检索和QA性能方面均持续超越最先进的QA方法。我们的代码可获取。

英摘: Recent advancements in Large Language Models (LLMs) have emphasized the critical role of fine-tuning (FT) techniques in adapting LLMs to specific tasks, especially when retraining from scratch is computationally infeasible. Fine-tuning enables LLMs to leverage task- or domain-specific data, producing models that more effectively meet the requirements of targeted applications. However, conventional FT approaches often suffer from catastrophic forgetting and suboptimal data efficiency, limiting their real-world applicability. To address these challenges, this paper proposes \textbf{DEAL}, a novel framework that integrates Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) with a continuous fine-tuning strategy. By incorporating knowledge retention and adaptive parameter update modules, the framework mitigates the limitations of existing FT methods while maintaining efficiency. Experiments on 15 diverse datasets show that DEAL consistently outperforms baseline methods, yielding substantial gains in task accuracy and resource efficiency. These findings demonstrate the potential of our approach to advance continual adaptation in LLMs by enhancing task performance while improving resource efficiency. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/zzm-black/DEAL-Continuous-Low-Rank-Fine-Tuning.
中摘: 最近在大型语言模型（LLM）方面的进展强调了微调（FT）技术在适应特定任务中的关键作用，特别是在从头开始重新训练计算上不可行的情况下。微调使LLM能够利用特定于任务或领域的数据，从而产生更有效地满足目标应用需求的模型。然而，传统的FT方法通常遭受灾难性遗忘和次优的数据效率问题，限制了它们在现实世界的应用。为了解决这些挑战，本文提出了一种名为**DEAL**的新型框架，该框架将低秩适应（LoRA）与连续微调策略相结合。通过整合知识保留和自适应参数更新模块，该框架缓解了现有FT方法的局限性，同时保持了高效性。在15个不同数据集上的实验表明，DEAL始终优于基线方法，在任务准确性和资源效率方面取得了显著提升。这些发现展示了我们方法的潜力，即通过增强任务性能并提高资源效率，推进LLM中的持续适应。源代码可在以下网址公开获取：https://github.com/zzm-black/DEAL-Continuous-Low-Rank-Fine-Tuning。

[No.76] Matching Markets Meet LLMs: Algorithmic Reasoning with Ranked Preferences
来源: NeurIPS 2025

英摘: The rise of Large Language Models (LLMs) has driven progress in reasoning tasks -- from program synthesis to scientific hypothesis generation -- yet their ability to handle ranked preferences and structured algorithms in combinatorial domains remains underexplored. We study matching markets, a core framework behind applications like resource allocation and ride-sharing, which require reconciling individual ranked preferences to ensure stable outcomes. We evaluate several state-of-the-art models on a hierarchy of preference-based reasoning tasks -- ranging from stable-matching generation to instability detection, instability resolution, and fine-grained preference queries -- to systematically expose their logical and algorithmic limitations in handling ranked inputs. Surprisingly, even top-performing models with advanced reasoning struggle to resolve instability in large markets, often failing to identify blocking pairs or execute algorithms iteratively. [No.77] Scalable In-context Ranking with Generative Models
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 基于生成模型的可扩展上下文排名
类别: deep learning
作者: Nilesh Gupta;Chong You;Srinadh Bhojanapalli;Sanjiv Kumar;Inderjit S Dhillon;Felix X. Yu;
机构: University of Texas at Austin;;Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=zj45hoQhjD
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.05396
英摘: In-context Ranking (ICR) is an emerging paradigm for Information Retrieval (IR), which leverages contextual understanding of LLMs by directly incorporating the task description, candidate documents, and the query into the model's input prompt and tasking the LLM to identify relevant document(s). While it is effective, efficiency is a significant challenge in this paradigm, especially as the candidate list grows due to quadratic/super-linear scaling of attention operation with context length. To this end, this paper first identifies inherent and exploitable structures in the attention of LLMs finetuned for ICR: (1) inter-document block sparsity: attention is dense within each document block but sparse across different documents in the context; and (2) query-document block relevance: the attention scores from certain query tokens to a document block in middle layers strongly correlate with that document's actual relevance. Motivated by these observations, we introduce BlockRank (Blockwise In-context Ranking), a novel method that adapts the attention operation in an LLM by (a) architecturally enforcing the observed inter-document block sparsity, reducing attention complexity from quadratic to linear without loss in performance, and (b) optimizing query-document block relevance for true relevant documents during fine-tuning using an auxiliary contrastive training objective, improving retrieval in attention. Experiments on BEIR, MSMarco and NQ with Mistral-7B demonstrate that BlockRank Mistral matches or outperforms existing SOTA listwise rankers and controlled fine-tuned baseline while being significantly more efficient at inference (4.7x for 100 MSMarco documents in context) and scaling gracefully to long-context shortlists, around 500 documents in-context (approximately 100K context length) within a second, presenting a scalable and effective solution for ICR.
中摘: 在情境排名（In-context Ranking，简称ICR）是信息检索（Information Retrieval，简称IR）领域新兴的一种范式，它通过将任务描述、候选文档和查询直接整合进模型的输入提示中，利用大型语言模型（LLMs）的情境理解能力，使LLMs能够识别相关的文档。尽管这一方法有效，但在这一范式下效率是一个重大挑战，尤其是在候选列表增长时，由于注意力操作与上下文长度呈二次或超线性扩展，导致效率问题尤为突出。为此，本文首先揭示并利用了为ICR微调后的LLMs在注意力机制中的内在可利用结构：(1) 文档间块稀疏性：注意力在每个文档块内部密集，但在不同文档之间呈现稀疏；(2) 查询-文档块相关性：中间层从特定查询词到文档块的注意力得分与该文档的实际相关性高度相关。基于这些观察，我们引入了BlockRank（基于块的在情境排名），这是一种新颖的方法，通过(a) 在架构上强制执行观察到的文档间块稀疏性，将注意力复杂度从二次降低至线性，而不会损失性能，并(b) 在微调过程中使用辅助对比训练目标优化查询-文档块相关性，以提高真正相关文档的检索效率，在注意力层面进行优化。在BEIR、MSMarco和NQ数据集上的实验表明，Mistral-7B上的BlockRank Mistral匹配或超越现有最先进(listwise)排名器及控制性微调基线，同时在推理时显著更高效（对于包含100个MSMarco文档的情境，加速约4.7倍），并能优雅地扩展到长上下文短名单，大约500个文档在情境中（大约10万上下文长度）内，只需一秒钟，为ICR提供了一个可扩展且有效的解决方案。

[No.78] Aligning Transformers with Continuous Feedback via Energy Rank Alignment
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 通过能量排名对齐调整变压器以实现连续反馈
类别: machine learning for sciences
作者: Shriram Chennakesavalu;Frank Hu;Sebastian Ibarraran;Grant M. Rotskoff;
机构: Stanford University;

链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=SXxlb1miXS
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.12961
英摘: Searching through chemical space is an exceptionally challenging problem because the number of possible molecules grows combinatorially with the number of atoms. Large, autoregressive models trained on databases of chemical compounds have yielded powerful generators, but we still lack robust strategies for generating molecules with desired properties. This molecular search problem closely resembles the "alignment" problem for large language models, though for many chemical tasks we have a specific and easily evaluable reward function. Here, we introduce an algorithm called energy rank alignment (ERA) that leverages an explicit reward function to produce a gradient-based objective that we use to optimize autoregressive policies. We show theoretically that this algorithm is closely related to proximal policy optimization (PPO) and direct preference optimization (DPO), but has a minimizer that converges to an ideal Gibbs-Boltzmann distribution with the reward playing the role of an energy function. Furthermore, this algorithm is highly scalable, does not require reinforcement learning, and performs well relative to DPO when the number of preference observations per pairing is small. We deploy this approach to align molecular transformers and protein language models to generate molecules and protein sequences, respectively, with externally specified properties and find that it does so robustly, searching through diverse parts of chemical space.
中摘: 在化学空间中搜索是一个异常艰巨的问题，因为可能的分子数量随着原子数目的增加而组合式增长。在化学化合物数据库上训练的大型自回归模型已经产生了强大的生成器，但我们仍然缺乏生成具有所需性质的分子的稳健策略。这个分子搜索问题与大型语言模型的“对齐”问题紧密相似，不过对于许多化学任务，我们有一个具体且易于评估的奖励函数。在这里，我们介绍了一种名为能量等级对齐（ERA）的算法，该算法利用显式的奖励函数来产生基于梯度的目标，我们用它来优化自回归策略。我们从理论上证明了此算法与近端策略优化（PPO）和直接偏好优化（DPO）密切相关，但它具有一个收敛至理想吉布斯-玻尔兹曼分布的最小化器，其中奖励函数扮演着能量函数的角色。此外，此算法高度可扩展，不需要强化学习，并且当每对偏好观察的数量较少时，其相对于DPO表现良好。我们将这种方法应用于对齐分子转换器和蛋白质语言模型，以分别生成具有外部指定性质的分子和蛋白质序列，并发现它能够稳健地做到这一点，在化学空间的多样部分进行搜索。

[No.79] Breaking the Frozen Subspace: Importance Sampling for Low-Rank Optimization in LLM Pretraining
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 打破冻结子空间：在大语言模型预训练中的低秩优化重要采样
类别: deep learning
作者: Haochen Zhang;Junze Yin;Guanchu Wang;Zirui Liu;Lin Yang;Tianyi Zhang;Anshumali Shrivastava;Vladimir Braverman;

英摘: Low-rank optimization has emerged as a promising approach to enabling memory-efficient training of large language models (LLMs). Existing low-rank optimization methods typically project gradients onto a low-rank subspace, reducing the memory cost of storing optimizer states. A key challenge in these methods is selecting suitable subspaces to ensure an effective optimization trajectory. Most existing approaches select the dominant subspace to preserve gradient information, as this intuitively provides the best approximation. However, we find that in practice, the dominant subspace stops changing during pretraining, thereby constraining weight updates to similar subspaces. In this paper, we propose importance sampling for low-rank optimization in LLM pretraining with a provable convergence guarantee, which the dominant subspace approach does not have. Empirically, we demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms previous methods in LLM pretraining tasks.
中摘: 低秩优化作为一种有前途的方法，已经崭露头角，能够实现大型语言模型（LLM）训练时的内存高效利用。现有的低秩优化方法通常将梯度投影到一个低秩子空间上，从而降低了存储优化器状态的内存成本。这些方法中的关键挑战在于选择合适的子空间以确保有效的优化路径。大多数现有方法会选择主导子空间来保留梯度信息，因为直观上这能提供最佳近似。然而，我们发现，在实践中，主导子空间在预训练过程中会停止变化，从而将权重更新限制在相似的子空间内。在这篇论文中，我们提出了一种具有可证明收敛保证的重要性抽样方法，用于LLM预训练中的低秩优化，而主导子空间方法则不具备这一保证。通过实证，我们展示了我们的方法在LLM预训练任务中显著优于先前的方法。

[No.81] QuanTA: Efficient High-Rank Fine-Tuning of LLMs with Quantum-Informed Tensor Adaptation
来源: NeurIPS 2024

[No.82] RankRAG: Unifying Context Ranking with Retrieval-Augmented Generation in LLMs
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: RankRAG：在大型语言模型中统一上下文排序与检索增强生成
类别: natural language processing
作者: Yue Yu;Wei Ping;Zihan Liu;Boxin Wang;Jiaxuan You;Chao Zhang;Mohammad Shoeybi;Bryan Catanzaro;
机构: Georgia Institute of Technology;NVIDIA;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/95135
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.02485
英摘: Large language models (LLMs) typically utilize the top-k contexts from a retriever in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). In this work, we propose a novel instruction fine-tuning framework RankRAG, which instruction-tunes a single LLM for the dual purpose of context ranking and answer generation in RAG. In particular, the instruction-tuned LLMs work surprisingly well by adding a small fraction of ranking data into the training blend, and outperform existing expert ranking models, including the same LLM exclusively fine-tuned on a large amount of ranking data. For generation, we compare our model with many strong baselines, including GPT-4-0613, GPT-4-turbo-2024-0409, and ChatQA-1.5, an open-sourced model with the state-of-the-art performance on RAG benchmarks. Specifically, our Llama3-RankRAG significantly outperforms Llama3-ChatQA-1.5 and GPT-4 models on nine knowledge-intensive benchmarks. In addition, it also performs comparably to GPT-4 on five RAG benchmarks in the biomedical domain without instruction fine-tuning on biomedical data, demonstrating its superb capability for generalization to new domains.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLM）通常在检索增强生成（RAG）中利用检索器提供的前k个上下文。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种新颖的指令微调框架RankRAG，该框架能够对单一的LLM进行指令微调，以实现RAG中上下文排序和答案生成的双重目的。具体来说，通过在训练混合数据中加入一小部分排名数据，指令微调后的LLM表现出惊人的效果，并且超越了现有的专家排名模型，包括在同一大量排名数据上独家微调的相同LLM。对于生成任务，我们将我们的模型与多个强大的基线模型进行了比较，包括GPT-4-0613、GPT-4-turbo-2024-0409以及ChatQA-1.5，这是一个开源模型，在RAG基准测试上具有最先进的性能。具体而言，我们的Llama3-RankRAG在九个知识密集型基准测试上显著超过了Llama3-ChatQA-1.5和GPT-4模型。此外，在没有在生物医学数据上进行指令微调的情况下，它在五个生物医学领域的RAG基准测试中的表现也与GPT-4相当，展示了其在新领域泛化能力的卓越性。

[No.84] Low-Rank Optimal Transport through Factor Relaxation with Latent Coupling
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 低秩最优传输通过因子放松与潜在耦合
类别: other
作者: Peter Halmos;Xinhao Liu;Julian Gold;Benjamin Raphael;
机构: Princeton University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/94081
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.10555
英摘: Optimal transport (OT) is a general framework for finding a minimum-cost transport plan, or coupling, between probability distributions, and has many applications in machine learning. A key challenge in applying OT to massive datasets is the quadratic scaling of the coupling matrix with the size of the dataset. [Forrow et al. 2019] introduced a factored coupling for the k-Wasserstein barycenter problem, which [Scetbon et al. 2021] adapted to solve the primal low-rank OT problem. We derive an alternative parameterization of the low-rank problem based on the $\textit{latent coupling}$ (LC) factorization previously introduced by [Lin et al. 2021] generalizing [Forrow et al. 2019]. The LC factorization has multiple advantages for low-rank OT including decoupling the problem into three OT problems and greater flexibility and interpretability. We leverage these advantages to derive a new algorithm $\textit{Factor Relaxation with Latent Coupling}$ (FRLC), which uses $\textit{coordinate}$ mirror descent to compute the LC factorization. FRLC handles multiple OT objectives (Wasserstein, Gromov-Wasserstein, Fused Gromov-Wasserstein), and marginal constraints (balanced, unbalanced, and semi-relaxed) with linear space complexity. We provide theoretical results on FRLC, and demonstrate superior performance on diverse applications -- including graph clustering and spatial transcriptomics -- while demonstrating its interpretability.
中摘: 最优传输（OT）是一个通用框架，用于在概率分布之间找到最低成本的传输计划或耦合，并在机器学习中有许多应用。将OT应用于大规模数据集的关键挑战之一是耦合矩阵的大小与数据集规模呈二次方增长。[Forrow等人，2019]为k-Wasserstein重心问题引入了一种分解耦合，而[Scetbon等人，2021]将其改编以解决原始低秩OT问题。我们基于[Forrow等人，2019]提出的[Lin等人，2021]的$\textit{潜在耦合}$（LC）分解推导出低秩问题的另一种参数化方法，该方法对低秩OT有多个优势，包括将问题分解为三个OT问题，以及更高的灵活性和可解释性。我们利用这些优势来推导一种新算法$\textit{带有潜在耦合的因子松弛}$（FRLC），它使用$\textit{坐标}$镜像下降法来计算LC分解。FRLC能够处理多种OT目标（Wasserstein，Gromov-Wasserstein，Fused Gromov-Wasserstein）以及边际约束（平衡，不平衡，半松弛），且具有线性空间复杂度。我们提供了关于FRLC的理论结果，并在各种应用上展示了其优越性能，包括图聚类和空间转录组学，同时证明了其可解释性。

[No.86] Reranking Laws for Language Generation: A Communication-Theoretic Perspective
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 语言生成中的重排序规则：从通信理论的角度看
类别: natural language processing
作者: António Farinhas;Haau-Sing Li;Andre Martins;
机构: Instituto Superior Técnico;Instituto de Telecomunicações;Unbabel;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/93420
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.07131
英摘: To ensure large language models (LLMs) are used safely, one must reduce their propensity to hallucinate or to generate unacceptable answers. A simple and often used strategy is to first let the LLM generate multiple hypotheses and then employ a reranker to choose the best one. In this paper, we draw a parallel between this strategy and the use of redundancy to decrease the error rate in noisy communication channels. We conceptualize the generator as a sender transmitting multiple descriptions of a message through parallel noisy channels. The receiver decodes the message by ranking the (potentially corrupted) descriptions and selecting the one found to be most reliable. We provide conditions under which this protocol is asymptotically error-free (i.e., yields an acceptable answer almost surely) even in scenarios where the reranker is imperfect (governed by Mallows or Zipf-Mandelbrot models) and the channel distributions are statistically dependent. We use our framework to obtain reranking laws which we validate empirically on two real-world tasks using LLMs: text-to-code generation with DeepSeek-Coder 7B and machine translation of medical data with TowerInstruct 13B.
中摘: 为了确保大型语言模型（LLM）的安全使用，必须降低它们产生幻觉或生成不可接受答案的倾向。一个简单且经常采用的策略是首先让LLM生成多个假设，然后使用重排序器来选择最佳的一个。在这篇论文中，我们将这一策略与在嘈杂通信信道中利用冗余减少错误率的方法进行了类比。我们把生成器概念化为发送者，通过并行的嘈杂信道传输消息的多个描述。接收者通过对接收到的（可能已被破坏的）描述进行排名，并选择被认为最可靠的描述来解码消息。我们提供了一些条件，在这些条件下，即使在重排序器不完美（受Mallows或Zipf-Mandelbrot模型控制）和信道分布统计上相关的情况下，该协议也能在渐近情况下无误（即，几乎肯定能给出可接受的答案）。我们利用我们的框架获得了重排序法则，并在两个实际任务中对这些法则进行了实证验证：使用DeepSeek-Coder 7B进行文本到代码的生成，以及使用TowerInstruct 13B进行医疗数据的机器翻译。

[No.90] Loki: Low-rank Keys for Efficient Sparse Attention
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 洛基: 低秩密钥以实现高效的稀疏注意力
类别: infrastructure
作者: Prajwal Singhania;Siddharth Singh;Shwai He;Soheil Feizi;Abhinav Bhatele;
机构: University of Maryland;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/93430
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.02542
英摘: Inference on large language models (LLMs) can be expensive in terms of the compute and memory costs involved, especially when long sequence lengths are used. In particular, the self-attention mechanism used in LLM inference contributes significantly to these costs, which has sparked an interest in approximating the self-attention computation to reduce such costs. In this work, we propose to approximate self-attention by focusing on the dimensionality of key vectors computed in the attention block. Our analysis reveals that key vectors lie in a significantly lower-dimensional space, consistently across several datasets and models. Exploiting this observation, we propose Loki, a novel sparse attention method that ranks and selects tokens in the KV-cache based on attention scores computed in low-dimensional space. Our evaluations show that Loki is able to speed up the attention computation due to reduced data movement (load/store) and compute costs while maintaining the efficacy of the models better than other popular approximation methods.
中摘: 在大型语言模型（LLM）上的推理可能因涉及的计算和内存成本而变得昂贵，尤其是在使用长序列长度时。特别是，LLM推理中使用的自注意力机制对这些成本做出了显著贡献，这激发了人们寻找近似自注意力计算以降低此类成本的兴趣。在这项工作中，我们提出通过关注在注意力模块中计算的关键向量的维度来近似自注意力。我们的分析揭示了关键向量始终位于一个显著低维的空间内，这一结论在多个数据集和模型上保持一致。利用这一观察结果，我们提出了Loki，这是一种新颖的稀疏注意力方法，它基于在低维空间中计算的注意力得分，在KV缓存中对令牌进行排名和选择。我们的评估表明，Loki能够通过减少数据移动（加载/存储）和计算成本来加速注意力计算，同时比其他流行的近似方法更好地保持模型的有效性。

[No.91] Efficient Minimum Bayes Risk Decoding using Low-Rank Matrix Completion Algorithms
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 使用低秩矩阵完成算法实现高效的最小贝叶斯风险解码
类别: natural language processing
作者: Firas Trabelsi;David Vilar;Mara Finkelstein;Markus Freitag;
机构: Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/96360
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.02832
英摘: Minimum Bayes Risk (MBR) decoding is a powerful decoding strategy widely used for text generation tasks, but its quadratic computational complexity limits its practical application. This paper presents a novel approach for approximating MBR decoding using matrix completion techniques, focusing on the task of machine translation. We formulate MBR decoding as a matrix completion problem, where the utility metric scores between candidate hypotheses and pseudo-reference translations form a low-rank matrix. First, we empirically show that the scores matrices indeed have a low-rank structure. Then, we exploit this by only computing a random subset of the scores and efficiently recover the missing entries in the matrix by applying the Alternating Least Squares (ALS) algorithm, thereby enabling a fast approximation of the MBR decoding process. Our experimental results on machine translation tasks demonstrate that the proposed method requires 1/16 utility metric computations compared to vanilla MBR decoding while achieving equal translation quality measured by COMET22 on the WMT22 dataset (en<>de and en<>ru). We also benchmark our method against other approximation methods and we show gains in quality when comparing to them.
中摘: 最小贝叶斯风险(MBR)解码是一种广泛用于文本生成任务的强大解码策略，但其二次计算复杂性限制了其实用应用。本文提出了一种使用矩阵补全技术来近似MBR解码的新方法，重点关注机器翻译任务。我们将MBR解码公式化为一个矩阵补全问题，其中候选假设和伪参考翻译之间的效用度量得分形成一个低秩矩阵。首先，我们通过实验证明得分矩阵确实具有低秩结构。然后，我们利用这一点，仅计算得分的一随机子集，并通过应用交替最小二乘(Alternating Least Squares, ALS)算法有效地恢复矩阵中的缺失条目，从而实现对MBR解码过程的快速近似。我们在机器翻译任务上的实验结果表明，与原始MBR解码相比，所提出的方法只需要1/16的效用度量计算，同时在WMT22数据集(en<>de和en<>ru)上，以COMET22为衡量标准，达到相同的翻译质量。我们还将我们的方法与其他近似方法进行基准测试，并显示在比较时质量有所提高。

[No.93] Aggregating Quantitative Relative Judgments: From Social Choice to Ranking Prediction
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 聚合定量相对判断：从社会选择到排名预测
类别: algorithmic game theory
作者: Yixuan Even Xu;Hanrui Zhang;Yu Cheng;Vincent Conitzer;
机构: Tsinghua University;Google;Brown University;University of Oxford;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/96770
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.05550
英摘: Quantitative Relative Judgment Aggregation (QRJA) is a new research topic in (computational) social choice. In the QRJA model, agents provide judgments on the relative quality of different candidates, and the goal is to aggregate these judgments across all agents. In this work, our main conceptual contribution is to explore the interplay between QRJA in a social choice context and its application to ranking prediction. We observe that in QRJA, judges do not have to be people with subjective opinions; for example, a race can be viewed as a "judgment" on the contestants' relative abilities. This allows us to aggregate results from multiple races to evaluate the contestants' true qualities. At a technical level, we introduce new aggregation rules for QRJA and study their structural and computational properties. We evaluate the proposed methods on data from various real races and show that QRJA-based methods offer effective and interpretable ranking predictions.
中摘: 量化相对判断聚合（Quantitative Relative Judgment Aggregation，简称QRJA）是计算社会选择领域的一个新兴研究主题。在QRJA模型中，代理人对不同候选人的相对质量提供判断，目标是在所有代理人之间聚合这些判断。在本工作中，我们的主要概念性贡献在于探索社会选择背景下的QRJA与其在排名预测应用之间的相互作用。我们注意到，在QRJA中，评判者不必是具有主观意见的人；例如，一场比赛可以被视为对参赛者相对能力的一种“判断”。这使我们能够从多场比赛的结果中进行聚合，以评估参赛者的真正素质。在技术层面上，我们为QRJA引入了新的聚合规则，并研究了它们的结构和计算属性。我们在来自各种真实比赛的数据上评估了所提出的方法，并证明基于QRJA的方法提供了有效且可解释的排名预测。

[No.94] High Rank Path Development: an approach to learning the filtration of stochastic processes
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 高级路径发展：一种学习随机过程滤波的方法
类别: probabilistic methods
作者: Jiajie Tao;Hao Ni;Chong Liu;
机构: University College London;ShanghaiTech University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/93179
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.14913
英摘: Since the weak convergence for stochastic processes does not account for the growth of information over time which is represented by the underlying filtration, a slightly erroneous stochastic model in weak topology may cause huge loss in multi-periods decision making problems. To address such discontinuities Aldous introduced the extended weak convergence, which can fully characterise all essential properties, including the filtration, of stochastic processes; however was considered to be hard to find efficient numerical implementations. In this paper, we introduce a novel metric called High Rank PCF Distance (HRPCFD) for extended weak convergence based on the high rank path development method from rough path theory, which also defines the characteristic function for measure-valued processes. We then show that such HRPCFD admits many favourable analytic properties which allows us to design an efficient algorithm for training HRPCFD from data and construct the HRPCF-GAN by using HRPCFD as the discriminator for conditional time series generation. Our numerical experiments on both hypothesis testing and generative modelling validate the out-performance of our approach compared with several state-of-the-art methods, highlighting its potential in broad applications of synthetic time series generation and in addressing classic financial and economic challenges, such as optimal stopping or utility maximisation problems.
中摘: 由于随机过程的弱收敛性没有考虑到随时间增长的信息，这些信息由底层滤波器表示，因此在弱拓扑下稍有误差的随机模型可能会在多期决策问题中造成巨大损失。为了解决这种不连续性，奥尔德斯(Aldous)引入了扩展弱收敛性，它可以完全描述随机过程的所有基本属性，包括滤波器；然而，被认为难以找到有效的数值实现方法。在这篇论文中，我们介绍了一种新的度量，称为高阶路径压缩函数距离(HRPCFD)，它是基于粗糙路径理论中的高阶路径展开方法来定义扩展弱收敛性的，同时也为测度值过程定义了特征函数。然后，我们证明了HRPCFD具有许多有利的分析性质，这使我们能够设计出一种从数据训练HRPCFD的有效算法，并通过使用HRPCFD作为条件时间序列生成的判别器，构建了HRPCF-GAN。我们在假设检验和生成建模方面的数值实验验证了我们的方法与几种最先进的方法相比的优越性，突出了其在合成时间序列生成的广泛应用以及解决经典金融和经济挑战（如最优停止或效用最大化问题）方面的潜力。

请注意，奥尔德斯(Aldous)、HRPCFD和HRPCF-GAN等术语在中文翻译中可能需要根据具体上下文进行适当调整以保持其专业性和准确性。

[No.95] LoRANN: Low-Rank Matrix Factorization for Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: LoRANN：低秩矩阵分解近似最近邻搜索
类别: infrastructure
作者: Elias Jääsaari;Ville Hyvönen;Teemu Roos;
机构: University of Helsinki;Aalto University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/93112
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18926
英摘: Approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search is a key component in many modern machine learning pipelines; recent use cases include retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) and vector databases. Clustering-based ANN algorithms, that use score computation methods based on product quantization (PQ), are often used in industrial-scale applications due to their scalability and suitability for distributed and disk-based implementations. However, they have slower query times than the leading graph-based ANN algorithms. In this work, we propose a new supervised score computation method based on the observation that inner product approximation is a multivariate (multi-output) regression problem that can be solved efficiently by reduced-rank regression. Our experiments show that on modern high-dimensional data sets, the proposed reduced-rank regression (RRR) method is superior to PQ in both query latency and memory usage. We also introduce LoRANN, a clustering-based ANN library that leverages the proposed score computation method. LoRANN is competitive with the leading graph-based algorithms and outperforms the state-of-the-art GPU ANN methods on high-dimensional data sets.
中摘: 近似最近邻（ANN）搜索是许多现代机器学习管道的关键组成部分；最近的应用案例包括检索增强生成（RAG）和向量数据库。基于聚类的ANN算法，使用基于产品量化（PQ）的得分计算方法，由于其可扩展性和适用于分布式及基于磁盘的实现，常用于工业级应用。然而，它们的查询时间比领先的基于图的ANN算法慢。在本工作中，我们提出了一种新的监督式得分计算方法，基于观察到内积逼近是一个可以由降秩回归高效解决的多元（多输出）回归问题。我们的实验表明，在现代高维数据集上，提出的降秩回归（RRR）方法在查询延迟和内存使用方面都优于PQ。我们还引入了LoRANN，一个基于聚类的ANN库，利用所提出的得分计算方法。LoRANN与领先的基于图的算法具有竞争力，并在高维数据集上超越了最先进的GPU ANN方法。

[No.96] Fast Tree-Field Integrators: From Low Displacement Rank to Topological Transformers
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 快速树域积分器：从低位移秩到拓扑变换器
类别: machine learning for other sciences and fields
作者: Krzysztof Marcin Choromanski;Arijit Sehanobish;Somnath Basu Roy Chowdhury;Han Lin;Kumar Avinava Dubey;Tamas Sarlos;Snigdha Chaturvedi;
机构: Google;Kensho Technologies;University of North Carolina;University of North Carolina at Chapel Hill;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/96014
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.15881
英摘: We present a new class of fast polylog-linear algorithms based on the theory of structured matrices (in particular low displacement rank) for integrating tensor fields defined on weighted trees. Several applications of the resulting fast tree-field integrators (FTFIs) are presented, including (a) approximation of graph metrics with tree metrics, (b) graph classification, (c) modeling on meshes, and finally (d) Topological Transformers (TTs) (Choromanski et al., 2022) for images. For Topological Transformers, we propose new relative position encoding (RPE) masking mechanisms with as few as three extra learnable parameters per Transformer layer, leading to 1.0-1.5%+ accuracy gains. Importantly, most of FTFIs are exact methods, thus numerically equivalent to their brute-force counterparts. When applied to graphs with thousands of nodes, those exact algorithms provide 5.7-13x speedups. We also provide an extensive theoretical analysis of our methods.
中摘: 我们提出了一类基于结构化矩阵理论（特别是低位移秩）的快速多项对数线性算法，用于在加权树上定义的张量场积分。展示了由此产生的快速树场积分器(FTFI)的多个应用，包括(a)用树度量近似图度量，(b)图分类，(c)网格上的建模，最后(d)用于图像的拓扑变换器(TTs)（Choromanski等人，2022）。对于拓扑变换器，我们提出了新的相对位置编码(RPE)屏蔽机制，每层变换器仅需额外学习三个参数，从而实现了1.0%-1.5%以上的准确率提升。重要的是，大多数FTFI方法都是精确方法，因此在数值上等同于其暴力计算的对应方法。当应用于具有数千个节点的图时，这些精确算法提供了5.7-13倍的速度提升。我们还提供了我们方法的广泛理论分析。

[No.97] FLoRA: Federated Fine-Tuning Large Language Models with Heterogeneous Low-Rank Adaptations
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: FLoRA：联邦异构低秩微调大型语言模型
类别: infrastructure
作者: Ziyao Wang;Zheyu Shen;Yexiao He;Guoheng Sun;Hongyi Wang;Lingjuan Lyu;Ang Li;
机构: University of Maryland;Sichuan University;Carnegie Mellon University;Sony Corporation;Duke University;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.05976
英摘: The rapid development of Large Language Models (LLMs) has been pivotal in advancing AI, with pre-trained LLMs being adaptable to diverse downstream tasks through fine-tuning. Federated learning (FL) further enhances fine-tuning in a privacy-aware manner by utilizing clients' local data through in-situ computation, eliminating the need for data movement. However, fine-tuning LLMs, given their massive scale of parameters, poses challenges for clients with constrained and heterogeneous resources in FL. Previous methods employed low-rank adaptation (LoRA) for efficient federated fine-tuning but utilized traditional FL aggregation strategies on LoRA adapters. These approaches led to mathematically inaccurate aggregation noise, reducing fine-tuning effectiveness and failing to address heterogeneous LoRAs. In this work, we first highlight the mathematical incorrectness of LoRA aggregation in existing federated fine-tuning methods. We introduce a new approach called FLORA that enables federated fine-tuning on heterogeneous LoRA adapters across clients through a novel stacking-based aggregation method. Our approach is noise-free and seamlessly supports heterogeneous LoRA adapters. Extensive experiments demonstrate FLORA' s superior performance in both homogeneous and heterogeneous settings, surpassing state-of-the-art methods. We envision this work as a milestone for efficient, privacy-preserving, and accurate federated fine-tuning of LLMs. Our code is available at https://github.com/ATP-1010/FederatedLLM.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLM）的快速发展在推动人工智能进步方面起到了关键作用，预训练的LLM通过微调能够适应各种下游任务。联邦学习（FL）通过利用客户端本地数据进行原地计算，以一种关注隐私的方式进一步增强了微调过程，避免了数据传输的需求。然而，鉴于LLM参数规模巨大，在联邦学习中，对于资源受限且异构的客户端而言，对LLM进行微调存在挑战。先前的方法使用低秩适应（LoRA）进行高效的联邦微调，但在LoRA适配器上采用了传统的联邦学习聚合策略。这些方法导致了数学上的不准确聚合噪声，降低了微调效果，并未能解决异构LoRA的问题。在本工作中，我们首先指出了现有联邦微调方法中LoRA聚合的数学不正确性。我们提出了一种名为FLORA的新方法，该方法通过一种创新的基于堆叠的聚合方式，使异构LoRA适配器能够在客户端间进行联邦微调。我们的方法无噪声，能无缝支持异构LoRA适配器。广泛的实验表明，无论是在同构还是异构环境下，FLORA都表现出优于现有最先进方法的卓越性能。我们视这项工作为高效、保护隐私且精确的联邦微调LLM的一个里程碑。我们的代码可在https://github.com/ATP-1010/FederatedLLM获取。

来源: NeurIPS 2024

英摘: Text-to-image diffusion has attracted vast attention due to its impressive image-generation capabilities. However, when it comes to human-centric text-to-image generation, particularly in the context of faces and hands, the results often fall short of naturalness due to insufficient training priors. We alleviate the issue in this work from two perspectives. 1) From the data aspect, we carefully collect a human-centric dataset comprising over one million high-quality human-in-the-scene images and two specific sets of close-up images of faces and hands. To validate the superiority of MoLE in the context of human-centric image generation compared to state-of-the-art, we construct two benchmarks and perform evaluations with diverse metrics and human studies. [No.100] Inferring stochastic low-rank recurrent neural networks from neural data
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 从神经数据推断随机低秩递归神经网络
类别: neuroscience and cognitive science
作者: Matthijs Pals;A Erdem Sağtekin;Felix C Pei;Manuel Gloeckler;Jakob H. Macke;
机构: Eberhard Karls University of Tübingen;University of Tuebingen;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/96170
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.16749
英摘: A central aim in computational neuroscience is to relate the activity of large populations of neurons to an underlying dynamical system. Models of these neural dynamics should ideally be both interpretable and fit the observed data well. Low-rank recurrent neural networks (RNNs) exhibit such interpretability by having tractable dynamics. However, it is unclear how to best fit low-rank RNNs to data consisting of noisy observations of an underlying stochastic system. Here, we propose to fit stochastic low-rank RNNs with variational sequential Monte Carlo methods. We validate our method on several datasets consisting of both continuous and spiking neural data, where we obtain lower dimensional latent dynamics than current state of the art methods. Additionally, for low-rank models with piecewise linear nonlinearities, we show how to efficiently identify all fixed points in polynomial rather than exponential cost in the number of units, making analysis of the inferred dynamics tractable for large RNNs. Our method both elucidates the dynamical systems underlying experimental recordings and provides a generative model whose trajectories match observed variability.
中摘: 计算神经科学的核心目标之一是将大量神经元的活动与潜在的动力系统联系起来。这些神经动力学模型在理想情况下既可解释又能很好地拟合观测数据。低秩循环神经网络（RNN）通过其可追踪的动力学表现出这种可解释性。然而，如何最好地将低秩RNN拟合到包含对潜在随机系统嘈杂观测的数据尚不清楚。在这里，我们提出使用变分序贯蒙特卡洛方法来拟合随机低秩RNN。我们在包含连续和尖峰神经数据的多个数据集上验证了我们的方法，在那里我们获得了比现有最先进方法更低维度的潜在动力学。此外，对于具有分段线性非线性的低秩模型，我们展示了如何以多项式而非指数成本在单位数量上有效地识别所有固定点，使得大型RNN的动力学分析变得可行。我们的方法不仅揭示了实验记录下潜在的动力系统，而且还提供了一个生成模型，其轨迹与观察到的变异性相匹配。

[No.102] Prediction-Powered Ranking of Large Language Models
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 大型语言模型的预测驱动排名
类别: evaluation
作者: Ivi Chatzi;Eleni Straitouri;Suhas Thejaswi;Manuel Gomez Rodriguez;
机构: Max Planck Institute for Software Systems;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/96445
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.17826
英摘: Large language models are often ranked according to their level of alignment with human preferences -- a model is better than other models if its outputs are more frequently preferred by humans. One of the popular ways to elicit human preferences utilizes pairwise comparisons between the outputs provided by different models to the same inputs. However, since gathering pairwise comparisons by humans is costly and time-consuming, it has become a common practice to gather pairwise comparisons by a strong large language model -- a model strongly aligned with human preferences. Surprisingly, practitioners cannot currently measure the uncertainty that any mismatch between human and model preferences may introduce in the constructed rankings. In this work, we develop a statistical framework to bridge this gap. Given a (small) set of pairwise comparisons by humans and a large set of pairwise comparisons by a model, our framework provides a rank-set -- a set of possible ranking positions -- for each of the models under comparison. Moreover, it guarantees that, with a probability greater than or equal to a user-specified value, the rank-sets cover the true ranking consistent with the distribution of human pairwise preferences asymptotically. Using pairwise comparisons made by humans in the LMSYS Chatbot Arena platform and pairwise comparisons made by three strong large language models, we empirically demonstrate the effectivity of our framework and show that the rank-sets constructed using only pairwise comparisons by the strong large language models are often inconsistent with (the distribution of) human pairwise preferences.
中摘: 大型语言模型通常根据其与人类偏好的一致性程度进行排名——如果一个模型的输出更频繁地被人类所偏好，那么它就比其他模型更好。一种流行的方法来获取人类偏好是通过比较不同模型对相同输入产生的输出，进行两两对比。然而，由于由人类收集两两对比数据既昂贵又耗时，现在普遍的做法是使用一个强大的大型语言模型来收集这些对比数据——这个模型与人类偏好高度一致。令人惊讶的是，目前从业者无法衡量任何人类偏好与模型偏好不匹配可能在构建的排名中引入的不确定性。在这项工作中，我们开发了一种统计框架来填补这一空白。给定一组（较小的）由人类做出的两两对比和一组由模型生成的大量两两对比，我们的框架为每个待比较的模型提供了一个排名集合——即可能的排名位置集合。此外，它保证了以大于或等于用户指定值的概率，随着样本量的增加，这些排名集合将覆盖与人类两两偏好分布相一致的真实排名。利用LMSYS聊天机器人竞技平台中人类进行的两两对比以及三个强大的大型语言模型做出的两两对比，我们实证展示了我们框架的有效性，并表明仅使用强大语言模型的两两对比构建的排名集合往往与（人类的）两两偏好分布不一致。

[No.103] On Socially Fair Low-Rank Approximation and Column Subset Selection
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 社会公平低秩近似与列子集选择
类别: fairness
作者: Zhao Song;Ali Vakilian;David Woodruff;Samson Zhou;
机构: Adobe;Toyota Technological Institute at Chicago;Carnegie Mellon University;Texas A&M University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/96034
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06063
英摘: Low-rank approximation and column subset selection are two fundamental and related problems that are applied across a wealth of machine learning applications. In this paper, we study the question of socially fair low-rank approximation and socially fair column subset selection, where the goal is to minimize the loss over all sub-populations of the data. We show that surprisingly, even constant-factor approximation to fair low-rank approximation requires exponential time under certain standard complexity hypotheses. On the positive side, we give an algorithm for fair low-rank approximation that, for a constant number of groups and constant-factor accuracy, runs in $2^{\text{poly}(k)}$ time rather than the naïve $n^{\text{poly}(k)}$, which is a substantial improvement when the dataset has a large number $n$ of observations. We then show that there exist bicriteria approximation algorithms for fair low-rank approximation and fair column subset selection that run in polynomial time.
中摘: 低秩近似和列子集选择是两个基本且相关的问题，在众多机器学习应用中都有所应用。在这篇论文中，我们研究了社会公平的低秩近似和社会公平的列子集选择问题，目标是最小化数据所有子群体的损失。令人惊讶的是，我们证明了在某些标准复杂性假设下，即使是对公平低秩近似的常数因子逼近也需要指数时间。积极的一面是，我们给出了一种针对公平低秩近似的算法，对于固定数量的群体和常数因子精度，运行时间为$2^{\text{poly}(k)}$，而非天真的$n^{\text{poly}(k)}$，当数据集有大量观测值$n$时，这是一个显著的改进。然后我们证明存在多项式时间内运行的双准则逼近算法，适用于公平低秩近似和公平列子集选择。

这个结果表明，在追求社会公平性的背景下，解决低秩近似和列子集选择问题可能需要付出更高的计算代价，但仍然存在有效的算法来处理这一挑战。对于具有固定数量群体的情况，我们的算法能够以更合理的时间复杂度实现公平的低秩近似。此外，我们还展示了存在能够在多项式时间内运行的双准则逼近算法，这为实际应用中的大规模数据处理提供了可能性。

[No.104] Coherence-free Entrywise Estimation of Eigenvectors in Low-rank Signal-plus-noise Matrix Models
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 无一致性逐元素估计低秩信噪比矩阵模型中的特征向量
类别: probabilistic methods
作者: Hao Yan;Keith Levin;
机构: University of Wisconsin-Madison;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/96125
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.24195
英摘: Spectral methods are widely used to estimate eigenvectors of a low-rank signal matrix subject to noise. These methods use the leading eigenspace of an observed matrix to estimate this low-rank signal. Typically, the entrywise estimation error of these methods depends on the coherence of the low-rank signal matrix with respect to the standard basis. In this work, we present a novel method for eigenvector estimation that avoids this dependence on coherence. Assuming a rank-one signal matrix, under mild technical conditions, the entrywise estimation error of our method provably has no dependence on the coherence under Gaussian noise (i.e., in the spiked Wigner model), and achieves the optimal estimation rate up to logarithmic factors. Simulations demonstrate that our method performs well under non-Gaussian noise and that an extension of our method to the case of a rank-$r$ signal matrix has little to no dependence on the coherence. In addition, we derive new metric entropy bounds for rank-$r$ singular subspaces under $\ell_{2,\infty}$ distance, which may be of independent interest. We use these new bounds to improve the best known lower bound for rank-$r$ eigenspace estimation under $\ell_{2,\infty}$ distance.
中摘: 谱方法被广泛用于估计低秩信号矩阵在噪声下的特征向量。这些方法利用观测矩阵的主要特征子空间来估计这个低秩信号。通常，这些方法的条目估计误差依赖于低秩信号矩阵与标准基之间的连贯性。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种新的特征向量估计方法，避免了这种对连贯性的依赖。假设秩为一的信号矩阵，在温和的技术条件下，我们的方法在高斯噪声（即，在尖峰Wigner模型下）下的条目估计误差理论上不依赖于连贯性，并且达到了最优估计率，除了对数因子外。模拟表明，我们的方法在非高斯噪声下表现良好，而且我们方法对秩为$r$的信号矩阵的扩展几乎不依赖于连贯性。此外，我们推导出了新的秩为$r$奇异子空间在$\ell_{2,\infty}$距离下的度量熵界，这可能具有独立的兴趣。我们使用这些新界来改进已知的在$\ell_{2,\infty}$距离下秩为$r$的特征子空间估计的最优下界。

[No.105] Bridging The Gap between Low-rank and Orthogonal Adaptation via Householder Reflection Adaptation
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 通过豪斯霍勒反射适应来弥合低秩和正交适应之间的差距
类别: deep learning architectures
作者: Shen Yuan;Haotian Liu;Hongteng Xu;
机构: Renmin University of China;Beijing Institute of Technology;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/95554
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17484
英摘: While following different technical routes, both low-rank and orthogonal adaptation techniques can efficiently adapt large-scale pre-training models in specific tasks or domains based on a small piece of trainable parameters. In this study, we bridge the gap between these two techniques, proposing a simple but effective adaptation method based on Householder reflections. Given a pre-trained model, our method fine-tunes its layers by multiplying each frozen weight matrix with an orthogonal matrix constructed by a chain of learnable Householder reflections (HRs). This HR-based orthogonal fine-tuning is equivalent to an adaptive low-rank adaptation. Moreover, we show that the orthogonality of the reflection planes corresponding to the HRs impacts the model capacity and regularity. The analysis motivates us to regularize the orthogonality of the HRs, leading to different implementations of the proposed Householder reflection adaptation (HRA) method. Compared with state-of-the-art methods, HRA achieves superior performance with fewer learnable parameters when adapting large language models and conditional image generators. The code of the experiments is available at \url{https://github.com/DaShenZi721/HRA}, and the method has been merged into the \href{https://github.com/huggingface/peft}{PEFT} package.
中摘: 虽然遵循不同的技术路径，低秩和正交适应技术都能够基于少量可训练参数有效地在特定任务或领域中对大规模预训练模型进行适应。在这项研究中，我们填补了这两种技术之间的空白，提出了一种基于Householder反射的简单而有效的适应方法。对于一个预训练模型，我们的方法通过将每个冻结的权重矩阵与由一系列可学习的Householder反射（HRs）构建的正交矩阵相乘来微调其层。这种基于HR的正交微调等价于一种自适应的低秩适应。此外，我们展示了与HRs相对应的反射平面的正交性会影响模型的容量和规律性。分析激励我们去正则化HRs的正交性，从而导致所提出的Householder反射适应（HRA）方法的不同实现。与最先进的方法相比，在适应大型语言模型和条件图像生成器时，HRA以更少的可学习参数实现了更优的性能。实验代码可在\url{https://github.com/DaShenZi721/HRA}获取，该方法已经被合并到\href{https://github.com/huggingface/peft}{PEFT}包中。

为了清晰表达，我将上述翻译稍作调整：

尽管采取了不同的技术路线，低秩和正交适应技术都能有效利用少量可训练参数，在特定任务或领域内对大规模预训练模型进行高效适应。本研究旨在弥合这两种技术间的鸿沟，我们提出了一种基于Householder反射的简单而有效的适应策略。对于预训练模型，我们的方法通过将每个冻结的权重矩阵与由一系列可学习的Householder反射（HRs）构成的正交矩阵相乘，实现对其各层的微调。这种基于HR的正交微调实质上是一种自适应的低秩适应。进一步地，我们发现HRs对应的反射平面的正交性直接影响模型的容量和规律性。基于这一分析，我们引入了对HRs正交性的正则化处理，由此产生了所提出的Householder反射适应（HRA）方法的不同实施版本。相较于当前最先进方法，当应用于大型语言模型和条件图像生成器的适应时，HRA仅需较少的可学习参数便能展现出更优异的性能。实验代码可在以下链接获取：\url{https://github.com/DaShenZi721/HRA}，同时，该方法已被整合进\href{https://github.com/huggingface/peft}{PEFT}工具包中。

[No.106] Tensor-Based Synchronization and the Low-Rankness of the Block Trifocal Tensor
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 基于张量的同步与块三焦张量的低秩性
类别: machine vision
作者: Daniel Miao;Gilad Lerman;Joe Kileel;
机构: University of Minnesota;University of Texas at Austin;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/96088
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.09313
英摘: The block tensor of trifocal tensors provides crucial geometric information on the three-view geometry of a scene. The underlying synchronization problem seeks to recover camera poses (locations and orientations up to a global transformation) from the block trifocal tensor. We establish an explicit Tucker factorization of this tensor, revealing a low multilinear rank of $(6,4,4)$ independent of the number of cameras under appropriate scaling conditions. We prove that this rank constraint provides sufficient information for camera recovery in the noiseless case. The constraint motivates a synchronization algorithm based on the higher-order singular value decomposition of the block trifocal tensor. Experimental comparisons with state-of-the-art global synchronization methods on real datasets demonstrate the potential of this algorithm for significantly improving location estimation accuracy. Overall this work suggests that higher-order interactions in synchronization problems can be exploited to improve performance, beyond the usual pairwise-based approaches.
中摘: 三目张量的块张量为场景的三视图几何提供了关键的几何信息。底层的同步问题旨在从块三目张量中恢复相机姿态（位置和方向，考虑到全局变换）。我们在适当的缩放条件下，建立了这个张量的明确Tucker分解，揭示了一个与相机数量无关的低多重线性秩(6,4,4)。我们证明了在无噪声的情况下，这个秩约束为相机恢复提供了足够的信息。这一约束激发了一种基于块三目张量高阶奇异值分解的同步算法。通过在真实数据集上与最先进的全局同步方法进行实验比较，证明了该算法有潜力显著提高位置估计的准确性。总体而言，这项工作表明，在同步问题中可以利用高阶交互来提高性能，超越通常的基于两两交互的方法。

[No.107] BLoB: Bayesian Low-Rank Adaptation by Backpropagation for Large Language Models
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: BLoB：贝叶斯低秩适应性反向传播用于大型语言模型

作者: Yibin Wang;Haizhou Shi;Ligong Han;Dimitris N. Metaxas;Hao Wang;

英摘: Large Language Models (LLMs) often suffer from overconfidence during inference, particularly when adapted to downstream domain-specific tasks with limited data. [No.108] Higher-Rank Irreducible Cartesian Tensors for Equivariant Message Passing
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 高阶不可约笛卡尔张量在不变消息传递中的应用
类别: machine learning for physical sciences
作者: Viktor Zaverkin;Francesco Alesiani;Takashi Maruyama;Federico Errica;Henrik Christiansen;Makoto Takamoto;Nicolas Weber;Mathias Niepert;
机构: NEC Laboratories Europe;NEC Corporation;

链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/94197
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.14253
英摘: The ability to perform fast and accurate atomistic simulations is crucial for advancing the chemical sciences. By learning from high-quality data, machine-learned interatomic potentials achieve accuracy on par with ab initio and first-principles methods at a fraction of their computational cost. The success of machine-learned interatomic potentials arises from integrating inductive biases such as equivariance to group actions on an atomic system, e.g., equivariance to rotations and reflections. In particular, the field has notably advanced with the emergence of equivariant message passing. Most of these models represent an atomic system using spherical tensors, tensor products of which require complicated numerical coefficients and can be computationally demanding. Cartesian tensors offer a promising alternative, though state-of-the-art methods lack flexibility in message-passing mechanisms, restricting their architectures and expressive power. This work explores higher-rank irreducible Cartesian tensors to address these limitations. We integrate irreducible Cartesian tensor products into message-passing neural networks and prove the equivariance and traceless property of the resulting layers. Through empirical evaluations on various benchmark data sets, we consistently observe on-par or better performance than that of state-of-the-art spherical and Cartesian models.
中摘: 在化学科学领域取得进展的关键在于能够进行快速而精确的原子尺度模拟。通过从高质量数据中学习，机器学习得到的原子间势能可以达到与从头算和第一性原理方法相当的精度，同时计算成本却只有其一小部分。机器学习原子间势能的成功源于整合了归纳偏置，如对原子系统群作用下的等变性，例如旋转和反射等变性。特别是，随着等变消息传递的出现，这一领域取得了显著进展。这些模型大多使用球张量来表示原子系统，而球张量的张量积需要复杂的数值系数，计算上可能较为耗时。然而，笛卡尔张量提供了一个有前景的替代方案，尽管目前最先进的方法在消息传递机制上缺乏灵活性，限制了它们的架构和表达能力。本研究探讨了利用更高秩的不可约笛卡尔张量来克服这些局限性。我们将不可约笛卡尔张量积融入到消息传递神经网络中，并证明了由此产生的层的等变性和无迹性质。通过对各种基准数据集的实证评估，我们始终观察到，与最先进的球形和笛卡尔模型相比，我们的模型性能相当或更优。

[No.109] LoRA-GA: Low-Rank Adaptation with Gradient Approximation
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: LoRA-GA：基于梯度近似的低秩适应
类别: optimization for deep networks
作者: Shaowen Wang;Linxi Yu;Jian Li;
机构: Tsinghua University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/94885
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.05000
英摘: Fine-tuning large-scale pretrained models is prohibitively expensive in terms of computational and memory costs. LoRA, as one of the most popular Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) methods, offers a cost-effective alternative by fine-tuning an auxiliary low-rank model that has significantly fewer parameters. Although LoRA reduces the computational and memory requirements significantly at each iteration, extensive empirical evidence indicates that it converges at a considerably slower rate compared to full fine-tuning, ultimately leading to increased overall compute and often worse test performance. In our paper, we perform an in-depth investigation of the initialization method of LoRA and show that careful initialization (without any change of the architecture and the training algorithm) can significantly enhance both efficiency and performance. In particular, we introduce a novel initialization method, LoRA-GA (Low Rank Adaptation with Gradient Approximation), which aligns the gradients of low-rank matrix product with those of full fine-tuning at the first step. Our extensive experiments demonstrate that LoRA-GA achieves a convergence rate comparable to that of full fine-tuning (hence being significantly faster than vanilla LoRA as well as various recent improvements) while simultaneously attaining comparable or even better performance. For example, on the subset of the GLUE dataset with T5-Base, LoRA-GA outperforms LoRA by 5.69% on average. On larger models such as Llama 2-7B, LoRA-GA shows performance improvements of 0.34, 11.52%, and 5.05% on MT-bench, GSM8K, and Human-eval, respectively. Additionally, we observe up to 2-4 times convergence speed improvement compared to vanilla LoRA, validating its effectiveness in accelerating convergence and enhancing model performance. Code is available at https://github.com/Outsider565/LoRA-GA.
中摘: 大规模预训练模型的微调在计算和内存成本上极为昂贵。LoRA作为最受欢迎的参数高效微调（PEFT）方法之一，通过微调一个参数数量显著减少的辅助低秩模型，提供了一种经济实惠的替代方案。尽管LoRA在每次迭代时大幅降低了计算和内存需求，大量实证表明，与全量微调相比，它的收敛速度明显更慢，最终导致总计算量增加，并且测试性能通常较差。在我们的论文中，我们对LoRA的初始化方法进行了深入研究，并证明了精心的初始化（不改变架构和训练算法）可以显著提高效率和性能。具体而言，我们引入了一种新的初始化方法，LoRA-GA（基于梯度近似的低秩适应），它在第一步使低秩矩阵乘积的梯度与全量微调的梯度保持一致。我们的广泛实验表明，LoRA-GA达到了与全量微调相媲美的收敛率（因此比原始LoRA以及最近的各种改进都要快得多），同时实现了相当甚至更好的性能。例如，在使用T5-Base的GLUE数据集子集上，LoRA-GA平均超出LoRA 5.69%。在更大的模型如Llama 2-7B上，LoRA-GA在MT-bench、GSM8K和Human-eval上的性能分别提高了0.34、11.52%和5.05%。此外，我们观察到相比于原始LoRA，收敛速度提高了2-4倍，这证实了其在加速收敛和提升模型性能方面的有效性。代码可在https://github.com/Outsider565/LoRA-GA获取。

[No.110] Learning Discrete Latent Variable Structures with Tensor Rank Conditions
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 学习张量秩条件下的离散潜在变量结构
类别: causal inference
作者: Zhengming Chen;Ruichu Cai;Feng Xie;Jie Qiao;Anpeng Wu;Zijian Li;Zhifeng Hao;Kun Zhang;
机构: Guangdong University of Technology;Beijing Technology and Business University;Mohamed bin Zayed University of Artificial Intelligence;Shantou University;Carnegie Mellon University;

链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/96540
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.07020
英摘: Unobserved discrete data are ubiquitous in many scientific disciplines, and how to learn the causal structure of these latent variables is crucial for uncovering data patterns. Most studies focus on the linear latent variable model or impose strict constraints on latent structures, which fail to address cases in discrete data involving non-linear relationships or complex latent structures. To achieve this, we explore a tensor rank condition on contingency tables for an observed variable set $\mathbf{X}_p$, showing that the rank is determined by the minimum support of a specific conditional set (not necessary in $\mathbf{X}_p$) that d-separates all variables in $\mathbf{X}_p$. By this, one can locate the latent variable through probing the rank on different observed variables set, and further identify the latent causal structure under some structure assumptions. We present the corresponding identification algorithm and conduct simulated experiments to verify the effectiveness of our method. In general, our results elegantly extend the identification boundary for causal discovery with discrete latent variables and expand the application scope of causal discovery with latent variables.
中摘: 未观测的离散数据在许多科学领域中普遍存在，学习这些潜在变量的因果结构对于揭示数据模式至关重要。大多数研究集中在线性潜在变量模型上，或者对潜在结构施加严格的限制，这未能解决离散数据中涉及非线性关系或复杂潜在结构的情况。为此，我们探讨了在观察变量集$\mathbf{X}_p$的列联表上的一种张量秩条件，表明秩由一个特定条件集合（不一定是$\mathbf{X}_p$中的）的最小支持决定，该条件集合d-分离了$\mathbf{X}_p$中的所有变量。通过这一点，我们可以通过探测不同观察变量集上的秩来定位潜在变量，并在一些结构假设下进一步识别潜在的因果结构。我们给出了相应的识别算法，并进行了模拟实验以验证我们方法的有效性。总体而言，我们的成果优雅地扩展了具有离散潜在变量的因果发现的识别边界，并扩大了具有潜在变量的因果发现的应用范围。

[No.111] The Limits of Transfer Reinforcement Learning with Latent Low-rank Structure
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 具有潜在低秩结构的迁移强化学习的局限性
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Tyler Sam;Yudong Chen;Christina Yu;
机构: Cornell University;University of Wisconsin-Madison;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/93563
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.21601
英摘: Many reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms are too costly to use in practice due to the large sizes $S, A$ of the problem's state and action space. To resolve this issue, we study transfer RL with latent low rank structure. We consider the problem of transferring a latent low rank representation when the source and target MDPs have transition kernels with Tucker rank $(S , d, A )$, $(S , S , d), (d, S, A )$, or $(d , d , d )$. In each setting, we introduce the transfer-ability coefficient $α$ that measures the difficulty of representational transfer. Our algorithm learns latent representations in each source MDP and then exploits the linear structure to remove the dependence on $S, A $, or $S A$ in the target MDP regret bound. We complement our positive results with information theoretic lower bounds that show our algorithms (excluding the ($d, d, d$) setting) are minimax-optimal with respect to $α$.
中摘: 许多强化学习（RL）算法在实际应用中过于昂贵，因为问题的状态空间和动作空间大小 $S, A$ 非常大。为了解决这个问题，我们研究了具有潜在低秩结构的迁移强化学习。我们考虑了当源MDP和目标MDP的转移核具有Tucker秩 $(S , d, A )$，$(S , S , d)$，$(d, S, A )$ 或 $(d , d , d )$ 时，如何转移潜在的低秩表示。在每种情况下，我们引入了可迁移性系数 $α$ 来衡量表示迁移的难度。我们的算法在每个源MDP中学习潜在表示，然后利用线性结构来消除目标MDP后悔界对 $S, A $ 或 $S A$ 的依赖。我们通过信息理论的下界补充了我们的正面结果，这些下界表明我们的算法（除了($d, d, d$)设置外）在 $α$ 方面是极小极大最优的。

[No.113] Effective Rank Analysis and Regularization for Enhanced 3D Gaussian Splatting
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 有效的秩分析和正则化以增强3D高斯点绘制
类别: machine vision
作者: Junha Hyung;Susung Hong;Sungwon Hwang;Jaeseong Lee;Jaegul Choo;Jin-Hwa Kim;
机构: Korea Advanced Institute of Science and Technology;Korea University;NAVER Corporation;

英摘: 3D reconstruction from multi-view images is one of the fundamental challenges in computer vision and graphics. Recently, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a promising technique capable of real-time rendering with high-quality 3D reconstruction. This method utilizes 3D Gaussian representation and tile-based splatting techniques, bypassing the expensive neural field querying. Despite its potential, 3DGS encounters challenges such as needle-like artifacts, suboptimal geometries, and inaccurate normals caused by the Gaussians converging into anisotropic shapes with one dominant variance. We propose using the effective rank analysis to examine the shape statistics of 3D Gaussian primitives, and identify the Gaussians indeed converge into needle-like shapes with the effective rank 1. To address this, we introduce the effective rank as a regularization, which constrains the structure of the Gaussians. Our new regularization method enhances normal and geometry reconstruction while reducing needle-like artifacts. The approach can be integrated as an add-on module to other 3DGS variants, improving their quality without compromising visual fidelity. The project page is available at https://junhahyung.github.io/erankgs.github.io.
中摘: 从多视角图像进行3D重建是计算机视觉和图形学中的基本挑战之一。最近，3D高斯喷涂（3DGS）作为一种有前景的技术崭露头角，它能够在实时渲染的同时实现高质量的3D重建。该方法利用了3D高斯表示和基于瓦片的喷涂技术，避免了昂贵的神经场查询。尽管潜力巨大，3DGS仍面临诸如针状伪影、次优几何形状以及因高斯分布收敛为具有一个主导方差的各向异性形状而导致的不准确法线等挑战。我们建议使用有效秩分析来检查3D高斯基元的形状统计，并识别出高斯分布确实会收敛成有效秩为1的针状形状。为了解决这一问题，我们引入有效秩作为正则化手段，以约束高斯分布的结构。我们的新正则化方法在减少针状伪影的同时提升了法线和几何形状的重建质量。这种方法可以作为附加模块整合到其他3DGS变体中，在不牺牲视觉保真度的前提下提升它们的质量。项目页面可在https://junhahyung.github.io/erankgs.github.io上访问。
