[No.114] Beating Adversarial Low-Rank MDPs with Unknown Transition and Bandit Feedback
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 未知转移概率和带宽反馈情况下击败 adversarial 低秩马尔可夫决策过程
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Haolin Liu;Zakaria Mhammedi;Chen-Yu Wei;Julian Zimmert;
机构: University of Virginia;Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/96516
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.06739
英摘: We consider regret minimization in low-rank MDPs with fixed transition and adversarial losses. Previous work has investigated this problem under either full-information loss feedback with unknown transitions (Zhao et al., 2024), or bandit loss feedback with known transition (Foster et al., 2022). First, we improve the $poly(d, A, H)T^{5/6}$ regret bound of Zhao et al. (2024) to $poly(d, A, H)T^{2/3}$ for the full-information unknown transition setting, where d is the rank of the transitions, A is the number of actions, H is the horizon length, and T is the number of episodes. Next, we initiate the study on the setting with bandit loss feedback and unknown transitions. Assuming that the loss has a linear structure, we propose both model based and model free algorithms achieving $poly(d, A, H)T^{2/3}$ regret, though they are computationally inefficient. We also propose oracle-efficient model-free algorithms with $poly(d, A, H)T^{4/5}$ regret. We show that the linear structure is necessary for the bandit case without structure on the reward function, the regret has to scale polynomially with the number of states. This is contrary to the full-information case (Zhao et al., 2024), where the regret can be independent of the number of states even for unstructured reward function.
中摘: 我们考虑在具有固定转移和对抗性损失的低秩马尔可夫决策过程(MDPs)中的后悔最小化问题。之前的研究要么是在未知转移下的全信息损失反馈环境中探讨该问题（如赵等人的研究，2024），要么是在已知转移下的强盗损失反馈环境中研究它（如福斯特等人的工作，2022）。首先，我们将赵等人（2024）在全信息未知转移设置下的$poly(d, A, H)T^{5/6}$的后悔界改进为$poly(d, A, H)T^{2/3}$，其中d是转移矩阵的秩，A是动作的数量，H是时间范围长度，而T是回合数。接下来，我们开始研究在强盗损失反馈和未知转移设置下的情况。假设损失具有线性结构，我们提出了基于模型和无模型算法，它们能达到$poly(d, A, H)T^{2/3}$的后悔界，尽管这些算法计算上效率不高。此外，我们还提出了一种计算高效、无模型的算法，其后悔界为$poly(d, A, H)T^{4/5}$。我们证明了对于没有奖励函数结构的强盗情况而言，线性结构是必要的；否则，后悔值必须随状态数量呈多项式增长。这与全信息情况（赵等人的研究，2024）形成对比，在全信息情况下，即使奖励函数没有特定结构，后悔值也可以不依赖于状态数量。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.115] Weight decay induces low-rank attention layers
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 权重衰减诱导低秩注意力层
类别: optimization for deep networks
作者: Seijin Kobayashi;Yassir Akram;Johannes von Oswald;
机构: ETH Zurich;Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/93637
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.23819
英摘: The effect of regularizers such as weight decay when training deep neural networks is not well understood. We study the influence of weight decay as well as $L2$-regularization when training neural network models in which parameter matrices interact multiplicatively. This combination is of particular interest as this parametrization is common in attention layers, the workhorse of transformers. Here, key-query, as well as value-projection parameter matrices, are multiplied directly with each other: $W_K^TW_Q$ and $PW_V$. We extend previous results and show on one hand that any local minimum of a $L2$-regularized loss of the form $L(AB^\top) + λ(\|A\|^2 + \|B\|^2)$ coincides with a minimum of the nuclear norm-regularized loss $L(AB^\top) + λ\|AB^\top\|_*$, and on the other hand that the 2 losses become identical exponentially quickly during training. We thus complement existing works linking $L2$-regularization with low-rank regularization, and in particular, explain why such regularization on the matrix product affects early stages of training. Based on these theoretical insights, we verify empirically that the key-query and value-projection matrix products $W_K^TW_Q, PW_V$ within attention layers, when optimized with weight decay, as usually done in vision tasks and language modelling, indeed induce a significant reduction in the rank of $W_K^TW_Q$ and $PW_V$, even in fully online training. We find that, in accordance with existing work, inducing low rank in attention matrix products can damage language model performance, and observe advantages when decoupling weight decay in attention layers from the rest of the parameters.
中摘: 在训练深度神经网络时，诸如权重衰减等正则化器的作用尚未得到充分理解。我们研究了在训练参数矩阵以乘法方式交互的神经网络模型时，权重衰减以及$L2$正则化的影响。这种组合尤其引人关注，因为这种参数化在注意力层中很常见，而注意力层是变压器（transformers）的核心组成部分。在这里，键查询（key-query）以及值投影（value-projection）参数矩阵直接相乘：$W_K^TW_Q$和$PW_V$。我们扩展了先前的结果，一方面表明任何形式为$L(AB^\top) + λ(\|A\|^2 + \|B\|^2)$的$L2$正则化损失的局部最小值与核范数正则化损失$L(AB^\top) + λ\|AB^\top\|_*$的最小值相吻合；另一方面，这两种损失在训练过程中迅速呈指数级地变得相同。因此，我们补充了现有将$L2$正则化与低秩正则化联系起来的工作，并特别解释了为何对矩阵乘积的这种正则化会影响训练的早期阶段。基于这些理论见解，我们实证验证了在注意力层内的键查询和值投影矩阵乘积$W_K^TW_Q, PW_V$，当使用权重衰减进行优化时，正如视觉任务和语言建模中通常所做的那样，确实导致$W_K^TW_Q$和$PW_V$的秩显著降低，即使是在完全在线训练的情况下也是如此。我们发现，与现有工作一致的是，在注意力矩阵乘积中诱导低秩可能会损害语言模型性能，并观察到在注意力层中将权重衰减与其余参数分离时的优势。

[No.117] FouRA: Fourier Low-Rank Adaptation
来源: NeurIPS 2024

类别: generative models

英摘: While Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has proven beneficial for efficiently fine-tuning large models, LoRA fine-tuned text-to-image diffusion models lack diversity in the generated images, as the model tends to copy data from the observed training samples. This effect becomes more pronounced at higher values of adapter strength and for adapters with higher ranks which are fine-tuned on smaller datasets. To address these challenges, we present FouRA, a novel low-rank method that learns projections in the Fourier domain along with learning a flexible input-dependent adapter rank selection strategy. [No.118] CoMERA: Computing- and Memory-Efficient Training via Rank-Adaptive Tensor Optimization
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: CoMERA：通过秩适应张量优化实现的计算和内存高效训练
类别: optimization for deep networks
作者: Zi Yang;Ziyue Liu;Samridhi Choudhary;Xinfeng Xie;Cao Gao;Siegfried Kunzmann;Zheng Zhang;

英摘: Training large AI models such as LLMs and DLRMs costs massive GPUs and computing time. The high training cost has become only affordable to big tech companies, meanwhile also causing increasing concerns about the environmental impact. This paper presents CoMERA, a Computing- and Memory-Efficient training method via Rank-Adaptive tensor optimization. CoMERA achieves rank-adaptive tensor-compressed (pre)-training via a multi-objective optimization formulation and improves the training to provide both a high compression ratio and excellent accuracy in the training process. [No.119] Conformal Prediction for Class-wise Coverage via Augmented Label Rank Calibration
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 类别覆盖率增强标签秩校准的保真预测
类别: safety in machine learning
作者: Yuanjie Shi;SUBHANKAR GHOSH;Taha Belkhouja;Jana Doppa;Yan Yan;
机构: Washington State University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/95055
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.06818
英摘: Conformal prediction (CP) is an emerging uncertainty quantification framework that allows us to construct a prediction set to cover the true label with a pre-specified marginal or conditional probability. Although the valid coverage guarantee has been extensively studied for classification problems, CP often produces large prediction sets which may not be practically useful. This issue is exacerbated for the setting of class-conditional coverage on imbalanced classification tasks with many and/or imbalanced classes. This paper proposes the Rank Calibrated Class-conditional CP (RC3P) algorithm to reduce the prediction set sizes to achieve class-conditional coverage, where the valid coverage holds for each class. In contrast to the standard class-conditional CP (CCP) method that uniformly thresholds the class-wise conformity score for each class, the augmented label rank calibration step allows RC3P to selectively iterate this class-wise thresholding subroutine only for a subset of classes whose class-wise top-k error is small. We prove that agnostic to the classifier and data distribution, RC3P achieves class-wise coverage. We also show that RC3P reduces the size of prediction sets compared to the CCP method. Comprehensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that RC3P achieves class-wise coverage and 26.25% reduction in prediction set sizes on average.
中摘: 一致性预测（CP）是一种新兴的不确定性量化框架，它允许我们构建一个预测集，以预设的边缘或条件概率覆盖真实标签。尽管对分类问题的有效覆盖率保证已经得到了广泛研究，但CP通常会产生较大的预测集，这可能在实际应用中并不实用。对于不平衡分类任务中的许多和/或类别不平衡的情况，在类条件覆盖率设置下，这一问题变得更加严重。本文提出了一种名为“排名校准类条件CP”（RC3P）的算法，旨在缩小预测集的大小，以实现类条件覆盖率，其中有效覆盖率对每个类都成立。与标准的类条件CP（CCP）方法不同，后者对每个类均匀地阈值化类别的符合性得分，RC3P通过增强的标签排名校准步骤，有选择地仅对那些类内前k错误较小的子集类迭代此类别的阈值化子程序。我们证明了无论分类器和数据分布如何，RC3P都能实现类级别的覆盖率。我们还展示了RC3P相比CCP方法能减少预测集的大小。在多个现实世界数据集上的全面实验表明，RC3P实现了类级别的覆盖率，并平均减少了26.25%的预测集大小。

[No.120] SeTAR: Out-of-Distribution Detection with Selective Low-Rank Approximation
来源: NeurIPS 2024

英摘: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is crucial for the safe deployment of neural networks. [No.121] Efficient LLM Scheduling by Learning to Rank
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 高效的LLM调度通过学习排序实现
类别: infrastructure
作者: Yichao Fu;Siqi Zhu;Runlong Su;Aurick Qiao;Ion Stoica;Hao Zhang;

英摘: In Large Language Model (LLM) inference, the output length of an LLM request is typically regarded as not known a priori. Consequently, most LLM serving systems employ a simple First-come-first-serve (FCFS) scheduling strategy, leading to Head-Of-Line (HOL) blocking and reduced throughput and service quality. In this paper, we reexamine this assumption -- we show that, although predicting the exact generation length of each request is infeasible, it is possible to predict the relative ranks of output lengths in a batch of requests, using learning to rank. The ranking information offers valuable guidance for scheduling requests. Building on this insight, we develop a novel scheduler for LLM inference and serving that can approximate the shortest-job-first (SJF) schedule better than existing approaches. We integrate this scheduler with the state-of-the-art LLM serving system and show significant performance improvement in several important applications: 2.8x lower latency in chatbot serving and 6.5x higher throughput in synthetic data generation. 中摘: 在大型语言模型（LLM）推理中，LLM请求的输出长度通常被视为无法预先确定。因此，大多数LLM服务系统采用简单的先来先服务（FCFS）调度策略，这会导致队列首部阻塞（HOL），并降低吞吐量和服务质量。在这篇论文中，我们重新审视了这一假设——我们表明，虽然预测每个请求的确切生成长度是不可行的，但可以使用学习排序方法预测一批请求中输出长度的相对排名。排名信息为调度请求提供了宝贵的指导。基于这一见解，我们开发了一种新型的LLM推理和服务调度器，它能比现有方法更好地逼近最短作业优先（SJF）调度。我们将此调度器与最先进的LLM服务系统集成，并在几个重要应用中展示了显著的性能提升：聊天机器人服务中的延迟降低了2.8倍，合成数据生成中的吞吐量提高了6.5倍。我们的代码可在https://github.com/hao-ai-lab/vllm-ltr.git获取。

[No.122] VeLoRA: Memory Efficient Training using Rank-1 Sub-Token Projections
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: VeLoRA：使用秩1子令牌投影的高效内存训练
类别: optimization for deep networks
作者: Roy Miles;Pradyumna Reddy;Ismail Elezi;Jiankang Deng;
机构: Huawei;Imperial College London;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17991
英摘: Large language models (LLMs) have recently emerged as powerful tools for tackling many language-processing tasks. Despite their success, training and fine-tuning these models is still far too computationally and memory intensive. In this paper, we identify and characterise the important components needed for effective model convergence using gradient descent. In doing so we find that the intermediate activations used to implement backpropagation can be excessively compressed without incurring any degradation in performance. This result leads us to a cheap and memory-efficient algorithm for both fine-tuning and pre-training LLMs. The proposed algorithm simply divides the tokens up into smaller sub-tokens before projecting them onto a fixed 1-dimensional subspace during the forward pass. [No.123] RankUp: Boosting Semi-Supervised Regression with an Auxiliary Ranking Classifier
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: RankUp：利用辅助排名分类器提升半监督回归性能
类别: other
作者: Pin-Yen Huang;Szu-Wei Fu;Yu Tsao;
机构: Microsoft;Academia Sinica;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/94365

英摘: State-of-the-art (SOTA) semi-supervised learning techniques, such as FixMatch and it's variants, have demonstrated impressive performance in classification tasks. In this paper, we present RankUp, a simple yet effective approach that adapts existing semi-supervised classification techniques to enhance the performance of regression tasks. This auxiliary ranking classifier outputs a classification result, thus enabling integration with existing semi-supervised classification methods. Moreover, we introduce regression distribution alignment (RDA), a complementary technique that further enhances RankUp's performance by refining pseudo-labels through distribution alignment. Despite its simplicity, RankUp, with or without RDA, achieves SOTA results in across a range of regression benchmarks, including computer vision, audio, and natural language processing tasks. Our code and log data are open-sourced at https://github.com/pm25/semi-supervised-regression.
中摘: 最先进的半监督学习技术，如FixMatch及其变体，在分类任务中展现出了令人印象深刻的表现。然而，这些方法并不能直接应用于回归任务。在本文中，我们提出了RankUp，一种简单而有效的方法，它将现有的半监督分类技术适应并增强回归任务的性能。RankUp通过将原始的回归任务转化为排名问题，并与原始的回归目标同时进行训练来实现这一点。这个辅助的排名分类器输出分类结果，从而能够与现有的半监督分类方法融合。此外，我们引入了回归分布对齐（RDA），这是一种互补的技术，通过分布对齐来细化伪标签，进一步提升了RankUp的性能。尽管RankUp方法简单，无论是否使用RDA，它都在一系列回归基准测试中实现了最先进水平的结果，涵盖计算机视觉、音频和自然语言处理任务。我们的代码和日志数据已在https://github.com/pm25/semi-supervised-regression开源。

[No.125] LoQT: Low-Rank Adapters for Quantized Pretraining
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: LoQT：量化预训练的低秩适配器
类别: optimization for deep networks
作者: Sebastian Bugge Loeschcke;Mads Toftrup;Michael Kastoryano;Serge Belongie;Vésteinn Snæbjarnarson;
机构: University of Copenhagen;Aarhus University;IT University of Copenhagen;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/95286
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.16528
英摘: Despite advances using low-rank adapters and quantization, pretraining of large models on consumer hardware has not been possible without model sharding, offloading during training, or per-layer gradient updates. To address these limitations, we propose Low-Rank Adapters for Quantized Training (LoQT), a method for efficiently training quantized models. LoQT uses gradient-based tensor factorization to initialize low-rank trainable weight matrices that are periodically merged into quantized full-rank weight matrices. Our approach is suitable for both pretraining and fine-tuning models. We demonstrate this for language modeling and downstream task adaptation, finding that LoQT enables efficient training of models up to 7B parameters on a 24GB GPU. We also demonstrate the feasibility of training a 13B model using per-layer gradient updates on the same hardware.
中摘: 尽管在使用低秩适配器和量化技术方面取得了进展，但如果没有模型分片、训练期间的卸载或逐层梯度更新，大型模型在消费级硬件上的预训练仍然是不可能的。为了解决这些限制，我们提出了低秩适配器用于量化训练（LoQT），这是一种有效训练量化模型的方法。LoQT利用基于梯度的张量分解来初始化低秩可训练权重矩阵，这些矩阵会周期性地合并到量化全秩权重矩阵中。我们的方法适用于模型的预训练和微调。我们通过语言建模和下游任务适应展示了这一点，发现LoQT能够在24GB显卡上高效训练参数量高达70亿的模型。我们还展示了在相同硬件上使用逐层梯度更新训练130亿参数模型的可行性。

[No.126] Differentially Private Graph Diffusion with Applications in Personalized PageRanks
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 差分隐私图扩散及其在个性化PageRanks中的应用
类别: other
作者: Rongzhe Wei;Eli Chien;Pan Li;
机构: Georgia Institute of Technology;Purdue University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/94531
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.00077
英摘: Graph diffusion, which iteratively propagates real-valued substances among the graph, is used in numerous graph/network-involved applications. However, releasing diffusion vectors may reveal sensitive linking information in the data such as transaction information in financial network data. However, protecting the privacy of graph data is challenging due to its interconnected nature. This work proposes a novel graph diffusion framework with edge-level differential privacy guarantees by using noisy diffusion iterates. The algorithm injects Laplace noise per diffusion iteration and adopts a degree-based thresholding function to mitigate the high sensitivity induced by low-degree nodes. Our privacy loss analysis is based on Privacy Amplification by Iteration (PABI), which to our best knowledge, is the first effort that analyzes PABI with Laplace noise and provides relevant applications. We also introduce a novel Infinity-Wasserstein distance tracking method, which tightens the analysis of privacy leakage and makes PABI more applicable in practice. We evaluate this framework by applying it to Personalized Pagerank computation for ranking tasks. Experiments on real-world network data demonstrate the superiority of our method under stringent privacy conditions.
中摘: 图扩散，它通过迭代在图中传播实数值物质，在众多涉及图/网络的应用中被使用。然而，发布扩散向量可能会泄露数据中的敏感链接信息，例如金融网络数据中的交易信息。但是，由于图数据的互联特性，保护其隐私具有挑战性。本工作提出了一种新颖的图扩散框架，通过使用带有噪声的扩散迭代，提供了边级别的差分隐私保证。该算法在每次扩散迭代中注入拉普拉斯噪声，并采用基于度数的阈值函数来缓解低度节点引起的高敏感性。我们的隐私损失分析基于迭代隐私放大（PABI），据我们所知，这是首次尝试分析带有拉普拉斯噪声的PABI并提供相关应用。我们还引入了一种新的无穷 Wasserstein 距离跟踪方法，这收紧了隐私泄露的分析，并使PABI在实践中更具适用性。我们通过将其应用于个性化PageRank计算以完成排名任务来评估这一框架。在真实世界网络数据上的实验表明，在严格的隐私条件下，我们的方法具有优越性。

[No.127] Compressing Large Language Models using Low Rank and Low Precision Decomposition
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 使用低秩和低精度分解压缩大型语言模型
类别: deep learning architectures
作者: Rajarshi Saha;Naomi Sagan;Varun Srivastava;Andrea Goldsmith;Mert Pilanci;
机构: Stanford University;Princeton University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/93805
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.18886
英摘: The prohibitive sizes of Large Language Models (LLMs) today make it difficult to deploy them on memory-constrained edge devices. This work introduces $\rm CALDERA$ -- a new post-training LLM compression algorithm that harnesses the inherent low-rank structure of a weight matrix $\mathbf{W}$ by approximating it via a low-rank, low-precision decomposition as $\mathbf{W} \approx \mathbf{Q} + \mathbf{L}\mathbf{R}$. Here, $\mathbf{L}$ and $\mathbf{R}$ are low rank factors, and the entries of $\mathbf{Q}$, $\mathbf{L}$ and $\mathbf{R}$ are quantized. The model is compressed by substituting each layer with its $\mathbf{Q} + \mathbf{L}\mathbf{R}$ decomposition, and the zero-shot performance of the compressed model is evaluated. Additionally, $\mathbf{L}$ and $\mathbf{R}$ are readily amenable to low-rank adaptation, consequently enhancing the zero-shot performance. $\rm CALDERA$ obtains this decomposition by formulating it as an optimization problem $\min_{\mathbf{Q},\mathbf{L},\mathbf{R}}\lVert(\mathbf{Q} + \mathbf{L}\mathbf{R} - \mathbf{W})\mathbf{X}^\top\rVert_{\rm F}^2$, where $\mathbf{X}$ is the calibration data, and $\mathbf{Q}, \mathbf{L}, \mathbf{R}$ are constrained to be representable using low-precision formats. Theoretical upper bounds on the approximation error of $\rm CALDERA$ are established using a rank-constrained regression framework, and the tradeoff between compression ratio and model performance is studied by analyzing the impact of target rank and quantization bit budget. Results illustrate that compressing LlaMa-$2$ $7$B/$13B$/$70$B and LlaMa-$3$ $8$B models using $\rm CALDERA$ outperforms existing post-training LLM compression techniques in the regime of less than $2.5$ bits per parameter. The implementation is available at: https://github.com/pilancilab/caldera.
中摘: 大型语言模型(LLM)如今庞大的尺寸使得在内存受限的边缘设备上部署它们变得困难。本研究引入了$\rm CALDERA$——一种新的后训练LLM压缩算法，通过近似权重矩阵$\mathbf{W}$为低秩、低精度分解的形式$\mathbf{W} \approx \mathbf{Q} + \mathbf{L}\mathbf{R}$来利用其固有的低秩结构。其中，$\mathbf{L}$和$\mathbf{R}$是低秩因子，而$\mathbf{Q}$、$\mathbf{L}$和$\mathbf{R}$的条目经过量化处理。模型通过用其$\mathbf{Q} + \mathbf{L}\mathbf{R}$分解替换每一层来进行压缩，并评估压缩模型的零样本性能。此外，$\mathbf{L}$和$\mathbf{R}$易于进行低秩适应，从而显著提升零样本性能。$\rm CALDERA$通过将其作为优化问题$\min_{\mathbf{Q},\mathbf{L},\mathbf{R}}\lVert(\mathbf{Q} + \mathbf{L}\mathbf{R} - \mathbf{W})\mathbf{X}^\top\rVert_{\rm F}^2$来获得这种分解，其中$\mathbf{X}$是校准数据，而$\mathbf{Q}, \mathbf{L}, \mathbf{R}$受到使用低精度格式表示的约束。我们使用一个秩约束回归框架建立了$\rm CALDERA$的近似误差理论上限，并通过分析目标秩和量化比特预算的影响来研究压缩比率与模型性能之间的权衡。结果显示，在每个参数少于$2.5$比特的条件下，使用$\rm CALDERA$对LlaMa-$2$ $7$B/$13B$/$70$B以及LlaMa-$3$ $8$B模型进行压缩优于现有的后训练LLM压缩技术。该实现可在以下网址获取：https://github.com/pilancilab/caldera。

[No.128] SLTrain: a sparse plus low rank approach for parameter and memory efficient pretraining
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: SLTrain：一种稀疏加低秩的方法，用于参数和内存高效预训练
类别: natural language processing
作者: Andi Han;Jiaxiang Li;Wei Huang;Mingyi Hong;Akiko Takeda;Pratik Jawanpuria;Bamdev Mishra;
机构: RIKEN;University of Minnesota;Microsoft;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/95512
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.02214
英摘: Large language models (LLMs) have shown impressive capabilities across various tasks. However, training LLMs from scratch requires significant computational power and extensive memory capacity. Recent studies have explored low-rank structures on weights for efficient fine-tuning in terms of parameters and memory, either through low-rank adaptation or factorization. While effective for fine-tuning, low-rank structures are generally less suitable for pretraining because they restrict parameters to a low-dimensional subspace. In this work, we propose to parameterize the weights as a sum of low-rank and sparse matrices for pretraining, which we call SLTrain. The low-rank component is learned via matrix factorization, while for the sparse component, we employ a simple strategy of uniformly selecting the sparsity support at random and learning only the non-zero entries with the fixed support. While being simple, the random fixed-support sparse learning strategy significantly enhances pretraining when combined with low-rank learning. Our results show that SLTrain adds minimal extra parameters and memory costs compared to pretraining with low-rank parameterization, yet achieves substantially better performance, which is comparable to full-rank training. Remarkably, when combined with quantization and per-layer updates, SLTrain can reduce memory requirements by up to 73% when pretraining the LLaMA 7B model.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLM）在各种任务上展现出了令人印象深刻的能力。然而，从零开始训练LLM需要巨大的计算能力和大量的内存容量。最近的研究探索了权重上的低秩结构，以实现参数和内存高效微调，无论是通过低秩适应还是分解。虽然对微调有效，但低秩结构通常不太适合预训练，因为它们将参数限制在低维子空间中。在这项工作中，我们提出将权重表示为低秩矩阵和稀疏矩阵之和进行预训练，我们称之为SLTrain。低秩部分通过矩阵分解学习，而对于稀疏部分，我们采用一个简单的策略，即随机均匀选择稀疏支持，并仅学习固定支持下的非零条目。尽管简单，当与低秩学习结合时，这种随机固定支持的稀疏学习策略显著增强了预训练效果。我们的结果显示，与仅使用低秩参数化预训练相比，SLTrain添加的额外参数和内存成本极小，却能取得明显更好的性能，接近全秩训练的效果。值得注意的是，当与量化和逐层更新相结合时，SLTrain在预训练LLaMA 7B模型时，可以将内存需求降低高达73%。

[No.130] Entrywise error bounds for low-rank approximations of kernel matrices
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 核矩阵的低秩逼近的逐元素误差界
类别: learning theory
作者: Alexander Modell;
机构: Imperial College London;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/92940
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.14494
英摘: In this paper, we derive entrywise error bounds for low-rank approximations of kernel matrices obtained using the truncated eigen-decomposition (or singular value decomposition). While this approximation is well-known to be optimal with respect to the spectral and Frobenius norm error, little is known about the statistical behaviour of individual entries. Our error bounds fill this gap. A key technical innovation is a delocalisation result for the eigenvectors of the kernel matrix corresponding to small eigenvalues, which takes inspiration from the field of Random Matrix Theory. Finally, we validate our theory with an empirical study of a collection of synthetic and real-world datasets.
中摘: 在本文中，我们推导了使用截断的特征值分解（或奇异值分解）获得的核矩阵的低秩近似中每个元素的误差界限。尽管众所周知，这种近似在谱范数和弗罗贝尼乌斯范数误差方面是最优的，但我们对单个条目的统计行为知之甚少。我们的误差界限填补了这一空白。一个关键的技术创新是对应该小特征值的核矩阵的特征向量的非局域化结果，这从随机矩阵理论领域汲取灵感。最后，我们通过对一系列合成和真实数据集的实证研究来验证我们的理论。

[No.131] PageRank Bandits for Link Prediction
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: PageRank 神经网络用于链接预测
类别: machine learning for social sciences
作者: Yikun Ban;Jiaru Zou;Zihao Li;Yunzhe Qi;Dongqi Fu;Jian Kang;Hanghang Tong;Jingrui He;
机构: University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;University of Rochester;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/94897
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.01410
英摘: Link prediction is a critical problem in graph learning with broad applications such as recommender systems and knowledge graph completion. Numerous research efforts have been directed at solving this problem, including approaches based on similarity metrics and Graph Neural Networks (GNN). However, most existing solutions are still rooted in conventional supervised learning, which makes it challenging to adapt over time to changing customer interests and to address the inherent dilemma of exploitation versus exploration in link prediction. To tackle these challenges, this paper reformulates link prediction as a sequential decision-making process, where each link prediction interaction occurs sequentially. We propose a novel fusion algorithm, PRB (PageRank Bandits), which is the first to combine contextual bandits with PageRank for collaborative exploitation and exploration. We also introduce a new reward formulation and provide a theoretical performance guarantee for PRB. Finally, we extensively evaluate PRB in both online and offline settings, comparing it with bandit-based and graph-based methods. The empirical success of PRB demonstrates the value of the proposed fusion approach. Our code is released at https://github.com/jiaruzouu/PRB.
中摘: 链接预测是在图学习中一个关键问题，具有广泛的应用，如推荐系统和知识图谱补全。大量的研究工作致力于解决这一问题，包括基于相似性度量的方法和图神经网络（GNN）。然而，大多数现有解决方案仍然基于传统的监督学习，这使得它们难以适应随时间变化的客户兴趣，并且难以解决链接预测中固有的利用与探索之间的困境。为了解决这些挑战，本文将链接预测重新定义为一个序列决策过程，其中每次链接预测交互都是依次发生的。我们提出了一种新颖的融合算法，即PRB（PageRank Bandits），这是首次将上下文多臂赌博机与PageRank结合用于协同利用和探索。我们还引入了一种新的奖励公式，并为PRB提供了理论性能保证。最后，我们在在线和离线环境中对PRB进行了广泛的评估，将其与基于多臂赌博机和图的方法进行比较。PRB的经验成功证明了所提出的融合方法的价值。我们的代码已在https://github.com/jiaruzouu/PRB发布。

[No.132] Diff-eRank: A Novel Rank-Based Metric for Evaluating Large Language Models
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: Diff-eRank：一种新型基于排名的评估指标，用于评价大型语言模型

作者: Lai Wei;Zhiquan Tan;Chenghai Li;Jindong Wang;Weiran Huang;

链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/93654
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.17139
英摘: Large Language Models (LLMs) have transformed natural language processing and extended their powerful capabilities to multi-modal domains. As LLMs continue to advance, it is crucial to develop diverse and appropriate metrics for their evaluation. In this paper, we introduce a novel rank-based metric, Diff-eRank, grounded in information theory and geometry principles. Diff-eRank assesses LLMs by analyzing their hidden representations, providing a quantitative measure of how efficiently they eliminate redundant information during training. We demonstrate the applicability of Diff-eRank in both single-modal (e.g., language) and multi-modal settings. For language models, our results show that Diff-eRank increases with model size and correlates well with conventional metrics such as loss and accuracy. 中摘: 大型语言模型（LLM）已经革新了自然语言处理领域，并将其强大的能力扩展到了多模态领域。随着LLM的持续发展，开发多样且适当的评估指标变得至关重要。在这篇论文中，我们引入了一种基于信息理论和几何原理的新型排名指标，Diff-eRank。Diff-eRank通过分析LLM的隐藏表示来评估它们，在训练过程中对消除冗余信息的效率提供定量测量。我们在单模态（例如，语言）和多模态设置中展示了Diff-eRank的应用性。对于语言模型，我们的结果显示Diff-eRank随着模型大小的增加而增加，并与诸如损失和准确率等传统指标有良好的相关性。在多模态背景下，我们提出了一种基于eRank的对齐评估方法，并验证了当前的多模态LLM根据我们的方法展现出强劲的对齐性能。我们的代码可在https://github.com/waltonfuture/Diff-eRank公开获取。

[No.133] Sparse High Rank Adapters
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 稀疏高秩适配器
类别: generative models
作者: Kartikeya Bhardwaj;Nilesh Prasad Pandey;Sweta Priyadarshi;Viswanath Ganapathy;Shreya Kadambi;Rafael Esteves;Shubhankar Borse;Paul Whatmough;Risheek Garrepalli;Mart Van Baalen;Harris Teague;Markus Nagel;
机构: Qualcomm;Qualcomm Incorporated;Harvard University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/96503
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.13175
英摘: Low Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has gained massive attention in the recent generative AI research. One of the main advantages of LoRA is its ability to be fused with pretrained models, adding no overhead during inference. However, from a mobile deployment standpoint, we can either avoid inference overhead in the fused mode but lose the ability to switch adapters rapidly, or suffer significant (up to 30% higher) inference latency while enabling rapid switching in the unfused mode. LoRA also exhibits concept-loss when multiple adapters are used concurrently. In this paper, we propose Sparse High Rank Adapters (SHiRA), a new paradigm which incurs no inference overhead, enables rapid switching, and significantly reduces concept-loss. Specifically, SHiRA can be trained by directly tuning only 1-2% of the base model weights while leaving others unchanged. This results in a highly sparse adapter which can be switched directly in the fused mode. We further provide theoretical and empirical insights on how high sparsity in SHiRA can aid multi-adapter fusion by reducing concept loss. Our extensive experiments on LVMs and LLMs demonstrate that finetuning only a small fraction of the parameters in the base model significantly outperforms LoRA while enabling both rapid switching and multi-adapter fusion. Finally, we provide a latency- and memory-efficient SHiRA implementation based on Parameter-Efficient Finetuning (PEFT) Library which trains at nearly the same speed as LoRA while consuming up to 16% lower peak GPU memory, thus making SHiRA easy to adopt for practical use cases. To demonstrate rapid switching benefits during inference, we show that loading SHiRA on a base model can be 5x-16x faster than LoRA fusion on a CPU.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）在近期的生成式AI研究中引起了广泛关注。LoRA的主要优势之一在于能够与预训练模型融合，在推理过程中不增加额外负担。然而，从移动部署的角度来看，我们可以在融合模式下避免推理开销，但会失去快速切换适配器的能力；或者在非融合模式下允许快速切换，但会遭受显著增加（高达30%）的推理延迟。同时，当多个适配器并行使用时，LoRA会出现概念丢失的问题。在这篇论文中，我们提出了稀疏高秩适配器（SHiRA），这是一种新的范式，它不会产生推理开销，支持快速切换，并显著减少概念丢失。具体而言，SHiRA可以通过直接调整基础模型中仅1-2%的权重来训练，而其余权重保持不变。这导致了一个高度稀疏的适配器，可以直接在融合模式下进行切换。我们进一步提供了理论和实证见解，说明了SHiRA中的高稀疏性如何通过减少概念损失来辅助多适配器融合。我们在LVM和LLM上的广泛实验表明，仅对基础模型中的小部分参数进行微调，相比于LoRA有显著的性能提升，同时支持快速切换和多适配器融合。最后，我们提供了一个基于参数高效微调（PEFT）库的延迟和内存效率高的SHiRA实现，其训练速度几乎与LoRA相同，但峰值GPU内存消耗降低了多达16%，使得SHiRA易于实际应用。为了展示在推理期间快速切换的好处，我们展示了在CPU上加载SHiRA到基础模型的速度可以比LoRA融合快5至16倍。

[No.134] Prompt Tuning Strikes Back: Customizing Foundation Models with Low-Rank Prompt Adaptation
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 提示调优卷土重来：通过低秩提示适应定制基础模型
类别: generative models
作者: Abhinav Jain;Swarat Chaudhuri;Thomas Reps;Chris Jermaine;
机构: Rice University;University of Texas at Austin;University of Wisconsin-Madison;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/95065
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15282
英摘: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) has become the standard for customising Foundation Models (FMs) to user-specific downstream tasks. However, typical PEFT methods require storing multiple task-specific adapters, creating scalability issues as these adapters must be housed and run at the FM server. Traditional prompt tuning offers a potential solution by customising them through task-specific input prefixes, but it under-performs compared to other PEFT methods like LoRA. To address this gap, we propose Low-Rank Prompt Adaptation (LoPA), a prompt-tuning-based approach that performs on par with state-of-the-art PEFT methods and full fine-tuning while being more parameter-efficient and not requiring a server-based adapter. LoPA generates soft prompts by balancing between sharing task-specific information across instances and customization for each instance. It uses a low-rank decomposition of the soft-prompt component encoded for each instance to achieve parameter efficiency. We provide a comprehensive evaluation on multiple natural language understanding and code generation and understanding tasks across a wide range of foundation models with varying sizes.
中摘: 参数高效微调（PEFT）已成为将基础模型（FMs）针对用户特定下游任务进行定制的标准方法。然而，典型的PEFT方法需要存储多个任务特定的适配器，这在适配器必须在基础模型服务器上存储和运行时带来了可扩展性问题。传统的提示微调通过任务特定的输入前缀来定制模型，提供了一种潜在解决方案，但其性能相比其他PEFT方法如LoRA要差。为了解决这一差距，我们提出了低秩提示适应（LoPA），这是一种基于提示微调的方法，它在参数效率更高、无需基于服务器的适配器的情况下，与最先进的PEFT方法和全量微调相匹敌。LoPA通过在实例间共享任务特定信息与每个实例的定制化之间取得平衡来生成软提示。它利用对每个实例编码的软提示组件的低秩分解来实现参数效率。我们在一系列不同大小的基础模型上的自然语言理解和代码生成及理解任务上进行了全面评估。

LoPA是一种创新的提示微调技术，旨在解决PEFT方法中的可扩展性和性能问题。通过在实例级别的任务信息共享与个性化调整之间找到最佳平衡点，LoPA能够以更少的参数量达到与现有PEFT方法相同的微调效果，同时避免了在服务器端存储大量任务适配器的需求。这种低秩分解策略使得LoPA在处理大规模基础模型时，特别是在自然语言理解和代码相关任务上，展现出色的性能。我们的实验结果证明了LoPA的有效性和通用性，为PEFT领域提供了新的研究方向。

来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank

英摘: Deep neural networks, including Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and Visual Transformers (ViT), have achieved stunning success in medical image domain. [No.137] Model-free Low-Rank Reinforcement Learning via Leveraged Entry-wise Matrix Estimation
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 无模型低秩强化学习通过加权逐元素矩阵估计实现
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Stefan Stojanovic;Yassir Jedra;Alexandre Proutiere;
机构: KTH Royal Institute of Technology;Massachusetts Institute of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/95824
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.23434
英摘: We consider the problem of learning an $\varepsilon$-optimal policy in controlled dynamical systems with low-rank latent structure. For this problem, we present LoRa-PI (Low-Rank Policy Iteration), a model-free learning algorithm alternating between policy improvement and policy evaluation steps. In the latter, the algorithm estimates the low-rank matrix corresponding to the (state, action) value function of the current policy using the following two-phase procedure. The entries of the matrix are first sampled uniformly at random to estimate, via a spectral method, the leverage scores of its rows and columns. These scores are then used to extract a few important rows and columns whose entries are further sampled. The algorithm exploits these new samples to complete the matrix estimation using a CUR-like method. For this leveraged matrix estimation procedure, we establish entry-wise guarantees that remarkably, do not depend on the coherence of the matrix but only on its spikiness. These guarantees imply that LoRa-PI learns an $\varepsilon$-optimal policy using $\widetilde{O}({S+A\over \mathrm{poly}(1-γ)\varepsilon^2})$ samples where $S$ (resp. $A$) denotes the number of states (resp. actions) and $γ$ the discount factor. Our algorithm achieves this order-optimal (in $S$, $A$ and $\varepsilon$) sample complexity under milder conditions than those assumed in previously proposed approaches.
中摘: 我们考虑在具有低秩潜在结构的受控动力系统中学习ε-最优策略的问题。针对这一问题，我们提出了LoRa-PI（低秩策略迭代），一种无需模型的学习算法，交替进行策略改进和策略评估步骤。在策略评估阶段，算法采用以下两阶段过程来估计当前策略的状态动作价值函数对应的低秩矩阵。首先，随机均匀采样矩阵的条目，通过谱方法估算其行和列的杠杆分数。然后利用这些分数抽取几行和几列的重要条目进行进一步采样。算法利用这些新样本，采用类似CUR的方法完成矩阵估计。对于这种基于杠杆分数的矩阵估计过程，我们建立了条目级保证，值得注意的是，这些保证不依赖于矩阵的连贯性，而仅依赖于其尖峰性。这些保证意味着LoRa-PI使用~O((S+A)/(多项式(1-γ)ε^2))个样本就能学习到ε-最优策略，其中S（分别A）表示状态数（分别行动数），γ表示折扣因子。我们的算法在比之前提出的方法假设的条件更温和的情况下实现了关于S、A和ε的最优样本复杂度。

[No.138] Preference Learning Algorithms Do Not Learn Preference Rankings
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: rank
标题: 偏好学习算法并不学习偏好排名
类别: natural language processing
作者: Angelica Chen;Sadhika Malladi;Lily H Zhang;Xinyi Chen;Qiuyi Zhang;Rajesh Ranganath;Kyunghyun Cho;
机构: New York University;Princeton University;Google;Genentech;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/94675
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.19534
英摘: Preference learning algorithms (e.g., RLHF and DPO) are frequently used to steer LLMs to produce generations that are more preferred by humans, but our understanding of their inner workings is still limited. In this work, we study the conventional wisdom that preference learning trains models to assign higher likelihoods to more preferred outputs than less preferred outputs, measured via ranking accuracy. Surprisingly, we find that most state-of-the-art preference-tuned models achieve a ranking accuracy of less than 60% on common preference datasets. We furthermore derive the idealized ranking accuracy that a preference-tuned LLM would achieve if it optimized the DPO or RLHF objective perfectly. We demonstrate that existing models exhibit a significant alignment gap -- i.e., a gap between the observed and idealized ranking accuracies. We attribute this discrepancy to the DPO objective, which is empirically and theoretically ill-suited to fix even mild ranking errors in the reference model, and derive a simple and efficient formula for quantifying the difficulty of learning a given preference datapoint. Finally, we demonstrate that ranking accuracy strongly correlates with the empirically popular win rate metric when the model is close to the reference model used in the objective, shedding further light on the differences between on-policy (e.g., RLHF) and off-policy (e.g., DPO) preference learning algorithms.
中摘: 偏好学习算法（例如RLHF和DPO）经常被用来引导大型语言模型（LLMs）生成人类更偏好的内容，但我们对它们内部运作的理解仍然有限。在这项工作中，我们研究了普遍认为偏好学习训练模型为更受青睐的输出分配更高概率的观点，这一观点通过排名准确性来衡量。令人惊讶的是，我们发现大多数最先进的偏好调优模型在常见的偏好数据集上的排名准确率低于60%。此外，我们推导出如果完美优化DPO或RLHF目标，偏好调优的LLM理论上应达到的理想化排名准确率。我们证明现有模型存在显著的对齐差距——即观察到的与理想化的排名准确率之间的差距。我们将这种差异归因于DPO目标，它在实证和理论上都不适合修正参考模型中的轻微排名错误，并推导出一个简单且高效的方法来量化学习特定偏好数据点的难度。最后，我们展示了当模型接近用于目标的参考模型时，排名准确率与实证上流行的胜率指标之间有强烈的相关性，进一步揭示了在线策略（如RLHF）与离线策略（如DPO）偏好学习算法之间的区别。

[No.139] Bayesian Extensive-Rank Matrix Factorization with Rotational Invariant Priors
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 旋转不变先验的贝叶斯广泛秩矩阵分解
类别: 
作者: Farzad Pourkamali;Nicolas Macris;
机构: EPFL;Ecole Polytechnique Federale de Lausanne;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/71087
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.04592
英摘: We consider a statistical model for matrix factorization in a regime where the rank of the two hidden matrix factors grows linearly with their dimension and their product is corrupted by additive noise. Despite various approaches, statistical and algorithmic limits of such problems have remained elusive. We study a Bayesian setting with the assumptions that (a) one of the matrix factors is symmetric, (b) both factors as well as the additive noise have rotational invariant priors, (c) the priors are known to the statistician. We derive analytical formulas for Rotation Invariant Estimators to reconstruct the two matrix factors, and conjecture that these are optimal in the large-dimension limit, in the sense that they minimize the average mean-square-error. We provide numerical checks which confirm the optimality conjecture when confronted to Oracle Estimators which are optimal by definition, but involve the ground-truth. Our derivation relies on a combination of tools, namely random matrix theory transforms, spherical integral formulas, and the replica method from statistical mechanics.
中摘: 我们考虑了一种矩阵分解的统计模型，在这种模型中，两个隐藏矩阵因子的秩与其维度呈线性增长，并且它们的乘积受到加性噪声的破坏。尽管有各种方法，但这类问题的统计和算法极限一直难以捉摸。我们研究了一个贝叶斯设置，其中假设（a）其中一个矩阵因子是对称的，（b）两个因子以及加性噪声都具有旋转不变先验分布，（c）这些先验分布对统计学家是已知的。我们推导了旋转不变估计量的解析公式来重构两个矩阵因子，并猜想在大维度极限下，这些估计量是最优的，即它们最小化平均均方误差。我们提供了数值检验，当与先知估计量（按定义是最优的，但涉及真实值）对比时，证实了最优性的猜想。我们的推导依赖于一组工具的组合，包括随机矩阵理论变换、球面积分公式以及来自统计力学的副本方法。

[No.140] Rank-N-Contrast: Learning Continuous Representations for Regression
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: Rank-N-对比：学习回归的连续表示
类别: 
作者: Kaiwen Zha;Peng Cao;Jeany Son;Yuzhe Yang;Dina Katabi;
机构: Massachusetts Institute of Technology;Gwangju Institute of Science and Technology;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/71416
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2210.01189
英摘: Deep regression models typically learn in an end-to-end fashion without explicitly emphasizing a regression-aware representation. Consequently, the learned representations exhibit fragmentation and fail to capture the continuous nature of sample orders, inducing suboptimal results across a wide range of regression tasks. To fill the gap, we propose Rank-N-Contrast (RNC), a framework that learns continuous representations for regression by contrasting samples against each other based on their rankings in the target space. We demonstrate, theoretically and empirically, that RNC guarantees the desired order of learned representations in accordance with the target orders, enjoying not only better performance but also significantly improved robustness, efficiency, and generalization. Extensive experiments using five real-world regression datasets that span computer vision, human-computer interaction, and healthcare verify that RNC achieves state-of-the-art performance, highlighting its intriguing properties including better data efficiency, robustness to spurious targets and data corruptions, and generalization to distribution shifts. Code is available at: https://github.com/kaiwenzha/Rank-N-Contrast.
中摘: 深度回归模型通常以端到端的方式学习，没有明确强调回归意识的表示。因此，所学的表示显示出碎片化，并且未能捕捉样本顺序的连续性，导致在广泛的回归任务中产生次优的结果。为填补这一空白，我们提出了Rank-N-Contrast（RNC）框架，该框架通过根据目标空间中的排名对比样本来学习连续的表示用于回归。我们从理论上和经验上证明，RNC保证了所学表示的目标顺序，不仅性能更佳，而且显著提高了鲁棒性、效率和泛化能力。使用涵盖计算机视觉、人机交互和医疗保健领域的五个真实世界回归数据集的大量实验验证了RNC达到了最先进的性能，突出了其引人注目的特性，包括更好的数据效率、对错误目标和数据损坏的鲁棒性以及对分布偏移的泛化能力。代码可在以下网址获取：https://github.com/kaiwenzha/Rank-N-Contrast。

[No.141] Hypernetwork-based Meta-Learning for Low-Rank Physics-Informed Neural Networks
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 基于超网络的元学习方法在低秩物理知情神经网络中的应用
类别: 
作者: Woojin Cho;Kookjin Lee;Donsub Rim;Noseong Park;

英摘: In various engineering and applied science applications, repetitive numerical simulations of partial differential equations (PDEs) for varying input parameters are often required (e.g., aircraft shape optimization over many design parameters) and solvers are required to perform rapid execution. In this study, we suggest a path that potentially opens up a possibility for physics-informed neural networks (PINNs), emerging deep-learning-based solvers, to be considered as one such solver. 中摘: 在各种工程和应用科学领域中，经常需要对偏微分方程（PDEs）进行针对不同输入参数的重复数值模拟（例如，在多个设计参数下优化飞机形状），并且要求求解器能够快速执行。在这项研究中，我们提出了一种潜在路径，这可能为物理信息神经网络（PINNs）作为新兴的基于深度学习的求解器之一打开了可能性。尽管PINNs开创了深度学习与科学计算的恰当结合，但它们需要对神经网络进行耗时的重复训练，这并不适合多查询场景。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一种轻量级低秩PINNs，仅包含数百个模型参数，以及一个基于超网络的元学习算法，这允许高效地近似不同范围的PDE输入参数下的PDE解。此外，我们证明所提出的方法在克服一个被称为PINNs“失效模式”的挑战性问题上是有效的。

[No.142] Kissing to Find a Match: Efficient Low-Rank Permutation Representation
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 亲吻以寻找匹配：高效的低秩排列表示
类别: 
作者: Hannah Dröge;Zorah Lähner;Yuval Bahat;Onofre Martorell Nadal;Felix Heide;Michael Moeller;
机构: University of Siegen;Princeton University;Algolux;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/71139
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2308.13252
英摘: Permutation matrices play a key role in matching and assignment problems across the fields, especially in computer vision and robotics. However, memory for explicitly representing permutation matrices grows quadratically with the size of the problem, prohibiting large problem instances. In this work, we propose to tackle the curse of dimensionality of large permutation matrices by approximating them using low-rank matrix factorization, followed by a nonlinearity. To this end, we rely on the Kissing number theory to infer the minimal rank required for representing a permutation matrix of a given size, which is significantly smaller than the problem size. This leads to a drastic reduction in computation and memory costs, e.g., up to $3$ orders of magnitude less memory for a problem of size $n=20000$, represented using $8.4\times10^5$ elements in two small matrices instead of using a single huge matrix with $4\times 10^8$ elements. The proposed representation allows for accurate representations of large permutation matrices, which in turn enables handling large problems that would have been infeasible otherwise. We demonstrate the applicability and merits of the proposed approach through a series of experiments on a range of problems that involve predicting permutation matrices, from linear and quadratic assignment to shape matching problems.
中摘: 置换矩阵在各领域中的匹配和分配问题中扮演着关键角色，特别是在计算机视觉和机器人学中。然而，明确表示置换矩阵所需的内存随着问题规模的增大而呈平方级增长，这限制了大规模问题实例的处理。在这项工作中，我们提出通过低秩矩阵分解来逼近大规模置换矩阵，随后应用非线性变换，以此应对维度灾难。为此，我们依赖于亲吻数理论来推断表示特定大小置换矩阵所需的最小秩，这一秩显著小于问题的规模。这导致计算和内存成本的大幅降低，例如，对于一个大小为n=20000的问题，使用两个小矩阵中的8.4×10^5个元素来表示，而不是使用一个包含4×10^8个元素的巨大矩阵，内存消耗可减少高达3个数量级。所提出的表示方法允许对大规模置换矩阵进行精确表示，进而使得原本不可能处理的大规模问题得以解决。我们通过一系列实验展示了所提出方法的适用性和优势，这些实验涵盖了涉及预测置换矩阵的各种问题，从线性、二次分配问题到形状匹配问题。

我们的方法不仅减少了资源需求，还提高了处理大规模数据集的效率和可行性。通过对不同问题的实验，如线性和二次分配问题以及形状匹配任务，我们证明了这种方法的有效性和实用性。通过低秩矩阵分解结合非线性变换，我们能够以更少的计算资源实现对大规模置换矩阵的准确近似，从而解决了传统方法在处理大规模问题时面临的瓶颈。这种创新的表示方式开辟了新的可能性，使我们在计算机视觉和机器人学等领域能够处理更为复杂和庞大的数据集，推动了相关技术的发展。

[No.145] Towards Higher Ranks via Adversarial Weight Pruning
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 通过对抗性权重修剪提升更高层次
类别: 
作者: Yuchuan Tian;Hanting Chen;Tianyu Guo;Chao Xu;Yunhe Wang;
机构: Peking University;Huawei;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/71320
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.17493
英摘: Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) are hard to deploy on edge devices due to its high computation and storage complexities. As a common practice for model compression, network pruning consists of two major categories: unstructured and structured pruning, where unstructured pruning constantly performs better. However, unstructured pruning presents a structured pattern at high pruning rates, which limits its performance. To this end, we propose a Rank-based PruninG (RPG) method to maintain the ranks of sparse weights in an adversarial manner. In each step, we minimize the low-rank approximation error for the weight matrices using singular value decomposition, and maximize their distance by pushing the weight matrices away from its low rank approximation. This rank-based optimization objective guides sparse weights towards a high-rank topology. The proposed method is conducted in a gradual pruning fashion to stabilize the change of rank during training. Experimental results on various datasets and different tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of our algorithm in high sparsity. The proposed RPG outperforms the state-of-the-art performance by 1.13% top-1 accuracy on ImageNet in ResNet-50 with 98% sparsity. The codes are available at https://github.com/huawei-noah/Efficient-Computing/tree/master/Pruning/RPG and https://gitee.com/mindspore/models/tree/master/research/cv/RPG.
中摘: 卷积神经网络（CNNs）由于其高计算和存储复杂性，难以在边缘设备上部署。模型压缩作为一种通用做法，网络剪枝主要包括两类：无结构剪枝和有结构剪枝，其中无结构剪枝通常表现更优。然而，在高剪枝率下，无结构剪枝呈现出有结构的模式，这限制了其性能。为此，我们提出了一种基于秩的剪枝方法（Rank-based PruninG，简称RPG），以对抗方式保持稀疏权重的秩。每一步中，我们使用奇异值分解来最小化权重矩阵的低秩近似误差，并通过将权重矩阵远离其低秩近似来最大化它们之间的距离。这种基于秩的优化目标引导稀疏权重向高秩拓扑发展。所提出的方法以渐进式剪枝方式进行，以稳定训练过程中秩的变化。在不同数据集和任务上的实验结果表明，我们的算法在高稀疏度下具有有效性。在ResNet-50模型中，提出的RPG在ImageNet数据集上以98%的稀疏度实现了比现有技术高出1.13%的top-1准确率。代码可在以下地址获取：https://github.com/huawei-noah/Efficient-Computing/tree/master/Pruning/RPG 和 https://gitee.com/mindspore/models/tree/master/research/cv/RPG。

[No.146] Spectral Entry-wise Matrix Estimation for Low-Rank Reinforcement Learning
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 谱特征项矩阵估计在低秩强化学习中的应用
类别: 
作者: Stefan Stojanovic;Yassir Jedra;Alexandre Proutiere;
机构: KTH Royal Institute of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/71206
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.06793
英摘: We study matrix estimation problems arising in reinforcement learning (RL) with low-rank structure. In low-rank bandits, the matrix to be recovered specifies the expected arm rewards, and for low-rank Markov Decision Processes (MDPs), it may for example characterize the transition kernel of the MDP. In both cases, each entry of the matrix carries important information, and we seek estimation methods with low entry-wise error. Importantly, these methods further need to accommodate for inherent correlations in the available data (e.g. for MDPs, the data consists of system trajectories). We investigate the performance of simple spectral-based matrix estimation approaches: we show that they efficiently recover the singular subspaces of the matrix and exhibit nearly-minimal entry-wise error. These new results on low-rank matrix estimation make it possible to devise reinforcement learning algorithms that fully exploit the underlying low-rank structure. We provide two examples of such algorithms: a regret minimization algorithm for low-rank bandit problems, and a best policy identification algorithm for reward-free RL in low-rank MDPs. Both algorithms yield state-of-the-art performance guarantees.
中摘: 我们研究了在强化学习（RL）中出现的具有低秩结构的矩阵估计问题。在低秩多臂赌博机问题中，待恢复的矩阵指定了预期的手臂奖励，而对于低秩马尔可夫决策过程（MDPs），它可能例如描述了MDP的转移核。在这两种情况下，矩阵的每个条目都携带重要信息，我们寻求的是具有低条目误差的估计方法。重要的是，这些方法还需要适应可用数据中的固有相关性（例如，对于MDPs，数据由系统轨迹组成）。我们研究了简单基于谱的矩阵估计方法的性能：我们表明，它们能够有效地恢复矩阵的奇异子空间，并表现出几乎最小的条目误差。关于低秩矩阵估计的这些新结果使得设计充分利用底层低秩结构的强化学习算法成为可能。我们提供了两种此类算法的例子：一种是在低秩多臂赌博机问题中最小化后悔的算法，另一种是在低秩MDPs的无奖励RL中识别最佳策略的算法。这两种算法都给出了最前沿的性能保证。

[No.149] Learning List-Level Domain-Invariant Representations for Ranking
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 学习列表级别领域不变表示以进行排名
类别: 
作者: Ruicheng Xian;Honglei Zhuang;Zhen Qin;Hamed Zamani;Jing Lu;Ji Ma;Kai Hui;Han Zhao;Xuanhui Wang;Michael Bendersky;
机构: University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;Google;DeepMind;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/70563
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2212.10764
英摘: Domain adaptation aims to transfer the knowledge learned on (data-rich) source domains to (low-resource) target domains, and a popular method is invariant representation learning, which matches and aligns the data distributions on the feature space. Although this method is studied extensively and applied on classification and regression problems, its adoption on ranking problems is sporadic, and the few existing implementations lack theoretical justifications. This paper revisits invariant representation learning for ranking. Upon reviewing prior work, we found that they implement what we call item-level alignment, which aligns the distributions of the items being ranked from all lists in aggregate but ignores their list structure. However, the list structure should be leveraged, because it is intrinsic to ranking problems where the data and the metrics are defined and computed on lists, not the items by themselves. To close this discrepancy, we propose list-level alignment -- learning domain-invariant representations at the higher level of lists. The benefits are twofold: it leads to the first domain adaptation generalization bound for ranking, in turn providing theoretical support for the proposed method, and it achieves better empirical transfer performance for unsupervised domain adaptation on ranking tasks, including passage reranking.
中摘: 领域适应旨在将从（数据丰富）源域学到的知识转移到（资源匮乏）目标域，一种流行的方法是不变表示学习，它在特征空间上匹配和对齐数据分布。尽管这种方法在分类和回归问题上被广泛研究和应用，但在排序问题上的采用却零星分散，而且现有的少数实现缺乏理论依据。本文重新审视了用于排序的不变表示学习。回顾先前的工作后，我们发现它们实施的是我们所谓的项目级对齐，即汇总所有列表中的待排序项目的分布，但忽略了列表结构。然而，应该利用列表结构，因为它是排序问题固有的，其中数据和度量是在列表上定义和计算的，而不仅仅是单个项目本身。为了弥补这一差异，我们提出了列表级对齐——在列表的更高层次上学习领域不变表示。这样做有双重好处：它导出了排序问题的第一个领域适应泛化界限，反过来为所提出的方法提供了理论支持，并且在包括段落重排序在内的无监督领域适应排序任务上实现了更好的经验转移性能。

[No.150] SpatialRank: Urban Event Ranking with NDCG Optimization on Spatiotemporal Data
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: SpatialRank：基于时空数据NDCG优化的城市事件排名
类别: 
作者: BANG AN;Xun Zhou;Yongjian Zhong;Tianbao Yang;
机构: University of Iowa;Texas A&M University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/70628
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.00270
英摘: The problem of urban event ranking aims at predicting the top-k most risky locations of future events such as traffic accidents and crimes. This problem is of fundamental importance to public safety and urban administration especially when limited resources are available. The problem is, however, challenging due to complex and dynamic spatio-temporal correlations between locations, uneven distribution of urban events in space, and the difficulty to correctly rank nearby locations with similar features. Prior works on event forecasting mostly aim at accurately predicting the actual risk score or counts of events for all the locations. Rankings obtained as such usually have low quality due to prediction errors. Learning-to-rank methods directly optimize measures such as Normalized Discounted Cumulative Gain (NDCG), but cannot handle the spatiotemporal autocorrelation existing among locations. In this paper, we bridge the gap by proposing a novel spatial event ranking approach named SpatialRank. SpatialRank features adaptive graph convolution layers that dynamically learn the spatiotemporal dependencies across locations from data. In addition, the model optimizes through surrogates a hybrid NDCG loss with a spatial component to better rank neighboring spatial locations. We design an importance-sampling with a spatial filtering algorithm to effectively evaluate the loss during training. Comprehensive experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate that SpatialRank can effectively identify the top riskiest locations of crimes and traffic accidents and outperform state-of-art methods in terms of NDCG by up to 12.7%.
中摘: 城市事件排名问题旨在预测未来事件（如交通事故和犯罪）的前k个风险最高的地点。当资源有限时，这个问题对公共安全和城市管理尤为重要。然而，由于地点之间复杂且动态的空间时间相关性、城市事件在空间上的不均匀分布以及正确排名具有相似特征的邻近地点的难度，该问题极具挑战性。之前关于事件预测的工作大多集中在为所有地点准确预测实际风险分数或事件数量上。通过这种方式获得的排名通常质量较低，因为存在预测误差。学习排序方法直接优化诸如规范化折扣累积增益（NDCG）等指标，但无法处理地点间存在的空间时间自相关性。在这篇论文中，我们通过提出一种名为SpatialRank的新型空间事件排名方法来填补这一空白。SpatialRank具有自适应图卷积层，可以从数据中动态地学习跨地点的空间时间依赖关系。此外，模型通过替代损失函数优化混合NDCG损失，其中包含空间成分，以更好地对邻近的空间位置进行排名。我们设计了一种结合空间过滤的重要性采样算法，在训练期间有效地评估损失。在三个真实世界数据集上的全面实验表明，SpatialRank可以有效识别犯罪和交通事故的风险最高地点，并在NDCG方面超越最先进的方法，最高提升12.7%。

[No.151] Spuriosity Rankings: Sorting Data to Measure and Mitigate Biases
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 好奇心排名：排序数据以衡量和减轻偏见
类别: 
作者: Mazda Moayeri;Wenxiao Wang;Sahil Singla;Soheil Feizi;
机构: University of Maryland;Sony;Google;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/70691
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2212.02648
英摘: We present a simple but effective method to measure and mitigate model biases caused by reliance on spurious cues. Instead of requiring costly changes to one's data or model training, our method better utilizes the data one already has by sorting them. Specifically, we rank images within their classes based on spuriosity (the degree to which common spurious cues are present), proxied via deep neural features of an interpretable network. With spuriosity rankings, it is easy to identify minority subpopulations (i.e. low spuriosity images) and assess model bias as the gap in accuracy between high and low spuriosity images. One can even efficiently remove a model's bias at little cost to accuracy by finetuning its classification head on low spuriosity images, resulting in fairer treatment of samples regardless of spuriosity. We demonstrate our method on ImageNet, annotating $5000$ class-feature dependencies ($630$ of which we find to be spurious) and generating a dataset of $325k$ soft segmentations for these features along the way. Having computed spuriosity rankings via the identified spurious neural features, we assess biases for $89$ diverse models and find that class-wise biases are highly correlated across models. Our results suggest that model bias due to spurious feature reliance is influenced far more by what the model is trained on than how it is trained.
中摘: 我们提出了一种简单而有效的方法来测量和减轻因依赖于误导性线索而导致的模型偏见。我们的方法不是要求对数据或模型训练进行成本高昂的更改，而是通过排序更好地利用已有的数据。具体而言，我们根据误导性（常见误导性线索存在的程度）在每个类别内对图像进行排名，通过可解释网络的深度神经特征作为代理。有了误导性排名，很容易识别出少数子群体（即低误导性图像），并评估高误导性和低误导性图像之间的准确性差距作为模型偏见。甚至可以通过在低误导性图像上微调模型的分类头，以极小的准确性损失高效地消除模型的偏见，从而无论误导性如何都能更公平地对待样本。我们在ImageNet上展示了我们的方法，标注了5000个类-特征依赖关系（其中630个发现为误导性），并沿途生成了一个包含325000个这些特征的软分割数据集。通过识别的误导性神经特征计算了误导性排名后，我们评估了89种不同模型的偏见，并发现跨模型的类别偏见高度相关。我们的结果表明，由于依赖误导性特征导致的模型偏见受到模型所训练内容的影响远大于其训练方式的影响。

[No.152] Switching Autoregressive Low-rank Tensor Models
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 自回归低秩张量模型切换
类别: 
作者: Hyun Dong Lee;Andrew Warrington;Joshua I Glaser;Scott Linderman;
机构: Stanford University;Northwestern University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/70915
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.03291
英摘: An important problem in time-series analysis is modeling systems with time-varying dynamics. Probabilistic models with joint continuous and discrete latent states offer interpretable, efficient, and experimentally useful descriptions of such data. Commonly used models include autoregressive hidden Markov models (ARHMMs) and switching linear dynamical systems (SLDSs), each with its own advantages and disadvantages. ARHMMs permit exact inference and easy parameter estimation, but are parameter intensive when modeling long dependencies, and hence are prone to overfitting. In contrast, SLDSs can capture long-range dependencies in a parameter efficient way through Markovian latent dynamics, but present an intractable likelihood and a challenging parameter estimation task. In this paper, we propose switching autoregressive low-rank tensor (SALT) models, which retain the advantages of both approaches while ameliorating the weaknesses. SALT parameterizes the tensor of an ARHMM with a low-rank factorization to control the number of parameters and allow longer range dependencies without overfitting. We prove theoretical and discuss practical connections between SALT, linear dynamical systems, and SLDSs. We empirically demonstrate quantitative advantages of SALT models on a range of simulated and real prediction tasks, including behavioral and neural datasets. Furthermore, the learned low-rank tensor provides novel insights into temporal dependencies within each discrete state.
中摘: 在时间序列分析中，建模具有时变动力学的系统是一个重要的问题。带有联合连续和离散隐状态的概率模型为这类数据提供了可解释、高效且实验上实用的描述。常用模型包括自回归隐藏马尔可夫模型（ARHMMs）和切换线性动态系统（SLDSs），它们各有优缺点。ARHMMs允许精确推断和易于参数估计，但在模拟长依赖关系时参数密集，因此容易过拟合。相比之下，SLDSs通过马尔可夫隐动态以参数高效的方式捕捉长程依赖，但呈现出不可行的似然性和挑战性的参数估计任务。在这篇论文中，我们提出了切换自回归低秩张量（SALT）模型，它保留了两种方法的优点，同时缓解了它们的弱点。SALT通过对ARHMM的张量使用低秩分解来控制参数数量，并允许在不过拟合的情况下建立更长程的依赖关系。我们证明了SALT与线性动态系统及SLDS之间的理论联系，并讨论了实际联系。我们在一系列模拟和真实预测任务中实证展示了SALT模型的量化优势，包括行为和神经数据集。此外，学习得到的低秩张量为每个离散状态内的时间依赖关系提供了新颖的见解。

[No.153] Efficient Model-Free Exploration in Low-Rank MDPs
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 低秩MDP中的高效无模型探索
类别: 
作者: Zakaria Mhammedi;Adam Block;Dylan J Foster;Alexander Rakhlin;
机构: Massachusetts Institute of Technology;Microsoft;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/72107
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2307.03997
英摘: A major challenge in reinforcement learning is to develop practical, sample-efficient algorithms for exploration in high-dimensional domains where generalization and function approximation is required. Low-Rank Markov Decision Processes -- where transition probabilities admit a low-rank factorization based on an unknown feature embedding -- offer a simple, yet expressive framework for RL with function approximation, but existing algorithms are either (1) computationally intractable, or (2) reliant upon restrictive statistical assumptions such as latent variable structure, access to model-based function approximation, or reachability. In this work, we propose the first provably sample-efficient algorithm for exploration in Low-Rank MDPs that is both computationally efficient and model-free, allowing for general function approximation and requiring no additional structural assumptions. Our algorithm, VoX, uses the notion of a barycentric spanner for the feature embedding as an efficiently computable basis for exploration, performing efficient barycentric spanner computation by interleaving representation learning and policy optimization. Our analysis -- which is appealingly simple and modular -- carefully combines several techniques, including a new approach to error-tolerant barycentric spanner computation and an improved analysis of a certain minimax representation learning objective found in prior work.
中摘: 在强化学习中，一个主要挑战是在需要泛化和函数逼近的高维领域中开发实用且样本效率高的探索算法。基于未知特征嵌入的低秩马尔可夫决策过程（Low-Rank Markov Decision Processes）为带有函数逼近的强化学习提供了一个简单而富有表现力的框架，但现有的算法要么（1）计算上不可行，要么（2）依赖于诸如潜在变量结构、基于模型的函数逼近访问或可达性等限制性的统计假设。在这项工作中，我们提出了首个被证明样本效率高、计算高效且无需额外结构假设的低秩MDP探索算法，允许进行通用函数逼近，并且是无模型的。我们的算法，VoX，利用特征嵌入的巴里中心跨度器（barycentric spanner）作为探索的有效计算基础，通过交织表示学习和策略优化来执行高效的巴里中心跨度器计算。我们的分析——令人愉悦地简单和模块化——仔细结合了几种技术，包括一种新的容错巴里中心跨度器计算方法，以及对先前工作中发现的某种极小极大表示学习目标的改进分析。

[No.154] Hardness of Low Rank Approximation of Entrywise Transformed Matrix Products
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 低秩逼近的条目变换矩阵乘积硬度
类别: 
作者: Tamas Sarlos;Xingyou Song;David Woodruff;Qiuyi Zhang;
机构: Google;Carnegie Mellon University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/71970
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.01960
英摘: Inspired by fast algorithms in natural language processing, we study low rank approximation in the entrywise transformed setting where we want to find a good rank $k$ approximation to $f(U \cdot V)$, where $U, V^\top \in \mathbb{R}^{n \times r}$ are given, $r = O(\log(n))$, and $f(x)$ is a general scalar function. Previous work in sublinear low rank approximation has shown that if both (1) $U = V^\top$ and (2) $f(x)$ is a PSD kernel function, then there is an $O(nk^{ω-1})$ time constant relative error approximation algorithm, where $ω\approx 2.376$ is the exponent of matrix multiplication. We give the first conditional time hardness results for this problem, demonstrating that both conditions (1) and (2) are in fact necessary for getting better than $n^{2-o(1)}$ time for a relative error low rank approximation for a wide class of functions. We give novel reductions from the Strong Exponential Time Hypothesis (SETH) that rely on lower bounding the leverage scores of flat sparse vectors and hold even when the rank of the transformed matrix $f(UV)$ and the target rank are $n^{o(1)}$, and when $U = V^\top$. Furthermore, even when $f(x) = x^p$ is a simple polynomial, we give runtime lower bounds in the case when $U \neq V^\top$ of the form $Ω(\min(n^{2-o(1)}, Ω(2^p)))$. Lastly, we demonstrate that our lower bounds are tight by giving an $O(n \cdot \text{poly}(k, 2^p, 1/ε))$ time relative error approximation algorithm and a fast $O(n \cdot \text{poly}(k, p, 1/ε))$ additive error approximation using fast tensor-based sketching. Additionally, since our low rank algorithms rely on matrix-vector product subroutines, our lower bounds extend to show that computing $f(UV)W$, for even a small matrix $W$, requires $Ω(n^{2-o(1)})$ time.
中摘: 受自然语言处理中快速算法的启发，我们研究了在条目级变换设置下的低秩近似问题，即我们希望找到$f(U \cdot V)$的一个好的秩$k$近似值，其中$U, V^\top \in \mathbb{R}^{n \times r}$已知，$r = O(\log(n))$，而$f(x)$是一个一般的标量函数。之前关于次线性低秩近似的工作表明，如果同时满足(1) $U = V^\top$和(2) $f(x)$是一个PSD核函数，则存在一个时间复杂度为$O(nk^{ω-1})$的常数相对误差近似算法，其中$ω\approx 2.376$是矩阵乘法的指数。我们给出了该问题的第一个条件时间难度结果，证明了两个条件(1)和(2)实际上是得到比$n^{2-o(1)}$时间更好的相对误差低秩近似所必需的，对于广泛类别的函数而言。我们从强指数时间假设(SETH)出发，给出了新颖的归约，这些归约依赖于对扁平稀疏向量的杠杆分数进行下界估计，并且即使当变换矩阵$f(UV)$的秩和目标秩都是$n^{o(1)}$，以及当$U = V^\top$时也成立。此外，即使当$f(x) = x^p$是一个简单的多项式时，我们也给出了当$U \neq V^\top$时的运行时间下界，形式为$Ω(\min(n^{2-o(1)}, Ω(2^p)))$。最后，我们通过给出一个时间复杂度为$O(n \cdot \text{poly}(k, 2^p, 1/ε))$的相对误差近似算法和一个使用快速张量基速写实现的快速$O(n \cdot \text{poly}(k, p, 1/ε))$加性误差近似来证明我们的下界是紧致的。另外，由于我们的低秩算法依赖于矩阵-向量乘法子程序，我们的下界扩展到显示计算$f(UV)W$，即使是小矩阵$W$，也需要$Ω(n^{2-o(1)})$的时间。

[No.155] Robust low-rank training via approximate orthonormal constraints
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 通过近似正交约束实现稳健的低秩训练
类别: 
作者: Dayana Savostianova;Emanuele Zangrando;Gianluca Ceruti;Francesco Tudisco;
机构: Gran Sasso Science Institute;EPFL;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/71869
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.01485
英摘: With the growth of model and data sizes, a broad effort has been made to design pruning techniques that reduce the resource demand of deep learning pipelines, while retaining model performance. In order to reduce both inference and training costs, a prominent line of work uses low-rank matrix factorizations to represent the network weights. Although able to retain accuracy, we observe that low-rank methods tend to compromise model robustness against adversarial perturbations. By modeling robustness in terms of the condition number of the neural network, we argue that this loss of robustness is due to the exploding singular values of the low-rank weight matrices. Thus, we introduce a robust low-rank training algorithm that maintains the network's weights on the low-rank matrix manifold while simultaneously enforcing approximate orthonormal constraints. The resulting model reduces both training and inference costs while ensuring well-conditioning and thus better adversarial robustness, without compromising model accuracy. This is shown by extensive numerical evidence and by our main approximation theorem that shows the computed robust low-rank network well-approximates the ideal full model, provided a highly performing low-rank sub-network exists.
中摘: 随着模型和数据规模的增长，人们做出了广泛的努力来设计剪枝技术，这些技术可以减少深度学习管道对资源的需求，同时保持模型性能。为了同时降低推理和训练成本，一个突出的研究方向是使用低秩矩阵分解来表示网络权重。尽管能够保持准确性，我们观察到低秩方法往往会在对抗性扰动方面牺牲模型的鲁棒性。通过将鲁棒性建模为神经网络的条件数，我们论证了这种鲁棒性的损失是由于低秩权重矩阵的奇异值爆炸所导致的。因此，我们引入了一种鲁棒低秩训练算法，该算法在保持网络权重位于低秩矩阵流形上的同时，同时施加近似正交约束。由此产生的模型既降低了训练和推理成本，又确保了良好的条件数，从而提高了对抗性鲁棒性，而且不牺牲模型的准确性。这一点通过广泛的数值证据以及我们的主要逼近定理得以证明，该定理表明计算出的鲁棒低秩网络能很好地逼近理想的全模型，前提是存在一个表现优异的低秩子网络。

[No.156] Model Spider: Learning to Rank Pre-Trained Models Efficiently
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 模型蜘蛛：高效学习排序预训练模型
类别: 
作者: Yi-Kai Zhang;Ting-Ji Huang;Yao-Xiang Ding;De-Chuan Zhan;Han-Jia Ye;
机构: Nanjing University;Zhejiang University;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/70932
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.03900
英摘: Figuring out which Pre-Trained Model (PTM) from a model zoo fits the target task is essential to take advantage of plentiful model resources. With the availability of numerous heterogeneous PTMs from diverse fields, efficiently selecting the most suitable PTM is challenging due to the time-consuming costs of carrying out forward or backward passes over all PTMs. In this paper, we propose Model Spider, which tokenizes both PTMs and tasks by summarizing their characteristics into vectors to enable efficient PTM selection. By leveraging the approximated performance of PTMs on a separate set of training tasks, Model Spider learns to construct tokens and measure the fitness score between a model-task pair via their tokens. The ability to rank relevant PTMs higher than others generalizes to new tasks. With the top-ranked PTM candidates, we further learn to enrich task tokens with their PTM-specific semantics to re-rank the PTMs for better selection. Model Spider balances efficiency and selection ability, making PTM selection like a spider preying on a web. Model Spider demonstrates promising performance in various configurations of model zoos.
中摘: 确定模型库中的预训练模型（PTM）哪个最适合目标任务是利用丰富模型资源的关键。由于在所有PTM上执行前向或后向传递的耗时成本，从不同领域提供的大量异构PTM中高效选择最合适的模型具有挑战性。在这篇论文中，我们提出了Model Spider，它通过将PTM和任务的特征总结为向量来实现高效的PTM选择，从而对两者进行标记。通过利用PTM在独立的训练任务集上的近似性能，Model Spider学习构建标记并计算模型-任务对之间的适应度得分。排名相关PTM高于其他模型的能力可以推广到新任务。基于排名最高的PTM候选，我们进一步学习将特定于PTM的语义融入任务标记，以重新排序PTM，实现更优的选择。Model Spider平衡了效率和选择能力，使PTM的选择过程像蜘蛛在网中捕食一样。Model Spider在各种模型库配置中展示了有希望的性能。

[No.157] MKOR: Momentum-Enabled Kronecker-Factor-Based Optimizer Using Rank-1 Updates
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 动量增强的基于克罗内克因子的使用秩1更新的优化器
类别: 
作者: Mohammad Mozaffari;Sikan Li;Zhao Zhang;Maryam Mehri Dehnavi;
机构: University of Toronto;University of Texas at Austin;Unknown Institution;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/70683
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.01685
英摘: This work proposes a Momentum-Enabled Kronecker-Factor-Based Optimizer Using Rank-1 updates, called MKOR, that improves the training time and convergence properties of deep neural networks (DNNs). Second-order techniques, while enjoying higher convergence rates vs first-order counterparts, have cubic complexity with respect to either the model size and/or the training batch size. Hence they exhibit poor scalability and performance in transformer models, e.g. large language models (LLMs), because the batch sizes in these models scale by the attention mechanism sequence length, leading to large model size and batch sizes. MKOR's complexity is quadratic with respect to the model size, alleviating the computation bottlenecks in second-order methods. Because of their high computation complexity, state-of-the-art implementations of second-order methods can only afford to update the second order information infrequently, and thus do not fully exploit the promise of better convergence from these updates. By reducing the communication complexity of the second-order updates as well as achieving a linear communication complexity, MKOR increases the frequency of second order updates. We also propose a hybrid version of MKOR (called MKOR-H) that mid-training falls backs to a first order optimizer if the second order updates no longer accelerate convergence. Our experiments show that MKOR outperforms state -of-the-art first order methods, e.g. the LAMB optimizer, and best implementations of second-order methods, i.e. KAISA/KFAC, up to 2.57x and 1.85x respectively on BERT-Large-Uncased on 64 GPUs.
中摘: 本研究提出了一种名为MKOR（Momentum-Enabled Kronecker-Factor-Based Optimizer Using Rank-1 updates）的优化器，它通过秩一更新改进了深度神经网络（DNNs）的训练时间和收敛特性。二阶技术虽然相对于一阶方法具有更高的收敛率，但其复杂度与模型大小和/或训练批次大小呈立方关系。因此，在诸如大型语言模型（LLMs）等变换器模型中，它们表现出较差的可扩展性和性能，因为在这些模型中的批次大小会随着注意力机制的序列长度而增加，导致模型大小和批次大小增大。MKOR的复杂度与模型大小呈二次关系，从而缓解了二阶方法中的计算瓶颈。由于计算复杂度高，目前最先进的二阶方法实现只能偶尔更新二阶信息，因此无法充分利用这些更新带来的更好收敛性的潜力。通过降低二阶更新的通信复杂度并实现线性通信复杂度，MKOR提高了二阶更新的频率。我们还提出了MKOR的一个混合版本（称为MKOR-H），在训练中期如果二阶更新不再加速收敛，则回退到一阶优化器。我们的实验表明，MKOR在64个GPU上对BERT-Large-Uncased的表现优于最先进的第一阶方法（如LAMB优化器）以及最佳的二阶方法实现（即KAISA/KFAC），分别达到了2.57倍和1.85倍的提升。

[No.159] A Theoretical Analysis of the Test Error of Finite-Rank Kernel Ridge Regression
来源: NeurIPS 2023

[No.160] Learning Adversarial Low-rank Markov Decision Processes with Unknown Transition and Full-information Feedback
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 学习未知转移概率和完全信息反馈的对抗低秩马尔可夫决策过程
类别: 
作者: Canzhe Zhao;Ruofeng Yang;Baoxiang Wang;Xuezhou Zhang;Shuai Li;
机构: Shanghai Jiao Tong University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/71239
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.07876
英摘: In this work, we study the low-rank MDPs with adversarially changed losses in the full-information feedback setting. In particular, the unknown transition probability kernel admits a low-rank matrix decomposition \citep{REPUCB22}, and the loss functions may change adversarially but are revealed to the learner at the end of each episode. We propose a policy optimization-based algorithm POLO, and we prove that it attains the $\widetilde{O}(K^{\frac{5}{6}}A^{\frac{1}{2}}d\ln(1+M)/(1-γ)^2)$ regret guarantee, where $d$ is rank of the transition kernel (and hence the dimension of the unknown representations), $A$ is the cardinality of the action space, $M$ is the cardinality of the model class, and $γ$ is the discounted factor. Notably, our algorithm is oracle-efficient and has a regret guarantee with no dependence on the size of potentially arbitrarily large state space. Furthermore, we also prove an $Ω(\frac{γ^2}{1-γ} \sqrt{d A K})$ regret lower bound for this problem, showing that low-rank MDPs are statistically more difficult to learn than linear MDPs in the regret minimization setting. To the best of our knowledge, we present the first algorithm that interleaves representation learning, exploration, and exploitation to achieve the sublinear regret guarantee for RL with nonlinear function approximation and adversarial losses.
中摘: 在本研究中，我们探讨了在完全信息反馈设置下，具有低秩马尔可夫决策过程(MDP)和对抗性变化损失的情况。具体而言，未知的转移概率核可以分解为低秩矩阵\citep{REPUCB22}，而损失函数可能以对抗性方式改变，但在每个回合结束时会向学习者揭示。我们提出了一种基于策略优化的算法POLO，并证明其获得了$\widetilde{O}(K^{\frac{5}{6}}A^{\frac{1}{2}}d\ln(1+M)/(1-γ)^2)$的遗憾保证，其中$d$是转移核的秩（即未知表示的维度），$A$是动作空间的基数，$M$是模型类的基数，$γ$是折扣因子。值得注意的是，我们的算法具有高效的计算复杂度，并且其遗憾保证与潜在的任意大状态空间大小无关。此外，我们也证明了这个问题的遗憾下界为$Ω(\frac{γ^2}{1-γ} \sqrt{d A K})$，表明低秩MDP在遗憾最小化设置下比线性MDP在统计上更难学习。据我们所知，我们提出了第一个算法，它将表示学习、探索和利用交织在一起，实现了非线性函数逼近和对抗性损失下的子线性遗憾保证，这是强化学习领域的一个首次尝试。

[No.161] Active Bipartite Ranking
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 活跃双部图排名
类别: 
作者: James Cheshire;Vincent Laurent;Stephan Clémençon;
机构: Télécom ParisTech;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/72547
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.24263
英摘: In this article, bipartite ranking, a statistical learning problem involved in many applications and widely studied in the passive context, is approached in a much more general \textit{active setting} than the discrete one previously considered in the literature. While the latter assumes that the conditional distribution is piece wise constant, the framework we develop permits in contrast to deal with continuous conditional distributions, provided that they fulfill a Hölder smoothness constraint. We first show that a naive approach based on discretisation at a uniform level, fixed \textit{a priori} and consisting in applying next the active strategy designed for the discrete setting generally fails. Instead, we propose a novel algorithm, referred to as smooth-rank and designed for the continuous setting, which aims to minimise the distance between the ROC curve of the estimated ranking rule and the optimal one w.r.t. the $\sup$ norm. We show that, for a fixed confidence level $ε>0$ and probability $δ\in (0,1)$, smooth-rank is PAC$(ε,δ)$. In addition, we provide a problem dependent upper bound on the expected sampling time of smooth-rank and establish a problem dependent lower bound on the expected sampling time of any PAC$(ε,δ)$ algorithm. Beyond the theoretical analysis carried out, numerical results are presented, providing solid empirical evidence of the performance of the algorithm proposed, which compares favorably with alternative approaches.
中摘: 在本文中，我们探讨了二部排序问题，这是一个在许多应用中涉及的统计学习问题，并且在被动背景下被广泛研究。与文献中先前考虑的离散场景相比，我们以更为通用的主动设置来处理这个问题。虽然离散场景假设条件分布是分段常数，但我们的框架相反地允许处理连续的条件分布，只要它们满足Hölder光滑性约束即可。我们首先表明，基于预先固定均匀水平的简单离散化方法，接下来应用为离散场景设计的主动策略通常会失败。相反，我们提出了一种新的算法，称为平滑排序(smooth-rank)，专为连续场景设计，旨在最小化估计的排序规则的ROC曲线与最优规则之间的距离，以$\sup$范数衡量。我们证明了对于固定的置信水平$ε>0$和概率$δ\in(0,1)$，平滑排序是PAC$(ε,δ)$的。此外，我们提供了平滑排序预期采样时间的问题依赖上界，并建立任何PAC$(ε,δ)$算法预期采样时间的问题依赖下界。除了进行的理论分析之外，还呈现了数值结果，提供了算法性能的坚实实证证据，其效果优于替代方法。

请注意，文中"PAC"可能代表“概率近似正确”（Probably Approximately Correct），这是机器学习领域中的一个概念。

[No.162] Matrix Compression via Randomized Low Rank and Low Precision Factorization
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 矩阵压缩通过随机化低秩和低精度因子分解
类别: 
作者: Rajarshi Saha;Varun Srivastava;Mert Pilanci;
机构: Stanford University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/70291
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.11028
英摘: Matrices are exceptionally useful in various fields of study as they provide a convenient framework to organize and manipulate data in a structured manner. However, modern matrices can involve billions of elements, making their storage and processing quite demanding in terms of computational resources and memory usage. Although prohibitively large, such matrices are often approximately low rank. We propose an algorithm that exploits this structure to obtain a low rank decomposition of any matrix $\mathbf{A}$ as $\mathbf{A} \approx \mathbf{L}\mathbf{R}$, where $\mathbf{L}$ and $\mathbf{R}$ are the low rank factors. The total number of elements in $\mathbf{L}$ and $\mathbf{R}$ can be significantly less than that in $\mathbf{A}$. Furthermore, the entries of $\mathbf{L}$ and $\mathbf{R}$ are quantized to low precision formats $--$ compressing $\mathbf{A}$ by giving us a low rank and low precision factorization. Our algorithm first computes an approximate basis of the range space of $\mathbf{A}$ by randomly sketching its columns, followed by a quantization of the vectors constituting this basis. It then computes approximate projections of the columns of $\mathbf{A}$ onto this quantized basis. We derive upper bounds on the approximation error of our algorithm, and analyze the impact of target rank and quantization bit-budget. The tradeoff between compression ratio and approximation accuracy allows for flexibility in choosing these parameters based on specific application requirements. We empirically demonstrate the efficacy of our algorithm in image compression, nearest neighbor classification of image and text embeddings, and compressing the layers of LlaMa-$7$b. Our results illustrate that we can achieve compression ratios as aggressive as one bit per matrix coordinate, all while surpassing or maintaining the performance of traditional compression techniques.
中摘: 矩阵在各种研究领域中极为有用，因为它们为数据的组织和操作提供了一个方便的框架。然而，现代矩阵可能包含数十亿个元素，这对计算资源和内存使用提出了相当高的要求。尽管规模巨大，但这些矩阵通常近似于低秩。我们提出了一种算法，利用这种结构来获得任何矩阵$\mathbf{A}$的低秩分解，即$\mathbf{A} \approx \mathbf{L}\mathbf{R}$，其中$\mathbf{L}$和$\mathbf{R}$是低秩因子。$\mathbf{L}$和$\mathbf{R}$中的元素总数可以远小于$\mathbf{A}$中的元素数。此外，$\mathbf{L}$和$\mathbf{R}$的条目被量化为低精度格式，压缩$\mathbf{A}$，为我们提供了低秩和低精度的因子化。我们的算法首先通过随机绘制$\mathbf{A}$的列来计算其范围空间的近似基底，然后对构成该基底的向量进行量化。接着，它计算$\mathbf{A}$的列在这一量化基底上的近似投影。我们推导出我们算法的近似误差上限，并分析了目标秩和量化比特预算的影响。压缩比与近似精度之间的权衡允许根据具体应用需求灵活选择这些参数。我们在图像压缩、图像和文本嵌入的最近邻分类以及压缩LlaMa-$7$b的层等方面的经验结果证明了我们算法的有效性。我们的结果表明，我们可以在每矩阵坐标使用一个比特的激进压缩比率下，超越或保持传统压缩技术的性能。

[No.164] The Rank-Reduced Kalman Filter: Approximate Dynamical-Low-Rank Filtering In High Dimensions
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 降秩卡尔曼滤波器：高维下的近似动态低秩滤波
类别: 
作者: Jonathan Schmidt;Philipp Hennig;Jörg Nick;Filip Tronarp;
机构: Eberhard Karls University of Tübingen;University of Tübingen;University of Tuebingen;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/71005
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.07774
英摘: Inference and simulation in the context of high-dimensional dynamical systems remain computationally challenging problems. Some form of dimensionality reduction is required to make the problem tractable in general. In this paper, we propose a novel approximate Gaussian filtering and smoothing method which propagates low-rank approximations of the covariance matrices. This is accomplished by projecting the Lyapunov equations associated with the prediction step to a manifold of low-rank matrices, which are then solved by a recently developed, numerically stable, dynamical low-rank integrator. Meanwhile, the update steps are made tractable by noting that the covariance update only transforms the column space of the covariance matrix, which is low-rank by construction. The algorithm differentiates itself from existing ensemble-based approaches in that the low-rank approximations of the covariance matrices are deterministic, rather than stochastic. Crucially, this enables the method to reproduce the exact Kalman filter as the low-rank dimension approaches the true dimensionality of the problem. Our method reduces computational complexity from cubic (for the Kalman filter) to \emph{quadratic} in the state-space size in the worst-case, and can achieve \emph{linear} complexity if the state-space model satisfies certain criteria. Through a set of experiments in classical data-assimilation and spatio-temporal regression, we show that the proposed method consistently outperforms the ensemble-based methods in terms of error in the mean and covariance with respect to the exact Kalman filter. This comes at no additional cost in terms of asymptotic computational complexity.
中摘: 在处理高维动力系统时，推理和模拟仍然是计算上的挑战性问题。通常需要某种形式的降维以使问题变得可处理。在这篇论文中，我们提出了一种新颖的近似高斯滤波和平滑方法，该方法传播协方差矩阵的低秩近似值。这是通过将预测步骤相关的Lyapunov方程投影到低秩矩阵流形上实现的，然后使用最近开发的数值稳定的动态低秩积分器来求解这些方程。同时，更新步骤通过注意到协方差更新仅变换协方差矩阵的列空间而变得可处理，这个空间构造上是低秩的。与现有的基于集合的方法不同，该算法中协方差矩阵的低秩近似是确定性的，而非随机的。关键的是，这使得当低秩维度接近问题的真实维度时，该方法能够重现精确的卡尔曼滤波。我们的方法在最坏的情况下将计算复杂度从立方（对于卡尔曼滤波）降低至\emph{平方}状态空间大小，并且如果状态空间模型满足某些条件，可以达到\emph{线性}复杂度。通过一系列在经典数据同化和时空回归中的实验，我们证明了所提出的方法在平均值和协方差误差方面相对于精确的卡尔曼滤波一致地优于基于集合的方法，而且在渐近计算复杂度方面没有额外成本。
