[No.167] Rank-DETR for High Quality Object Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 基于排名的DETR高質量物体检测
类别: 
作者: Yifan Pu;Weicong Liang;Yiduo Hao;Yuhui Yuan;Yukang Yang;Chao Zhang;Han Hu;Gao Huang;
机构: Tsinghua University;Peking University;Microsoft;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/71408
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.08854
英摘: Modern detection transformers (DETRs) use a set of object queries to predict a list of bounding boxes, sort them by their classification confidence scores, and select the top-ranked predictions as the final detection results for the given input image. A highly performant object detector requires accurate ranking for the bounding box predictions. For DETR-based detectors, the top-ranked bounding boxes suffer from less accurate localization quality due to the misalignment between classification scores and localization accuracy, thus impeding the construction of high-quality detectors. In this work, we introduce a simple and highly performant DETR-based object detector by proposing a series of rank-oriented designs, combinedly called Rank-DETR. Our key contributions include: (i) a rank-oriented architecture design that can prompt positive predictions and suppress the negative ones to ensure lower false positive rates, as well as (ii) a rank-oriented loss function and matching cost design that prioritizes predictions of more accurate localization accuracy during ranking to boost the AP under high IoU thresholds. We apply our method to improve the recent SOTA methods (e.g., H-DETR and DINO-DETR) and report strong COCO object detection results when using different backbones such as ResNet-$50$, Swin-T, and Swin-L, demonstrating the effectiveness of our approach. Code is available at \url{https://github.com/LeapLabTHU/Rank-DETR}.
中摘: 现代检测变压器（DETRs）使用一组对象查询来预测一系列边界框，根据它们的分类置信度分数进行排序，并选择排名最高的预测作为给定输入图像的最终检测结果。高性能的对象检测器需要对边界框预测进行准确的排序。对于基于DETR的检测器，由于分类得分与定位准确性之间的不一致，排名最高的边界框定位质量不够准确，从而阻碍了高质量检测器的构建。在本工作中，我们通过提出一系列以排名为导向的设计，统称为Rank-DETR，引入了一种简单且高性能的基于DETR的对象检测器。我们的主要贡献包括：(i) 一种以排名为导向的架构设计，能够促进正面预测并抑制负面预测，确保较低的误报率；以及(ii) 一种以排名为导向的损失函数和匹配成本设计，在排序过程中优先考虑定位准确性更高的预测，以提高高IoU阈值下的平均精度(AP)。我们将该方法应用于改进最近的最先进方法（如H-DETR和DINO-DETR），并在使用不同骨干网络（如ResNet-50、Swin-T和Swin-L）时报告了强大的COCO对象检测结果，证明了我们方法的有效性。代码可在以下网址获取：\url{https://github.com/LeapLabTHU/Rank-DETR}。

[No.168] Sharpness-Aware Minimization Leads to Low-Rank Features
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 锐化意识最小化导致低秩特征
类别: 
作者: Maksym Andriushchenko;Dara Bahri;Hossein Mobahi;Nicolas Flammarion;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.16292
英摘: Sharpness-aware minimization (SAM) is a recently proposed method that minimizes the sharpness of the training loss of a neural network. While its generalization improvement is well-known and is the primary motivation, we uncover an additional intriguing effect of SAM: reduction of the feature rank which happens at different layers of a neural network. We show that this low-rank effect occurs very broadly: for different architectures such as fully-connected networks, convolutional networks, vision transformers and for different objectives such as regression, classification, language-image contrastive training. To better understand this phenomenon, we provide a mechanistic understanding of how low-rank features arise in a simple two-layer network. We observe that a significant number of activations gets entirely pruned by SAM which directly contributes to the rank reduction. We confirm this effect theoretically and check that it can also occur in deep networks, although the overall rank reduction mechanism can be more complex, especially for deep networks with pre-activation skip connections and self-attention layers. We make our code available at https://github.com/tml-epfl/sam-low-rank-features.
中摘: 尖锐度感知最小化（SAM）是一种最近提出的方法，旨在最小化神经网络训练损失的尖锐度。虽然其改善泛化的优点广为人知，且是主要动机，但我们揭示了SAM的一个额外引人注目的效果：它能降低神经网络不同层的特征秩。我们证明这种低秩效应具有广泛的适用性：对于全连接网络、卷积网络、视觉变换器等不同架构，以及回归、分类、语言图像对比训练等不同目标。为了更好地理解这一现象，我们对一个简单两层网络中如何产生低秩特征提供了机制性的解释。我们观察到，SAM显著地剪枝了大量的激活值，这直接导致了特征秩的减少。我们在理论上证实了这一效果，并检查发现即使在深层网络中，这一现象也可能会发生，尽管整体的特征秩减少机制可能更为复杂，特别是对于带有预激活跳过连接和自注意力层的深层网络。我们的代码可在以下网址获取：https://github.com/tml-epfl/sam-low-rank-features。

[No.170] Robust Second-Order Nonconvex Optimization and Its Application to Low Rank Matrix Sensing
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 稳健的二阶非凸优化及其在低秩矩阵感知中的应用
类别: 
作者: Shuyao Li;Yu Cheng;Ilias Diakonikolas;Jelena Diakonikolas;Rong Ge;Stephen Wright;
机构: University of Wisconsin-Madison;Brown University;University of Wisconsin;Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/72944
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.10547
英摘: Finding an approximate second-order stationary point (SOSP) is a well-studied and fundamental problem in stochastic nonconvex optimization with many applications in machine learning. However, this problem is poorly understood in the presence of outliers, limiting the use of existing nonconvex algorithms in adversarial settings.
  In this paper, we study the problem of finding SOSPs in the strong contamination model, where a constant fraction of datapoints are arbitrarily corrupted. We introduce a general framework for efficiently finding an approximate SOSP with \emph{dimension-independent} accuracy guarantees, using $\widetilde{O}({D^2}/ε)$ samples where $D$ is the ambient dimension and $ε$ is the fraction of corrupted datapoints.
  As a concrete application of our framework, we apply it to the problem of low rank matrix sensing, developing efficient and provably robust algorithms that can tolerate corruptions in both the sensing matrices and the measurements. In addition, we establish a Statistical Query lower bound providing evidence that the quadratic dependence on $D$ in the sample complexity is necessary for computationally efficient algorithms.
中摘: 寻找近似的二阶稳定点（SOSP）是在随机非凸优化中一个被广泛研究且基础性的问题，它在机器学习中有诸多应用。然而，在存在异常值的情况下，这个问题尚未得到充分理解，这限制了现有非凸算法在对抗性环境中的使用。

在这篇论文中，我们研究了在强污染模型下寻找SOSP的问题，其中有一部分数据点被任意破坏。我们提出了一种通用框架，可以有效地找到具有维数无关精度保证的近似SOSP，使用\(\widetilde{O}(D^2/ε)\)个样本，其中\(D\)是环境维度，\(ε\)是被破坏数据点的比例。

作为我们框架的具体应用，我们将之应用于低秩矩阵感应问题，开发出高效且理论上稳健的算法，能够容忍传感矩阵和测量值中的腐蚀。此外，我们建立了一个统计查询的下界，提供了证据表明在计算效率算法的样本复杂度中，对\(D\)的二次依赖是必要的。

[No.171] Provably Efficient Algorithm for Nonstationary Low-Rank MDPs
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 可验证高效算法的非稳态低秩MDP问题
类别: 
作者: Yuan Cheng;Jing Yang;Yingbin Liang;

类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/70509
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2308.05471
英摘: Reinforcement learning (RL) under changing environment models many real-world applications via nonstationary Markov Decision Processes (MDPs), and hence gains considerable interest. However, theoretical studies on nonstationary MDPs in the literature have mainly focused on tabular and linear (mixture) MDPs, which do not capture the nature of unknown representation in deep RL. In this paper, we make the first effort to investigate nonstationary RL under episodic low-rank MDPs, where both transition kernels and rewards may vary over time, and the low-rank model contains unknown representation in addition to the linear state embedding function. We first propose a parameter-dependent policy optimization algorithm called PORTAL, and further improve PORTAL to its parameter-free version of Ada-PORTAL, which is able to tune its hyper-parameters adaptively without any prior knowledge of nonstationarity. For both algorithms, we provide upper bounds on the average dynamic suboptimality gap, which show that as long as the nonstationarity is not significantly large, PORTAL and Ada-PORTAL are sample-efficient and can achieve arbitrarily small average dynamic suboptimality gap with polynomial sample complexity.
中摘: 在变化环境下的强化学习（RL）通过非平稳马尔可夫决策过程（MDPs）模拟了许多现实世界的应用，因此引起了极大的关注。然而，文献中对非平稳MDPs的理论研究主要集中在表格和线性（混合）MDPs上，这些并没有捕捉到深度强化学习中未知表示的本质。在这篇论文中，我们首次尝试研究在周期性低秩MDPs下的非平稳RL，其中转移核和奖励可能随时间变化，而低秩模型除了线性状态嵌入函数之外还包含未知表示。我们首先提出了一种参数依赖的策略优化算法，称为PORTAL，并进一步将PORTAL改进为无参数版本Ada-PORTAL，后者能够在没有任何关于非平稳性的先验知识的情况下自适应地调整其超参数。对于这两种算法，我们都提供了平均动态次优性差距的上限，这表明只要非平稳性不是显著大，PORTAL和Ada-PORTAL都是样本高效的，并且可以用多项式样本复杂度实现任意小的平均动态次优性差距。

[No.172] Scaling Up Differentially Private LASSO Regularized Logistic Regression via Faster Frank-Wolfe Iterations
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 通过更快的弗兰克-沃尔夫迭代扩展差分隐私LASSO正则化逻辑回归的性能
类别: 
作者: Edward Raff;Amol Ashish Khanna;Fred Lu;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.19978
英摘: To the best of our knowledge, there are no methods today for training differentially private regression models on sparse input data. In doing so, we reduce the training time of the algorithm from $\mathcal{O}( T D S + T N S)$ to $\mathcal{O}(N S + T \sqrt{D} \log{D} + T S^2)$, where $T$ is the number of iterations and a sparsity rate $S$ of a dataset with $N$ rows and $D$ features. [No.173] Low Tensor Rank Learning of Neural Dynamics

标题: 低张量秩学习神经动力学
类别:

链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/71401
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2308.11567
英摘: Learning relies on coordinated synaptic changes in recurrently connected populations of neurons. Therefore, understanding the collective evolution of synaptic connectivity over learning is a key challenge in neuroscience and machine learning. In particular, recent work has shown that the weight matrices of task-trained RNNs are typically low rank, but how this low rank structure unfolds over learning is unknown. To address this, we investigate the rank of the 3-tensor formed by the weight matrices throughout learning. By fitting RNNs of varying rank to large-scale neural recordings during a motor learning task, we find that the inferred weights are low-tensor-rank and therefore evolve over a fixed low-dimensional subspace throughout the entire course of learning. We next validate the observation of low-tensor-rank learning on an RNN trained to solve the same task. Finally, we present a set of mathematical results bounding the matrix and tensor ranks of gradient descent learning dynamics which show that low-tensor-rank weights emerge naturally in RNNs trained to solve low-dimensional tasks. Taken together, our findings provide insight on the evolution of population connectivity over learning in both biological and artificial neural networks, and enable reverse engineering of learning-induced changes in recurrent dynamics from large-scale neural recordings.
中摘: 学习依赖于在反复连接的神经元群体中协同的突触变化。因此，理解学习过程中突触连通性集体演化的机制是神经科学和机器学习领域的一个关键挑战。特别是，最近的研究表明，执行任务训练的循环神经网络（RNN）的权重矩阵通常具有低秩特性，但这种低秩结构在学习过程中的展开方式尚不清楚。为了解决这一问题，我们研究了整个学习过程中由权重矩阵构成的三阶张量的秩。通过将不同秩的RNN拟合到大规模神经记录数据上，这些数据来自于一项运动学习任务，我们发现推断出的权重具有低张量秩的特性，这意味着它们在整个学习过程中在一个固定的低维子空间内演化。接下来，我们在一个被训练来解决相同任务的RNN上验证了低张量秩学习的观察结果。最后，我们提出了一系列数学结果，这些结果界定了梯度下降学习动态下的矩阵秩和张量秩，显示了在训练解决低维任务的RNN中，低张量秩的权重自然出现。综合来看，我们的发现提供了对生物和人工神经网络中学习过程中群体连通性演化的洞察，并使我们能够从大规模神经记录中反向工程出学习诱导的循环动力学变化。

[No.174] Transformed Low-Rank Parameterization Can Help Robust Generalization for Tensor Neural Networks
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 低秩参数化变换有助于张量神经网络的稳健泛化
类别: 
作者: Andong Wang;Chao Li;Mingyuan Bai;Zhong Jin;Guoxu Zhou;Qibin Zhao;
机构: RIKEN;Nanjing University of Science and Technology;Guangdong University of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/70301
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2303.00196
英摘: Achieving efficient and robust multi-channel data learning is a challenging task in data science. By exploiting low-rankness in the transformed domain, i.e., transformed low-rankness, tensor Singular Value Decomposition (t-SVD) has achieved extensive success in multi-channel data representation and has recently been extended to function representation such as Neural Networks with t-product layers (t-NNs). However, it still remains unclear how t-SVD theoretically affects the learning behavior of t-NNs. This paper is the first to answer this question by deriving the upper bounds of the generalization error of both standard and adversarially trained t-NNs. It reveals that the t-NNs compressed by exact transformed low-rank parameterization can achieve a sharper adversarial generalization bound. In practice, although t-NNs rarely have exactly transformed low-rank weights, our analysis further shows that by adversarial training with gradient flow (GF), the over-parameterized t-NNs with ReLU activations are trained with implicit regularization towards transformed low-rank parameterization under certain conditions. We also establish adversarial generalization bounds for t-NNs with approximately transformed low-rank weights. Our analysis indicates that the transformed low-rank parameterization can promisingly enhance robust generalization for t-NNs.
中摘: 实现高效且稳健的多通道数据学习在数据科学中是一项挑战性的任务。通过利用变换域中的低秩特性，即变换后的低秩性，张量奇异值分解（t-SVD）在多通道数据表示上取得了广泛的成功，并最近被扩展到函数表示，例如带有t积层的神经网络（t-NNs）。然而，t-SVD如何从理论上影响t-NN的学习行为仍不清楚。本文首次回答了这一问题，推导出了标准和对抗性训练t-NN的一般化误差上限。研究揭示，通过精确变换后的低秩参数化压缩的t-NNs可以达到更紧致的对抗性泛化边界。实际上，尽管t-NNs很少具有精确的变换后低秩权重，但我们的分析进一步表明，在某些条件下，通过梯度流（GF）的对抗性训练，具有ReLU激活的过参数化的t-NNs将以隐式正则化的方式趋向于变换后的低秩参数化。我们还为具有近似变换后低秩权重的t-NNs建立了对抗性泛化界限。我们的分析表明，变换后的低秩参数化有望显著增强t-NNs的鲁棒泛化能力。

[No.175] Unified Off-Policy Learning to Rank: a Reinforcement Learning Perspective
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 统一离策学习排序：从强化学习视角
类别: 
作者: Zeyu Zhang;Yi Su;Hui Yuan;Yiran Wu;Rishab Balasubramanian;Qingyun Wu;Huazheng Wang;Mengdi Wang;

英摘: Off-policy Learning to Rank (LTR) aims to optimize a ranker from data collected by a deployed logging policy. However, existing off-policy learning to rank methods often make strong assumptions about how users generate the click data, i.e., the click model, and hence need to tailor their methods specifically under different click models. In this paper, we unified the ranking process under general stochastic click models as a Markov Decision Process (MDP), and the optimal ranking could be learned with offline reinforcement learning (RL) directly. Building upon this, we leverage offline RL techniques for off-policy LTR and propose the Click Model-Agnostic Unified Off-policy Learning to Rank (CUOLR) method, which could be easily applied to a wide range of click models. Through a dedicated formulation of the MDP, we show that offline RL algorithms can adapt to various click models without complex debiasing techniques and prior knowledge of the model. Results on various large-scale datasets demonstrate that CUOLR consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art off-policy learning to rank algorithms while maintaining consistency and robustness under different click models.
中摘: 离线学习排序（Off-policy Learning to Rank，简称 LTR）旨在从由部署的日志策略收集的数据中优化排序器。然而，现有的离线学习排序方法通常对用户如何生成点击数据，即点击模型，做出强烈的假设，并因此需要针对不同的点击模型专门定制其方法。在本文中，我们将一般随机点击模型下的排序过程统一为马尔科夫决策过程（Markov Decision Process，简称 MDP），并且可以利用离线强化学习（Reinforcement Learning，简称 RL）直接学习最优排序。基于此，我们利用离线 RL 技术进行离线 LTR 学习，并提出了一种不依赖点击模型的统一离线学习排序（Click Model-Agnostic Unified Off-policy Learning to Rank，简称 CUOLR）方法，该方法可以轻松应用于广泛的点击模型。通过专门的 MDP 公式化，我们证明了离线 RL 算法可以适应各种点击模型，无需复杂的去偏技术以及对模型的先验知识。在各种大型数据集上的结果显示，CUOLR 在保持一致性和鲁棒性的同时，在不同点击模型下始终超越最先进的离线学习排序算法。

[No.176] Rank-1 Matrix Completion with Gradient Descent and Small Random Initialization
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 基于梯度下降和小随机初始化的一阶矩阵完成算法

英摘: The nonconvex formulation of the matrix completion problem has received significant attention in recent years due to its affordable complexity compared to the convex formulation. Gradient Descent (GD) is a simple yet efficient baseline algorithm for solving nonconvex optimization problems. The success of GD has been witnessed in many different problems in both theory and practice when it is combined with random initialization. However, previous works on matrix completion require either careful initialization or regularizers to prove the convergence of GD. In this paper, we study the rank-1 symmetric matrix completion and prove that GD converges to the ground truth when small random initialization is used. We show that in a logarithmic number of iterations, the trajectory enters the region where local convergence occurs. We provide an upper bound on the initialization size that is sufficient to guarantee the convergence, and show that a larger initialization can be used as more samples are available. We observe that the implicit regularization effect of GD plays a critical role in the analysis, and for the entire trajectory, it prevents each entry from becoming much larger than the others.
中摘: 近年来，矩阵补全问题的非凸形式因其与凸形式相比具有较低的复杂度而受到广泛关注。梯度下降（GD）是一种简单但高效的基线算法，用于解决非凸优化问题。当与随机初始化结合时，GD在理论和实践中的许多不同问题上都取得了成功。然而，之前关于矩阵补全的工作要么需要精心的初始化，要么需要正则化项来证明GD的收敛性。在这篇论文中，我们研究了秩为1的对称矩阵补全，并证明了当使用小的随机初始化时，GD能够收敛到真实值。我们表明，在对数级别的迭代次数后，轨迹进入局部收敛的区域。我们提供了一个初始化大小的上限，这个上限足以保证收敛性，并且我们发现，当有更多样本可用时，可以使用更大的初始化。我们观察到，GD的隐式正则化效应在分析中扮演了关键角色，对于整个轨迹，它防止了任何一项变得远大于其他项。

这段文本描述了在矩阵补全问题中，使用梯度下降算法（GD）结合随机初始化的收敛性分析。作者指出，在秩为1的对称矩阵补全问题中，即使使用小的随机初始化，GD也能收敛到真实值。他们还讨论了初始化大小对收敛的影响，以及GD的隐式正则化作用如何在整个过程中保持各元素间的平衡。

[No.179] A Unified Framework for Rank-based Loss Minimization
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 基于排名的损失最小化统一框架
类别: 
作者: Rufeng Xiao;Yuze Ge;Rujun Jiang;Yifan Yan;

链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/71265
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.17237
英摘: The empirical loss, commonly referred to as the average loss, is extensively utilized for training machine learning models. However, in order to address the diverse performance requirements of machine learning models, the use of the rank-based loss is prevalent, replacing the empirical loss in many cases. The rank-based loss comprises a weighted sum of sorted individual losses, encompassing both convex losses like the spectral risk, which includes the empirical risk and conditional value-at-risk, and nonconvex losses such as the human-aligned risk and the sum of the ranked range loss. In this paper, we introduce a unified framework for the optimization of the rank-based loss through the utilization of a proximal alternating direction method of multipliers. We demonstrate the convergence and convergence rate of the proposed algorithm under mild conditions. Experiments conducted on synthetic and real datasets illustrate the effectiveness and efficiency of the proposed algorithm.
中摘: 经验损失，通常被称为平均损失，被广泛用于训练机器学习模型。然而，为了满足机器学习模型多样化的性能需求，基于排名的损失函数在很多情况下取代了经验损失，得到了广泛应用。基于排名的损失是由排序后的个体损失加权求和构成的，既包括像谱风险这样的凸损失（其中包含了经验风险和条件风险价值），也包括像人类对齐风险和排名范围损失之和这样的非凸损失。在这篇论文中，我们介绍了一个统一的框架，通过使用近端交替方向乘子法来优化基于排名的损失。我们在温和条件下证明了所提算法的收敛性和收敛率。在合成数据和真实数据集上的实验说明了所提算法的有效性和高效性。

We refer to this scenario as decentralized multi-concept customization, which involves single-client concept tuning and center-node concept fusion. In this paper, we propose a new framework called Mix-of-Show that addresses the challenges of decentralized multi-concept customization, including concept conflicts resulting from existing single-client LoRA tuning and identity loss during model fusion. [No.181] RRHF: Rank Responses to Align Language Models with Human Feedback
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: RRHF：基于排名的响应对齐，用于将语言模型与人类反馈对齐
类别: 
作者: Hongyi Yuan;Zheng Yuan;Chuanqi Tan;Wei Wang;Songfang Huang;Fei Huang;
机构: Tsinghua University;Alibaba Group;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/72308
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2304.05302
英摘: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) facilitates the alignment of large language models with human preferences, significantly enhancing the quality of interactions between humans and models. InstructGPT implements RLHF through several stages, including Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), reward model training, and Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO). However, PPO is sensitive to hyperparameters and requires multiple models in its standard implementation, making it hard to train and scale up to larger parameter counts. In contrast, we propose a novel learning paradigm called RRHF, which scores sampled responses from different sources via a logarithm of conditional probabilities and learns to align these probabilities with human preferences through ranking loss. RRHF can leverage sampled responses from various sources including the model responses from itself, other large language model responses, and human expert responses to learn to rank them. RRHF only needs 1 to 2 models during tuning and can efficiently align language models with human preferences robustly without complex hyperparameter tuning. Additionally, RRHF can be considered an extension of SFT and reward model training while being simpler than PPO in terms of coding, model counts, and hyperparameters. We evaluate RRHF on the Helpful and Harmless dataset, demonstrating comparable alignment performance with PPO by reward model score and human labeling. Extensive experiments show that the performance of RRHF is highly related to sampling quality which suggests RRHF is a best-of-n learner. Codes available at https://github.com/GanjinZero/RRHF.
中摘: 从人类反馈中学习强化（Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback，简称RLHF）促进了大型语言模型与人类偏好的对齐，显著提升了人类与模型交互的质量。InstructGPT通过几个阶段实现了RLHF，包括监督微调（Supervised Fine-Tuning，简称SFT）、奖励模型训练以及近端策略优化（Proximal Policy Optimization，简称PPO）。然而，PPO对超参数敏感，并且在标准实现中需要多个模型，这使得其难以训练并扩展到更大的参数规模。相比之下，我们提出了一种新的学习范式，称为RRHF，它通过条件概率的对数来评分来自不同来源的采样响应，并通过排名损失学习将这些概率与人类偏好对齐。RRHF可以利用来自多种来源的采样响应进行学习和排序，包括模型自身的响应、其他大型语言模型的响应以及人类专家的响应。RRHF在调整过程中仅需1到2个模型，能够有效地、稳健地使语言模型与人类偏好对齐，无需复杂的超参数调整。此外，RRHF可以被视为SFT和奖励模型训练的延伸，但在编码、模型数量和超参数方面比PPO更简单。我们在Helpful和Harmless数据集上评估了RRHF，通过奖励模型得分和人工标注展示了与PPO相当的对齐性能。广泛的实验表明，RRHF的性能高度依赖于采样质量，这表明RRHF是一种基于n个最佳样本的学习方法。代码可在https://github.com/GanjinZero/RRHF获取。

[No.182] AdaptSSR: Pre-training User Model with Augmentation-Adaptive Self-Supervised Ranking
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: AdaptSSR：通过增强自适应半监督排名预训练用户模型
类别: 
作者: Yang Yu;Qi Liu;Kai Zhang;Yuren Zhang;Chao Song;Min Hou;Yuqing Yuan;ZHIhao Ye;ZAIXI ZHANG;Sanshi Lei Yu;
机构: University of Science and Technology of China;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/71432
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.09706
英摘: User modeling, which aims to capture users' characteristics or interests, heavily relies on task-specific labeled data and suffers from the data sparsity issue. Several recent studies tackled this problem by pre-training the user model on massive user behavior sequences with a contrastive learning task. Generally, these methods assume different views of the same behavior sequence constructed via data augmentation are semantically consistent, i.e., reflecting similar characteristics or interests of the user, and thus maximizing their agreement in the feature space. However, due to the diverse interests and heavy noise in user behaviors, existing augmentation methods tend to lose certain characteristics of the user or introduce noisy behaviors. Thus, forcing the user model to directly maximize the similarity between the augmented views may result in a negative transfer. To this end, we propose to replace the contrastive learning task with a new pretext task: Augmentation-Adaptive SelfSupervised Ranking (AdaptSSR), which alleviates the requirement of semantic consistency between the augmented views while pre-training a discriminative user model. Specifically, we adopt a multiple pairwise ranking loss which trains the user model to capture the similarity orders between the implicitly augmented view, the explicitly augmented view, and views from other users. We further employ an in-batch hard negative sampling strategy to facilitate model training. Moreover, considering the distinct impacts of data augmentation on different behavior sequences, we design an augmentation-adaptive fusion mechanism to automatically adjust the similarity order constraint applied to each sample based on the estimated similarity between the augmented views. Extensive experiments on both public and industrial datasets with six downstream tasks verify the effectiveness of AdaptSSR.
中摘: 用户建模，旨在捕捉用户的特征或兴趣，严重依赖于特定任务的标记数据，并受到数据稀疏性问题的影响。最近的一些研究通过在大规模用户行为序列上预训练用户模型并采用对比学习任务来解决这一问题。通常，这些方法假设通过数据增强构建的同一行为序列的不同视图在语义上是一致的，即反映了用户相似的特征或兴趣，因此在特征空间中最大化它们的一致性。然而，由于用户行为中兴趣的多样性和大量噪声，现有的增强方法倾向于丢失某些用户特征或引入嘈杂的行为。因此，迫使用户模型直接最大化增强视图之间的相似性可能导致负面迁移。为此，我们提出用一个新的预设任务替换对比学习任务：增强自适应自我监督排序（AdaptSSR），它在预训练一个判别性的用户模型时减轻了对增强视图之间语义一致性的要求。具体而言，我们采用了一种多对比较的排名损失，训练用户模型捕捉隐式增强视图、显式增强视图与其他用户视图之间的相似度顺序。我们进一步使用批次内的难例采样策略以促进模型训练。此外，考虑到数据增强对不同行为序列的不同影响，我们设计了一种增强自适应融合机制，根据估计的增强视图之间的相似度自动调整应用于每个样本的相似度顺序约束。在公共和工业数据集上的广泛实验，以及六项下游任务验证了AdaptSSR的有效性。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.183] Adversarial Attacks on Online Learning to Rank with Click Feedback
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 面向在线学习排序的点击反馈对抗攻击
类别: 
作者: Jinhang Zuo;Zhiyao Zhang;Zhiyong Wang;Shuai Li;Mohammad Hajiesmaili;Adam Wierman;
机构: California Institute of Technology;Southeast University;Chinese University of Hong Kong;Shanghai Jiao Tong University;University of Massachusetts Amherst;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/71922
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.17071
英摘: Online learning to rank (OLTR) is a sequential decision-making problem where a learning agent selects an ordered list of items and receives feedback through user clicks. Although potential attacks against OLTR algorithms may cause serious losses in real-world applications, little is known about adversarial attacks on OLTR. This paper studies attack strategies against multiple variants of OLTR. Our first result provides an attack strategy against the UCB algorithm on classical stochastic bandits with binary feedback, which solves the key issues caused by bounded and discrete feedback that previous works can not handle. Building on this result, we design attack algorithms against UCB-based OLTR algorithms in position-based and cascade models. Finally, we propose a general attack strategy against any algorithm under the general click model. Each attack algorithm manipulates the learning agent into choosing the target attack item $T-o(T)$ times, incurring a cumulative cost of $o(T)$. Experiments on synthetic and real data further validate the effectiveness of our proposed attack algorithms.
中摘: 在线学习排序（OLTR）是一个序列决策问题，其中学习代理选择一个有序的项目列表，并通过用户点击接收反馈。尽管对OLTR算法的潜在攻击在实际应用中可能造成严重损失，但我们对OLTR的对抗性攻击知之甚少。本文研究了针对OLTR多种变体的攻击策略。我们的第一个成果提供了一种针对具有二进制反馈的经典随机强盗问题中的UCB算法的攻击策略，这解决了先前工作无法处理的由有界和离散反馈引起的关键问题。基于这一成果，我们设计了针对位置基础和级联模型中基于UCB的OLTR算法的攻击算法。最后，我们提出了一种针对一般点击模型下任何算法的通用攻击策略。每个攻击算法操纵学习代理选择目标攻击项目$T-o(T)$次，累计成本为$o(T)$。在合成数据和真实数据上的实验进一步验证了我们提出的攻击算法的有效性。

[No.184] Unbalanced Low-rank Optimal Transport Solvers
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 不平衡低秩最优传输求解器
类别: 
作者: Meyer Scetbon;Michal Klein;Giovanni Palla;marco cuturi;
机构: Microsoft;Apple;Technische Universität München;ENSAE ParisTech;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/71052
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.19727
英摘: The relevance of optimal transport methods to machine learning has long been hindered by two salient limitations. First, the $O(n^3)$ computational cost of standard sample-based solvers (when used on batches of $n$ samples) is prohibitive. Second, the mass conservation constraint makes OT solvers too rigid in practice: because they must match \textit{all} points from both measures, their output can be heavily influenced by outliers. A flurry of recent works in OT has addressed these computational and modelling limitations, but has resulted in two separate strains of methods: While the computational outlook was much improved by entropic regularization, more recent $O(n)$ linear-time \textit{low-rank} solvers hold the promise to scale up OT further. On the other hand, modelling rigidities have been eased owing to unbalanced variants of OT, that rely on penalization terms to promote, rather than impose, mass conservation. The goal of this paper is to merge these two strains, to achieve the promise of \textit{both} versatile/scalable unbalanced/low-rank OT solvers. We propose custom algorithms to implement these extensions for the linear OT problem and its Fused-Gromov-Wasserstein generalization, and demonstrate their practical relevance to challenging spatial transcriptomics matching problems.
中摘: 最优传输方法与机器学习的相关性长期以来受到两个显著限制的影响。首先，当应用于包含n个样本的批次时，标准样本基础求解器的计算成本为O(n^3)，这是极其昂贵的。其次，质量守恒约束使得OT求解器在实际应用中过于僵化：由于它们必须匹配来自两种度量的所有点，因此其输出可能受到异常值的严重影响。近期OT领域的一系列工作已经解决了这些计算和建模上的局限性，但导致了两种独立的方法：一方面，通过熵正则化极大地改善了计算前景；另一方面，最近的O(n)线性时间低秩求解器有望进一步扩大OT的应用范围。另一方面，模型的僵硬性得到了缓解，这得益于不平衡的OT变体，它依赖于惩罚项来促进而非强制执行质量守恒。本文的目标是融合这两种方法，实现既灵活又可扩展的不平衡/低秩OT求解器的承诺。我们为线性OT问题及其Fused-Gromov-Wasserstein泛化提出了定制算法，并展示了它们对具有挑战性的空间转录组学匹配问题的实际相关性。

[No.185] Perceptual adjustment queries and an inverted measurement paradigm for low-rank metric learning
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 感知调整查询和反转测量范式在低秩度量学习中的应用

英摘: We introduce a new type of query mechanism for collecting human feedback, called the perceptual adjustment query ( PAQ). Being both informative and cognitively lightweight, the PAQ adopts an inverted measurement scheme, and combines advantages from both cardinal and ordinal queries. We showcase the PAQ in the metric learning problem, where we collect PAQ measurements to learn an unknown Mahalanobis distance. This gives rise to a high-dimensional, low-rank matrix estimation problem to which standard matrix estimators cannot be applied. Consequently, we develop a two-stage estimator for metric learning from PAQs, and provide sample complexity guarantees for this estimator. We present numerical simulations demonstrating the performance of the estimator and its notable properties.
中摘: 我们引入了一种新的查询机制，用于收集人类反馈，称为感知调整查询（PAQ）。由于既具有信息性又在认知上不繁重，PAQ采用了一种倒置的测量方案，并结合了基数查询和序数查询的优点。我们在度量学习问题中展示了PAQ，其中我们收集PAQ测量值以学习未知的马哈拉诺比斯距离。这产生了一个高维、低秩矩阵估计问题，标准矩阵估计器无法应用于此类问题。因此，我们为从PAQ中进行度量学习开发了一个两阶段估计器，并为该估计器提供了样本复杂度保证。我们展示了数值模拟，演示了估计器的性能及其显著特性。

[No.186] Efficient Low-rank Backpropagation for Vision Transformer Adaptation
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 高效低秩反向传播用于视觉变换器的适应性调整
类别: 
作者: Yuedong Yang;Hung-Yueh Chiang;Guihong Li;Diana Marculescu;Radu Marculescu;
机构: University of Texas at Austin;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/71863
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2309.15275
英摘: The increasing scale of vision transformers (ViT) has made the efficient fine-tuning of these large models for specific needs a significant challenge in various applications. This issue originates from the computationally demanding matrix multiplications required during the backpropagation process through linear layers in ViT. In this paper, we tackle this problem by proposing a new Low-rank BackPropagation via Walsh-Hadamard Transformation (LBP-WHT) method. Intuitively, LBP-WHT projects the gradient into a low-rank space and carries out backpropagation. This approach substantially reduces the computation needed for adapting ViT, as matrix multiplication in the low-rank space is far less resource-intensive. We conduct extensive experiments with different models (ViT, hybrid convolution-ViT model) on multiple datasets to demonstrate the effectiveness of our method. For instance, when adapting an EfficientFormer-L1 model on CIFAR100, our LBP-WHT achieves 10.4% higher accuracy than the state-of-the-art baseline, while requiring 9 MFLOPs less computation. As the first work to accelerate ViT adaptation with low-rank backpropagation, our LBP-WHT method is complementary to many prior efforts and can be combined with them for better performance.
中摘: 视觉转换器（ViT）规模的不断扩大使得针对特定需求对这些大型模型进行高效微调在各种应用中成为一个显著挑战。这个问题源于ViT中线性层反向传播过程中所需的计算密集型矩阵乘法。在这篇论文中，我们通过提出一种新的低秩反向传播通过Walsh-Hadamard变换（LBP-WHT）方法来解决这一问题。直观上，LBP-WHT将梯度投影到一个低秩空间，并在此执行反向传播。这种方法大大减少了适应ViT所需计算量，因为在低秩空间中的矩阵乘法远不那么资源密集。我们在多个数据集上对不同模型（ViT，混合卷积-ViT模型）进行了广泛实验，以证明我们方法的有效性。例如，在CIFAR100上调整EfficientFormer-L1模型时，我们的LBP-WHT方法比最先进的基线提高了10.4%的准确率，同时减少了9百万次浮点运算（MFLOPs）的计算需求。作为首次利用低秩反向传播加速ViT适应的工作，我们的LBP-WHT方法与许多先前的努力互补，并可以与它们结合以获得更好的性能。

[No.187] Learning-to-Rank Meets Language: Boosting Language-Driven Ordering Alignment for Ordinal Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: rank
标题: 学习排序遇上语言：增强基于语言驱动的排序对齐以提升序数分类

英摘: We present a novel language-driven ordering alignment method for ordinal classification. The labels in ordinal classification contain additional ordering relations, making them prone to overfitting when relying solely on training data. Recent developments in pre-trained vision-language models inspire us to leverage the rich ordinal priors in human language by converting the original task into a visionlanguage alignment task. Consequently, we propose L2RCLIP, which fully utilizes the language priors from two perspectives. First, we introduce a complementary prompt tuning technique called RankFormer, designed to enhance the ordering relation of original rank prompts. It employs token-level attention with residual-style prompt blending in the word embedding space. Second, to further incorporate language priors, we revisit the approximate bound optimization of vanilla cross-entropy loss and restructure it within the cross-modal embedding space. Consequently, we propose a cross-modal ordinal pairwise loss to refine the CLIP feature space, where texts and images maintain both semantic alignment and ordering alignment. Extensive experiments on three ordinal classification tasks, including facial age estimation, historical color image (HCI) classification, and aesthetic assessment demonstrate its promising performance. The code is available at https://github.com/raywang335/L2RCLIP.
中摘: 我们提出了一种新颖的语言驱动的排序对齐方法，用于序数分类。序数分类中的标签包含了额外的排序关系，这使得它们在仅依赖训练数据时容易发生过拟合。最近预训练视觉语言模型的发展启发了我们，通过将原始任务转化为视觉语言对齐任务，利用人类语言中丰富的序数先验。因此，我们提出了L2RCLIP，它从两个角度充分利用了语言先验。首先，我们引入了一种称为RankFormer的补充提示调优技术，旨在增强原始排名提示的排序关系。它在词嵌入空间中使用了基于残差风格的提示融合的令牌级注意力机制。其次，为了进一步结合语言先验，我们重新审视了标准交叉熵损失的近似边界优化，并在跨模态嵌入空间内重构了它。因此，我们提出了一种跨模态序数对损失，用于细化CLIP特征空间，在该空间中，文本和图像保持语义对齐和排序对齐。在三个序数分类任务上的广泛实验，包括面部年龄估计、历史彩色图像（HCI）分类和美学评估，展示了其有前景的性能。代码可在https://github.com/raywang335/L2RCLIP上获取。

[No.190] SD-LoRA: Scalable Decoupled Low-Rank Adaptation for Class Incremental Learning
来源: ICLR 2025

英摘: Continual Learning (CL) with foundation models has recently emerged as a promising paradigm to exploit abundant knowledge acquired during pre-training for tackling sequential tasks. However, existing prompt-based and Low-Rank Adaptation-based (LoRA-based) methods often require expanding a prompt/LoRA pool or retaining samples of previous tasks, which poses significant scalability challenges as the number of tasks grows. To address these limitations, we propose Scalable Decoupled LoRA (SD-LoRA) for class incremental learning, which continually separates the learning of the magnitude and direction of LoRA components without rehearsal. Our empirical and theoretical analysis reveals that SD-LoRA tends to follow a low-loss trajectory and converges to an overlapping low-loss region for all learned tasks, resulting in an excellent stability-plasticity trade-off. Building upon these insights, we introduce two variants of SD-LoRA with further improved parameter efficiency. All parameters of SD-LoRAs can be end-to-end optimized for CL objectives. Meanwhile, they support efficient inference by allowing direct evaluation with the finally trained model, obviating the need for component selection. Extensive experiments across multiple CL benchmarks and foundation models consistently validate the effectiveness of SD-LoRA. The code is available at https://github.com/WuYichen-97/SD-Lora-CL.

[No.192] PaRa: Personalizing Text-to-Image Diffusion via Parameter Rank Reduction
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 个性化文本到图像扩散通过参数秩减少
类别: generative models
作者: Shangyu Chen;Zizheng Pan;Jianfei Cai;Dinh Phung;
机构: ;DeepSeek;Monash University;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30047
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.05641
英摘: Personalizing a large-scale pretrained Text-to-Image (T2I) diffusion model is challenging as it typically struggles to make an appropriate trade-off between its training data distribution and the target distribution, i.e., learning a novel concept with only a few target images to achieve personalization (aligning with the personalized target) while preserving text editability (aligning with diverse text prompts). In this paper, we propose PaRa, an effective and efficient Parameter Rank Reduction approach for T2I model personalization by explicitly controlling the rank of the diffusion model parameters to restrict its initial diverse generation space into a small and well-balanced target space. Our design is motivated by the fact that taming a T2I model toward a novel concept such as a specific art style implies a small generation space. To this end, by reducing the rank of model parameters during finetuning, we can effectively constrain the space of the denoising sampling trajectories towards the target. With comprehensive experiments, we show that PaRa achieves great advantages over existing finetuning approaches on single/multi-subject generation as well as single-image editing. Notably, compared to the prevailing fine-tuning technique LoRA, PaRa achieves better parameter efficiency (2x fewer learnable parameters) and much better target image alignment.
中摘: 个性化大规模预训练的文到图（Text-to-Image，简称T2I）扩散模型极具挑战性，因为它通常难以在训练数据分布和目标分布之间做出适当的权衡，即在仅用少量目标图像学习新概念以实现个性化（与个性化目标对齐）的同时，保持文本可编辑性（与多样化的文本提示对齐）。在这篇论文中，我们提出了一种名为PaRa的有效且高效的参数秩降低方法，用于T2I模型的个性化。通过显式控制扩散模型参数的秩，我们将初始多样的生成空间限制在一个小而平衡的目标空间内。我们的设计灵感来源于驯化T2I模型以适应新概念，如特定的艺术风格，意味着存在一个较小的生成空间。为此，通过在微调过程中减少模型参数的秩，我们可以有效地将去噪采样轨迹的空间约束向目标靠近。通过全面的实验，我们证明了PaRa在单/多主题生成以及单张图像编辑方面相比现有微调方法具有显著优势。值得注意的是，与主流的LoRA微调技术相比，PaRa实现了更好的参数效率（学习参数数量减少了2倍），并且在目标图像对齐上表现更佳。

[No.193] SVDQuant: Absorbing Outliers by Low-Rank Component for 4-Bit Diffusion Models
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: SVDQuant：低秩成分吸收异常值的4位扩散模型
类别: generative models
作者: Muyang Li;Yujun Lin;Zhekai Zhang;Tianle Cai;Xiuyu Li;Junxian Guo;Enze Xie;Chenlin Meng;Jun-Yan Zhu;Song Han;
机构: Massachusetts Institute of Technology;;Princeton University;University of California, Berkeley;NVIDIA;Stanford University;Carnegie Mellon University;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/27906
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.05007
英摘: Diffusion models can effectively generate high-quality images. However, as they scale, rising memory demands and higher latency pose substantial deployment challenges. In this work, we aim to accelerate diffusion models by quantizing their weights and activations to 4 bits. At such an aggressive level, both weights and activations are highly sensitive, where existing post-training quantization methods like smoothing become insufficient. To overcome this limitation, we propose SVDQuant, a new 4-bit quantization paradigm. Different from smoothing, which redistributes outliers between weights and activations, our approach absorbs these outliers using a low-rank branch. We first consolidate the outliers by shifting them from activations to weights. Then, we use a high-precision, low-rank branch to take in the weight outliers with Singular Value Decomposition (SVD), while a low-bit quantized branch handles the residuals. This process eases the quantization on both sides. However, naively running the low-rank branch independently incurs significant overhead due to extra data movement of activations, negating the quantization speedup. To address this, we co-design an inference engine Nunchaku that fuses the kernels of the low-rank branch into those of the low-bit branch to cut off redundant memory access. It can also seamlessly support off-the-shelf low-rank adapters (LoRAs) without re-quantization. Extensive experiments on SDXL, PixArt-$Σ$, and FLUX.1 validate the effectiveness of SVDQuant in preserving image quality. We reduce the memory usage for the 12B FLUX.1 models by 3.5$\times$, achieving 3.0$\times$ speedup over the 4-bit weight-only quantization (W4A16) baseline on the 16GB laptop 4090 GPU with INT4 precision. On the latest RTX 5090 desktop with Blackwell architecture, we achieve a 3.1$\times$ speedup compared to the W4A16 model using NVFP4 precision.
中摘: 扩散模型能有效地生成高质量图像。然而，随着模型规模的增大，不断增加的内存需求和更高的延迟带来了显著的部署挑战。在本工作中，我们的目标是通过将模型的权重和激活量化至4位来加速扩散模型。在如此激进的程度下，权重和激活都变得极其敏感，现有的一些后训练量化方法，如平滑处理，变得不够充分。为了解决这一限制，我们提出了SVDQuant，一种新的4位量化范式。不同于平滑处理，它在权重和激活之间重新分配异常值，我们的方法使用低秩分支来吸收这些异常值。我们首先通过将异常值从激活转移到权重来整合它们。然后，我们采用高精度的低秩分支，利用奇异值分解（SVD）来接收权重中的异常值，而低比特量化的分支则处理剩余部分。这个过程使两边的量化变得更加容易。但是，简单地独立运行低秩分支会因为激活数据的额外移动带来显著的开销，从而抵消了量化带来的加速效果。为解决这个问题，我们共同设计了一个推理引擎Nunchaku，它将低秩分支的内核与低比特分支的内核融合，以切断冗余的内存访问。它还可以无缝支持现成的低秩适配器（LoRAs），无需重新量化。在SDXL、PixArt-$\Sigma$和FLUX.1上的广泛实验验证了SVDQuant在保持图像质量方面的有效性。我们将12B的FLUX.1模型的内存使用减少了3.5倍，在配备16GB显存的4090笔记本GPU上，使用INT4精度时，相较于仅4位权重量化（W4A16）的基线，实现了3.0倍的速度提升。在最新的带有Blackwell架构的RTX 5090台式机上，使用NVFP4精度，我们相比W4A16模型达到了3.1倍的速度提升。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.194] LoRA-Pro: Are Low-Rank Adapters Properly Optimized?
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: LoRA-Pro：低秩适配器是否得到了适当的优化？
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Zhengbo Wang;Jian Liang;Ran He;Zilei Wang;Tieniu Tan;
机构: University of Science and Technology of China;Chinese Academy of Sciences;;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28819
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.18242
英摘: Low-rank adaptation, also known as LoRA, has emerged as a prominent method for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of foundation models. Despite its computational efficiency, LoRA still yields inferior performance compared to full fine-tuning. In this paper, we first uncover a fundamental connection between the optimization processes of LoRA and full fine-tuning: using LoRA for optimization is mathematically equivalent to full fine-tuning using a low-rank gradient for parameter updates. And this low-rank gradient can be expressed in terms of the gradients of the two low-rank matrices in LoRA. Leveraging this insight, we introduce LoRA-Pro, a method that enhances LoRA's performance by strategically adjusting the gradients of these low-rank matrices. This adjustment allows the low-rank gradient to more accurately approximate the full fine-tuning gradient, thereby narrowing the performance gap between LoRA and full fine-tuning. Furthermore, we theoretically derive the optimal solutions for adjusting the gradients of the low-rank matrices, applying them during fine-tuning in LoRA-Pro. We conduct extensive experiments across natural language understanding, dialogue generation, mathematical reasoning, code generation, and image classification tasks, demonstrating that LoRA-Pro substantially improves LoRA's performance, effectively narrowing the gap with full fine-tuning. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/mrflogs/LoRA-Pro.
中摘: 低秩适应，也被称为LoRA，作为一种参数高效微调基础模型的显著方法已经崭露头角。尽管其计算效率高，但LoRA在性能上仍然逊色于全量微调。在这篇论文中，我们首先揭示了LoRA优化过程与全量微调之间的一个基本联系：使用LoRA进行优化在数学上等价于使用低秩梯度进行参数更新的全量微调。这个低秩梯度可以用LoRA中的两个低秩矩阵的梯度来表示。基于这一洞察，我们引入了LoRA-Pro，这是一种通过战略性地调整这些低秩矩阵的梯度来增强LoRA性能的方法。这种调整使低秩梯度能够更准确地逼近全量微调梯度，从而缩小LoRA和全量微调之间的性能差距。此外，我们从理论上推导出调整低秩矩阵梯度的最优解，并在LoRA-Pro的微调过程中应用它们。我们在自然语言理解、对话生成、数学推理、代码生成以及图像分类任务上进行了广泛实验，证明LoRA-Pro实质上提高了LoRA的性能，有效地缩小了与全量微调的差距。代码公开可获取，地址为https://github.com/mrflogs/LoRA-Pro。

[No.195] Enhancing Zeroth-order Fine-tuning for Language Models with Low-rank Structures
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 通过低秩结构增强语言模型的零阶微调
类别: optimization
作者: Yiming Chen;Yuan Zhang;Liyuan Cao;Kun Yuan;Zaiwen Wen;
机构: Peking University;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30716
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07698
英摘: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) significantly reduces memory costs when adapting large language models (LLMs) for downstream applications. However, traditional first-order (FO) fine-tuning algorithms incur substantial memory overhead due to the need to store activation values for back-propagation during gradient computation, particularly in long-context fine-tuning tasks. Zeroth-order (ZO) algorithms offer a promising alternative by approximating gradients using finite differences of function values, thus eliminating the need for activation storage. Nevertheless, existing ZO methods struggle to capture the low-rank gradient structure common in LLM fine-tuning, leading to suboptimal performance. This paper proposes a low-rank ZO gradient estimator and introduces a novel low-rank ZO algorithm (LOZO) that effectively captures this structure in LLMs. We provide convergence guarantees for LOZO by framing it as a subspace optimization method. Additionally, its low-rank nature enables LOZO to integrate with momentum techniques while incurring negligible extra memory costs. Extensive experiments across various model sizes and downstream tasks demonstrate that LOZO and its momentum-based variant outperform existing ZO methods and closely approach the performance of FO algorithms.
中摘: 参数高效微调（PEFT）在将大型语言模型（LLM）适应下游应用时显著降低了内存成本。然而，传统的基于一阶（FO）微调算法在计算梯度时需要存储激活值以进行反向传播，在长上下文微调任务中导致了大量的内存开销。零阶（ZO）算法通过使用函数值的有限差分来近似梯度，从而消除了存储激活值的需求，提供了一个有前景的替代方案。然而，现有的ZO方法难以捕捉LLM微调中常见的低秩梯度结构，导致次优性能。本文提出了一种低秩ZO梯度估计器，并引入了一种新颖的低秩ZO算法（LOZO），该算法能有效捕捉LLM中的这种结构。我们通过将其框架视为子空间优化方法，为LOZO提供了收敛性保证。此外，其低秩特性使LOZO能够与动量技术相结合，而几乎不增加额外的内存成本。在不同模型大小和下游任务上的广泛实验表明，LOZO及其基于动量的变体优于现有ZO方法，并且接近FO算法的性能。

[No.196] RaSA: Rank-Sharing Low-Rank Adaptation
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: RaSA：秩共享低秩适应
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Zhiwei He;Zhaopeng Tu;Xing Wang;Xingyu Chen;Zhijie Wang;Jiahao Xu;Tian Liang;Wenxiang Jiao;Zhuosheng Zhang;Rui Wang;

链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30271
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12576
英摘: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has been prominently employed for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs). However, the limited expressive capacity of LoRA, stemming from the low-rank constraint, has been recognized as a bottleneck, particularly in rigorous tasks like code generation and mathematical reasoning. To address this limitation, we introduce Rank-Sharing Low-Rank Adaptation (RaSA), an innovative extension that enhances the expressive capacity of LoRA by leveraging partial rank sharing across layers. By forming a shared rank pool and applying layer-specific weighting, RaSA effectively increases the number of ranks without augmenting parameter overhead. Our theoretically grounded and empirically validated approach demonstrates that RaSA not only maintains the core advantages of LoRA but also significantly boosts performance in challenging code and math tasks. Code, data and scripts are available at: https://github.com/zwhe99/RaSA.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）已被广泛用于大型语言模型（LLM）的参数高效微调。然而，源于低秩约束的LoRA表达能力有限，这被公认为是一个瓶颈，尤其是在代码生成和数学推理等严格任务中。为了解决这一限制，我们引入了秩共享低秩适应（RaSA），这是一种创新性的扩展，通过跨层部分秩共享来增强LoRA的表达能力。通过形成一个共享秩池并应用特定于层的加权，RaSA有效地增加了秩的数量，而无需增加参数开销。我们的理论基础和实证验证方法表明，RaSA不仅保持了LoRA的核心优势，而且在具有挑战性的代码和数学任务中显著提高了性能。代码、数据和脚本可在以下网址获取：https://github.com/zwhe99/RaSA。

[No.197] Efficient Learning with Sine-Activated Low-Rank Matrices
来源: ICLR 2025

机构: ;University of Adelaide;Australian National University;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.19243
英摘: Low-rank decomposition has emerged as a vital tool for enhancing parameter efficiency in neural network architectures, gaining traction across diverse applications in machine learning. These techniques significantly lower the number of parameters, striking a balance between compactness and performance. However, a common challenge has been the compromise between parameter efficiency and the accuracy of the model, where reduced parameters often lead to diminished accuracy compared to their full-rank counterparts. In this work, we propose a novel theoretical framework that integrates a sinusoidal function within the low-rank decomposition process. This approach not only preserves the benefits of the parameter efficiency characteristic of low-rank methods but also increases the decomposition's rank, thereby enhancing model performance. [No.198] AdaRankGrad: Adaptive Gradient Rank and Moments for Memory-Efficient LLMs Training and Fine-Tuning
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: AdaRankGrad：自适应梯度排名和矩方法用于高效内存大型语言模型训练与微调
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Yehonathan Refael;Jonathan Svirsky;Boris Shustin;Wasim Huleihel;Ofir Lindenbaum;
机构: Tel Aviv University;Bar-Ilan University;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29966
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.17881
英摘: Training and fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) come with challenges related to memory and computational requirements due to the increasing size of the model weights and the optimizer states. Various techniques have been developed to tackle these challenges, such as low-rank adaptation (LoRA), which involves introducing a parallel trainable low-rank matrix to the fixed pre-trained weights at each layer. However, these methods often fall short compared to the full-rank weight training approach, as they restrict the parameter search to a low-rank subspace. This limitation can disrupt training dynamics and require a full-rank warm start to mitigate the impact. In this paper, we introduce a new method inspired by a phenomenon we formally prove: as training progresses, the rank of the estimated layer gradients gradually decreases, and asymptotically approaches rank one. Leveraging this, our approach involves adaptively reducing the rank of the gradients during Adam optimization steps, using an efficient online-updating low-rank projections rule. We further present a randomized SVD scheme for efficiently finding the projection matrix. Our technique enables full-parameter fine-tuning with adaptive low-rank gradient updates, significantly reducing overall memory requirements during training compared to state-of-the-art methods while improving model performance in both pretraining and fine-tuning. Finally, we provide a convergence analysis of our method and demonstrate its merits for training and fine-tuning language and biological foundation models.
中摘: 训练和微调大型语言模型（LLM）伴随着因模型权重和优化器状态规模不断增大而产生的内存和计算需求方面的挑战。为应对这些挑战，已开发出多种技术，例如低秩适应（LoRA），它涉及在每一层的固定预训练权重中引入并行可训练的低秩矩阵。然而，与全秩权重训练方法相比，这些方法通常表现欠佳，因为它们将参数搜索限制在低秩子空间内。这一局限性可能会干扰训练动态，并可能需要全秩预热启动来减轻影响。在本文中，我们介绍了一种新方法，该方法受到我们正式证明的一种现象的启发：随着训练的进行，估计的层梯度的秩逐渐降低，并且渐近地接近秩一。利用这一点，我们的方法包括在Adam优化步骤期间自适应地减少梯度的秩，使用一种高效的在线更新低秩投影规则。我们进一步提出了一种随机SVD方案，以高效地找到投影矩阵。我们的技术实现了带有自适应低秩梯度更新的全参数微调，在训练过程中相比最先进方法大幅减少了整体内存需求，同时在预训练和微调阶段提高了模型性能。最后，我们提供了我们方法的收敛性分析，并展示了其在训练和微调语言及生物基础模型时的优点。

[No.201] LoLCATs: On Low-Rank Linearizing of Large Language Models
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: LoLCATs：大型语言模型的低秩线性化
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Michael Zhang;Simran Arora;Rahul Chalamala;Benjamin Frederick Spector;Alan Wu;Krithik Ramesh;Aaryan Singhal;Christopher Re;
机构: Stanford University;University of Pennsylvania;California Institute of Technology;Broad Institute;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30763
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10254
英摘: Recent works show we can linearize large language models (LLMs) -- swapping the quadratic attentions of popular Transformer-based LLMs with subquadratic analogs, such as linear attention -- avoiding the expensive pretraining costs. However, linearizing LLMs often significantly degrades model quality, still requires training over billions of tokens, and remains limited to smaller 1.3B to 7B LLMs. We thus propose Low-rank Linear Conversion via Attention Transfer (LoLCATs), a simple two-step method that improves LLM linearizing quality with orders of magnitudes less memory and compute. We base these steps on two findings. First, we can replace an LLM's softmax attentions with closely-approximating linear attentions, simply by training the linear attentions to match their softmax counterparts with an output MSE loss ("attention transfer"). Then, this enables adjusting for approximation errors and recovering LLM quality simply with low-rank adaptation (LoRA). LoLCATs significantly improves linearizing quality, training efficiency, and scalability. We significantly reduce the linearizing quality gap and produce state-of-the-art subquadratic LLMs from Llama 3 8B and Mistral 7B v0.1, leading to 20+ points of improvement on 5-shot MMLU. Furthermore, LoLCATs does so with only 0.2% of past methods' model parameters and 0.4% of their training tokens. Finally, we apply LoLCATs to create the first linearized 70B and 405B LLMs (50x larger than prior work). When compared with prior approaches under the same compute budgets, LoLCATs significantly improves linearizing quality, closing the gap between linearized and original Llama 3.1 70B and 405B LLMs by 77.8% and 78.1% on 5-shot MMLU.
中摘: 近期的研究表明，我们能够使大型语言模型（LLM）线性化——即用次平方级的注意力机制替换流行的基于Transformer的LLM中的二次方级注意力机制，例如线性注意力——从而避免昂贵的预训练成本。然而，使LLM线性化通常会显著降低模型质量，仍然需要在数十亿个令牌上进行训练，并且局限于较小规模的13亿至70亿参数的LLM。因此，我们提出了低秩线性转换通过注意力传递（LoLCATs），这是一种简单两步法，能够在数量级上减少内存和计算的情况下提升LLM线性化的质量。这些步骤基于两个发现。首先，我们可以通过训练线性注意力来匹配其softmax对应物的输出均方误差损失（"注意力传递"），以此替代LLM的softmax注意力，以达到近似效果。然后，这使得我们能够通过低秩适应（LoRA）调整近似误差并恢复LLM的质量。LoLCATs显著提升了线性化质量、训练效率和可扩展性。我们大幅缩小了线性化质量差距，并从Llama 3 80亿和Mistral 70亿 v0.1版本中产生了最前沿的次平方级LLM，导致在5-shot MMLU上提升了20多分。此外，LoLCATs仅使用过去方法的0.2%模型参数和0.4%的训练令牌就实现了这一目标。最后，我们将LoLCATs应用于创建首个线性化的700亿和4050亿参数的LLM（比先前工作大50倍）。在相同计算预算下，与先前的方法相比，LoLCATs显著提高了线性化质量，将线性化和原始Llama 3.1 700亿和4050亿LLM之间的差距在5-shot MMLU上分别缩小了77.8%和78.1%。

[No.202] Offline Model-Based Optimization by Learning to Rank
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 基于离线模型的优化通过学习排序
类别: optimization
作者: Rong-Xi Tan;Ke Xue;Shen-Huan Lyu;Haopu Shang;yaowang;Yaoyuan Wang;Fu Sheng;Chao Qian;
机构: Nanjing University;Huawei;Hohai University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28117
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11502
英摘: Offline model-based optimization (MBO) aims to identify a design that maximizes a black-box function using only a fixed, pre-collected dataset of designs and their corresponding scores. A common approach in offline MBO is to train a regression-based surrogate model by minimizing mean squared error (MSE) and then find the best design within this surrogate model by different optimizers (e.g., gradient ascent). However, a critical challenge is the risk of out-of-distribution errors, i.e., the surrogate model may typically overestimate the scores and mislead the optimizers into suboptimal regions. Prior works have attempted to address this issue in various ways, such as using regularization techniques and ensemble learning to enhance the robustness of the model, but it still remains. In this paper, we argue that regression models trained with MSE are not well-aligned with the primary goal of offline MBO, which is to select promising designs rather than to predict their scores precisely. Notably, if a surrogate model can maintain the order of candidate designs based on their relative score relationships, it can produce the best designs even without precise predictions. To validate it, we conduct experiments to compare the relationship between the quality of the final designs and MSE, finding that the correlation is really very weak. In contrast, a metric that measures order-maintaining quality shows a significantly stronger correlation. Based on this observation, we propose learning a ranking-based model that leverages learning to rank techniques to prioritize promising designs based on their relative scores. We show that the generalization error on ranking loss can be well bounded. Empirical results across diverse tasks demonstrate the superior performance of our proposed ranking-based models than twenty existing methods.
中摘: 基于模型的离线优化（MBO）旨在仅使用固定、预先收集的设计及其对应分数的数据集来识别能够最大化黑盒函数的设计。在离线MBO中常见的方法是通过最小化均方误差（MSE）训练回归代理模型，然后通过不同的优化器（如梯度上升）在该代理模型内找到最佳设计。然而，一个关键挑战是分布外错误的风险，即代理模型可能通常会高估分数，从而误导优化器进入次优区域。先前的工作尝试以各种方式解决这个问题，例如使用正则化技术和集成学习来增强模型的鲁棒性，但问题仍然存在。在这篇论文中，我们指出用MSE训练的回归模型与离线MBO的主要目标并不完全一致，离线MBO的目标是选择有潜力的设计，而不是精确预测它们的分数。值得注意的是，如果代理模型能够根据候选设计之间的相对分数关系保持其顺序，即使没有精确预测，它也能产生最佳设计。为了验证这一点，我们进行实验比较最终设计的质量与MSE之间的关系，发现两者之间的相关性确实非常弱。相反，衡量保持顺序质量的指标显示出显著更强的相关性。基于这一观察，我们提出学习一个基于排名的模型，利用学习排序技术根据相对分数优先考虑有潜力的设计。我们证明了排名损失上的泛化误差可以得到很好的限定。跨多种任务的实证结果表明，我们提出的基于排名的模型在性能上优于现有的二十种方法。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[No.206] MoS: Unleashing Parameter Efficiency of Low-Rank Adaptation with Mixture of Shards
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: MoS：利用碎片混合释放低秩适应性参数效率
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Sheng Wang;Liheng Chen;Pengan CHEN;Jingwei Dong;Boyang XUE;Jiyue Jiang;Lingpeng Kong;Chuan Wu;
机构: University of Hong Kong;Chinese University of Hong Kong;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/31177
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.00938
英摘: The rapid scaling of large language models necessitates more lightweight finetuning methods to reduce the explosive GPU memory overhead when numerous customized models are served simultaneously. Targeting more parameter-efficient low-rank adaptation (LoRA), parameter sharing presents a promising solution. Empirically, our research into high-level sharing principles highlights the indispensable role of differentiation in reversing the detrimental effects of pure sharing. Guided by this finding, we propose Mixture of Shards (MoS), incorporating both inter-layer and intra-layer sharing schemes, and integrating four nearly cost-free differentiation strategies, namely subset selection, pair dissociation, vector sharding, and shard privatization. Briefly, it selects a designated number of shards from global pools with a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)-like routing mechanism before sequentially concatenating them to low-rank matrices. Hence, it retains all the advantages of LoRA while offering enhanced parameter efficiency, and effectively circumvents the drawbacks of peer parameter-sharing methods. Our empirical experiments demonstrate approximately 8x parameter savings in a standard LoRA setting. The ablation study confirms the significance of each component. Our insights into parameter sharing and MoS method may illuminate future developments of more parameter-efficient finetuning methods. The code is officially available at https://github.com/Forence1999/MoS.
中摘: 大型语言模型的快速扩展需要更轻量级的微调方法，以减少在同时提供大量定制模型时GPU内存的爆炸性开销。针对更参数高效的低秩适应（LoRA），参数共享呈现出一种有前景的解决方案。从我们的研究来看，我们对高级共享原则的实证研究突显了差异化在逆转纯共享的不利影响中的不可或缺的作用。基于这一发现，我们提出了混合碎片（MoS）方法，它结合了层间和层内的共享方案，并集成了四种几乎无成本的差异化策略，即子集选择、对解耦、向量分片和碎片私有化。简而言之，它使用类似专家混合（MoE）的路由机制从全局池中选择指定数量的碎片，然后将它们顺序连接到低秩矩阵中。因此，它保留了LoRA的所有优点，提供了增强的参数效率，并有效避免了同类参数共享方法的缺点。我们的实验证明，在标准的LoRA设置下，大约可以节省8倍的参数。消融研究确认了每个组件的重要性。我们对参数共享的见解以及MoS方法可能为未来开发更参数高效的微调方法提供启示。官方代码可在https://github.com/Forence1999/MoS获取。

[No.207] SaLoRA: Safety-Alignment Preserved Low-Rank Adaptation
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: SaLoRA: 安全对齐保留的低秩适应
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Mingjie Li;Wai Man Si;Michael Backes;Yang Zhang;Yisen Wang;

类型: Poster

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.01765
英摘: As advancements in large language models (LLMs) continue and the demand for personalized models increases, parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods (e.g., LoRA) will become essential due to their efficiency in reducing computation costs. However, recent studies have raised alarming concerns that LoRA fine-tuning could potentially compromise the safety alignment in LLMs, posing significant risks for the model owner. In this paper, we first investigate the underlying mechanism by analyzing the changes in safety alignment related features before and after fine-tuning. Then, we propose a fixed safety module calculated by safety data and a task-specific initialization for trainable parameters in low-rank adaptations, termed Safety-alignment preserved Low-Rank Adaptation (SaLoRA). Unlike previous LoRA methods and their variants, SaLoRA enables targeted modifications to LLMs without disrupting their original alignments. Our experiments show that SaLoRA outperforms various adapters-based approaches across various evaluation metrics in different fine-tuning tasks.
中摘: 随着大型语言模型（LLM）的进步持续进行，以及对个性化模型需求的增加，参数高效微调（PEFT）方法（例如，LoRA）将变得至关重要，因为它们在降低计算成本方面具有高效率。然而，最近的研究提出了令人担忧的问题，即LoRA微调可能损害LLM中的安全对齐性，对模型所有者构成重大风险。在这篇论文中，我们首先通过分析微调前后与安全对齐性相关特征的变化来探究其背后的机制。然后，我们提出了一种称为“安全对齐性保持低秩适应”（SaLoRA）的方法，该方法基于安全数据计算固定的安全模块，并为低秩适应中的可训练参数提供任务特定初始化。与之前的LoRA方法及其变体不同，SaLoRA能够在不破坏原有对齐性的情况下，对LLM进行有针对性的修改。我们的实验表明，在不同的微调任务中，SaLoRA在各种评估指标上均优于多种基于适配器的方法。

[No.208] RankSHAP: Shapley Value Based Feature Attributions for Learning to Rank
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: RankSHAP：基于Shapley值的特征归因方法在排序学习中的应用
类别: interpretability and explainable AI
作者: Tanya Chowdhury;Yair Zick;James Allan;
机构: University of Massachusetts Amherst;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/31045
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.01848
英摘: Numerous works propose post-hoc, model-agnostic explanations for learning to rank, focusing on ordering entities by their relevance to a query through feature attribution methods. However, these attributions often weakly correlate or contradict each other, confusing end users. We adopt an axiomatic game-theoretic approach, popular in the feature attribution community, to identify a set of fundamental axioms that every ranking-based feature attribution method should satisfy. We then introduce Rank-SHAP, extending classical Shapley values to ranking. We evaluate the RankSHAP framework through extensive experiments on two datasets, multiple ranking methods and evaluation metrics. Additionally, a user study confirms RankSHAP's alignment with human intuition. We also perform an axiomatic analysis of existing rank attribution algorithms to determine their compliance with our proposed axioms. Ultimately, our aim is to equip practitioners with a set of axiomatically backed feature attribution methods for studying IR ranking models, that ensure generality as well as consistency.
中摘: 许多工作提出了学习排序的后验、模型不可知的解释，专注于通过特征归因方法根据实体与查询的相关性对其进行排序。然而，这些归因经常彼此微弱相关或相互矛盾，使最终用户感到困惑。我们采用了一种公理化的博弈论方法，这种方法在特征归因社区中很受欢迎，以确定每一种基于排名的特征归因方法都应满足的一组基本公理。然后，我们引入了Rank-SHAP，将经典的夏普利值扩展到排名领域。我们通过在两个数据集、多种排序方法和评估指标上的广泛实验来评估RankSHAP框架。此外，一项用户研究证实了RankSHAP与人类直觉的一致性。我们还对现有的排名归因算法进行了公理分析，以确定它们是否符合我们提出的公理。最终，我们的目标是为实践者提供一组有公理支持的特征归因方法，用于研究信息检索（IR）排名模型，确保其通用性和一致性。

[No.209] Process Reward Model with Q-value Rankings
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 基于Q值排名的过程奖励模型
类别: other topics in machine learning (i.e., none of the above)
作者: Wendi Li;Yixuan Li;
机构: Huazhong University of Science and Technology;Cornell University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/27847
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11287
英摘: Process Reward Modeling (PRM) is critical for complex reasoning and decision-making tasks where the accuracy of intermediate steps significantly influences the overall outcome. Existing PRM approaches, primarily framed as classification problems, employ cross-entropy loss to independently evaluate each step's correctness. This method can lead to suboptimal reward distribution and does not adequately address the interdependencies among steps. To address these limitations, we introduce the Process Q-value Model (PQM), a novel framework that redefines PRM in the context of a Markov Decision Process. PQM optimizes Q-value rankings based on a novel comparative loss function, enhancing the model's ability to capture the intricate dynamics among sequential decisions. This approach provides a more granular and theoretically grounded methodology for process rewards. Our extensive empirical evaluations across various sampling policies, language model backbones, and multi-step reasoning benchmarks show that PQM outperforms classification-based PRMs. The effectiveness of the comparative loss function is highlighted in our comprehensive ablation studies, confirming PQM's practical efficacy and theoretical advantage.
中摘: 过程奖励建模（PRM）对于复杂推理和决策任务至关重要，在这些任务中，中间步骤的准确性对整体结果有重大影响。现有的PRM方法主要以分类问题的形式呈现，使用交叉熵损失独立评估每个步骤的正确性。这种方法可能导致次优的奖励分配，并且没有充分解决步骤间的相互依赖关系。为了解决这些限制，我们引入了过程Q值模型（PQM），这是一种新颖的框架，它在马尔可夫决策过程的背景下重新定义了PRM。PQM基于一种新颖的比较损失函数优化Q值排名，增强了模型捕捉连续决策间复杂动态的能力。这种方法为过程奖励提供了一种更精细、理论基础更扎实的方法。我们在各种采样策略、语言模型后端以及多步推理基准上的广泛实证评估表明，PQM的表现优于基于分类的PRM。我们的全面消融研究突出展示了比较损失函数的有效性，证实了PQM的实际有效性和理论优势。

[No.210] LoR-VP: Low-Rank Visual Prompting for Efficient Vision Model Adaptation
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: LoR-VP：低秩视觉提示以实现高效的视觉模型适应

英摘: Visual prompting has gained popularity as a method for adapting pre-trained models to specific tasks, particularly in the realm of parameter-efficient tuning. However, existing visual prompting techniques often pad the prompt parameters around the image, limiting the interaction between the visual prompts and the original image to a small set of patches while neglecting the inductive bias present in shared information across different patches. In this study, we conduct a thorough preliminary investigation to identify and address these limitations. We propose a novel visual prompt design, introducing Low-Rank matrix multiplication for Visual Prompting (LoR-VP), which enables shared and patch-specific information across rows and columns of image pixels. Extensive experiments across seven network architectures and four datasets demonstrate significant improvements in both performance and efficiency compared to state-of-the-art visual prompting methods, achieving up to 6 times faster training times, utilizing 18 times fewer visual prompt parameters, and delivering a 3.1% improvement in performance. The code is available as https://github.com/jincan333/LoR-VP.

[No.212] OATS: Outlier-Aware Pruning Through Sparse and Low Rank Decomposition
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: OATS：基于稀疏和低秩分解的异常值感知剪枝
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Stephen Zhang;Vardan Papyan;
机构: University of Toronto;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30463
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.13652
英摘: The recent paradigm shift to large-scale foundation models has brought about a new era for deep learning that, while has found great success in practice, has also been plagued by prohibitively expensive costs in terms of high memory consumption and compute. To mitigate these issues, there has been a concerted effort in post-hoc neural network pruning techniques that do not require costly retraining. Despite the considerable progress being made, existing methods often exhibit a steady drop in model performance as the compression increases. In this paper, we present a novel approach to compressing large transformers, coined OATS, that utilizes the second moment information in the input embeddings to decompose the model weights into a sum of sparse and low-rank matrices. Without any retraining, OATS achieves state-of-the-art performance when compressing models by up to $60\%$ on large language models such as Llama-3 and Phi-3 and vision transformers such as ViT and DINOv2 while delivering up to $1.37\times$ the CPU acceleration versus a model that was comparably pruned.
中摘: 最近向大规模基础模型转变的范式带来了深度学习的新时代，虽然在实践中取得了巨大成功，但也因高内存消耗和计算成本而产生了难以承受的费用。为了解决这些问题，人们集中精力研究事后神经网络剪枝技术，这些技术不需要昂贵的重新训练。尽管已经取得了一定的进展，现有方法在压缩比率增加时，往往会表现出模型性能的持续下降。在这篇论文中，我们提出了一种新的压缩大型变压器的方法，称为OATS，它利用输入嵌入中的二阶矩信息将模型权重分解为稀疏矩阵和低秩矩阵之和。无需任何重新训练，OATS在压缩如Llama-3和Phi-3这样的大型语言模型以及ViT和DINOv2这样的视觉变压器时，即使压缩高达60%，也能实现最前沿的性能，同时与类似剪枝的模型相比，能提供最多1.37倍的CPU加速效果。

[No.213] CoRNStack: High-Quality Contrastive Data for Better Code Retrieval and Reranking
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: CoRNStack：高质量对比数据以提升代码检索和重排序性能
类别: unsupervised, self-supervised, semi-supervised, and supervised representation learning
作者: Tarun Suresh;Revanth Gangi Reddy;Yifei Xu;Zach Nussbaum;Andriy Mulyar;Brandon Duderstadt;Heng Ji;
机构: Unknown Institution;University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;Nomic AI;;University of Illinois;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28662
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01007
英摘: Effective code retrieval plays a crucial role in advancing code generation, bug fixing, and software maintenance, particularly as software systems increase in complexity. While current code embedding models have demonstrated promise in retrieving code snippets for small-scale, well-defined tasks, they often underperform in more demanding real-world applications such as bug localization within GitHub repositories. We hypothesize that a key issue is their reliance on noisy and inconsistent datasets for training, which impedes their ability to generalize to more complex retrieval scenarios. To address these limitations, we introduce CoRNStack, a large-scale, high-quality contrastive training dataset for code that spans multiple programming languages. This dataset is curated using consistency filtering to eliminate noisy positives and is further enriched with mined hard negatives, thereby facilitating more effective learning. We demonstrate that contrastive training of embedding models using CoRNStack leads to state-of-the-art performance across a variety of code retrieval tasks. Furthermore, the dataset can be leveraged for training code reranking models, a largely underexplored area compared to text reranking. Our finetuned code reranking model significantly improves the ranking quality over the retrieved results. Finally, by employing our code retriever and reranker together, we demonstrate significant improvements in function localization for GitHub issues, an important component of real-world software development.
中摘: 有效的代码检索在推动代码生成、修复漏洞和软件维护方面发挥着关键作用，尤其是在软件系统复杂性不断增加的情况下。尽管当前的代码嵌入模型在为小规模、定义明确的任务检索代码片段方面已显示出潜力，但它们在更苛刻的实际应用中往往表现不佳，例如在GitHub仓库中定位漏洞。我们假设一个主要问题是它们依赖于嘈杂且不一致的数据集进行训练，这阻碍了它们对更复杂的检索场景的泛化能力。为了解决这些限制，我们引入了CoRNStack，这是一个大规模、高质量的对比学习训练数据集，涵盖了多种编程语言。该数据集通过一致性过滤进行精心挑选，以消除嘈杂的正例，并进一步丰富了挖掘出的困难反例，从而促进更有效的学习。我们证明，使用CoRNStack对比训练嵌入模型可以实现在各种代码检索任务上的最先进性能。此外，该数据集可用于训练代码重新排序模型，这一领域与文本重新排序相比，研究尚少。我们微调的代码重新排序模型显著提高了检索结果的排名质量。最后，通过结合我们的代码检索器和重新排序器，我们在GitHub问题的功能定位上展示了显著的改进，这是现实世界软件开发的重要组成部分。

[No.215] Lambda-Skip Connections: the architectural component that prevents Rank Collapse
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: Lambda-Skip 连接：防止秩塌陷的架构组件
类别: learning theory
作者: Federico Arangath Joseph;Jerome Sieber;Melanie Zeilinger;Carmen Amo Alonso;
机构: ETH Zurich;Stanford University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/31171
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10609
英摘: Rank collapse, a phenomenon where embedding vectors in sequence models rapidly converge to a uniform token or equilibrium state, has recently gained attention in the deep learning literature. This phenomenon leads to reduced expressivity and potential training instabilities due to vanishing gradients. Empirical evidence suggests that architectural components like skip connections, LayerNorm, and MultiLayer Perceptrons (MLPs) play critical roles in mitigating rank collapse. While this issue is well-documented for transformers, alternative sequence models, such as State Space Models (SSMs), which have recently gained prominence, have not been thoroughly examined for similar vulnerabilities. This paper extends the theory of rank collapse from transformers to SSMs using a unifying framework that captures both architectures. We study how a parametrized version of the classic skip connection component, which we call \emph{lambda-skip connections}, provides guarantees for rank collapse prevention. Through analytical results, we present a sufficient condition to guarantee prevention of rank collapse across all the aforementioned architectures. We also study the necessity of this condition via ablation studies and analytical examples. To our knowledge, this is the first study that provides a general guarantee to prevent rank collapse, and that investigates rank collapse in the context of SSMs, offering valuable understanding for both theoreticians and practitioners. Finally, we validate our findings with experiments demonstrating the crucial role of architectural components such as skip connections and gating mechanisms in preventing rank collapse.
中摘: 最近，在深度学习文献中，序列模型中的嵌入向量快速收敛至统一标记或平衡状态的现象——秩坍缩（Rank collapse），引起了人们的关注。这一现象会导致表达能力下降和由于梯度消失而可能产生的训练不稳定性。经验证据表明，架构组件如跳过连接（skip connections）、层归一化（LayerNorm）以及多层感知器（MLPs）在缓解秩坍缩方面扮演着关键角色。尽管对于变压器（transformers）而言，这个问题已经被广泛记录，但对于近期逐渐受到重视的替代序列模型，例如状态空间模型（SSMs），其是否同样存在类似脆弱性尚未得到充分研究。本文通过一个统一框架将秩坍缩理论从变压器扩展到了SSMs，该框架能够同时捕捉两种架构。我们研究了经典跳过连接组件的一种参数化版本，我们称之为\emph{lambda-跳过连接}，它为防止秩坍缩提供了保证。通过分析结果，我们呈现了一个充分条件，以确保上述所有架构都能防止秩坍缩。我们还通过消融研究和分析示例探讨了这个条件的必要性。据我们所知，这是首次提供一个通用保证来防止秩坍缩的研究，并且也是首次在SSMs背景下探究秩坍缩，为理论家和实践者提供了宝贵的理解。最后，我们通过实验验证了我们的发现，展示了诸如跳过连接和门控机制等架构组件在防止秩坍缩中至关重要的作用。

[No.216] SaRA: High-Efficient Diffusion Model Fine-tuning with Progressive Sparse Low-Rank Adaptation
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: SaRA: 高效扩散模型渐进稀疏低秩适应微调
类别: generative models
作者: Teng Hu;Jiangning Zhang;Ran Yi;Hongrui Huang;Yabiao Wang;Lizhuang Ma;
机构: Shanghai Jiao Tong University;Tencent;Harbin Institute of Technology;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/27860
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.06633
英摘: In recent years, the development of diffusion models has led to significant progress in image and video generation tasks, with pre-trained models like the Stable Diffusion series playing a crucial role. Inspired by model pruning which lightens large pre-trained models by removing unimportant parameters, we propose a novel model fine-tuning method to make full use of these ineffective parameters and enable the pre-trained model with new task-specified capabilities. In this work, we first investigate the importance of parameters in pre-trained diffusion models, and discover that the smallest 10% to 20% of parameters by absolute values do not contribute to the generation process. Based on this observation, we propose a method termed SaRA that re-utilizes these temporarily ineffective parameters, equating to optimizing a sparse weight matrix to learn the task-specific knowledge. To mitigate overfitting, we propose a nuclear-norm-based low-rank sparse training scheme for efficient fine-tuning. Furthermore, we design a new progressive parameter adjustment strategy to make full use of the re-trained/finetuned parameters. Finally, we propose a novel unstructural backpropagation strategy, which significantly reduces memory costs during fine-tuning. Our method enhances the generative capabilities of pre-trained models in downstream applications and outperforms traditional fine-tuning methods like LoRA in maintaining model's generalization ability. We validate our approach through fine-tuning experiments on SD models, demonstrating significant improvements. SaRA also offers a practical advantage that requires only a single line of code modification for efficient implementation and is seamlessly compatible with existing methods.
中摘: 近年来，扩散模型的发展在图像和视频生成任务上取得了显著进展，预训练模型如Stable Diffusion系列扮演了关键角色。受模型剪枝启发，该方法通过移除不重要的参数来减轻大型预训练模型的负担，我们提出了一种新颖的模型微调方法，充分利用这些无效参数，使预训练模型具备针对新任务的能力。在这项工作中，我们首先研究了预训练扩散模型中参数的重要性，并发现绝对值最小的10%至20%的参数对生成过程没有贡献。基于这一观察，我们提出了一个名为SaRA的方法，重新利用这些暂时无效的参数，相当于优化一个稀疏权重矩阵以学习特定于任务的知识。为缓解过拟合，我们提出了一种基于核范数的低秩稀疏训练方案，用于高效的微调。此外，我们设计了一种新的渐进式参数调整策略，充分使用重新训练/微调的参数。最后，我们提出了一种新颖的非结构化反向传播策略，在微调期间显著降低了内存成本。我们的方法增强了预训练模型在下游应用中的生成能力，并在保持模型泛化能力方面优于传统的微调方法，如LoRA。我们通过在SD模型上的微调实验验证了我们的方法，展示了显著的改进。SaRA还提供了一个实用优势，只需修改一行代码即可实现高效实施，并与现有方法无缝兼容。

[No.217] RandLoRA: Full rank parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large models
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: RandLoRA: 高秩参数高效微调大模型
类别: applications to computer vision, audio, language, and other modalities
作者: Paul Albert;Frederic Z. Zhang;Hemanth Saratchandran;Cristian Rodriguez-Opazo;Anton van den Hengel;Ehsan Abbasnejad;
机构: University of Adelaide;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30212
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.00987
英摘: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) and its variants have shown impressive results in reducing the number of trainable parameters and memory requirements of large transformer networks while maintaining fine-tuning performance. The low-rank nature of the weight update inherently limits the representation power of fine-tuned models, however, thus potentially compromising performance on complex tasks. This raises a critical question: when a performance gap between LoRA and standard fine-tuning is observed, is it due to the reduced number of trainable parameters or the rank deficiency? This paper aims to answer this question by introducing RandLoRA, a parameter-efficient method that performs full-rank updates using a learned linear combinations of low-rank, non-trainable random matrices. Our method limits the number of trainable parameters by restricting optimization to diagonal scaling matrices applied to the fixed random matrices. This allows us to effectively overcome the low-rank limitations while maintaining parameter and memory efficiency during training. Through extensive experimentation across vision, language, and vision-language benchmarks, we systematically evaluate the limitations of LoRA and existing random basis methods. Our findings reveal that full-rank updates are beneficial across vision and language tasks individually, and even more so for vision-language tasks, where RandLoRA significantly reduces -- and sometimes eliminates -- the performance gap between standard fine-tuning and LoRA, demonstrating its efficacy.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）及其变体在减少大型Transformer网络的可训练参数数量和内存需求方面展现了显著成果，同时保持微调性能。然而，权重更新的低秩性质本质上限制了微调模型的表现力，因此可能在复杂任务上影响性能。这引发了一个关键问题：当观察到LoRA与标准微调之间的性能差距时，这是由于可训练参数数量的减少还是秩的不足导致的？本文通过引入RandLoRA来解答这个问题，这是一种参数高效的方法，使用学习得到的线性组合对低秩、非可训练的随机矩阵进行全秩更新。我们的方法通过将优化限制在应用于固定随机矩阵的对角缩放矩阵上，从而限制了可训练参数的数量。这使我们能够在训练期间有效克服低秩限制，同时保持参数和内存效率。通过在视觉、语言和视觉语言基准上的广泛实验，我们系统地评估了LoRA和现有随机基方法的局限性。我们的发现揭示了对于单独的视觉和语言任务，全秩更新都是有益的，而对于视觉语言任务，这种益处更加明显，RandLoRA显著减小——有时甚至消除了——标准微调和LoRA之间的性能差距，证明了其有效性。

[No.219] Selective Aggregation for Low-Rank Adaptation in Federated Learning
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 联邦学习中选择性聚合的低秩适应机制
类别: transfer learning, meta learning, and lifelong learning
作者: Pengxin Guo;Shuang Zeng;Yanran Wang;Huijie Fan;Feifei Wang;Liangqiong Qu;
机构: University of Hong Kong;;Shenyang Institute of Automation;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28693
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01463
英摘: We investigate LoRA in federated learning through the lens of the asymmetry analysis of the learned $A$ and $B$ matrices. In doing so, we uncover that $A$ matrices are responsible for learning general knowledge, while $B$ matrices focus on capturing client-specific knowledge. Based on this finding, we introduce Federated Share-A Low-Rank Adaptation (FedSA-LoRA), which employs two low-rank trainable matrices $A$ and $B$ to model the weight update, but only $A$ matrices are shared with the server for aggregation. Moreover, we delve into the relationship between the learned $A$ and $B$ matrices in other LoRA variants, such as rsLoRA and VeRA, revealing a consistent pattern. Consequently, we extend our FedSA-LoRA method to these LoRA variants, resulting in FedSA-rsLoRA and FedSA-VeRA. In this way, we establish a general paradigm for integrating LoRA with FL, offering guidance for future work on subsequent LoRA variants combined with FL. Extensive experimental results on natural language understanding and generation tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method. Our code is available at https://github.com/Pengxin-Guo/FedSA-LoRA.
中摘: 我们通过分析学习得到的不对称性矩阵 $A$ 和 $B$，来探究LoRA在联邦学习中的应用。我们发现，$A$ 矩阵负责学习通用知识，而 $B$ 矩阵则专注于捕捉特定于客户端的知识。基于这一发现，我们引入了“联邦共享低秩适应”（FedSA-LoRA），它利用两个低秩可训练矩阵 $A$ 和 $B$ 来建模权重更新，但仅将 $A$ 矩阵与服务器共享以进行聚合。此外，我们深入探讨了在其他LoRA变体（如rsLoRA和VeRA）中学习到的 $A$ 和 $B$ 矩阵之间的关系，揭示了一种一致的模式。因此，我们将FedSA-LoRA方法扩展到了这些LoRA变体上，产生了FedSA-rsLoRA和FedSA-VeRA。这样，我们建立了一个将LoRA与联邦学习（FL）整合的一般范式，为未来在后续LoRA变体与FL结合的工作提供了指导。在自然语言理解和生成任务上的广泛实验结果证明了所提出方法的有效性。我们的代码可在https://github.com/Pengxin-Guo/FedSA-LoRA上获取。
