[No.220] Adaptive Rank Allocation: Speeding Up Modern Transformers with RaNA Adapters
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 自适应秩分配：通过RaNA适配器加速现代变压器模型
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Roberto Garcia;Jerry Weihong Liu;Daniel Sorvisto;Sabri Eyuboglu;
机构: Stanford University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/27996
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18216
英摘: Large Language Models (LLMs) are computationally intensive, particularly during inference. Neuron-adaptive techniques, which selectively activate neurons in Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) layers, offer some speedups but suffer from limitations in modern Transformers. These include reliance on sparse activations, incompatibility with attention layers, and the use of costly neuron masking techniques. To address these issues, we propose the Adaptive Rank Allocation framework and introduce the Rank and Neuron Allocator (RaNA) adapter. RaNA adapters leverage rank adapters, which operate on linear layers by applying both low-rank matrix decompositions and adaptive masking to efficiently allocate compute without depending on activation sparsity. This enables RaNA to be generally applied to MLPs and linear components of attention modules, while eliminating the need for expensive maskers found in neuron-adaptive methods. Notably, when compared to neuron adapters, RaNA improves perplexity by up to 7 points and increases accuracy by up to 8 percentage-points when reducing FLOPs by $\sim$44% in state-of-the-art Transformer architectures. These results position RaNA as a robust solution for improving inference efficiency in modern Transformer architectures.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLM）计算密集，特别是在推理阶段。神经元自适应技术，选择性地在多层感知器（MLP）层激活神经元，提供了一些加速，但在现代Transformer中存在局限性。这些局限包括依赖稀疏激活、与注意力层不兼容以及使用昂贵的神经元屏蔽技术。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了自适应秩分配框架，并引入了秩和神经元分配器（RaNA）适配器。RaNA适配器利用秩适配器，它在线性层上操作，通过应用低秩矩阵分解和自适应屏蔽来有效分配计算资源，而不依赖于激活的稀疏性。这使得RaNA能够普遍应用于MLP和注意力模块的线性部分，同时消除了神经元自适应方法中昂贵的屏蔽器需求。值得注意的是，与神经元适配器相比，RaNA在最先进的Transformer架构中减少约44%的FLOPs时，将困惑度提高了多达7点，准确率提高了多达8个百分点。这些结果使RaNA成为提高现代Transformer架构推理效率的稳健解决方案。

[No.221] Merging LoRAs like Playing LEGO: Pushing the Modularity of LoRA to Extremes Through Rank-Wise Clustering
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 像玩乐高一样合并LoRA：通过按秩聚类来极致推进LoRA的模块化
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Ziyu Zhao;Tao Shen;Didi Zhu;Zexi Li;Jing Su;Xuwu Wang;Fei Wu;
机构: Zhejiang University;;ByteDance;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28655
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.16167
英摘: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as a popular technique for fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) to various domains due to its modular design and widespread availability on platforms like Huggingface. This modularity has sparked interest in combining multiple LoRAs to enhance LLM capabilities. However, existing methods for LoRA composition primarily focus on task-specific adaptations that require additional training, and current model merging techniques often fail to fully leverage LoRA's modular nature, leading to parameter interference and performance degradation. In this paper, we investigate the feasibility of disassembling and reassembling multiple LoRAs at a finer granularity, analogous to assembling LEGO blocks. We introduce the concept of Minimal Semantic Units (MSUs), where the parameters corresponding to each rank in LoRA function as independent units. These MSUs demonstrate permutation invariance and concatenation-summation equivalence properties, enabling flexible combinations to create new LoRAs. Building on these insights, we propose the LoRA-LEGO framework. This framework conducts rank-wise parameter clustering by grouping MSUs from different LoRAs into $k$ clusters. The centroid of each cluster serves as a representative MSU, enabling the assembly of a merged LoRA with an adjusted rank of $k$. Additionally, we apply a dual reweighting strategy to optimize the scale of the merged LoRA. Experiments across various benchmarks demonstrate that our method outperforms existing approaches in LoRA merging.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）作为一种热门技术，因其模块化设计和在Huggingface等平台上的广泛应用，已经成为针对不同领域微调大型语言模型（LLM）的首选方法。这种模块化激发了将多个LoRA结合以增强LLM能力的兴趣。然而，现有的LoRA组合方法主要集中在需要额外训练的任务特定适应上，而当前的模型融合技术往往无法充分利用LoRA的模块化特性，导致参数干扰和性能下降。在这篇论文中，我们探讨了以更精细的粒度拆解和重组多个LoRA的可行性，类似于组装乐高积木。我们引入了最小语义单元（MSU）的概念，其中LoRA中的每一级对应的参数作为独立单元发挥作用。这些MSU展示了置换不变性和拼接-求和等价性属性，使得能够灵活地组合创建新的LoRA。基于这些见解，我们提出了LoRA-LEGO框架。该框架通过将来自不同LoRA的MSU分组为k个簇，进行逐级参数聚类。每个簇的中心点作为代表性的MSU，从而可以组装一个合并后的LoRA，其调整后的级别为k。此外，我们应用了一种双重重新加权策略来优化合并后LoRA的规模。在多个基准测试上的实验表明，我们的方法在LoRA融合方面优于现有方法。

[No.223] A Differentiable Rank-Based Objective for Better Feature Learning
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 一种可微分的基于秩的目标函数，以提高更好的特征学习
类别: unsupervised, self-supervised, semi-supervised, and supervised representation learning
作者: Krunoslav Lehman Pavasovic;Giulio Biroli;Levent Sagun;
机构: ;Ecole Normale Superieure;Meta;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30039
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.09445
英摘: In this paper, we leverage existing statistical methods to better understand feature learning from data. We tackle this by modifying the model-free variable selection method, Feature Ordering by Conditional Independence (FOCI), which is introduced in \cite{azadkia2021simple}. While FOCI is based on a non-parametric coefficient of conditional dependence, we introduce its parametric, differentiable approximation. With this approximate coefficient of correlation, we present a new algorithm called difFOCI, which is applicable to a wider range of machine learning problems thanks to its differentiable nature and learnable parameters. We present difFOCI in three contexts: (1) as a variable selection method with baseline comparisons to FOCI, (2) as a trainable model parametrized with a neural network, and (3) as a generic, widely applicable neural network regularizer, one that improves feature learning with better management of spurious correlations. We evaluate difFOCI on increasingly complex problems ranging from basic variable selection in toy examples to saliency map comparisons in convolutional networks. We then show how difFOCI can be incorporated in the context of fairness to facilitate classifications without relying on sensitive data.
中摘: 在本文中，我们利用现有的统计方法来更好地理解从数据中学习特征的过程。我们通过修改无模型变量选择方法，即条件独立特征排序（FOCI），来解决这一问题，该方法在\cite{azadkia2021simple}中被引入。虽然FOCI基于非参数条件依赖系数，但我们引入了其参数化、可微分的近似值。借助这个相关系数的近似值，我们提出了一种新的算法，称为difFOCI，由于其可微分性和可学习参数，它适用于更广泛的机器学习问题。我们在三个背景下展示了difFOCI：(1)作为一种与FOCI进行基线比较的变量选择方法，(2)作为一个用神经网络参数化的可训练模型，以及(3)作为一种通用的、广泛适用的神经网络正则化器，它通过更好地管理虚假相关性来改善特征学习。我们在复杂程度逐渐增加的问题上评估difFOCI，从玩具示例中的基本变量选择到卷积网络中的显著性图比较。然后，我们展示了如何将difFOCI融入公平性的背景下，以促进分类而不依赖敏感数据。

[No.224] Ensembles of Low-Rank Expert Adapters
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 低秩专家适配器集合
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Yinghao Li;Vianne R. Gao;Chao Zhang;MohamadAli Torkamani;
机构: Georgia Institute of Technology;Amazon;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28544
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.00089
英摘: The training and fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs) often involve diverse textual data from multiple sources, which poses challenges due to conflicting gradient directions, hindering optimization and specialization. These challenges can undermine model generalization across tasks, resulting in reduced downstream performance. Recent research suggests that fine-tuning LLMs on carefully selected, task-specific subsets of data can match or even surpass the performance of using the entire dataset. Building on these insights, we propose the Ensembles of Low-Rank Expert Adapters (ELREA) framework to improve the model's capability to handle diverse tasks. ELREA clusters the training instructions based on their gradient directions, representing different areas of expertise and thereby reducing conflicts during optimization. Expert adapters are then trained on these clusters, utilizing the low-rank adaptation (LoRA) technique to ensure training efficiency and model scalability. During inference, ELREA combines predictions from the most relevant expert adapters based on the input data's gradient similarity to the training clusters, ensuring optimal adapter selection for each task. Experiments show that our method outperforms baseline LoRA adapters trained on the full dataset and other ensemble approaches with similar training and inference complexity across a range of domain-specific tasks.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLM）的训练和微调通常涉及来自多个来源的多样化文本数据，这会因梯度方向冲突而带来挑战，阻碍优化和专业化。这些挑战可能削弱模型在不同任务间的泛化能力，导致下游性能下降。最近的研究表明，在精心挑选、与特定任务相关的数据子集上对LLM进行微调，可以达到甚至超过使用整个数据集的性能。基于这些见解，我们提出了低秩专家适配器集合（ELREA）框架，以提升模型处理多样任务的能力。ELREA根据梯度方向对训练指令进行聚类，代表不同的专业领域，从而减少优化过程中的冲突。然后，利用低秩适应（LoRA）技术在这些聚类上训练专家适配器，确保训练效率和模型可扩展性。在推理阶段，ELREA根据输入数据与训练聚类的梯度相似性，结合最相关的专家适配器的预测，为每个任务选择最优的适配器。实验表明，我们的方法在一系列领域特定任务上，其性能超过了在完整数据集上训练的基线LoRA适配器和其他具有类似训练和推理复杂度的集合方法。

[No.227] Efficient Alternating Minimization with Applications to Weighted Low Rank Approximation
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 高效的交替最小化方法及其在加权低秩逼近中的应用
类别: optimization
作者: Zhao Song;Mingquan Ye;Junze Yin;Lichen Zhang;
机构: University of California, Berkeley;;Rice University;Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28163
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.04169
英摘: Weighted low rank approximation is a fundamental problem in numerical linear algebra, and it has many applications in machine learning. Given a matrix $M \in \mathbb{R}^{n \times n}$, a non-negative weight matrix $W \in \mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}^{n \times n}$, a parameter $k$, the goal is to output two matrices $X,Y\in \mathbb{R}^{n \times k}$ such that $\| W \circ (M - X Y^\top) \|_F$ is minimized, where $\circ$ denotes the Hadamard product. It naturally generalizes the well-studied low rank matrix completion problem. Such a problem is known to be NP-hard and even hard to approximate assuming the Exponential Time Hypothesis [GG11, RSW16]. Meanwhile, alternating minimization is a good heuristic solution for weighted low rank approximation. In particular, [LLR16] shows that, under mild assumptions, alternating minimization does provide provable guarantees. In this work, we develop an efficient and robust framework for alternating minimization that allows the alternating updates to be computed approximately. For weighted low rank approximation, this improves the runtime of [LLR16] from $\|W\|_0k^2$ to $\|W\|_0 k$ where $\|W\|_0$ denotes the number of nonzero entries of the weight matrix. At the heart of our framework is a high-accuracy multiple response regression solver together with a robust analysis of alternating minimization.
中摘: 加权低秩近似是数值线性代数中的一个基本问题，并在机器学习中有许多应用。给定一个矩阵 $M \in \mathbb{R}^{n \times n}$，一个非负权重矩阵 $W \in \mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}^{n \times n}$，以及一个参数 $k$，目标是输出两个矩阵 $X,Y\in \mathbb{R}^{n \times k}$，使得 $\| W \circ (M - X Y^\top) \|_F$ 最小化，其中 $\circ$ 表示Hadamard积。这个问题自然地推广了广为研究的低秩矩阵补全问题。已知该问题是NP难的，甚至在假设指数时间假设的情况下，难以逼近 [GG11, RSW16]。同时，交替最小化是加权低秩近似的一个良好启发式解法。具体而言，[LLR16]表明，在温和的假设下，交替最小化确实提供了可证明的保证。在本工作中，我们开发了一个高效且稳健的交替最小化框架，允许交替更新被近似计算。对于加权低秩近似，这将 [LLR16] 的运行时间从 $\|W\|_0k^2$ 改进到了 $\|W\|_0 k$，其中 $\|W\|_0$ 表示权重矩阵的非零条目数量。我们的框架的核心是一个高精度的多响应回归求解器，以及对交替最小化的稳健分析。

[No.228] Computational Limits of Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) Fine-Tuning for Transformer Models
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 低秩适应（LoRA）微调对变换器模型的计算限制
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Jerry Yao-Chieh Hu;Maojiang Su;En-jui kuo;Zhao Song;Han Liu;
机构: ;National Yang Ming Chiao Tung University;University of California, Berkeley;Northwestern University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29984
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.03136
英摘: We study the computational limits of Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) for finetuning transformer-based models using fine-grained complexity theory. Our key observation is that the existence of low-rank decompositions within the gradient computation of LoRA adaptation leads to possible algorithmic speedup. This allows us to (i) identify a phase transition behavior of efficiency assuming the Strong Exponential Time Hypothesis (SETH), and (ii) prove the existence of almost linear algorithms by controlling the LoRA update computation term by term. For the former, we identify a sharp transition in the efficiency of all possible rank-$r$ LoRA update algorithms for transformers, based on specific norms resulting from the multiplications of the input sequence $X$, pretrained weights ${W^\star}$, and adapter matrices $αB A/r$. Specifically, we derive a shared upper bound threshold for such norms, and show that efficient (sub-quadratic) approximation algorithms of LoRA exist only below this threshold. For the latter, we prove the existence of almost linear approximation algorithms for LoRA adaptation by utilizing the hierarchical low-rank structures of LoRA gradients and approximating the gradients with a series of chained low-rank approximations. To showcase our theory, we consider two practical scenarios: partial (e.g., only $W_V$ and $W_Q$) and full adaptations (e.g., $W_Q$, $W_V$, and $W_K$) of weights in attention heads.
中摘: 我们利用精细复杂性理论研究了低秩适应（LoRA）在微调基于变换器的模型时的计算极限。我们的关键观察是，LoRA适应中的梯度计算存在低秩分解，这可能导致算法加速。这使我们能够（i）假设强指数时间假设（SETH）下识别效率的相变行为，并（ii）通过逐项控制LoRA更新计算证明几乎线性算法的存在。对于前者，我们根据输入序列$X$、预训练权重${W^\star}$和适配器矩阵$αB A/r$的乘积产生的特定范数，确定了所有可能的秩$r$ LoRA更新算法在效率上的急剧转变。具体来说，我们推导出了此类范数的共享上界阈值，并表明只有在这个阈值以下，LoRA的有效（次二次）近似算法才存在。对于后者，我们通过利用LoRA梯度的层次低秩结构并使用一系列连锁低秩逼近来逼近梯度，证明了LoRA适应中几乎线性近似算法的存在。为了展示我们的理论，我们考虑了两种实际场景：部分适应（例如，仅$W_V$和$W_Q$）和完全适应（例如，$W_Q$、$W_V$和$W_K$）注意力头中的权重。

[No.230] SEBRA : Debiasing through Self-Guided Bias Ranking
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: SEBRA : 自我引导偏见排序去偏化
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Adarsh Kappiyath;Abhra Chaudhuri;AJAY KUMAR JAISWAL;Ziquan Liu;Yunpeng Li;Xiatian Zhu;Lu Yin;
机构: ;Fujitsu;University of Texas at Austin;Queen Mary, University of London;Beijing University of Posts and Telecommunications;University of Surrey;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29907
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.18277
英摘: Ranking samples by fine-grained estimates of spuriosity (the degree to which spurious cues are present) has recently been shown to significantly benefit bias mitigation, over the traditional binary biased-\textit{vs}-unbiased partitioning of train sets. However, this spuriosity ranking comes with the requirement of human supervision. In this paper, we propose a debiasing framework based on our novel \ul{Se}lf-Guided \ul{B}ias \ul{Ra}nking (\emph{Sebra}), that mitigates biases (spurious correlations) via an automatic ranking of data points by spuriosity within their respective classes. Sebra leverages a key local symmetry in Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM) training -- the ease of learning a sample via ERM inversely correlates with its spuriousity; the fewer spurious correlations a sample exhibits, the harder it is to learn, and vice versa. However, globally across iterations, ERM tends to deviate from this symmetry. Sebra dynamically steers ERM to correct this deviation, facilitating the sequential learning of attributes in increasing order of difficulty, \ie, decreasing order of spuriosity. As a result, the sequence in which Sebra learns samples naturally provides spuriousity rankings. We use the resulting fine-grained bias characterization in a contrastive learning framework to mitigate biases from multiple sources. Extensive experiments show that Sebra consistently outperforms previous state-of-the-art unsupervised debiasing techniques across multiple standard benchmarks, including UrbanCars, BAR, CelebA, and ImageNet-1K. Code, pre-trained models, and training logs are available at https://kadarsh22.github.io/sebra_iclr25/.
中摘: 最近的研究表明，通过精细估计的虚假性（即存在虚假线索的程度）对样本进行排名，能显著提升偏见缓解的效果，相较于传统的将训练集二分为有偏和无偏的方法有明显优势。然而，这种虚假性排名需要人工监督。在本文中，我们提出了一种基于我们创新的自我引导偏见排名（简称Sebra）的去偏框架，该框架通过自动对各类别中的数据点按虚假性进行排名来减轻偏见（虚假关联）。Sebra利用了经验风险最小化（ERM）训练中的一个关键局部对称性——通过ERM学习样本的难易程度与样本的虚假性成反比；样本展现的虚假关联越少，学习就越困难，反之亦然。然而，在迭代过程中全局来看，ERM倾向于偏离这一对称性。Sebra动态地引导ERM修正这种偏差，有助于按照难度递增的顺序逐步学习属性，即按虚假性递减的顺序。因此，Sebra学习样本的序列自然提供了虚假性排名。我们使用这种细粒度的偏见特征描述，在对比学习框架中缓解来自多个来源的偏见。广泛的实验表明，Sebra在多个标准基准上，包括UrbanCars、BAR、CelebA和ImageNet-1K，始终超越之前最先进的无监督去偏技术。代码、预训练模型和训练日志可在https://kadarsh22.github.io/sebra_iclr25/获取。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.231] A Statistical Framework for Ranking LLM-based Chatbots
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 基于统计框架的大型语言模型聊天机器人排名方法
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Siavash Ameli;Siyuan Zhuang;Ion Stoica;Michael W. Mahoney;
机构: International Computer Science Institute;;University of California, Berkeley;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28215
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.18407
英摘: Large language models (LLMs) have transformed natural language processing, with frameworks like Chatbot Arena providing pioneering platforms for evaluating these models. By facilitating millions of pairwise comparisons based on human judgments, Chatbot Arena has become a cornerstone in LLM evaluation, offering rich datasets for ranking models in open-ended conversational tasks. Building upon this foundation, we propose a statistical framework that incorporates key advancements to address specific challenges in pairwise comparison analysis. First, we introduce a factored tie model that enhances the ability to handle ties -- an integral aspect of human-judged comparisons -- significantly improving the model's fit to observed data. Second, we extend the framework to model covariance between competitors, enabling deeper insights into performance relationships and facilitating intuitive groupings into performance tiers. Third, we resolve optimization challenges arising from parameter non-uniqueness by introducing novel constraints, ensuring stable and interpretable parameter estimation. Through rigorous evaluation and extensive experimentation, our framework demonstrates substantial improvements over existing methods in modeling pairwise comparison data. To support reproducibility and practical adoption, we release leaderbot, an open-source Python package implementing our models and analyses.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLM）已经革新了自然语言处理领域，像Chatbot Arena这样的框架提供了开创性的平台来评估这些模型。通过促进基于人类判断的数百万次两两比较，Chatbot Arena已成为LLM评估的基石，为开放式对话任务中的模型排名提供了丰富的数据集。在此基础上，我们提出了一种统计框架，结合了关键进展以解决两两比较分析中的特定挑战。首先，我们引入了一个因子平局模型，增强了处理平局的能力——这是人类判断比较的一个重要方面——显著提高了模型对观察数据的拟合度。其次，我们将框架扩展以建模竞争对手之间的协方差，使我们能够深入了解性能关系，并直观地将它们分组到不同的性能层级。第三，我们通过引入新的约束解决了因参数非唯一性引起的优化挑战，确保了稳定和可解释的参数估计。经过严格的评估和广泛的实验，我们的框架在建模两两比较数据方面相较于现有方法展示了实质性的改进。为了支持可重复性和实际应用，我们发布了leaderbot，这是一个开源的Python包，实现了我们的模型和分析。

这个包可以帮助研究人员和开发者更好地理解和应用大型语言模型在对话场景下的表现，同时也促进了该领域的透明度和协作。通过使用leaderbot，用户可以轻松地对不同模型进行对比分析，识别出哪些模型在特定任务或对话类型中表现更佳，从而为实际应用提供指导。此外，该框架还能够揭示模型之间的相关性和差异，为进一步研究和模型改进提供了有价值的见解。总之，这一统计框架和相应的开源工具为自然语言处理社区提供了一个强大的资源，推动了对话AI的发展和进步。

[No.232] SLoPe: Double-Pruned Sparse Plus Lazy Low-Rank Adapter Pretraining of LLMs
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: SLoPe：双剪枝稀疏增强和懒惰低秩适配器预训练的大语言模型
类别: other topics in machine learning (i.e., none of the above)
作者: Mohammad Mozaffari;Amir Yazdanbakhsh;Zhao Zhang;Maryam Mehri Dehnavi;
机构: University of Toronto;Google;Rutgers University;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28497
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.16325
英摘: We propose SLoPe, a Double-Pruned Sparse Plus Lazy Low-rank Adapter Pretraining method for LLMs that improves the accuracy of sparse LLMs while accelerating their pretraining and inference and reducing their memory footprint. Sparse pretraining of LLMs reduces the accuracy of the model, to overcome this, prior work uses dense models during fine-tuning. SLoPe improves the accuracy of sparsely pretrained models by adding low-rank adapters in the final 1% iterations of pretraining without adding significant overheads to the model pretraining and inference. In addition, SLoPe uses a double-pruned backward pass formulation that prunes the transposed weight matrix using N:M sparsity structures to enable an accelerated sparse backward pass. SLoPe accelerates the training and inference of models with billions of parameters up to $1.25\times$ and $1.54\times$ respectively (OPT-33B and OPT-66B) while reducing their memory usage by up to $0.63\times$ and $0.61\times$ for training and inference respectively.
中摘: 我们提出了一种名为SLoPe的方法，即双剪枝稀疏加懒惰低秩适配器预训练方法，专为大型语言模型(LLMs)设计。该方法在提高稀疏LLMs的准确性的同时，加速了它们的预训练和推理过程，并减少了内存占用。大型语言模型的稀疏预训练会降低模型的准确性，为了克服这一问题，先前的研究在微调阶段使用了密集型模型。SLoPe通过在预训练的最后1%迭代中添加低秩适配器来提升稀疏预训练模型的准确性，而不会给模型的预训练和推理带来显著的额外负担。此外，SLoPe采用了一种双剪枝反向传播公式，利用N:M稀疏结构对转置权重矩阵进行剪枝，以实现加速的稀疏反向传播。SLoPe在不影响模型准确性的情况下，将拥有数十亿参数的模型的训练和推理速度分别提高了最多1.25倍和1.54倍（针对OPT-33B和OPT-66B），同时将训练和推理的内存使用量分别降低了最多0.63倍和0.61倍。

[No.233] Pareto Low-Rank Adapters: Efficient Multi-Task Learning with Preferences
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 帕累托低秩适配器：具有偏好性的高效多任务学习
类别: transfer learning, meta learning, and lifelong learning
作者: Nikolaos Dimitriadis;Pascal Frossard;François Fleuret;
机构: EPFL;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28684
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.08056
英摘: Multi-task trade-offs in machine learning can be addressed via Pareto Front Learning (PFL) methods that parameterize the Pareto Front (PF) with a single model. PFL permits to select the desired operational point during inference, contrary to traditional Multi-Task Learning (MTL) that optimizes for a single trade-off decided prior to training. However, recent PFL methodologies suffer from limited scalability, slow convergence, and excessive memory requirements, while exhibiting inconsistent mappings from preference to objective space. We introduce PaLoRA, a novel parameter-efficient method that addresses these limitations in two ways. First, we augment any neural network architecture with task-specific low-rank adapters and continuously parameterize the PF in their convex hull. Our approach steers the original model and the adapters towards learning general and task-specific features, respectively. Second, we propose a deterministic sampling schedule of preference vectors that reinforces this division of labor, enabling faster convergence and strengthening the validity of the mapping from preference to objective space throughout training. Our experiments show that PaLoRA outperforms state-of-the-art MTL and PFL baselines across various datasets, scales to large networks, reducing the memory overhead $23.8-31.7$ times compared with competing PFL baselines in scene understanding benchmarks.
中摘: 在机器学习中，多任务之间的权衡可以通过帕累托前沿学习（PFL）方法来解决，这些方法使用单一模型参数化帕累托前沿（PF）。与传统的多任务学习（MTL）不同，后者在训练前就决定了单一的权衡，PFL允许在推理过程中选择所需的操作点。然而，近期的PFL方法存在可扩展性有限、收敛速度慢以及内存需求过高的问题，并且从偏好空间到目标空间的映射不稳定。我们引入了PaLoRA，这是一种新颖且参数效率高的方法，通过两种方式解决了这些限制。首先，我们在任何神经网络架构中加入任务特定的低秩适配器，并在其凸包内连续参数化PF。我们的方法引导原始模型和适配器分别学习通用特征和任务特定特征。其次，我们提出了一种确定性的偏好向量采样计划，这加强了这种劳动分工，使收敛速度加快，并在训练过程中强化了从偏好空间到目标空间映射的有效性。我们的实验表明，PaLoRA在多个数据集上超越了最先进的MTL和PFL基线，在场景理解基准测试中，相比竞争的PFL基线，PaLoRA将内存开销降低了23.8至31.7倍，同时能够扩展到大型网络。

[No.234] R-Sparse: Rank-Aware Activation Sparsity for Efficient LLM Inference
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: R-稀疏：基于秩的激活稀疏性以实现高效的LLM推理
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Zhenyu Zhang;Zechun Liu;Yuandong Tian;Harshit Khaitan;Zhangyang Wang;Steven Li;
机构: University of Texas at Austin;;Meta;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30686
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.19449
英摘: Large Language Models (LLMs), while demonstrating remarkable capabilities across various applications, present significant challenges during inference due to their substantial model size, especially when deployed on edge devices. Activation sparsity offers a promising solution to reduce computation and memory movement, enabling more efficient inference, particularly for small-batch on-device applications. However, current approaches face limitations with non-ReLU activation function, which are foundational to most advanced LLMs, or require heavy continual training. Additionally, the difficulty in predicting active channels and limited achievable sparsity ratios constrain the effectiveness of activation sparsity-based methods. In this paper, we introduce R-Sparse, a training-free activation sparsity approach capable of achieving high sparsity levels in advanced LLMs. We conducted two preliminary investigations into how different components contribute to the output within a single linear layer and found two key observations: (i) the non-sparse components of the input function can be regarded as a few bias terms, and (ii) The full computation can be effectively approximated by an appropriate combination of input channels and weight singular values. Building on this, we replace the linear layers in LLMs with a rank-aware sparse inference method that leverages the sparsity of input channels and singular value components, eliminating the need for active channel prediction like the output sparsity based approaches. Experiments on Llama-2/3 and Mistral models across ten diverse tasks demonstrate that R-Sparse achieves comparable performance at 50% model-level sparsity, resulting in a significant 43% end-to-end efficient improvements with customized kernels.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLM）在各种应用中展现出卓越的能力，但其庞大的模型尺寸在推理过程中带来了显著挑战，尤其是在边缘设备上部署时。激活稀疏性提供了一个有前景的解决方案，可以减少计算和内存移动，从而实现更高效的推理，特别适用于小批量的设备端应用。然而，当前的方法在非ReLU激活函数方面存在局限，这些函数对于大多数先进的LLM是基础性的，或者需要大量的持续训练。此外，预测活跃通道的难度和可达到的稀疏比限制了基于激活稀疏性的方法的有效性。在这篇论文中，我们引入了R-Sparse，这是一种无需训练的激活稀疏性方法，能够在先进的LLM中实现高稀疏水平。我们对单个线性层内不同组件如何贡献输出进行了两项初步研究，并发现了两个关键观察：(i) 输入函数的非稀疏部分可以被视为少量的偏置项，以及 (ii) 整个计算可以通过输入通道和权重奇异值的适当组合有效近似。基于此，我们将LLM中的线性层替换为一种基于秩感知的稀疏推理方法，利用输入通道和奇异值成分的稀疏性，消除了像基于输出稀疏性的方法那样需要预测活跃通道的需求。在Llama-2/3和Mistral模型上跨十个多样任务的实验表明，R-Sparse在50%的模型级稀疏度下实现了与原模型相当的性能，通过定制内核带来了显著的43%端到端效率提升。

[No.235] LoRanPAC: Low-rank Random Features and Pre-trained Models for Bridging Theory and Practice in Continual Learning
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: LoRanPAC：低秩随机特征与预训练模型在连续学习中理论与实践的桥梁
类别: transfer learning, meta learning, and lifelong learning
作者: Liangzu Peng;Juan Elenter;Joshua Agterberg;Alejandro Ribeiro;Rene Vidal;
机构: University of Pennsylvania;Spotify;University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;Amazon;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29087
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.00645
英摘: The goal of continual learning (CL) is to train a model that can solve multiple tasks presented sequentially. Recent CL approaches have achieved strong performance by leveraging large pre-trained models that generalize well to downstream tasks. However, such methods lack theoretical guarantees, making them prone to unexpected failures. Conversely, principled CL approaches often fail to achieve competitive performance. In this work, we aim to bridge this gap between theory and practice by designing a simple CL method that is theoretically sound and highly performant. Specifically, we lift pre-trained features into a higher dimensional space and formulate an over-parametrized minimum-norm least-squares problem. We find that the lifted features are highly ill-conditioned, potentially leading to large training errors (numerical instability) and increased generalization errors. We address these challenges by continually truncating the singular value decomposition of the lifted features. Our approach, termed LoRanPAC, is stable with respect to the choice of hyperparameters, can handle hundreds of tasks, and outperforms state-of-the-art CL methods on multiple datasets. Importantly, our method satisfies a recurrence relation throughout its continual learning process, which allows us to prove it maintains small training and test errors by appropriately truncating a fraction of SVD factors. This results in a stable continual learning method with strong empirical performance and theoretical guarantees. Code available: https://github.com/liangzu/loranpac.
中摘: 持续学习（CL）的目标是训练一个模型，能够解决连续呈现的多个任务。最近的CL方法通过利用大型预训练模型取得了优异的表现，这些模型能很好地泛化到下游任务。然而，这类方法缺乏理论保障，使得它们容易出现意外的失败。相反，基于原理的CL方法往往无法达到竞争性的性能。在本文中，我们旨在弥合理论与实践之间的差距，设计一种既理论上严谨又高度有效的简单CL方法。具体来说，我们将预训练特征提升到更高维度的空间，并将问题表述为过度参数化的最小范数最小二乘问题。我们发现，提升后的特征高度不适定，可能导致较大的训练误差（数值不稳定性）和泛化误差增加。我们通过持续截断提升特征的奇异值分解来应对这些挑战。我们的方法，称为LoRanPAC，在超参数选择上稳定，可以处理数百个任务，并在多个数据集上超越了最先进的CL方法。重要的是，我们的方法在其持续学习过程中满足递推关系，这使我们能够证明通过适当截断一部分SVD因子，它可以保持较小的训练和测试误差。这导致了一个具有强大实证表现和理论保障的稳定持续学习方法。代码可获取：https://github.com/liangzu/loranpac。

[No.236] Decoupling Angles and Strength in Low-rank Adaptation
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 低秩适应中的角度与强度解耦
类别: transfer learning, meta learning, and lifelong learning
作者: Massimo Bini;Leander Girrbach;Zeynep Akata;
机构: ;Helmholtz Zentrum München;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29327
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18225
英摘: Parameter-Efficient FineTuning (PEFT) methods have recently gained significant popularity thanks to the widespread availability of large-scale pretrained models. These methods allow for quick adaptation to downstream tasks with minimal computational cost. However, popular finetuning methods such as LoRA exhibit limited robustness when it comes to hyperparameter choices or extended training regimes, preventing optimal out-of-the-box performance. In contrast, bounded approaches, such as ETHER, provide greater robustness but are limited to extremely low-rank adaptations and fixed-strength transformations, reducing their adaptation expressive power. In this work, we propose Decoupled Low-rank Adaptation (DeLoRA), a novel finetuning method that normalizes and scales learnable low-rank matrices. By bounding the distance of the transformation, DeLoRA effectively decouples the angular learning from the adaptation strength, enhancing robustness without compromising performance. Through evaluations on subject-driven image generation, natural language understanding, and instruction tuning, we show that DeLoRA matches or surpasses performance of competing PEFT methods, while exhibiting stronger robustness. Code is available at https://github.com/ExplainableML/DeLoRA.
中摘: 最近，由于大规模预训练模型的广泛应用，参数高效微调（PEFT）方法获得了显著的流行。这些方法能够以最小的计算成本快速适应下游任务。然而，像LoRA这样的流行微调方法在超参数选择或扩展训练方案方面表现出有限的鲁棒性，阻碍了开箱即用性能的最优化。相比之下，受限方法如ETHER提供了更大的鲁棒性，但局限于极低秩的适应性和固定强度的转换，降低了它们的适应表达能力。在这项工作中，我们提出了解耦低秩适应（DeLoRA），一种新颖的微调方法，它对可学习的低秩矩阵进行归一化和缩放。通过限制转换的距离，DeLoRA有效地将角度学习与适应强度解耦，从而在不牺牲性能的情况下增强鲁棒性。通过对主题驱动的图像生成、自然语言理解和指令微调的评估，我们证明DeLoRA匹配甚至超越了竞争PEFT方法的性能，同时表现出更强的鲁棒性。代码可在https://github.com/ExplainableML/DeLoRA获取。

[No.237] Dynamic Low-Rank Sparse Adaptation for Large Language Models
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 动态低秩稀疏适应性调整大型语言模型
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Weizhong Huang;Yuxin Zhang;Xiawu Zheng;Liuyang;Jing Lin;Yiwu Yao;Rongrong Ji;
机构: Xiamen University;Huawei;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28352
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.14816
英摘: Despite the efficacy of network sparsity in alleviating the deployment strain of Large Language Models (LLMs), it endures significant performance degradation. Applying Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to fine-tune the sparse LLMs offers an intuitive approach to counter this predicament, while it holds shortcomings include: 1) The inability to integrate LoRA weights into sparse LLMs post-training, and 2) Insufficient performance recovery at high sparsity ratios. In this paper, we introduce dynamic Low-rank Sparse Adaptation (LoSA), a novel method that seamlessly integrates low-rank adaptation into LLM sparsity within a unified framework, thereby enhancing the performance of sparse LLMs without increasing the inference latency. In particular, LoSA dynamically sparsifies the LoRA outcomes based on the corresponding sparse weights during fine-tuning, thus guaranteeing that the LoRA module can be integrated into the sparse LLMs post-training. Besides, LoSA leverages Representation Mutual Information (RMI) as an indicator to determine the importance of layers, thereby efficiently determining the layer-wise sparsity rates during fine-tuning. Predicated on this, LoSA adjusts the rank of the LoRA module based on the variability in layer-wise reconstruction errors, allocating an appropriate fine-tuning for each layer to reduce the output discrepancies between dense and sparse LLMs. Extensive experiments tell that LoSA can efficiently boost the efficacy of sparse LLMs within a few hours, without introducing any additional inferential burden. For example, LoSA reduced the perplexity of sparse LLaMA-2-7B by 68.73 and increased zero-shot accuracy by 16.32$\%$, achieving a 2.60$\times$ speedup on CPU and 2.23$\times$ speedup on GPU, requiring only 45 minutes of fine-tuning on a single NVIDIA A100 80GB GPU. Code is available at https://github.com/wzhuang-xmu/LoSA.
中摘: 尽管网络稀疏性在减轻大型语言模型（LLM）部署压力方面具有显著效果，但它仍面临性能显著下降的问题。将低秩适应（LoRA）应用于微调稀疏LLM提供了一种直观的方法来应对这一困境，但其缺点包括：1）无法在训练后将LoRA权重整合到稀疏LLM中，以及2）在高稀疏比下性能恢复不足。在这篇论文中，我们提出了动态低秩稀疏适应（LoSA），一种创新方法，它在一个统一框架内无缝地将低秩适应融入LLM的稀疏性中，从而在不增加推理延迟的情况下提升稀疏LLM的性能。具体而言，LoSA在微调过程中根据相应的稀疏权重动态稀释LoRA的结果，确保训练后LoRA模块可以整合到稀疏LLM中。此外，LoSA利用表示互信息（RMI）作为指标来确定层的重要性，从而在微调期间有效地确定每层的稀疏率。基于此，LoSA根据层间重建误差的变化调整LoRA模块的秩，为每一层分配适当的微调以减少密集和稀疏LLM之间的输出差异。广泛的实验表明，LoSA可以在几小时内有效提升稀疏LLM的效能，而不引入任何额外的推理负担。例如，LoSA将稀疏LLaMA-2-7B的困惑度降低了68.73，并提高了零样本准确率16.32%，在CPU上实现了2.60倍的速度提升，在GPU上实现了2.23倍的速度提升，仅需在单个NVIDIA A100 80GB GPU上进行45分钟的微调。代码可在https://github.com/wzhuang-xmu/LoSA获取。

[No.238] Attention in Large Language Models Yields Efficient Zero-Shot Re-Rankers
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 大型语言模型中的注意力机制产生了高效的零样本重排序器
类别: applications to computer vision, audio, language, and other modalities
作者: Shijie Chen;Bernal Jimenez Gutierrez;Yu Su;
机构: Ohio State University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/27696
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.02642
英摘: Information retrieval (IR) systems have played a vital role in modern digital life and have cemented their continued usefulness in this new era of generative AI via retrieval-augmented generation. With strong language processing capabilities and remarkable versatility, large language models (LLMs) have become popular choices for zero-shot re-ranking in IR systems. So far, LLM-based re-ranking methods rely on strong generative capabilities, which restricts their use to either specialized or powerful proprietary models. Given these restrictions, we ask: is autoregressive generation necessary and optimal for LLMs to perform re-ranking? We hypothesize that there are abundant signals relevant to re-ranking within LLMs that might not be used to their full potential via generation. To more directly leverage such signals, we propose in-context re-ranking (ICR), a novel method that leverages the change in attention pattern caused by the search query for accurate and efficient re-ranking. To mitigate the intrinsic biases in LLMs, we propose a calibration method using a content-free query. Due to the absence of generation, ICR only requires two ($O(1)$) forward passes to re-rank $N$ documents, making it substantially more efficient than generative re-ranking methods that require at least $O(N)$ forward passes. Our novel design also enables ICR to be applied to any LLM without specialized training while guaranteeing a well-formed ranking. Extensive experiments with two popular open-weight LLMs on standard single-hop and multi-hop information retrieval benchmarks show that ICR outperforms RankGPT while cutting the latency by more than 60% in practice. Through detailed analyses, we show that ICR's performance is specially strong on tasks that require more complex re-ranking signals. Our findings call for further exploration on novel ways of utilizing open-weight LLMs beyond text generation.
中摘: 信息检索（IR）系统在现代数字生活中扮演了至关重要的角色，并通过检索增强生成，在这个生成式AI的新时代巩固了其持续的实用性。凭借强大的语言处理能力和卓越的多功能性，大型语言模型（LLM）已成为IR系统中零样本重排序的热门选择。迄今为止，基于LLM的重排序方法依赖于强大的生成能力，这限制了它们的应用，仅限于专门或强大的专有模型。鉴于这些限制，我们提出疑问：对于LLM执行重排序而言，自回归生成是否必要且最优？我们假设LLM内部存在大量与重排序相关的信号，但通过生成方式可能未能充分利用这些信号。为了更直接地利用此类信号，我们提出了基于上下文的重排序（ICR），这是一种新颖的方法，它利用由搜索查询引起的注意力模式变化进行准确和高效的重排序。为了减轻LLM固有的偏见，我们提出了一种使用内容无关查询的校准方法。由于没有生成过程，ICR仅需两次($O(1)$)前向传播即可对$N$个文档进行重排序，这使其比需要至少$O(N)$次前向传播的生成式重排序方法效率高得多。我们创新的设计还使ICR能够应用于任何LLM，无需专门训练，同时保证排名的合理性。通过使用两个流行的开源权重LLM在标准的单跳和多跳信息检索基准上的广泛实验，我们发现ICR在实际应用中不仅超越了RankGPT，而且将延迟减少了超过60%。通过详细分析，我们展示了ICR在需要更复杂重排序信号的任务上表现尤为出色。我们的发现呼吁进一步探索利用开源权重LLM的创新方式，而不仅仅局限于文本生成。

[No.239] Batched Low-Rank Adaptation of Foundation Models
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: rank
标题: 批量低秩适应的基础模型
类别: generative models
作者: Yeming Wen;Swarat Chaudhuri;
机构: University of Texas at Austin;
类型: Oral
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/17508
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.05677
英摘: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has recently gained attention for fine-tuning foundation models by incorporating trainable low-rank matrices, thereby reducing the number of trainable parameters. While LoRA offers numerous advantages, its applicability for real-time serving to a diverse and global user base is constrained by its incapability to handle multiple task-specific adapters efficiently. This imposes a performance bottleneck in scenarios requiring personalized, task-specific adaptations for each incoming request. To mitigate this constraint, we introduce Fast LoRA (FLoRA), a framework in which each input example in a minibatch can be associated with its unique low-rank adaptation weights, allowing for efficient batching of heterogeneous requests. We empirically demonstrate that FLoRA retains the performance merits of LoRA, showcasing competitive results on the MultiPL-E code generation benchmark spanning over 8 languages and a multilingual speech recognition task across 6 languages.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）最近因其通过引入可训练的低秩矩阵来微调基础模型而受到关注，这种方法减少了需要训练的参数数量。尽管LoRA提供了诸多优势，但其在为全球多样化用户群进行实时服务时的应用受到了限制，因为它无法有效地处理多个任务特定的适配器。这在需要对每个传入请求进行个性化、任务特定适应的情况下造成了性能瓶颈。为了解决这一限制，我们引入了快速LoRA（FLoRA），一个框架，在这个框架中，小批量数据中的每个输入示例都可以与其独特的低秩适应权重相关联，从而允许异构请求的有效批处理。我们通过实证展示了FLoRA保留了LoRA的性能优势，在涵盖8种语言的MultiPL-E代码生成基准和跨越6种语言的多语言语音识别任务上展现出了有竞争力的结果。

[No.241] The Truth is in There: Improving Reasoning in Language Models with Layer-Selective Rank Reduction
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: rank
标题: 真相就在其中：通过层选择性秩减少提高语言模型的推理能力
类别: generative models
作者: Pratyusha Sharma;Jordan T. Ash;Dipendra Misra;
机构: Massachusetts Institute of Technology;Microsoft;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/17798
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.13558
英摘: Transformer-based Large Language Models (LLMs) have become a fixture in modern machine learning. Correspondingly, significant resources are allocated towards research that aims to further advance this technology, typically resulting in models of increasing size that are trained on increasing amounts of data. This work, however, demonstrates the surprising result that it is often possible to significantly improve the performance of LLMs by selectively removing higher-order components of their weight matrices. This simple intervention, which we call LAyer-SElective Rank reduction (LASER), can be done on a model after training has completed, and requires no additional parameters or data. We show extensive experiments demonstrating the generality of this finding across language models and datasets, and provide in-depth analyses offering insights into both when LASER is effective and the mechanism by which it operates.
中摘: 基于Transformer的大型语言模型（LLMs）已成为现代机器学习的标配。相应地，大量资源被分配用于旨在进一步推进这项技术的研究，通常这会导致训练数据量不断增加的更大规模模型的产生。然而，本研究却展示了令人惊讶的结果：通过有选择性地移除权重矩阵中的高阶成分，往往可以显著提升LLMs的性能。我们将其称为LAyer-SElective Rank reduction（LASER）的这一简单干预措施，可以在模型训练完成后进行，无需额外参数或数据。我们展示了广泛的实验，证明了这一发现的普遍性，涵盖了不同语言模型和数据集，并提供了深入分析，揭示了LASER在何时有效以及其运作机制的见解。

[No.242] LQ-LoRA: Low-rank plus Quantized Matrix Decomposition for Efficient Language Model Finetuning
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: rank
标题: LQ-LoRA：低秩矩阵分解加量化矩阵分解以实现高效的语言模型微调
类别: general machine learning (i.e., none of the above)
作者: Han Guo;Philip Greengard;Eric Xing;Yoon Kim;
机构: Carnegie Mellon University;Massachusetts Institute of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/17436
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.12023
英摘: We propose a simple approach for memory-efficient adaptation of pretrained language models. Our approach uses an iterative algorithm to decompose each pretrained matrix into a high-precision low-rank component and a memory-efficient quantized component. During finetuning, the quantized component remains fixed and only the low-rank component is updated. We present an integer linear programming formulation of the quantization component which enables dynamic configuration of quantization parameters (e.g., bit-width, block size) for each matrix given an overall target memory budget. We further explore a data-aware version of the algorithm which uses an approximation of the Fisher information matrix to weight the reconstruction objective during matrix decomposition. Experiments on finetuning RoBERTa and LLaMA-2 (7B and 70B) demonstrate that our low-rank plus quantized matrix decomposition approach (LQ-LoRA) outperforms strong QLoRA and GPTQ-LoRA baselines and enables aggressive quantization to sub-3 bits with only minor performance degradations. When finetuned on a language modeling calibration dataset, LQ-LoRA can also be used for model compression; in this setting our 2.75-bit LLaMA-2-70B model (which has 2.85 bits on average when including the low-rank components and requires 27GB of GPU memory) performs respectably compared to the 16-bit baseline.
中摘: 我们提出了一种简单的预训练语言模型高效内存适应方法。我们的方法使用迭代算法将每个预训练矩阵分解为高精度低秩组件和内存高效的量化组件。在微调过程中，量化组件保持固定，仅更新低秩组件。我们提出了量化组件的整数线性规划公式，这使得可以根据总体目标内存预算动态配置每个矩阵的量化参数（例如，位宽、块大小）。我们进一步探索了数据感知版本的算法，该算法利用Fisher信息矩阵的近似值，在矩阵分解期间对重构目标进行加权。在RoBERTa和LLaMA-2（7B和70B）的微调实验中证明，我们的低秩加量化矩阵分解方法（LQ-LoRA）优于强大的QLoRA和GPTQ-LoRA基线，并且能够激进地量化至3比特以下，仅有轻微的性能下降。当在语言建模校准数据集上进行微调时，LQ-LoRA也可用于模型压缩；在这种情况下，我们2.75比特的LLaMA-2-70B模型（包括低秩组件后平均有2.85比特，需要27GB的GPU内存）与16比特基线相比表现良好。

[No.243] MT-Ranker: Reference-free machine translation evaluation by inter-system ranking
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: rank
标题: 参考自由机器翻译评估通过系统间排名进行
类别: general machine learning (i.e., none of the above)
作者: Ibraheem Muhammad Moosa;Rui Zhang;Wenpeng Yin;
机构: Pennsylvania State University;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/18633
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.17099
英摘: Traditionally, Machine Translation (MT) Evaluation has been treated as a regression problem -- producing an absolute translation-quality score. This approach has two limitations: i) the scores lack interpretability, and human annotators struggle with giving consistent scores; ii) most scoring methods are based on (reference, translation) pairs, limiting their applicability in real-world scenarios where references are absent. In practice, we often care about whether a new MT system is better or worse than some competitors. In addition, reference-free MT evaluation is increasingly practical and necessary. Unfortunately, these two practical considerations have yet to be jointly explored. In this work, we formulate the reference-free MT evaluation into a pairwise ranking problem. Given the source sentence and a pair of translations, our system predicts which translation is better. In addition to proposing this new formulation, we further show that this new paradigm can demonstrate superior correlation with human judgments by merely using indirect supervision from natural language inference and weak supervision from our synthetic data. In the context of reference-free evaluation, MT-Ranker, trained without any human annotations, achieves state-of-the-art results on the WMT Shared Metrics Task benchmarks DARR20, MQM20, and MQM21. On a more challenging benchmark, ACES, which contains fine-grained evaluation criteria such as addition, omission, and mistranslation errors, MT-Ranker marks state-of-the-art against reference-free as well as reference-based baselines.
中摘: 传统上，机器翻译（MT）评估被视为回归问题——生成绝对的翻译质量分数。这种方法存在两个局限性：i）分数缺乏可解释性，人工标注者难以给出一致的评分；ii）大多数评分方法基于(参考译文，翻译结果)对，这限制了它们在现实场景中的应用，因为在这些场景中往往没有参考译文。实际上，我们经常关心的是一个新的MT系统是否优于某些竞争对手。此外，无参考的MT评估正变得越来越实用和必要。不幸的是，这两个实际考虑尚未被联合探索。在这项工作中，我们将无参考的MT评估转化为一个成对排序问题。给定源句和一对翻译结果，我们的系统预测哪个翻译更好。除了提出这一新公式，我们还进一步展示了这种新范式仅通过自然语言推理的间接监督和我们合成数据的弱监督就能与人工判断表现出更高的相关性。在无参考评估的背景下，无需任何人工注释训练的MT-Ranker，在WMT共享指标任务基准DARR20、MQM20和MQM21上取得了最先进的结果。在一个更具挑战性的基准ACES上，其中包含详细的评价标准，如增删和误译错误，MT-Ranker在无参考以及有参考的基线上均标记为最先进水平。

[No.244] Statistically Optimal $K$-means Clustering via Nonnegative Low-rank Semidefinite Programming
来源: ICLR 2024

标题: 统计最优 $K$-均值聚类通过非负低秩半定规划

作者: Yubo Zhuang;Xiaohui Chen;Yun Yang;Richard Y. Zhang;
机构: University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;University of Southern California;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.18436
英摘: $K$-means clustering is a widely used machine learning method for identifying patterns in large datasets. Recently, semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxations have been proposed for solving the $K$-means optimization problem, which enjoy strong statistical optimality guarantees. However, the prohibitive cost of implementing an SDP solver renders these guarantees inaccessible to practical datasets. In contrast, nonnegative matrix factorization (NMF) is a simple clustering algorithm widely used by machine learning practitioners, but it lacks a solid statistical underpinning and theoretical guarantees. In this paper, we consider an NMF-like algorithm that solves a nonnegative low-rank restriction of the SDP-relaxed $K$-means formulation using a nonconvex Burer--Monteiro factorization approach. The resulting algorithm is as simple and scalable as state-of-the-art NMF algorithms while also enjoying the same strong statistical optimality guarantees as the SDP. In our experiments, we observe that our algorithm achieves significantly smaller mis-clustering errors compared to the existing state-of-the-art while maintaining scalability.
中摘: $k$-均值聚类是一种广泛使用的机器学习方法，用于在大型数据集中识别模式。最近，有人提出使用半正定规划（SDP）松弛法来解决$k$-均值优化问题，这种方法具有强大的统计最优性保证。然而，实现SDP求解器的高昂成本使得这些保证对于实际数据集难以触及。相比之下，非负矩阵分解（NMF）是一种简单且被机器学习实践者广泛使用的聚类算法，但它缺乏坚实的统计基础和理论保证。在这篇论文中，我们考虑了一种类似NMF的算法，它使用非凸Burer-Monteiro因子分解方法，解决了SDP松弛$k$-均值公式中的非负低秩限制。得到的算法与最先进的NMF算法一样简单且可扩展，同时又具有与SDP相同的强大统计最优性保证。在我们的实验中，我们观察到，与现有最先进算法相比，我们的算法实现了显著较小的错误聚类误差，同时保持了可扩展性。

[No.245] Are Transformers with One Layer Self-Attention Using Low-Rank Weight Matrices Universal Approximators?
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: rank
标题: 具有单层自注意力且使用低秩权重矩阵的Transformer是否是通用逼近器？

作者: Tokio Kajitsuka;Issei Sato;

英摘: Existing analyses of the expressive capacity of Transformer models have required excessively deep layers for data memorization, leading to a discrepancy with the Transformers actually used in practice. This is primarily due to the interpretation of the softmax function as an approximation of the hardmax function. 中摘: 现有对Transformer模型表达能力的分析在数据记忆方面要求过深的层次，导致与实际应用中的Transformers存在差异。这主要是由于将softmax函数解释为hardmax函数的近似。通过阐明softmax函数与Boltzmann算子之间的联系，我们证明了一个具有低秩权重矩阵的自注意力单层具备完全捕捉整个输入序列上下文的能力。因此，我们表明一层和单头的Transformers对于有限样本具有记忆能力，并且由一个自注意力层和两个前馈神经网络组成的Transformers是在紧致域上连续置换等变函数的通用逼近器。

[No.247] The Expressive Power of Low-Rank Adaptation
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: rank
标题: 低秩适应的表达能力
类别: learning theory
作者: Yuchen Zeng;Kangwook Lee;
机构: Microsoft;KRAFTON Inc.;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/17923
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.17513
英摘: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), a parameter-efficient fine-tuning method that leverages low-rank adaptation of weight matrices, has emerged as a prevalent technique for fine-tuning pre-trained models such as large language models and diffusion models. Despite its huge success in practice, the theoretical underpinnings of LoRA have largely remained unexplored. This paper takes the first step to bridge this gap by theoretically analyzing the expressive power of LoRA. We prove that, for fully connected neural networks, LoRA can adapt any model $f$ to accurately represent any smaller target model $\overline{f}$ if LoRA-rank $\geq(\text{width of }f) \times \frac{\text{depth of }\overline{f}}{\text{depth of }f}$. We also quantify the approximation error when LoRA-rank is lower than the threshold. For Transformer networks, we show any model can be adapted to a target model of the same size with rank-$(\frac{\text{embedding size}}{2})$ LoRA adapters.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）作为一种参数高效的微调方法，通过权重矩阵的低秩适应，已成为微调预训练模型（如大型语言模型和扩散模型）的流行技术。尽管在实践中取得了巨大成功，但LoRA的理论基础在很大程度上仍未被探索。本文首次尝试填补这一空白，通过理论分析来探讨LoRA的表现力。我们证明了对于全连接神经网络，如果LoRA秩≥(f的宽度)×(目标模型$\overline{f}$的深度)/(f的深度)，LoRA可以准确地将任何模型$f$适应为更小的目标模型$\overline{f}$。我们也量化了当LoRA秩低于阈值时的近似误差。对于Transformer网络，我们表明任何模型都可以通过秩-(embedding size/2)的LoRA适配器调整为目标模型的相同大小。

来源: ICLR 2024

英摘: Developing autonomous agents that can interact with changing environments is an open challenge in machine learning. Robustness is particularly important in these settings as agents are often fit offline on expert demonstrations but deployed online where they must generalize to the closed feedback loop within the environment. In this work, we explore the application of recurrent neural networks to tasks of this nature and understand how a parameterization of their recurrent connectivity influences robustness in closed-loop settings. Specifically, we represent the recurrent connectivity as a function of rank and sparsity and show both theoretically and empirically that modulating these two variables has desirable effects on network dynamics. The proposed low-rank, sparse connectivity induces an interpretable prior on the network that proves to be most amenable for a class of models known as closed-form continuous-time neural networks (CfCs). We find that CfCs with fewer parameters can outperform their full-rank, fully-connected counterparts in the online setting under distribution shift. This yields memory-efficient and robust agents while opening a new perspective on how we can modulate network dynamics through connectivity.

[No.249] QA-LoRA: Quantization-Aware Low-Rank Adaptation of Large Language Models
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: rank
标题: QA-LoRA：量化感知低秩大型语言模型适应技术
类别: unsupervised, self-supervised, semi-supervised, and supervised representation learning
作者: Yuhui Xu;Lingxi Xie;Xiaotao Gu;Xin Chen;Heng Chang;Hengheng Zhang;Zhengsu Chen;XIAOPENG ZHANG;Qi Tian;

英摘: Recently years have witnessed a rapid development of large language models (LLMs). Despite the strong ability in many language-understanding tasks, the heavy computational burden largely restricts the application of LLMs especially when one needs to deploy them onto edge devices. In this paper, we propose a quantization-aware low-rank adaptation (QA-LoRA) algorithm. The motivation lies in the imbalanced degrees of freedom of quantization and adaptation, and the solution is to use group-wise operators which increase the degree of freedom of quantization meanwhile decreasing that of adaptation. QA-LoRA is easily implemented with a few lines of code, and it equips the original LoRA with two-fold abilities: (i) during fine-tuning, the LLM's weights are quantized (e.g., into INT4) to reduce time and memory usage; (ii) after fine-tuning, the LLM and auxiliary weights are naturally integrated into a quantized model without loss of accuracy. We apply QA-LoRA to the LLaMA and LLaMA2 model families and validate its effectiveness in different fine-tuning datasets and downstream scenarios. Code will be made available at https://github.com/yuhuixu1993/qa-lora.
中摘: 近年来见证了大型语言模型（LLM）的迅速发展。尽管在许多语言理解任务上具有强大的能力，但沉重的计算负担极大地限制了LLM的应用，特别是在需要将其部署到边缘设备的情况下。在这篇论文中，我们提出了一种量化感知的低秩适应（QA-LoRA）算法。其动机在于量化和适应之间自由度的不平衡，解决方案是使用组级别运算符，这同时增加了量化的自由度并减少了适应的自由度。QA-LoRA可以用几行代码轻松实现，它为原始的LoRA赋予了两方面的能力：(i) 在微调期间，LLM的权重被量化（例如，量化为INT4），以减少时间和内存消耗；(ii) 微调后，LLM和辅助权重自然地整合到一个量化模型中，而不会损失准确性。我们将QA-LoRA应用于LLaMA和LLaMA2模型家族，并在不同的微调数据集和下游场景中验证了其有效性。代码将在https://github.com/yuhuixu1993/qa-lora上提供。

[No.250] Koopman-based generalization bound: New aspect for full-rank weights
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: rank
标题: 基于柯普曼的泛化界：全秩权重的新视角
类别: learning theory
作者: Yuka Hashimoto;Sho Sonoda;Isao Ishikawa;Atsushi Nitanda;Taiji Suzuki;
机构: NTT Corporation;Ehime University;Agency for Science, Technology and Research;University of Tokyo;RIKEN;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/18944
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2302.05825
英摘: We propose a new bound for generalization of neural networks using Koopman operators. Whereas most of existing works focus on low-rank weight matrices, we focus on full-rank weight matrices. Our bound is tighter than existing norm-based bounds when the condition numbers of weight matrices are small. Especially, it is completely independent of the width of the network if the weight matrices are orthogonal. Our bound does not contradict to the existing bounds but is a complement to the existing bounds. As supported by several existing empirical results, low-rankness is not the only reason for generalization. Furthermore, our bound can be combined with the existing bounds to obtain a tighter bound. Our result sheds new light on understanding generalization of neural networks with full-rank weight matrices, and it provides a connection between operator-theoretic analysis and generalization of neural networks.
中摘: 我们提出了一种使用Koopman算子来推断神经网络泛化能力的新界限。与大多数现有研究聚焦于低秩权重矩阵不同，我们关注的是满秩权重矩阵。当权重矩阵的条件数较小时，我们的界限比现有的基于范数的界限更紧。特别是，如果权重矩阵是正交的，该界限完全不依赖于网络的宽度。我们的界限并不与现有界限相冲突，而是对现有界限的一种补充。正如多项现有实证结果所支持的那样，低秩性并不是泛化的唯一原因。此外，我们的界限可以与现有界限结合，以获得更紧的界限。我们的成果为理解具有满秩权重矩阵的神经网络的泛化能力提供了新的视角，并在算子理论分析和神经网络泛化之间建立了联系。

[No.251] Beyond IID weights: sparse and low-rank deep Neural Networks are also Gaussian Processes
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: rank
标题: 超越IID权重：稀疏和低秩深度神经网络也是高斯过程
类别: general machine learning (i.e., none of the above)
作者: Thiziri Nait Saada;Alireza Naderi;Jared Tanner;
机构: University of Oxford;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/19584
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.16597
英摘: The infinitely wide neural network has been proven a useful and manageable mathematical model that enables the understanding of many phenomena appearing in deep learning. One example is the convergence of random deep networks to Gaussian processes that allows a rigorous analysis of the way the choice of activation function and network weights impacts the training dynamics. In this paper, we extend the seminal proof of Matthews et al. (2018) to a larger class of initial weight distributions (which we call PSEUDO-IID), including the established cases of IID and orthogonal weights, as well as the emerging low-rank and structured sparse settings celebrated for their computational speed-up benefits. We show that fully-connected and convolutional networks initialized with PSEUDO-IID distributions are all effectively equivalent up to their variance. Using our results, one can identify the Edge-of-Chaos for a broader class of neural networks and tune them at criticality in order to enhance their training. Moreover, they enable the posterior distribution of Bayesian Neural Networks to be tractable across these various initialization schemes.
中摘: 无限宽神经网络已被证明是一个有用且可管理的数学模型，它使我们能够理解出现在深度学习中的许多现象。一个例子是随机深层网络收敛到高斯过程，这允许我们严格分析激活函数的选择和网络权重如何影响训练动态。在这篇论文中，我们将Matthews等人（2018）的开创性证明扩展到了更广泛的初始权重分布类别（我们称之为PSEUDO-IID），包括已知的IID和正交权重情况，以及新兴的低秩和结构化稀疏设置，这些设置因其计算加速优势而受到关注。我们证明，使用PSEUDO-IID分布初始化的全连接和卷积网络在方差上都是实质等效的。利用我们的结果，可以为更广泛的神经网络类识别混沌边缘，并在临界点对其进行调整以增强训练。此外，它们使得在各种初始化方案下，贝叶斯神经网络的后验分布变得可处理。

[No.252] Zeroth-Order Optimization Meets Human Feedback: Provable Learning via Ranking Oracles
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: rank
标题: 零阶优化遇见人类反馈：可验证的学习通过排名或acles
类别: optimization
作者: Zhiwei Tang;Dmitry Rybin;Tsung-Hui Chang;
机构: Chinese University of Hong Kong;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/18570
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2303.03751
英摘: In this study, we delve into an emerging optimization challenge involving a black-box objective function that can only be gauged via a ranking oracle-a situation frequently encountered in real-world scenarios, especially when the function is evaluated by human judges. Such challenge is inspired from Reinforcement Learning with Human Feedback (RLHF), an approach recently employed to enhance the performance of Large Language Models (LLMs) using human guidance. We introduce ZO-RankSGD, an innovative zeroth-order optimization algorithm designed to tackle this optimization problem, accompanied by theoretical assurances. Our algorithm utilizes a novel rank-based random estimator to determine the descent direction and guarantees convergence to a stationary point. Moreover, ZO-RankSGD is readily applicable to policy optimization problems in Reinforcement Learning (RL), particularly when only ranking oracles for the episode reward are available. Last but not least, we demonstrate the effectiveness of ZO-RankSGD in a novel application: improving the quality of images generated by a diffusion generative model with human ranking feedback. Throughout experiments, we found that ZO-RankSGD can significantly enhance the detail of generated images with only a few rounds of human feedback. Overall, our work advances the field of zeroth-order optimization by addressing the problem of optimizing functions with only ranking feedback, and offers a new and effective approach for aligning Artificial Intelligence (AI) with human intentions.
中摘: 在本研究中，我们深入探讨了一个新兴的优化挑战，涉及一个只能通过排名神谕来评估的黑盒目标函数，这种情况在现实世界场景中经常遇到，尤其是在函数由人类裁判进行评价时。这种挑战灵感来源于最近使用人类反馈增强大型语言模型(LLM)性能的方法，即基于人类反馈的强化学习(RLHF)。我们引入了ZO-RankSGD，这是一种创新的零阶优化算法，旨在解决这一优化问题，并附有理论保证。我们的算法利用了一种新颖的基于排名的随机估计器来确定下降方向，并确保收敛至稳定点。此外，ZO-RankSGD可直接应用于强化学习(RL)中的策略优化问题，特别是在只有剧集奖励的排名神谕可用的情况下。最后但同样重要的是，我们在一个新应用中展示了ZO-RankSGD的有效性：利用人类排名反馈提高扩散生成模型生成图像的质量。实验中发现，ZO-RankSGD仅需几轮人类反馈即可显著提升生成图像的细节。总体而言，我们的工作通过解决仅依赖于排名反馈优化函数的问题，推动了零阶优化领域的发展，并为使人工智能(AI)与人类意图相一致提供了新的有效途径。

[No.254] Low Rank Matrix Completion via Robust Alternating Minimization in Nearly Linear Time
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: rank
标题: 低秩矩阵完成通过健壮交替最小化在接近线性时间内实现
类别: optimization
作者: Yuzhou Gu;Zhao Song;Junze Yin;Lichen Zhang;
机构: Institute for Advanced Study;Adobe;Boston University;Amazon;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/18801
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2302.11068
英摘: Given a matrix $M\in \mathbb{R}^{m\times n}$, the low rank matrix completion problem asks us to find a rank-$k$ approximation of $M$ as $UV^\top$ for $U\in \mathbb{R}^{m\times k}$ and $V\in \mathbb{R}^{n\times k}$ by only observing a few entries specified by a set of entries $Ω\subseteq [m]\times [n]$. In particular, we examine an approach that is widely used in practice -- the alternating minimization framework. Jain, Netrapalli, and Sanghavi [JNS13] showed that if $M$ has incoherent rows and columns, then alternating minimization provably recovers the matrix $M$ by observing a nearly linear in $n$ number of entries. While the sample complexity has been subsequently improved [GLZ17], alternating minimization steps are required to be computed exactly. This hinders the development of more efficient algorithms and fails to depict the practical implementation of alternating minimization, where the updates are usually performed approximately in favor of efficiency.
  In this paper, we take a major step towards a more efficient and error-robust alternating minimization framework. To this end, we develop an analytical framework for alternating minimization that can tolerate a moderate amount of errors caused by approximate updates. Moreover, our algorithm runs in time $\widetilde O(|Ω| k)$, which is nearly linear in the time to verify the solution while preserving the sample complexity. This improves upon all prior known alternating minimization approaches which require $\widetilde O(|Ω| k^2)$ time.
中摘: 给定一个矩阵 $M\in \mathbb{R}^{m\times n}$，低秩矩阵补全问题要求我们仅通过观察由一组条目$Ω\subseteq [m]\times [n]$指定的少量条目，找到$M$的秩为$k$的近似值$UV^\top$，其中$U\in \mathbb{R}^{m\times k}$和$V\in \mathbb{R}^{n\times k}$。具体而言，我们研究了一种在实践中广泛使用的方法——交替最小化框架。Jain、Netrapalli和Sanghavi [JNS13]证明了如果$M$具有不相干的行和列，那么通过观察接近线性于$n$数量级的条目，交替最小化可证明地恢复矩阵$M$。虽然样本复杂度已经被后续的研究[GLZ17]改进，但交替最小化步骤需要精确计算，这阻碍了更高效算法的发展，并且不能反映实际的交替最小化实现，在实际中更新通常为了效率而近似进行。

在这篇论文中，我们朝着更高效和容错的交替最小化框架迈出了重要一步。为此，我们开发了一个分析框架，可以容忍由于近似更新引起的适度错误。此外，我们的算法运行时间为$\widetilde O(|Ω| k)$，这在验证解决方案所需时间上几乎是线性的，同时保持了样本复杂度。这比所有已知的先前交替最小化方法都有所改进，那些方法需要$\widetilde O(|Ω| k^2)$的时间。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.255] Provably Efficient CVaR RL in Low-rank MDPs
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: rank
标题: 可验证高效的风险价值(RVaR)强化学习在低秩MDP中
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Yulai Zhao;Wenhao Zhan;Xiaoyan Hu;Ho-fung Leung;Farzan Farnia;Wen Sun;Jason D. Lee;
机构: Princeton University;Chinese University of Hong Kong;Cornell University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/19279
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.11965
英摘: We study risk-sensitive Reinforcement Learning (RL), where we aim to maximize the Conditional Value at Risk (CVaR) with a fixed risk tolerance $τ$. Prior theoretical work studying risk-sensitive RL focuses on the tabular Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) setting. To extend CVaR RL to settings where state space is large, function approximation must be deployed. We study CVaR RL in low-rank MDPs with nonlinear function approximation. Low-rank MDPs assume the underlying transition kernel admits a low-rank decomposition, but unlike prior linear models, low-rank MDPs do not assume the feature or state-action representation is known. We propose a novel Upper Confidence Bound (UCB) bonus-driven algorithm to carefully balance the interplay between exploration, exploitation, and representation learning in CVaR RL. We prove that our algorithm achieves a sample complexity of $\tilde{O}\left(\frac{H^7 A^2 d^4}{τ^2 ε^2}\right)$ to yield an $ε$-optimal CVaR, where $H$ is the length of each episode, $A$ is the capacity of action space, and $d$ is the dimension of representations. Computational-wise, we design a novel discretized Least-Squares Value Iteration (LSVI) algorithm for the CVaR objective as the planning oracle and show that we can find the near-optimal policy in a polynomial running time with a Maximum Likelihood Estimation oracle. To our knowledge, this is the first provably efficient CVaR RL algorithm in low-rank MDPs.
中摘: 我们研究风险敏感型强化学习（RL），目标是在固定的风险容忍度τ下最大化条件价值风险（CVaR）。先前理论研究聚焦于表格型马尔可夫决策过程（MDPs）设置下的风险敏感型RL。为了将CVaR RL扩展到状态空间较大的场景，必须采用函数逼近。我们在具有非线性函数逼近的低秩MDPs中研究CVaR RL。低秩MDPs假设底层转换核具有低秩分解，但与之前的线性模型不同，低秩MDPs不假设特征或状态动作表示已知。我们提出了一种新颖的上置信界（UCB）奖励驱动算法，以仔细平衡CVaR RL中的探索、利用和表示学习之间的相互作用。我们证明了我们的算法能够达到\(\tilde{O}\left(\frac{H^7 A^2 d^4}{τ^2 ε^2}\right)\)的样本复杂度，以获得ε-最优CVaR，其中\(H\)是每集的长度，\(A\)是动作空间的容量，而\(d\)是表示的维度。从计算角度来看，我们设计了一种新颖的离散最小二乘值迭代（LSVI）算法作为CVaR目标的规划预言，并证明了我们可以在多项式运行时间内找到近似最优策略，借助最大似然估计预言。据我们所知，这是第一个在低秩MDPs中被证明效率的CVaR RL算法。

[No.256] Adaptive Regularization of Representation Rank as an Implicit Constraint of Bellman Equation
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: rank
标题: 自适应表示秩正则化作为贝尔曼方程的一种隐式约束
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Qiang He;Tianyi Zhou;Meng Fang;Setareh Maghsudi;
机构: Ruhr-Universität Bochum;University of Maryland;Eindhoven University of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/18353
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.12754
英摘: Representation rank is an important concept for understanding the role of Neural Networks (NNs) in Deep Reinforcement learning (DRL), which measures the expressive capacity of value networks. Existing studies focus on unboundedly maximizing this rank; nevertheless, that approach would introduce overly complex models in the learning, thus undermining performance. Hence, fine-tuning representation rank presents a challenging and crucial optimization problem. To address this issue, we find a guiding principle for adaptive control of the representation rank. We employ the Bellman equation as a theoretical foundation and derive an upper bound on the cosine similarity of consecutive state-action pairs representations of value networks. We then leverage this upper bound to propose a novel regularizer, namely BEllman Equation-based automatic rank Regularizer (BEER). This regularizer adaptively regularizes the representation rank, thus improving the DRL agent's performance. We first validate the effectiveness of automatic control of rank on illustrative experiments. Then, we scale up BEER to complex continuous control tasks by combining it with the deterministic policy gradient method. Among 12 challenging DeepMind control tasks, BEER outperforms the baselines by a large margin. Besides, BEER demonstrates significant advantages in Q-value approximation. Our code is available at https://github.com/sweetice/BEER-ICLR2024.
中摘: 表示秩是在深度强化学习（DRL）中理解神经网络（NNs）作用的重要概念，它衡量了价值网络的表现能力。现有的研究集中在无界地最大化这一秩；然而，这种方法会在学习过程中引入过于复杂的模型，从而影响性能。因此，微调表示秩提出了一个既具挑战性又至关重要的优化问题。为了解决这个问题，我们找到了一个指导原则，用于自适应控制表示秩。我们以贝尔曼方程作为理论基础，推导出了价值网络连续状态动作对表示之间的余弦相似度的一个上界。然后，我们利用这个上界提出了一种新的正则化器，即基于贝尔曼方程的自动秩正则化器（BEER）。这种正则化器能够自适应地调节表示秩，从而提升DRL代理的表现。我们首先在示例实验中验证了自动控制秩的有效性。之后，通过将BEER与确定性策略梯度方法相结合，将其扩展到复杂的连续控制任务中。在12个具有挑战性的DeepMind控制任务中，BEER以显著的优势超越了基线方法。此外，BEER在Q值逼近方面也展现了显著的优势。我们的代码可在https://github.com/sweetice/BEER-ICLR2024找到。

[No.257] ReLoRA: High-Rank Training Through Low-Rank Updates
来源: ICLR 2024

英摘: Despite the dominance and effectiveness of scaling, resulting in large networks with hundreds of billions of parameters, the necessity to train overparameterized models remains poorly understood, while training costs grow exponentially. In this paper, we explore parameter-efficient training techniques as an approach to training large neural networks. We introduce a novel method called ReLoRA, which utilizes low-rank updates to train high-rank networks. 中摘: 尽管扩展性在很大程度上占据主导并展现出高效性，导致拥有数百亿参数的大规模网络的产生，但对过度参数化模型训练的必要性仍理解不足，同时训练成本呈指数级增长。在这篇论文中，我们探索了参数高效训练技术，作为训练大型神经网络的一种方法。我们提出了一种名为ReLoRA的新方法，它利用低秩更新来训练高秩网络。我们将ReLoRA应用于训练参数高达13亿的转换器语言模型，并证明其性能与常规神经网络训练相当。ReLoRA每块GPU最多可节省5.5GB的RAM，并根据模型大小和硬件设置，将训练速度提高9%-40%。我们的发现展示了参数高效技术在大规模预训练中的潜力。

[No.258] DQ-LoRe: Dual Queries with Low Rank Approximation Re-ranking for In-Context Learning
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: rank
标题: DQ-LoRe：基于低秩近似的双重查询重排ranking方法用于上下文学习
类别: representation learning for computer vision, audio, language, and other modalities
作者: Jing Xiong;Zixuan Li;Chuanyang Zheng;Zhijiang Guo;Yichun Yin;Enze Xie;Zhicheng YANG;Qingxing Cao;Haiming Wang;Xiongwei Han;Jing Tang;Chengming Li;Xiaodan Liang;
机构: Sun Yat-sen University;Chinese University of Hong Kong;University of Cambridge;Huawei;Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;Shenzhen MSU-BIT University;
类型: Poster

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.02954
英摘: Recent advances in natural language processing, primarily propelled by Large Language Models (LLMs), have showcased their remarkable capabilities grounded in in-context learning. A promising avenue for guiding LLMs in intricate reasoning tasks involves the utilization of intermediate reasoning steps within the Chain-of-Thought (CoT) paradigm. Nevertheless, the central challenge lies in the effective selection of exemplars for facilitating in-context learning. In this study, we introduce a framework that leverages Dual Queries and Low-rank approximation Re-ranking (DQ-LoRe) to automatically select exemplars for in-context learning. Dual Queries first query LLM to obtain LLM-generated knowledge such as CoT, then query the retriever to obtain the final exemplars via both question and the knowledge. Moreover, for the second query, LoRe employs dimensionality reduction techniques to refine exemplar selection, ensuring close alignment with the input question's knowledge. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that DQ-LoRe significantly outperforms prior state-of-the-art methods in the automatic selection of exemplars for GPT-4, enhancing performance from 92.5% to 94.2%. Our comprehensive analysis further reveals that DQ-LoRe consistently outperforms retrieval-based approaches in terms of both performance and adaptability, especially in scenarios characterized by distribution shifts. DQ-LoRe pushes the boundary of in-context learning and opens up new avenues for addressing complex reasoning challenges. Our code is released at https://github.com/menik1126/DQ-LoRe
中摘: 近来在自然语言处理领域的进展，主要由大型语言模型（LLM）推动，展示了它们基于情境学习的卓越能力。引导LLM进行复杂推理任务的一个有前景的方法是在“思维链”(Chain-of-Thought, CoT)范式中利用中间推理步骤。然而，核心挑战在于有效选择示例以促进情境学习。在这项研究中，我们引入了一个框架，该框架通过双重查询和低秩逼近重排序（DQ-LoRe）自动为情境学习选择示例。首先，双重查询向LLM查询以获取LLM生成的知识，如CoT，然后向检索器查询，通过问题和知识获得最终示例。此外，对于第二次查询，LoRe使用降维技术来优化示例选择，确保与输入问题的知识紧密对齐。通过广泛的实验，我们证明了DQ-LoRe在为GPT-4自动选择示例方面显著超越了先前的最先进方法，性能从92.5%提升至94.2%。我们的全面分析进一步表明，DQ-LoRe在性能和适应性方面始终优于基于检索的方法，特别是在存在分布偏移的情况下。DQ-LoRe推进了情境学习的边界，并开辟了应对复杂推理挑战的新途径。我们的代码已发布在https://github.com/menik1126/DQ-LoRe上。

[No.259] AutoCast++: Enhancing World Event Prediction with Zero-shot Ranking-based Context Retrieval
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: rank
标题: AutoCast++：利用零样本排名基于的上下文检索增强全球事件预测
类别: representation learning for computer vision, audio, language, and other modalities
作者: Qi Yan;Raihan Seraj;Jiawei He;Lili Meng;Tristan Sylvain;
机构: University of British Columbia;McGill University;Borealis AI;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/19174
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.01880
英摘: Machine-based prediction of real-world events is garnering attention due to its potential for informed decision-making. Whereas traditional forecasting predominantly hinges on structured data like time-series, recent breakthroughs in language models enable predictions using unstructured text. In particular, (Zou et al., 2022) unveils AutoCast, a new benchmark that employs news articles for answering forecasting queries. Nevertheless, existing methods still trail behind human performance. The cornerstone of accurate forecasting, we argue, lies in identifying a concise, yet rich subset of news snippets from a vast corpus. With this motivation, we introduce AutoCast++, a zero-shot ranking-based context retrieval system, tailored to sift through expansive news document collections for event forecasting. Our approach first re-ranks articles based on zero-shot question-passage relevance, honing in on semantically pertinent news. Following this, the chosen articles are subjected to zero-shot summarization to attain succinct context. Leveraging a pre-trained language model, we conduct both the relevance evaluation and article summarization without needing domain-specific training. Notably, recent articles can sometimes be at odds with preceding ones due to new facts or unanticipated incidents, leading to fluctuating temporal dynamics. To tackle this, our re-ranking mechanism gives preference to more recent articles, and we further regularize the multi-passage representation learning to align with human forecaster responses made on different dates. Empirical results underscore marked improvements across multiple metrics, improving the performance for multiple-choice questions (MCQ) by 48% and true/false (TF) questions by up to 8%. Code is available at https://github.com/BorealisAI/Autocast-plus-plus.
中摘: 基于机器的现实世界事件预测因其在决策中的潜在价值而受到关注。尽管传统预测主要依赖于如时间序列等结构化数据，近期在语言模型方面的突破使得利用非结构化文本进行预测成为可能。特别是，(Zou等人, 2022)介绍了AutoCast，这是一个使用新闻文章来回答预测查询的新基准。然而，现有的方法仍然落后于人类的表现。我们认为，准确预测的关键在于从庞大的文档库中识别出一个既精炼又信息丰富的新闻片段子集。基于这一动机，我们引入了AutoCast++，一个零样本排名式的上下文检索系统，专门设计用于从广泛的新闻文档集合中筛选事件预测所需的信息。我们的方法首先根据零样本问题-段落相关性对文章进行重新排序，以聚焦于语义相关的新闻。之后，所选文章通过零样本摘要获得简洁的上下文。利用预训练的语言模型，我们在不需要特定领域训练的情况下执行相关性评估和文章摘要。值得注意的是，由于新事实或意外事件的出现，近期的文章有时会与之前的相矛盾，导致时间动态的波动。为了解决这个问题，我们的重新排序机制倾向于更近的文章，并且我们进一步对多段落表示学习进行了正则化，使其与不同日期上人类预报员的回答保持一致。实证结果强调了在多个指标上的显著改进，对于选择题（MCQ）提高了48%的性能，而对于判断题（TF）最多提高了8%。代码可在https://github.com/BorealisAI/Autocast-plus-plus获取。

[No.260] Implicit Bias of Large Depth Networks: a Notion of Rank for Nonlinear Functions
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: rank
标题: 深层网络的隐含偏见：非线性函数的一种秩的概念
类别: 
作者: Arthur Jacot;
机构: New York University;
类型: Top-25%
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/10988
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2209.15055
英摘: We show that the representation cost of fully connected neural networks with homogeneous nonlinearities - which describes the implicit bias in function space of networks with $L_2$-regularization or with losses such as the cross-entropy - converges as the depth of the network goes to infinity to a notion of rank over nonlinear functions. We then inquire under which conditions the global minima of the loss recover the `true' rank of the data: we show that for too large depths the global minimum will be approximately rank 1 (underestimating the rank); we then argue that there is a range of depths which grows with the number of datapoints where the true rank is recovered. Finally, we discuss the effect of the rank of a classifier on the topology of the resulting class boundaries and show that autoencoders with optimal nonlinear rank are naturally denoising.
中摘: 我们证明了具有齐次非线性特性的全连接神经网络的表示成本——这描述了带有L_2正则化或诸如交叉熵等损失函数在网络空间中的隐式偏置——当网络深度趋于无穷大时，会收敛到对非线性函数的一种秩的概念。然后，我们探讨在什么条件下，损失的全局最小值能恢复数据的“真实”秩：我们表明，对于过大的深度，全局最小值将大约为秩1（低估了秩）；然后我们论证存在一个随着数据点数量增长的深度范围，在这个范围内可以恢复真实的秩。最后，我们讨论分类器的秩对其所产生类别边界拓扑结构的影响，并证明具有最优非线性秩的自编码器天然具有去噪能力。

[No.261] Online Low Rank Matrix Completion
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: rank
标题: 在线低秩矩阵完成
类别: 
作者: Soumyabrata Pal;Prateek Jain;
机构: Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/11229
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2209.03997
英摘: We study the problem of {\em online} low-rank matrix completion with $\mathsf{M}$ users, $\mathsf{N}$ items and $\mathsf{T}$ rounds. In each round, the algorithm recommends one item per user, for which it gets a (noisy) reward sampled from a low-rank user-item preference matrix. The goal is to design a method with sub-linear regret (in $\mathsf{T}$) and nearly optimal dependence on $\mathsf{M}$ and $\mathsf{N}$. The problem can be easily mapped to the standard multi-armed bandit problem where each item is an {\em independent} arm, but that leads to poor regret as the correlation between arms and users is not exploited. On the other hand, exploiting the low-rank structure of reward matrix is challenging due to non-convexity of the low-rank manifold. We first demonstrate that the low-rank structure can be exploited using a simple explore-then-commit (ETC) approach that ensures a regret of $O(\mathsf{polylog} (\mathsf{M}+\mathsf{N}) \mathsf{T}^{2/3})$. That is, roughly only $\mathsf{polylog} (\mathsf{M}+\mathsf{N})$ item recommendations are required per user to get a non-trivial solution. We then improve our result for the rank-$1$ setting which in itself is quite challenging and encapsulates some of the key issues. Here, we propose \textsc{OCTAL} (Online Collaborative filTering using iterAtive user cLustering) that guarantees nearly optimal regret of $O(\mathsf{polylog} (\mathsf{M}+\mathsf{N}) \mathsf{T}^{1/2})$. OCTAL is based on a novel technique of clustering users that allows iterative elimination of items and leads to a nearly optimal minimax rate.
中摘: 我们研究了在包含\(\mathsf{M}\)个用户、\(\mathsf{N}\)项物品和\(\mathsf{T}\)轮次的环境下，低秩矩阵补全的在线问题。每轮中，算法为每个用户推荐一项物品，从而获得来自低秩用户-物品偏好矩阵的（含噪声）奖励。目标是设计一种方法，其在\(\mathsf{T}\)中的遗憾为亚线性，并且对\(\mathsf{M}\)和\(\mathsf{N}\)的依赖接近最优。这个问题可以很容易地映射到标准的多臂赌博机问题上，其中每个物品都是一个独立的手臂，但这会导致较差的遗憾，因为没有利用手臂与用户之间的相关性。另一方面，由于低秩流形的非凸性，利用奖励矩阵的低秩结构具有挑战性。首先，我们展示了如何使用一个简单的探索-然后-承诺(ETC)方法来利用低秩结构，确保遗憾为\(O(\mathsf{polylog} (\mathsf{M}+\mathsf{N}) \mathsf{T}^{2/3})\)。也就是说，大致上每个用户只需要\(\mathsf{polylog} (\mathsf{M}+\mathsf{N})\)次物品推荐就能得到一个非平凡的解决方案。接着，我们针对秩-1设置改进了我们的结果，这一设置本身就相当有挑战性，并涵盖了部分关键问题。在这里，我们提出了\textsc{OCTAL}（使用迭代用户聚类的在线协同过滤），它保证了几乎最优的遗憾为\(O(\mathsf{polylog} (\mathsf{M}+\mathsf{N}) \mathsf{T}^{1/2})\)。OCTAL基于一种新颖的用户聚类技术，允许迭代消除物品，从而达到几乎最优的极小极大率。

[No.262] Deep Ranking Ensembles for Hyperparameter Optimization
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: rank
标题: 深度排名集成用于超参数优化
类别: 
作者: Abdus Salam Khazi;Sebastian Pineda Arango;Josif Grabocka;
机构: University of Freiburg;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/12039
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2303.15212
英摘: Automatically optimizing the hyperparameters of Machine Learning algorithms is one of the primary open questions in AI. Existing work in Hyperparameter Optimization (HPO) trains surrogate models for approximating the response surface of hyperparameters as a regression task. In contrast, we hypothesize that the optimal strategy for training surrogates is to preserve the ranks of the performances of hyperparameter configurations as a Learning to Rank problem. As a result, we present a novel method that meta-learns neural network surrogates optimized for ranking the configurations' performances while modeling their uncertainty via ensembling. In a large-scale experimental protocol comprising 12 baselines, 16 HPO search spaces and 86 datasets/tasks, we demonstrate that our method achieves new state-of-the-art results in HPO.
中摘: 自动优化机器学习算法的超参数是人工智能领域的主要开放问题之一。现有的超参数优化（HPO）工作通过训练代理模型来近似超参数响应面，将其作为回归任务处理。相反，我们假设训练代理的最佳策略是将超参数配置的性能排名保留下来，作为一个学习排序问题。因此，我们提出了一种新颖的方法，该方法通过元学习神经网络代理，专门用于对配置性能进行排名，同时通过集成建模其不确定性。在包含12个基线、16个HPO搜索空间和86个数据集/任务的大规模实验协议中，我们证明了我们的方法在HPO中实现了新的最先进成果。
