[No.265] Contextual bandits with concave rewards, and an application to fair ranking
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: rank
标题: 基于上下文的带约束奖励强化学习及其在公平排名中的应用
类别: 
作者: Virginie Do;Elvis Dohmatob;Matteo Pirotta;Alessandro Lazaric;Nicolas Usunier;
机构: Meta;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/11848
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2210.09957
英摘: We consider Contextual Bandits with Concave Rewards (CBCR), a multi-objective bandit problem where the desired trade-off between the rewards is defined by a known concave objective function, and the reward vector depends on an observed stochastic context. We present the first algorithm with provably vanishing regret for CBCR without restrictions on the policy space, whereas prior works were restricted to finite policy spaces or tabular representations. Our solution is based on a geometric interpretation of CBCR algorithms as optimization algorithms over the convex set of expected rewards spanned by all stochastic policies. Building on Frank-Wolfe analyses in constrained convex optimization, we derive a novel reduction from the CBCR regret to the regret of a scalar-reward bandit problem. We illustrate how to apply the reduction off-the-shelf to obtain algorithms for CBCR with both linear and general reward functions, in the case of non-combinatorial actions. Motivated by fairness in recommendation, we describe a special case of CBCR with rankings and fairness-aware objectives, leading to the first algorithm with regret guarantees for contextual combinatorial bandits with fairness of exposure.
中摘: 我们研究了具有凹奖励的上下文强盗问题（CBCR），这是一个多目标强盗问题，其中所期望的奖励之间的权衡由一个已知的凹目标函数定义，而奖励向量依赖于观察到的随机上下文。我们提出了第一个在CBCR中理论上可证其懊悔趋于零的算法，且该算法对策略空间没有限制，而之前的工作局限于有限策略空间或表格表示。我们的解决方案基于将CBCR算法解释为在所有随机策略生成的预期奖励凸集上进行优化的算法。基于Frank-Wolfe分析在受约束的凸优化中的应用，我们推导出了一个新的从CBCR懊悔到单奖励强盗问题懊悔的转换方法。我们说明了如何直接应用这一转换来获得处理非组合动作的CBCR算法，这些算法既适用于线性奖励函数也适用于一般奖励函数。受到推荐系统公平性的启发，我们描述了CBCR的一个特殊情况，即包含排序和公平性意识目标的CBCR，这导致了第一个对于考虑曝光公平性的上下文组合强盗问题具有懊悔保证的算法。

为了更好地理解和应用这个理论，让我们先解释一下几个关键概念：

- **上下文强盗问题（Contextual Bandits）**：这是强盗问题的一个扩展，其中每个时间点的奖励不仅取决于选择的动作，还依赖于当前的“上下文”信息。这种问题常见于推荐系统、广告投放等场景，其中上下文可以是用户特征、时间、地点等。

- **多目标强盗问题**：与传统的单目标强盗问题不同，这里的每个动作可能产生多个奖励，并且目标是在不同的奖励之间找到最优的权衡。

- **凹奖励函数**：在多目标优化中，如果目标函数是凹的，那么找到全局最优解的可能性会增加。这是因为凹函数具有一些良好的数学性质，比如局部最优解通常也是全局最优解。

- **Frank-Wolfe分析**：这是一种优化技术，用于解决在凸集上受限的优化问题。它通过迭代地寻找梯度方向上的最佳点来逼近全局最优解。

- **非组合动作**：这意味着每个动作不是由其他基础动作组合而成的，而是独立的。例如，在推荐系统中，选择一个项目推荐给用户就是一个非组合动作的例子。

- **曝光公平性**：在推荐系统中，确保所有项目都有公平的曝光机会是一个重要的问题。例如，不应该总是推荐热门项目而忽视了冷门项目，即使它们的质量可能同样高。

综上所述，这篇论文提出了一种新的算法，能够有效地解决具有凹奖励的上下文强盗问题，特别是在不限制策略空间的情况下。该算法不仅适用于线性和更一般的奖励函数，而且还特别关注了推荐系统的公平性问题，提供了一种处理上下文组合强盗问题的新方法，同时保证了曝光的公平性。这对于构建更加智能和公正的推荐系统具有重要意义。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.266] Learning Sparse and Low-Rank Priors for Image Recovery via Iterative Reweighted Least Squares Minimization
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: rank
标题: 基于迭代加权最小二乘法实现的图像恢复中的稀疏性和低秩先验学习
类别: 
作者: Stamatios Lefkimmiatis;Iaroslav Sergeevich Koshelev;
机构: Huawei;Skolkovo Institute of Science and Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/11996
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2304.10536
英摘: We introduce a novel optimization algorithm for image recovery under learned sparse and low-rank constraints, which we parameterize as weighted extensions of the $\ell_p^p$-vector and $\mathcal S_p^p$ Schatten-matrix quasi-norms for $0\!<p\!\le1$, respectively. Our proposed algorithm generalizes the Iteratively Reweighted Least Squares (IRLS) method, used for signal recovery under $\ell_1$ and nuclear-norm constrained minimization. Further, we interpret our overall minimization approach as a recurrent network that we then employ to deal with inverse low-level computer vision problems. Thanks to the convergence guarantees that our IRLS strategy offers, we are able to train the derived reconstruction networks using a memory-efficient implicit back-propagation scheme, which does not pose any restrictions on their effective depth. To assess our networks' performance, we compare them against other existing reconstruction methods on several inverse problems, namely image deblurring, super-resolution, demosaicking and sparse recovery. Our reconstruction results are shown to be very competitive and in many cases outperform those of existing unrolled networks, whose number of parameters is orders of magnitude higher than that of our learned models.
中摘: 我们介绍了一种新颖的优化算法，用于在学习得到的稀疏和低秩约束下恢复图像，我们将这些约束参数化为加权扩展的$\ell_p^p$-向量和$\mathcal S_p^p$ Schatten矩阵准范数，其中$0<p\le1$。我们提出的算法推广了迭代重加权最小二乘法（IRLS），该方法用于在$\ell_1$和核范数约束下的信号恢复。此外，我们将整体最小化方法解释为一个递归网络，然后我们使用它来处理逆向低级计算机视觉问题。由于我们的IRLS策略提供的收敛保证，我们能够使用内存高效的隐式反向传播方案训练得出的重建网络，这不会对它们的有效深度施加任何限制。为了评估我们网络的性能，我们将其与其他现有重建方法在多个逆问题上进行比较，包括图像去模糊、超分辨率、去马赛克和稀疏恢复。我们的重建结果被证明极具竞争力，在许多情况下，其表现超过了现有的展开网络，后者的参数数量比我们学习模型的参数数量高出几个数量级。

[No.267] Towards a Unified Theoretical Understanding of Non-contrastive Learning via Rank Differential Mechanism
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: rank
标题: 通过排名差异机制统一无对比学习的理论理解
类别: 
作者: Zhijian Zhuo;Yifei Wang;Jinwen Ma;Yisen Wang;
机构: Peking University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/11129
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2303.02387
英摘: Recently, a variety of methods under the name of non-contrastive learning (like BYOL, SimSiam, SwAV, DINO) show that when equipped with some asymmetric architectural designs, aligning positive pairs alone is sufficient to attain good performance in self-supervised visual learning. Despite some understandings of some specific modules (like the predictor in BYOL), there is yet no unified theoretical understanding of how these seemingly different asymmetric designs can all avoid feature collapse, particularly considering methods that also work without the predictor (like DINO). In this work, we propose a unified theoretical understanding for existing variants of non-contrastive learning. Our theory named Rank Differential Mechanism (RDM) shows that all these asymmetric designs create a consistent rank difference in their dual-branch output features. This rank difference will provably lead to an improvement of effective dimensionality and alleviate either complete or dimensional feature collapse. Different from previous theories, our RDM theory is applicable to different asymmetric designs (with and without the predictor), and thus can serve as a unified understanding of existing non-contrastive learning methods. Besides, our RDM theory also provides practical guidelines for designing many new non-contrastive variants. We show that these variants indeed achieve comparable performance to existing methods on benchmark datasets, and some of them even outperform the baselines. Our code is available at \url{https://github.com/PKU-ML/Rank-Differential-Mechanism}.
中摘: 最近，一系列名为非对比学习的方法（如BYOL、SimSiam、SwAV、DINO）表明，当配备某些不对称架构设计时，仅对正样本对进行对齐就足以在自监督视觉学习中获得良好的性能。尽管我们对某些特定模块（如BYOL中的预测器）有了一定的理解，但目前还没有统一的理论来理解这些看似不同的不对称设计如何都能避免特征崩溃，特别是考虑到那些即使没有预测器也能工作的方法（如DINO）。在这项工作中，我们提出了现有非对比学习变体的统一理论理解。我们的理论，名为秩差机制（RDM），揭示了所有这些不对称设计在其双分支输出特征中创建了一致的秩差。这种秩差将可证明地导致有效维度性的提升，并缓解完全或维度上的特征崩溃。与以往的理论不同，我们的RDM理论适用于不同的不对称设计（有和无预测器），因此可以作为现有非对比学习方法的统一理解。此外，我们的RDM理论还为设计许多新的非对比学习变体提供了实用指南。我们证明，这些变体确实能在基准数据集上达到与现有方法相当的性能，其中一些甚至超越了基线。我们的代码可在\url{https://github.com/PKU-ML/Rank-Differential-Mechanism}获取。

[No.269] Tensor-Based Sketching Method for the Low-Rank Approximation of Data Streams.
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: rank
标题: 基于张量的抽样方法在数据流的低秩逼近中的应用。

英摘: Low-rank approximation in data streams is a fundamental and significant task in computing science, machine learning and statistics. Multiple streaming algorithms have emerged over years and most of them are inspired by randomized algorithms, more specifically, sketching methods. However, many algorithms are not able to leverage information of data streams and consequently suffer from low accuracy. Existing data-driven methods improve accuracy but the training cost is expensive in practice. In this paper, from a subspace perspective, we propose a tensor-based sketching method for low-rank approximation of data streams. 中摘: 在计算科学、机器学习和统计学中，数据流的低秩近似是一项基本且重要的任务。多年来涌现了多种流算法，其中大多数受到随机化算法的启发，更具体地说，是素描方法。然而，许多算法无法充分利用数据流的信息，因此精度较低。现有的数据驱动方法虽然提高了精度，但在实践中训练成本昂贵。在这篇论文中，从子空间的角度出发，我们提出了一种基于张量的素描方法，用于数据流的低秩近似。所提出的算法充分利用了数据流的结构，并通过在训练数据上进行张量分解来获得准最优的素描矩阵。进行了一系列实验，结果显示，所提出的基于张量的方法比先前的工作更准确且速度快得多。

[No.270] Improved Sample Complexity for Reward-free Reinforcement Learning under Low-rank MDPs
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: rank
标题: 改进的样本复杂度：基于低秩MDP的无奖励强化学习
类别: 
作者: Yuan Cheng;Ruiquan Huang;Yingbin Liang;Jing Yang;
机构: University of Science and Technology of China;Pennsylvania State University;Ohio State University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/11380
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2303.10859
英摘: In reward-free reinforcement learning (RL), an agent explores the environment first without any reward information, in order to achieve certain learning goals afterwards for any given reward. In this paper we focus on reward-free RL under low-rank MDP models, in which both the representation and linear weight vectors are unknown. Although various algorithms have been proposed for reward-free low-rank MDPs, the corresponding sample complexity is still far from being satisfactory. In this work, we first provide the first known sample complexity lower bound that holds for any algorithm under low-rank MDPs. This lower bound implies it is strictly harder to find a near-optimal policy under low-rank MDPs than under linear MDPs. We then propose a novel model-based algorithm, coined RAFFLE, and show it can both find an $ε$-optimal policy and achieve an $ε$-accurate system identification via reward-free exploration, with a sample complexity significantly improving the previous results. Such a sample complexity matches our lower bound in the dependence on $ε$, as well as on $K$ in the large $d$ regime, where $d$ and $K$ respectively denote the representation dimension and action space cardinality. Finally, we provide a planning algorithm (without further interaction with true environment) for RAFFLE to learn a near-accurate representation, which is the first known representation learning guarantee under the same setting.
中摘: 在无奖励强化学习（RL）中，智能体首先在没有任何奖励信息的情况下探索环境，以便之后对于任何给定的奖励达成特定的学习目标。本文我们关注低秩马尔科夫决策过程（MDP）模型下的无奖励RL，其中表示和线性权重向量都是未知的。尽管已经为低秩MDP提出了各种算法，但相应的样本复杂度仍然远未令人满意。在此工作中，我们首先提供了首个已知的样本复杂度下界，该下界适用于所有低秩MDP下的算法。这个下界表明，在低秩MDP下找到近似最优策略比在线性MDP下要严格更难。然后，我们提出了一种新颖的基于模型的算法，命名为RAFFLE，并证明它既能通过无奖励探索找到ε-最优策略，也能实现ε-准确的系统识别，其样本复杂度显著优于先前的结果。这种样本复杂度在依赖于ε以及在大d情况下对K的依赖上与我们的下界相匹配，其中d和K分别表示表示维度和动作空间的基数。最后，我们为RAFFLE提供了一个规划算法（无需与真实环境进一步交互），以学习接近准确的表示，这是在相同设置下首个已知的表示学习保证。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.271] A Stronger Mixture of Low-Rank Experts for Fine-Tuning Foundation Models
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 面向基础模型微调的一种更强的低秩专家混合方法
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Mengyang Sun;Yihao Wang;Tao Feng;Dan Zhang;Yifan Zhu;Jie Tang;
机构: Tsinghua University;Beijing Infomation Science and Technology University;Beijing University of Posts and Telecommunications;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/43508
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.15828
英摘: In order to streamline the fine-tuning of foundation models, Low-Rank Adapters (LoRAs) have been substantially adopted across various fields, including instruction tuning and domain adaptation. The underlying concept of LoRA involves decomposing a full-rank matrix into the product of two lower-rank matrices, which reduces storage consumption and accelerates the training process. Furthermore, to address the limited expressive capacity of LoRA, the Mixture-of-Expert (MoE) has been introduced for incorporating multiple LoRA adapters. The integration of LoRA experts leads to a visible improvement across several downstream scenes. However, the mixture of LoRAs (MoE-LoRA) still exhibits its low robustness during tuning and inferring. Inspired by the Riemannian Preconditioners which train LoRA as a sub-space projector, we propose a new training strategy for MoE-LoRA, to stabilize and boost its feature learning procedure by multi-space projections. Examinations on SGD and AdamW optimizers demonstrate the effectiveness of our methodology. Source code is available at https://github.com/THUDM/MoELoRA_Riemannian.
中摘: 为了简化基础模型的微调过程，低秩适配器（LoRA）已在包括指令微调和领域适应在内的多个领域得到了广泛应用。LoRA的基本概念涉及将满秩矩阵分解为两个较低秩矩阵的乘积，这减少了存储消耗并加速了训练过程。此外，为了解决LoRA表达能力有限的问题，引入了专家混合体（MoE）来整合多个LoRA适配器。LoRA专家的集成在多个下游场景中带来了显著的提升。然而，LoRA的混合体（MoE-LoRA）在微调和推理过程中仍然表现出较低的鲁棒性。受黎曼预调节器的启发，这些预调节器将LoRA作为子空间投影器进行训练，我们提出了一种新的MoE-LoRA训练策略，通过多空间投影来稳定和增强其特征学习过程。在SGD和AdamW优化器上的测试证明了我们方法的有效性。源代码可在https://github.com/THUDM/MoELoRA_Riemannian上获取。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.272] GL-LowPopArt: A Nearly Instance-Wise Minimax-Optimal Estimator for Generalized Low-Rank Trace Regression
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: GL-LowPopArt：一种针对广义低秩迹回归的几乎实例最优的最小最大优化估计器
类别: theory->learning theory
作者: Junghyun Lee;Kyoungseok Jang;Kwang-Sung Jun;Milan Vojnovic;Se-Young Yun;
机构: Korea Advanced Institute of Science and Technology;Adobe;Chung-Ang University;University of Milan;University of Arizona;London School of Economics;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45149
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.03074
英摘: We present `GL-LowPopArt`, a novel Catoni-style estimator for generalized low-rank trace regression. Building on `LowPopArt` (Jang et al., 2024), it employs a two-stage approach: nuclear norm regularization followed by matrix Catoni estimation. We establish state-of-the-art estimation error bounds, surpassing existing guarantees (Fan et al., 2019; Kang et al., 2022), and reveal a novel experimental design objective, $\mathrm{GL}(π)$. The key technical challenge is controlling bias from the nonlinear inverse link function, which we address by our two-stage approach. We prove a *local* minimax lower bound, showing that our `GL-LowPopArt` enjoys instance-wise optimality up to the condition number of the ground-truth Hessian. Applications include generalized linear matrix completion, where `GL-LowPopArt` achieves a state-of-the-art Frobenius error guarantee, and **bilinear dueling bandits**, a novel setting inspired by general preference learning (Zhang et al., 2024). Our analysis of a `GL-LowPopArt`-based explore-then-commit algorithm reveals a new, potentially interesting problem-dependent quantity, along with improved Borda regret bound than vectorization (Wu et al., 2024).
中摘: 我们提出了`GL-LowPopArt`，这是一种用于广义低秩迹回归的新型Catoni风格估计器。基于`LowPopArt`（Jang等人，2024年），它采用两阶段方法：首先是核范数正则化，然后是矩阵Catoni估计。我们建立了最先进的估计误差界限，超越了现有保证（Fan等人，2019年；Kang等人，2022年），并揭示了一个新的实验设计目标，$\mathrm{GL}(π)$。主要的技术挑战在于控制非线性逆链接函数带来的偏差，我们通过两阶段方法解决了这一问题。我们证明了一个*局部*最小最大下界，表明我们的`GL-LowPopArt`在真实Hessian条件数的基础上实现了实例级最优性。应用包括广义线性矩阵补全，其中`GL-LowPopArt`达到了最先进的Frobenius误差保证，以及**双线性对决强盗**，这是一个受一般偏好学习启发的新场景（Zhang等人，2024年）。我们对基于`GL-LowPopArt`的探索-然后-承诺算法的分析揭示了一个新的、可能有趣的问题相关量，以及比向量化方法改进的Borda遗憾界限（Wu等人，2024年）。

[No.273] Improving Your Model Ranking on Chatbot Arena by Vote Rigging
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 通过操控投票提升你在聊天机器人竞技场中的模型排名
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Rui Min;Tianyu Pang;Chao Du;Qian Liu;Minhao Cheng;Min Lin;
机构: Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;Sea AI Lab;TikTok;Pennsylvania State University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46419
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.17858
英摘: Chatbot Arena is a popular platform for evaluating LLMs by pairwise battles, where users vote for their preferred response from two randomly sampled anonymous models. While Chatbot Arena is widely regarded as a reliable LLM ranking leaderboard, we show that crowdsourced voting can be rigged to improve (or decrease) the ranking of a target model $m_{t}$. We first introduce a straightforward target-only rigging strategy that focuses on new battles involving $m_{t}$, identifying it via watermarking or a binary classifier, and exclusively voting for $m_{t}$ wins. However, this strategy is practically inefficient because there are over $190$ models on Chatbot Arena and on average only about $1\%$ of new battles will involve $m_{t}$. To overcome this, we propose omnipresent rigging strategies, exploiting the Elo rating mechanism of Chatbot Arena that any new vote on a battle can influence the ranking of the target model $m_{t}$, even if $m_{t}$ is not directly involved in the battle. We conduct experiments on around $1.7$ million historical votes from the Chatbot Arena Notebook, showing that omnipresent rigging strategies can improve model rankings by rigging only hundreds of new votes. While we have evaluated several defense mechanisms, our findings highlight the importance of continued efforts to prevent vote rigging. Our code is available at https://github.com/sail-sg/Rigging-ChatbotArena.
中摘: 聊天机器人竞技场（Chatbot Arena）是一个流行的平台，通过两两对决的方式评估大型语言模型（LLM），用户为两个随机抽样的匿名模型中更偏好的回答投票。尽管聊天机器人竞技场通常被视为一个可靠的语言模型排名榜单，我们展示出众包投票可以被操纵以提升（或降低）目标模型$m_{t}$的排名。我们首先介绍了一种简单的仅针对目标模型的操纵策略，该策略专注于涉及$m_{t}$的新对决，通过水印或二元分类器识别$m_{t}$，并专门投票给$m_{t}$获胜。然而，这种策略在实际中效率低下，因为聊天机器人竞技场上有超过190个模型，而平均而言，新对决中涉及$m_{t}$的比例仅为约1%。为了克服这一问题，我们提出了全境操纵策略，利用了聊天机器人竞技场的Elo评级机制，即任何对决中的新投票都可能影响目标模型$m_{t}$的排名，即使$m_{t}$没有直接参与该对决。我们在聊天机器人竞技场笔记中大约170万条历史投票数据上进行了实验，结果显示全境操纵策略仅需操纵数百次新投票就能提升模型排名。虽然我们已经评估了几种防御机制，但我们的发现强调了持续努力防止投票操纵的重要性。我们的代码可在https://github.com/sail-sg/Rigging-ChatbotArena获取。

[No.275] Representative Ranking for Deliberation in the Public Sphere
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 公共领域中的审议代表性排名
类别: social aspects
作者: Manon Revel;Smitha Milli;Tyler Lu;Jamelle Watson-Daniels;Maximilian Nickel;
机构: Harvard University;Meta;Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45374
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18962
英摘: Online comment sections, such as those on news sites or social media, have the potential to foster informal public deliberation, However, this potential is often undermined by the frequency of toxic or low-quality exchanges that occur in these settings. To combat this, platforms increasingly leverage algorithmic ranking to facilitate higher-quality discussions, e.g., by using civility classifiers or forms of prosocial ranking. Yet, these interventions may also inadvertently reduce the visibility of legitimate viewpoints, undermining another key aspect of deliberation: representation of diverse views. We seek to remedy this problem by introducing guarantees of representation into these methods. In particular, we adopt the notion of justified representation (JR) from the social choice literature and incorporate a JR constraint into the comment ranking setting. We find that enforcing JR leads to greater inclusion of diverse viewpoints while still being compatible with optimizing for user engagement or other measures of conversational quality.
中摘: 在线评论区，如新闻网站或社交媒体上的评论区，有可能促进非正式的公众审议。然而，这种潜力常常被这些场合中频繁出现的有毒或低质量交流所削弱。为了解决这一问题，平台越来越多地利用算法排名来促进更高质量的讨论，例如，通过使用文明分类器或亲社会排名的形式。然而，这些干预措施也可能无意间降低了合法观点的可见度，损害了审议的另一个关键方面：多元观点的代表性。我们试图通过在这些方法中引入代表性保证来解决这个问题。具体而言，我们采纳了社会选择文献中的正当代表性（Justified Representation, JR）概念，并将JR约束纳入评论排名的场景中。我们发现，实施JR会导致更多样化观点的包容，同时仍与优化用户参与度或其他对话质量指标相兼容。

[No.276] Low-Rank Adapting Models for Sparse Autoencoders
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 低秩适应模型Sparse Autoencoders的稀疏自编码器
类别: social aspects->accountability transparency and interpretability
作者: Matthew Chen;Joshua Engels;Max Tegmark;
机构: Massachusetts Institute of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44210
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.19406
英摘: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) decompose language model representations into a sparse set of linear latent vectors. Recent works have improved SAEs using language model gradients, but these techniques require many expensive backward passes during training and still cause a significant increase in cross entropy loss when SAE reconstructions are inserted into the model. In this work, we improve on these limitations by taking a fundamentally different approach: we use low-rank adaptation (LoRA) to finetune the \textit{language model itself} around a previously trained SAE. We analyze our method across SAE sparsity, SAE width, language model size, LoRA rank, and model layer on the Gemma Scope family of SAEs. In these settings, our method reduces the cross entropy loss gap by 30\% to 55\% when SAEs are inserted during the forward pass. We also find that compared to end-to-end (e2e) SAEs, our approach achieves the same downstream cross entropy loss 3$\times$ to 20$\times$ faster on \gemma and 2$\times$ to 10$\times$ faster on \llama. We further show that our technique improves downstream metrics and can adapt multiple SAEs at once without harming general language model capabilities. Our results demonstrate that improving model interpretability is not limited to post-hoc SAE training; Pareto improvements can also be achieved by directly optimizing the model itself.
中摘: 稀疏自编码器（SAEs）将语言模型表示分解为一组稀疏的线性潜在向量。近期的研究通过使用语言模型梯度改进了SAEs，但这些技术在训练过程中需要进行多次昂贵的反向传播，并且当将SAE重构插入模型时，仍然会导致交叉熵损失显著增加。在本工作中，我们通过采用根本不同的方法来改进这些限制：我们使用低秩适应（LoRA）对先前训练的SAE周围的语言模型本身进行微调。我们在Gemma Scope系列SAEs的不同设置下分析了我们的方法，包括SAE的稀疏性、SAE宽度、语言模型大小、LoRA秩以及模型层。在这种情况下，我们的方法在正向传播期间插入SAEs时，可以将交叉熵损失差距减少30％至55％。我们还发现，与端到端（e2e）SAEs相比，我们的方法在Gemma上的下游交叉熵损失提高3倍至20倍的速度，在Llama上提高2倍至10倍的速度。此外，我们证明了我们的技术能够改善下游指标，并且可以同时适应多个SAEs，而不会损害通用语言模型的能力。我们的结果表明，提高模型可解释性不仅仅局限于事后SAE训练；通过直接优化模型本身，也可以实现帕累托改进。

[No.277] Low-Rank Thinning
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 低秩稀疏化
类别: theory->probabilistic methods
作者: Annabelle Michael Carrell;Albert Gong;Abhishek Shetty;Raaz Dwivedi;Lester Mackey;
机构: University of Cambridge;Cornell University;University of California, Berkeley;Microsoft;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44371
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.12063
英摘: The goal in thinning is to summarize a dataset using a small set of representative points. Remarkably, sub-Gaussian thinning algorithms like Kernel Halving and Compress can match the quality of uniform subsampling while substantially reducing the number of summary points. However, existing guarantees cover only a restricted range of distributions and kernel-based quality measures and suffer from pessimistic dimension dependence. To address these deficiencies, we introduce a new low-rank analysis of sub-Gaussian thinning that applies to any distribution and any kernel, guaranteeing high-quality compression whenever the kernel or data matrix is approximately low-rank. To demonstrate the broad applicability of the techniques, we design practical sub-Gaussian thinning approaches that improve upon the best known guarantees for approximating attention in transformers, accelerating stochastic gradient training through reordering, and distinguishing distributions in near-linear time.
中摘: 在数据精简中的目标是使用一小集合的代表性点来概括数据集。令人惊讶的是，像Kernel Halving和Compress这样的子高斯（Sub-Gaussian）精简算法能在大幅减少概要点的数量的同时，匹配均匀采样的质量。然而，现有的保证仅覆盖了有限范围的分布和基于核的质量度量，并且受到悲观的维度依赖性的影响。为了解决这些不足，我们引入了一种新的子高斯精简的低秩分析，该分析适用于任何分布和任何核函数，保证在核或数据矩阵近似低秩时能实现高质量的压缩。为了展示技术的广泛适用性，我们设计了实用的子高斯精简方法，这些方法在近似变换器（transformers）中的注意力、通过重新排序加速随机梯度训练以及在接近线性时间内区分分布等方面，改进了已知的最佳保证。

[No.278] LapSum - One Method to Differentiate Them All: Ranking, Sorting and Top-k Selection
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: LapSum - 一种区分一切的方法：排名、排序和Top-k选择
类别: deep learning->algorithms
作者: Łukasz Struski;Michal B. Bednarczyk;Igor T. Podolak;Jacek Tabor;
机构: Jagiellonian University;University of Illinois;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46060
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06242
英摘: We present a novel technique for constructing differentiable order-type operations, including soft ranking, soft top-k selection, and soft permutations. Our approach leverages an efficient closed-form formula for the inverse of the function LapSum, defined as the sum of Laplace distributions. This formulation ensures low computational and memory complexity in selecting the highest activations, enabling losses and gradients to be computed in $O(n\log{}n)$ time. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art techniques for high-dimensional vectors and large $k$ values. Furthermore, we provide efficient implementations for both CPU and CUDA environments, underscoring the practicality and scalability of our method for large-scale ranking and differentiable ordering problems.
中摘: 我们提出了一种新颖的技术，用于构建可微分的序类型操作，包括柔和排序、柔和前k选择和柔和排列。我们的方法利用了LapSum函数逆的有效闭式公式，该函数定义为拉普拉斯分布的和。这种公式确保在选择最高激活值时具有较低的计算和内存复杂度，使损失和梯度能够在$O(n\log{}n)$时间内被计算。通过广泛的实验，我们证明了我们的方法在高维向量和大的$k$值情况下优于最先进的技术。此外，我们为CPU和CUDA环境提供了高效的实现，强调了我们的方法在大规模排序和可微分排序问题中的实用性和可扩展性。

[No.280] Learning Cascade Ranking as One Network
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 学习级联排序作为一个网络
类别: applications->everything else
作者: Yunli Wang;ZhenZhang;Zhiqiang Wang;Zixuan Yang;Yu Li;Jian Yang;Shiyang Wen;Peng Jiang;Kun Gai;
机构: Kuaishou Technology;;Kwai Technology;Beijing University of Posts and Telecommunications;Alibaba Group;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44498
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.09492
英摘: Cascade Ranking is a prevalent architecture in large-scale top-k selection systems like recommendation and advertising platforms. Traditional training methods focus on single-stage optimization, neglecting interactions between stages. Recent advances have introduced interaction-aware training paradigms, but still struggle to 1) align training objectives with the goal of the entire cascade ranking (i.e., end-to-end recall of ground-truth items) and 2) learn effective collaboration patterns for different stages. To address these challenges, we propose LCRON, which introduces a novel surrogate loss function derived from the lower bound probability that ground truth items are selected by cascade ranking, ensuring alignment with the overall objective of the system. According to the properties of the derived bound, we further design an auxiliary loss for each stage to drive the reduction of this bound, leading to a more robust and effective top-k selection. LCRON enables end-to-end training of the entire cascade ranking system as a unified network. Experimental results demonstrate that LCRON achieves significant improvement over existing methods on public benchmarks and industrial applications, addressing key limitations in cascade ranking training and significantly enhancing system performance.
中摘: 瀑布式排名是在大规模top-k选择系统（如推荐和广告平台）中流行的一种架构。传统的训练方法侧重于单阶段优化，忽视了各阶段之间的交互。最近的研究进展引入了考虑交互的训练范式，但仍难以1）使训练目标与整个瀑布式排名的目标（即，从头至尾召回真实项目）对齐，以及2）学习不同阶段间有效协作模式。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了LCRON，它引入了一种新颖的代理损失函数，该函数源自真实项目被瀑布式排名选中的概率下界，确保了与系统的整体目标对齐。根据所导出下界的特点，我们进一步为每个阶段设计了辅助损失，以驱动这个下界的减少，从而实现更稳健有效的top-k选择。LCRON使得整个瀑布式排名系统能够作为一个统一网络进行端到端的训练。实验结果表明，LCRON在公开基准测试和工业应用上相比现有方法有显著提升，解决了瀑布式排名训练的关键局限，并显著增强了系统性能。

[No.281] Vision Graph Prompting via Semantic Low-Rank Decomposition
来源: ICML 2025

英摘: Vision GNN (ViG) demonstrates superior performance by representing images as graph structures, providing a more natural way to capture irregular semantic patterns beyond traditional grid or sequence-based representations. To efficiently adapt ViG to downstream tasks, parameter-efficient fine-tuning techniques like visual prompting become increasingly essential. However, existing prompting methods are primarily designed for Transformer-based models, neglecting the rich topological relationships among nodes and edges in graph-based representations, limiting their capacity to model complex semantics. [No.282] LIFT the Veil for the Truth: Principal Weights Emerge after Rank Reduction for Reasoning-Focused Supervised Fine-Tuning
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 揭开真相的面纱：推理导向的监督微调在秩减少后凸显主要权重
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Zihang Liu;Tianyu Pang;Oleg Balabanov;Chaoqun Yang;Tianjin Huang;Lu Yin;Yaoqing Yang;Shiwei Liu;
机构: University of California, Berkeley;Dartmouth College;Nanjing University;Lawrence Berkeley National Laboratory;Tsinghua University;University of Exeter;University of Surrey;University of Oxford;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44967
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00772
英摘: Recent studies have shown that supervised fine-tuning of LLMs on a small number of high-quality datasets can yield strong reasoning capabilities. However, full fine-tuning (Full FT), while powerful, is computationally expensive and susceptible to overfitting and catastrophic forgetting, particularly when data is limited. Sparse fine-tuning, which previously achieved notable success by updating only a small subset of model parameters, offers a promising trade-off between efficiency and effectiveness. Yet, it has lagged behind in the LLM era due to the difficulty of identifying parameters truly critical for reasoning. In this work, we state that weights with the largest magnitude after low-rank approximation are critical weights for fine-tuning, which we call Principal Weights. Surprisingly, while magnitude-based sparse fine-tuning performs poorly as a baseline on LLM fine-tuning, it becomes highly effective after rank reduction. These insights motivate our method: Low-rank Informed Sparse Fine-Tuning (LIFT). LIFT only updates the top 5% Principal Weights throughout training and consistently achieves better performance on reasoning tasks than Full FT, while maintaining memory efficiency on par with popular parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods. In addition to strong performance on target domains such as arithmetic reasoning, LIFT also retains up to 20% more source-domain knowledge, compared to Full FT and LoRA. Our code is available at: https://github.com/zihanghliu/LIFT.
中摘: 最近的研究表明，在少量高质量数据集上对LLM（大型语言模型）进行有监督的微调可以产生强大的推理能力。然而，全量微调（Full FT），虽然强大，但计算成本高，并且在数据有限时容易发生过拟合和灾难性遗忘。稀疏微调，之前通过仅更新模型参数的一小部分取得了显著的成功，提供了一个效率与效果之间有希望的权衡。然而，在LLM时代，由于难以识别对推理真正关键的参数，它落后了。在本工作中，我们指出经过低秩近似后具有最大幅度的权重是微调的关键权重，我们称之为“主权重”。令人惊讶的是，尽管基于幅度的稀疏微调作为基线在LLM微调上的表现不佳，但在降低秩之后变得非常有效。这些见解激发了我们的方法：低秩指导下的稀疏微调（LIFT）。LIFT在整个训练过程中仅更新前5%的主权重，并且在推理任务上的表现始终优于全量微调，同时保持与流行的参数高效微调方法相当的记忆效率。除了在目标领域如算术推理的强大表现，LIFT还比全量微调和LoRA保留多达20%更多的源域知识。我们的代码可在以下网址获取：https://github.com/zihanghliu/LIFT。

[No.284] RepLoRA: Reparameterizing Low-rank Adaptation via the Perspective of Mixture of Experts
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: RepLoRA: 基于专家混合视角重构低秩适应参数化
类别: general machine learning->transfer multitask and metalearning
作者: Tuan Truong;Chau Nguyen;Huy Nguyen;Minh Le;Trung Le;Nhat Ho;
机构: University of British Columbia;;University of Texas at Austin;Qualcomm Incorporated;VinAI Research;Monash University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45214
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.03044
英摘: Low-rank Adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as a powerful method for fine-tuning large-scale foundation models. Despite its popularity, the theoretical understanding of LoRA has remained limited. This paper presents a theoretical analysis of LoRA by examining its connection to the Mixture of Experts models. Under this framework, we show that simple reparameterizations of the LoRA matrices can notably accelerate the low-rank matrix estimation process. In particular, we prove that reparameterization can reduce the data needed to achieve a desired estimation error from an exponential to a polynomial scale. Motivated by this insight, we propose Reparameterized Low-Rank Adaptation (RepLoRA), which incorporates lightweight MLPs to reparameterize the LoRA matrices. Extensive experiments across multiple domains demonstrate that RepLoRA consistently outperforms vanilla LoRA. Notably, with limited data, RepLoRA surpasses LoRA by a margin of up to 40.0% and achieves LoRA's performance with only 30.0% of the training data, highlighting both the theoretical and empirical robustness of our PEFT method.

[No.285] Flat-LoRA: Low-Rank Adaptation over a Flat Loss Landscape
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 平展洛拉：平坦损失景观上的低秩适应
类别: optimization
作者: Tao Li;Zhengbao He;Yujun Li;Yasheng Wang;Lifeng Shang;Xiaolin Huang;
机构: Shanghai Jiao Tong University;Huawei;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46534
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.14396
英摘: Fine-tuning large-scale pre-trained models is prohibitively expensive in terms of computation and memory costs. Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), a popular Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) method, offers an efficient solution by optimizing only low-rank matrices. Despite recent progress in improving LoRA's performance, the relationship between the LoRA optimization space and the full parameter space is often overlooked. A solution that appears flat in the loss landscape of the LoRA space may still exhibit sharp directions in the full parameter space, potentially compromising generalization. We introduce Flat-LoRA, which aims to identify a low-rank adaptation situated in a flat region of the full parameter space. Instead of adopting the well-established sharpness-aware minimization approach, which incurs significant computation and memory overheads, we employ a Bayesian expectation loss objective to preserve training efficiency. Further, we design a refined random perturbation generation strategy for improved performance and carefully manage memory overhead using random seeds. Experiments across diverse tasks-including mathematical reasoning, coding abilities, dialogue generation, instruction following, and text-to-image generation-demonstrate that Flat-LoRA improves both in-domain and out-of-domain generalization. Code is available at https://github.com/nblt/Flat-LoRA.
中摘: 微调大规模预训练模型在计算和内存成本上过于昂贵。低秩适应（LoRA）作为一种流行的参数高效微调（PEFT）方法，通过仅优化低秩矩阵提供了高效的解决方案。尽管近期在提升LoRA性能方面取得了进展，但往往忽视了LoRA优化空间与全参数空间之间的关系。在LoRA空间的损失景观中看似平坦的解，在全参数空间中仍可能表现出尖锐的方向，这可能损害泛化能力。我们引入了Flat-LoRA，旨在识别位于全参数空间平坦区域中的低秩适应。我们没有采用已建立的敏锐度感知最小化方法，该方法会带来显著的计算和内存开销，而是使用贝叶斯期望损失目标来保持训练效率。此外，我们设计了一种改进的随机扰动生成策略以提升性能，并通过随机种子仔细管理内存开销。在包括数学推理、编码能力、对话生成、指令遵循和文本到图像生成等多样任务上的实验表明，Flat-LoRA提高了领域内和领域外的泛化能力。代码可在https://github.com/nblt/Flat-LoRA获取。

[No.286] Mind the Gap: a Spectral Analysis of Rank Collapse and Signal Propagation in Attention Layers
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 注意差距：注意力层中排名崩溃和信号传播的光谱分析
类别: theory->deep learning
作者: Thiziri Nait Saada;Alireza Naderi;Jared Tanner;
机构: University of Oxford;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/43837
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07799
英摘: Attention layers are the core component of transformers, the current state-of-the-art neural network architecture. Alternatives to softmax-based attention are being explored due to its tendency to hinder effective information flow. Even at initialisation, it remains poorly understood why the propagation of signals and gradients through these random networks can be pathological, resulting in issues known as (i) vanishing/exploding gradients and (ii) rank collapse $\textit{in depth}$, i.e. when all tokens converge to a single representation along layers. While rank collapse in depth naturally arises from repeated matrix multiplications$\unicode{x2013}$a common pattern across various architectures$\unicode{x2013}$we identify an additional and previously unknown challenge unique to softmax attention layers: (iii) rank collapse $\textit{in width}$, which occurs as the context length increases. Using Random Matrix Theory, we conduct a rigorous analysis that uncovers a spectral gap between the two largest singular values of the attention matrix as the cause of (iii), which in turn exacerbates (i) and (ii). Building on this insight, we propose a novel yet simple practical solution to mitigate rank collapse in width by removing the outlier eigenvalue(s). Our theoretical framework offers a fresh perspective on recent practical studies, such as (Ye et al., 2024; Ali et al., 2023), whose ad hoc solutions can now be interpreted as implicit efforts to address the spectral gap issue. This work provides valuable theoretical support for ongoing large-scale empirical research, bringing theory and practice one step closer in the understanding of transformers.
中摘: 注意力层是当前最先进的神经网络架构——Transformer的核心组成部分。由于基于softmax的注意力机制倾向于阻碍信息的有效流动，因此人们正在探索其替代方案。即使在初始化阶段，我们仍不清楚为何信号和梯度通过这些随机网络的传播会存在问题，导致所谓的(i) 梯度消失/爆炸 和 (ii) 深度上的秩坍塌，即当所有令牌沿层次结构收敛为单一表示时。尽管深度上的秩坍塌自然地源自于矩阵乘法的重复应用——这是在各种架构中常见的模式——我们识别到了一个额外的、之前未知的独特挑战，仅与基于softmax的注意力层相关：(iii) 宽度上的秩坍塌，它随着上下文长度的增加而发生。借助随机矩阵理论，我们进行了一项严谨的分析，揭示了注意力矩阵两个最大奇异值之间的谱间隙是(iii)的原因，这反过来又加剧了(i)和(ii)的问题。基于这一见解，我们提出了一种新颖但简单的实际解决方案，通过移除异常特征值来缓解宽度上的秩坍塌。我们的理论框架为最近的实际研究提供了新的视角，例如（Ye等人，2024；Ali等人，2023），其中的临时解决方案现在可以被解释为隐含地解决谱间隙问题的努力。这项工作为正在进行的大规模实证研究提供了宝贵的理论支持，将理论与实践在理解Transformer方面拉近了一步。

[No.288] Permutation-based Rank Test in the Presence of Discretization and Application in Causal Discovery with Mixed Data
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 基于排列的秩检验在离散化存在情况下的应用及其在混合数据因果发现中的使用
类别: general machine learning->causality
作者: Xinshuai Dong;Ignavier Ng;Boyang Sun;Haoyue Dai;Guang-Yuan Hao;Shunxing Fan;Peter Spirtes;Yumou Qiu;Kun Zhang;
机构: Carnegie Mellon University;Mohamed bin Zayed University of Artificial Intelligence;Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;Iowa State University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45077
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.18990
英摘: Recent advances have shown that statistical tests for the rank of cross-covariance matrices play an important role in causal discovery. These rank tests include partial correlation tests as special cases and provide further graphical information about latent variables. Existing rank tests typically assume that all the continuous variables can be perfectly measured, and yet, in practice many variables can only be measured after discretization. For example, in psychometric studies, the continuous level of certain personality dimensions of a person can only be measured after being discretized into order-preserving options such as disagree, neutral, and agree. Motivated by this, we propose Mixed data Permutation-based Rank Test (MPRT), which properly controls the statistical errors even when some or all variables are discretized. Theoretically, we establish the exchangeability and estimate the asymptotic null distribution by permutations; as a consequence, MPRT can effectively control the Type I error in the presence of discretization while previous methods cannot. Empirically, our method is validated by extensive experiments on synthetic data and real-world data to demonstrate its effectiveness as well as applicability in causal discovery.
中摘: 最近的研究进展表明，用于协方差矩阵秩的统计检验在因果发现中扮演着重要角色。这些秩检验包括偏相关检验作为特殊情况，并提供了关于潜在变量的进一步图形信息。现有的秩检验通常假设所有连续变量都能被完美测量，然而，在实践中许多变量只能在离散化后才能被测量。例如，在心理测量研究中，一个人某些人格维度的连续水平只能在被离散化为保持顺序的选项如不同意、中立和同意后才能被测量。受此启发，我们提出了混合数据排列秩检验（Mixed data Permutation-based Rank Test, MPRT），即使部分或全部变量被离散化，也能适当地控制统计误差。理论上，我们建立了可交换性并通过对排列的估计来确定渐近空分布；因此，MPRT能够在存在离散化的情况下有效控制第一类错误，而先前的方法则不能。经验上，我们的方法通过在合成数据和真实世界数据上的广泛实验得到了验证，证明了其在因果发现中的有效性和适用性。

[No.289] Bipartite Ranking From Multiple Labels: On Loss Versus Label Aggregation
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 基于多标签的二部排名：关于损失函数与标签聚合的关系
类别: general machine learning->supervised learning
作者: Michal Lukasik;Lin Chen;Harikrishna Narasimhan;Aditya Krishna Menon;Wittawat Jitkrittum;Felix X. Yu;Sashank J. Reddi;Gang Fu;Mohammadhossein Bateni;Sanjiv Kumar;
机构: Google;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44399
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.11284
英摘: Bipartite ranking is a fundamental supervised learning problem, with the goal of learning a ranking over instances with maximal Area Under the ROC Curve (AUC) against a single binary target label. However, one may often observe multiple binary target labels, e.g., from distinct human annotators. How can one synthesize such labels into a single coherent ranking? In this work, we formally analyze two approaches to this problem -- loss aggregation and label aggregation -- by characterizing their Bayes-optimal solutions. We show that while both approaches can yield Pareto-optimal solutions, loss aggregation can exhibit label dictatorship: one can inadvertently (and undesirably) favor one label over others. This suggests that label aggregation can be preferable to loss aggregation, which we empirically verify.
中摘: 二部排名是一个基本的有监督学习问题，目标是学习一个实例的排名，以最大化针对单一二元目标标签的接收者操作特征曲线下的面积（AUC）。然而，我们经常观察到多个二元目标标签，例如，来自不同的标注员。如何将这些标签综合成一个连贯的排名？在本工作中，我们正式分析了解决这个问题的两种方法——损失聚合和标签聚合，通过描述它们的贝叶斯最优解来实现。我们表明，虽然这两种方法都能产生帕累托最优解，但损失聚合可能会表现出标签独裁：人们可能会不经意间（且不希望地）偏爱一个标签而不是其他标签。这表明标签聚合可能优于损失聚合，我们在经验上证实了这一点。

[No.291] IntLoRA: Integral Low-rank Adaptation of Quantized Diffusion Models
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: IntLoRA: 整体低秩量化扩散模型适应
类别: applications->computer vision
作者: Hang Guo;Yawei Li;Tao Dai;Shu-Tao Xia;Luca Benini;
机构: Tsinghua University;ETH Zurich;Shenzhen University;University of Bologna;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45657
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.21759
英摘: Fine-tuning pre-trained diffusion models under limited budgets has gained great success. In particular, the recent advances that directly fine-tune the quantized weights using Low-rank Adaptation (LoRA) further reduces training costs. Despite these progress, we point out that existing adaptation recipes are not inference-efficient. Specifically, additional post-training quantization (PTQ) on tuned weights is needed during deployment, which results in noticeable performance drop when the bit-width is low. Based on this observation, we introduce IntLoRA, which adapts quantized diffusion models with integer-type low-rank parameters, to include inference efficiency during tuning. [No.292] From Low Rank Gradient Subspace Stabilization to Low-Rank Weights: Observations, Theories, and Applications
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 从低秩梯度子空间稳定化到低秩权重：观察、理论与应用
类别: deep learning->foundation models
作者: AJAY KUMAR JAISWAL;Yifan Wang;Lu Yin;Shiwei Liu;Runjin Chen;Jiawei Zhao;Ananth Grama;Yuandong Tian;Zhangyang Wang;
机构: Apple;University of Texas at Austin;Purdue University;University of Surrey;University of Oxford;;Meta;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44501
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.11239
英摘: Large Language Models' (LLMs) weight matrices can often be expressed in low-rank form with potential to relax memory and compute resource requirements. Unlike prior efforts that focus on developing novel matrix decompositions, in this work we study the non-uniform low-rank properties of weight matrices in LLMs through the lens of stabilizing gradient subspace. First, we provide a theoretical framework to understand the stabilization of gradient subspaces through Hessian analysis. Second, we empirically establish an important relationship between gradient dynamics and low-rank expressiveness of weight matrices. Our findings reveal that different LLM components exhibit varying levels of converged low-rank structures, necessitating variable rank reduction across them to minimize drop in performance due to compression. Drawing on this result, we present Weight Low-Rank Projection(WeLore) that unifies weight compression and memory-efficient fine-tuning into one, in a data-agnostic and one-shot manner. When used as a compression technique, WeLore categorizes weight matrices into Low-rank Components (LRCs) and Non-Low-rank Components (N-LRCs) and suitably encodes them for minimum performance loss. Our gradient dynamics perspective illustrates that LRCs tend to have better fine-tuning capabilities and their standalone fine-tuning can closely mimic and sometimes outperform the training loss trajectory and performance of full fine-tuning with notable memory and compute footprint reduction. Codes are available at https://github.com/VITA-Group/WeLore.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLM）的权重矩阵通常可以以低秩形式表示，这有可能减轻内存和计算资源的需求。与以往专注于开发新型矩阵分解的工作不同，本文通过稳定梯度子空间的视角，研究了LLM中权重矩阵的非均匀低秩特性。首先，我们提供了一个理论框架，通过Hessian分析来理解梯度子空间的稳定化。其次，我们实证地建立了一个重要的关系，即梯度动态与权重矩阵的低秩表达性之间的联系。我们的发现揭示了不同的LLM组件展现出不同程度的收敛低秩结构，这要求在它们之间进行可变的秩降低，以最小化因压缩导致的性能下降。基于这一结果，我们提出了“权重低秩投影”（WeLore），它将权重压缩和高效的微调统一在一个数据无关且一次性完成的过程中。作为压缩技术使用时，WeLore将权重矩阵分类为低秩组件（LRC）和非低秩组件（N-LRC），并对其进行适当的编码以实现最小的性能损失。从梯度动态的角度来看，LRC往往具有更好的微调能力，其单独微调可以紧密模仿甚至有时超过全量微调的训练损失轨迹和性能，同时显著减少内存和计算占用。代码可在https://github.com/VITA-Group/WeLore获取。

[No.293] Pivoting Factorization: A Compact Meta Low-Rank Representation of Sparsity for Efficient Inference in Large Language Models
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 旋转因子分解：一种高效的大型语言模型推理中的紧凑元低秩稀疏表示
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Jialin Zhao;Yingtao Zhang;Carlo Vittorio Cannistraci;
机构: Tsinghua University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46433
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.19090
英摘: The rapid growth of Large Language Models has driven demand for effective model compression techniques to reduce memory and computation costs. Low-rank pruning has gained attention for its GPU compatibility across all densities. However, low-rank pruning struggles to match the performance of semi-structured pruning, often doubling perplexity at similar densities. In this paper, we propose Pivoting Factorization (PIFA), a novel lossless meta low-rank representation that unsupervisedly learns a compact form of any low-rank representation, effectively eliminating redundant information. PIFA identifies pivot rows (linearly independent rows) and expresses non-pivot rows as linear combinations, achieving 24.2% additional memory savings and 24.6% faster inference over low-rank layers at rank = 50% of dimension. To mitigate the performance degradation caused by low-rank pruning, we introduce a novel, retraining-free reconstruction method that minimizes error accumulation (M). MPIFA, combining M and PIFA into an end-to-end framework, significantly outperforms existing low-rank pruning methods, and achieves performance comparable to semi-structured pruning, while surpassing it in GPU efficiency and compatibility. Our code is available at https://github.com/biomedical-cybernetics/pivoting-factorization.
中摘: 大型语言模型的快速发展推动了对有效模型压缩技术的需求，以降低内存和计算成本。低秩剪枝因其在所有密度下与GPU的良好兼容性而受到关注。然而，低秩剪枝在性能上难以匹敌半结构化剪枝，在相似的密度下往往使困惑度翻倍。在这篇论文中，我们提出了一种名为Pivoting Factorization（PIFA）的新型无损元低秩表示方法，该方法可以无监督地学习任何低秩表示的紧凑形式，有效地消除了冗余信息。PIFA识别出支点行（线性独立的行），并将其余非支点行表达为这些支点行的线性组合，当低秩层的秩等于维度的50%时，这种方法能实现额外24.2%的内存节省和24.6%更快的推理速度。为了缓解低秩剪枝带来的性能下降，我们引入了一种新颖的、无需重新训练的重构方法，该方法最小化误差累积。将这种重构方法与PIFA结合到一个端到端框架中，即MPIFA，显著超越了现有的低秩剪枝方法，并且在性能上可与半结构化剪枝相媲美，同时在GPU效率和兼容性方面超过它。我们的代码可在https://github.com/biomedical-cybernetics/pivoting-factorization获取。

[No.297] Rank-One Modified Value Iteration
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 一维修改的价值迭代
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Arman Sharifi Kolarijani;Tolga Ok;Peyman Mohajerin Esfahani;Mohamad Amin Sharifi Kolarijani;
机构: Delft University of Technology;University of Toronto;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46510
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.01828
英摘: In this paper, we provide a novel algorithm for solving planning and learning problems of Markov decision processes. The proposed algorithm follows a policy iteration-type update by using a rank-one approximation of the transition probability matrix in the policy evaluation step. This rank-one approximation is closely related to the stationary distribution of the corresponding transition probability matrix, which is approximated using the power method. We provide theoretical guarantees for the convergence of the proposed algorithm to optimal (action-)value function with the same rate and computational complexity as the value iteration algorithm in the planning problem and as the Q-learning algorithm in the learning problem. Through our extensive numerical simulations, however, we show that the proposed algorithm consistently outperforms first-order algorithms and their accelerated versions for both planning and learning problems.
中摘: 在本文中，我们为解决马尔科夫决策过程的规划和学习问题提供了一种新颖的算法。所提出的算法采用策略迭代型更新，在策略评估步骤中使用转移概率矩阵的一阶近似。这一阶近似与相应的转移概率矩阵的稳定分布密切相关，该稳定分布通过幂法进行近似。我们为所提算法提供了理论保证，证明其在规划问题中以与价值迭代算法相同的速度和计算复杂度收敛至最优（动作）价值函数，在学习问题中则与Q学习算法相同。然而，通过我们广泛的数值模拟，我们展示了对于规划和学习问题，所提算法始终优于一阶算法及其加速版本。

[No.299] Does Low Rank Adaptation Lead to Lower Robustness against Training-Time Attacks?
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 低秩适应会导致对抗训练时间攻击的鲁棒性降低吗？
类别: social aspects->security
作者: Zi Liang;Haibo Hu;Qingqing Ye;Yaxin Xiao;RongHua Li;
机构: Hong Kong Polytechnic University;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44219
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12871
英摘: Low rank adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as a prominent technique for fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) thanks to its superb efficiency gains over previous methods. While extensive studies have examined the performance and structural properties of LoRA, its behavior upon training-time attacks remain underexplored, posing significant security risks. In this paper, we theoretically investigate the security implications of LoRA's low-rank structure during fine-tuning, in the context of its robustness against data poisoning and backdoor attacks. We propose an analytical framework that models LoRA's training dynamics, employs the neural tangent kernel to simplify the analysis of the training process, and applies information theory to establish connections between LoRA's low rank structure and its vulnerability against training-time attacks. Our analysis indicates that LoRA exhibits better robustness to backdoor attacks than full fine-tuning, while becomes more vulnerable to untargeted data poisoning due to its over-simplified information geometry. Extensive experimental evaluations have corroborated our theoretical findings.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）作为一种微调大型语言模型（LLM）的突出技术，因其相比先前方法的卓越效率提升而崭露头角。尽管已有大量研究探讨了LoRA的性能和结构特性，但在训练时攻击对其行为的影响仍然研究不足，这构成了重大的安全风险。本文从理论上探讨了LoRA在微调过程中的低秩结构对数据中毒和后门攻击鲁棒性的安全影响。我们提出了一种分析框架，该框架建模了LoRA的训练动态，利用神经切线核简化了训练过程的分析，并运用信息论建立了LoRA的低秩结构与训练时攻击脆弱性之间的联系。我们的分析表明，相比于全量微调，LoRA对后门攻击展现出更好的鲁棒性，但因信息几何过度简化而对无目标的数据中毒攻击变得更加脆弱。广泛的实验评估证实了我们的理论发现。

[No.301] Ehrenfeucht-Haussler Rank and Chain of Thought
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 厄伦费尔特-豪斯勒秩和思维链
类别: theory->learning theory
作者: Pablo Barcelo;Alexander Kozachinskiy;Tomasz Steifer;
机构: Pontificia Universidad Católica;Pontificia Universidad Catolica de Chile;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46252
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.12997
英摘: The notion of \emph{rank} of a Boolean function has been a cornerstone in PAC learning theory, enabling quasipolynomial-time learning algorithms for polynomial-size decision trees. We present a novel characterization of rank, grounded in the well-known Transformer architecture. We show that the rank of a function $f$ corresponds to the minimum number of \emph{Chain of Thought} (CoT) steps required by a single-layer Transformer with hard attention to compute $f$. Based on this characterization we establish tight bounds on the number of CoT steps required for specific problems, showing that \(\ell\)-fold function composition necessitates exactly \(\ell\) CoT steps. Furthermore, we analyze the problem of identifying the position of the \(k\)-th occurrence of 1 in a Boolean sequence, proving that it requires \(k\) CoT steps. Finally, we introduce the notion of the multi-head rank that captures multi-head single-layer transformers, and perform the analysis of PAC-learnability of the classes of functions with bounded multi-head rank.
中摘: 布尔函数的\emph{秩}概念一直是PAC学习理论的基石，使得能够以准多项式时间学习算法来学习多项式大小的决策树。我们提出了一种对秩的新颖表征，基于广为人知的Transformer架构。我们证明了一个函数$f$的秩对应于单层Transformer使用硬注意力计算$f$所需的最小\emph{思考链}（CoT）步骤数。基于这一表征，我们为特定问题建立了关于所需CoT步骤数的紧密界限，表明$\ell$次函数复合需要恰好$\ell$个CoT步骤。此外，我们分析了确定布尔序列中第$k$次出现的1的位置的问题，证明它需要$k$个CoT步骤。最后，我们引入了多头秩的概念，涵盖了多头单层变换器，并对具有有限多头秩的函数类的PAC可学习性进行了分析。

为了清晰起见，这里的“思考链”（Chain of Thought，简称CoT）是指在Transformer模型中处理逻辑和推理任务时的一系列内部操作或步骤，而“多头秩”则是针对多头注意力机制下的单层Transformer模型的一个扩展概念，用于描述这类模型处理复杂函数的能力。我们的研究揭示了这些概念与布尔函数学习之间的深层联系。

[No.303] Universal Biological Sequence Reranking for Improved De Novo Peptide Sequencing
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 通用生物序列重新排序以提高从头肽测序
类别: applications->everything else
作者: Zijie Qiu;Jiaqi Wei;Xiang Zhang;Sheng Xu;Kai Zou;Zhi Jin;ZhiQiang Gao;Nanqing Dong;Siqi Sun;
机构: Fudan University;Zhejiang University;;Shanghai AI Laboratory;Netmind;Protagolabs;Shanghai Innovation Institute;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45758
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17552
英摘: De novo peptide sequencing is a critical task in proteomics. However, the performance of current deep learning-based methods is limited by the inherent complexity of mass spectrometry data and the heterogeneous distribution of noise signals, leading to data-specific biases. We present RankNovo, the first deep reranking framework that enhances de novo peptide sequencing by leveraging the complementary strengths of multiple sequencing models. RankNovo employs a list-wise reranking approach, modeling candidate peptides as multiple sequence alignments and utilizing axial attention to extract informative features across candidates. Additionally, we introduce two new metrics, PMD (Peptide Mass Deviation) and RMD (residual Mass Deviation), which offer delicate supervision by quantifying mass differences between peptides at both the sequence and residue levels. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RankNovo not only surpasses its base models used to generate training candidates for reranking pre-training, but also sets a new state-of-the-art benchmark. Moreover, RankNovo exhibits strong zero-shot generalization to unseen models whose generations were not exposed during training, highlighting its robustness and potential as a universal reranking framework for peptide sequencing. Our work presents a novel reranking strategy that fundamentally challenges existing single-model paradigms and advances the frontier of accurate de novo sequencing. Our source code is provided on GitHub.
中摘: 从头开始的肽序列分析是蛋白质组学中的一个关键任务。然而，当前基于深度学习的方法的表现受限于质谱数据的内在复杂性和噪声信号的异质分布，这导致了数据特异性偏见。我们提出了RankNovo，这是首个深度重排序框架，通过利用多种序列模型的互补优势来增强从头开始的肽序列分析。RankNovo采用列表式的重排序方法，将候选肽建模为多个序列比对，并使用轴向注意力提取跨候选肽的信息特征。此外，我们引入了两个新的指标，PMD（肽质量偏差）和RMD（残余质量偏差），它们通过量化肽在序列和残基水平上的质量差异提供精细的监督。广泛的实验表明，RankNovo不仅超越了用于生成重排序预训练候选肽的基础模型，而且树立了一个新的最先进基准。更重要的是，RankNovo展现出强大的零样本泛化能力，即使对于训练过程中未接触的未知模型生成的数据，也表现出了其鲁棒性和作为肽序列分析通用重排序框架的潜力。我们的工作提出了一种新颖的重排序策略，从根本上挑战现有的单一模型范式，并推动了准确的从头开始序列分析的前沿发展。我们的源代码已在GitHub上提供。

英摘: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as a leading parameter-efficient fine-tuning technique for adapting large foundation models, yet it often locks adapters into suboptimal minima near their initialization. By stochastically deactivating adapters, CoTo encourages more balanced optimization and broader exploration of the loss landscape. [No.306] ResQ: Mixed-Precision Quantization of Large Language Models with Low-Rank Residuals
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: ResQ：具有低秩残差的大型语言模型混合精度量化
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Utkarsh Saxena;Sayeh Sharify;Kaushik Roy;Xin Wang;
机构: Purdue University;d-Matrix;d-Matrix Corp.;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46466
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.14363
英摘: Post-training quantization (PTQ) of large language models (LLMs) holds the promise in reducing the prohibitive computational cost at inference time. Quantization of all weight, activation and key-value (KV) cache tensors to 4-bit without significantly degrading generalizability is challenging, due to the high quantization error caused by extreme outliers in activations. To tackle this problem, we propose ResQ, a PTQ method that pushes further the state-of-the-art. By means of principal component analysis (PCA), it identifies a low-rank subspace (in practice 1/8 of the hidden dimension) in which activation variances are highest, and keep the coefficients within this subspace in high precision, e.g. 8-bit, while quantizing the rest to 4-bit. Within each subspace, invariant random rotation is applied to further suppress outliers. We show that this is a provably optimal mixed precision quantization scheme that minimizes error. With the Llama and Qwen2.5 families of models, we demonstrate that ResQ outperforms recent uniform and mixed precision PTQ methods on a variety of benchmarks, achieving up to 33\% lower perplexity on Wikitext than the next best method SpinQuant, and upto 3\times speedup over 16-bit baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/utkarsh-dmx/project-resq.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLM）的后训练量化（PTQ）有望降低推理时的计算成本。将所有权重、激活和键值（KV）缓存张量量化为4位而不显著降低泛化能力具有挑战性，这是因为激活中的极端异常值导致的高量化误差。为了解决这个问题，我们提出ResQ，这是一种推动了现有技术界限的PTQ方法。通过主成分分析（PCA），它识别出激活方差最高的低秩子空间（实践中约为隐藏维度的1/8），并在这个子空间内保持系数的高精度，例如8位，而将其余部分量化为4位。在每个子空间内，应用不变随机旋转以进一步抑制异常值。我们证明这是一种可证明的最优混合精度量化方案，可以最小化误差。通过Llama和Qwen2.5系列模型，我们展示了ResQ在多种基准测试中优于近期的均匀和混合精度PTQ方法，在Wikitext上的困惑度比次佳方法SpinQuant低33%，并且相比16位基线速度提高了最多3倍。代码可在https://github.com/utkarsh-dmx/project-resq获取。

英摘: Test-time adaptation aims to adapt to realistic environments in an online manner by learning during test time. 英摘: In this paper, we focus on a matrix factorization-based approach to recover low-rank {\it asymmetric} matrices from corrupted measurements. [No.314] OrthoRank: Token Selection via Sink Token Orthogonality for Efficient LLM inference
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 正交排名：基于Sink token正交性的高效LLM推理中的词元选择
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Seungjun Shin;Jaehoon Oh;Dokwan Oh;
机构: Samsung;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46443
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.03865
英摘: Attention mechanisms are central to the success of large language models (LLMs), enabling them to capture intricate token dependencies and implicitly assign importance to each token. Recent studies have revealed the sink token, which receives disproportionately high attention despite their limited semantic role. In this paper, we first expand the relationship between the sink token and other tokens, moving beyond attention to explore their similarity in hidden states, considering the layer depth. We observe that as the layers get deeper, the cosine similarity between the normalized hidden states of the sink token and those of other tokens increases, and that the normalized hidden states of the sink token exhibit negligible changes. These imply that other tokens consistently are directed toward the sink token throughout the layers. Next, we propose a dynamic token selection method, called OrthoRank, using these findings to select important tokens. Specifically, in a certain layer, we define token importance by the speed at which the token moves toward the sink token. This is converted into orthogonality with the sink token, meaning that tokens that are more orthogonal to the sink token are assigned greater importance. Finally, through extensive experiments, we demonstrated that our method results in lower perplexity and higher zero-shot accuracy compared to layer pruning methods at the same sparsity ratio with comparable throughput, while also achieving superior performance on LongBench.
中摘: 注意力机制对于大型语言模型（LLM）的成功至关重要，使它们能够捕捉复杂的令牌依赖关系，并隐式地为每个令牌分配重要性。最近的研究揭示了“sink”令牌，尽管其语义作用有限，却获得了不成比例的高关注。在这篇论文中，我们首先扩展了“sink”令牌与其他令牌之间的关系，超越了注意力层面，探索它们在隐藏状态中的相似性，同时考虑了层深度。我们观察到，随着层数的加深，“sink”令牌与其它令牌之间归一化隐藏状态的余弦相似性增加，且“sink”令牌的归一化隐藏状态几乎不变。这表明，在各层中，其他令牌持续地向“sink”令牌靠拢。接下来，我们提出了一种动态令牌选择方法，称为OrthoRank，利用这些发现来选择重要令牌。具体而言，在某一特定层中，我们通过令牌向“sink”令牌移动的速度来定义令牌的重要性。这转化为与“sink”令牌的正交性，即与“sink”令牌更加正交的令牌被赋予更高的重要性。最后，通过广泛的实验，我们证明了我们的方法在相同的稀疏比下，相比层级剪枝方法，可以实现更低的困惑度和更高的零样本准确性，同时保持相当的吞吐量，并在LongBench上取得了更优异的表现。

[No.316] SLiM: One-shot Quantization and Sparsity with Low-rank Approximation for LLM Weight Compression
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: SLiM：基于低秩逼近的一次性量化和稀疏化以压缩LLM权重
类别: deep learning
作者: Mohammad Mozaffari;Amir Yazdanbakhsh;Maryam Mehri Dehnavi;
机构: University of Toronto;Google;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46479
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09615
英摘: Conventional model compression techniques for LLMs address high memory consumption and slow inference challenges but typically require computationally expensive retraining to preserve accuracy. In contrast, one-shot compression methods eliminate retraining cost, but struggle to achieve accuracy comparable to dense models. This paper presents SLIM, a new one-shot compression framework that holistically integrates hardware-friendly quantization, sparsity, and low-rank approximation into a unified process. First, we formulate the quantization process using a probabilistic approach (SLIM-Quant) that enables us to apply uniform quantization. Then, we use an existing one-shot pruning method to apply semi-structured sparsity on top of the quantized weights. Finally, to compensate for the introduced aggregated quantization and sparsity error, we use a novel saliency function with unique invertible and additive features that enables us to mathematically compute the value of low-rank adapters. SLIM improves model accuracy by up to 5.66% (LLaMA-2-7B) for 2:4 sparsity with 4-bit weight quantization, outperforming prior methods. Models compressed with SLIM achieve up to 4.3x and 3.8x on Nvidia RTX3060 and A100 GPUs, respectively. Additionally, they achieve up to 0.23x end-to-end memory reduction in comparison to their dense counterparts. We also propose an optional PEFT recipe that further improves accuracy by up to 1.66% (LLaMA-2-13B) compared to SLIM without fine-tuning.
中摘: 传统的大型语言模型（LLM）模型压缩技术解决了高内存消耗和推理速度慢的问题，但通常需要耗费大量计算资源的重新训练以保持准确性。相比之下，一步到位的压缩方法消除了重新训练的成本，但在达到与密集模型相媲美的精度方面存在困难。本文介绍了一种名为SLIM的新的一次性压缩框架，它将对硬件友好的量化、稀疏性和低秩近似整体集成到一个统一的过程中。首先，我们使用概率方法来制定量化过程（SLIM-Quant），使我们能够应用均匀量化。然后，我们采用现有的一步到位剪枝方法，在量化权重上实现半结构化稀疏性。最后，为了补偿引入的综合量化和稀疏性误差，我们使用了一个具有独特可逆性和加法特性的新颖显著性函数，使我们能够在数学上计算低秩适配器的值。SLIM在2:4稀疏性与4位权重量化的情况下，将模型精度提高了最多5.66%（对于LLaMA-2-7B），超过了先前的方法。使用SLIM压缩的模型在Nvidia RTX3060和A100 GPU上分别达到了最高4.3倍和3.8倍的性能提升。此外，它们相比密集模型实现了最高0.23倍的端到端内存减少。我们还提出了一种可选的PEFT（Prompt Encoder Fine-Tuning）配方，与未进行微调的SLIM相比，进一步将精度提高了最多1.66%（对于LLaMA-2-13B）。

[No.318] Ranked from Within: Ranking Large Multimodal Models Without Labels
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 来自内部排名：无需标签排名大型多模态模型
类别: social aspects->robustness
作者: Weijie Tu;Weijian Deng;Dylan Campbell;Yu Yao;Jiyang Zheng;Tom Gedeon;Tongliang Liu;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06461
英摘: Can the relative performance of a pre-trained large multimodal model (LMM) be predicted without access to labels? As LMMs proliferate, it becomes increasingly important to develop efficient ways to choose between them when faced with new data or tasks. The usual approach does the equivalent of giving the models an exam and marking them. We opt to avoid marking and the associated labor of determining the ground-truth answers. Instead, we explore other signals elicited and ascertain how well the models know their own limits, evaluating the effectiveness of these signals at unsupervised model ranking. We evaluate $47$ state-of-the-art LMMs (\eg, LLaVA) across $9$ visual question answering benchmarks, analyzing how well uncertainty-based metrics can predict relative model performance. [No.320] Secant Line Search for Frank-Wolfe Algorithms

英摘: We present a new step-size strategy based on the secant method for Frank-Wolfe algorithms. [No.322] Adaptively Learning to Select-Rank in Online Platforms
来源: ICML 2024
命中: rank
标题: 自适应学习在线平台中的选择排序
类别: 
作者: Jingyuan Wang;Perry Dong;Ying Jin;Ruohan Zhan;Zhengyuan Zhou;
机构: New York University;Stanford University;Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/33517
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.05017
英摘: Ranking algorithms are fundamental to various online platforms across e-commerce sites to content streaming services. Our research addresses the challenge of adaptively ranking items from a candidate pool for heterogeneous users, a key component in personalizing user experience. We develop a user response model that considers diverse user preferences and the varying effects of item positions, aiming to optimize overall user satisfaction with the ranked list. We frame this problem within a contextual bandits framework, with each ranked list as an action. Our approach incorporates an upper confidence bound to adjust predicted user satisfaction scores and selects the ranking action that maximizes these adjusted scores, efficiently solved via maximum weight imperfect matching. We demonstrate that our algorithm achieves a cumulative regret bound of $O(d\sqrt{NKT})$ for ranking $K$ out of $N$ items in a $d$-dimensional context space over $T$ rounds, under the assumption that user responses follow a generalized linear model. This regret alleviates dependence on the ambient action space, whose cardinality grows exponentially with $N$ and $K$ (thus rendering direct application of existing adaptive learning algorithms -- such as UCB or Thompson sampling -- infeasible). Experiments conducted on both simulated and real-world datasets demonstrate our algorithm outperforms the baseline.
中摘: 排名算法对于从电子商务网站到内容流媒体服务的各种在线平台至关重要。我们的研究针对了为异质性用户从候选池中自适应地对项目进行排名的挑战，这是个性化用户体验的关键组成部分。我们开发了一个用户响应模型，考虑了多样化的用户偏好以及项目位置的差异效应，旨在优化排名列表的整体用户满意度。我们将这个问题置于上下文强盗框架内，将每个排名列表视为一个动作。我们的方法结合了置信上界，以调整预测的用户满意度得分，并选择能最大化这些调整后得分的排名动作，通过最大权重不完美匹配有效地解决。我们证明了在假设用户响应遵循广义线性模型的情况下，我们的算法在d维上下文空间中对N个物品中的K个进行排名，在T轮中实现了累计遗憾界限为$O(d\sqrt{NKT})$。这种遗憾缓解了对随N和K指数增长的环境动作空间的依赖（这使得直接应用现有的自适应学习算法，如UCB或汤普森采样，在此场景下不可行）。在模拟数据集和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，我们的算法优于基线。

这段翻译尽可能保持了原文的专业性和准确性，将英文的学术论文摘要翻译成了中文。

[No.323] Amortizing Pragmatic Program Synthesis with Rankings
来源: ICML 2024
命中: rank
标题: 基于排名的实用程序程序合成的摊销化
类别: 
作者: Yewen Pu;Saujas Vaduguru;Priyan Vaithilingam;Elena Glassman;Daniel Fried;
机构: Autodesk;Harvard University;Carnegie Mellon University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/32658
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2309.03225
英摘: In program synthesis, an intelligent system takes in a set of user-generated examples and returns a program that is logically consistent with these examples. The usage of Rational Speech Acts (RSA) framework has been successful in building \emph{pragmatic} program synthesizers that return programs which -- in addition to being logically consistent -- account for the fact that a user chooses their examples informatively. However, the computational burden of running the RSA algorithm has restricted the application of pragmatic program synthesis to domains with a small number of possible programs. This work presents a novel method of amortizing the RSA algorithm by leveraging a \emph{global pragmatic ranking} -- a single, total ordering of all the hypotheses. We prove that for a pragmatic synthesizer that uses a single demonstration, our global ranking method exactly replicates RSA's ranked responses. We further empirically show that global rankings effectively approximate the full pragmatic synthesizer in an online, multi-demonstration setting. Experiments on two program synthesis domains using our pragmatic ranking method resulted in orders of magnitudes of speed ups compared to the RSA synthesizer, while outperforming the standard, non-pragmatic synthesizer.
中摘: 在程序合成领域，智能系统接收一组用户生成的例子，并返回与这些例子逻辑上一致的程序。利用理性言语行为（Rational Speech Acts，简称RSA）框架已经成功地构建了“实用主义”程序合成器，这类合成器返回的程序不仅逻辑上一致，而且考虑到用户选择例子时具有信息性。然而，运行RSA算法的计算负担限制了实用主义程序合成的应用范围，仅限于可能程序数量较小的领域。本文提出了一种新颖的方法来分摊RSA算法的计算成本，即利用“全局实用主义排序”——一种对所有假设进行单一、总排序的方式。我们证明了对于使用单一演示的实用主义合成器，我们的全局排序方法完全复制了RSA的排序响应。此外，我们在在线、多演示设置中进一步实证表明，全局排序有效地近似了完整的实用主义合成器。在两个程序合成领域使用我们的实用主义排序方法进行的实验结果表明，与RSA合成器相比，速度提高了几个数量级，同时在性能上超过了标准的非实用主义合成器。

[No.324] Assessing the Brittleness of Safety Alignment via Pruning and Low-Rank Modifications
来源: ICML 2024
命中: rank
标题: 通过剪枝和低秩修改评估安全对齐的脆性
类别: 
作者: Boyi Wei;Kaixuan Huang;Yangsibo Huang;Tinghao Xie;Xiangyu Qi;Mengzhou Xia;Prateek Mittal;Mengdi Wang;Peter Henderson;
机构: Princeton University;Meta;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/34354
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.05162
英摘: Large language models (LLMs) show inherent brittleness in their safety mechanisms, as evidenced by their susceptibility to jailbreaking and even non-malicious fine-tuning. This study explores this brittleness of safety alignment by leveraging pruning and low-rank modifications. We develop methods to identify critical regions that are vital for safety guardrails, and that are disentangled from utility-relevant regions at both the neuron and rank levels. Surprisingly, the isolated regions we find are sparse, comprising about $3\%$ at the parameter level and $2.5\%$ at the rank level. Removing these regions compromises safety without significantly impacting utility, corroborating the inherent brittleness of the model's safety mechanisms. Moreover, we show that LLMs remain vulnerable to low-cost fine-tuning attacks even when modifications to the safety-critical regions are restricted. These findings underscore the urgent need for more robust safety strategies in LLMs.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLM）在其安全机制中显示出固有的脆弱性，这一点通过它们容易被越狱甚至非恶意微调所证实。本研究通过利用剪枝和低秩修改来探索这种安全对齐的脆弱性。我们开发了识别对于安全护栏至关重要的关键区域的方法，并在神经元和秩级别上将这些区域与与效用相关的区域分离。令人惊讶的是，我们发现的孤立区域很稀疏，在参数级别上约占3%，在秩级别上约占2.5%。移除这些区域会损害安全性，而不会显著影响效用，这证实了模型安全机制的固有脆弱性。此外，我们表明，即使限制对安全关键区域的修改，LLM仍然容易受到低成本微调攻击。这些发现突显了在LLM中迫切需要更强大的安全策略。

[No.325] Asymmetry in Low-Rank Adapters of Foundation Models
来源: ICML 2024
命中: rank
标题: 基础模型低秩适配器中的不对称性
类别: 
作者: Jiacheng Zhu;Kristjan Greenewald;Kimia Nadjahi;Haitz Sáez de Ocáriz Borde;Rickard Brüel Gabrielsson;Leshem Choshen;Marzyeh Ghassemi;Mikhail Yurochkin;Justin Solomon;
机构: Massachusetts Institute of Technology;IBM;University of Oxford;International Business Machines Corporation;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/32849
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.16842
英摘: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning optimizes large, pre-trained foundation models by updating a subset of parameters; in this class, Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is particularly effective. Inspired by an effort to investigate the different roles of LoRA matrices during fine-tuning, this paper characterizes and leverages unexpected asymmetry in the importance of low-rank adapter matrices. Specifically, when updating the parameter matrices of a neural network by adding a product $BA$, we observe that the $B$ and $A$ matrices have distinct functions: $A$ extracts features from the input, while $B$ uses these features to create the desired output. Based on this observation, we demonstrate that fine-tuning $B$ is inherently more effective than fine-tuning $A$, and that a random untrained $A$ should perform nearly as well as a fine-tuned one. Using an information-theoretic lens, we also bound the generalization of low-rank adapters, showing that the parameter savings of exclusively training $B$ improves the bound. We support our conclusions with experiments on RoBERTa, BART-Large, LLaMA-2, and ViTs.
中摘: 参数高效的微调通过更新预训练基础模型的一小部分参数来优化这些大型模型；在此类别中，低秩适应（LoRA）特别有效。受探究LoRA矩阵在微调过程中不同作用的努力启发，本文表征并利用了低秩适配器矩阵重要性的意外不对称性。具体来说，当通过添加乘积$BA$来更新神经网络的参数矩阵时，我们观察到$B$和$A$矩阵具有不同的功能：$A$从输入中提取特征，而$B$则使用这些特征来生成所需的输出。基于这一观察，我们证明了微调$B$本质上比微调$A$更有效，并且随机未训练的$A$应该几乎与经过微调的一样好。采用信息论的角度，我们还界定了低秩适配器的泛化能力，表明仅训练$B$的参数节省改善了界限。我们通过在RoBERTa、BART-Large、LLaMA-2和ViTs上的实验支持我们的结论。

[No.326] BOtied: Multi-objective Bayesian optimization with tied multivariate ranks
来源: ICML 2024
命中: rank
标题: 多目标贝叶斯优化与绑定多元排名
类别: 
作者: Ji Won Park;Natasa Tagasovska;Michael Maser;Stephen Ra;Kyunghyun Cho;
机构: Genentech;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/33580
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.00344
英摘: Many scientific and industrial applications require the joint optimization of multiple, potentially competing objectives. Multi-objective Bayesian optimization (MOBO) is a sample-efficient framework for identifying Pareto-optimal solutions. At the heart of MOBO is the acquisition function, which determines the next candidate to evaluate by navigating the best compromises among the objectives. In this paper, we show a natural connection between non-dominated solutions and the extreme quantile of the joint cumulative distribution function (CDF). Motivated by this link, we propose the Pareto-compliant CDF indicator and the associated acquisition function, BOtied. BOtied inherits desirable invariance properties of the CDF, and an efficient implementation with copulas allows it to scale to many objectives. Our experiments on a variety of synthetic and real-world problems demonstrate that BOtied outperforms state-of-the-art MOBO acquisition functions while being computationally efficient for many objectives.
中摘: 许多科学和工业应用需要对多个可能相互竞争的目标进行联合优化。多目标贝叶斯优化（MOBO）是一个样本高效的框架，用于识别帕累托最优解。MOBO的核心是获取函数，它通过在各目标间寻找最佳折衷来确定下一个要评估的候选方案。在这篇论文中，我们展示了非支配解与联合累积分布函数（CDF）的极端分位数之间自然的联系。受此联系的启发，我们提出了符合帕累托原则的CDF指标及其相关获取函数，即BOtied。BOtied继承了CDF所具有的理想不变性属性，并且利用copulas的有效实现在处理多个目标时具有扩展性。我们在一系列合成和现实问题上的实验表明，BOtied在计算效率高的同时，其性能超越了现有最先进的MOBO获取函数。

[No.327] Causal Customer Churn Analysis with Low-rank Tensor Block Hazard Model
来源: ICML 2024

类别:

英摘: This study introduces an innovative method for analyzing the impact of various interventions on customer churn, using the potential outcomes framework. We present a new causal model, the tensorized latent factor block hazard model, which incorporates tensor completion methods for a principled causal analysis of customer churn. A crucial element of our approach is the formulation of a 1-bit tensor completion for the parameter tensor. [No.328] CoLoRA: Continuous low-rank adaptation for reduced implicit neural modeling of parameterized partial differential equations
来源: ICML 2024
命中: rank
标题: CoLoRA：连续低秩适应方法用于参数化偏微分方程的简化隐式神经建模
类别: 
作者: Jules Berman;Benjamin Peherstorfer;
机构: New York University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/33364
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.14646
英摘: This work introduces reduced models based on Continuous Low Rank Adaptation (CoLoRA) that pre-train neural networks for a given partial differential equation and then continuously adapt low-rank weights in time to rapidly predict the evolution of solution fields at new physics parameters and new initial conditions. The adaptation can be either purely data-driven or via an equation-driven variational approach that provides Galerkin-optimal approximations. Because CoLoRA approximates solution fields locally in time, the rank of the weights can be kept small, which means that only few training trajectories are required offline so that CoLoRA is well suited for data-scarce regimes. Predictions with CoLoRA are orders of magnitude faster than with classical methods and their accuracy and parameter efficiency is higher compared to other neural network approaches.
中摘: 本工作介绍了基于持续低秩适应（CoLoRA）的简化模型，该模型首先对给定的偏微分方程进行神经网络预训练，然后连续地在时间上调整低秩权重，以快速预测在新的物理参数和初值条件下的解场演变。这种适应既可以是纯数据驱动的，也可以通过方程驱动的变分方法实现，该方法提供了伽辽金最优逼近。由于CoLoRA在时间上局部逼近解场，权重的秩可以保持很小，这意味着仅需离线准备少量的训练轨迹，因此CoLoRA非常适合数据稀缺的情况。与经典方法相比，使用CoLoRA进行预测的速度快了几个数量级，其准确性和参数效率也高于其他神经网络方法。

[No.329] Compressible Dynamics in Deep Overparameterized Low-Rank Learning & Adaptation
来源: ICML 2024
命中: rank
标题: 可压缩动力学在深度过参数化低秩学习与适应中的作用
类别: 
作者: Can Yaras;Peng Wang;Laura Balzano;Qing Qu;
机构: University of Michigan;
类型: Oral
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/32837
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.04112
英摘: While overparameterization in machine learning models offers great benefits in terms of optimization and generalization, it also leads to increased computational requirements as model sizes grow. In this work, we show that by leveraging the inherent low-dimensional structures of data and compressible dynamics within the model parameters, we can reap the benefits of overparameterization without the computational burdens. In practice, we demonstrate the effectiveness of this approach for deep low-rank matrix completion as well as fine-tuning language models. Our approach is grounded in theoretical findings for deep overparameterized low-rank matrix recovery, where we show that the learning dynamics of each weight matrix are confined to an invariant low-dimensional subspace. Consequently, we can construct and train compact, highly compressed factorizations possessing the same benefits as their overparameterized counterparts. In the context of deep matrix completion, our technique substantially improves training efficiency while retaining the advantages of overparameterization. For language model fine-tuning, we propose a method called "Deep LoRA", which improves the existing low-rank adaptation (LoRA) technique, leading to reduced overfitting and a simplified hyperparameter setup, while maintaining comparable efficiency. We validate the effectiveness of Deep LoRA on natural language tasks, particularly when fine-tuning with limited data. Our code is available at https://github.com/cjyaras/deep-lora-transformers.
中摘: 虽然机器学习模型中的过度参数化在优化和泛化方面带来了巨大优势，但也随着模型规模的增长导致计算需求增加。在这项工作中，我们证明了通过利用数据内在的低维结构和模型参数内的可压缩动态，可以在没有计算负担的情况下享受过度参数化的益处。实际上，我们为深度低秩矩阵补全以及语言模型微调展示了这种方法的有效性。我们的方法基于对深度过度参数化低秩矩阵恢复的理论发现，在这些发现中，我们表明每个权重矩阵的学习动态被限制在一个不变的低维子空间内。因此，我们可以构建并训练紧凑、高度压缩的分解，这些分解具有与过度参数化对应物相同的优势。在深度矩阵补全的背景下，我们的技术显著提高了训练效率，同时保留了过度参数化的优点。对于语言模型微调，我们提出了一种称为“Deep LoRA”的方法，它改进了现有的低秩适应（LoRA）技术，从而减少了过拟合，简化了超参数设置，同时保持了相当的效率。我们在自然语言任务上验证了Deep LoRA的有效性，特别是在使用有限数据进行微调时。我们的代码可在https://github.com/cjyaras/deep-lora-transformers获取。

[No.330] Conformal Prediction for Deep Classifier via Label Ranking
来源: ICML 2024
命中: rank
标题: 基于标签排名的深度分类器同构预测
类别: 
作者: Jianguo Huang;HuaJun Xi;Linjun Zhang;Huaxiu Yao;Yue Qiu;Hongxin Wei;
机构: ShanghaiTech University;Southern University of Science and Technology;Rutgers University;University of North Carolina at Chapel Hill;Chongqing University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/33656
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.06430
英摘: Conformal prediction is a statistical framework that generates prediction sets containing ground-truth labels with a desired coverage guarantee. The predicted probabilities produced by machine learning models are generally miscalibrated, leading to large prediction sets in conformal prediction. To address this issue, we propose a novel algorithm named $\textit{Sorted Adaptive Prediction Sets}$ (SAPS), which discards all the probability values except for the maximum softmax probability. The key idea behind SAPS is to minimize the dependence of the non-conformity score on the probability values while retaining the uncertainty information. In this manner, SAPS can produce compact prediction sets and communicate instance-wise uncertainty. Extensive experiments validate that SAPS not only lessens the prediction sets but also broadly enhances the conditional coverage rate of prediction sets.
中摘: 一致性预测是一种统计框架，生成包含所需覆盖保证的真实标签的预测集。机器学习模型产生的预测概率通常校准不当，导致一致性预测中的预测集过大。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一种名为《排序自适应预测集》（SAPS）的新算法，该算法除了最大softmax概率值外，舍弃所有其他概率值。SAPS背后的关键思想是在保留不确定性信息的同时，最小化非一致性得分对概率值的依赖性。通过这种方式，SAPS可以产生紧凑的预测集，并传达实例级别的不确定性。广泛的实验验证了SAPS不仅减小了预测集，还普遍提高了预测集的条件覆盖率。

[No.331] DoRA: Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation

英摘: Among the widely used parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, LoRA and its variants have gained considerable popularity because of avoiding additional inference costs.
