[No.333] Efficient Low-Rank Matrix Estimation, Experimental Design, and Arm-Set-Dependent Low-Rank Bandits
来源: ICML 2024
命中: rank
标题: 高效低秩矩阵估计、实验设计以及臂集依赖的低秩多臂 bandit 问题
类别: 
作者: Kyoungseok Jang;Chicheng Zhang;Kwang-Sung Jun;
机构: New York University;University of Arizona;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/34022
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.11156
英摘: We study low-rank matrix trace regression and the related problem of low-rank matrix bandits. Assuming access to the distribution of the covariates, we propose a novel low-rank matrix estimation method called LowPopArt and provide its recovery guarantee that depends on a novel quantity denoted by B(Q) that characterizes the hardness of the problem, where Q is the covariance matrix of the measurement distribution. We show that our method can provide tighter recovery guarantees than classical nuclear norm penalized least squares (Koltchinskii et al., 2011) in several problems. To perform efficient estimation with a limited number of measurements from an arbitrarily given measurement set A, we also propose a novel experimental design criterion that minimizes B(Q) with computational efficiency. We leverage our novel estimator and design of experiments to derive two low-rank linear bandit algorithms for general arm sets that enjoy improved regret upper bounds. This improves over previous works on low-rank bandits, which make somewhat restrictive assumptions that the arm set is the unit ball or that an efficient exploration distribution is given. To our knowledge, our experimental design criterion is the first one tailored to low-rank matrix estimation beyond the naive reduction to linear regression, which can be of independent interest.
中摘: 我们研究了低秩矩阵迹回归及其相关问题——低秩矩阵多臂赌博机。假设可以访问协变量的分布，我们提出了一种名为LowPopArt的新型低秩矩阵估计方法，并提供了其恢复保证，该保证依赖于一个新颖的数量B(Q)，这个数量表征了问题的难度，其中Q是测量分布的协方差矩阵。我们证明，在多个问题中，我们的方法能够提供比经典的核范数惩罚最小二乘法（Koltchinskii等人，2011）更紧的恢复保证。为了从任意给定的测量集A中有限的测量值进行高效估计，我们还提出了一个新的实验设计标准，它以计算效率的方式最小化B(Q)。我们利用我们新颖的估计器和实验设计，推导出两种适用于一般臂集的低秩线性多臂赌博机算法，这些算法具有改进的遗憾上限。这超越了之前关于低秩多臂赌博机的工作，这些工作在某种程度上做出了限制性的假设，比如臂集是单位球，或者给出了有效的探索分布。据我们所知，我们的实验设计标准是第一个专门针对低秩矩阵估计的，它超出了简单的线性回归还原，可能具有独立的兴趣。

[No.334] Efficient Pareto Manifold Learning with Low-Rank Structure
来源: ICML 2024
命中: rank
标题: 高效的帕累托流形学习与低秩结构
类别: 
作者: Weiyu Chen;James Kwok;

链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/33704
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.20734
英摘: Multi-task learning, which optimizes performance across multiple tasks, is inherently a multi-objective optimization problem. Various algorithms are developed to provide discrete trade-off solutions on the Pareto front. However, it suffers from scalability issues when the number of tasks is large. 中摘: 多任务学习，旨在跨多个任务优化性能，本质上是一个多目标优化问题。已开发出各种算法，在帕累托前沿提供离散的权衡解决方案。最近，使用基础网络线性组合进行连续帕累托前沿逼近的方法成为了一种有吸引力的策略。然而，当任务数量庞大时，这种方法会遇到可扩展性问题。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种新颖的方法，该方法将主网络与几个低秩矩阵相结合，以高效地学习帕累托流形。这大大减少了参数的数量，并促进了共享特征的提取。我们还引入了正交正则化来进一步增强性能。广泛的实验结果表明，所提出的方法在大量任务数据集上尤其优于最先进的基线方法。

[No.335] Exploring Training on Heterogeneous Data with Mixture of Low-rank Adapters
来源: ICML 2024
命中: rank
标题: 探索异质数据上的混合低秩适配器训练
类别: 
作者: Yuhang Zhou;Zihua Zhao;Siyuan Du;Haolin li;Jiangchao Yao;Ya Zhang;Yanfeng Wang;

英摘: Training a unified model to take multiple targets into account is a trend towards artificial general intelligence. However, how to efficiently mitigate the training conflicts among heterogeneous data collected from different domains or tasks remains under-explored. In this study, we explore to leverage Mixture of Low-rank Adapters (MoLA) to mitigate conflicts in heterogeneous data training, which requires to jointly train the multiple low-rank adapters and their shared backbone. Specifically, we introduce two variants of MoLA, namely, MoLA-Grad and MoLA-Router, to respectively handle the target-aware and target-agnostic scenarios during inference. We conduct comprehensive experiments to verify the superiority of MoLA over previous state-of-the-art methods and present in-depth analysis on its working mechanism. [No.338] Flora: Low-Rank Adapters Are Secretly Gradient Compressors
来源: ICML 2024
命中: rank
标题:  flora: 低秩适配器实际上是秘密的梯度压缩器

作者: Yongchang Hao;Yanshuai Cao;Lili Mou;
机构: University of Alberta;Borealis AI;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/32804
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.03293
英摘: Despite large neural networks demonstrating remarkable abilities to complete different tasks, they require excessive memory usage to store the optimization states for training. To alleviate this, the low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is proposed to reduce the optimization states by training fewer parameters. However, LoRA restricts overall weight update matrices to be low-rank, limiting the model performance. In this work, we investigate the dynamics of LoRA and identify that it can be approximated by a random projection. Based on this observation, we propose Flora, which is able to achieve high-rank updates by resampling the projection matrices while enjoying the sublinear space complexity of optimization states. We conduct experiments across different tasks and model architectures to verify the effectiveness of our approach.
中摘: 尽管大型神经网络在完成不同任务方面展现出惊人的能力，但它们需要过度的内存使用来存储训练所需的优化状态。为了解决这个问题，提出了低秩适应（LoRA）方法，通过训练较少的参数来减少优化状态。然而，LoRA将整体权重更新矩阵限制为低秩，这限制了模型性能。在这项工作中，我们研究了LoRA的动力学，并发现它可以被近似为随机投影。基于这一观察，我们提出了Flora，它能够通过重新采样投影矩阵实现高秩更新，同时享受优化状态的次线性空间复杂度。我们在不同的任务和模型架构上进行了实验，以验证我们方法的有效性。

[No.339] GaLore: Memory-Efficient LLM Training by Gradient Low-Rank Projection
来源: ICML 2024
命中: rank
标题: GaLore：基于梯度低秩投影的记忆高效LLM训练
类别: 
作者: Jiawei Zhao;Zhenyu Zhang;Beidi Chen;Zhangyang Wang;Anima Anandkumar;Yuandong Tian;
机构: California Institute of Technology;University of Texas at Austin;Meta;
类型: Oral
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/33390
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.03507
英摘: Training Large Language Models (LLMs) presents significant memory challenges, predominantly due to the growing size of weights and optimizer states. Common memory-reduction approaches, such as low-rank adaptation (LoRA), add a trainable low-rank matrix to the frozen pre-trained weight in each layer, reducing trainable parameters and optimizer states. However, such approaches typically underperform training with full-rank weights in both pre-training and fine-tuning stages since they limit the parameter search to a low-rank subspace and alter the training dynamics, and further, may require full-rank warm start. In this work, we propose Gradient Low-Rank Projection (GaLore), a training strategy that allows full-parameter learning but is more memory-efficient than common low-rank adaptation methods such as LoRA. Our approach reduces memory usage by up to 65.5% in optimizer states while maintaining both efficiency and performance for pre-training on LLaMA 1B and 7B architectures with C4 dataset with up to 19.7B tokens, and on fine-tuning RoBERTa on GLUE tasks. Our 8-bit GaLore further reduces optimizer memory by up to 82.5% and total training memory by 63.3%, compared to a BF16 baseline. Notably, we demonstrate, for the first time, the feasibility of pre-training a 7B model on consumer GPUs with 24GB memory (e.g., NVIDIA RTX 4090) without model parallel, checkpointing, or offloading strategies.
中摘: 训练大型语言模型（LLM）带来了显著的内存挑战，主要源于权重和优化器状态不断增长的规模。常见的内存减少方法，如低秩适应（LoRA），在每一层的冻结预训练权重上添加一个可训练的低秩矩阵，从而减少了可训练参数和优化器状态的数量。然而，这类方法通常在预训练和微调阶段的表现都不如使用全秩权重进行训练，因为它们将参数搜索限制在一个低秩子空间内，改变了训练动态，并且可能需要全秩的热启动。在这项工作中，我们提出了梯度低秩投影（GaLore），一种训练策略，它允许进行全参数学习，但比常见的低秩适应方法（如LoRA）更节省内存。我们的方法在保持效率和性能的同时，将优化器状态的内存使用量减少了高达65.5%，适用于使用C4数据集与最多197亿个令牌对LLaMA 1B和7B架构进行预训练，以及对RoBERTa在GLUE任务上的微调。我们的8位GaLore进一步将优化器内存减少至多82.5%，并将总训练内存减少63.3%，相比BF16基线而言。值得注意的是，我们首次展示了在没有模型并行、检查点或卸载策略的情况下，在具有24GB内存的消费者级GPU（例如，NVIDIA RTX 4090）上预训练7B模型的可行性。

[No.340] High-Dimensional Geometric Streaming for Nearly Low Rank Data
来源: ICML 2024
命中: rank
标题: 高维几何流计算几乎低秩数据
类别: 
作者: Hossein Esfandiari;Praneeth Kacham;Vahab Mirrokni;David Woodruff;Peilin Zhong;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.02910
英摘: We study streaming algorithms for the $\ell_p$ subspace approximation problem. Given points $a_1, \ldots, a_n$ as an insertion-only stream and a rank parameter $k$, the $\ell_p$ subspace approximation problem is to find a $k$-dimensional subspace $V$ such that $(\sum_{i=1}^n d(a_i, V)^p)^{1/p}$ is minimized, where $d(a, V)$ denotes the Euclidean distance between $a$ and $V$ defined as $\min_{v \in V}\|{a - v}\|_{\infty}$. When $p = \infty$, we need to find a subspace $V$ that minimizes $\max_i d(a_i, V)$. For $\ell_{\infty}$ subspace approximation, we give a deterministic strong coreset construction algorithm and show that it can be used to compute a $\text{poly}(k, \log n)$ approximate solution. We show that the distortion obtained by our coreset is nearly tight for any sublinear space algorithm. For $\ell_p$ subspace approximation, we show that suitably scaling the points and then using our $\ell_{\infty}$ coreset construction, we can compute a $\text{poly}(k, \log n)$ approximation. Our algorithms are easy to implement and run very fast on large datasets. We also use our strong coreset construction to improve the results in a recent work of Woodruff and Yasuda (FOCS 2022) which gives streaming algorithms for high-dimensional geometric problems such as width estimation, convex hull estimation, and volume estimation.
中摘: 我们研究了针对$\ell_p$子空间逼近问题的流算法。给定点$a_1, \ldots, a_n$作为仅插入流以及一个秩参数$k$，$\ell_p$子空间逼近问题的目标是找到一个$k$维子空间$V$，使得$(\sum_{i=1}^n d(a_i, V)^p)^{1/p}$最小化，其中$d(a, V)$表示$a$与$V$之间的欧几里得距离，定义为$\min_{v \in V}\|{a - v}\|_{\infty}$。当$p = \infty$时，我们需要找到一个子空间$V$，以最小化$\max_i d(a_i, V)$。对于$\ell_{\infty}$子空间逼近，我们给出了一种确定性的强核心集构造算法，并证明它可以用来计算一个$\text{poly}(k, \log n)$近似解。我们还表明，我们的核心集得到的失真对于任何次线性空间算法几乎是紧致的。对于$\ell_p$子空间逼近，我们表明，适当缩放点并使用我们的$\ell_{\infty}$核心集构造，我们可以计算出一个$\text{poly}(k, \log n)$近似值。我们的算法易于实现，在大型数据集上运行非常快。我们还利用我们的强核心集构造改进了Woodruff和Yasuda（FOCS 2022）最近工作中关于高维几何问题如宽度估计、凸包估计和体积估计的流算法的结果。

[No.341] Identifiability Matters: Revealing the Hidden Recoverable Condition in Unbiased Learning to Rank
来源: ICML 2024
命中: rank
标题: 可识别性很重要：揭示无偏学习排序中的隐藏可恢复条件
类别: 
作者: Mouxiang Chen;Chenghao Liu;Zemin Liu;Zhuo Li;Jianling Sun;
机构: Zhejiang University;Salesforce;National University of Singapore;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/34374
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2309.15560
英摘: Unbiased Learning to Rank (ULTR) aims to train unbiased ranking models from biased click logs, by explicitly modeling a generation process for user behavior and fitting click data based on examination hypothesis. Previous research found empirically that the true latent relevance is mostly recoverable through click fitting. However, we demonstrate that this is not always achievable, resulting in a significant reduction in ranking performance. This research investigates the conditions under which relevance can be recovered from click data in the first principle. We initially characterize a ranking model as identifiable if it can recover the true relevance up to a scaling transformation, a criterion sufficient for the pairwise ranking objective. Subsequently, we investigate an equivalent condition for identifiability, articulated as a graph connectivity test problem: the recovery of relevance is feasible if and only if the identifiability graph (IG), derived from the underlying structure of the dataset, is connected. The presence of a disconnected IG may lead to degenerate cases and suboptimal ranking performance. To tackle this challenge, we introduce two methods, namely node intervention and node merging, designed to modify the dataset and restore the connectivity of the IG. Empirical results derived from a simulated dataset and two real-world LTR benchmark datasets not only validate our proposed theory but also demonstrate the effectiveness of our methods in alleviating data bias when the relevance model is unidentifiable.
中摘: 无偏学习排序（Unbiased Learning to Rank，简称 ULTR）旨在从有偏见的点击日志中训练无偏见的排名模型，通过明确建模用户行为的生成过程并基于可检测性假设来拟合点击数据。先前的研究经验性地发现，通过点击拟合大多能恢复真实的潜在相关性。然而，我们证明这并非总能实现，导致排名性能显著下降。本研究首先探究了在基本原则下，从点击数据中恢复相关性的条件。我们初步将排名模型定义为可识别的，如果它能通过缩放变换恢复真实的相关性，这一标准对于两两比较的排名目标来说是充分的。随后，我们探讨了一个等价的可识别条件，表述为一个图连通性测试问题：只有当可识别图（Identifiability Graph，简称 IG），即从数据集底层结构导出的图是连通的，相关性的恢复才可行。不连通的 IG 可能会导致退化情况和次优的排名性能。为解决这一难题，我们引入了两种方法，即节点干预和节点合并，旨在修改数据集以恢复 IG 的连通性。从模拟数据集和两个现实世界的学习排序基准数据集中得出的经验结果不仅验证了我们提出的理论，而且展示了我们的方法在相关性模型不可识别时有效缓解数据偏差的能力。

[No.342] LoRA+: Efficient Low Rank Adaptation of Large Models
来源: ICML 2024
命中: rank
标题: LoRA+: 大型模型的高效低秩适应
类别: 
作者: Soufiane Hayou;Nikhil Ghosh;Bin Yu;
机构: National University of Singapore;University of California, Berkeley;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/34209
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.12354
英摘: In this paper, we show that Low Rank Adaptation (LoRA) as originally introduced in Hu et al. (2021) leads to suboptimal finetuning of models with large width (embedding dimension). This is due to the fact that adapter matrices A and B in LoRA are updated with the same learning rate. Using scaling arguments for large width networks, we demonstrate that using the same learning rate for A and B does not allow efficient feature learning. We then show that this suboptimality of LoRA can be corrected simply by setting different learning rates for the LoRA adapter matrices A and B with a well-chosen ratio. We call this proposed algorithm LoRA$+$. In our extensive experiments, LoRA$+$ improves performance (1-2 $\%$ improvements) and finetuning speed (up to $\sim$ 2X SpeedUp), at the same computational cost as LoRA.
中摘: 在本文中，我们证明了最初在Hu等人(2021)中引入的低秩适应（LoRA）会导致对宽度较大（嵌入维度）的模型进行次优的微调。这是由于LoRA中的适配器矩阵A和B使用相同的 学习率 进行更新。通过为大宽度网络提出缩放论据，我们证明了对于A和B使用相同的学习率不允许高效地学习特征。然后，我们表明通过为LoRA适配器矩阵A和B设置不同学习率与适当选择的比例，可以简单地修正LoRA的这种次优性。我们将这个提出的算法称为LoRA$+$。在我们广泛的实验中，LoRA$+$在性能上提高了1-2%，并加快了微调速度（最多约2倍加速），计算成本与LoRA相同。

[No.343] Low-Rank Bandits via Tight Two-to-Infinity Singular Subspace Recovery
来源: ICML 2024
命中: rank
标题: 低秩贝叶斯通过紧致的二至无穷奇异子空间恢复
类别: 
作者: Yassir Jedra;William Réveillard;Stefan Stojanovic;Alexandre Proutiere;
机构: Massachusetts Institute of Technology;KTH Royal Institute of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/34615
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.15739
英摘: We study contextual bandits with low-rank structure where, in each round, if the (context, arm) pair $(i,j)\in [m]\times [n]$ is selected, the learner observes a noisy sample of the $(i,j)$-th entry of an unknown low-rank reward matrix. Successive contexts are generated randomly in an i.i.d. manner and are revealed to the learner. For such bandits, we present efficient algorithms for policy evaluation, best policy identification and regret minimization. For policy evaluation and best policy identification, we show that our algorithms are nearly minimax optimal. For instance, the number of samples required to return an $\varepsilon$-optimal policy with probability at least $1-δ$ typically scales as ${r(m+n)\over \varepsilon^2}\log(1/δ)$. Our regret minimization algorithm enjoys minimax guarantees typically scaling as $r^{7/4}(m+n)^{3/4}\sqrt{T}$, which improves over existing algorithms. All the proposed algorithms consist of two phases: they first leverage spectral methods to estimate the left and right singular subspaces of the low-rank reward matrix. We show that these estimates enjoy tight error guarantees in the two-to-infinity norm. This in turn allows us to reformulate our problems as a misspecified linear bandit problem with dimension roughly $r(m+n)$ and misspecification controlled by the subspace recovery error, as well as to design the second phase of our algorithms efficiently.
中摘: 我们研究了具有低秩结构的情境多臂赌博机问题，在每一轮中，如果选择的（情境，臂）对$(i,j)\in [m]\times [n]$，学习者会观察到一个未知低秩奖励矩阵的$(i,j)$-th条目的噪声样本。连续的情境以独立同分布的方式随机生成，并向学习者揭示。对于这类多臂赌博机，我们提出了用于策略评估、最佳策略识别和遗憾最小化的高效算法。对于策略评估和最佳策略识别，我们证明我们的算法几乎是极小极大最优的。例如，返回一个$\varepsilon$-最优策略所需样本数量通常按${r(m+n)\over \varepsilon^2}\log(1/δ)$的规模增长，其中以至少$1-δ$的概率获得该策略。我们的遗憾最小化算法享有极小极大保证，通常按$r^{7/4}(m+n)^{3/4}\sqrt{T}$的规模增长，这比现有算法有所改进。所有提出的算法都包含两个阶段：它们首先利用谱方法来估计低秩奖励矩阵的左奇异子空间和右奇异子空间。我们证明这些估计在二至无穷范数下具有紧致的误差保证。这反过来允许我们将问题重新表述为一个失真的线性多臂赌博机问题，其维度大致为$r(m+n)$，失真由子空间恢复误差控制，并且使我们能够有效地设计算法的第二阶段。

[No.345] LQER: Low-Rank Quantization Error Reconstruction for LLMs
来源: ICML 2024
命中: rank
标题: 低秩量化误差重构对于大型语言模型（LLMs）
类别: 
作者: Cheng Zhang;Jianyi Cheng;George Anthony Constantinides;Yiren Zhao;

类型: Poster

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.02446
英摘: Post-training quantization of Large Language Models (LLMs) is challenging. In this work, we introduce Low-rank Quantization Error Reduction (LQER), which combines quantization and low-rank approximation to recover the model capability. LQER leverages an activation-induced scale matrix to drive the singular value distribution of quantization error towards a desirable distribution, which enables nearly-lossless W4A8 quantization on various LLMs and downstream tasks without the need for knowledge distillation, grid search, or gradient-base iterative optimization. [No.346] Maestro: Uncovering Low-Rank Structures via Trainable Decomposition
来源: ICML 2024
命中: rank
标题: 大师：通过可训练分解揭示低秩结构
类别: 
作者: Samuel Horváth;Stefanos Laskaridis;Shashank Rajput;Hongyi Wang;
机构: Mohamed bin Zayed University of Artificial Intelligence;Brave Software;University of Wisconsin;Carnegie Mellon University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/34878
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2308.14929
英摘: Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) have been a large driver for AI breakthroughs in recent years. However, these models have been getting increasingly large as they become more accurate and safe. This means that their training becomes increasingly costly and time-consuming and typically yields a single model to fit all targets. Various techniques have been proposed in the literature to mitigate this, including pruning, sparsification, or quantization of model weights and updates. While achieving high compression rates, they often incur significant computational overheads at training or lead to non-negligible accuracy penalty. Alternatively, factorization methods have been leveraged for low-rank compression of DNNs. Similarly, such techniques (e.g., SVD) frequently rely on heavy iterative decompositions of layers and are potentially sub-optimal for non-linear models, such as DNNs. We take a further step in designing efficient low-rank models and propose Maestro, a framework for trainable low-rank layers. Instead of iteratively applying a priori decompositions, the low-rank structure is baked into the training process through LoD, a low-rank ordered decomposition. Not only is this the first time importance ordering via sampling is applied on the decomposed DNN structure, but it also allows selecting ranks at a layer granularity. Our theoretical analysis demonstrates that in special cases LoD recovers the SVD decomposition and PCA. Applied to DNNs, Maestro enables the extraction of lower footprint models that preserve performance. Simultaneously, it enables the graceful trade-off between accuracy-latency for deployment to even more constrained devices without retraining.
中摘: 深度神经网络（DNN）近年来一直是推动人工智能突破的主要动力。然而，随着这些模型在准确性和安全性方面的提升，它们的规模也在不断增大。这意味着训练成本和时间消耗也随之增加，并且通常产生的是适用于所有目标的单一模型。文献中提出了多种技术来缓解这一问题，包括修剪、模型权重和更新的稀疏化或量化等。尽管这些方法可以实现高压缩率，但往往在训练时带来显著的计算开销，或者导致不可忽视的准确性损失。另一方面，分解方法已被用于深度神经网络的低秩压缩。类似地，这些技术（如SVD）经常依赖于对层的繁重迭代分解，并且对于非线性模型如DNN而言可能不是最优选择。我们在设计高效的低秩模型方面更进一步，提出了一种名为Maestro的框架，用于可训练的低秩层。我们没有采用先验分解的迭代应用，而是通过LoD（低秩有序分解）将低秩结构融入到训练过程中。这是首次在分解的DNN结构上应用通过采样进行的重要性排序，并允许在层的粒度上选择秩。我们的理论分析表明，在特殊情况下，LoD能够恢复SVD分解和PCA。应用于DNN，Maestro能够提取出保持性能的同时具有更小体积的模型。同时，它使得在部署到更为受限的设备时，能够在精度与延迟之间优雅地权衡，无需重新训练。

[No.347] Operator SVD with Neural Networks via Nested Low-Rank Approximation
来源: ICML 2024
命中: rank
标题: 通过嵌套低秩逼近实现的SVD运算神经网络方法

英摘: Computing eigenvalue decomposition (EVD) of a given linear operator, or finding its leading eigenvalues and eigenfunctions, is a fundamental task in many machine learning and scientific computing problems. For high-dimensional eigenvalue problems, training neural networks to parameterize the eigenfunctions is considered as a promising alternative to the classical numerical linear algebra techniques. This paper proposes a new optimization framework based on the low-rank approximation characterization of a truncated singular value decomposition, accompanied by new techniques called \emph{nesting} for learning the top-$L$ singular values and singular functions in the correct order. The proposed method promotes the desired orthogonality in the learned functions implicitly and efficiently via an unconstrained optimization formulation, which is easy to solve with off-the-shelf gradient-based optimization algorithms. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed optimization framework for use cases in computational physics and machine learning.

[No.349] Reweighted Solutions for Weighted Low Rank Approximation
来源: ICML 2024
命中: rank
标题: 加权低秩逼近的重加权解法
类别: 
作者: David Woodruff;Taisuke Yasuda;
机构: Carnegie Mellon University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/32968
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.02431
英摘: Weighted low rank approximation (WLRA) is an important yet computationally challenging primitive with applications ranging from statistical analysis, model compression, and signal processing. To cope with the NP-hardness of this problem, prior work considers heuristics, bicriteria, or fixed parameter tractable algorithms to solve this problem. In this work, we introduce a new relaxed solution to WLRA which outputs a matrix that is not necessarily low rank, but can be stored using very few parameters and gives provable approximation guarantees when the weight matrix has low rank. Our central idea is to use the weight matrix itself to reweight a low rank solution, which gives an extremely simple algorithm with remarkable empirical performance in applications to model compression and on synthetic datasets. Our algorithm also gives nearly optimal communication complexity bounds for a natural distributed problem associated with this problem, for which we show matching communication lower bounds. Together, our communication complexity bounds show that the rank of the weight matrix provably parameterizes the communication complexity of WLRA. We also obtain the first relative error guarantees for feature selection with a weighted objective.
中摘: 加权低秩逼近（WLRA）是一个重要但计算上具有挑战性的基本问题，其应用范围涵盖了从统计分析、模型压缩到信号处理等多个领域。为应对这一问题的NP难性质，先前的研究考虑了启发式算法、双准则或固定参数可解算法来解决这个问题。在本工作中，我们引入了一种新的WLRA松弛解法，输出的矩阵不一定具有低秩特性，但可以用非常少的参数进行存储，并且当权重矩阵具有低秩时能提供可证明的近似保证。我们的核心思想是利用权重矩阵本身对低秩解进行重新加权，这产生了一个极其简单的算法，在应用于模型压缩和合成数据集的应用中展现出卓越的实际性能。我们的算法还为与该问题相关的自然分布式问题提供了几乎最优的通信复杂度界，对此我们展示了匹配的通信下界。综合来看，我们的通信复杂度界表明，权重矩阵的秩可证明地参数化了WLRA的通信复杂度。此外，我们还获得了加权目标特征选择的第一个相对误差保证。

[No.350] Sarah Frank-Wolfe: Methods for Constrained Optimization with Best Rates and Practical Features
来源: ICML 2024
命中: rank
标题: 萨拉·弗朗克-沃尔夫：约束优化方法的最佳率与实用特性
类别: 
作者: Aleksandr Beznosikov;David Dobre;Gauthier Gidel;
机构: Quebec Artificial Intelligence Institute;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/33715
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2304.11737
英摘: The Frank-Wolfe (FW) method is a popular approach for solving optimization problems with structured constraints that arise in machine learning applications. In recent years, stochastic versions of FW have gained popularity, motivated by large datasets for which the computation of the full gradient is prohibitively expensive. In this paper, we present two new variants of the FW algorithms for stochastic finite-sum minimization. Our algorithms have the best convergence guarantees of existing stochastic FW approaches for both convex and non-convex objective functions. Our methods do not have the issue of permanently collecting large batches, which is common to many stochastic projection-free approaches. Moreover, our second approach does not require either large batches or full deterministic gradients, which is a typical weakness of many techniques for finite-sum problems. The faster theoretical rates of our approaches are confirmed experimentally.
中摘: 弗兰克-沃尔夫（Frank-Wolfe，简称FW）方法是在机器学习应用中解决具有结构化约束的优化问题的一种流行方法。近年来，随机版本的FW方法越来越受欢迎，这主要是因为对于大规模数据集而言，计算完整梯度的成本过高。在本文中，我们提出了两种新的FW算法变体，用于随机有限和最小化问题。我们的算法在凸性和非凸性目标函数的情况下，都具有现有随机FW方法中最佳的收敛保证。我们的方法不会出现持续收集大数据批次的问题，这是许多随机无投影方法常见的问题。此外，我们的第二种方法既不需要大数据批次，也不需要完整的确定性梯度，而这通常是处理有限和问题的许多技术的一个典型弱点。我们的方法在理论上的更快收敛率已在实验中得到证实。

[No.352] Stability and Multigroup Fairness in Ranking with Uncertain Predictions
来源: ICML 2024
命中: rank
标题: 不确定预测条件下的排名稳定性和多群体公平性
类别: 
作者: Siddartha Devic;Aleksandra Korolova;David Kempe;Vatsal Sharan;
机构: Amazon;University of Southern California;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/33765
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.09326
英摘: Rankings are ubiquitous across many applications, from search engines to hiring committees. In practice, many rankings are derived from the output of predictors. However, when predictors trained for classification tasks have intrinsic uncertainty, it is not obvious how this uncertainty should be represented in the derived rankings. Our work considers ranking functions: maps from individual predictions for a classification task to distributions over rankings. We focus on two aspects of ranking functions: stability to perturbations in predictions and fairness towards both individuals and subgroups. Not only is stability an important requirement for its own sake, but -- as we show -- it composes harmoniously with individual fairness in the sense of Dwork et al. (2012). While deterministic ranking functions cannot be stable aside from trivial scenarios, we show that the recently proposed uncertainty aware (UA) ranking functions of Singh et al. (2021) are stable. Our main result is that UA rankings also achieve multigroup fairness through successful composition with multiaccurate or multicalibrated predictors. Our work demonstrates that UA rankings naturally interpolate between group and individual level fairness guarantees, while simultaneously satisfying stability guarantees important whenever machine-learned predictions are used.
中摘: 排名在许多应用中无处不在，从搜索引擎到招聘委员会。实际上，许多排名都是从预测器的输出中得出的。然而，当用于分类任务的训练有素的预测器具有内在不确定性时，这种不确定性如何在派生的排名中体现并不明显。我们的工作考虑了排名函数：将分类任务的单个预测映射到排名分布的映射。我们关注排名函数的两个方面：对预测扰动的稳定性以及对个体和子群体的公平性。稳定性本身就是一个重要的要求，而且，正如我们所展示的那样，它与Dwork等人（2012）意义上的个体公平性和谐地结合在一起。除了在平凡场景之外，确定性的排名函数无法保持稳定，但我们证明了Singh等人最近提出的不确定性感知（UA）排名函数（2021）是稳定的。我们的主要结果是，UA排名通过与多准确或多校准预测器的成功组合也实现了多群体公平。我们的工作证明了UA排名自然地在群体和个体层面的公平性保证之间进行插值，同时满足使用机器学习预测时至关重要的稳定性保证。

[No.355] Active Ranking of Experts Based on their Performances in Many Tasks
来源: ICML 2023
命中: rank
标题: 基于专家在多种任务中的表现进行主动排序
类别: 
作者: El Mehdi Saad;Nicolas Verzelen;Alexandra Carpentier;
机构: INRAE;Otto von Guericke University Magdeburg;
类型: Oral
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2023/poster/23554
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.02628
英摘: We consider the problem of ranking n experts based on their performances on d tasks. We make a monotonicity assumption stating that for each pair of experts, one outperforms the other on all tasks. We consider the sequential setting where in each round, the learner has access to noisy evaluations of actively chosen pair of expert-task, given the information available up to the actual round. Given a confidence parameter $δ$ $\in$ (0, 1), we provide strategies allowing to recover the correct ranking of experts and develop a bound on the total number of queries made by our algorithm that hold with probability at least 1 -- $δ$. We show that our strategy is adaptive to the complexity of the problem (our bounds are instance dependent), and develop matching lower bounds up to a poly-logarithmic factor. Finally, we adapt our strategy to the relaxed problem of best expert identification and provide numerical simulation consistent with our theoretical results.
中摘: 我们考虑了根据专家在d项任务上的表现来对n位专家进行排名的问题。我们做出单调性假设，即对于每一对专家而言，在所有任务上，其中一位的表现都优于另一位。我们考虑的是序列设置，即在每一轮中，学习者可以根据当前轮次之前获得的信息，主动选择一对专家-任务组合，并得到其评价的噪声值。给定一个置信度参数$δ$ $\in$ (0, 1)，我们提供了策略以恢复专家的正确排名，并开发了我们算法查询总数的界限，该界限成立的概率至少为1 -- $δ$。我们证明了我们的策略能够适应问题的复杂性（我们的界限依赖于具体实例），并开发了匹配的下界，直到多对数因子。最后，我们将策略调整到最佳专家识别的放松问题上，并提供了与理论结果一致的数值模拟。

[No.356] Coder Reviewer Reranking for Code Generation
来源: ICML 2023
命中: rank
标题: 代码生成中的编码审阅排序
类别: 
作者: Tianyi Zhang;Tao Yu;Tatsunori Hashimoto;Mike Lewis;Wen-tau Yih;Daniel Fried;Sida Wang;
机构: Stanford University;University of Hong Kong;Meta;Carnegie Mellon University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2023/poster/24463
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2211.16490
英摘: Sampling diverse programs from a code language model and reranking with model likelihood is a popular method for code generation but it is prone to preferring degenerate solutions. Inspired by collaborative programming, we propose Coder-Reviewer reranking. We augment Coder language models from past work, which generate programs given language instructions, with Reviewer models, which evaluate the likelihood of the instruction given the generated programs. We perform an extensive study across six datasets with eight models from three model families. Experimental results show that Coder-Reviewer reranking leads to consistent and significant improvement (up to 17% absolute accuracy gain) over reranking with the Coder model only. When combined with executability filtering, Coder-Reviewer reranking can often outperform the minimum Bayes risk method. Coder-Reviewer reranking is easy to implement by prompting, can generalize to different programming languages, and works well with off-the-shelf hyperparameters.
中摘: 从代码语言模型中采样多样化的程序并根据模型可能性重新排序是一种流行的代码生成方法，但它倾向于选择退化解决方案。受协作编程的启发，我们提出了“编码者-评审者”重新排序方法。我们在先前工作的基础上对编码者语言模型进行了增强，这些模型可以根据语言指令生成程序，并添加了评审者模型，用于评估给定生成程序后指令的可能性。我们在六个数据集上进行了广泛的研究，涵盖了来自三个模型家族的八种模型。实验结果表明，与仅使用编码者模型重新排序相比，“编码者-评审者”重新排序能够带来一致且显著的改进（最高可达17%的绝对准确度提升）。当与可执行性过滤结合时，“编码者-评审者”重新排序通常能超越最小贝叶斯风险方法。通过提示，“编码者-评审者”重新排序易于实现，可以泛化到不同的编程语言，并且即使使用现成的超参数也表现良好。

[No.358] LoSparse: Structured Compression of Large Language Models based on Low-Rank and Sparse Approximation
来源: ICML 2023
命中: rank
标题: LoSparse：基于低秩和稀疏近似的大型语言模型结构压缩
类别: 
作者: Yixiao Li;Yifan Yu;Qingru Zhang;Chen Liang;Pengcheng He;Weizhu Chen;Tuo Zhao;
机构: Georgia Institute of Technology;Microsoft;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2023/poster/23722
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.11222
英摘: Transformer models have achieved remarkable results in various natural language tasks, but they are often prohibitively large, requiring massive memories and computational resources. To reduce the size and complexity of these models, we propose LoSparse (Low-Rank and Sparse approximation), a novel model compression technique that approximates a weight matrix by the sum of a low-rank matrix and a sparse matrix. Our method combines the advantages of both low-rank approximations and pruning, while avoiding their limitations. Low-rank approximation compresses the coherent and expressive parts in neurons, while pruning removes the incoherent and non-expressive parts in neurons. Pruning enhances the diversity of low-rank approximations, and low-rank approximation prevents pruning from losing too many expressive neurons. We evaluate our method on natural language understanding, question answering, and natural language generation tasks. We show that it significantly outperforms existing compression methods.
中摘: 变压器模型在各种自然语言任务中取得了显著成果，但它们通常过于庞大，需要大量的内存和计算资源。为了减少这些模型的大小和复杂性，我们提出了LoSparse（低秩和稀疏逼近），这是一种新颖的模型压缩技术，通过低秩矩阵与稀疏矩阵之和来近似权重矩阵。我们的方法结合了低秩逼近和剪枝的优点，同时避免了它们的局限性。低秩逼近压缩神经元中的连贯性和表达部分，而剪枝则移除神经元中的不连贯和非表达部分。剪枝增强了低秩逼近的多样性，而低秩逼近防止剪枝丢失过多的表达神经元。我们在自然语言理解、问题回答和自然语言生成任务上评估了我们的方法，结果显示它显著优于现有的压缩方法。

[No.359] Multi-User Reinforcement Learning with Low Rank Rewards
来源: ICML 2023
命中: rank
标题: 低秩奖励下的多用户强化学习
类别: 
作者: Dheeraj Mysore Nagaraj;Suhas S Kowshik;Naman Agarwal;Praneeth Netrapalli;Prateek Jain;

英摘: In this work, we consider the problem of collaborative multi-user reinforcement learning. In this setting there are multiple users with the same state-action space and transition probabilities but with different rewards. Under the assumption that the reward matrix of the $N$ users has a low-rank structure -- a standard and practically successful assumption in the offline collaborative filtering setting -- the question is can we design algorithms with significantly lower sample complexity compared to the ones that learn the MDP individually for each user. Our main contribution is an algorithm which explores rewards collaboratively with $N$ user-specific MDPs and can learn rewards efficiently in two key settings: tabular MDPs and linear MDPs. When $N$ is large and the rank is constant, the sample complexity per MDP depends logarithmically over the size of the state-space, which represents an exponential reduction (in the state-space size) when compared to the standard ``non-collaborative'' algorithms.
中摘: 在本研究中，我们探讨了协作式多用户强化学习的问题。在这种设定下，存在多个用户，他们具有相同的状态-动作空间和转移概率，但奖励不同。假设N个用户的奖励矩阵具有低秩结构——这是在线下协同过滤环境中一个标准且实际成功的假设——问题在于我们能否设计出相比为每个用户单独学习马尔科夫决策过程(MDP)的算法，样本复杂度显著降低的算法。我们的主要贡献是一种算法，它能够与N个特定于用户的MDP协作探索奖励，并在两个关键设置中高效地学习奖励：表格型MDP和线性MDP。当N较大且秩为常数时，每个MDP的样本复杂度依赖于状态空间大小的对数，这与标准的“非协作”算法相比，在状态空间大小方面实现了指数级的减少。

简而言之，我们在协作式多用户强化学习领域提出了一个新算法，该算法通过利用用户间奖励矩阵的低秩特性，能够在表格型和线性MDP环境下更有效地学习。当参与用户数量巨大且奖励矩阵的秩保持不变时，我们的方法可以大幅降低每个MDP的学习成本，相较于传统独立学习策略，对状态空间大小的依赖呈现出指数级的下降。

[No.360] Over-parametrization via Lifting for Low-rank Matrix Sensing: Conversion of Spurious Solutions to Strict Saddle Points
来源: ICML 2023
命中: rank
标题: 通过提升实现的过度参数化：虚假解转换为严格鞍点的低秩矩阵感知方法
类别: 
作者: Ziye Ma;Igor Molybog;Javad Lavaei;Somayeh Sojoudi;
机构: University of California, Berkeley;

链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2023/poster/24785
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2302.07828
英摘: This paper studies the role of over-parametrization in solving non-convex optimization problems. The focus is on the important class of low-rank matrix sensing, where we propose an infinite hierarchy of non-convex problems via the lifting technique and the Burer-Monteiro factorization. This contrasts with the existing over-parametrization technique where the search rank is limited by the dimension of the matrix and it does not allow a rich over-parametrization of an arbitrary degree. We show that although the spurious solutions of the problem remain stationary points through the hierarchy, they will be transformed into strict saddle points (under some technical conditions) and can be escaped via local search methods. This is the first result in the literature showing that over-parametrization creates a negative curvature for escaping spurious solutions. We also derive a bound on how much over-parametrization is requited to enable the elimination of spurious solutions.
中摘: 本文研究了过度参数化在解决非凸优化问题中的作用。重点在于重要的低秩矩阵感知类别，我们通过提升技术和Burer-Monteiro因子分解提出了一个无限层次的非凸问题。这与现有的过度参数化技术形成对比，现有技术中搜索的秩受到矩阵维度的限制，并且不允许任意程度的丰富过度参数化。我们证明，尽管问题的虚假解在整个层次结构中仍然是稳定点，但它们将被转化为严格鞍点（在一些技术条件下），并通过局部搜索方法逃离。这是文献中首次表明过度参数化为逃离虚假解创造了负曲率的结果。我们还推导出了一个界限，说明需要多少过度参数化才能实现虚假解的消除。

英摘: In this paper, we study representation learning in partially observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs), where the agent learns a decoder function that maps a series of high-dimensional raw observations to a compact representation and uses it for more efficient exploration and planning.
  We focus our attention on the sub-classes of \textit{$γ$-observable} and \textit{decodable POMDPs}, for which it has been shown that statistically tractable learning is possible, but there has not been any computationally efficient algorithm. We first present an algorithm for decodable POMDPs that combines maximum likelihood estimation (MLE) and optimism in the face of uncertainty (OFU) to perform representation learning and achieve efficient sample complexity, while only calling supervised learning computational oracles. We then show how to adapt this algorithm to also work in the broader class of $γ$-observable POMDPs.
中摘: 在本文中，我们研究了部分可观察马尔科夫决策过程（POMDPs）中的表示学习，其中代理学习一个解码函数，该函数将一系列高维原始观测值映射到紧凑的表示，并利用它进行更有效的探索和规划。我们将注意力集中在\textit{$\gamma$-可观测}和\textit{可解码POMDPs}的子类上，对于这些子类，已经证明统计上可行的学习是可能的，但之前并没有任何计算高效的算法。首先，我们为可解码POMDPs提出了一种算法，该算法结合了最大似然估计（MLE）和面对不确定性的乐观主义（OFU），以执行表示学习并实现高效样本复杂度，同时仅调用监督学习的计算神谕。然后，我们展示了如何将此算法适应更广泛的$\gamma$-可观测POMDPs类别。

[No.363] RankMe: Assessing the Downstream Performance of Pretrained Self-Supervised Representations by Their Rank
来源: ICML 2023
命中: rank
标题: RankMe：通过排名评估预训练自监督表示的下游性能
类别: 
作者: Quentin Garrido;Randall Balestriero;Laurent Najman;Yann LeCun;
机构: Meta;Ecole Supérieure d'Ingénieurs en Electronique et Electrotechnique;New York University;
类型: Oral
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2023/poster/24011
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2210.02885
英摘: Joint-Embedding Self Supervised Learning (JE-SSL) has seen a rapid development, with the emergence of many method variations but only few principled guidelines that would help practitioners to successfully deploy them. The main reason for that pitfall comes from JE-SSL's core principle of not employing any input reconstruction therefore lacking visual cues of unsuccessful training. Adding non informative loss values to that, it becomes difficult to deploy SSL on a new dataset for which no labels can help to judge the quality of the learned representation. In this study, we develop a simple unsupervised criterion that is indicative of the quality of the learned JE-SSL representations: their effective rank. Albeit simple and computationally friendly, this method -- coined RankMe -- allows one to assess the performance of JE-SSL representations, even on different downstream datasets, without requiring any labels. A further benefit of RankMe is that it does not have any training or hyper-parameters to tune. Through thorough empirical experiments involving hundreds of training episodes, we demonstrate how RankMe can be used for hyperparameter selection with nearly no reduction in final performance compared to the current selection method that involve a dataset's labels. We hope that RankMe will facilitate the deployment of JE-SSL towards domains that do not have the opportunity to rely on labels for representations' quality assessment.
中摘: 联合嵌入自监督学习（JE-SSL）发展迅速，出现了许多方法变体，但缺乏帮助实践者成功部署的系统性指导原则。这一困境的主要原因在于JE-SSL的核心原则是不使用任何输入重构，因此缺少训练失败的视觉提示。加上无信息的损失值，这使得在新数据集上部署SSL变得困难，而这些数据集没有标签可以用来判断所学表示的质量。在这项研究中，我们开发了一个简单的无监督标准，用于指示所学JE-SSL表示的质量：它们的有效秩。尽管简单且计算友好，这种方法——命名为RankMe——允许评估JE-SSL表示的性能，即使是在不同的下游数据集上，也不需要任何标签。RankMe的一个额外好处是它没有任何训练或超参数需要调整。通过涉及数百个训练周期的全面实证实验，我们展示了如何使用RankMe进行超参数选择，与当前涉及数据集标签的选择方法相比，几乎不会降低最终性能。我们希望RankMe能够促进JE-SSL在无法依赖标签来评估表示质量的领域中的应用。

[No.364] Reinforcement Learning in Low-rank MDPs with Density Features
来源: ICML 2023
命中: rank
标题: 低秩MDP中的强化学习与密度特征
类别: 
作者: Audrey Huang;Jinglin Chen;Nan Jiang;
机构: University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2023/poster/24617
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2302.02252
英摘: MDPs with low-rank transitions -- that is, the transition matrix can be factored into the product of two matrices, left and right -- is a highly representative structure that enables tractable learning. The left matrix enables expressive function approximation for value-based learning and has been studied extensively. In this work, we instead investigate sample-efficient learning with density features, i.e., the right matrix, which induce powerful models for state-occupancy distributions. This setting not only sheds light on leveraging unsupervised learning in RL, but also enables plug-in solutions for convex RL. In the offline setting, we propose an algorithm for off-policy estimation of occupancies that can handle non-exploratory data. Using this as a subroutine, we further devise an online algorithm that constructs exploratory data distributions in a level-by-level manner. As a central technical challenge, the additive error of occupancy estimation is incompatible with the multiplicative definition of data coverage. In the absence of strong assumptions like reachability, this incompatibility easily leads to exponential error blow-up, which we overcome via novel technical tools. Our results also readily extend to the representation learning setting, when the density features are unknown and must be learned from an exponentially large candidate set.
中摘: 具有低秩转换的马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）——即，转换矩阵可以分解为两个矩阵的乘积，左边和右边——是一种高度代表性的结构，它使学习变得可行。左矩阵为基于价值的学习提供了富有表现力的功能逼近，并已被广泛研究。在本工作中，我们反而研究了使用密度特征的样本高效学习，即右矩阵，它诱导出强大的模型来描述状态占用分布。这种设置不仅揭示了在强化学习（RL）中利用无监督学习的可能性，而且还为凸性RL问题提供了插件解决方案。在离线设置下，我们提出了一种算法，用于处理非探索性数据的策略外占用估计。以此为子程序，我们进一步设计了一种在线算法，以逐级构建探索性数据分布。作为一个核心的技术挑战，占用估计的加性误差与数据覆盖的乘性定义不兼容。在没有如可达性等强假设的情况下，这种不兼容性很容易导致指数级的误差放大，我们通过新颖的技术工具克服了这一难题。我们的结果也直接适用于表示学习设置，当密度特征未知且必须从一个指数级大的候选集中学习时。

[No.365] Robust Consensus in Ranking Data Analysis: Definitions, Properties and Computational Issues
来源: ICML 2023
命中: rank
标题: 稳健排序数据分析中的一致共识：定义、性质和计算问题

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2303.12878
英摘: As the issue of robustness in AI systems becomes vital, statistical learning techniques that are reliable even in presence of partly contaminated data have to be developed. Preference data, in the form of (complete) rankings in the simplest situations, are no exception and the demand for appropriate concepts and tools is all the more pressing given that technologies fed by or producing this type of data (e.g. search engines, recommending systems) are now massively deployed. However, the lack of vector space structure for the set of rankings (i.e. the symmetric group $\mathfrak{S}_n$) and the complex nature of statistics considered in ranking data analysis make the formulation of robustness objectives in this domain challenging. In this paper, we introduce notions of robustness, together with dedicated statistical methods, for Consensus Ranking the flagship problem in ranking data analysis, aiming at summarizing a probability distribution on $\mathfrak{S}_n$ by a median ranking. Precisely, we propose specific extensions of the popular concept of breakdown point, tailored to consensus ranking, and address the related computational issues. Beyond the theoretical contributions, the relevance of the approach proposed is supported by an experimental study.
中摘: 随着AI系统中的鲁棒性问题变得至关重要，需要开发即使在数据部分受到污染的情况下也能保持可靠的统计学习技术。偏好数据，以最简单情况下的（完整）排名形式出现，也不例外；考虑到如今大量部署了由这种类型数据驱动或产生的技术（例如搜索引擎、推荐系统），对适当概念和工具的需求更加迫切。然而，由于排名集合（即对称群$\mathfrak{S}_n$）缺乏向量空间结构，以及在排名数据分析中考虑的统计量的复杂性质，这使得在这个领域中制定鲁棒性目标充满挑战。在这篇论文中，我们引入了针对排名数据分析旗舰问题——共识排名的鲁棒性概念，以及专门的统计方法，旨在通过中位排名来概括$\mathfrak{S}_n$上的概率分布。具体而言，我们提出了针对共识排名定制的流行崩溃点概念的特定扩展，并解决了相关的计算问题。除了理论贡献外，我们提出的方案的相关性得到了实验研究的支持。

[No.366] Subset Selection Based On Multiple Rankings in the Presence of Bias: Effectiveness of Fairness Constraints for Multiwinner Voting Score Functions
来源: ICML 2023
命中: rank
标题: 基于多重排名的偏见存在下子集选择：公平性约束对多胜者投票评分函数的有效性
类别: 
作者: Niclas Boehmer;L. Elisa Celis;Lingxiao Huang;Anay Mehrotra;Nisheeth K Vishnoi;

类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2023/poster/24160
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.09835
英摘: We consider the problem of subset selection where one is given multiple rankings of items and the goal is to select the highest ``quality'' subset. Score functions from the multiwinner voting literature have been used to aggregate rankings into quality scores for subsets. We study this setting of subset selection problems when, in addition, rankings may contain systemic or unconscious biases toward a group of items. For a general model of input rankings and biases, we show that requiring the selected subset to satisfy group fairness constraints can improve the quality of the selection with respect to unbiased rankings. Importantly, we show that for fairness constraints to be effective, different multiwinner score functions may require a drastically different number of rankings: While for some functions, fairness constraints need an exponential number of rankings to recover a close-to-optimal solution, for others, this dependency is only polynomial. This result relies on a novel notion of ``smoothness'' of submodular functions in this setting that quantifies how well a function can ``correctly'' assess the quality of items in the presence of bias. The results in this paper can be used to guide the choice of multiwinner score functions for the subset selection setting considered here; we additionally provide a tool to empirically enable this.
中摘: 我们考虑了子集选择问题，在这个问题中，给定多个物品的排名，目标是选择“质量”最高的子集。来自多胜利者投票文献的评分函数已被用于将排名聚合为子集的质量分数。我们研究了在子集选择问题的这种设置下，排名可能对一组物品存在系统性或无意识偏见的情况。对于输入排名和偏见的一般模型，我们表明，要求所选子集满足群体公平性约束可以提高相对于无偏排名的选择质量。重要的是，我们表明，为了使公平性约束有效，不同的多胜利者评分函数可能需要截然不同数量的排名：虽然对于某些函数，公平性约束需要指数数量的排名来恢复接近最优的解决方案，而对于其他函数，这种依赖性仅为多项式。这一结果依赖于在这个设置中对子模函数的“平滑性”的新颖概念，该概念量化了一个函数在存在偏见的情况下“正确”评估物品质量的能力。本文的结果可用于指导在此处考虑的子集选择设置中选择多胜利者评分函数；此外，我们还提供了一种工具以实证地实现这一点。

[No.368] The Power of Preconditioning in Overparameterized Low-Rank Matrix Sensing
来源: ICML 2023
命中: rank
标题: 先验条件在过参数化低秩矩阵感知中的作用
类别: 
作者: Xingyu Xu;Yandi Shen;Yuejie Chi;Cong Ma;
机构: University of Chicago;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2023/poster/24940
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2302.01186
英摘: We propose $\textsf{ScaledGD($λ$)}$, a preconditioned gradient descent method to tackle the low-rank matrix sensing problem when the true rank is unknown, and when the matrix is possibly ill-conditioned. Using overparametrized factor representations, $\textsf{ScaledGD($λ$)}$ starts from a small random initialization, and proceeds by gradient descent with a specific form of damped preconditioning to combat bad curvatures induced by overparameterization and ill-conditioning. At the expense of light computational overhead incurred by preconditioners, $\textsf{ScaledGD($λ$)}$ is remarkably robust to ill-conditioning compared to vanilla gradient descent ($\textsf{GD}$) even with overprameterization. Specifically, we show that, under the Gaussian design, $\textsf{ScaledGD($λ$)}$ converges to the true low-rank matrix at a constant linear rate after a small number of iterations that scales only logarithmically with respect to the condition number and the problem dimension. This significantly improves over the convergence rate of vanilla $\textsf{GD}$ which suffers from a polynomial dependency on the condition number. Our work provides evidence on the power of preconditioning in accelerating the convergence without hurting generalization in overparameterized learning.
中摘: 我们提出了一种预调节梯度下降方法$\textsf{ScaledGD(λ)}$，用于解决在真实秩未知且矩阵可能条件不良的情况下低秩矩阵感应问题。通过使用过度参数化的因子表示，$\textsf{ScaledGD(λ)}$从一个较小的随机初始化开始，然后通过具有特定形式的阻尼预调节进行梯度下降，以对抗由过度参数化和条件不良引起的不良曲率。尽管预调节器会带来轻微的计算开销，但与朴素梯度下降（$\textsf{GD}$）相比，即使在过度参数化的情况下，$\textsf{ScaledGD(λ)}$对条件不良显示出惊人的鲁棒性。具体而言，我们证明了，在高斯设计下，$\textsf{ScaledGD(λ)}$在经过少量迭代后以常数线性速率收敛到真实的低秩矩阵，这些迭代次数仅以条件数和问题维度的对数方式增长。这比朴素$\textsf{GD}$的收敛率有了显著改善，后者受到条件数多项式依赖的影响。我们的工作提供了预调节在加速收敛速度而不损害过度参数化学习中的泛化能力方面的强大作用的证据。

[No.369] Theoretical Bounds on the Network Community Profile from Low-rank Semi-definite Programming
来源: ICML 2023

英摘: We study a new connection between a technical measure called $μ$-conductance that arises in the study of Markov chains for sampling convex bodies and the network community profile that characterizes size-resolved properties of clusters and communities in social and information networks. [No.370] Tight Certification of Adversarially Trained Neural Networks via Nonconvex Low-Rank Semidefinite Relaxations
来源: ICML 2023
命中: rank
标题: 对抗训练神经网络的紧凑认证通过非凸低秩半定松弛
类别: 
作者: Hong-Ming Chiu;Richard Y. Zhang;
机构: University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2023/poster/25034
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2211.17244
英摘: Adversarial training is well-known to produce high-quality neural network models that are empirically robust against adversarial perturbations. Nevertheless, once a model has been adversarially trained, one often desires a certification that the model is truly robust against all future attacks. Unfortunately, when faced with adversarially trained models, all existing approaches have significant trouble making certifications that are strong enough to be practically useful. Linear programming (LP) techniques in particular face a "convex relaxation barrier" that prevent them from making high-quality certifications, even after refinement with mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) and branch-and-bound (BnB) techniques. In this paper, we propose a nonconvex certification technique, based on a low-rank restriction of a semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxation. The nonconvex relaxation makes strong certifications comparable to much more expensive SDP methods, while optimizing over dramatically fewer variables comparable to much weaker LP methods. Despite nonconvexity, we show how off-the-shelf local optimization algorithms can be used to achieve and to certify global optimality in polynomial time. Our experiments find that the nonconvex relaxation almost completely closes the gap towards exact certification of adversarially trained models.
中摘: 对抗训练以生成高质量的神经网络模型而闻名，这些模型在经验上能够抵抗对抗性扰动。然而，一旦模型经过了对抗性训练，人们通常希望获得一个认证，证明该模型确实能抵御所有未来的攻击。不幸的是，面对对抗性训练的模型时，所有现有方法在做出足够强大、具有实际应用价值的认证方面都存在重大困难。特别是线性规划(LP)技术面临着“凸松弛障碍”，这阻止它们做出高质量的认证，即使使用混合整数线性规划(MILP)和分支定界(BnB)技术进行细化也无法克服。在这篇论文中，我们提出了一种基于半正定规划(SDP)松弛的低秩限制的非凸认证技术。这种非凸松弛技术可以做出与成本远高的SDP方法相当的强大认证，同时优化的变量数量远少于成本较低但效果弱得多的LP方法。尽管是非凸的，但我们展示了如何使用现成的局部优化算法在多项式时间内实现并认证全局最优解。我们的实验发现，非凸松弛几乎完全填补了精确认证对抗性训练模型的差距。

[No.371] Which Tricks are Important for Learning to Rank?
来源: ICML 2023
命中: rank
标题: 哪些技巧对于学习排名很重要？

英摘: Nowadays, state-of-the-art learning-to-rank methods are based on gradient-boosted decision trees (GBDT). The most well-known algorithm is LambdaMART which was proposed more than a decade ago. Recently, several other GBDT-based ranking algorithms were proposed. In this paper, we thoroughly analyze these methods in a unified setup. In particular, we address the following questions. We also propose a simple improvement of the YetiRank approach that allows for optimizing specific ranking loss functions. As a result, we gain insights into learning-to-rank techniques and obtain a new state-of-the-art algorithm.
中摘: 如今，最先进的学习排序方法基于梯度提升决策树（GBDT）。最为人熟知的算法是LambdaMART，它在十多年前被提出。最近，提出了几种其他的基于GBDT的排序算法。在这篇论文中，我们在统一的框架下彻底分析了这些方法。具体而言，我们关注以下问题：直接优化平滑的排序损失是否优于优化凸替代函数？如何恰当地构建和平滑替代排序损失？为了解答这些问题，我们将LambdaMART与YetiRank和StochasticRank方法及其变体进行比较。我们还提出了一种对YetiRank方法的简单改进，使其能够优化特定的排序损失函数。结果，我们深入了解了学习排序技术，并获得了一种新的最先进算法。

[No.376] Privacy-Preserving Low-Rank Adaptation Against Membership Inference Attacks for Latent Diffusion Models
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: 隐私保护低秩适应以抵御成员推断攻击的潜在扩散模型
类别: computer vision v
作者: Zihao Luo;Xilie Xu;Feng Liu;Yun Sing Koh;Di Wang;Jingfeng Zhang;
机构: University of Auckland;National University of Singapore;University of Melbourne;King Abdullah University of Science and Technology;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32628
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.11989
英摘: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is an efficient strategy for adapting latent diffusion models (LDMs) on a private dataset to generate specific images by minimizing the adaptation loss. However, the LoRA-adapted LDMs are vulnerable to membership inference (MI) attacks that can judge whether a particular data point belongs to the private dataset, thus leading to the privacy leakage. To defend against MI attacks, we first propose a straightforward solution: Membership-Privacy-preserving LoRA (MP-LoRA). MP-LoRA is formulated as a min-max optimization problem where a proxy attack model is trained by maximizing its MI gain while the LDM is adapted by minimizing the sum of the adaptation loss and the MI gain of the proxy attack model. However, we empirically find that MP-LoRA has the issue of unstable optimization, and theoretically analyze that the potential reason is the unconstrained local smoothness, which impedes the privacy-preserving adaptation. To mitigate this issue, we further propose a Stable Membership-Privacy-preserving LoRA (SMP-LoRA) that adapts the LDM by minimizing the ratio of the adaptation loss to the MI gain. Besides, we theoretically prove that the local smoothness of SMP-LoRA can be constrained by the gradient norm, leading to improved convergence. Our experimental results corroborate that SMP-LoRA can indeed defend against MI attacks and generate high-quality images. Our Code is available at \url{https://github.com/WilliamLUO0/StablePrivateLoRA}.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）是一种有效策略，用于在私有数据集上调整潜在扩散模型（LDMs），通过最小化适应损失来生成特定图像。然而，经过LoRA调整的LDMs容易受到成员推断（MI）攻击，这种攻击可以判断特定数据点是否属于私有数据集，从而导致隐私泄露。为了防御MI攻击，我们首先提出了一种直接解决方案：成员隐私保护LoRA（MP-LoRA）。MP-LoRA被表述为一个极小极大优化问题，其中通过最大化其MI收益来训练代理攻击模型，而LDM则通过最小化适应损失与代理攻击模型的MI收益之和来进行调整。但是，我们经验性地发现MP-LoRA存在优化不稳定的问题，并从理论上分析了潜在的原因是无约束的局部平滑度，这阻碍了隐私保护适应。为了解决这个问题，我们进一步提出了稳定成员隐私保护LoRA（SMP-LoRA），它通过最小化适应损失与MI收益的比例来调整LDM。此外，我们从理论上证明了SMP-LoRA的局部平滑度可以通过梯度范数进行约束，从而改善收敛性。我们的实验结果证实，SMP-LoRA确实能够防御MI攻击并生成高质量图像。我们的代码可在以下网址获取：\url{https://github.com/WilliamLUO0/StablePrivateLoRA}。

英摘: Neural ranking models (NRMs) have been shown to be highly effective in terms of retrieval performance. Our approach starts by identifying anchor documents with higher ranking positions than the target document as nodes in the reasoning chain. [No.382] From Pairwise to Ranking: Climbing the Ladder to Ideal Collaborative Filtering with Pseudo-Ranking
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: 从成对比较到排名：攀登理想的协作过滤阶梯——伪排名的方法
类别: data mining knowledge management ii
作者: Yuhan Zhao;Rui Chen;Li Chen;Shuang Zhang;Qilong Han;Hongtao Song;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.18168
英摘: Intuitively, an ideal collaborative filtering (CF) model should learn from users' full rankings over all items to make optimal top-K recommendations. Due to the absence of such full rankings in practice, most CF models rely on pairwise loss functions to approximate full rankings, resulting in an immense performance gap. In this paper, we provide a novel analysis using the multiple ordinal classification concept to reveal the inevitable gap between a pairwise approximation and the ideal case. However, bridging the gap in practice encounters two formidable challenges: (1) none of the real-world datasets contains full ranking information; (2) there does not exist a loss function that is capable of consuming ranking information. To overcome these challenges, we propose a pseudo-ranking paradigm (PRP) that addresses the lack of ranking information by introducing pseudo-rankings supervised by an original noise injection mechanism. Additionally, we put forward a new ranking loss function designed to handle ranking information effectively. To ensure our method's robustness against potential inaccuracies in pseudo-rankings, we equip the ranking loss function with a gradient-based confidence mechanism to detect and mitigate abnormal gradients. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets demonstrate that PRP significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods.
中摘: 直观上，理想的协同过滤（CF）模型应该从用户对所有项目的完整排名中学习，以做出最优的前K项推荐。然而，在实践中由于缺乏这样的完整排名，大多数CF模型依赖于成对损失函数来近似完整排名，导致巨大的性能差距。在本文中，我们采用多级序分类的概念提供了一种新颖的分析，揭示了成对近似与理想情况之间不可避免的差距。然而，在实际中弥合这一差距面临着两大严峻挑战：(1)没有任何现实世界的数据集包含完整的排名信息；(2)不存在能够处理排名信息的损失函数。为克服这些挑战，我们提出了一种伪排名范式（PRP），通过引入由原始噪声注入机制监督的伪排名来解决排名信息缺失的问题。此外，我们提出了一种新的排名损失函数，旨在有效处理排名信息。为了确保我们的方法能够抵御伪排名潜在的不准确性，我们在排名损失函数中装备了一个基于梯度的置信机制，用于检测和缓解异常梯度。在四个真实世界数据集上的广泛实验表明，PRP显著优于最先进的方法。

[No.385] Bayesian Low-Rank Learning (Bella): A Practical Approach to Bayesian Neural Networks
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: 贝叶斯低秩学习（Bella）：一种实用的贝叶斯神经网络方法
类别: machine learning i
作者: Bao Gia Doan;Afshar Shamsi;Xiao-Yu Guo;Arash Mohammadi;Hamid Alinejad-Rokny;Dino Sejdinovic;Damien Teney;Damith C. Ranasinghe;Ehsan Abbasnejad;

类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33790
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.20891
英摘: Computational complexity of Bayesian learning is impeding its adoption in practical, large-scale tasks. Despite demonstrations of significant merits such as improved robustness and resilience to unseen or out-of-distribution inputs over their non- Bayesian counterparts, their practical use has faded to near insignificance. In this study, we introduce an innovative framework to mitigate the computational burden of Bayesian neural networks (BNNs). Our approach follows the principle of Bayesian techniques based on deep ensembles, but significantly reduces their cost via multiple low-rank perturbations of parameters arising from a pre-trained neural network. Both vanilla version of ensembles as well as more sophisticated schemes such as Bayesian learning with Stein Variational Gradient Descent (SVGD), previously deemed impractical for large models, can be seamlessly implemented within the proposed framework, called Bayesian Low-Rank LeArning (Bella). In a nutshell, i) Bella achieves a dramatic reduction in the number of trainable parameters required to approximate a Bayesian posterior; and ii) it not only maintains, but in some instances, surpasses the performance of conventional Bayesian learning methods and non-Bayesian baselines. Our results with large-scale tasks such as ImageNet, CAMELYON17, DomainNet, VQA with CLIP, LLaVA demonstrate the effectiveness and versatility of Bella in building highly scalable and practical Bayesian deep models for real-world applications.
中摘:     概率学习的计算复杂性阻碍了其在实际、大规模任务中的应用。尽管与非贝叶斯方法相比，贝叶斯学习展现出显著优势，如增强对未见或分布外输入的鲁棒性和抗干扰能力，但其实际应用已几乎微不足道。本研究提出了一种创新框架，旨在减轻贝叶斯神经网络（BNN）的计算负担。我们的方法遵循基于深度集合的贝叶斯技术原理，但通过从预训练神经网络参数出发的多次低秩扰动，大幅降低了成本。以前被认为在大型模型中不切实际的方法，如使用Stein变分梯度下降（SVGD）的贝叶斯学习，现在可以无缝地在我们提出的框架内实施，该框架被称为贝叶斯低秩学习（Bella）。简而言之，i) Bella实现了对贝叶斯后验概率逼近所需的可训练参数数量的显著减少；ii) 它不仅保持，在某些情况下甚至超越了传统贝叶斯学习方法和非贝叶斯基线的表现。我们在ImageNet、CAMELYON17、DomainNet、结合CLIP的VQA以及LLaVA等大规模任务上的实验结果，证明了Bella在构建高度可扩展且适用于现实世界应用的贝叶斯深度模型方面的有效性和通用性。

[No.386] A Wander Through the Multimodal Landscape: Efficient Transfer Learning via Low-rank Sequence Multimodal Adapter
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: 穿越多模态景观：通过低秩序列多模态适配器实现高效的迁移学习

英摘: Efficient transfer learning methods such as adapter-based methods have shown great success in unimodal models and vision-language models. Secondly, they exhibit limited exploitation of interactions between modalities and lack efficiency. To address these issues, in this paper, we propose the loW-rank sequence multimodal adapter (Wander). With these designs, Wander enables token-level interactions between sequences of different modalities in a parameter-efficient way. The results fully demonstrate the effectiveness, efficiency and universality of Wander.

[No.390] RILQ: Rank-Insensitive LoRA-Based Quantization Error Compensation for Boosting 2-Bit Large Language Model Accuracy
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: Rank-Insensitive LoRA-Based量化误差补偿技术以提升2位宽大型语言模型的准确性

英摘: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has become the dominant method for parameter-efficient LLM fine-tuning, with LoRA-based quantization error compensation (LQEC) emerging as a powerful tool for recovering accuracy in compressed LLMs. However, LQEC has underperformed in sub-4-bit scenarios, with no prior investigation into understanding this limitation. We propose RILQ (Rank-Insensitive LoRA-based Quantization Error Compensation) to understand fundamental limitation and boost 2-bit LLM accuracy. Based on rank analysis revealing model-wise activation discrepancy loss's rank-insensitive nature, RILQ employs this loss to adjust adapters cooperatively across layers, enabling robust error compensation with low-rank adapters. RILQ maintains computational efficiency comparable to existing LoRA methods, enabling adapter-merged weight-quantized LLM inference with significantly enhanced accuracy, making it a promising approach for boosting 2-bit LLM performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/aiha-lab/RILQ.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）已成为参数高效的大语言模型（LLM）微调的主要方法，基于LoRA的量化误差补偿（LQEC）作为一种恢复压缩LLM精度的强大工具应运而生。然而，LQEC在低于4位的情况下表现不佳，此前没有研究深入探讨这一限制。我们提出了RILQ（秩不敏感LoRA量化误差补偿），以理解基本限制并提高2位LLM的准确性。基于秩分析揭示了模型级激活差异损失的秩不敏感性质，RILQ利用这种损失来跨层协同调整适配器，使低秩适配器能够实现稳健的误差补偿。在LLaMA-2和LLaMA-3上的评估表明，RILQ在各种最先进的量化器的2位量化推理中持续提高了性能，并在特定任务的微调中增强了准确度。RILQ保持与现有LoRA方法相当的计算效率，使合并适配器的权重量化LLM推理具有显著增强的准确性，使其成为提升2位LLM性能的有前景的方法。我们的代码可在https://github.com/aiha-lab/RILQ获取。

[No.393] In-depth Analysis of Low-rank Matrix Factorisation in a Federated Setting
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: 在联邦学习环境中对低秩矩阵因子分解的深入分析
类别: machine learning v
作者: Constantin Philippenko;Kevin Scaman;Laurent Massoulié;
机构: INRIA Paris;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/34192
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.08771
英摘: We analyze a distributed algorithm to compute a low-rank matrix factorization on $N$ clients, each holding a local dataset $\mathbf{S}^i \in \mathbb{R}^{n_i \times d}$, mathematically, we seek to solve $min_{\mathbf{U}^i \in \mathbb{R}^{n_i\times r}, \mathbf{V}\in \mathbb{R}^{d \times r} } \frac{1}{2} \sum_{i=1}^N \|\mathbf{S}^i - \mathbf{U}^i \mathbf{V}^\top\|^2_{\text{F}}$. Considering a power initialization of $\mathbf{V}$, we rewrite the previous smooth non-convex problem into a smooth strongly-convex problem that we solve using a parallel Nesterov gradient descent potentially requiring a single step of communication at the initialization step. For any client $i$ in $\{1, \dots, N\}$, we obtain a global $\mathbf{V}$ in $\mathbb{R}^{d \times r}$ common to all clients and a local variable $\mathbf{U}^i$ in $\mathbb{R}^{n_i \times r}$. We provide a linear rate of convergence of the excess loss which depends on $σ_{\max} / σ_{r}$, where $σ_{r}$ is the $r^{\mathrm{th}}$ singular value of the concatenation $\mathbf{S}$ of the matrices $(\mathbf{S}^i)_{i=1}^N$. This result improves the rates of convergence given in the literature, which depend on $σ_{\max}^2 / σ_{\min}^2$. We provide an upper bound on the Frobenius-norm error of reconstruction under the power initialization strategy. We complete our analysis with experiments on both synthetic and real data.
中摘: 我们分析了一种分布式算法，用于在N个客户端上计算低秩矩阵分解，每个客户端持有一个局部数据集$\mathbf{S}^i \in \mathbb{R}^{n_i \times d}$。从数学上讲，我们的目标是求解$min_{\mathbf{U}^i \in \mathbb{R}^{n_i\times r}, \mathbf{V}\in \mathbb{R}^{d \times r} } \frac{1}{2} \sum_{i=1}^N \|\mathbf{S}^i - \mathbf{U}^i \mathbf{V}^\top\|^2_{\text{F}}$。考虑到$\mathbf{V}$的幂级数初始化，我们将之前的平滑非凸问题重写为一个平滑强凸问题，并使用并行的Nesterov梯度下降法解决该问题，可能只需要在初始化阶段进行一次通信步骤。对于任何客户端$i$在$\{1, \dots, N\}$中，我们得到一个所有客户端共有的全局变量$\mathbf{V}$在$\mathbb{R}^{d \times r}$和一个局部变量$\mathbf{U}^i$在$\mathbb{R}^{n_i \times r}$。我们提供了过量损失收敛的线性速率，它依赖于$σ_{\max} / σ_{r}$，其中$σ_{r}$是矩阵$(\mathbf{S}^i)_{i=1}^N$的拼接$\mathbf{S}$的$r^{\mathrm{th}}$奇异值。这一结果改进了文献中给出的收敛率，这些收敛率依赖于$σ_{\max}^2 / σ_{\min}^2$。我们还提供了在幂级数初始化策略下重构的Frobenius范数误差的上界。我们的分析通过在合成数据和真实数据上的实验得以完善。

[No.394] Enhancing Trustworthiness of Graph Neural Networks with Rank-Based Conformal Training
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: 基于排名的可信性训练增强图神经网络的信任worthiness
类别: machine learning vi
作者: Ting Wang;Zhixin Zhou;Rui Luo;

英摘: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) has been widely used in a variety of fields because of their great potential in representing graph-structured data. However, lacking of rigorous uncertainty estimations limits their application in high-stakes. Conformal Prediction (CP) can produce statistically guaranteed uncertainty estimates by using the classifier's probability estimates to obtain prediction sets, which contains the true class with a user-specified probability. In this paper, we propose a Rank-based CP during training framework to GNNs (RCP-GNN) for reliable uncertainty estimates to enhance the trustworthiness of GNNs in the node classification scenario. By exploiting rank information of the classifier's outcome, prediction sets with desired coverage rate can be efficiently constructed. The strategy of CP during training with differentiable rank-based conformity loss function is further explored to adapt prediction sets according to network topology information. In this way, the composition of prediction sets can be guided by the goal of jointly reducing inefficiency and probability estimation errors. Extensive experiments on several real-world datasets show that our model achieves any pre-defined target marginal coverage while significantly reducing the inefficiency compared with state-of-the-art methods.
中摘: 图神经网络（GNNs）因其在表示图结构数据方面的巨大潜力而在众多领域得到广泛应用。然而，缺乏严谨的不确定性估计限制了其在高风险场景中的应用。一致预测（Conformal Prediction，CP）能够通过利用分类器的概率估计来生成预测集，这些预测集以用户指定的概率包含真实类别，从而提供统计上保证的不确定性估计。本文提出了一种基于排名的一致预测训练框架应用于GNNs（简称RCP-GNN），以获得可靠的不确定性估计，增强GNNs在节点分类场景下的可信度。通过利用分类器输出的排名信息，可以高效地构建具有期望覆盖率的预测集。进一步探索了训练过程中使用可微分的基于排名的一致性损失函数的CP策略，以根据网络拓扑信息调整预测集。这样，预测集的组成可以在减少效率低下和概率估计误差的目标指导下进行优化。在几个真实世界数据集上的广泛实验表明，我们的模型实现了任何预定义的目标边际覆盖率，同时与最先进的方法相比显著降低了效率低下问题。

[No.395] MTL-LoRA: Low-Rank Adaptation for Multi-Task Learning
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: MTL-LoRA：多任务学习中的低秩适应
类别: machine learning vi
作者: Yaming Yang;Dilxat Muhtar;Yelong Shen;Yuefeng Zhan;Jianfeng Liu;Yujing Wang;Hao Sun;Weiwei Deng;Feng Sun;Qi Zhang;Weizhu Chen;Yunhai Tong;
机构: Peking University;Nanjing University;Microsoft;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/35509
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09437
英摘: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has been widely employed for domain adaptation, with LoRA being one of the most prominent methods due to its simplicity and effectiveness. However, in multi-task learning (MTL) scenarios, LoRA tends to obscure the distinction between tasks by projecting sparse high-dimensional features from different tasks into the same dense low-dimensional intrinsic space. This leads to task interference and suboptimal performance for LoRA and its variants. To tackle this challenge, we propose MTL-LoRA, which retains the advantages of low-rank adaptation while significantly enhancing MTL capabilities. MTL-LoRA augments LoRA by incorporating additional task-adaptive parameters that differentiate task-specific information and capture shared knowledge across various tasks within low-dimensional spaces. This approach enables pre-trained models to jointly adapt to different target domains with a limited number of trainable parameters. Comprehensive experimental results, including evaluations on public academic benchmarks for natural language understanding, commonsense reasoning, and image-text understanding, as well as real-world industrial text Ads relevance datasets, demonstrate that MTL-LoRA outperforms LoRA and its various variants with comparable or even fewer learnable parameters in MTL setting.
中摘: 参数高效微调（PEFT）在领域适应中得到了广泛应用，LoRA因其简单性和有效性而成为最突出的方法之一。然而，在多任务学习（MTL）场景下，LoRA倾向于将不同任务的稀高维特征映射到同一密集低维内在空间中，模糊了任务间的界限。这会导致任务干扰和LoRA及其变体的性能次优。为了解决这一挑战，我们提出了MTL-LoRA，它保留了低秩适应的优点，同时显著增强了MTL能力。MTL-LoRA通过引入额外的任务自适应参数来增强LoRA，这些参数区分了特定于任务的信息，并在低维空间内捕获了跨各种任务的共享知识。这种方法使预训练模型能够在有限数量的可训练参数下，共同适应不同的目标领域。全面的实验结果，包括对自然语言理解、常识推理和图像文本理解的公共学术基准以及现实世界工业文本广告相关性数据集的评估，证明了在MTL设置下，MTL-LoRA在可比甚至更少的学习参数下，优于LoRA及其多种变体。

[No.396] OTLRM: Orthogonal Learning-based Low-Rank Metric for Multi-Dimensional Inverse Problems
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: 正交学习为基础的低秩度量多维逆问题
类别: machine learning vi
作者: Xiangming Wang;Haijin Zeng;Jiaoyang Chen;Sheng Liu;Yongyong Chen;Guoqing Chao;
机构: Harbin Institute of Technology;Ghent University;University of Electronic Science and Technology of China;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/35427
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11165
英摘: In real-world scenarios, complex data such as multispectral images and multi-frame videos inherently exhibit robust low-rank property. This property is vital for multi-dimensional inverse problems, such as tensor completion, spectral imaging reconstruction, and multispectral image denoising. Existing tensor singular value decomposition (t-SVD) definitions rely on hand-designed or pre-given transforms, which lack flexibility for defining tensor nuclear norm (TNN). The TNN-regularized optimization problem is solved by the singular value thresholding (SVT) operator, which leverages the t-SVD framework to obtain the low-rank tensor. However, it is quite complicated to introduce SVT into deep neural networks due to the numerical instability problem in solving the derivatives of the eigenvectors. In this paper, we introduce a novel data-driven generative low-rank t-SVD model based on the learnable orthogonal transform, which can be naturally solved under its representation. Prompted by the linear algebra theorem of the Householder transformation, our learnable orthogonal transform is achieved by constructing an endogenously orthogonal matrix adaptable to neural networks, optimizing it as arbitrary orthogonal matrices. Additionally, we propose a low-rank solver as a generalization of SVT, which utilizes an efficient representation of generative networks to obtain low-rank structures. Extensive experiments highlight its significant restoration enhancements.
中摘: 在现实场景中，复杂数据如多光谱图像和多帧视频天然展现出强大的低秩特性。这一特性对于多维反问题至关重要，例如张量补全、光谱成像重建和多光谱图像去噪。现有的张量奇异值分解（t-SVD）定义依赖于手工设计或预设的变换，这为定义张量核范数（TNN）缺乏灵活性。TNN正则化的优化问题通过奇异值阈值（SVT）算子解决，该算子利用t-SVD框架来获取低秩张量。然而，由于求解特征向量导数时的数值不稳定性问题，将SVT引入深度神经网络相当复杂。本文提出了一种基于可学习正交变换的新型数据驱动生成低秩t-SVD模型，该模型可以在其表示下自然求解。受Householder变换线性代数定理的启发，我们通过构建内生正交矩阵实现了可学习正交变换，这种矩阵适应神经网络，并将其优化为任意正交矩阵。此外，我们还提出了一种低秩求解器作为SVT的泛化，它利用生成网络的有效表示来获得低秩结构。广泛的实验突显了其显著的恢复增强效果。

[No.397] LoRID: Low-Rank Iterative Diffusion for Adversarial Purification
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: 低秩迭代扩散对抗净化
类别: machine learning vii
作者: Geigh Zollicoffer;Minh N. Vu;Ben Nebgen;Juan Castorena;Boian Alexandrov;Manish Bhattarai;
机构: Georgia Institute of Technology;Los Alamos National Laboratory;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/34472
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.08255
英摘: This work presents an information-theoretic examination of diffusion-based purification methods, the state-of-the-art adversarial defenses that utilize diffusion models to remove malicious perturbations in adversarial examples. By theoretically characterizing the inherent purification errors associated with the Markov-based diffusion purifications, we introduce LoRID, a novel Low-Rank Iterative Diffusion purification method designed to remove adversarial perturbation with low intrinsic purification errors. LoRID centers around a multi-stage purification process that leverages multiple rounds of diffusion-denoising loops at the early time-steps of the diffusion models, and the integration of Tucker decomposition, an extension of matrix factorization, to remove adversarial noise at high-noise regimes. Consequently, LoRID increases the effective diffusion time-steps and overcomes strong adversarial attacks, achieving superior robustness performance in CIFAR-10/100, CelebA-HQ, and ImageNet datasets under both white-box and black-box settings.
中摘: 本研究从信息论的角度审视了基于扩散的净化方法，这是一种利用扩散模型去除对抗样本中有害扰动的前沿防御技术。通过理论分析与马尔科夫模型相关的扩散净化固有的误差，我们引入了LoRID，一种新颖的低秩迭代扩散净化方法，旨在以较低的内在净化误差消除对抗性扰动。LoRID围绕一个多阶段净化过程构建，该过程在扩散模型的早期时间步长中利用多轮扩散去噪循环，并结合了图克分解（Tucker decomposition），即矩阵分解的一种扩展，用于在高噪声条件下去除对抗性噪声。因此，LoRID增加了有效的扩散时间步长，克服了强大的对抗性攻击，在CIFAR-10/100、CelebA-HQ和ImageNet数据集下，在白盒和黑盒设置中均实现了优越的鲁棒性能。

[No.398] ScaleOT: Privacy-utility-scalable Offsite-tuning with Dynamic LayerReplace and Selective Rank Compression
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: ScaleOT：隐私与实用性可调节的离线调优，结合动态层替换和选择性秩压缩
类别: machine learning vii
作者: Kai Yao;Zhaorui Tan;Tiandi Ye;Lichun Li;Yuan Zhao;Wenyan Liu;Wei Wang;Jianke Zhu;

类型: Technical

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09812
英摘: Offsite-tuning is a privacy-preserving method for tuning large language models (LLMs) by sharing a lossy compressed emulator from the LLM owners with data owners for downstream task tuning. This approach protects the privacy of both the model and data owners. However, current offsite tuning methods often suffer from adaptation degradation, high computational costs, and limited protection strength due to uniformly dropping LLM layers or relying on expensive knowledge distillation. To address these issues, we propose ScaleOT, a novel privacy-utility-scalable offsite-tuning framework that effectively balances privacy and utility. ScaleOT introduces a novel layerwise lossy compression algorithm that uses reinforcement learning to obtain the importance of each layer. It employs lightweight networks, termed harmonizers, to replace the raw LLM layers. By combining important original LLM layers and harmonizers in different ratios, ScaleOT generates emulators tailored for optimal performance with various model scales for enhanced privacy protection. Additionally, we present a rank reduction method to further compress the original LLM layers, significantly enhancing privacy with negligible impact on utility. Comprehensive experiments show that ScaleOT can achieve nearly lossless offsite tuning performance compared with full fine-tuning while obtaining better model privacy.
中摘: 离站调优（Offsite-tuning）是一种保护隐私的方法，用于通过与数据所有者共享大型语言模型（LLM）所有者的有损压缩模拟器来调整大型语言模型，以完成下游任务调优。这种方法同时保护了模型和数据所有者的隐私。然而，当前的离站调优方法通常受到适应性退化、高计算成本以及因统一删除LLM层或依赖昂贵的知识蒸馏而带来的有限保护强度的影响。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了ScaleOT，这是一种新颖的隐私-效用可扩展离站调优框架，能够有效平衡隐私和效用。ScaleOT引入了一种创新的逐层有损压缩算法，使用强化学习获取每一层的重要性。它采用轻量级网络，称为调谐器（harmonizers），来替换原始的LLM层。通过以不同比例结合重要的原始LLM层和调谐器，ScaleOT生成了针对不同模型规模优化性能的模拟器，从而增强了隐私保护。此外，我们提出了一种秩降低方法来进一步压缩原始LLM层，显著增强了隐私保护，对效用的影响微乎其微。全面的实验表明，ScaleOT能够在获得更好的模型隐私的同时，实现几乎无损失的离站调优性能，与完全微调相比几乎无差异。

[No.399] Gradient Weight-normalized Low-rank Projection for Efficient LLM Training
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: 渐变归一化权重低秩投影以实现高效的LLM训练
类别: natural language processing ii
作者: Jia-Hong Huang;Yixian Shen;Hongyi Zhu;Stevan Rudinac;Evangelos Kanoulas;

英摘: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown remarkable performance across various tasks, but the escalating demands on computational resources pose significant challenges, particularly in the extensive utilization of full fine-tuning for downstream tasks. To address this, parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods have been developed, but they often underperform compared to full fine-tuning and struggle with memory efficiency. In this work, we introduce Gradient Weight-Normalized Low-Rank Projection (GradNormLoRP), a novel approach that enhances both parameter and memory efficiency while maintaining comparable performance to full fine-tuning. GradNormLoRP normalizes the weight matrix to improve gradient conditioning, facilitating better convergence during optimization. Additionally, it applies low-rank approximations to the weight and gradient matrices, significantly reducing memory usage during training. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our 8-bit GradNormLoRP reduces optimizer memory usage by up to 89.5% and enables the pre-training of large LLMs, such as LLaMA 7B, on consumer-level GPUs like the NVIDIA RTX 4090, without additional inference costs. Moreover, GradNormLoRP outperforms existing low-rank methods in fine-tuning tasks. For instance, when fine-tuning the RoBERTa model on all GLUE tasks with a rank of 8, GradNormLoRP achieves an average score of 80.65, surpassing LoRA's score of 79.23. These results underscore GradNormLoRP as a promising alternative for efficient LLM pre-training and fine-tuning. Source code: https://github.com/Jhhuangkay/Gradient-Weight-normalized-Low-rank-Projection-for-Efficient-LLM-Training
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在各种任务上展现了卓越的表现，但对计算资源日益增长的需求带来了显著挑战，特别是在下游任务中广泛使用全微调时。为了解决这一问题，已经开发了参数高效微调（PEFT）方法，但它们往往比全微调表现差，并且在内存效率方面存在困难。在这项工作中，我们引入了一种名为梯度权重归一化低秩投影（GradNormLoRP）的新方法，它在保持与全微调相当的性能的同时，提高了参数和内存效率。GradNormLoRP通过对权重矩阵进行归一化以改善梯度条件，从而在优化过程中实现更好的收敛。此外，它对权重和梯度矩阵应用低秩近似，大幅减少了训练过程中的内存使用。广泛的实验表明，我们的8位GradNormLoRP将优化器的内存使用量减少了高达89.5%，并使得能够在消费者级别的GPU上，如NVIDIA RTX 4090，预训练大型LLMs，例如LLaMA 7B，而无需增加推理成本。此外，GradNormLoRP在微调任务中超越了现有的低秩方法。例如，在所有GLUE任务上，当使用8的秩对RoBERTa模型进行微调时，GradNormLoRP达到了平均80.65的分数，超过了LoRA的79.23分。这些结果凸显了GradNormLoRP作为高效LLM预训练和微调的有前景替代方案的地位。源代码：https://github.com/Jhhuangkay/Gradient-Weight-normalized-Low-rank-Projection-for-Efficient-LLM-Training

[No.401] Is There No Such Thing as a Bad Question? H4R: HalluciBot for Ratiocination, Rewriting, Ranking, and Routing
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: 《没有所谓的坏问题吗？》H4R：用于推理、重写、排序和路由的幻觉机器人

英摘: Hallucination continues to be one of the most critical challenges in the institutional adoption journey of Large Language Models (LLMs). While prior studies have primarily focused on the post-generation analysis and refinement of outputs, this paper centers on the effectiveness of queries in eliciting accurate responses from LLMs. We present HalluciBot, a model that estimates the query's propensity to hallucinate before generation, without invoking any LLMs during inference. HalluciBot can serve as a proxy reward model for query rewriting, offering a general framework to estimate query quality based on accuracy and consensus. In essence, HalluciBot investigates how poorly constructed queries can lead to erroneous outputs - moreover, by employing query rewriting guided by HalluciBot's empirical estimates, we demonstrate that 95.7% output accuracy can be achieved for Multiple Choice questions. The training procedure for HalluciBot consists of perturbing 369,837 queries n times, employing n+1 independent LLM agents, sampling an output from each query, conducting a Multi-Agent Monte Carlo simulation on the sampled outputs, and training an encoder classifier. The idea of perturbation is the outcome of our ablation studies that measures the increase in output diversity (+12.5 agreement spread) by perturbing a query in lexically different but semantically similar ways. Therefore, HalluciBot paves the way to ratiocinate (76.0% test F1 score, 46.6% in saved computation on hallucinatory queries), rewrite (+30.2% positive class transition from hallucinatory to non-hallucinatory), rank (+50.6% positive class transition from hallucinatory to non-hallucinatory), and route queries to effective pipelines.

[No.407] LRANet: Towards Accurate and Efficient Scene Text Detection with Low-Rank Approximation Network
来源: AAAI 2024
命中: rank
标题: LRANet：基于低秩逼近网络的准确高效场景文本检测
类别: computer vision iv
作者: Yuchen Su;Zhineng Chen;Zhiwen Shao;Yuning Du;Zhilong Ji;Jinfeng Bai;Yong Zhou;Yu-Gang Jiang;

英摘: Recently, regression-based methods, which predict parameterized text shapes for text localization, have gained popularity in scene text detection. However, the existing parameterized text shape methods still have limitations in modeling arbitrary-shaped texts due to ignoring the utilization of text-specific shape information. Moreover, the time consumption of the entire pipeline has been largely overlooked, leading to a suboptimal overall inference speed. To address these issues, we first propose a novel parameterized text shape method based on low-rank approximation. Unlike other shape representation methods that employ data-irrelevant parameterization, our approach utilizes singular value decomposition and reconstructs the text shape using a few eigenvectors learned from labeled text contours. By exploring the shape correlation among different text contours, our method achieves consistency, compactness, simplicity, and robustness in shape representation. Next, we propose a dual assignment scheme for speed acceleration. It adopts a sparse assignment branch to accelerate the inference speed, and meanwhile, provides ample supervised signals for training through a dense assignment branch. Building upon these designs, we implement an accurate and efficient arbitrary-shaped text detector named LRANet. Extensive experiments are conducted on several challenging benchmarks, demonstrating the superior accuracy and efficiency of LRANet compared to state-of-the-art methods. Code is available at: \url{https://github.com/ychensu/LRANet.git}
中摘: 最近，基于回归的方法在场景文本检测中的文本定位预测参数化文本形状变得流行。然而，现有的参数化文本形状方法在建模任意形状的文本时仍存在局限性，因为忽略了利用文本特有的形状信息。此外，整个流程的时间消耗被大大忽视，导致整体推理速度次优。为了解决这些问题，我们首先提出了一种基于低秩近似的新颖参数化文本形状方法。与使用与数据无关的参数化的其他形状表示方法不同，我们的方法利用奇异值分解，并使用从标记的文本轮廓中学到的少数特征向量来重建文本形状。通过探索不同文本轮廓之间的形状相关性，我们的方法在形状表示上实现了连贯性、紧凑性、简单性和鲁棒性。接下来，我们提出了一种双分配方案以加速速度。它采用稀疏分配分支来加快推理速度，同时，通过密集分配分支提供充足的监督信号用于训练。基于这些设计，我们实现了一个准确且高效的任意形状文本检测器，名为LRANet。在几个具有挑战性的基准上进行了广泛的实验，证明了LRANet相比最先进方法的优越准确性和效率。代码可在以下链接获取：\url{https://github.com/ychensu/LRANet.git} 

（注：\url{}部分通常用于插入URL，但在中文翻译中一般直接写出网址，故此处翻译为“代码可在以下链接获取：https://github.com/ychensu/LRANet.git”）
