[No.408] Learning to Rank in Generative Retrieval
来源: AAAI 2024
命中: rank
标题: 生成式检索中的学习排序
类别: data mining knowledge management
作者: Yongqi Li;Nan Yang;Liang Wang;Furu Wei;Wenjie Li;
机构: Hong Kong Polytechnic University;Microsoft;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/28717
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.15222
英摘: Generative retrieval stands out as a promising new paradigm in text retrieval that aims to generate identifier strings of relevant passages as the retrieval target. This generative paradigm taps into powerful generative language models, distinct from traditional sparse or dense retrieval methods. However, only learning to generate is insufficient for generative retrieval. Generative retrieval learns to generate identifiers of relevant passages as an intermediate goal and then converts predicted identifiers into the final passage rank list. The disconnect between the learning objective of autoregressive models and the desired passage ranking target leads to a learning gap. To bridge this gap, we propose a learning-to-rank framework for generative retrieval, dubbed LTRGR. LTRGR enables generative retrieval to learn to rank passages directly, optimizing the autoregressive model toward the final passage ranking target via a rank loss. This framework only requires an additional learning-to-rank training phase to enhance current generative retrieval systems and does not add any burden to the inference stage. We conducted experiments on three public benchmarks, and the results demonstrate that LTRGR achieves state-of-the-art performance among generative retrieval methods. The code and checkpoints are released at https://github.com/liyongqi67/LTRGR.
中摘: 生成式检索作为一种新兴且前景广阔的文本检索范式，其目标是生成相关段落的标识字符串作为检索目标。这种生成式范式利用了强大的生成语言模型，与传统的稀疏或密集检索方法截然不同。然而，仅学习生成是不足以实现生成式检索的。生成式检索将学习生成相关段落的标识作为中间目标，然后将预测的标识转换为最终的段落排名列表。自回归模型的学习目标与所期望的段落排名目标之间的不匹配导致了学习差距。为了解决这一差距，我们提出了一种名为LTRGR（Learning-to-Rank for Generative Retrieval）的生成式检索学习排序框架。LTRGR使生成式检索能够直接学习段落的排序，通过排名损失优化自回归模型以达到最终的段落排名目标。该框架只需要额外的学习排序训练阶段来增强当前的生成式检索系统，并不会给推理阶段增加任何负担。我们在三个公开基准数据集上进行了实验，结果表明LTRGR在生成式检索方法中实现了最先进的性能。代码和检查点已发布在https://github.com/liyongqi67/LTRGR。

来源: AAAI 2024

类别: data mining knowledge management
作者: Zhiguang Yang;Liufang Sang;Haoran Wang;Wenlong Chen;Lu Wang;Jie He;Changping Peng;Zhangang Lin;Chun Gan;Jingping Shao;

英摘: "Creativity is the heart and soul of advertising services". With the advent of AI-Generated Content, advertisers now can produce vast amounts of creative content at a minimal cost. The current challenge lies in how advertising systems can select the most pertinent creative in real-time for each user personally. Existing methods typically perform serial ranking of ads or creatives, limiting the creative module in terms of both effectiveness and efficiency. In this paper, we propose for the first time a novel architecture for online parallel estimation of ads and creatives ranking, as well as the corresponding offline joint optimization model. 中摘: “创造力是广告服务的心脏和灵魂”。有效的创意可以创造双赢的局面：广告商能够更有效地触达目标用户并实现营销目标，用户可以更快地找到感兴趣的产品，平台则可以产生更多的广告收入。随着AI生成内容的出现，广告商现在可以以极低的成本生产大量的创意内容。当前的挑战在于广告系统如何为每个用户实时选择最相关的创意。现有的方法通常进行串行的广告或创意排名，这在效果和效率上都限制了创意模块。在这篇论文中，我们首次提出了一种新颖的在线并行评估广告和创意排名的架构，以及相应的离线联合优化模型。在线架构使复杂的个性化创意建模成为可能，同时降低了整体延迟。用于点击率（CTR）估计的离线联合模型允许广告和创意之间的相互感知和协同优化。此外，我们针对广告创意排名中涉及的隐式反馈排序任务优化了离线评估指标。我们进行了广泛的实验来比较我们的方法与两种最先进的方法。结果表明，我们的方法在响应时间、CTR和CPM方面，在离线评估和现实世界的在线广告平台上均有效。

[No.410] Perturbation-Invariant Adversarial Training for Neural Ranking Models: Improving the Effectiveness-Robustness Trade-Off
来源: AAAI 2024
命中: rank
标题: 神经排名模型的扰动不变对抗训练：提高有效性-稳健性权衡
类别: data mining knowledge management
作者: Yu-An Liu;Ruqing Zhang;Mingkun Zhang;Wei Chen;Maarten de Rijke;Jiafeng Guo;Xueqi Cheng;
机构: Chinese Academy of Sciences;University of Chinese Academy of Sciences;University of Amsterdam;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/28730
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.10329
英摘: Neural ranking models (NRMs) have shown great success in information retrieval (IR). But their predictions can easily be manipulated using adversarial examples, which are crafted by adding imperceptible perturbations to legitimate documents. This vulnerability raises significant concerns about their reliability and hinders the widespread deployment of NRMs. By incorporating adversarial examples into training data, adversarial training has become the de facto defense approach to adversarial attacks against NRMs. However, this defense mechanism is subject to a trade-off between effectiveness and adversarial robustness. In this study, we establish theoretical guarantees regarding the effectiveness-robustness trade-off in NRMs. We decompose the robust ranking error into two components, i.e., a natural ranking error for effectiveness evaluation and a boundary ranking error for assessing adversarial robustness. Then, we define the perturbation invariance of a ranking model and prove it to be a differentiable upper bound on the boundary ranking error for attainable computation. Informed by our theoretical analysis, we design a novel \emph{perturbation-invariant adversarial training} (PIAT) method for ranking models to achieve a better effectiveness-robustness trade-off. We design a regularized surrogate loss, in which one term encourages the effectiveness to be maximized while the regularization term encourages the output to be smooth, so as to improve adversarial robustness. Experimental results on several ranking models demonstrate the superiority of PITA compared to existing adversarial defenses.
中摘: 神经排名模型（NRMs）在信息检索（IR）领域取得了巨大成功。但是，它们的预测很容易受到对抗性示例的操纵，这些示例通过向合法文档添加难以察觉的扰动来制造。这一脆弱性引起了对其可靠性的重大担忧，并阻碍了NRMs的大规模部署。通过将对抗性示例纳入训练数据，对抗性训练已成为防御针对NRMs的对抗性攻击的事实标准方法。然而，这种防御机制在有效性与对抗性鲁棒性之间存在权衡。在这项研究中，我们建立了关于NRMs中有效性-鲁棒性权衡的理论保证。我们将鲁棒排名错误分解为两个组成部分，即用于评估有效性的自然排名错误和用于评估对抗性鲁棒性的边界排名错误。然后，我们定义了排名模型的扰动不变性，并证明它是在可计算条件下对边界排名错误的可微上界。基于我们的理论分析，我们设计了一种新颖的“扰动不变性对抗性训练”（PIAT）方法，用于排名模型以实现更好的有效性-鲁棒性权衡。我们设计了一个正则化的替代损失函数，其中一项鼓励最大化有效性，而正则化项鼓励输出平滑，以提高对抗性鲁棒性。在几种排名模型上的实验结果表明，PITA相比现有的对抗性防御方法具有优越性。

[No.412] Eliciting Kemeny Rankings
来源: AAAI 2024
命中: rank
标题: 引出肯梅尼排名
类别: machine learning ii
作者: Anne-Marie George;Christos Dimitrakakis;
机构: University of Oslo;University of Neuchatel;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/29105
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.11663
英摘: We formulate the problem of eliciting agents' preferences with the goal of finding a Kemeny ranking as a Dueling Bandits problem. Here the bandits' arms correspond to alternatives that need to be ranked and the feedback corresponds to a pairwise comparison between alternatives by a randomly sampled agent. We consider both sampling with and without replacement, i.e., the possibility to ask the same agent about some comparison multiple times or not.
  We find approximation bounds for Kemeny rankings dependant on confidence intervals over estimated winning probabilities of arms. Based on these we state algorithms to find Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) solutions and elaborate on their sample complexity for sampling with or without replacement. Furthermore, if all agents' preferences are strict rankings over the alternatives, we provide means to prune confidence intervals and thereby guide a more efficient elicitation. We formulate several adaptive sampling methods that use look-aheads to estimate how much confidence intervals (and thus approximation guarantees) might be tightened. All described methods are compared on synthetic data.
中摘: 我们将诱导代理人偏好以寻找肯米尼排序的问题公式化为决斗强盗问题。在这里，强盗的手臂对应于需要排序的选项，而反馈则对应于随机抽取的代理人对选项进行的两两比较。我们考虑了有放回和无放回的抽样方式，即是否可能多次向同一个代理人询问某个比较，或者不行。

我们发现肯米尼排序的近似界取决于对手臂估计获胜概率的置信区间。基于这些，我们陈述了找到可能近似正确（PAC）解的算法，并详细讨论了在有或无放回抽样情况下它们的样本复杂度。此外，如果所有代理人的偏好都是对选项的严格排序，我们提供了修剪置信区间的方法，从而指导更有效的诱导过程。我们提出了几种自适应抽样方法，使用前瞻估计置信区间（以及因此的近似保证）可能会被收紧的程度。所有描述的方法都在合成数据上进行了比较。

[No.413] BiPFT: Binary Pre-trained Foundation Transformer with Low-Rank Estimation of Binarization Residual Polynomials
来源: AAAI 2024
命中: rank
标题: BiPFT：二进制预训练基础变换器与二值化残差多项式的低秩估计
类别: machine learning v
作者: Xingrun Xing;Li Du;Xinyuan Wang;Xianlin Zeng;Yequan Wang;Zheng Zhang;Jiajun Zhang;
机构: Chinese Academy of Sciences;Beijing Academy of Artificial Intelligence;Beihang University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/29542
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.08937
英摘: Pretrained foundation models offer substantial benefits for a wide range of downstream tasks, which can be one of the most potential techniques to access artificial general intelligence. However, scaling up foundation transformers for maximal task-agnostic knowledge has brought about computational challenges, especially on resource-limited devices such as mobiles. This work proposes the first Binary Pretrained Foundation Transformer (BiPFT) for natural language understanding (NLU) tasks, which remarkably saves 56 times operations and 28 times memory. In contrast to previous task-specific binary transformers, BiPFT exhibits a substantial enhancement in the learning capabilities of binary neural networks (BNNs), promoting BNNs into the era of pre-training. Benefiting from extensive pretraining data, we further propose a data-driven binarization method. Specifically, we first analyze the binarization error in self-attention operations and derive the polynomials of binarization error. To simulate full-precision self-attention, we define binarization error as binarization residual polynomials, and then introduce low-rank estimators to model these polynomials. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of BiPFTs, surpassing task-specific baseline by 15.4% average performance on the GLUE benchmark. BiPFT also demonstrates improved robustness to hyperparameter changes, improved optimization efficiency, and reduced reliance on downstream distillation, which consequently generalize on various NLU tasks and simplify the downstream pipeline of BNNs. Our code and pretrained models are publicly available at https://github.com/Xingrun-Xing/BiPFT.
中摘: 预训练的基础模型为广泛下游任务提供了显著优势，这可能是通向通用人工智能最具潜力的技术之一。然而，为了获取最大化的任务无关知识而扩大基础Transformer模型规模带来了计算挑战，尤其是在资源受限设备如手机上。本研究提出了首个二值预训练基础Transformer（BiPFT），用于自然语言理解（NLU）任务，其显著减少了56倍的操作和28倍的内存消耗。与以往的任务特定二值Transformer相比，BiPFT在二值神经网络（BNNs）的学习能力上展现出重大提升，推动BNNs进入预训练时代。得益于大规模预训练数据，我们进一步提出了一种数据驱动的二值化方法。具体而言，我们首先分析了自注意力操作中的二值化误差，并推导出二值化误差的多项式表达。为了模拟全精度自注意力，我们将二值化误差定义为二值化残差多项式，然后引入低秩估计器来建模这些多项式。大量实验验证了BiPFT的有效性，在GLUE基准测试中平均性能超过任务特定基线15.4%。BiPFT还展示了对超参数变化更强的鲁棒性、优化效率的提升以及对下游蒸馏技术依赖性的减少，从而在各种NLU任务上实现了泛化并简化了BNNs的下游流程。我们的代码和预训练模型可在https://github.com/Xingrun-Xing/BiPFT公开获取。

[No.416] LAMPAT: Low-Rank Adaption for Multilingual Paraphrasing Using Adversarial Training
来源: AAAI 2024
命中: rank
标题: LAMPAT：低秩适应用于多语言改写对抗训练
类别: natural language processing i
作者: Khoi M. Le;Trinh Pham;Tho Quan;Anh Tuan Luu;
机构: VinAI Research;Ho Chi Minh City University of Technology;Nanyang Technological University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/29804
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.04348
英摘: Paraphrases are texts that convey the same meaning while using different words or sentence structures. It can be used as an automatic data augmentation tool for many Natural Language Processing tasks, especially when dealing with low-resource languages, where data shortage is a significant problem. To generate a paraphrase in multilingual settings, previous studies have leveraged the knowledge from the machine translation field, i.e., forming a paraphrase through zero-shot machine translation in the same language. Despite good performance on human evaluation, those methods still require parallel translation datasets, thus making them inapplicable to languages that do not have parallel corpora. To mitigate that problem, we proposed the first unsupervised multilingual paraphrasing model, LAMPAT ($\textbf{L}$ow-rank $\textbf{A}$daptation for $\textbf{M}$ultilingual $\textbf{P}$araphrasing using $\textbf{A}$dversarial $\textbf{T}$raining), by which monolingual dataset is sufficient enough to generate a human-like and diverse sentence. Throughout the experiments, we found out that our method not only works well for English but can generalize on unseen languages as well. Data and code are available at https://github.com/VinAIResearch/LAMPAT.
中摘:     意译是指在使用不同词汇或句子结构的同时传达相同意义的文本。它可以用作许多自然语言处理任务的自动数据增强工具，特别是在处理低资源语言时，数据短缺是一个重大问题。为了在多语言环境下生成意译，在先前的研究中，人们借鉴了机器翻译领域的知识，即通过同一语言的零样本机器翻译形成意译。尽管在人工评估中表现出良好性能，但这些方法仍然需要并行翻译数据集，因此不适用于没有并行语料库的语言。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了第一个无监督的多语言意译模型，LAMPAT（基于对抗性训练的低秩适应用于多语言意译），仅使用单语数据集就足以生成类似人类且多样化的句子。在实验过程中，我们发现我们的方法不仅对英语表现良好，而且可以泛化到未见过的语言。数据和代码可在https://github.com/VinAIResearch/LAMPAT获取。

[No.417] Exploring Post-training Quantization in LLMs from Comprehensive Study to Low Rank Compensation
来源: AAAI 2024
命中: rank
标题: 从全面研究到低秩补偿：探索大语言模型（LLMs）的后训练量化
类别: natural language processing ii
作者: Zhewei Yao;Xiaoxia Wu;Cheng Li;Stephen Youn;Yuxiong He;
机构: Microsoft;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/29908
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2303.08302
英摘: Post-training quantization (PTQ) has emerged as a promising technique for mitigating memory consumption and computational costs in large language models (LLMs). However, a systematic examination of various quantization schemes, model families, and quantization bit precision has been absent from the literature. In this paper, we conduct a comprehensive analysis of these factors by investigating the effects of PTQ on weight-only, activation-only, and weight-and-activation quantization using diverse methods such as round-to-nearest (RTN), GPTQ, ZeroQuant, and their variants. We apply these methods to two distinct model families with parameters ranging from 125M to 176B. Our contributions include: (1) a sensitivity analysis revealing that activation quantization is generally more susceptible to weight quantization, with smaller models often outperforming larger models in terms of activation quantization; (2) an evaluation and comparison of existing PTQ methods to optimize model size reduction while minimizing the impact on accuracy, revealing that none of the current methods can achieve the original model quality for quantization with either INT4-weight or INT4-weight-and-INT8-activation; (3) based on these insights, we propose an optimized method called Low-Rank Compensation (LoRC), which employs low-rank matrices to enhance model quality recovery with a minimal increase in model size.
中摘: 后训练量化（PTQ）作为一种有前景的技术出现，用于减轻大型语言模型（LLM）中的内存消耗和计算成本。然而，系统地研究各种量化方案、模型家族以及量化比特精度在文献中一直缺失。在这篇论文中，我们通过考察PTQ对仅权重、仅激活和权重与激活量化的综合影响，对这些因素进行了全面分析，使用了多种方法，如最接近四舍五入（RTN）、GPTQ、ZeroQuant及其变体。我们将这些方法应用于参数范围从1.25亿到1760亿的两个不同模型家族。我们的贡献包括：(1)敏感性分析显示，激活量化通常比权重量化更易受影响，小模型在激活量化方面往往超过大模型；(2)评估和比较现有PTQ方法以优化模型尺寸减小的同时最小化对准确性的冲击，发现目前没有方法能在INT4权重或INT4权重与INT8激活量化时达到原始模型的质量；(3)基于这些见解，我们提出了一种称为低秩补偿（LoRC）的优化方法，该方法利用低秩矩阵来增强模型质量恢复，同时仅轻微增加模型大小。

[No.418] Preference Ranking Optimization for Human Alignment
来源: AAAI 2024
命中: rank
标题: 人力偏好排序优化对齐
类别: natural language processing ii
作者: Feifan Song;Bowen Yu;Minghao Li;Haiyang Yu;Fei Huang;Yongbin Li;Houfeng Wang;
机构: Peking University;Alibaba Group;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/29865
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.17492
英摘: Large language models (LLMs) often contain misleading content, emphasizing the need to align them with human values to ensure secure AI systems. Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has been employed to achieve this alignment. However, it encompasses two main drawbacks: (1) RLHF exhibits complexity, instability, and sensitivity to hyperparameters in contrast to SFT. (2) Despite massive trial-and-error, multiple sampling is reduced to pair-wise contrast, thus lacking contrasts from a macro perspective. In this paper, we propose Preference Ranking Optimization (PRO) as an efficient SFT algorithm to directly fine-tune LLMs for human alignment. PRO extends the pair-wise contrast to accommodate preference rankings of any length. By iteratively contrasting candidates, PRO instructs the LLM to prioritize the best response while progressively ranking the rest responses. In this manner, PRO effectively transforms human alignment into aligning the probability ranking of n responses generated by LLM with the preference ranking of humans towards these responses. Experiments have shown that PRO outperforms baseline algorithms, achieving comparable results to ChatGPT and human responses through automatic-based, reward-based, GPT-4, and human evaluations.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLM）往往包含误导性内容，强调了将它们与人类价值观对齐以确保安全AI系统的重要性。为了实现这种对齐，已采用基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）。然而，它存在两个主要缺点：(1)与SFT相比，RLHF表现出复杂性、不稳定性以及对超参数的敏感性。(2)尽管进行了大量的试错，多重采样被简化为两两对比，因此缺乏宏观层面的对比。在本文中，我们提出偏好排序优化（PRO）作为有效的SFT算法，直接微调LLM以实现与人类的对齐。PRO将两两对比扩展为适应任何长度的偏好排序。通过迭代对比候选方案，PRO指导LLM优先考虑最佳响应，同时逐步对其他响应进行排序。这样，PRO有效地将人类对齐转化为将LLM生成的n个响应的概率排名与人类对这些响应的偏好排名对齐。实验表明，PRO超越了基线算法，在自动评估、奖励评估、GPT-4和人类评估中，其表现可与ChatGPT和人类响应相媲美。

[No.419] Probabilistic Offline Policy Ranking with Approximate Bayesian Computation
来源: AAAI 2024
命中: rank
标题: 基于近似贝叶斯计算的离线策略概率排名
类别: reasoning under uncertainty
作者: Longchao Da;Porter Jenkins;Trevor Schwantes;Jeffrey Dotson;Hua Wei;
机构: Arizona State University;Brigham Young University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/30019
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.11551
英摘: In practice, it is essential to compare and rank candidate policies offline before real-world deployment for safety and reliability. Prior work seeks to solve this offline policy ranking (OPR) problem through value-based methods, such as Off-policy evaluation (OPE). However, they fail to analyze special cases performance (e.g., worst or best cases), due to the lack of holistic characterization of policies performance. It is even more difficult to estimate precise policy values when the reward is not fully accessible under sparse settings. In this paper, we present Probabilistic Offline Policy Ranking (POPR), a framework to address OPR problems by leveraging expert data to characterize the probability of a candidate policy behaving like experts, and approximating its entire performance posterior distribution to help with ranking. POPR does not rely on value estimation, and the derived performance posterior can be used to distinguish candidates in worst, best, and average-cases. To estimate the posterior, we propose POPR-EABC, an Energy-based Approximate Bayesian Computation (ABC) method conducting likelihood-free inference. POPR-EABC reduces the heuristic nature of ABC by a smooth energy function, and improves the sampling efficiency by a pseudo-likelihood. We empirically demonstrate that POPR-EABC is adequate for evaluating policies in both discrete and continuous action spaces across various experiment environments, and facilitates probabilistic comparisons of candidate policies before deployment.
中摘: 在实际操作中，为了确保安全性和可靠性，在真实世界部署前，对候选策略进行离线比较和排名至关重要。先前的研究试图通过基于价值的方法来解决这种离线策略排名（OPR）问题，例如离策略评估（OPE）。然而，它们未能分析特殊案例的表现（如最坏或最佳情况），这是由于缺乏对策略性能的整体描述。在奖励信号稀疏的情况下，精确估计策略价值变得更加困难。在这篇论文中，我们提出了概率离线策略排名（POPR），这是一个框架，通过利用专家数据来表征候选策略表现得像专家的概率，并近似其整个性能后验分布以帮助排名，以此来解决OPR问题。POPR不依赖于价值估计，所导出的性能后验可以用于区分最坏、最佳和平均情况下的候选策略。为了估计后验，我们提出了POPR-EABC，这是一种基于能量的近似贝叶斯计算（ABC）方法，执行无需似然的推理。POPR-EABC通过平滑的能量函数减少了ABC的启发式性质，并通过伪似然提高了采样效率。我们在各种实验环境中实证展示了POPR-EABC适用于离散和连续动作空间中的策略评估，并有助于在部署前对候选策略进行概率比较。

[No.420] The Expected Loss of Preconditioned Langevin Dynamics Reveals the Hessian Rank
来源: AAAI 2024
命中: rank
标题: 预条件拉angevin动力学的期望损失揭示了海森矩阵的秩
类别: reasoning under uncertainty
作者: Amitay Bar;Rotem Mulayoff;Tomer Michaeli;Ronen Talmon;
机构: Technion – Israel Institute of Technology;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/30014
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.13810
英摘: Langevin dynamics (LD) is widely used for sampling from distributions and for optimization. In this work, we derive a closed-form expression for the expected loss of preconditioned LD near stationary points of the objective function. We use the fact that at the vicinity of such points, LD reduces to an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process, which is amenable to convenient mathematical treatment. Our analysis reveals that when the preconditioning matrix satisfies a particular relation with respect to the noise covariance, LD's expected loss becomes proportional to the rank of the objective's Hessian. We illustrate the applicability of this result in the context of neural networks, where the Hessian rank has been shown to capture the complexity of the predictor function but is usually computationally hard to probe. Finally, we use our analysis to compare SGD-like and Adam-like preconditioners and identify the regimes under which each of them leads to a lower expected loss.
中摘: 朗之万动力学（LD）在从分布中采样和优化中被广泛应用。在这项工作中，我们推导出了目标函数接近稳定点时预调节LD预期损失的闭式表达式。我们利用了在这些点附近，LD退化为奥恩斯坦-乌伦贝克过程的事实，这便于进行数学处理。我们的分析揭示，当预调节矩阵相对于噪声协方差满足特定关系时，LD的预期损失与目标函数Hessian矩阵的秩成正比。我们在神经网络的背景下展示了这一结果的应用性，在这个背景下，Hessian矩阵的秩已经被证明能够捕捉预测函数的复杂性，但通常计算上难以探究。最后，我们利用我们的分析比较了类似SGD和类似Adam的预调节器，并确定了每种情况下导致较低预期损失的条件。

  

以上文本的翻译如下：

朗之万动力学（LD）在从概率分布中抽样以及优化问题中得到了广泛应用。在此工作中，我们推导出了一种封闭形式的表达式，用于描述目标函数接近稳定点时预调节LD的预期损失。我们利用了一个事实：在这些点的邻域内，LD可以简化为一个奥恩斯坦-乌伦贝克过程，这种过程便于数学上的处理。通过分析我们发现，当预调节矩阵与噪声协方差满足某种特定关系时，LD的预期损失将与目标函数海森矩阵的秩成比例。我们以神经网络为例，说明了该结果的应用价值，在神经网络中，海森矩阵的秩被认为能反映预测函数的复杂程度，但通常难以计算。最后，我们运用分析结果比较了类似SGD和类似Adam的预调节方法，并识别出每种方法在何种条件下会导致更低的预期损失。

[No.422] RankDNN: Learning to Rank for Few-Shot Learning
来源: AAAI 2023
命中: rank
标题: RankDNN：少样本学习中的排序学习
类别: computer vision i
作者: Qianyu Guo;Gong Haotong;Xujun Wei;Yanwei Fu;Yizhou Yu;Wenqiang Zhang;Weifeng Ge;
机构: Fudan University;Shanghai Key Laboratory of Intelligent Information Processing;University of Hong Kong;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2211.15320
英摘: This paper introduces a new few-shot learning pipeline that casts relevance ranking for image retrieval as binary ranking relation classification. In comparison to image classification, ranking relation classification is sample efficient and domain agnostic. Besides, it provides a new perspective on few-shot learning and is complementary to state-of-the-art methods. The core component of our deep neural network is a simple MLP, which takes as input an image triplet encoded as the difference between two vector-Kronecker products, and outputs a binary relevance ranking order. The proposed RankMLP can be built on top of any state-of-the-art feature extractors, and our entire deep neural network is called the ranking deep neural network, or RankDNN. Meanwhile, RankDNN can be flexibly fused with other post-processing methods. During the meta test, RankDNN ranks support images according to their similarity with the query samples, and each query sample is assigned the class label of its nearest neighbor. Experiments demonstrate that RankDNN can effectively improve the performance of its baselines based on a variety of backbones and it outperforms previous state-of-the-art algorithms on multiple few-shot learning benchmarks, including miniImageNet, tieredImageNet, Caltech-UCSD Birds, and CIFAR-FS. Furthermore, experiments on the cross-domain challenge demonstrate the superior transferability of RankDNN.The code is available at: https://github.com/guoqianyu-alberta/RankDNN.
中摘: 本文介绍了一种新的小样本学习流程，将图像检索的相关性排序视为二元排序关系分类。与图像分类相比，排序关系分类具有样本效率高和领域无关的特点。此外，它为小样本学习提供了新的视角，并且与现有技术方法相辅相成。我们深度神经网络的核心组件是一个简单的多层感知器（MLP），它以图像三元组作为输入，这些三元组编码为两个向量克罗内克积之差，并输出一个二元相关性排序顺序。所提出的RankMLP可以基于任何最先进的特征提取器构建，而我们的整个深度神经网络被称为排序深度神经网络，或RankDNN。同时，RankDNN可以灵活地与其他后处理方法融合。在元测试期间，RankDNN根据支持图像与查询样本之间的相似性对它们进行排序，每个查询样本被分配其最近邻的类别标签。实验表明，RankDNN能够有效地提高基于各种骨干网络的基线性能，并在多个小样本学习基准上超越了以前的最先进算法，包括miniImageNet、tieredImageNet、Caltech-UCSD Birds和CIFAR-FS。此外，在跨域挑战上的实验展示了RankDNN的优越迁移能力。代码可在以下链接获取：https://github.com/guoqianyu-alberta/RankDNN。

[No.424] Domain Decorrelation with Potential Energy Ranking
来源: AAAI 2023
命中: rank
标题: 势能排名领域的领域脱相关性
类别: computer vision ii
作者: Sen Pei;Jiaxi Sun;Richard Yi Da Xu;Shiming Xiang;Gaofeng Meng;
机构: Chinese Academy of Sciences;University of Chinese Academy of Sciences;Hong Kong Baptist University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/25294
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2207.12194
英摘: Machine learning systems, especially the methods based on deep learning, enjoy great success in modern computer vision tasks under experimental settings. Generally, these classic deep learning methods are built on the \emph{i.i.d.} assumption, supposing the training and test data are drawn from a similar distribution independently and identically. However, the aforementioned \emph{i.i.d.} assumption is in general unavailable in the real-world scenario, and as a result, leads to sharp performance decay of deep learning algorithms. Behind this, domain shift is one of the primary factors to be blamed. In order to tackle this problem, we propose using \textbf{Po}tential \textbf{E}nergy \textbf{R}anking (PoER) to decouple the object feature and the domain feature (\emph{i.e.,} appearance feature) in given images, promoting the learning of label-discriminative features while filtering out the irrelevant correlations between the objects and the background. PoER helps the neural networks to capture label-related features which contain the domain information first in shallow layers and then distills the label-discriminative representations out progressively, enforcing the neural networks to be aware of the characteristic of objects and background which is vital to the generation of domain-invariant features. PoER reports superior performance on domain generalization benchmarks, improving the average top-1 accuracy by at least 1.20\% compared to the existing methods. Moreover, we use PoER in the ECCV 2022 NICO Challenge\footnote{https://nicochallenge.com}, achieving top place with only a vanilla ResNet-18. The code has been made available at https://github.com/ForeverPs/PoER.
中摘: 机器学习系统，尤其是基于深度学习的方法，在实验设定下的现代计算机视觉任务中取得了巨大成功。通常，这些经典的深度学习方法建立在独立同分布（\emph{i.i.d.}）假设上，即假设训练数据和测试数据是从相似分布中独立且相同地抽取的。然而，在现实世界的情境下，上述的\emph{i.i.d.}假设往往不成立，从而导致深度学习算法性能急剧下降。造成这一现象的主要因素之一是领域偏移。为了解决这个问题，我们提出使用\textbf{Po}tential \textbf{E}nergy \textbf{R}anking（PoER）来分离给定图像中的对象特征和领域特征（即外观特征），促进标签判别特征的学习，同时过滤掉对象与背景之间的无关联系。PoER帮助神经网络首先在浅层捕捉包含领域信息的标签相关特征，然后逐步提炼出标签判别的表示，强制神经网络意识到对象和背景的特性，这对生成领域不变特征至关重要。PoER在领域泛化基准上的表现优异，与现有方法相比，平均top-1准确率至少提高了1.20\%。此外，我们在ECCV 2022 NICO挑战赛\footnote{https://nicochallenge.com}中使用了PoER，仅凭基础的ResNet-18就获得了最高排名。代码已在https://github.com/ForeverPs/PoER公开。

英摘: With the development of the online education system, personalized education recommendation has played an essential role. In this paper, we focus on developing path recommendation systems that aim to generating and recommending an entire learning path to the given user in each session. Noticing that existing approaches fail to consider the correlations of concepts in the path, we propose a novel framework named Set-to-Sequence Ranking-based Concept-aware Learning Path Recommendation (SRC), which formulates the recommendation task under a set-to-sequence paradigm. Specifically, we first design a concept-aware encoder module which can capture the correlations among the input learning concepts. The outputs are then fed into a decoder module that sequentially generates a path through an attention mechanism that handles correlations between the learning and target concepts. Our recommendation policy is optimized by policy gradient. In addition, we also introduce an auxiliary module based on knowledge tracing to enhance the model's stability by evaluating students' learning effects on learning concepts. We conduct extensive experiments on two real-world public datasets and one industrial dataset, and the experimental results demonstrate the superiority and effectiveness of SRC. Code will be available at https://gitee.com/mindspore/models/tree/master/research/recommend/SRC.
中摘: 随着在线教育系统的发展，个性化教育推荐扮演了至关重要的角色。本文专注于开发旨在为每个会话中的给定用户生成和推荐完整学习路径的路径推荐系统。我们注意到现有方法未能考虑路径中概念间的关联性，因此提出了一种名为基于集合到序列排名的概念感知学习路径推荐（SRC）的创新框架，该框架将推荐任务置于集合到序列的范式下。具体而言，我们首先设计了一个概念感知编码器模块，能够捕捉输入学习概念之间的关联。然后，输出被送入解码器模块，通过处理学习概念与目标概念之间关联性的注意力机制，以序列方式生成路径。我们的推荐策略通过策略梯度进行优化。此外，我们还引入了一个基于知识追踪的辅助模块，通过评估学生在学习概念上的学习效果来增强模型的稳定性。我们在两个现实世界的公开数据集和一个工业数据集上进行了广泛的实验，实验结果证明了SRC的优越性和有效性。代码将在https://gitee.com/mindspore/models/tree/master/research/recommend/SRC提供。

[No.428] Rank Aggregation Using Scoring Rules
来源: AAAI 2023
命中: rank
标题: 基于评分规则的排名聚合
类别: game theory and economic paradigms
作者: Niclas Boehmer;Robert Bredereck;Dominik Peters;
机构: Technische Universität Berlin;Technische Universität Clausthal;CNRS;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/25685
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2209.08856
英摘: To aggregate rankings into a social ranking, one can use scoring systems such as Plurality, Veto, and Borda. We distinguish three types of methods: ranking by score, ranking by repeatedly choosing a winner that we delete and rank at the top, and ranking by repeatedly choosing a loser that we delete and rank at the bottom. The latter method captures the frequently studied voting rules Single Transferable Vote (aka Instant Runoff Voting), Coombs, and Baldwin. In an experimental analysis, we show that the three types of methods produce different rankings in practice. We also provide evidence that sequentially selecting winners is most suitable to detect the "true" ranking of candidates. For different rules in our classes, we then study the (parameterized) computational complexity of deciding in which positions a given candidate can appear in the chosen ranking. As part of our analysis, we also consider the Winner Determination problem for STV, Coombs, and Baldwin and determine their complexity when there are few voters or candidates.
中摘: 为了将排名汇总成社会排名，可以使用诸如多数制（Plurality）、否决制（Veto）和博尔达计分法（Borda）等评分系统。我们区分了三种类型的方法：通过得分进行排名、通过反复选择并删除胜者然后将其排在顶部、以及通过反复选择并删除败者然后将其排在底部进行排名。后一种方法涵盖了经常研究的投票规则，如单一可转移投票（即即时复选投票，Single Transferable Vote）、库姆斯（Coombs）和鲍德温（Baldwin）。在实验分析中，我们表明这三种类型的方法在实践中会产生不同的排名。我们还提供了证据证明，依次选择胜者最适于检测候选人的“真实”排名。对于我们类别中的不同规则，我们随后研究了决定给定候选人在选定排名中可能出现的位置的（参数化）计算复杂性。作为我们分析的一部分，我们也考虑了STV、库姆斯和鲍德温的胜者确定问题，并确定了当有少数选民或候选人时它们的复杂性。

[No.429] Min-Max Submodular Ranking for Multiple Agents
来源: AAAI 2023
命中: rank
标题: 多智能体的最小-最大子模态排名
类别: machine learning i
作者: Qingyun Chen;Sungjin Im;Benjamin Moseley;Chenyang Xu;Ruilong Zhang;
机构: University of California, Merced;Carnegie Mellon University;East China Normal University;Zhejiang University;City University of Hong Kong;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/25862
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2212.07682
英摘: In the submodular ranking (SR) problem, the input consists of a set of submodular functions defined on a ground set of elements. The goal is to order elements for all the functions to have value above a certain threshold as soon on average as possible, assuming we choose one element per time. The problem is flexible enough to capture various applications in machine learning, including decision trees.
  This paper considers the min-max version of SR where multiple instances share the ground set. With the view of each instance being associated with an agent, the min-max problem is to order the common elements to minimize the maximum objective of all agents -- thus, finding a fair solution for all agents. We give approximation algorithms for this problem and demonstrate their effectiveness in the application of finding a decision tree for multiple agents.
中摘: 在子模函数排序（Submodular Ranking，简称SR）问题中，输入包括一组定义在元素基集上的子模函数。目标是在所有函数的价值达到某一阈值以上的情况下，尽可能早地平均排序元素，假设我们每次选择一个元素。这个问题足够灵活，能够涵盖机器学习中的多种应用，包括决策树。

本文考虑的是SR问题的最小最大值版本，其中多个实例共享相同的基集。将每个实例视为与一个代理相关联，最小最大值问题的目标是排列这些共同的元素，以最小化所有代理的最大目标值——从而为所有代理找到一个公平的解决方案。我们为此问题提供了近似算法，并在为多个代理寻找决策树的应用中展示了它们的有效性。

[No.430] Robust and Fast Measure of Information via Low-Rank Representation
来源: AAAI 2023
命中: rank
标题: 基于低秩表示的稳健快速信息度量
类别: machine learning i
作者: Yuxin Dong;Tieliang Gong;Shujian Yu;Hong Chen;Chen Li;
机构: Xi'an Jiao Tong University;Vrije Universiteit Amsterdam;Huazhong Agricultural University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/25906
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2211.16784
英摘: The matrix-based Rényi's entropy allows us to directly quantify information measures from given data, without explicit estimation of the underlying probability distribution. This intriguing property makes it widely applied in statistical inference and machine learning tasks. However, this information theoretical quantity is not robust against noise in the data, and is computationally prohibitive in large-scale applications. To address these issues, we propose a novel measure of information, termed low-rank matrix-based Rényi's entropy, based on low-rank representations of infinitely divisible kernel matrices. The proposed entropy functional inherits the specialty of of the original definition to directly quantify information from data, but enjoys additional advantages including robustness and effective calculation. Specifically, our low-rank variant is more sensitive to informative perturbations induced by changes in underlying distributions, while being insensitive to uninformative ones caused by noises. Moreover, low-rank Rényi's entropy can be efficiently approximated by random projection and Lanczos iteration techniques, reducing the overall complexity from $\mathcal{O}(n^3)$ to $\mathcal{O}(n^2 s)$ or even $\mathcal{O}(ns^2)$, where $n$ is the number of data samples and $s \ll n$. We conduct large-scale experiments to evaluate the effectiveness of this new information measure, demonstrating superior results compared to matrix-based Rényi's entropy in terms of both performance and computational efficiency.
中摘: 基于矩阵的Rényi熵使我们能够直接从给定数据量化信息度量，无需显式估计潜在的概率分布。这一引人注目的特性使其在统计推断和机器学习任务中得到广泛应用。然而，这种信息理论量对数据中的噪声不具鲁棒性，并且在大规模应用中计算上难以承受。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了一种新的信息度量，称为低秩矩阵基Rényi熵，基于无穷可分核矩阵的低秩表示。所提出的熵函数继承了原始定义的特性，可以直接从数据量化信息，但还具有额外的优点，包括鲁棒性和有效计算。具体而言，我们的低秩变体对由潜在分布变化引起的有信息性的扰动更加敏感，而对由噪声引起的无信息性扰动不敏感。此外，通过随机投影和Lanczos迭代技术，低秩Rényi熵可以被高效地近似，将总体复杂度从$\mathcal{O}(n^3)$降低到$\mathcal{O}(n^2 s)$甚至$\mathcal{O}(ns^2)$，其中$n$是数据样本的数量，而$s \ll n$。我们进行了大规模实验来评估这个新信息度量的有效性，与基于矩阵的Rényi熵相比，在性能和计算效率方面都表现出优越的结果。

[No.436] HALOC: Hardware-Aware Automatic Low-Rank Compression for Compact Neural Networks
来源: AAAI 2023
命中: rank
标题: HALOC：硬件感知自动低秩压缩以实现紧凑型神经网络
类别: machine learning iv
作者: Jinqi Xiao;Chengming Zhang;Yu Gong;Miao Yin;Yang Sui;Lizhi Xiang;Dingwen Tao;Bo Yuan;
机构: Rutgers University;Indiana University;Washington State University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/26244
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2301.09422
英摘: Low-rank compression is an important model compression strategy for obtaining compact neural network models. In general, because the rank values directly determine the model complexity and model accuracy, proper selection of layer-wise rank is very critical and desired. To date, though many low-rank compression approaches, either selecting the ranks in a manual or automatic way, have been proposed, they suffer from costly manual trials or unsatisfied compression performance. In addition, all of the existing works are not designed in a hardware-aware way, limiting the practical performance of the compressed models on real-world hardware platforms.
  To address these challenges, in this paper we propose HALOC, a hardware-aware automatic low-rank compression framework. By interpreting automatic rank selection from an architecture search perspective, we develop an end-to-end solution to determine the suitable layer-wise ranks in a differentiable and hardware-aware way. We further propose design principles and mitigation strategy to efficiently explore the rank space and reduce the potential interference problem.
  Experimental results on different datasets and hardware platforms demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed approach. On CIFAR-10 dataset, HALOC enables 0.07% and 0.38% accuracy increase over the uncompressed ResNet-20 and VGG-16 models with 72.20% and 86.44% fewer FLOPs, respectively. On ImageNet dataset, HALOC achieves 0.9% higher top-1 accuracy than the original ResNet-18 model with 66.16% fewer FLOPs. HALOC also shows 0.66% higher top-1 accuracy increase than the state-of-the-art automatic low-rank compression solution with fewer computational and memory costs. In addition, HALOC demonstrates the practical speedups on different hardware platforms, verified by the measurement results on desktop GPU, embedded GPU and ASIC accelerator.
中摘: 低秩压缩是获取紧凑神经网络模型的重要模型压缩策略。通常，因为秩值直接决定了模型复杂度和模型精度，因此合理选择逐层的秩是非常关键和理想的。然而，迄今为止，尽管已经提出了许多低秩压缩方法，无论是手动还是自动选择秩，它们都面临着昂贵的手动试验或不满意的压缩性能的问题。此外，所有现有工作都没有以硬件感知的方式设计，限制了在现实世界硬件平台上压缩模型的实际性能。

为了解决这些挑战，本文提出了一种硬件感知的自动低秩压缩框架HALOC。通过从架构搜索的角度解释自动秩选择，我们开发了一个端到端解决方案，以可微分和硬件感知的方式确定合适的逐层秩。我们进一步提出了设计原则和缓解策略，以高效地探索秩空间并减少潜在的干扰问题。

在不同数据集和硬件平台上的实验结果证明了我们所提出方法的有效性。在CIFAR-10数据集上，与未压缩的ResNet-20和VGG-16模型相比，HALOC分别实现了0.07%和0.38%的准确度提升，同时分别减少了72.20%和86.44%的浮点运算数（FLOPs）。在ImageNet数据集上，HALOC的Top-1准确率比原始ResNet-18模型高0.9%，并且减少了66.16%的FLOPs。与最先进的自动低秩压缩解决方案相比，HALOC显示出更高的Top-1准确率提升，达到0.66%，同时计算和内存成本更低。此外，HALOC在不同的硬件平台上展示了实际的速度提升，这一结论得到了桌面GPU、嵌入式GPU和ASIC加速器测量结果的验证。

[No.440] Massively Parallel Single-Source SimRanks in O(log N) Rounds
来源: IJCAI 2024
命中: rank
标题: 大规模并行单源SimRanks在O(log N)轮次内
类别: Data Mining
作者: Siqiang Luo;Zulun Zhu;
机构: Nanyang Technological University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/249
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2304.04015
英摘: SimRank is one of the most fundamental measures that evaluate the structural similarity between two nodes in a graph and has been applied in a plethora of data management tasks. These tasks often involve single-source SimRank computation that evaluates the SimRank values between a source node $s$ and all other nodes. Due to its high computation complexity, single-source SimRank computation for large graphs is notoriously challenging, and hence recent studies resort to distributed processing. To our surprise, although SimRank has been widely adopted for two decades, theoretical aspects of distributed SimRanks with provable results have rarely been studied.
  In this paper, we conduct a theoretical study on single-source SimRank computation in the Massive Parallel Computation (MPC) model, which is the standard theoretical framework modeling distributed systems such as MapReduce, Hadoop, or Spark. Existing distributed SimRank algorithms enforce either $Ω(\log n)$ communication round complexity or $Ω(n)$ machine space for a graph of $n$ nodes. We overcome this barrier. Particularly, given a graph of $n$ nodes, for any query node $v$ and constant error $ε>\frac{3}{n}$, we show that using $O(\log^2 \log n)$ rounds of communication among machines is almost enough to compute single-source SimRank values with at most $ε$ absolute errors, while each machine only needs a space sub-linear to $n$. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first single-source SimRank algorithm in MPC that can overcome the $Θ(\log n)$ round complexity barrier with provable result accuracy.
中摘: SimRank是最基本的评估图中两个节点结构相似性的度量之一，并已被应用于大量的数据管理任务。这些任务通常涉及单源SimRank计算，即评估源节点$s$与其他所有节点之间的SimRank值。由于其计算复杂性高，对于大规模图的单源SimRank计算极具挑战性，因此近期的研究转向了分布式处理。令人惊讶的是，尽管SimRank在二十年间被广泛采用，但具有可证明结果的分布式SimRank的理论研究却鲜有开展。

在这篇论文中，我们对Massive Parallel Computation（MPC）模型中的单源SimRank计算进行了理论研究，MPC模型是用于模拟如MapReduce、Hadoop或Spark等分布式系统的标准理论框架。现有的分布式SimRank算法要么强制执行$Ω(\log n)$的通信轮次复杂性，要么要求每台机器有$Ω(n)$的空间，对于$n$个节点的图而言。我们突破了这一障碍。具体而言，对于一个$n$个节点的图，对于任何查询节点$v$和常数误差$ε>\frac{3}{n}$，我们表明，在机器间的通信轮次为$O(\log^2 \log n)$的情况下，几乎可以计算出单源SimRank值，其绝对误差不超过$ε$，同时每台机器只需要小于线性于$n$的空间。据我们所知，这是首个在MPC中能够克服$Θ(\log n)$轮次复杂性障碍并保证结果准确性的单源SimRank算法。

[No.442] Toward Completing the Picture of Control in Schulze and Ranked Pairs Elections
来源: IJCAI 2024
命中: rank
标题: 完成关于舒尔ZE和分级对选举控制图的拼图
类别: Game Theory and Economic Paradigms
作者: Cynthia Maushagen;David Niclaus;Paul Nüsken;Jörg Rothe;Tessa Seeger;
机构: Heinrich-Heine-Universität Düsseldorf;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/326
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.08956
英摘: Both Schulze and ranked pairs are voting rules that satisfy many natural, desirable axioms. Many standard types of electoral control (with a chair seeking to change the outcome of an election by interfering with the election structure) have already been studied. However, for control by replacing candidates or voters and for (exact) multimode control that combines multiple standard attacks, many questions remain open. We solve a number of these open cases for Schulze and ranked pairs. In addition, we fix a flaw in the reduction of Menton and Singh [IJCAI 2013] showing that Schulze is resistant to constructive control by deleting candidates and re-establish a vulnerability result for destructive control by deleting candidates. In some of our proofs, we study variants of s-t vertex cuts in graphs that are related to our control problems.
中摘: 舒尔茨法和排序对法都是满足许多自然且期望的公理的投票规则。许多标准类型的选择控制（主席试图通过干预选举结构来改变选举结果）已经被研究过。然而，对于替换候选人或选民的控制，以及将多种标准攻击结合在一起的确切多模式控制，仍有许多问题尚未解决。我们为舒尔茨法和排序对法解决了许多这些未解的情况。此外，我们修正了Menton和Singh在2013年IJCAI会议上提出的证明中的一个缺陷，该证明表明舒尔茨法能够抵御通过删除候选人进行的建设性控制，并重新确立了通过删除候选人进行破坏性控制的脆弱性结果。在我们的部分证明中，我们研究了与我们的控制问题相关的图中的s-t顶点割的变体。

[No.446] Rank and Align: Towards Effective Source-free Graph Domain Adaptation
来源: IJCAI 2024
命中: rank
标题: 排名与对齐：迈向有效的源无监督图域适应
类别: Machine Learning
作者: Junyu Luo;Zhiping Xiao;Yifan Wang;Xiao Luo;Jingyang Yuan;Wei Ju;Langechuan Liu;Ming Zhang;
机构: Peking University;University of California, Los Angeles;University of International Business and Economics;Anker Innovations;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/520
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.12185
英摘: Graph neural networks (GNNs) have achieved impressive performance in graph domain adaptation. However, extensive source graphs could be unavailable in real-world scenarios due to privacy and storage concerns. To this end, we investigate an underexplored yet practical problem of source-free graph domain adaptation, which transfers knowledge from source models instead of source graphs to a target domain. To solve this problem, we introduce a novel GNN-based approach called Rank and Align (RNA), which ranks graph similarities with spectral seriation for robust semantics learning, and aligns inharmonic graphs with harmonic graphs which close to the source domain for subgraph extraction. In particular, to overcome label scarcity, we employ the spectral seriation algorithm to infer the robust pairwise rankings, which can guide semantic learning using a similarity learning objective. To depict distribution shifts, we utilize spectral clustering and the silhouette coefficient to detect harmonic graphs, which the source model can easily classify. To reduce potential domain discrepancy, we extract domain-invariant subgraphs from inharmonic graphs by an adversarial edge sampling process, which guides the invariant learning of GNNs. Extensive experiments on several benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed RNA.
中摘: 图神经网络（GNN）在图域适应方面取得了令人印象深刻的表现。然而，在现实场景中，由于隐私和存储方面的担忧，大量的源图可能无法获得。为此，我们研究了一个尚未充分探索但实际存在的问题：无源图的图域适应，即将知识从源模型而非源图转移到目标域。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一种基于GNN的新方法，称为排序与对齐（RNA），它通过谱序列化对图相似性进行排序以实现稳健的语义学习，并将接近源域的谐波图与不谐波图对齐以便于子图提取。具体而言，为了克服标签稀缺的问题，我们采用谱序列化算法推断稳健的两两排序，这可以指导使用相似性学习目标的语义学习。为了描绘分布变化，我们利用谱聚类和轮廓系数来检测谐波图，这些图源模型可以轻松分类。为了减少潜在的域差异，我们通过对抗性的边采样过程从不谐波图中提取域不变的子图，从而引导GNN的不变性学习。在几个基准数据集上的大量实验表明了我们提出的RNA的有效性。

[No.449] Sampling Ex-Post Group-Fair Rankings
来源: IJCAI 2023
命中: rank
标题: 事后抽样分组公平排名
类别: AI Ethics, Trust, Fairness
作者: Sruthi Gorantla;Amit Deshpande;Anand Louis;
机构: Indian Institute of Science;Microsoft;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2023/46
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2203.00887
英摘: Randomized rankings have been of recent interest to achieve ex-ante fairer exposure and better robustness than deterministic rankings. We propose a set of natural axioms for randomized group-fair rankings and prove that there exists a unique distribution $D$ that satisfies our axioms and is supported only over ex-post group-fair rankings, i.e., rankings that satisfy given lower and upper bounds on group-wise representation in the top-$k$ ranks. Our problem formulation works even when there is implicit bias, incomplete relevance information, or only ordinal ranking is available instead of relevance scores or utility values.
  We propose two algorithms to sample a random group-fair ranking from the distribution $D$ mentioned above. Our first dynamic programming-based algorithm samples ex-post group-fair rankings uniformly at random in time $O(k^2\ell)$, where $\ell$ is the number of groups. Our second random walk-based algorithm samples ex-post group-fair rankings from a distribution $δ$-close to $D$ in total variation distance and has expected running time $O^*(k^2\ell^2)$, when there is a sufficient gap between the given upper and lower bounds on the group-wise representation. The former does exact sampling, but the latter runs significantly faster on real-world data sets for larger values of $k$. We give empirical evidence that our algorithms compare favorably against recent baselines for fairness and ranking utility on real-world data sets.
中摘: 随机排名最近引起了人们的兴趣，目的是在事前实现比确定性排名更公平的曝光度和更好的鲁棒性。我们提出了一套自然公理，用于随机群体公平排名，并证明存在一个唯一分布D，满足我们的公理，且仅支持事后群体公平排名，即，满足在前k名排名中对群体表示的给定下限和上限的排名。即使在存在隐性偏见、相关性信息不完整或只有序数排名可用而非相关性分数或效用值的情况下，我们的问题表述仍然适用。

我们提出了两种算法，从上述提到的分布D中采样随机群体公平排名。我们的第一个基于动态规划的算法可以在O(k^2l)的时间内以均匀随机的方式采样事后群体公平排名，其中l是群体的数量。我们的第二个基于随机游走的算法在总体变异性距离上从一个与D相差δ的分布中采样事后群体公平排名，当群体表示的给定上限和下限之间有足够的差距时，其预期运行时间为O*(k^2l^2)。前者进行精确采样，但后者在现实数据集上的较大k值情况下运行速度显著更快。我们提供了实证证据，证明我们的算法在公平性和排名效用方面相对于近期的基准，在真实世界的数据集上表现更优。

[No.451] Ranking-based Argumentation Semantics Applied to Logical Argumentation
来源: IJCAI 2023
命中: rank
标题: 基于排名的论证语义在逻辑论证中的应用
类别: Knowledge Representation and Reasoning
作者: Jesse Heyninck;Badran Raddaoui;Christian Straßer;
机构: Open Universiteit;T ´el´ecom SudParis;Ruhr-Universität Bochum;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2023/364
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2307.16780
英摘: In formal argumentation, a distinction can be made between extension-based semantics, where sets of arguments are either (jointly) accepted or not, and ranking-based semantics, where grades of acceptability are assigned to arguments. Another important distinction is that between abstract approaches, that abstract away from the content of arguments, and structured approaches, that specify a method of constructing argument graphs on the basis of a knowledge base. While ranking-based semantics have been extensively applied to abstract argumentation, few work has been done on ranking-based semantics for structured argumentation. In this paper, we make a systematic investigation into the behaviour of ranking-based semantics applied to existing formalisms for structured argumentation. We show that a wide class of ranking-based semantics gives rise to so-called culpability measures, and are relatively robust to specific choices in argument construction methods.
中摘: 在正式论证中，可以区分出基于扩展的语义和基于排名的语义。基于扩展的语义中，论证集要么被（共同）接受，要么不被接受；而基于排名的语义则为论证分配可接受性的等级。另一个重要的区别在于抽象方法与结构化方法之间，抽象方法从论证内容中抽象出来，而结构化方法则根据知识库指定构建论证图的方法。虽然基于排名的语义已经被广泛应用于抽象论证，但对于结构化论证的基于排名的语义的研究工作却很少。在这篇论文中，我们对将基于排名的语义应用于现有的结构化论证形式主义的行为进行了系统性的研究。我们表明，一大类基于排名的语义产生了所谓的责任度量，并且对特定选择在论证构建方法上相对稳健。

[No.452] A Two-Stage Adaptation of Large Language Models for Text Ranking
来源: ACL 2024
命中: rank
标题: 大型语言模型的两阶段适应以进行文本排名
类别: 
作者: Longhui Zhang;Yanzhao Zhang;Dingkun Long;Pengjun Xie;Meishan Zhang;Min Zhang;
机构: Harbin Institute of Technology;;
类型: Findings
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-acl.706/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.16720
英摘: Text ranking is a critical task in information retrieval. Recent advances in pre-trained language models (PLMs), especially large language models (LLMs), present new opportunities for applying them to text ranking. While supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with ranking data has been widely explored to better align PLMs with text ranking goals, previous studies have focused primarily on encoder-only and encoder-decoder PLMs. Research on leveraging decoder-only LLMs for text ranking remains scarce. An exception to this is RankLLaMA, which uses direct SFT to explore LLaMA's potential for text ranking. In this work, we propose a two-stage progressive paradigm to better adapt LLMs to text ranking. First, we conduct continual pre-training (CPT) of LLMs on a large weakly-supervised corpus. Second, we perform SFT, and propose an improved optimization strategy building upon RankLLaMA. Our experimental results on multiple benchmarks show that our approach outperforms previous methods in both in-domain and out-domain scenarios.
中摘: 文本排名是信息检索中的关键任务。近期在预训练语言模型（PLM）方面的进展，特别是大型语言模型（LLM），为将其应用于文本排名提供了新的机遇。尽管使用排名数据进行有监督微调（SFT）已经被广泛研究以更好地使PLM与文本排名目标对齐，但先前的研究主要集中在仅编码器和编码解码结构的PLM上。对于利用仅解码器结构的LLM进行文本排名的研究仍然很少。RankLLaMA是一个例外，它通过直接的SFT来探索LLaMA在文本排名上的潜力。在此工作中，我们提出了一种两阶段渐进式范例，以更好地使LLM适应文本排名。首先，我们在一个大规模弱监督语料库上对LLM进行持续预训练（CPT）。其次，我们执行SFT，并提出了基于RankLLaMA的改进优化策略。我们的实验结果在多个基准测试上表明，在领域内和领域外的情况下，我们的方法都优于之前的方法。

[No.454] DoRA: Enhancing Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning with Dynamic Rank Distribution
来源: ACL 2024
命中: rank
标题: DoRA：通过动态秩分布增强参数高效微调
类别: 
作者: Yulong Mao;Kaiyu Huang;Changhao Guan;Ganglin Bao;Fengran Mo;Jinan Xu;
机构: Beijing Key Lab of Traffic Data Analysis and Mining;Beijing Jiao Tong University;Université de Montréal;
类型: Long
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-long.626/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17357
英摘: Fine-tuning large-scale pre-trained models is inherently a resource-intensive task. While it can enhance the capabilities of the model, it also incurs substantial computational costs, posing challenges to the practical application of downstream tasks. Existing parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods such as Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) rely on a bypass framework that ignores the differential parameter budget requirements across weight matrices, which may lead to suboptimal fine-tuning outcomes. To address this issue, we introduce the Dynamic Low-Rank Adaptation (DoRA) method. DoRA decomposes high-rank LoRA layers into structured single-rank components, allowing for dynamic pruning of parameter budget based on their importance to specific tasks during training, which makes the most of the limited parameter budget. Experimental results demonstrate that DoRA can achieve competitive performance compared with LoRA and full model fine-tuning, and outperform various strong baselines with the same storage parameter budget. Our code is available at https://github.com/MIkumikumi0116/DoRA
中摘: 微调大规模预训练模型本质上是一项资源密集型任务。虽然它可以增强模型的能力，但也带来了巨大的计算成本，对下游任务的实际应用构成了挑战。现有的参数高效微调（PEFT）方法，如低秩适应（LoRA），依赖于一种绕行框架，忽略了权重矩阵之间不同的参数预算需求，这可能导致次优的微调结果。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了动态低秩适应（DoRA）方法。DoRA将高阶LoRA层分解为结构化的单秩组件，允许根据它们在训练过程中对特定任务的重要性动态修剪参数预算，从而充分利用有限的参数预算。实验结果表明，DoRA能够实现与LoRA和全模型微调相竞争的性能，并且在相同的存储参数预算下超越各种强大的基线。我们的代码可在https://github.com/MIkumikumi0116/DoRA获取。

[No.455] EcoRank: Budget-Constrained Text Re-ranking Using Large Language Models
来源: ACL 2024
命中: rank
标题: EcoRank：使用大型语言模型的预算约束文本重排序
类别: 
作者: Muhammad Rashid;Jannat Meem;Yue Dong;Vagelis Hristidis;
机构: University of California, Riverside;
类型: Findings
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-acl.773/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.10866
英摘: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved state-of-the-art performance in text re-ranking. This process includes queries and candidate passages in the prompts, utilizing pointwise, listwise, and pairwise prompting strategies. A limitation of these ranking strategies with LLMs is their cost: the process can become expensive due to API charges, which are based on the number of input and output tokens. We study how to maximize the re-ranking performance given a budget, by navigating the vast search spaces of prompt choices, LLM APIs, and budget splits. We propose a suite of budget-constrained methods to perform text re-ranking using a set of LLM APIs. Our most efficient method, called EcoRank, is a two-layered pipeline that jointly optimizes decisions regarding budget allocation across prompt strategies and LLM APIs. Our experimental results on four popular QA and passage reranking datasets show that EcoRank outperforms other budget-aware supervised and unsupervised baselines.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLM）在文本重排序方面已达到业界领先的表现。这一过程包括在提示中使用查询和候选段落，同时运用点对点、列表和配对提示策略。使用LLM进行排名策略的一个限制是成本：由于API费用基于输入和输出令牌的数量，整个流程可能会变得昂贵。我们研究了如何在给定预算下最大化重排序性能，通过探索提示选择、LLM API和预算分配的广阔搜索空间。我们提出了一套受预算约束的方法，利用一组LLM API进行文本重排序。我们最高效的方法，称为EcoRank，是一个双层管道，能够联合优化在提示策略和LLM API之间的预算分配决策。我们在四个流行的问答和段落重排序数据集上的实验结果显示，EcoRank在受预算约束的监督和非监督基线方法中表现最优。

[No.457] Functional Overlap Reranking for Neural Code Generation
来源: ACL 2024
命中: rank
标题: 神经代码生成的功能重排序
类别: 
作者: Hung Quoc To;Minh Huynh Nguyen;Nghi D. Q. Bui;
机构: FPT Software;
类型: Findings
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-acl.220/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.03366
英摘: Code Large Language Models (CodeLLMs) have ushered in a new era in code generation advancements. However, selecting the best code solutions from all possible CodeLLM outputs remains a challenge. Previous methods often overlooked the intricate functional similarities and interactions between solution clusters. We introduce SRank, a novel reranking strategy for selecting the best solutions from code generation, focusing on modeling the relationships between clusters of solutions. By quantifying the functional overlap between solution clusters, our approach provides a better ranking strategy for code solutions. Empirical results show that our method achieves remarkable results on the pass@1 score. For instance, on the Human-Eval benchmark, we achieve 69.66% in pass@1 with Codex002, 75.31% with WizardCoder, 53.99% with StarCoder, and 60.55% with CodeGen, surpassing state-of-the-art code generation reranking methods such as CodeT and Coder-Reviewer on the same CodeLLM by a significant margin (approximately 6.1% improvement on average). Even in scenarios with a limited number of sampled solutions and test cases, our approach demonstrates robustness and superiority, marking a new benchmark in code generation reranking. Our implementation can be found at https://github.com/FSoft-AI4Code/SRank-CodeRanker.
中摘: 大型语言模型（CodeLLMs）在代码生成领域开启了新的篇章。然而，从所有可能的CodeLLM输出中挑选出最优的代码解决方案仍然是一项挑战。以往的方法常常忽视了不同解决方案集群之间复杂的功能相似性和交互性。我们引入了SRank，这是一种创新的重排序策略，专注于从代码生成中选择最佳方案，着重于建模解决方案集群之间的关系。通过量化解决方案集群间的功能重叠，我们的方法提供了一种更优的代码解决方案排名策略。实验结果显示，我们的方法在pass@1得分上取得了显著的成绩。例如，在Human-Eval基准测试中，我们使用Codex002达到了69.66%的pass@1，使用WizardCoder达到75.31%，使用StarCoder达到53.99%，使用CodeGen达到60.55%，这些成绩均超过了诸如CodeT和Coder-Reviewer等现有代码生成重排序方法在相同CodeLLM上的表现，平均提高了大约6.1%。即使在采样解决方案数量有限和测试案例较少的情况下，我们的方法也展示了其稳健性和优越性，为代码生成的重排序树立了新的标杆。我们的实现可在https://github.com/FSoft-AI4Code/SRank-CodeRanker找到。

[No.458] Instruction Tuning with Retrieval-based Examples Ranking for Aspect-based Sentiment Analysis
来源: ACL 2024
命中: rank
标题: 基于检索的实例排序方面的意见情感分析指令调优

英摘: Aspect-based sentiment analysis (ABSA) identifies sentiment information related to specific aspects and provides deeper market insights to businesses and organizations. With the emergence of large language models (LMs), recent studies have proposed using fixed examples for instruction tuning to reformulate ABSA as a generation task. However, the performance is sensitive to the selection of in-context examples; several retrieval methods are based on surface similarity and are independent of the LM generative objective. This study proposes an instruction learning method with retrieval-based example ranking for ABSA tasks. For each target sample, an LM was applied as a scorer to estimate the likelihood of the output given the input and a candidate example as the prompt, and training examples were labeled as positive or negative by ranking the scores. The LM is used for both scoring and inference, improving the generation efficiency without incurring additional computational costs or training difficulties. Code and data are released at https://github.com/zgMin/IT-RER-ABSA.

[No.459] Lightweight reranking for language model generations
来源: ACL 2024
命中: rank
标题: 轻量级语言模型生成重排序
类别: 
作者: Siddhartha Jain;Xiaofei Ma;Anoop Deoras;Bing Xiang;

类型: Long
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-long.376/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2307.06857
英摘: Large Language Models (LLMs) can exhibit considerable variation in the quality of their sampled outputs. Reranking and selecting the best generation from the sampled set is a popular way of obtaining strong gains in generation quality. In this paper, we present a novel approach for reranking LLM generations. Unlike other techniques that might involve additional inferences or training a specialized reranker, our approach relies on easy to compute pairwise statistics between the generations that have minimal compute overhead. We show that our approach can be formalized as an extension of self-consistency and analyze its performance in that framework, theoretically as well as via simulations. We show strong improvements for selecting the best k generations for code generation tasks as well as robust improvements for the best generation for the tasks of autoformalization, summarization, and translation. While our approach only assumes black-box access to LLMs, we show that additional access to token probabilities can improve performance even further.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在它们生成样本的质量上可能表现出显著的差异。重新排序并从生成的样本集中挑选最佳生成结果是提高生成质量的一种流行方法。在这篇论文中，我们提出了一种对LLM生成结果进行重新排序的新方法。与可能涉及额外推理或训练专门的重新排序器的其他技术不同，我们的方法依赖于计算生成结果之间的成对统计，这些统计易于计算且具有最小的计算开销。我们证明了我们的方法可以形式化为自我一致性概念的扩展，并在此框架下对其性能进行了理论和模拟分析。对于代码生成任务，我们展示了在选择前k个最佳生成结果时取得了显著改进；对于自动形式化、摘要和翻译任务的最佳生成结果，我们也展示了稳健的改进。虽然我们的方法仅假定对LLMs有黑盒访问权限，但我们证明了额外获取令牌概率的访问权限可以进一步改善性能。

[No.461] LoRAPrune: Structured Pruning Meets Low-Rank Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning
来源: ACL 2024
命中: rank
标题: LoRAPrune：结构化剪枝与低秩参数高效微调相结合
类别: 
作者: Mingyang Zhang;Hao Chen;Chunhua Shen;Zhen Yang;Linlin Ou;Xinyi Yu;Bohan Zhuang;
机构: Zhejiang University;Zhejiang University of Technology;
类型: Findings
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-acl.178/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.18403
英摘: Large Language Models (LLMs), such as LLaMA and T5, have shown exceptional performance across various tasks through fine-tuning. Although low-rank adaption (LoRA) has emerged to cheaply fine-tune these LLMs on downstream tasks, their deployment is still hindered by the vast model scale and computational costs. Post-training model pruning offers a way to compress LLMs. However, the current pruning methods designed for LLMs are not compatible with LoRA. This is due to their utilization of unstructured pruning on LLMs, impeding the merging of LoRA weights, or their dependence on the gradients of pre-trained weights to guide pruning, which can impose significant memory overhead. To this end, we propose LoRAPrune, a new framework that delivers an accurate structured pruned model in a highly memory-efficient manner. Specifically, we first design a LoRA-guided pruning criterion, which uses the weights and gradients of LoRA, rather than the gradients of pre-trained weights for importance estimation. We subsequently integrate this criterion into an iterative pruning process, effectively removing redundant channels and heads. Extensive experimental results demonstrate the superior performance of our LoRAPrune over existing approaches on the LLaMA series models. At a 50\% compression rate, LoRAPrune demonstrates superior performance over LLM-Pruner, achieving a reduction in perplexity by 4.81 on WikiText2 and 3.46 on PTB, while also decreasing memory usage by 52.6%. Besides, LoRAPrune also matches semi-structural pruning across multiple LLMs, proving its wide applicability. The code is available at https://github.com/aim-uofa/LoRAPrune.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLM），如LLaMA和T5，通过微调在各种任务上展现出卓越的表现。尽管低秩适应（LoRA）作为一种低成本的方法出现，用于在下游任务上对这些LLM进行微调，但其部署仍受到庞大模型规模和计算成本的阻碍。后训练模型剪枝提供了一种压缩LLM的方式。然而，当前为LLM设计的剪枝方法与LoRA不兼容。这是由于它们在LLM上使用了非结构化剪枝，妨碍了LoRA权重的合并，或者依赖于预训练权重的梯度来指导剪枝，这可能带来显著的内存开销。为此，我们提出了一种名为LoRAPrune的新框架，以高度内存高效的方式提供了准确的结构化剪枝模型。具体而言，我们首先设计了一个由LoRA引导的剪枝准则，该准则利用LoRA的权重和梯度，而不是预训练权重的梯度来进行重要性估计。随后，我们将这个准则整合到一个迭代剪枝过程中，有效地移除了冗余的通道和头部。广泛的实验结果证明，在LLaMA系列模型上，我们的LoRAPrune相较于现有方法具有优越性能。在50%的压缩率下，LoRAPrune在WikiText2上的困惑度降低了4.81，在PTB上降低了3.46，同时将内存使用减少了52.6%，表现优于LLM-Pruner。此外，LoRAPrune在多个LLM上也匹配了半结构化剪枝，证明了其广泛的适用性。代码可在https://github.com/aim-uofa/LoRAPrune获取。

[No.462] MELoRA: Mini-Ensemble Low-Rank Adapters for Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning
来源: ACL 2024
命中: rank
标题: MELoRA：迷你集成低秩适配器参数高效微调
类别: 
作者: Pengjie Ren;Chengshun Shi;Shiguang Wu;Mengqi Zhang;Zhaochun Ren;Maarten de Rijke;Zhumin Chen;Jiahuan Pei;
机构: Shandong University;Leiden University;University of Amsterdam;Centrum Wiskunde & Informatica;
类型: Long
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-long.168/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.17263
英摘: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) is a popular method for tailoring pre-trained large language models (LLMs), especially as the models' scale and the diversity of tasks increase. Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is based on the idea that the adaptation process is intrinsically low-dimensional, i.e., significant model changes can be represented with relatively few parameters. However, decreasing the rank encounters challenges with generalization errors for specific tasks when compared to full-parameter fine-tuning. We present MELoRA, a mini-ensemble low-rank adapters that uses fewer trainable parameters while maintaining a higher rank, thereby offering improved performance potential. The core idea is to freeze original pretrained weights and train a group of mini LoRAs with only a small number of parameters. This can capture a significant degree of diversity among mini LoRAs, thus promoting better generalization ability. We conduct a theoretical analysis and empirical studies on various NLP tasks. Our experimental results show that, compared to LoRA, MELoRA achieves better performance with 8 times fewer trainable parameters on natural language understanding tasks and 36 times fewer trainable parameters on instruction following tasks, which demonstrates the effectiveness of MELoRA.
中摘: 参数高效微调（PEFT）是一种流行的方法，用于定制预训练的大规模语言模型（LLM），尤其是在模型规模和任务多样性增加的情况下。低秩适应（LoRA）基于这样一个理念：适应过程本质上是低维的，即，显著的模型变化可以用相对较少的参数表示。然而，降低秩在与全参数微调相比时，在特定任务上会遇到泛化误差的挑战。我们提出了MELoRA，一种小型集合低秩适配器，它使用更少的可训练参数，同时保持较高的秩，从而提供更好的性能潜力。核心思想是冻结原始预训练权重，并仅用少量参数训练一组小型LoRA。这可以捕捉小型LoRA之间的显著多样性程度，从而促进更好的泛化能力。我们在各种自然语言处理任务上进行了理论分析和实证研究。我们的实验结果显示，与LoRA相比，MELoRA在自然语言理解任务上使用了少8倍的可训练参数，在指令跟随任务上使用了少36倍的可训练参数，实现了更好的性能，这证明了MELoRA的有效性。

[No.465] Pruning Large Language Models to Intra-module Low-rank Architecture with Transitional Activations
来源: ACL 2024
命中: rank
标题: 将大型语言模型修剪为具有过渡激活的模块内低秩架构
类别: 
作者: Bowen Shen;Zheng Lin;Daren Zha;Wei Liu;Jian Luan;Bin Wang;Weiping Wang;
机构: Chinese Academy of Sciences;University of Chinese Academy of Sciences;Xiaomi AI Lab;
类型: Findings
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-acl.582/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.05690
英摘: Structured pruning fundamentally reduces computational and memory overheads of large language models (LLMs) and offers a feasible solution for end-side LLM deployment. Structurally pruned models remain dense and high-precision, highly compatible with further tuning and compression. However, as the coarse-grained structured pruning poses large damage to the highly interconnected model, achieving a high compression ratio for scaled-up LLMs remains a challenge. In this paper, we introduce a task-agnostic structured pruning approach coupled with a compact Transformer architecture design. The proposed approach, named TransAct, reduces transitional activations inside multi-head attention (MHA) and multi-layer perceptron (MLP) modules, while preserving the inter-module activations that are sensitive to perturbations. Hence, the LLM is pruned into an intra-module low-rank architecture, significantly reducing weights, KV Cache and attention computation. TransAct is implemented on the LLaMA model and evaluated on downstream benchmarks. Results verify the optimality of our approach at high compression with respect to both efficiency and performance. Further, ablation studies reveal the strength of activation-guided iterative pruning and provide experimental analysis on the redundancy of MHA and MLP modules.
中摘: 结构化剪枝从根本上减少了大型语言模型（LLM）的计算和内存开销，并为端侧LLM部署提供了一个可行的解决方案。结构化剪枝后的模型保持密集和高精度，与进一步的微调和压缩高度兼容。然而，由于粗粒度的结构化剪枝对高度互联的模型造成了巨大损害，对于规模扩大的LLM实现高压缩比仍然是一个挑战。在本文中，我们提出了一种与紧凑型Transformer架构设计相结合的任务无关结构化剪枝方法。所提出的方法，命名为TransAct，减少了多头注意力（MHA）和多层感知器（MLP）模块内的过渡激活，同时保留了对扰动敏感的跨模块激活。因此，LLM被剪枝为一个模块内的低秩架构，显著减少了权重、KV缓存和注意力计算。TransAct在LLaMA模型上实现，并在下游基准上进行了评估。结果验证了我们的方法在高压缩率下，在效率和性能方面都具有最优性。此外，消融研究揭示了激活引导的迭代剪枝的力量，并提供了关于MHA和MLP模块冗余性的实验分析。

[No.467] Ranking Entities along Conceptual Space Dimensions with LLMs: An Analysis of Fine-Tuning Strategies
来源: ACL 2024
命中: rank
标题: 使用大语言模型按概念空间维度对实体进行排名：关于微调策略的分析
类别:

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.15337
英摘: Conceptual spaces represent entities in terms of their primitive semantic features. Such representations are highly valuable but they are notoriously difficult to learn, especially when it comes to modelling perceptual and subjective features. Distilling conceptual spaces from Large Language Models (LLMs) has recently emerged as a promising strategy, but existing work has been limited to probing pre-trained LLMs using relatively simple zero-shot strategies. We focus in particular on the task of ranking entities according to a given conceptual space dimension. 中摘: 概念空间以其实质性的语义特征来表示实体。这类表示极其宝贵，但学习起来却异常困难，特别是在模拟感知和主观特征时。从大型语言模型（LLMs）中提炼概念空间最近成为一种有前景的策略，但现有工作仅限于使用相对简单的零样本策略来探查预训练的LLMs。我们特别关注根据给定的概念空间维度对实体进行排名的任务。遗憾的是，我们无法直接针对这一任务对LLMs进行微调，因为概念空间维度的真实排名数据非常稀少。因此，我们使用更易获得的特征作为训练数据，并分析由此产生的模型的排名能力是否能转移到感知和主观特征上。我们发现情况确实如此，但程度有限，但在训练数据中至少包含一些感知和主观特征似乎对于取得最佳结果至关重要。

[No.468] Ranking Large Language Models without Ground Truth
来源: ACL 2024
命中: rank
标题: 无需 ground truth 排名大型语言模型
类别: 
作者: Amit Dhurandhar;Rahul Nair;Moninder Singh;Elizabeth Daly;Karthikeyan Natesan Ramamurthy;
机构: IBM;
类型: Findings
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-acl.143/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.14860
英摘: Evaluation and ranking of large language models (LLMs) has become an important problem with the proliferation of these models and their impact. Evaluation methods either require human responses which are expensive to acquire or use pairs of LLMs to evaluate each other which can be unreliable. In this paper, we provide a novel perspective where, given a dataset of prompts (viz. questions, instructions, etc.) and a set of LLMs, we rank them without access to any ground truth or reference responses. Inspired by real life where both an expert and a knowledgeable person can identify a novice our main idea is to consider triplets of models, where each one of them evaluates the other two, correctly identifying the worst model in the triplet with high probability. We also analyze our idea and provide sufficient conditions for it to succeed. Applying this idea repeatedly, we propose two methods to rank LLMs. In experiments on different generative tasks (summarization, multiple-choice, and dialog), our methods reliably recover close to true rankings without reference data. This points to a viable low-resource mechanism for practical use.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLM）的评估与排名已成为一个重要的问题，随着这些模型的普及及其影响的扩大。评估方法要么需要昂贵的人工响应，要么使用一对LLM相互评估，这可能不可靠。在本文中，我们提供了一种新颖的观点，在给定一组提示（如问题、指令等）和一系列LLM的情况下，我们无需访问任何真相或参考响应即可对它们进行排名。受到现实生活的启发，在那里，专家和知识渊博的人都能识别出新手，我们的主要想法是考虑三个模型的组合，其中每一个都评估另外两个，以高概率正确识别出三者中最差的模型。我们还分析了我们的想法，并提供了它成功的充分条件。反复应用这一想法，我们提出了两种排名LLM的方法。在不同生成任务（摘要、多项选择和对话）的实验中，我们的方法在没有参考数据的情况下可靠地恢复接近真实的排名。这指向了一个实用的低资源机制，可用于实际应用。

[No.470] ResLoRA: Identity Residual Mapping in Low-Rank Adaption
来源: ACL 2024
命中: rank
标题: ResLoRA：低秩适应中的身份残差映射
类别: 
作者: Shuhua Shi;Shaohan Huang;Minghui Song;Zhoujun Li;Zihan Zhang;Haizhen Huang;Furu Wei;Weiwei Deng;Feng Sun;Qi Zhang;

英摘: As one of the most popular parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is commonly applied to fine-tune large language models (LLMs). However, updating the weights of LoRA blocks effectively and expeditiously is challenging due to the long calculation path in the original model. To address this, we propose ResLoRA, an improved framework of LoRA. By adding residual paths during training and using merging approaches to eliminate these extra paths during inference, our method can achieve better results in fewer training steps without any extra trainable parameters or inference cost compared to LoRA. The experiments on NLG, NLU, and text-to-image tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of our method. To the best of our knowledge, ResLoRA is the first work that combines the residual path with LoRA. The code of our method is available at https://github.com/microsoft/LMOps/tree/main/reslora .
中摘: 作为最受欢迎的参数高效微调（PEFT）方法之一，低秩适应（LoRA）常被用于微调大型语言模型（LLM）。然而，由于原始模型中的长计算路径，有效地且迅速地更新LoRA模块的权重颇具挑战。为此，我们提出了ResLoRA，一种改进的LoRA框架。通过在训练过程中添加残差路径，并在推理时使用合并方法消除这些额外路径，我们的方法能在不增加任何额外可训练参数或推理成本的情况下，比LoRA用更少的训练步骤达到更好的效果。在自然语言生成（NLG）、自然语言理解（NLU）和文到图任务上的实验验证了我们方法的有效性。据我们所知，ResLoRA是首个将残差路径与LoRA结合的工作。我们的方法代码可在以下链接获取：https://github.com/microsoft/LMOps/tree/main/reslora 。

[No.472] Zero-Shot Cross-Domain Dialogue State Tracking via Dual Low-Rank Adaptation
来源: ACL 2024
命中: rank
标题: 零样本跨域对话状态跟踪通过双低秩适应
类别: 
作者: Xiang Luo;Zhiwen Tang;Jin Wang;Xuejie Zhang;
机构: Yunnan University;
类型: Long
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-long.312/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.21633
英摘: Zero-shot dialogue state tracking (DST) seeks to enable dialogue systems to transition to unfamiliar domains without manual annotation or extensive retraining. Prior research has approached this objective by embedding prompts into language models (LMs). Common methodologies include integrating prompts at the input layer or introducing learnable variables at each transformer layer. Nonetheless, each strategy exhibits inherent limitations. Prompts integrated at the input layer risk underutilization, with their impact potentially diminishing across successive transformer layers. Conversely, the addition of learnable variables to each layer can complicate the training process and increase inference latency. To tackle the issues mentioned above, this paper proposes Dual Low-Rank Adaptation (DualLoRA), a plug-and-play architecture designed for zero-shot DST. DualLoRA incorporates two distinct Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) components, targeting both dialogue context processing and prompt optimization, to ensure the comprehensive influence of prompts throughout the transformer model layers. This is achieved without incurring additional inference latency, showcasing an efficient integration into existing architectures. Through rigorous evaluation on the MultiWOZ and SGD datasets, DualLoRA demonstrates notable improvements across multiple domains, outperforming traditional baseline methods in zero-shot settings. Our code is accessible at: \url{https://github.com/suntea233/DualLoRA}.
中摘: 零样本对话状态跟踪（DST）旨在使对话系统能够在无需人工标注或大量重新训练的情况下转向不熟悉的领域。先前的研究通过将提示嵌入语言模型（LMs）来实现这一目标。常用的方法包括在输入层整合提示，或者在每个变压器层引入可学习变量。然而，每种策略都有其内在局限性。在输入层集成的提示可能会被利用不足，其影响可能在后续变压器层中逐渐减弱。相反，在每一层添加可学习变量可能会使训练过程复杂化，并增加推理延迟。为了解决上述问题，本文提出了一种名为“双重低秩适应”（DualLoRA）的即插即用架构，专为零样本DST设计。DualLoRA结合了两个独立的低秩适应（LoRA）组件，分别针对对话上下文处理和提示优化，以确保在整个变压器模型层中提示的全面影响。这一过程不会产生额外的推理延迟，展示了与现有架构的有效集成。通过对MultiWOZ和SGD数据集进行严格评估，DualLoRA在多个领域显示出显著改进，在零样本设置下超越了传统基线方法。我们的代码可在以下网址获取：\url{https://github.com/suntea233/DualLoRA}。

[No.474] Bidirectional Transformer Reranker for Grammatical Error Correction
来源: ACL 2023
命中: rank
标题: 双向变换器排序器在语法错误修正中的应用
类别: 
作者: Ying Zhang;Hidetaka Kamigaito;Manabu Okumura;
机构: Tokyo Institute of Technology;Nara Institute of Science and Technology;RIKEN;
类型: Findings
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2023.findings-acl.234/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.13000
英摘: Pre-trained seq2seq models have achieved state-of-the-art results in the grammatical error correction task. However, these models still suffer from a prediction bias due to their unidirectional decoding. Thus, we propose a bidirectional Transformer reranker (BTR), that re-estimates the probability of each candidate sentence generated by the pre-trained seq2seq model. The BTR preserves the seq2seq-style Transformer architecture but utilizes a BERT-style self-attention mechanism in the decoder to compute the probability of each target token by using masked language modeling to capture bidirectional representations from the target context. For guiding the reranking, the BTR adopts negative sampling in the objective function to minimize the unlikelihood. During inference, the BTR gives final results after comparing the reranked top-1 results with the original ones by an acceptance threshold. Experimental results show that, in reranking candidates from a pre-trained seq2seq model, T5-base, the BTR on top of T5-base could yield 65.47 and 71.27 F0.5 scores on the CoNLL-14 and BEA test sets, respectively, and yield 59.52 GLEU score on the JFLEG corpus, with improvements of 0.36, 0.76 and 0.48 points compared with the original T5-base. Furthermore, when reranking candidates from T5-large, the BTR on top of T5-base improved the original T5-large by 0.26 points on the BEA test set.
中摘: 预训练的序列到序列(seq2seq)模型在语法错误校正任务上已经达到了最先进的成果。然而，这些模型仍然受到单向解码带来的预测偏差的影响。因此，我们提出了一种双向Transformer重排序器(BTR)，它重新评估由预训练seq2seq模型生成的每个候选句子的概率。BTR保留了seq2seq风格的Transformer架构，但在解码器中利用BERT风格的自注意力机制，通过掩码语言建模来捕捉目标上下文的双向表示，从而计算每个目标标记的概率。为了引导重排序，BTR在目标函数中采用负采样以最小化不可能性。在推理过程中，BTR通过接受阈值比较重排序后的前1名结果与原始结果后给出最终结果。实验结果显示，在从预训练的seq2seq模型T5-base重排序候选结果时，基于T5-base之上的BTR在CoNLL-14和BEA测试集上分别获得了65.47和71.27的F0.5分数，在JFLEG语料库上获得了59.52的GLEU分数，相比原始T5-base分别提高了0.36、0.76和0.48点。此外，当从T5-large重排序候选结果时，基于T5-base之上的BTR在BEA测试集上比原始T5-large提高了0.26点。

[No.476] Claim-Dissector: An Interpretable Fact-Checking System with Joint Re-ranking and Veracity Prediction
来源: ACL 2023
命中: rank
标题: 论点分解者：一种具有联合重排序和可信度预测的可解释事实核查系统
类别: 
作者: Martin Fajcik;Petr Motlicek;Pavel Smrz;
机构: Idiap Research Institute;Brno University of Technology;
类型: Findings
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2023.findings-acl.647/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2207.14116
英摘: We present Claim-Dissector: a novel latent variable model for fact-checking and analysis, which given a claim and a set of retrieved evidences jointly learns to identify: (i) the relevant evidences to the given claim, (ii) the veracity of the claim. We propose to disentangle the per-evidence relevance probability and its contribution to the final veracity probability in an interpretable way -- the final veracity probability is proportional to a linear ensemble of per-evidence relevance probabilities. In this way, the individual contributions of evidences towards the final predicted probability can be identified. In per-evidence relevance probability, our model can further distinguish whether each relevant evidence is supporting (S) or refuting (R) the claim. This allows to quantify how much the S/R probability contributes to the final verdict or to detect disagreeing evidence.
  Despite its interpretable nature, our system achieves results competitive with state-of-the-art on the FEVER dataset, as compared to typical two-stage system pipelines, while using significantly fewer parameters. It also sets new state-of-the-art on FAVIQ and RealFC datasets. Furthermore, our analysis shows that our model can learn fine-grained relevance cues while using coarse-grained supervision, and we demonstrate it in 2 ways. (i) We show that our model can achieve competitive sentence recall while using only paragraph-level relevance supervision. (ii) Traversing towards the finest granularity of relevance, we show that our model is capable of identifying relevance at the token level. To do this, we present a new benchmark TLR-FEVER focusing on token-level interpretability -- humans annotate tokens in relevant evidences they considered essential when making their judgment. Then we measure how similar are these annotations to the tokens our model is focusing on.
中摘: 我们提出了“Claim-Dissector”：一个用于事实核查和分析的创新隐变量模型，该模型针对给定的声明和一组检索到的证据，联合学习以识别：(i)与给定声明相关的证据，(ii)声明的真实性。我们提议以一种可解释的方式分解每个证据的相关性概率及其对最终真实性概率的贡献——最终的真实性概率正比于每个证据相关性概率的线性组合。这样，可以确定证据对最终预测概率的单独贡献。在每个证据的相关性概率中，我们的模型进一步区分了每个相关证据是否支持（S）或反驳（R）声明。这使得能够量化S/R概率对最终判决的贡献程度，或者检测存在争议的证据。

尽管具有可解释性，我们的系统在FEVER数据集上实现了与最先进方法竞争的结果，相比典型的两阶段系统管道，在使用显著较少参数的情况下达到这一成就。它还在FAVIQ和RealFC数据集上创造了新的最先进记录。此外，我们的分析表明，即使使用粗粒度的监督，我们的模型也能学习到细粒度的相关性线索，并且我们通过两种方式证明了这一点。(i) 我们展示了我们的模型仅使用段落级的相关性监督就能实现有竞争力的句子召回率。(ii) 向着相关性的最细粒度推进，我们展示了我们的模型能够在词汇级别识别相关性。为此，我们提出了一项新的基准测试TLR-FEVER，专注于词汇级别的可解释性——人类标注者标记了他们在做出判断时认为至关重要的相关证据中的词汇。然后，我们测量这些标注与我们的模型关注的词汇之间的相似度。

[No.477] COCKATIEL: COntinuous Concept ranKed ATtribution with Interpretable ELements for explaining neural net classifiers on NLP
来源: ACL 2023
命中: rank
标题: 考拉鸟：连续概念可解释属性排序与可解读元素方法在自然语言处理中解释神经网络分类器
类别: 
作者: Fanny Jourdan;Agustin Picard;Thomas Fel;Laurent Risser;Jean-Michel Loubes;Nicholas Asher;
机构: Université Paul-Sabatier;IRT Saint-Exupéry;Brown University;SNCF;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.06754
英摘: Transformer architectures are complex and their use in NLP, while it has engendered many successes, makes their interpretability or explainability challenging. Recent debates have shown that attention maps and attribution methods are unreliable (Pruthi et al., 2019; Brunner et al., 2019). In this paper, we present some of their limitations and introduce COCKATIEL, which successfully addresses some of them. COCKATIEL is a novel, post-hoc, concept-based, model-agnostic XAI technique that generates meaningful explanations from the last layer of a neural net model trained on an NLP classification task by using Non-Negative Matrix Factorization (NMF) to discover the concepts the model leverages to make predictions and by exploiting a Sensitivity Analysis to estimate accurately the importance of each of these concepts for the model. It does so without compromising the accuracy of the underlying model or requiring a new one to be trained. We conduct experiments in single and multi-aspect sentiment analysis tasks and we show COCKATIEL's superior ability to discover concepts that align with humans' on Transformer models without any supervision, we objectively verify the faithfulness of its explanations through fidelity metrics, and we showcase its ability to provide meaningful explanations in two different datasets.
中摘: 变换器架构复杂，在自然语言处理（NLP）中的应用虽然取得了许多成功，但这也使得它们的可解释性或可说明性充满挑战。最近的讨论表明，注意力图和归因方法并不可靠（Pruthi 等人，2019；Brunner 等人，2019）。在本文中，我们提出了一些它们的局限性，并引入了 COCKATIEL，它成功地解决了一些问题。COCKATIEL 是一种新颖的、事后处理的、基于概念的、模型无关的可解释人工智能（XAI）技术，它通过使用非负矩阵分解（NMF）来发现模型用于做出预测的概念，并利用敏感性分析准确估计这些概念对模型的重要性，从而从训练有素的神经网络模型的最后一层生成有意义的解释。这一过程不会损害基础模型的准确性，也不需要重新训练新的模型。我们在单方面和多方面的观点情感分析任务中进行了实验，展示了 COCKATIEL 在没有任何监督的情况下，优于人类在变换器模型上发现概念的能力，我们通过保真度指标客观验证了其解释的忠实度，并展示了其在两个不同数据集上提供有意义解释的能力。

[No.479] Discrete Prompt Optimization via Constrained Generation for Zero-shot Re-ranker
来源: ACL 2023
命中: rank
标题: 通过受限生成进行离散提示优化以实现零样本重新排名
类别:

英摘: Re-rankers, which order retrieved documents with respect to the relevance score on the given query, have gained attention for the information retrieval (IR) task. Rather than fine-tuning the pre-trained language model (PLM), the large-scale language model (LLM) is utilized as a zero-shot re-ranker with excellent results. While LLM is highly dependent on the prompts, the impact and the optimization of the prompts for the zero-shot re-ranker are not explored yet.
