[No.480] EEL: Efficiently Encoding Lattices for Reranking
来源: ACL 2023
命中: rank
标题: EEL：高效编码格子进行重排序
类别: 
作者: Prasann Singhal;Jiacheng Xu;Xi Ye;Greg Durrett;
机构: University of Texas at Austin;Salesforce;
类型: Long
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2023.acl-long.517/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.00947
英摘: Standard decoding approaches for conditional text generation tasks typically search for an output hypothesis with high model probability, but this may not yield the best hypothesis according to human judgments of quality. Reranking to optimize for "downstream" metrics can better optimize for quality, but many metrics of interest are computed with pre-trained language models, which are slow to apply to large numbers of hypotheses. We explore an approach for reranking hypotheses by using Transformers to efficiently encode lattices of generated outputs, a method we call EEL. With a single Transformer pass over the entire lattice, we can approximately compute a contextualized representation of each token as if it were only part of a single hypothesis in isolation. We combine this approach with a new class of token-factored rerankers (TFRs) that allow for efficient extraction of high reranker-scoring hypotheses from the lattice. Empirically, our approach incurs minimal degradation error compared to the exponentially slower approach of encoding each hypothesis individually. When applying EEL with TFRs across three text generation tasks, our results show both substantial speedup compared to naive reranking and often better performance on downstream metrics than comparable approaches.
中摘: 标准的解码方法在处理条件文本生成任务时，通常会寻找具有高模型概率的输出假设，但这可能并不会产生根据人类对质量的判断所认为的最佳假设。通过重新排序以优化“下游”指标可以更好地提高质量，但许多感兴趣的指标都是通过预训练的语言模型计算得出的，这在大量假设上应用时速度很慢。我们探索了一种通过使用Transformer高效编码生成输出的格子（lattice）来重新排序假设的方法，我们称之为EEL。通过对整个格子进行一次Transformer传递，我们可以近似地计算出每个令牌的上下文化表示，就好像它只是孤立单一假设的一部分。我们将这种方法与一类新的基于令牌的重新排序器（TFRs）相结合，这种重新排序器允许从格子中有效提取得分高的假设。实验表明，我们的方法相比指数级更慢的单独编码每个假设的方法，在降级误差方面几乎没有任何损失。当在三个文本生成任务中应用EEL和TFRs时，我们的结果显示，相比于简单的重新排序，这种方法不仅大大提高了速度，而且在下游指标上的表现往往优于可比较的方法。

[No.481] Expand, Rerank, and Retrieve: Query Reranking for Open-Domain Question Answering
来源: ACL 2023
命中: rank
标题: 扩展、重排和检索：开放域查询重排序
类别: 
作者: Yung-Sung Chuang;Wei Fang;Shang-Wen Li;Wen-tau Yih;James Glass;
机构: Massachusetts Institute of Technology;Meta;
类型: Findings
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2023.findings-acl.768/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.17080
英摘: We propose EAR, a query Expansion And Reranking approach for improving passage retrieval, with the application to open-domain question answering. EAR first applies a query expansion model to generate a diverse set of queries, and then uses a query reranker to select the ones that could lead to better retrieval results. Motivated by the observation that the best query expansion often is not picked by greedy decoding, EAR trains its reranker to predict the rank orders of the gold passages when issuing the expanded queries to a given retriever. By connecting better the query expansion model and retriever, EAR significantly enhances a traditional sparse retrieval method, BM25. Empirically, EAR improves top-5/20 accuracy by 3-8 and 5-10 points in in-domain and out-of-domain settings, respectively, when compared to a vanilla query expansion model, GAR, and a dense retrieval model, DPR.
中摘: 我们提出了一种名为EAR（查询扩展与重排序）的方法，用于改进段落检索，特别应用于开放域问题回答。EAR首先使用查询扩展模型生成一组多样化的查询，然后利用查询重排序器选择那些能够带来更好检索结果的查询。受到最佳查询扩展通常不会被贪心解码选中的观察启发，EAR训练其重排序器预测在向给定检索器发出扩展查询时，金标准段落的排名顺序。通过更好地连接查询扩展模型和检索器，EAR显著增强了传统的稀疏检索方法，即BM25。实证上，与简单的查询扩展模型GAR和密集检索模型DPR相比，EAR在领域内和领域外设置下分别提高了前5/20准确率3-8分和5-10分。

[No.483] Few-shot Reranking for Multi-hop QA via Language Model Prompting
来源: ACL 2023

标题: 通过语言模型提示实现的Few-shot多跳问答重排

英摘: We study few-shot reranking for multi-hop QA with open-domain questions. To alleviate the need for a large number of labeled question-document pairs for retriever training, we propose PromptRank, which relies on large language models prompting for multi-hop path reranking. [No.485] Generating EDU Extracts for Plan-Guided Summary Re-Ranking
来源: ACL 2023
命中: rank
标题: 生成计划引导摘要重排序的EDU提取
类别: 
作者: Griffin Adams;Alex Fabbri;Faisal Ladhak;Noémie Elhadad;Kathleen McKeown;
机构: Salesforce;Columbia University;
类型: Long
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2023.acl-long.151/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.17779
英摘: Two-step approaches, in which summary candidates are generated-then-reranked to return a single summary, can improve ROUGE scores over the standard single-step approach. Yet, standard decoding methods (i.e., beam search, nucleus sampling, and diverse beam search) produce candidates with redundant, and often low quality, content. In this paper, we design a novel method to generate candidates for re-ranking that addresses these issues. We ground each candidate abstract on its own unique content plan and generate distinct plan-guided abstracts using a model's top beam. More concretely, a standard language model (a BART LM) auto-regressively generates elemental discourse unit (EDU) content plans with an extractive copy mechanism. The top K beams from the content plan generator are then used to guide a separate LM, which produces a single abstractive candidate for each distinct plan. We apply an existing re-ranker (BRIO) to abstractive candidates generated from our method, as well as baseline decoding methods. We show large relevance improvements over previously published methods on widely used single document news article corpora, with ROUGE-2 F1 gains of 0.88, 2.01, and 0.38 on CNN / Dailymail, NYT, and Xsum, respectively. A human evaluation on CNN / DM validates these results. Similarly, on 1k samples from CNN / DM, we show that prompting GPT-3 to follow EDU plans outperforms sampling-based methods by 1.05 ROUGE-2 F1 points. Code to generate and realize plans is available at https://github.com/griff4692/edu-sum.
中摘: 两步法，即先生成摘要候选，然后重新排序以返回单一摘要，相较于标准的单步方法可以提高ROUGE分数。然而，标准解码方法（即束搜索、核抽样和多样化束搜索）产生的候选内容往往冗余且质量低下。在本文中，我们设计了一种新颖的方法来生成用于重新排序的候选摘要，解决了这些问题。我们为每个候选摘要提供其独特的内容计划作为基础，并使用模型的顶级束生成不同的计划引导的摘要。更具体地说，一个标准的语言模型（BART LM）自回归地生成元素话语单元（EDU）内容计划，采用抽取式复制机制。然后，从内容计划生成器的前K束中，指导另一个独立的语言模型，为每个独特的计划产生一个抽象候选摘要。我们将现有的重新排序器（BRIO）应用于我们方法以及基线解码方法生成的抽象候选摘要。我们在广泛使用的单一新闻文章语料库上展示了与之前发表的方法相比有显著的相关性提升，在CNN/Dailymail、NYT和Xsum上的ROUGE-2 F1分别提高了0.88、2.01和0.38。在CNN/DM上的人工评估验证了这些结果。同样，在CNN/DM的1k样本上，我们表明提示GPT-3遵循EDU计划比基于抽样的方法在ROUGE-2 F1上高出1.05分。生成和实现计划的代码可在https://github.com/griff4692/edu-sum获取。

[No.486] Hybrid and Collaborative Passage Reranking
来源: ACL 2023
命中: rank
标题: 混合与协作段落重排
类别: 
作者: Zongmeng Zhang;Wengang Zhou;Jiaxin Shi;Houqiang Li;
机构: University of Science and Technology of China;Huawei;
类型: Findings
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2023.findings-acl.880/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.09313
英摘: In passage retrieval system, the initial passage retrieval results may be unsatisfactory, which can be refined by a reranking scheme. Existing solutions to passage reranking focus on enriching the interaction between query and each passage separately, neglecting the context among the top-ranked passages in the initial retrieval list. To tackle this problem, we propose a Hybrid and Collaborative Passage Reranking (HybRank) method, which leverages the substantial similarity measurements of upstream retrievers for passage collaboration and incorporates the lexical and semantic properties of sparse and dense retrievers for reranking. Besides, built on off-the-shelf retriever features, HybRank is a plug-in reranker capable of enhancing arbitrary passage lists including previously reranked ones. Extensive experiments demonstrate the stable improvements of performance over prevalent retrieval and reranking methods, and verify the effectiveness of the core components of HybRank.
中摘: 在段落检索系统中，初始的段落检索结果可能不尽如人意，这可以通过重排序方案进行优化。现有的段落重排序解决方案主要集中在单独丰富查询与每个段落之间的交互，而忽视了初始检索列表中排名靠前的段落之间的上下文联系。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一种混合协同段落重排序（HybRank）方法，该方法利用上游检索器对段落合作的实质性相似度测量，并结合稀疏和密集检索器的词汇和语义特性进行重排序。此外，基于现成的检索器特征，HybRank是一种插件式重排序器，能够增强任意的段落列表，包括之前已经重排序过的列表。广泛的实验表明，HybRank在性能上对流行的检索和重排序方法有稳定的提升，并验证了其核心组件的有效性。

[No.487] Joint Generator-Ranker Learning for Natural Language Generation
来源: ACL 2023
命中: rank
标题: 联合生成-排名学习方法用于自然语言生成
类别: 
作者: Weizhou Shen;Yeyun Gong;Yelong Shen;Song Wang;Xiaojun Quan;Nan Duan;Weizhu Chen;
机构: Sun Yat-sen University;Microsoft;
类型: Findings
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2023.findings-acl.486/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2206.13974
英摘: Generate-then-rank is a widely used mechanism for text generation, where a generator produces multiple text candidates and a ranker chooses the best one among the text candidates. However, existing methods usually train the generator and the ranker individually, neglecting the mutual feedback that could further enhance the generation quality. To tackle this limitation, we propose JGR, a novel joint training algorithm that integrates the generator and the ranker in a single framework. JGR optimizes the generator with a hybrid objective that combines data likelihood and ranker reward, and trains the ranker with a contrastive loss that compares the generator outputs. By iteratively updating the generator and the ranker, JGR can effectively harmonize their learning and enhance their quality jointly. We evaluate JGR on various text generation tasks and demonstrate that it surpasses existing methods on four public datasets across three common generation scenarios. Our code and models are publicly available at https://github.com/microsoft/ProphetNet/tree/master/JGR.
中摘: 生成-然后-排序是一种广泛用于文本生成的机制，其中生成器产生多个文本候选，而排序器则在这些文本候选中选择最佳的一个。然而，现有的方法通常分别训练生成器和排序器，忽略了相互反馈可能进一步提升生成质量的作用。为了解决这一局限性，我们提出了JGR，一种新颖的联合训练算法，它在一个单一框架内整合了生成器和排序器。JGR通过结合数据似然性和排序器奖励的混合目标来优化生成器，并使用对比损失来训练排序器，该对比损失比较生成器的输出。通过迭代更新生成器和排序器，JGR可以有效地协调它们的学习过程并共同提升它们的质量。我们在各种文本生成任务上评估了JGR，并证明它在三个常见的生成场景下，在四个公开数据集上超越了现有方法。我们的代码和模型可在https://github.com/microsoft/ProphetNet/tree/master/JGR上公开获取。

[No.488] Learning to Rank Utterances for Query-Focused Meeting Summarization
来源: ACL 2023
命中: rank
标题: 查询聚焦会议摘要中的话语排序学习
类别: 
作者: Xingxian Liu;Yajing Xu;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.12753
英摘: Query-focused meeting summarization(QFMS) aims to generate a specific summary for the given query according to the meeting transcripts. Due to the conflict between long meetings and limited input size, previous works mainly adopt extract-then-summarize methods, which use extractors to simulate binary labels or ROUGE scores to extract utterances related to the query and then generate a summary. However, the previous approach fails to fully use the comparison between utterances. To the extractor, comparison orders are more important than specific scores. In this paper, we propose a Ranker-Generator framework. It learns to rank the utterances by comparing them in pairs and learning from the global orders, then uses top utterances as the generator's input. We show that learning to rank utterances helps to select utterances related to the query effectively, and the summarizer can benefit from it. Experimental results on QMSum show that the proposed model outperforms all existing multi-stage models with fewer parameters.
中摘: 查询聚焦会议摘要（QFMS）旨在根据会议记录为给定的查询生成特定的摘要。由于长时间会议与有限输入大小之间的矛盾，先前的工作主要采用提取-然后-摘要的方法，使用提取器模拟二进制标签或ROUGE分数来抽取与查询相关的发言，然后生成摘要。然而，之前的方法未能充分利用发言之间的比较。对于提取器而言，比较顺序比具体分数更重要。在本文中，我们提出了一种排名者-生成器框架。它通过成对比较发言并从全局顺序中学习来对发言进行排序，然后将排名靠前的发言作为生成器的输入。我们证明了学习排名发言有助于有效地选择与查询相关的发言，而摘要器可以从其中受益。在QMSum上的实验结果显示，所提出的模型在参数较少的情况下超越了所有现有的多阶段模型。

[No.489] LLM-Blender: Ensembling Large Language Models with Pairwise Ranking and Generative Fusion
来源: ACL 2023
命中: rank
标题: LLM-Blender：基于成对排名和生成性融合的大语言模型集成
类别: 
作者: Dongfu Jiang;Xiang Ren;Bill Yuchen Lin;
机构: Zhejiang University;Allen Institute for Artificial Intelligence;University of Southern California;
类型: Long
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2023.acl-long.792/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.02561
英摘: We present LLM-Blender, an ensembling framework designed to attain consistently superior performance by leveraging the diverse strengths of multiple open-source large language models (LLMs). Our framework consists of two modules: PairRanker and GenFuser, addressing the observation that optimal LLMs for different examples can significantly vary. PairRanker employs a specialized pairwise comparison method to distinguish subtle differences between candidate outputs. It jointly encodes the input text and a pair of candidates, using cross-attention encoders to determine the superior one. Our results demonstrate that PairRanker exhibits the highest correlation with ChatGPT-based ranking. Then, GenFuser aims to merge the top-ranked candidates, generating an improved output by capitalizing on their strengths and mitigating their weaknesses. To facilitate large-scale evaluation, we introduce a benchmark dataset, MixInstruct, which is a mixture of multiple instruction datasets featuring oracle pairwise comparisons. Our LLM-Blender significantly outperform individual LLMs and baseline methods across various metrics, establishing a substantial performance gap.
中摘: 我们提出LLM-Blender，一个集成框架，旨在通过利用多个开源大型语言模型（LLMs）的多样化优势来实现一贯的卓越性能。我们的框架由两个模块组成：PairRanker和GenFuser，针对不同示例的最佳LLM可能显著变化这一观察结果。PairRanker采用专门的一对一比较方法来区分候选输出之间的细微差别。它同时编码输入文本和一对候选输出，使用交叉注意力编码器来确定更优的一个。我们的结果显示，PairRanker与基于ChatGPT的排名具有最高的相关性。然后，GenFuser的目标是合并排名靠前的候选输出，通过发挥它们的优势并减轻它们的弱点来生成改进后的输出。为了便于大规模评估，我们引入了一个基准数据集MixInstruct，它是包含有先知一对一比较的多个指令数据集的混合体。我们的LLM-Blender在各种指标上显著超越了单独的LLM和基线方法，确立了巨大的性能差距。

[No.492] PromptRank: Unsupervised Keyphrase Extraction Using Prompt
来源: ACL 2023
命中: rank
标题: PromptRank：使用提示的无监督关键短语提取
类别: 
作者: Aobo Kong;Shiwan Zhao;Hao Chen;Qicheng Li;Yong Qin;Ruiqi Sun;Xiaoyan Bai;
机构: Nankai University;Independent Researcher;Lenovo Research;
类型: Long
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2023.acl-long.545/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.04490
英摘: The keyphrase extraction task refers to the automatic selection of phrases from a given document to summarize its core content. State-of-the-art (SOTA) performance has recently been achieved by embedding-based algorithms, which rank candidates according to how similar their embeddings are to document embeddings. However, such solutions either struggle with the document and candidate length discrepancies or fail to fully utilize the pre-trained language model (PLM) without further fine-tuning. To this end, in this paper, we propose a simple yet effective unsupervised approach, PromptRank, based on the PLM with an encoder-decoder architecture. Specifically, PromptRank feeds the document into the encoder and calculates the probability of generating the candidate with a designed prompt by the decoder. We extensively evaluate the proposed PromptRank on six widely used benchmarks. PromptRank outperforms the SOTA approach MDERank, improving the F1 score relatively by 34.18%, 24.87%, and 17.57% for 5, 10, and 15 returned results, respectively. This demonstrates the great potential of using prompt for unsupervised keyphrase extraction. We release our code at https://github.com/HLT-NLP/PromptRank.
中摘: 关键词短语抽取任务指的是从给定文档中自动选择短语以概括其核心内容。最近，基于嵌入（embedding）的算法在最先进（state-of-the-art，SOTA）性能上取得了突破，它们通过比较候选短语的嵌入与文档嵌入之间的相似度来对候选短语进行排名。然而，这些解决方案要么难以应对文档和候选短语长度不一致的问题，要么未能充分利用预训练语言模型（PLM），除非进一步对其进行微调。为此，在本文中，我们提出了一种简单而有效的无监督方法，名为PromptRank，它基于具有编码器-解码器架构的PLM。具体来说，PromptRank将文档输入到编码器中，并通过设计的提示让解码器计算生成候选短语的概率。我们在六个广泛使用的基准数据集上对提出的PromptRank进行了全面评估。PromptRank超越了最先进的方法MDERank，对于返回5、10和15个结果的情况，分别相对提高了F1分数34.18%、24.87%和17.57%。这证明了使用提示进行无监督关键词短语抽取的巨大潜力。我们的代码已在https://github.com/HLT-NLP/PromptRank公开发布。

[No.493] RankCSE: Unsupervised Sentence Representations Learning via Learning to Rank
来源: ACL 2023
命中: rank
标题: RankCSE：通过学习排序学习无监督句子表示
类别: 
作者: Jiduan Liu;Jiahao Liu;Qifan Wang;Jingang Wang;Wei Wu;Yunsen Xian;Dongyan Zhao;Kai Chen;Rui Yan;
机构: Peking University;Meituan;Meta;National Key Laboratory of General Artificial Intelligence;Beijing Institute of General Artificial Intelligence;Renmin University of China;Ministry of Education;
类型: Long

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.16726
英摘: Unsupervised sentence representation learning is one of the fundamental problems in natural language processing with various downstream applications. Recently, contrastive learning has been widely adopted which derives high-quality sentence representations by pulling similar semantics closer and pushing dissimilar ones away. However, these methods fail to capture the fine-grained ranking information among the sentences, where each sentence is only treated as either positive or negative. In many real-world scenarios, one needs to distinguish and rank the sentences based on their similarities to a query sentence, e.g., very relevant, moderate relevant, less relevant, irrelevant, etc. In this paper, we propose a novel approach, RankCSE, for unsupervised sentence representation learning, which incorporates ranking consistency and ranking distillation with contrastive learning into a unified framework. In particular, we learn semantically discriminative sentence representations by simultaneously ensuring ranking consistency between two representations with different dropout masks, and distilling listwise ranking knowledge from the teacher. An extensive set of experiments are conducted on both semantic textual similarity (STS) and transfer (TR) tasks. Experimental results demonstrate the superior performance of our approach over several state-of-the-art baselines.
中摘: 无监督句子表示学习是自然语言处理中的一个基本问题，具有多种下游应用。最近，对比学习被广泛采用，通过拉近相似语义并推远离异语义来导出高质量的句子表示。然而，这些方法未能捕捉句子间的细粒度排序信息，其中每个句子仅被视为正面或负面。在许多现实世界的情景下，需要根据与查询句子的相似性来区分和排序句子，例如，非常相关、适度相关、不太相关、不相关等。在这篇论文中，我们提出了一种新颖的方法，RankCSE，用于无监督句子表示学习，它将排名一致性与对比学习和排名蒸馏整合到一个统一框架中。具体而言，我们通过同时确保两个具有不同dropout掩码的表示之间的排名一致性，并从教师那里提炼列表式的排名知识，来学习语义上具有判别力的句子表示。在语义文本相似性（STS）和转移（TR）任务上进行了一系列广泛的实验。实验结果证明了我们的方法在多个最先进基线上的优越性能。

[No.494] Ranking-Enhanced Unsupervised Sentence Representation Learning
来源: ACL 2023
命中: rank
标题: 基于排名增强的无监督句子表示学习
类别: 
作者: Yeon Seonwoo;Guoyin Wang;Changmin Seo;Sajal Choudhary;Jiwei Li;Xiang Li;Puyang Xu;Sunghyun Park;Alice Oh;
机构: Korea Advanced Institute of Science and Technology;Amazon;Zhejiang University;
类型: Long
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2023.acl-long.879/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2209.04333
英摘: Unsupervised sentence representation learning has progressed through contrastive learning and data augmentation methods such as dropout masking. Despite this progress, sentence encoders are still limited to using only an input sentence when predicting its semantic vector. In this work, we show that the semantic meaning of a sentence is also determined by nearest-neighbor sentences that are similar to the input sentence. Based on this finding, we propose a novel unsupervised sentence encoder, RankEncoder. RankEncoder predicts the semantic vector of an input sentence by leveraging its relationship with other sentences in an external corpus, as well as the input sentence itself. We evaluate RankEncoder on semantic textual benchmark datasets. From the experimental results, we verify that 1) RankEncoder achieves 80.07% Spearman's correlation, a 1.1% absolute improvement compared to the previous state-of-the-art performance, 2) RankEncoder is universally applicable to existing unsupervised sentence embedding methods, and 3) RankEncoder is specifically effective for predicting the similarity scores of similar sentence pairs.
中摘: 无监督句子表示学习已经通过对比学习和数据增强方法（如dropout屏蔽）取得了进展。尽管有了这些进步，句子编码器仍然仅限于在预测其语义向量时使用输入句子。在这项工作中，我们表明一个句子的语义意义也由与输入句子相似的最近邻句子决定。基于这一发现，我们提出了一种新颖的无监督句子编码器，RankEncoder。RankEncoder通过利用外部语料库中其他句子与输入句子的关系以及输入句子本身，来预测输入句子的语义向量。我们在语义文本基准数据集上评估了RankEncoder。从实验结果中，我们验证了1）RankEncoder实现了80.07%的斯皮尔曼相关系数，比之前最先进的性能提高了1.1%的绝对值，2）RankEncoder普遍适用于现有的无监督句子嵌入方法，以及3）RankEncoder特别有效地预测了相似句子对的相似度分数。

简而言之，RankEncoder是一种创新的无监督句子编码技术，它不仅考虑了输入句子自身的信息，还利用了外部语料库中的其他句子，特别是那些与输入句子相近的句子，以提高语义向量的预测准确性。这种方法在实验中表现出色，超越了现有技术，并且对于预测相似句子之间的相似度非常有效。

[No.496] Towards Imperceptible Document Manipulations against Neural Ranking Models
来源: ACL 2023
命中: rank
标题: 对抗神经排名模型的无形文档操纵
类别: 
作者: Xuanang Chen;Ben He;Zheng Ye;Le Sun;Yingfei Sun;
机构: University of Chinese Academy of Sciences;Chinese Academy of Sciences;South-Central University for Nationalities;
类型: Findings
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2023.findings-acl.416/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.01860
英摘: Adversarial attacks have gained traction in order to identify potential vulnerabilities in neural ranking models (NRMs), but current attack methods often introduce grammatical errors, nonsensical expressions, or incoherent text fragments, which can be easily detected. Additionally, current methods rely heavily on the use of a well-imitated surrogate NRM to guarantee the attack effect, which makes them difficult to use in practice. To address these issues, we propose a framework called Imperceptible DocumEnt Manipulation (IDEM) to produce adversarial documents that are less noticeable to both algorithms and humans. IDEM instructs a well-established generative language model, such as BART, to generate connection sentences without introducing easy-to-detect errors, and employs a separate position-wise merging strategy to balance relevance and coherence of the perturbed text. Experimental results on the popular MS MARCO benchmark demonstrate that IDEM can outperform strong baselines while preserving fluency and correctness of the target documents as evidenced by automatic and human evaluations. Furthermore, the separation of adversarial text generation from the surrogate NRM makes IDEM more robust and less affected by the quality of the surrogate NRM.
中摘: 对抗性攻击在识别神经排名模型（NRMs）潜在漏洞方面引起了关注，但当前的攻击方法经常引入语法错误、无意义的表达或不连贯的文本片段，这些很容易被检测出来。此外，现有方法严重依赖于使用精心模仿的代理NRM来保证攻击效果，这使得它们在实际应用中难以操作。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了一种名为“不易察觉文档操控”（IDEM）的框架，用于生成对算法和人类都不那么明显的对抗性文档。IDEM指导一个成熟生成语言模型（如BART）生成连接句子，而不引入容易检测的错误，并采用独立的位置合并策略来平衡扰动文本的相关性和连贯性。在流行的MS MARCO基准上的实验结果表明，IDEM能够在保持目标文档流利度和正确性的前提下超越强大的基线，这一点通过自动和人工评估得到了证实。此外，将对抗性文本生成与代理NRM分离使得IDEM更加稳健，较少受到代理NRM质量的影响。

[No.497] Towards Robust Ranker for Text Retrieval
来源: ACL 2023
命中: rank
标题: 面向稳健的文本检索排序器
类别: 
作者: Yucheng Zhou;Tao Shen;Xiubo Geng;Chongyang Tao;Can Xu;Guodong Long;Binxing Jiao;Daxin Jiang;
机构: University of Technology Sydney;Microsoft;
类型: Findings
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2023.findings-acl.332/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2206.08063
英摘: A ranker plays an indispensable role in the de facto 'retrieval & rerank' pipeline, but its training still lags behind -- learning from moderate negatives or/and serving as an auxiliary module for a retriever. In this work, we first identify two major barriers to a robust ranker, i.e., inherent label noises caused by a well-trained retriever and non-ideal negatives sampled for a high-capable ranker. Thereby, we propose multiple retrievers as negative generators improve the ranker's robustness, where i) involving extensive out-of-distribution label noises renders the ranker against each noise distribution, and ii) diverse hard negatives from a joint distribution are relatively close to the ranker's negative distribution, leading to more challenging thus effective training. To evaluate our robust ranker (dubbed R$^2$anker), we conduct experiments in various settings on the popular passage retrieval benchmark, including BM25-reranking, full-ranking, retriever distillation, etc. The empirical results verify the new state-of-the-art effectiveness of our model.
中摘: 排名器在事实上的“检索与重排”管道中扮演着不可或缺的角色，但其训练仍然落后——从适度的负面样本学习或作为检索器的辅助模块。在这项工作中，我们首先确定了排名器稳健性的两个主要障碍，即由训练有素的检索器引起的固有标签噪声和为高能力排名器采样的非理想负面样本。因此，我们提出使用多个检索器作为负面生成器来增强排名器的稳健性，其中i）涉及大量的分布外标签噪声使排名器能够抵抗每种噪声分布，ii）来自联合分布的多样化困难负面样本相对接近排名器的负面样本分布，导致更具挑战性从而更有效的训练。为了评估我们的稳健排名器（命名为R²anker），我们在流行篇章检索基准上进行了多种设置下的实验，包括BM25重排序、全排序、检索器蒸馏等。实证结果证实了我们模型的新颖性和最先进的有效性。

请注意，这里的R²anker是一个命名缩写，用来代表研究中的稳健排名器。在不同的实验设置下，这个排名器展示了其处理噪声和困难负面样本的能力，从而在篇章检索任务上达到了最先进的效果。这表明使用多个检索器生成多样化的负面样本对于训练一个更稳健、更有效的排名器至关重要。

[No.498] Unsupervised Summarization Re-ranking
来源: ACL 2023
命中: rank
标题: 无监督摘要重排序
类别: 
作者: Mathieu Ravaut;Shafiq Joty;Nancy Chen;

英摘: With the rise of task-specific pre-training objectives, abstractive summarization models like PEGASUS offer appealing zero-shot performance on downstream summarization tasks. However, the performance of such unsupervised models still lags significantly behind their supervised counterparts. Similarly to the supervised setup, we notice a very high variance in quality among summary candidates from these models while only one candidate is kept as the summary output. In this paper, we propose to re-rank summary candidates in an unsupervised manner, aiming to close the performance gap between unsupervised and supervised models. [No.499] A LLM-based Ranking Method for the Evaluation of Automatic Counter-Narrative Generation
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: 基于LLM的排名方法评估自动反叙事生成
类别: 
作者: Irune Zubiaga;Aitor Soroa;Rodrigo Agerri;
机构: University of the Basque Country;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.559/

英摘: This paper proposes a novel approach to evaluate Counter Narrative (CN) generation using a Large Language Model (LLM) as an evaluator. We show that traditional automatic metrics correlate poorly with human judgements and fail to capture the nuanced relationship between generated CNs and human perception. To alleviate this, we introduce a model ranking pipeline based on pairwise comparisons of generated CNs from different models, organized in a tournament-style format. The proposed evaluation method achieves a high correlation with human preference, with a $ρ$ score of 0.88. As an additional contribution, we leverage LLMs as zero-shot CN generators and provide a comparative analysis of chat, instruct, and base models, exploring their respective strengths and limitations. [No.500] A Study of Implicit Ranking Unfairness in Large Language Models
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: 大型语言模型中的隐含排名不公平性研究
类别: 
作者: Chen Xu;Wenjie Wang;Yuxin Li;Liang Pang;Jun Xu;Tat-Seng Chua;

英摘: Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated a superior ability to serve as ranking models. However, concerns have arisen as LLMs will exhibit discriminatory ranking behaviors based on users' sensitive attributes (\eg gender). Worse still, in this paper, we identify a subtler form of discrimination in LLMs, termed \textit{implicit ranking unfairness}, where LLMs exhibit discriminatory ranking patterns based solely on non-sensitive user profiles, such as user names. Such implicit unfairness is more widespread but less noticeable, threatening the ethical foundation. To comprehensively explore such unfairness, our analysis will focus on three research aspects: (1) We propose an evaluation method to investigate the severity of implicit ranking unfairness. (2) We uncover the reasons for causing such unfairness. (3) To mitigate such unfairness effectively, we utilize a pair-wise regression method to conduct fair-aware data augmentation for LLM fine-tuning. The experiment demonstrates that our method outperforms existing approaches in ranking fairness, achieving this with only a small reduction in accuracy. Lastly, we emphasize the need for the community to identify and mitigate the implicit unfairness, aiming to avert the potential deterioration in the reinforced human-LLMs ecosystem deterioration.
中摘: 最近，大型语言模型（LLM）展现出了作为排名模型的卓越能力。然而，人们担忧LLM会根据用户的敏感属性（如性别）展现出歧视性的排名行为。更糟糕的是，本文中我们识别出LLM中一种更为微妙的歧视形式，称为“隐性排名不公平”，其中LLM仅基于非敏感的用户资料，如用户名，就展现出歧视性的排名模式。这种隐性不公平更加普遍但也更不易察觉，威胁着道德基础。为了全面探索此类不公平现象，我们的分析将专注于三个研究方面：(1) 我们提出了一种评估方法来调查隐性排名不公平的严重程度。(2) 我们揭示了导致此类不公平的原因。(3) 为有效减轻此类不公平，我们利用配对回归方法进行公平意识的数据增强，用于LLM微调。实验表明，我们的方法在排名公平性上超越现有方法，仅以轻微的准确度下降为代价。最后，我们强调社区需要识别并缓解隐性不公平，旨在避免加强的人类-LLM生态系统潜在恶化。

[No.501] Adaptive Feature-based Low-Rank Compression of Large Language Models via Bayesian Optimization
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: 基于自适应特征的贝叶斯优化低秩压缩大型语言模型
类别: 
作者: Yixin Ji;Yang Xiang;Juntao Li;Qingrong Xia;Zi Ye;Xinyu Duan;Zhefeng Wang;Kehai Chen;Min Zhang;
机构: Soochow University;Huawei;Harbin Institute of Technology;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.240/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.10616
英摘: In recent years, large language models (LLMs) have driven advances in natural language processing. Still, their growing scale has increased the computational burden, necessitating a balance between efficiency and performance. Low-rank compression, a promising technique, reduces non-essential parameters by decomposing weight matrices into products of two low-rank matrices. Yet, its application in LLMs has not been extensively studied. The key to low-rank compression lies in low-rank factorization and low-rank dimensions allocation. To address the challenges of low-rank compression in LLMs, we conduct empirical research on the low-rank characteristics of large models. We propose a low-rank compression method suitable for LLMs. This approach involves precise estimation of feature distributions through pooled covariance matrices and a Bayesian optimization strategy for allocating low-rank dimensions. Experiments on the LLaMA-2 models demonstrate that our method outperforms existing strong structured pruning and low-rank compression techniques in maintaining model performance at the same compression ratio.
中摘: 近年来，大型语言模型（LLM）推动了自然语言处理领域的进展。然而，这些模型规模的不断增长也加重了计算负担，因此需要在效率与性能之间找到平衡点。低秩压缩作为一种有前景的技术，通过将权重矩阵分解为两个低秩矩阵的乘积来减少非必要的参数。然而，该技术在LLM中的应用尚未得到广泛研究。低秩压缩的核心在于低秩分解和低秩维度分配。为了应对LLM中低秩压缩的挑战，我们对大规模模型的低秩特性进行了实证研究，并提出了一种适用于LLM的低秩压缩方法。该方法涉及通过汇聚协方差矩阵精确估计特征分布，并采用贝叶斯优化策略来分配低秩维度。在LLaMA-2模型上的实验表明，我们的方法在相同的压缩比下，相比现有强大的结构化剪枝和低秩压缩技术，在保持模型性能方面具有优势。

[No.502] AGRaME: Any-Granularity Ranking with Multi-Vector Embeddings
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: Any-Granularity 排序与多向量嵌入
类别: 
作者: Revanth Gangi Reddy;Omar Attia;Yunyao Li;Heng Ji;Saloni Potdar;
机构: University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;Apple;Adobe;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.490/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15028
英摘: Ranking is a fundamental and popular problem in search. However, existing ranking algorithms usually restrict the granularity of ranking to full passages or require a specific dense index for each desired level of granularity. Such lack of flexibility in granularity negatively affects many applications that can benefit from more granular ranking, such as sentence-level ranking for open-domain question-answering, or proposition-level ranking for attribution. In this work, we introduce the idea of any-granularity ranking, which leverages multi-vector embeddings to rank at varying levels of granularity while maintaining encoding at a single (coarser) level of granularity. We propose a multi-granular contrastive loss for training multi-vector approaches, and validate its utility with both sentences and propositions as ranking units. Finally, we demonstrate the application of proposition-level ranking to post-hoc citation addition in retrieval-augmented generation, surpassing the performance of prompt-driven citation generation.
中摘: 排名是在搜索中一个基本且流行的问题。然而，现有的排名算法通常将排名的粒度限制在全文档或需要为每个期望的粒度级别构建特定的稠密索引。这种在粒度上的灵活性缺乏对许多可以从更细粒度排名中获益的应用产生了负面影响，例如，对于开放域问题回答的句子级排名，或者对于归因的命题级排名。在这项工作中，我们引入了任意粒度排名的概念，它利用多向量嵌入在保持单一（较粗）粒度级别的编码的同时，在不同粒度级别进行排名。我们提出了一种多粒度对比损失函数来训练多向量方法，并通过将句子和命题作为排名单元验证了其有效性。最后，我们将命题级排名应用于检索增强生成中的事后引用添加，超越了提示驱动引用生成的性能。

[No.503] Centrality-aware Product Retrieval and Ranking
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: 基于中心性的产品检索与排序
类别: 
作者: Hadeel Saadany;Swapnil Bhosale;Samarth Agrawal;Diptesh Kanojia;Constantin Orasan;Zhe Wu;

英摘: This paper addresses the challenge of improving user experience on e-commerce platforms by enhancing product ranking relevant to users' search queries. Ambiguity and complexity of user queries often lead to a mismatch between the user's intent and retrieved product titles or documents. Recent approaches have proposed the use of Transformer-based models, which need millions of annotated query-title pairs during the pre-training stage, and this data often does not take user intent into account. To tackle this, we curate samples from existing datasets at eBay, manually annotated with buyer-centric relevance scores and centrality scores, which reflect how well the product title matches the users' intent. We introduce a User-intent Centrality Optimization (UCO) approach for existing models, which optimises for the user intent in semantic product search. To that end, we propose a dual-loss based optimisation to handle hard negatives, i.e., product titles that are semantically relevant but do not reflect the user's intent. Our contributions include curating challenging evaluation sets and implementing UCO, resulting in significant product ranking efficiency improvements observed for different evaluation metrics. Our work aims to ensure that the most buyer-centric titles for a query are ranked higher, thereby, enhancing the user experience on e-commerce platforms.
中摘: 本文针对通过优化电商平台上的产品排名以提升用户体验的挑战，特别是使产品排名更贴近用户的搜索需求。用户查询的模糊性和复杂性常常导致用户意图与检索到的产品标题或文档不匹配。最近的方法提出了使用基于Transformer的模型，在预训练阶段需要数百万个标注过的查询-标题对，但这些数据通常并未考虑到用户的实际意图。为了解决这个问题，我们从eBay现有的数据集中精心挑选样本，并人工标注了买家导向的相关性分数和中心性分数，这些分数反映了产品标题与用户意图的匹配程度。我们为现有模型引入了一种用户意图中心性优化（UCO）方法，该方法在语义产品搜索中优化用户意图。为此，我们提出了一种基于双损失函数的优化策略来处理硬负例，即那些在语义上相关，但并不反映用户意图的产品标题。我们的贡献包括创建具有挑战性的评估集以及实施UCO，这导致在不同的评价指标下观察到了显著的产品排名效率提升。我们的工作旨在确保每个查询下的最具买家导向的标题获得更高的排名，从而增强电商平台上用户的体验。

[No.504] Clustering and Ranking: Diversity-preserved Instruction Selection through Expert-aligned Quality Estimation
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: 聚类和排名：通过专家对齐的质量估计保持多样性的指令选择
类别: 
作者: Yuan Ge;Yilun Liu;Chi Hu;Weibin Meng;Shimin Tao;Xiaofeng Zhao;Mahong Xia;Zhang Li;Boxing Chen;Hao Yang;Bei Li;Tong Xiao;JingBo Zhu;

类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.28/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.18191
英摘: With contributions from the open-source community, a vast amount of instruction tuning (IT) data has emerged. Given the significant resource allocation required for training and evaluating models, it is advantageous to have an efficient method for selecting high-quality IT data. However, existing methods for instruction data selection have limitations such as relying on fragile external APIs, being affected by biases in GPT models, or reducing the diversity of the selected instruction dataset. In this paper, we propose an industrial-friendly, expert-aligned and diversity-preserved instruction data selection method: Clustering and Ranking (CaR). CaR employs a two-step process: first, it ranks instruction pairs using a high-accuracy (84.25%) scoring model aligned with expert preferences; second, it preserves dataset diversity through clustering. In our experiment, CaR efficiently selected a mere 1.96% of Alpaca's IT data, yet the resulting AlpaCaR model surpassed Alpaca's performance by an average of 32.1% in GPT-4 evaluations. Moreover, we find that data selecting is a consistent paradigm whether the pre-trained model is more capable or the model parameters scaling up. Our approach employs compact models with 550M parameters and incurs just 11.2% of the financial outlay of current methods, enhancing its industrial deployability.
中摘: 得益于开源社区的贡献，大量的指令调优（IT）数据应运而生。考虑到训练和评估模型所需的重大资源分配，拥有一种高效的方法来筛选高质量的IT数据变得有利。然而，现有的指令数据选择方法存在局限性，如依赖于脆弱的外部API、受到GPT模型偏见的影响，或降低了所选指令数据集的多样性。在本文中，我们提出了一种工业友好、与专家偏好一致且保持多样性的指令数据选择方法：聚类和排名（CaR）。CaR采用两步法：首先，使用与专家偏好对齐的高精度（84.25%）评分模型对指令对进行排名；其次，通过聚类保持数据集的多样性。在我们的实验中，CaR有效地从Alpaca的IT数据中仅选择了1.96%，但由此产生的AlpaCaR模型在GPT-4评估中的平均性能超过了Alpaca 32.1%。此外，我们发现，无论预训练模型的能力更强还是模型参数增加，数据选择都是一种一致的范式。我们的方法采用了5.5亿参数的紧凑型模型，并且仅花费了现有方法11.2%的成本，从而增强了其在工业领域的可部署性。

[No.505] Consolidating Ranking and Relevance Predictions of Large Language Models through Post-Processing
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: 通过后处理整合大型语言模型的排名和相关性预测
类别: 
作者: Le Yan;Zhen Qin;Honglei Zhuang;Rolf Jagerman;Xuanhui Wang;Michael Bendersky;Harrie Oosterhuis;
机构: Google;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.25/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.11791
英摘: The powerful generative abilities of large language models (LLMs) show potential in generating relevance labels for search applications. Previous work has found that directly asking about relevancy, such as ``How relevant is document A to query Q?", results in sub-optimal ranking. Instead, the pairwise ranking prompting (PRP) approach produces promising ranking performance through asking about pairwise comparisons, e.g., ``Is document A more relevant than document B to query Q?". Thus, while LLMs are effective at their ranking ability, this is not reflected in their relevance label generation. In this work, we propose a post-processing method to consolidate the relevance labels generated by an LLM with its powerful ranking abilities. Our method takes both LLM generated relevance labels and pairwise preferences. The labels are then altered to satisfy the pairwise preferences of the LLM, while staying as close to the original values as possible. Our experimental results indicate that our approach effectively balances label accuracy and ranking performance. Thereby, our work shows it is possible to combine both the ranking and labeling abilities of LLMs through post-processing.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLM）强大的生成能力在为搜索应用生成相关性标签方面展现出潜力。先前的研究发现，直接询问相关性，如“文档A与查询Q的相关性如何？”会导致次优的排名。相反，通过询问两两比较，例如“文档A相对于文档B对查询Q是否更相关？”的配对排名提示（PRP）方法，在排名性能上产生了有希望的结果。因此，尽管LLM在其排名能力上有效，但这并没有反映在其相关性标签生成上。在本工作中，我们提出了一种后处理方法，以整合LLM生成的相关性标签与其强大的排名能力。我们的方法同时考虑了LLM生成的相关性标签和两两偏好。然后调整这些标签以满足LLM的两两偏好，同时尽可能地保持与原始值接近。我们的实验结果表明，我们的方法有效地平衡了标签准确性和排名性能。因此，我们的工作证明了通过后处理结合LLM的排名和标签生成能力是可能的。

[No.506] DL-QAT: Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Quantization-Aware Training for Large Language Models
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: DL-QAT: 基于权重分解的低秩量化感知训练（用于大型语言模型）
类别: 
作者: Wenjing Ke;Zhe Li;Dong Li;Lu Tian;Emad Barsoum;
机构: Advanced Micro Devices, Inc.;
类型: Industry
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-industry.10/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.09223
英摘: Improving the efficiency of inference in Large Language Models (LLMs) is a critical area of research. Post-training Quantization (PTQ) is a popular technique, but it often faces challenges at low-bit levels, particularly in downstream tasks. Quantization-aware Training (QAT) can alleviate this problem, but it requires significantly more computational resources. To tackle this, we introduced Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Quantization-Aware Training (DL-QAT), which merges the advantages of QAT while training only less than 1% of the total parameters. Specifically, we introduce a group-specific quantization magnitude to adjust the overall scale of each quantization group. Within each quantization group, we use LoRA matrices to update the weight size and direction in the quantization space. We validated the effectiveness of our method on the LLaMA and LLaMA2 model families. The results show significant improvements over our baseline method across different quantization granularities. For instance, for LLaMA-7B, our approach outperforms the previous state-of-the-art method by 4.2% in MMLU on 3-bit LLaMA-7B model. Additionally, our quantization results on pre-trained models also surpass previous QAT methods, demonstrating the superior performance and efficiency of our approach.
中摘: 提高大型语言模型（LLMs）的推理效率是研究的关键领域。后训练量化（PTQ）是一种流行的技术，但在低位数时常常在下游任务中遇到挑战。量化感知训练（QAT）可以缓解这一问题，但它需要显著更多的计算资源。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了权重分解低秩量化感知训练（DL-QAT），它结合了QAT的优点，同时仅需训练不到总参数量的1%。具体而言，我们引入了一个组特异性的量化幅度来调整每个量化组的整体规模。在每个量化组内，我们使用LoRA矩阵来更新量化空间中的权重大小和方向。我们在LLaMA和LLaMA2模型家族上验证了我们方法的有效性。结果表明，在不同的量化粒度下，我们的方法相比基线方法有了显著改进。例如，对于LLaMA-7B，我们的方法在3位数的LLaMA-7B模型上的MMLU性能比之前最先进的方法高出4.2%。此外，我们对预训练模型的量化结果也超越了之前的QAT方法，证明了我们方法的卓越性能和效率。

[No.507] Efficient Pointwise-Pairwise Learning-to-Rank for News Recommendation
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: 高效的点对点学习排序方法在新闻推荐中的应用
类别: 
作者: Nithish Kannen;Yao Ma;Gerrit J.j. Van Den Burg;Jean Baptiste Faddoul;
机构: Amazon;Zalando SE;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.723/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.17711
英摘: News recommendation is a challenging task that involves personalization based on the interaction history and preferences of each user. Recent works have leveraged the power of pretrained language models (PLMs) to directly rank news items by using inference approaches that predominately fall into three categories: pointwise, pairwise, and listwise learning-to-rank. While pointwise methods offer linear inference complexity, they fail to capture crucial comparative information between items that is more effective for ranking tasks. Conversely, pairwise and listwise approaches excel at incorporating these comparisons but suffer from practical limitations: pairwise approaches are either computationally expensive or lack theoretical guarantees, and listwise methods often perform poorly in practice. In this paper, we propose a novel framework for PLM-based news recommendation that integrates both pointwise relevance prediction and pairwise comparisons in a scalable manner. We present a rigorous theoretical analysis of our framework, establishing conditions under which our approach guarantees improved performance. Extensive experiments show that our approach outperforms the state-of-the-art methods on the MIND and Adressa news recommendation datasets.
中摘: 新闻推荐是一个具有挑战性的任务，它涉及到根据每位用户的交互历史和偏好进行个性化。近期的研究工作利用了预训练语言模型（PLMs）的力量，通过主要归类于三种方法：点对点、对对比较和列表排序的推断方法，直接对新闻项目进行排名。虽然点对点方法提供了线性的推断复杂度，但它们未能捕捉在排名任务中更为有效的项目间的比较信息。相反，对对比较和列表排序的方法擅长整合这些比较信息，但受到实际限制：对对比较的方法要么计算成本高昂，要么缺乏理论保障，而列表排序的方法在实践中往往表现不佳。在这篇论文中，我们提出了一种新的基于PLM的新闻推荐框架，以可扩展的方式同时整合了点对点相关性预测和对对比较。我们对框架进行了严谨的理论分析，确立了我们的方法在特定条件下保证提升性能的条件。广泛的实验表明，我们的方法在MIND和Adressa新闻推荐数据集上超越了现有的最先进方法。

[No.508] Eigen Attention: Attention in Low-Rank Space for KV Cache Compression
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: Eigen注意力：低秩空间中的KV缓存压缩注意力机制
类别: 
作者: Utkarsh Saxena;Gobinda Saha;Sakshi Choudhary;Kaushik Roy;
机构: Purdue University;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.899/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.05646
英摘: Large language models (LLMs) represent a groundbreaking advancement in the domain of natural language processing due to their impressive reasoning abilities. Recently, there has been considerable interest in increasing the context lengths for these models to enhance their applicability to complex tasks. However, at long context lengths and large batch sizes, the key-value (KV) cache, which stores the attention keys and values, emerges as the new bottleneck in memory usage during inference. To address this, we propose Eigen Attention, which performs the attention operation in a low-rank space, thereby reducing the KV cache memory overhead. Our proposed approach is orthogonal to existing KV cache compression techniques and can be used synergistically with them. Through extensive experiments over OPT, MPT, and Llama model families, we demonstrate that Eigen Attention results in up to 40% reduction in KV cache sizes and up to 60% reduction in attention operation latency with minimal drop in performance. Code is available at https://github.com/UtkarshSaxena1/EigenAttn.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLM）因其卓越的推理能力，在自然语言处理领域代表了开创性的进展。最近，人们对于增加这些模型的上下文长度产生了浓厚兴趣，以增强其在复杂任务中的适用性。然而，在长上下文长度和大批次大小的情况下，存储注意力键值对的缓存（KV缓存），在推理过程中成为了新的内存使用瓶颈。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了Eigen Attention，它在低秩空间中执行注意力操作，从而降低了KV缓存的内存开销。我们提出的方法与现有的KV缓存压缩技术相辅相成，可以协同使用。通过在OPT、MPT和Llama模型家族上的广泛实验，我们证明了Eigen Attention能够减少高达40%的KV缓存大小，以及最多降低60%的注意力操作延迟，同时性能下降极小。代码可在https://github.com/UtkarshSaxena1/EigenAttn获取。

[No.509] Enhancing Alignment using Curriculum Learning & Ranked Preferences
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: 使用课程学习与排序偏好增强对齐
类别: 
作者: Pulkit Pattnaik;Rishabh Maheshwary;Kelechi Ogueji;Vikas Yadav;Sathwik Tejaswi Madhusudhan;
机构: ServiceNow;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.754/

英摘: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) is an effective technique that leverages pairwise preference data (usually one chosen and rejected response pair per user prompt) to align LLMs to human preferences. In practice, multiple responses can exist for a given prompt with varying quality relative to each other. With availability of such quality ratings for multiple responses, we propose utilizing these responses to create multiple preference pairs for a given prompt. Our work focuses on systematically using the constructed multiple preference pair in DPO training via curriculum learning methodology. In particular, we order these multiple pairs of preference data from easy to hard (emulating curriculum training) according to various criteria. We show detailed comparisons of our proposed approach to the standard single-pair DPO setting. Our method, which we call Curry-DPO consistently shows increased performance gains on MTbench, Vicuna, WizardLM, and the UltraFeedback test set, highlighting its effectiveness. More specifically, Curry-DPO achieves a score of 7.43 on MT-bench with Zephy-7B model outperforming majority of existing LLMs with similar parameter size. Curry-DPO also achieves the highest adjusted win rates on Vicuna, WizardLM, and UltraFeedback test datasets (90.7%, 87.1%, and 87.9% respectively) in our experiments, with notable gains of upto 7.5% when compared to standard DPO technique. We release the preference pairs used in alignment at: https://huggingface.co/datasets/ServiceNow-AI/Curriculum_DPO_preferences
中摘: 直接偏好优化（DPO）是一种有效技术，利用两两偏好数据（通常每个用户提示选择和拒绝一个响应对）来使大型语言模型与人类偏好保持一致。在实践中，对于特定的提示可能存在多个响应，这些响应相对于彼此的质量各不相同。鉴于有这类质量评分的多个响应存在，我们提议利用这些响应为特定的提示创建多对偏好数据。我们的工作重点是通过课程学习方法系统地在DPO训练中使用构建的多对偏好数据。具体而言，我们根据各种标准将这些偏好数据对从易到难排序（模拟课程训练）。我们展示了我们的方法与标准单对DPO设置之间详细的比较。我们称之为Curry-DPO的方法，在MTbench、Vicuna、WizardLM以及UltraFeedback测试集上持续展现出性能提升，突显了其有效性。更具体地说，Curry-DPO在使用Zephy-7B模型的MT-bench上取得了7.43分的成绩，超越了大多数参数规模相似的现有大型语言模型。在我们的实验中，Curry-DPO在Vicuna、WizardLM以及UltraFeedback测试数据集上也实现了最高的调整后胜率（分别为90.7%，87.1%，和87.9%），与标准DPO技术相比，显著提高了最多7.5%的胜率。我们发布用于对齐的偏好对数据于：https://huggingface.co/datasets/ServiceNow-AI/Curriculum_DPO_preferences

[No.511] Exploring Automated Keyword Mnemonics Generation with Large Language Models via Overgenerate-and-Rank
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: 通过过生成和排序方法探索大规模语言模型在自动生成关键词助记词方面的应用
类别: 
作者: Jaewook Lee;Hunter McNichols;Andrew Lan;
机构: University of Massachusetts Amherst;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.316/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.13952
英摘: In this paper, we study an under-explored area of language and vocabulary learning: keyword mnemonics, a technique for memorizing vocabulary through memorable associations with a target word via a verbal cue. Typically, creating verbal cues requires extensive human effort and is quite time-consuming, necessitating an automated method that is more scalable. We propose a novel overgenerate-and-rank method via prompting large language models (LLMs) to generate verbal cues and then ranking them according to psycholinguistic measures and takeaways from a pilot user study. To assess cue quality, we conduct both an automated evaluation of imageability and coherence, as well as a human evaluation involving English teachers and learners. Results show that LLM-generated mnemonics are comparable to human-generated ones in terms of imageability, coherence, and perceived usefulness, but there remains plenty of room for improvement due to the diversity in background and preference among language learners.
中摘: 在本文中，我们研究了一个在语言和词汇学习领域中探索不足的领域：关键词助记法，这是一种通过口头提示与目标单词建立难忘联系来记忆词汇的技术。通常，创建口头提示需要大量的人力投入，并且相当耗时，因此需要一种更可扩展的自动化方法。我们提出了一种新颖的过生成和排序方法，通过提示大型语言模型（LLM）生成口头提示，然后根据心理语言学指标以及试点用户研究的反馈对它们进行排名。为了评估提示的质量，我们进行了自动评估，包括形象性和连贯性，同时也进行了涉及英语教师和学习者的人工评估。结果显示，在形象性、连贯性和感知的实用性方面，LLM生成的助记法与人工生成的助记法相当，但由于语言学习者背景和偏好的多样性，仍有很大的改进空间。

类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.359/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.13945
英摘: API call generation is the cornerstone of large language models' tool-using ability that provides access to the larger world. However, existing supervised and in-context learning approaches suffer from high training costs, poor data efficiency, and generated API calls that can be unfaithful to the API documentation and the user's request. To address these limitations, we propose an output-side optimization approach called FANTASE. Two of the unique contributions of FANTASE are its State-Tracked Constrained Decoding (SCD) and Reranking components. SCD dynamically incorporates appropriate API constraints in the form of Token Search Trie for efficient and guaranteed generation faithfulness with respect to the API documentation. The Reranking component efficiently brings in the supervised signal by leveraging a lightweight model as the discriminator to rerank the beam-searched candidate generations of the large language model. We demonstrate the superior performance of FANTASE in API call generation accuracy, inference efficiency, and context efficiency with DSTC8 and API Bank datasets.
中摘: API调用生成是大型语言模型工具使用能力的基石，为访问更广阔的世界提供了途径。然而，现有的监督学习和情境学习方法面临着高昂的训练成本、数据效率低下以及生成的API调用可能与API文档和用户请求不符的问题。为了解决这些局限性，我们提出了一种称为FANTASE的输出侧优化方法。FANTASE的两个独特贡献是其状态跟踪约束解码（SCD）和重排序组件。SCD以Token Search Trie的形式动态融入适当的API约束，以实现对API文档高效且有保证的生成忠实度。重排序组件通过利用轻量级模型作为判别器来重新排序大型语言模型的束搜索候选生成，从而高效地引入监督信号。我们在DSTC8和API Bank数据集上展示了FANTASE在API调用生成准确性、推理效率和上下文效率方面的优越性能。

[No.513] Fast Forwarding Low-Rank Training
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: 低秩训练快进
类别: 
作者: Adir Rahamim;Naomi Saphra;Sara Kangaslahti;Yonatan Belinkov;
机构: Technion – Israel Institute of Technology;Harvard University;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.535/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.04206
英摘: Parameter efficient finetuning methods like low-rank adaptation (LoRA) aim to reduce the computational costs of finetuning pretrained Language Models (LMs). Enabled by these low-rank settings, we propose an even more efficient optimization strategy: Fast Forward, a simple and effective approach to accelerate large segments of training. In a Fast Forward stage, we repeat the most recent optimizer step until the loss stops improving on a tiny validation set. By alternating between regular optimization steps and Fast Forward stages, Fast Forward provides up to an 87\% reduction in FLOPs and up to an 81\% reduction in train time over standard SGD with Adam. We validate Fast Forward by finetuning various models on different tasks and demonstrate that it speeds up training without compromising model performance. Additionally, we analyze when and how to apply Fast Forward.
中摘: 像低秩适应（LoRA）这样的参数高效微调方法旨在降低对预训练语言模型（LMs）进行微调的计算成本。借助这些低秩设置，我们提出了一种更为高效的优化策略：快速前进，这是一种简单而有效的方法，可以加速训练的大部分阶段。在快速前进阶段，我们重复最近的优化器步骤，直到在小型验证集上的损失不再改善为止。通过在标准优化步骤和快速前进阶段之间交替进行，快速前进相比传统的随机梯度下降（SGD）与Adam结合使用的情况下，最多可减少87%的浮点运算数（FLOPs），最多可减少81%的训练时间。我们通过对不同任务上各种模型进行微调来验证快速前进，并证明它可以在不牺牲模型性能的前提下加速训练。此外，我们还分析了何时以及如何应用快速前进。

[No.514] Few-shot Prompting for Pairwise Ranking: An Effective Non-Parametric Retrieval Model
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题:  few-shot 提示在对偶排名中的应用：一种有效的非参数检索模型
类别: 
作者: Nilanjan Sinhababu;Andrew Parry;Debasis Ganguly;Debasis Samanta;Pabitra Mitra;
机构: Indian Institute of Technology Kharagpur;University of Glasgow;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.720/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.17745
英摘: A supervised ranking model, despite its advantage of being effective, usually involves complex processing - typically multiple stages of task-specific pre-training and fine-tuning. This has motivated researchers to explore simpler pipelines leveraging large language models (LLMs) that are capable of working in a zero-shot manner. However, since zero-shot inference does not make use of a training set of pairs of queries and their relevant documents, its performance is mostly worse than that of supervised models, which are trained on such example pairs. Motivated by the existing findings that training examples generally improve zero-shot performance, in our work, we explore if this also applies to ranking models. More specifically, given a query and a pair of documents, the preference prediction task is improved by augmenting examples of preferences for similar queries from a training set. Our proposed pairwise few-shot ranker demonstrates consistent improvements over the zero-shot baseline on both in-domain (TREC DL) and out-domain (BEIR subset) retrieval benchmarks. Our method also achieves a close performance to that of a supervised model without requiring any complex training pipeline.
中摘: 监督排名模型，尽管其效果显著，通常涉及复杂的处理流程——通常包括针对特定任务的预训练和微调等多个阶段。这促使研究者探索利用大型语言模型（LLM）的更简单流程，这些模型能够以零样本方式工作。然而，由于零样本推理不利用查询及其相关文档的训练集对，其性能大多逊色于在这样的示例对上训练的有监督模型。受现有发现的启发，即训练示例通常能提升零样本性能，我们的工作探讨了这一规律是否同样适用于排名模型。具体而言，对于一个查询和一对文档，通过从训练集中添加类似查询的偏好示例，偏好预测任务得到改进。我们提出的配对少量样本排名器在领域内（TREC DL）和领域外（BEIR子集）检索基准上均展现出相对于零样本基线的一致性提升。此外，我们的方法在无需复杂训练流程的情况下，实现了与有监督模型相近的性能。

[No.515] FIRST: Faster Improved Listwise Reranking with Single Token Decoding
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: 第一：更快的单词解码改进列表级重排序
类别: 
作者: Revanth Gangi Reddy;JaeHyeok Doo;Yifei Xu;Md Arafat Sultan;Deevya Swain;Avirup Sil;Heng Ji;
机构: University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;Lapis Labs;IBM;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.491/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.15657
英摘: Large Language Models (LLMs) have significantly advanced the field of information retrieval, particularly for reranking. Listwise LLM rerankers have showcased superior performance and generalizability compared to existing supervised approaches. However, conventional listwise LLM reranking methods lack efficiency as they provide ranking output in the form of a generated ordered sequence of candidate passage identifiers. Further, they are trained with the typical language modeling objective, which treats all ranking errors uniformly--potentially at the cost of misranking highly relevant passages. Addressing these limitations, we introduce FIRST, a novel listwise LLM reranking approach leveraging the output logits of the first generated identifier to directly obtain a ranked ordering of the candidates. Further, we incorporate a learning-to-rank loss during training, prioritizing ranking accuracy for the more relevant passages. Empirical results demonstrate that FIRST accelerates inference by 50% while maintaining a robust ranking performance with gains across the BEIR benchmark. Finally, to illustrate the practical effectiveness of listwise LLM rerankers, we investigate their application in providing relevance feedback for retrievers during inference. Our results show that LLM rerankers can provide a stronger distillation signal compared to cross-encoders, yielding substantial improvements in retriever recall after relevance feedback.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLM）在信息检索领域，特别是在重排序方面取得了显著进展。列表式的LLM重排序器相较于现有的监督方法展现了更优的性能和泛化能力。然而，传统的列表式LLM重排序方法在效率上存在不足，因为它们以生成候选段落标识符的有序序列形式提供排名输出。此外，它们采用典型的语言建模目标进行训练，该目标将所有排名错误同等对待——这可能会以牺牲高度相关段落的正确排序为代价。针对这些局限性，我们引入了FIRST，这是一种创新的列表式LLM重排序方法，利用生成的第一个标识符的输出logit直接获得候选对象的排名顺序。进一步地，我们在训练过程中融入了学习排序损失，优先确保更相关段落的排名准确性。实证结果表明，FIRST可以加速推理过程50%，同时保持稳健的排名表现，并在BEIR基准测试中取得了一致的提升。最后，为了展示列表式LLM重排序器的实际有效性，我们研究了它们在推理期间为检索器提供相关性反馈的应用。我们的结果显示，LLM重排序器能提供比交叉编码器更强的蒸馏信号，从而在相关性反馈后，显著提高检索器的召回率。

[No.518] HyQE: Ranking Contexts with Hypothetical Query Embeddings
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: 基于假设查询嵌入的上下文排名
类别: 
作者: Weichao Zhou;Jiaxin Zhang;Hilaf Hasson;Anu Singh;Wenchao Li;
机构: Boston University;Intuit;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.761/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.15262
英摘: In retrieval-augmented systems, context ranking techniques are commonly employed to reorder the retrieved contexts based on their relevance to a user query. A standard approach is to measure this relevance through the similarity between contexts and queries in the embedding space. However, such similarity often fails to capture the relevance. Alternatively, large language models (LLMs) have been used for ranking contexts. However, they can encounter scalability issues when the number of candidate contexts grows and the context window sizes of the LLMs remain constrained. Additionally, these approaches require fine-tuning LLMs with domain-specific data. In this work, we introduce a scalable ranking framework that combines embedding similarity and LLM capabilities without requiring LLM fine-tuning. Our framework uses a pre-trained LLM to hypothesize the user query based on the retrieved contexts and ranks the context based on the similarity between the hypothesized queries and the user query. Our framework is efficient at inference time and is compatible with many other retrieval and ranking techniques. Experimental results show that our method improves the ranking performance across multiple benchmarks. The complete code and data are available at https://github.com/zwc662/hyqe
中摘: 在检索增强系统中，通常采用上下文排序技术来根据与用户查询的相关性重新排序检索到的上下文。一种标准方法是通过测量嵌入空间中上下文和查询之间的相似度来评估这种相关性。然而，这种相似度往往无法准确捕捉相关性。作为替代方案，大规模语言模型（LLMs）已被用于上下文排序。但是，当候选上下文数量增加且LLMs的上下文窗口大小受限时，它们可能会遇到可扩展性问题。此外，这些方法需要使用领域特定数据对LLMs进行微调。在这项工作中，我们引入了一个可扩展的排序框架，它结合了嵌入相似度和LLM的能力，而无需对LLMs进行微调。我们的框架利用预训练的LLM根据检索到的上下文推测用户查询，并基于推测查询与用户查询之间的相似度对上下文进行排序。我们的框架在推理时间效率高，且与许多其他检索和排序技术兼容。实验结果显示，我们的方法在多个基准测试上提高了排序性能。完整的代码和数据可在https://github.com/zwc662/hyqe获取。

[No.520] Learning to Rank Salient Content for Query-focused Summarization
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: 查询聚焦的内容排序与摘要生成学习
类别: 
作者: Sajad Sotudeh;Nazli Goharian;
机构: Georgetown University;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.838/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.00324
英摘: This study examines the potential of integrating Learning-to-Rank (LTR) with Query-focused Summarization (QFS) to enhance the summary relevance via content prioritization. Using a shared secondary decoder with the summarization decoder, we carry out the LTR task at the segment level. Compared to the state-of-the-art, our model outperforms on QMSum benchmark (all metrics) and matches on SQuALITY benchmark (2 metrics) as measured by Rouge and BertScore while offering a lower training overhead. Specifically, on the QMSum benchmark, our proposed system achieves improvements, particularly in Rouge-L (+0.42) and BertScore (+0.34), indicating enhanced understanding and relevance. While facing minor challenges in Rouge-1 and Rouge-2 scores on the SQuALITY benchmark, the model significantly excels in Rouge-L (+1.47), underscoring its capability to generate coherent summaries. Human evaluations emphasize the efficacy of our method in terms of relevance and faithfulness of the generated summaries, without sacrificing fluency. A deeper analysis reveals our model's superiority over the state-of-the-art for broad queries, as opposed to specific ones, from a qualitative standpoint. We further present an error analysis of our model, pinpointing challenges faced and suggesting potential directions for future research in this field.
中摘: 本研究探讨了将学习排序（LTR）与面向查询的摘要（QFS）相结合的潜力，通过内容优先级提高摘要的相关性。我们使用与摘要解码器共享的次级解码器，在片段级别上执行LTR任务。与最先进技术相比，我们的模型在QMSum基准测试中所有指标上都表现出色，并在SQuALITY基准测试中的两项指标上相匹配，这是通过Rouge和BertScore衡量的，同时提供了更低的训练开销。具体而言，在QMSum基准测试上，我们提出的方法取得了改进，特别是在Rouge-L（+0.42）和BertScore（+0.34）方面，这表明了增强的理解能力和相关性。虽然在SQuALITY基准测试的Rouge-1和Rouge-2分数上遇到了轻微挑战，但该模型在Rouge-L（+1.47）上显著胜出，强调了其生成连贯摘要的能力。人工评估强调了我们方法在生成摘要的相关性和忠实度方面的有效性，而不会牺牲流畅性。更深入的分析显示，从定性的角度来看，对于广泛查询而非特定查询，我们的模型优于最先进技术。我们还展示了我们模型的错误分析，指出了面临的挑战并为该领域的未来研究提出了潜在方向。

[No.521] Leveraging Estimated Transferability Over Human Intuition for Model Selection in Text Ranking
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: 利用估计的转移性而非人类直觉进行文本排序模型选择
类别: 
作者: Jun Bai;Zhuofan Chen;Zhenzi Li;Hanhua Hong;Jianfei Zhang;Chen Li;Chenghua Lin;Wenge Rong;
机构: Beihang University;University of Manchester;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.688/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.16198
英摘: Text ranking has witnessed significant advancements, attributed to the utilization of dual-encoder enhanced by Pre-trained Language Models (PLMs). Given the proliferation of available PLMs, selecting the most effective one for a given dataset has become a non-trivial challenge. As a promising alternative to human intuition and brute-force fine-tuning, Transferability Estimation (TE) has emerged as an effective approach to model selection. However, current TE methods are primarily designed for classification tasks, and their estimated transferability may not align well with the objectives of text ranking. To address this challenge, we propose to compute the expected rank as transferability, explicitly reflecting the model's ranking capability. Furthermore, to mitigate anisotropy and incorporate training dynamics, we adaptively scale isotropic sentence embeddings to yield an accurate expected rank score. Our resulting method, Adaptive Ranking Transferability (AiRTran), can effectively capture subtle differences between models. On challenging model selection scenarios across various text ranking datasets, it demonstrates significant improvements over previous classification-oriented TE methods, human intuition, and ChatGPT with minor time consumption.
中摘: 文本排名领域见证了显著的进步，这归功于预训练语言模型（PLM）增强的双编码器的应用。鉴于可选PLM的大量涌现，为特定数据集选择最有效的模型已成为一个非平凡的挑战。作为对人类直觉和暴力微调的一种有前景的替代方案，迁移性估计（TE）作为一种有效的模型选择方法应运而生。然而，当前的TE方法主要针对分类任务设计，它们所估计的迁移性可能与文本排名的目标不太一致。为了解决这一挑战，我们提出将预期排名作为迁移性计算，明确反映模型的排名能力。此外，为了缓解各向异性和融入训练动态，我们自适应地缩放等方差句子嵌入，以获得准确的预期排名分数。我们的方法，即自适应排名迁移性（AiRTran），能够有效地捕捉模型之间的细微差异。在各种文本排名数据集上的具有挑战性的模型选择场景中，它展示了相对于以往面向分类的TE方法、人类直觉以及消耗时间较少的ChatGPT的显著改进。

[No.522] LINKAGE: Listwise Ranking among Varied-Quality References for Non-Factoid QA Evaluation via LLMs
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: 链路：通过大型语言模型（LLMs）对 varied-quality 参考进行逐项排名以评估非事实型问答（Non-Factoid QA）
类别: 
作者: Sihui Yang;Keping Bi;Wanqing Cui;Jiafeng Guo;Xueqi Cheng;
机构: Chinese Academy of Sciences;University of Chinese Academy of Sciences;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.410/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.14744
英摘: Non-Factoid (NF) Question Answering (QA) is challenging to evaluate due to diverse potential answers and no objective criterion. The commonly used automatic evaluation metrics like ROUGE or BERTScore cannot accurately measure semantic similarities or answers from different perspectives. Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have been resorted to for NFQA evaluation due to their compelling performance on various NLP tasks. Common approaches include pointwise scoring of each candidate answer and pairwise comparisons between answers. Inspired by the evolution from pointwise to pairwise to listwise in learning-to-rank methods, we propose a novel listwise NFQA evaluation approach, that utilizes LLMs to rank candidate answers in a list of reference answers sorted by descending quality. Moreover, for NF questions that do not have multi-grade or any golden answers, we leverage LLMs to generate the reference answer list of various quality to facilitate the listwise evaluation. Extensive experimental results on three NFQA datasets, i.e., ANTIQUE, the TREC-DL-NF, and WebGLM show that our method has significantly higher correlations with human annotations compared to automatic scores and common pointwise and pairwise approaches.
中摘: 非事实性（NF）问题回答（QA）由于潜在答案的多样性和缺乏客观标准而难以评估。常用的自动评估指标，如ROUGE或BERTScore，无法准确衡量语义相似性或从不同角度给出的答案。最近，大型语言模型（LLMs）因其在各种自然语言处理（NLP）任务中的出色表现，被用于NFQA的评估。常用的方法包括对每个候选答案进行点对点评分和答案之间的成对比较。受排序学习方法从点对点到成对再到列表式发展的启发，我们提出了一种新颖的列表式NFQA评估方法，利用LLMs在按质量降序排列的参考答案列表中对候选答案进行排名。此外，对于没有多级或任何黄金标准答案的NF问题，我们利用LLMs生成不同质量级别的参考答案列表，以促进列表式的评估。在三个NFQA数据集上的广泛实验结果，即ANTIQUE、TREC-DL-NF和WebGLM表明，与自动评分和常见的点对点及成对比较方法相比，我们的方法与人工注释的相关性显著更高。

[No.526] LoRASC: Expressive and Generalizable Low-rank Adaptation for Large Models via Slow Cascaded Learning

[No.527] Make Large Language Model a Better Ranker
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: 提升大型语言模型的排名能力
类别: 
作者: Wen-Shuo Chao;Zhi Zheng;Hengshu Zhu;Hao Liu;
机构: Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;University of Science and Technology of China;Chinese Academy of Sciences;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.51/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.19181
英摘: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate robust capabilities across various fields, leading to a paradigm shift in LLM-enhanced Recommender System (RS). Research to date focuses on point-wise and pair-wise recommendation paradigms, which are inefficient for LLM-based recommenders due to high computational costs. However, existing list-wise approaches also fall short in ranking tasks due to misalignment between ranking objectives and next-token prediction. Moreover, these LLM-based methods struggle to effectively address the order relation among candidates, particularly given the scale of ratings. To address these challenges, this paper introduces the large language model framework with Aligned Listwise Ranking Objectives (ALRO). ALRO is designed to bridge the gap between the capabilities of LLMs and the nuanced requirements of ranking tasks. Specifically, ALRO employs explicit feedback in a listwise manner by introducing soft lambda loss, a customized adaptation of lambda loss designed for optimizing order relations. This mechanism provides more accurate optimization goals, enhancing the ranking process. Additionally, ALRO incorporates a permutation-sensitive learning mechanism that addresses position bias, a prevalent issue in generative models, without imposing additional computational burdens during inference. Our evaluative studies reveal that ALRO outperforms both existing embedding-based recommendation methods and LLM-based recommendation baselines.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLM）在各个领域展现出强大的能力，引领了LLM增强型推荐系统（RS）的范式转变。迄今为止的研究主要集中在点对点和成对推荐范式上，这些方法对于基于LLM的推荐器来说效率低下，因为计算成本高昂。然而，现有的列表级方法在排序任务上也存在不足，由于排名目标与下一个令牌预测之间的不一致。此外，这些基于LLM的方法难以有效处理候选者间的顺序关系，特别是在评级规模庞大的情况下。为了解决这些问题，本文引入了一种带有对齐列表级排名目标（ALRO）的大型语言模型框架。ALRO旨在弥合LLM的能力与排名任务的细微需求之间的差距。具体而言，ALRO通过引入软lambda损失，一种专为优化顺序关系设计的lambda损失定制版，以列表方式运用显式反馈。这种机制提供了更准确的优化目标，增强了排名过程。此外，ALRO结合了一个排列敏感的学习机制，该机制在推断过程中不增加额外计算负担的情况下解决了生成模型中常见的位置偏置问题。我们的评估研究表明，ALRO在性能上超越了现有的基于嵌入的推荐方法以及基于LLM的推荐基线。

[No.528] MERLIN: Multimodal Embedding Refinement via LLM-based Iterative Navigation for Text-Video Retrieval-Rerank Pipeline
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: 梅林：基于LLM迭代导航的多模态嵌入精炼用于文本-视频检索再排序管道
类别: 
作者: Donghoon Han;Eunhwan Park;Gisang Lee;Adam Lee;Nojun Kwak;
机构: Buzzni;Korea Advanced Institute of Science and Technology;Mathpresso Inc.;University of California, Berkeley;Fainders AI;Seoul National University;
类型: Industry
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-industry.41/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.12508
英摘: The rapid expansion of multimedia content has made accurately retrieving relevant videos from large collections increasingly challenging. Recent advancements in text-video retrieval have focused on cross-modal interactions, large-scale foundation model training, and probabilistic modeling, yet often neglect the crucial user perspective, leading to discrepancies between user queries and the content retrieved. To address this, we introduce MERLIN (Multimodal Embedding Refinement via LLM-based Iterative Navigation), a novel, training-free pipeline that leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) for iterative feedback learning. MERLIN refines query embeddings from a user perspective, enhancing alignment between queries and video content through a dynamic question answering process. Experimental results on datasets like MSR-VTT, MSVD, and ActivityNet demonstrate that MERLIN substantially improves Recall@1, outperforming existing systems and confirming the benefits of integrating LLMs into multimodal retrieval systems for more responsive and context-aware multimedia retrieval.
中摘: 多媒体内容的迅速扩展使得从大型集合中准确检索相关视频变得日益具有挑战性。近期在文本-视频检索领域的发展主要集中在跨模态交互、大规模基础模型训练和概率建模上，但往往忽视了至关重要的用户视角，导致用户查询与检索到的内容之间存在差异。为了解决这一问题，我们引入了MERLIN（通过基于大语言模型的迭代导航进行多模态嵌入精炼），这是一种创新的、无需训练的管道，它利用大型语言模型（LLMs）进行迭代反馈学习。MERLIN从用户的角度细化查询嵌入，通过动态问答过程增强查询与视频内容之间的对齐，从而提高匹配度。在MSR-VTT、MSVD和ActivityNet等数据集上的实验结果表明，MERLIN显著提高了Recall@1指标，超越了现有系统，证实了将LLMs整合到多模态检索系统中的优势，实现了更灵敏且更具情境感知能力的多媒体检索。

[No.529] mGTE: Generalized Long-Context Text Representation and Reranking Models for Multilingual Text Retrieval
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: mGTE：通用长上下文文本表示和重排序模型，用于多语言文本检索
类别: 
作者: Xin Zhang;Yanzhao Zhang;Dingkun Long;Wen Xie;Ziqi Dai;Jialong Tang;Huan Lin;Baosong Yang;Pengjun Xie;Fei Huang;Meishan Zhang;Wenjie Li;Min Zhang;
机构: Alibaba Group;Hong Kong Polytechnic University;
类型: Industry
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-industry.103/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.19669
英摘: We present systematic efforts in building long-context multilingual text representation model (TRM) and reranker from scratch for text retrieval. We first introduce a text encoder (base size) enhanced with RoPE and unpadding, pre-trained in a native 8192-token context (longer than 512 of previous multilingual encoders). Then we construct a hybrid TRM and a cross-encoder reranker by contrastive learning. Evaluations show that our text encoder outperforms the same-sized previous state-of-the-art XLM-R. Meanwhile, our TRM and reranker match the performance of large-sized state-of-the-art BGE-M3 models and achieve better results on long-context retrieval benchmarks. Further analysis demonstrate that our proposed models exhibit higher efficiency during both training and inference. We believe their efficiency and effectiveness could benefit various researches and industrial applications.
中摘: 我们从零开始构建长上下文多语言文本表示模型（Text Representation Model，简称TRM）和重排序器，用于文本检索。首先，我们介绍了一种增强版的文本编码器（基础大小），该编码器采用了旋转位置嵌入（RoPE）和取消填充（unpadding）技术，在原生8192令牌上下文中进行了预训练，这比之前多语言编码器的512令牌上下文更长。然后，通过对比学习，我们构建了一个混合TRM和交叉编码器重排序器。评估表明，我们的文本编码器在相同大小的情况下超越了先前最先进的XLM-R。同时，我们的TRM和重排序器与大型最先进的BGE-M3模型性能相当，并且在长上下文检索基准测试上取得了更好的结果。进一步的分析证明，我们提出的模型在训练和推理过程中具有更高的效率。我们相信，这些模型的高效性和有效性将对各种研究和工业应用产生积极影响。

[No.530] MiLoRA: Efficient Mixture of Low-Rank Adaptation for Large Language Models Fine-tuning
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: MiLoRA：高效低秩适应混合用于大型语言模型微调
类别: 
作者: Jingfan Zhang;Yi Zhao;Dan Chen;Xing Tian;Huanran Zheng;Wei Zhu;
机构: iFLYTEK Co., Ltd;University of Pennsylvania;Lenovo Connect Co., Ltd;Niuxin Network Technology Co., Ltd;East China Normal University;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.994/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18035
英摘: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) and its mixture-of-experts (MOE) variants are highly effective parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods. However, they introduce significant latency in multi-tenant settings due to the LoRA modules and MOE routers added to multiple linear modules in the Transformer layer. To address this issue, we propose Mixture of Low-Rank Adaptation (MiLoRA), a novel and efficient LoRA variant. MiLoRA differs from previous MOE-style LoRA methods by considering each LoRA module as an expert and employing a prompt-aware routing mechanism. This mechanism calculates expert routing results once before generating the first new token and reuses these results for subsequent tokens, reducing latency. Extensive experiments and analysis on commonsense reasoning tasks, math reasoning tasks, and widely used LLM evaluation benchmarks demonstrate that MiLoRA consistently outperforms strong PEFT baselines with comparable tunable parameter budgets. Additionally, MiLoRA significantly reduces latency in multi-tenant settings compared to previous LoRA-based methods.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）及其专家混合（MOE）变体是极其有效的参数高效微调（PEFT）方法。然而，在多租户环境下，由于在Transformer层的多个线性模块中添加了LoRA模块和MOE路由器，它们会引入显著的延迟。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了“低秩适应的专家混合”（MiLoRA），这是一种新颖且高效的LoRA变体。MiLoRA与以往的MOE风格LoRA方法不同，它将每个LoRA模块视为一个专家，并采用了一种基于提示的路由机制。该机制在生成第一个新令牌之前仅计算一次专家路由结果，并在后续令牌中重用这些结果，从而减少了延迟。在常识推理任务、数学推理任务以及广泛使用的大型语言模型评估基准上的大量实验和分析表明，MiLoRA在可调参数预算相似的情况下，始终超越强大的PEFT基线。此外，与以往基于LoRA的方法相比，MiLoRA在多租户设置下显著降低了延迟。

[No.531] Mixture-of-Subspaces in Low-Rank Adaptation
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: 低秩适应中的子空间混合
类别: 
作者: Taiqiang Wu;Jiahao Wang;Zhe Zhao;Ngai Wong;
机构: University of Hong Kong;Tencent;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.450/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.11909
英摘: In this paper, we introduce a subspace-inspired Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) method, which is computationally efficient, easy to implement, and readily applicable to large language, multimodal, and diffusion models. Initially, we equivalently decompose the weights of LoRA into two subspaces, and find that simply mixing them can enhance performance. To study such a phenomenon, we revisit it through a fine-grained subspace lens, showing that such modification is equivalent to employing a fixed mixer to fuse the subspaces. To be more flexible, we jointly learn the mixer with the original LoRA weights, and term the method Mixture-of-Subspaces LoRA (MoSLoRA). MoSLoRA consistently outperforms LoRA on tasks in different modalities, including commonsense reasoning, visual instruction tuning, and subject-driven text-to-image generation, demonstrating its effectiveness and robustness. Codes are available at https://github.com/wutaiqiang/MoSLoRA.
中摘: 在本文中，我们引入了一种受子空间启发的低秩适应（LoRA）方法，该方法计算效率高，易于实现，并且可以方便地应用于大型语言、多模态和扩散模型。起初，我们将LoRA的权重等效分解为两个子空间，并发现仅仅混合它们就可以增强性能。为了研究这种现象，我们通过精细的子空间视角重新审视它，表明这种修改相当于使用一个固定的混合器来融合子空间。为了更加灵活，我们与原始LoRA权重联合学习混合器，并将这种方法称为子空间混合LoRA（MoSLoRA）。在不同模态的任务上，MoSLoRA始终优于LoRA，包括常识推理、视觉指令调整以及主题驱动的文字转图片生成，这证明了其有效性和鲁棒性。代码可在https://github.com/wutaiqiang/MoSLoRA获取。

[No.533] Persuasiveness of Generated Free-Text Rationales in Subjective Decisions: A Case Study on Pairwise Argument Ranking
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: 生成的自由文本理由在主观决策中的说服力：一项关于成对论证排名的研究案例
类别: 
作者: Mohamed Elaraby;Diane Litman;Xiang Lorraine Li;Ahmed Magooda;
机构: University of Pittsburgh;Microsoft;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.836/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.13905
英摘: Generating free-text rationales is among the emergent capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). These rationales have been found to enhance LLM performance across various NLP tasks. Recently, there has been growing interest in using these rationales to provide insights for various important downstream tasks. In this paper, we analyze generated free-text rationales in tasks with subjective answers, emphasizing the importance of rationalization in such scenarios. We focus on pairwise argument ranking, a highly subjective task with significant potential for real-world applications, such as debate assistance. We evaluate the persuasiveness of rationales generated by nine LLMs to support their subjective choices. Our findings suggest that open-source LLMs, particularly Llama2-70B-chat, are capable of providing highly persuasive rationalizations, surpassing even GPT models. Additionally, our experiments show that rationale persuasiveness can be improved by controlling its parameters through prompting or through self-refinement.
中摘: 生成自由文本的理由是大型语言模型（LLM）新兴的能力之一。这些理由被发现能增强LLM在各种自然语言处理任务中的表现。最近，人们越来越感兴趣于利用这些理由为各种重要的下游任务提供见解。在这篇论文中，我们分析了在具有主观答案的任务中生成的自由文本理由，强调在这种情况下理性化的重要性。我们专注于成对论证排名，这是一个主观性极高的任务，具有显著的现实世界应用潜力，例如辩论辅助。我们评估了九个LLM生成的理由对其主观选择的支持力。我们的研究结果表明，开源LLM，特别是Llama2-70B-chat，能够提供极具说服力的理由，甚至超过了GPT模型。此外，我们的实验显示，通过提示或自我精炼来控制其参数，可以提高理由的说服力。

[No.535] QDyLoRA: Quantized Dynamic Low-Rank Adaptation for Efficient Large Language Model Tuning
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: 量化动态低秩适应技术以实现高效的大语言模型调优
类别: 
作者: Hossein Rajabzadeh;Mojtaba Valipour;Tianshu Zhu;Marzieh S. Tahaei;Hyock Ju Kwon;Ali Ghodsi;Boxing Chen;Mehdi Rezagholizadeh;
机构: University of Waterloo;Huawei;
类型: Industry
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-industry.53/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.10462
英摘: Finetuning large language models requires huge GPU memory, restricting the choice to acquire Larger models. While the quantized version of the Low-Rank Adaptation technique, named QLoRA, significantly alleviates this issue, finding the efficient LoRA rank is still challenging. Moreover, QLoRA is trained on a pre-defined rank and, therefore, cannot be reconfigured for its lower ranks without requiring further fine-tuning steps. This paper proposes QDyLoRA -Quantized Dynamic Low-Rank Adaptation-, as an efficient quantization approach for dynamic low-rank adaptation. Motivated by Dynamic LoRA, QDyLoRA is able to efficiently finetune LLMs on a set of pre-defined LoRA ranks. QDyLoRA enables fine-tuning Falcon-40b for ranks 1 to 64 on a single 32 GB V100-GPU through one round of fine-tuning. Experimental results show that QDyLoRA is competitive to QLoRA and outperforms when employing its optimal rank.
中摘: 微调大型语言模型需要巨大的GPU内存，这限制了获取更大模型的选择。尽管低秩适应技术的量化版本，即QLoRA，显著缓解了这一问题，但找到高效的LoRA秩仍然具有挑战性。此外，QLoRA在预定义的秩上进行训练，因此，如果不进行额外的微调步骤，就不能重新配置为更低的秩。本文提出了一种名为QDyLoRA（量化动态低秩适应）的有效量化方法，用于动态低秩适应。受动态LoRA的启发，QDyLoRA能够有效地在一组预定义的LoRA秩上微调LLMs。QDyLoRA使得通过一轮微调，就能在单个32GB V100-GPU上对Falcon-40b从秩1到64进行微调。实验结果表明，QDyLoRA与QLoRA具有竞争力，并且在其最优秩时表现更优。

[No.536] RaFe: Ranking Feedback Improves Query Rewriting for RAG
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: RaFe: 排名反馈改善了RAG的查询重写
类别: 
作者: Shengyu Mao;Yong Jiang;Boli Chen;Xiao Li;Peng Wang;Xinyu Wang;Pengjun Xie;Fei Huang;Huajun Chen;Ningyu Zhang;
机构: Zhejiang University;Alibaba Group;Nanjing University;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.49/

英摘: As Large Language Models (LLMs) and Retrieval Augmentation Generation (RAG) techniques have evolved, query rewriting has been widely incorporated into the RAG system for downstream tasks like open-domain QA. Many works have attempted to utilize small models with reinforcement learning rather than costly LLMs to improve query rewriting. However, current methods require annotations (e.g., labeled relevant documents or downstream answers) or predesigned rewards for feedback, which lack generalization, and fail to utilize signals tailored for query rewriting. In this paper, we propose ours, a framework for training query rewriting models free of annotations. By leveraging a publicly available reranker, ours~provides feedback aligned well with the rewriting objectives. 中摘: 随着大型语言模型（LLM）和检索增强生成（RAG）技术的发展，查询重写已被广泛融入RAG系统，用于下游任务如开放领域问答。许多研究尝试利用小型模型结合强化学习，而非成本高昂的LLM，以改进查询重写。然而，当前的方法需要注释（例如，标记的相关文档或下游答案）或预设奖励作为反馈，这缺乏泛化能力，并且未能利用针对查询重写定制的信号。在本文中，我们提出了一种无需注释训练查询重写模型的框架。通过利用公开可用的重新排序器，我们的方法提供了与重写目标高度一致的反馈。实验结果表明，我们的方法能获得优于基线的更好性能。

[No.537] Ranking Manipulation for Conversational Search Engines
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: 对话式搜索引擎中的排名操纵
类别: 
作者: Samuel Pfrommer;Yatong Bai;Tanmay Gautam;Somayeh Sojoudi;
机构: University of California, Berkeley;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.534/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.03589
英摘: Major search engine providers are rapidly incorporating Large Language Model (LLM)-generated content in response to user queries. These conversational search engines operate by loading retrieved website text into the LLM context for summarization and interpretation. Recent research demonstrates that LLMs are highly vulnerable to jailbreaking and prompt injection attacks, which disrupt the safety and quality goals of LLMs using adversarial strings. This work investigates the impact of prompt injections on the ranking order of sources referenced by conversational search engines. To this end, we introduce a focused dataset of real-world consumer product websites and formalize conversational search ranking as an adversarial problem. Experimentally, we analyze conversational search rankings in the absence of adversarial injections and show that different LLMs vary significantly in prioritizing product name, document content, and context position. We then present a tree-of-attacks-based jailbreaking technique which reliably promotes low-ranked products. Importantly, these attacks transfer effectively to state-of-the-art conversational search engines such as perplexity$.$ai. Given the strong financial incentive for website owners to boost their search ranking, we argue that our problem formulation is of critical importance for future robustness work.
中摘: 主要的搜索引擎提供商正在快速地将大型语言模型（LLM）生成的内容融入对用户查询的响应中。这些对话式搜索引擎通过将检索到的网站文本加载到LLM上下文中进行摘要和解释来运行。最近的研究表明，LLM极易受到越狱和提示注入攻击的影响，这些攻击利用对抗性字符串破坏了LLM的安全性和质量目标。本研究探讨了提示注入对对话式搜索引擎引用来源排名顺序的影响。为此，我们引入了一个聚焦于现实世界消费者产品网站的专门数据集，并将对话式搜索排名形式化为一个对抗性问题。实验上，我们在没有对抗性注入的情况下分析对话式搜索排名，显示不同的LLM在优先考虑产品名称、文档内容和上下文位置方面存在显著差异。然后，我们展示了一种基于攻击树的越狱技术，该技术能够可靠地提升排名较低的产品。重要的是，这些攻击能有效转移到如perplexity.ai等最先进的对话式搜索引擎上。考虑到网站所有者提高其搜索排名的强大经济动机，我们认为我们的问题定义对于未来鲁棒性工作至关重要。
