[No.538] RoseLoRA: Row and Column-wise Sparse Low-rank Adaptation of Pre-trained Language Model for Knowledge Editing and Fine-tuning
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: RoseLoRA：行和列稀疏低秩预训练语言模型的知识编辑和微调方法
类别: 
作者: Haoyu Wang;Tianci Liu;Ruirui Li;Monica Xiao Cheng;Tuo Zhao;Jing Gao;
机构: State University of New York at Albany;Purdue University;Amazon;Georgia Institute of Technology;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.57/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.10777
英摘: Pre-trained language models, trained on large-scale corpora, demonstrate strong generalizability across various NLP tasks. Fine-tuning these models for specific tasks typically involves updating all parameters, which is resource-intensive. Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, such as the popular LoRA family, introduce low-rank matrices to learn only a few parameters efficiently. However, during inference, the product of these matrices updates all pre-trained parameters, complicating tasks like knowledge editing that require selective updates. We propose a novel PEFT method, which conducts \textbf{r}ow and c\textbf{o}lumn-wise spar\textbf{se} \textbf{lo}w-\textbf{r}ank \textbf{a}daptation (RoseLoRA), to address this challenge. RoseLoRA identifies and updates only the most important parameters for a specific task, maintaining efficiency while preserving other model knowledge. By adding a sparsity constraint on the product of low-rank matrices and converting it to row and column-wise sparsity, we ensure efficient and precise model updates. Our theoretical analysis guarantees the lower bound of the sparsity with respective to the matrix product. Extensive experiments on five benchmarks across twenty datasets demonstrate that RoseLoRA outperforms baselines in both general fine-tuning and knowledge editing tasks.
中摘: 预训练语言模型在大规模语料库上进行训练，展现出在各种自然语言处理任务中的强大泛化能力。针对特定任务对这些模型进行微调通常涉及更新所有参数，这十分消耗资源。参数高效微调（PEFT）方法，如流行的LoRA家族，通过引入低秩矩阵来有效学习少量参数。然而，在推理过程中，这些矩阵的乘积会更新所有预训练参数，这给需要选择性更新的任务，如知识编辑，带来了复杂性。我们提出了一种新的PEFT方法，即行和列稀疏低秩适应（RoseLoRA），以应对这一挑战。RoseLoRA识别并仅更新对特定任务最重要的参数，保持效率的同时保留了其他模型知识。通过对低秩矩阵的乘积添加稀疏约束，并将其转换为行和列的稀疏性，我们确保了模型更新的高效性和精确性。我们的理论分析保证了相对于矩阵乘积的稀疏性的下限。在跨越二十个数据集的五个基准上的广泛实验表明，RoseLoRA在一般微调和知识编辑任务中均超越了基线方法。

[No.541] Uncertainty in Language Models: Assessment through Rank-Calibration
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: rank
标题: 语言模型中的不确定性评估：通过排名校准
类别: 
作者: Xinmeng Huang;Shuo Li;Mengxin Yu;Matteo Sesia;Hamed Hassani;Insup Lee;Osbert Bastani;Edgar Dobriban;
机构: University of Pennsylvania;University of Southern California;;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.18/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.03163
英摘: Language Models (LMs) have shown promising performance in natural language generation. However, as LMs often generate incorrect or hallucinated responses, it is crucial to correctly quantify their uncertainty in responding to given inputs. In addition to verbalized confidence elicited via prompting, many uncertainty measures ($e.g.$, semantic entropy and affinity-graph-based measures) have been proposed. However, these measures can differ greatly, and it is unclear how to compare them, partly because they take values over different ranges ($e.g.$, $[0,\infty)$ or $[0,1]$). In this work, we address this issue by developing a novel and practical framework, termed $Rank$-$Calibration$, to assess uncertainty and confidence measures for LMs. Our key tenet is that higher uncertainty (or lower confidence) should imply lower generation quality, on average. Rank-calibration quantifies deviations from this ideal relationship in a principled manner, without requiring ad hoc binary thresholding of the correctness score ($e.g.$, ROUGE or METEOR). The broad applicability and the granular interpretability of our methods are demonstrated empirically.
中摘: 语言模型（LMs）在自然语言生成方面展现出了令人鼓舞的表现。然而，由于语言模型经常产生错误或幻想性的回应，因此准确量化它们对给定输入的不确定性至关重要。除了通过提示获取的言语化信心之外，许多不确定性度量（例如，语义熵和基于亲和图的度量）已经被提出。然而，这些度量可能大相径庭，如何比较它们并不明确，部分原因在于它们在不同的范围内取值（例如，$(0,\infty)$ 或者 $[0,1]$）。在本工作中，我们通过开发一个新颖且实用的框架——称为“排名-校准”（$Rank$-$Calibration$），来解决这一问题，以评估语言模型的不确定性和置信度度量。我们的核心理念是，更高的不确定性（或更低的置信度）应该意味着平均生成质量较低。排名校准以有原则的方式量化偏离这种理想关系的程度，而无需对正确性得分（例如，ROUGE 或 METEOR）进行任意的二元阈值处理。我们方法的广泛适用性和细致的可解释性在实证上得到了展示。

[No.542] Sparse Low-rank Adaptation of Pre-trained Language Models
来源: EMNLP 2023
命中: rank
标题: 预训练语言模型的稀疏低秩适应
类别: 
作者: Ning Ding;Xingtai Lv;Qiaosen Wang;Yulin Chen;Bowen Zhou;Zhiyuan Liu;Maosong Sun;
机构: Tsinghua University;University of Chicago;JD.com;
类型: Long Main
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=jxgz7FEqWq
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.11696
英摘: Fine-tuning pre-trained large language models in a parameter-efficient manner is widely studied for its effectiveness and efficiency. The popular method of low-rank adaptation (LoRA) offers a notable approach, hypothesizing that the adaptation process is intrinsically low-dimensional. Although LoRA has demonstrated commendable performance, it is implemented with a fixed and unalterable intrinsic rank that might not always be the ideal choice. Recognizing the need for more flexible adaptation, we extend the methodology of LoRA to an innovative approach we call sparse low-rank adaptation (SoRA) that enables dynamic adjustments to the intrinsic rank during the adaptation process. We achieve this through the incorporation of a gate unit optimized with proximal gradient method in the training stage, controlling the cardinality of rank under the sparsity of the gate. In the subsequent inference stage, we eliminate the parameter blocks corresponding to the zeroed-out ranks, to reduce each SoRA module back to a concise yet rank-optimal LoRA. Our approach strengthens the representation power of LoRA by initializing it with a higher rank, while efficiently taming a temporarily increased number of parameters via updating in a sparse way. We further introduce a sparsifying scheduler for SoRA, aiming to examine the impact of the number of non-zero parameters on the model's memorization and generalization. Our experimental results demonstrate that SoRA can outperform other baselines even with 70% retained parameters and 70% training time.
中摘: 以参数高效的方式微调预训练的大规模语言模型因其有效性和效率而被广泛研究。低秩适应（LoRA）作为一种流行的方法，提供了一个显著的途径，假设适应过程本质上是低维的。尽管LoRA展示了令人称赞的表现，但它实施时具有固定且不可更改的内在秩，这可能并不总是理想的选择。为了实现更灵活的适应，我们将LoRA的方法扩展到一种创新的方法，我们称之为稀疏低秩适应（SoRA），它能够在适应过程中动态调整内在秩。我们通过在训练阶段加入一个用近端梯度方法优化的门控单元来实现这一点，该单元在门控的稀疏性下控制秩的基数。在后续的推理阶段，我们剔除对应于零化秩的参数块，将每个SoRA模块简化回简洁但秩最优的LoRA。我们的方法通过以更高的秩初始化LoRA来增强其表示能力，同时通过以稀疏方式更新，有效地控制了暂时增加的参数数量。我们还为SoRA引入了一个稀疏化调度器，旨在考察非零参数数量对模型记忆和泛化能力的影响。我们的实验结果表明，即使保留70%的参数和70%的训练时间，SoRA也能超越其他基线方法。

[No.549] Decomposed Prompt Tuning via Low-Rank Reparameterization

来源: EMNLP 2023
命中: rank
标题: 大型语言模型不是一个好的少样本信息提取器，但是一个好的困难样本排序器！
类别: 
作者: Yubo Ma;Yixin Cao;Yong Ching Hong;Aixin Sun;
机构: Singapore Management University;Nanyang Technological University;
类型: Long Findings
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=LUDljw5VVD
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2303.08559
英摘: Large Language Models (LLMs) have made remarkable strides in various tasks. Whether LLMs are competitive few-shot solvers for information extraction (IE) tasks, however, remains an open problem. In this work, we aim to provide a thorough answer to this question. Through extensive experiments on nine datasets across four IE tasks, we demonstrate that current advanced LLMs consistently exhibit inferior performance, higher latency, and increased budget requirements compared to fine-tuned SLMs under most settings. Therefore, we conclude that LLMs are not effective few-shot information extractors in general. Nonetheless, we illustrate that with appropriate prompting strategies, LLMs can effectively complement SLMs and tackle challenging samples that SLMs struggle with. And moreover, we propose an adaptive filter-then-rerank paradigm to combine the strengths of LLMs and SLMs. In this paradigm, SLMs serve as filters and LLMs serve as rerankers. By prompting LLMs to rerank a small portion of difficult samples identified by SLMs, our preliminary system consistently achieves promising improvements (2.4% F1-gain on average) on various IE tasks, with an acceptable time and cost investment.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在各种任务上取得了显著进展。然而，LLMs是否能成为信息抽取（IE）任务的有效少量样本求解者，仍然是一个开放问题。在这项工作中，我们旨在对这个问题给出全面的答案。通过对跨越四个IE任务的九个数据集进行广泛实验，我们证明了当前先进的LLMs在大多数设置下通常表现出较低的性能、更高的延迟和增加的预算需求，与微调的小型语言模型（SLMs）相比。因此，我们得出结论：总体而言，LLMs并不是有效的少量样本信息抽取器。不过，我们展示了通过适当的提示策略，LLMs可以有效地补充SLMs，并解决那些SLMs难以处理的难题样本。此外，我们提出了一种自适应的过滤-再排序范式，以结合LLMs和SLMs的优势。在这个范式中，SLMs作为过滤器，而LLMs作为再排序器。通过让LLMs对SLMs识别出的一小部分困难样本进行再排序，我们的初步系统在不同的IE任务上持续地实现了有希望的改进（平均2.4%的F1分数提升），并且在时间和成本投入方面是可以接受的。

[No.552] Learning to Rank Context for Named Entity Recognition Using a Synthetic Dataset
来源: EMNLP 2023
命中: rank
标题: 使用合成数据集学习命名实体识别的上下文排序方法
类别: 
作者: Arthur Amalvy;Vincent Labatut;Richard Dufour;
机构: Université d'Avignon;Nantes University;
类型: Long Main
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=N7R2emgl67
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.10118
英摘: While recent pre-trained transformer-based models can perform named entity recognition (NER) with great accuracy, their limited range remains an issue when applied to long documents such as whole novels. To alleviate this issue, a solution is to retrieve relevant context at the document level. Unfortunately, the lack of supervision for such a task means one has to settle for unsupervised approaches. Instead, we propose to generate a synthetic context retrieval training dataset using Alpaca, an instructiontuned large language model (LLM). Using this dataset, we train a neural context retriever based on a BERT model that is able to find relevant context for NER. We show that our method outperforms several retrieval baselines for the NER task on an English literary dataset composed of the first chapter of 40 books.
中摘: 尽管最近基于预训练Transformer的模型在命名实体识别（NER）任务上能够达到很高的准确性，但当应用于整本小说这样的长文档时，其有限的处理范围仍然存在问题。为了解决这个问题，一种方法是在文档级别检索相关上下文。不幸的是，由于缺乏此类任务的监督，人们不得不依赖无监督的方法。相反，我们提出使用Alpaca，一个指令调优的大规模语言模型（LLM），来生成合成的上下文检索训练数据集。利用这个数据集，我们训练了一个基于BERT模型的神经上下文检索器，它能够为NER找到相关上下文。我们证明了我们的方法在由40本书的第一章组成的英文文学数据集上的NER任务中，超越了几种基线检索方法。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.553] Task-Agnostic Low-Rank Adapters for Unseen English Dialects
来源: EMNLP 2023
命中: rank
标题: 面向未知英语方言的无任务特定低秩适配器
类别: 
作者: Zedian Xiao;William Barr Held;Yanchen Liu;Diyi Yang;
机构: Stanford University;Georgia Institute of Technology;Harvard University;
类型: Long Main
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=X570XzeYSW
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.00915
英摘: Large Language Models (LLMs) are trained on corpora disproportionally weighted in favor of Standard American English. As a result, speakers of other dialects experience significantly more failures when interacting with these technologies. In practice, these speakers often accommodate their speech to be better understood. Our work shares the belief that language technologies should be designed to accommodate the diversity in English dialects and not the other way around. However, prior works on dialect struggle with generalizing to evolving and emerging dialects in a scalable manner. To fill this gap, our method, HyperLoRA, leverages expert linguistic knowledge to enable resource-efficient adaptation via hypernetworks. By disentangling dialect-specific and cross-dialectal information, HyperLoRA improves generalization to unseen dialects in a task-agnostic fashion. Not only is HyperLoRA more scalable in the number of parameters, but it also achieves the best or most competitive performance across 5 dialects in a zero-shot setting. In this way, our approach facilitates access to language technology for billions of English dialect speakers who are traditionally underrepresented.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLM）在训练时使用的语料库过度倾向于标准美式英语。因此，其他方言的使用者在与这些技术互动时遇到的失败显著更多。实际上，这些使用者经常调整他们的语言以更好地被理解。我们的工作秉持着这样的信念：语言技术应该设计为适应英语方言的多样性，而不是相反。然而，先前关于方言的研究在以可扩展的方式对不断演变和新兴方言进行泛化方面存在困难。为填补这一空白，我们的方法HyperLoRA利用专家的语言学知识，通过超网络实现资源高效适应。通过分离方言特有信息和跨方言信息，HyperLoRA以任务无关的方式改善了对未见过的方言的泛化能力。HyperLoRA不仅在参数数量上更具可扩展性，而且在零样本设置下，在5种方言中实现了最佳或最具竞争力的表现。通过这种方式，我们的方法促进了数十亿传统上代表性不足的英语方言使用者对语言技术的访问。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.554] Auto-Instruct: Automatic Instruction Generation and Ranking for Black-Box Language Models
来源: EMNLP 2023
命中: rank
标题: 自动指令生成与排名：面向黑盒语言模型的指令自动生成和排名
类别: 
作者: Zhihan Zhang;Shuohang Wang;Wenhao Yu;Yichong Xu;Dan Iter;Qingkai Zeng;Yang Liu;Chenguang Zhu;Meng Jiang;
机构: Microsoft;University of Notre Dame;Tencent;Zoom Video Communications Inc.;
类型: Long Findings
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=ORHg3RKho0
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.13127
英摘: Large language models (LLMs) can perform a wide range of tasks by following natural language instructions, without the necessity of task-specific fine-tuning. Unfortunately, the performance of LLMs is greatly influenced by the quality of these instructions, and manually writing effective instructions for each task is a laborious and subjective process. In this paper, we introduce Auto-Instruct, a novel method to automatically improve the quality of instructions provided to LLMs. Our method leverages the inherent generative ability of LLMs to produce diverse candidate instructions for a given task, and then ranks them using a scoring model trained on a variety of 575 existing NLP tasks. In experiments on 118 out-of-domain tasks, Auto-Instruct surpasses both human-written instructions and existing baselines of LLM-generated instructions. Furthermore, our method exhibits notable generalizability even with other LLMs that are not incorporated into its training process.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLM）能够通过遵循自然语言指令执行广泛的任务，而无需针对特定任务进行微调。不幸的是，LLM的性能受到这些指令质量的极大影响，而为每个任务手动编写有效的指令是一项费力且主观的过程。在这篇论文中，我们引入了Auto-Instruct，一种改进提供给LLM的指令质量的新方法。我们的方法利用了LLM固有的生成能力，为给定任务产生多样化的候选指令，然后使用在575种现有NLP任务上训练的评分模型对它们进行排名。在118项领域外任务的实验中，Auto-Instruct超越了人工编写的指令和现有的LLM生成指令基线。此外，即使在未纳入其训练过程的其他LLM中，我们的方法也表现出显著的泛化能力。

[No.555] Dynamic Low-rank Estimation for Transformer-based Language Models

类别: 
作者: Ting Hua;Xiao Li;Shangqian Gao;Yen-Chang Hsu;Yilin Shen;Hongxia Jin;

[No.556] Statistical Depth for Ranking and Characterizing Transformer-Based Text Embeddings
来源: EMNLP 2023
命中: rank
标题: 基于统计深度对变压器模型文本嵌入进行排序与特征描述
类别: 
作者: Parker Seegmiller;Sarah Masud Preum;
机构: Dartmouth College;
类型: Long Main
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=PffUQuD8sn
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.15010
英摘: The popularity of transformer-based text embeddings calls for better statistical tools for measuring distributions of such embeddings. One such tool would be a method for ranking texts within a corpus by centrality, i.e. assigning each text a number signifying how representative that text is of the corpus as a whole. However, an intrinsic center-outward ordering of high-dimensional text representations is not trivial. A statistical depth is a function for ranking k-dimensional objects by measuring centrality with respect to some observed k-dimensional distribution. We adopt a statistical depth to measure distributions of transformer-based text embeddings, transformer-based text embedding (TTE) depth, and introduce the practical use of this depth for both modeling and distributional inference in NLP pipelines. We first define TTE depth and an associated rank sum test for determining whether two corpora differ significantly in embedding space. We then use TTE depth for the task of in-context learning prompt selection, showing that this approach reliably improves performance over statistical baseline approaches across six text classification tasks. Finally, we use TTE depth and the associated rank sum test to characterize the distributions of synthesized and human-generated corpora, showing that five recent synthetic data augmentation processes cause a measurable distributional shift away from associated human-generated text.
中摘: 基于变换器的文本嵌入的流行要求我们拥有更好的统计工具来衡量这类嵌入的分布。一种这样的工具是通过中心性对语料库中的文本进行排名的方法，即为每篇文本分配一个数字，表示该文本在整体语料库中的代表性程度。然而，在高维文本表示中固有的从中心向外的排序并不简单。统计深度是一种函数，用于通过测量相对于某个观察到的k维分布的中心性来对k维对象进行排名。我们采用统计深度来衡量基于变换器的文本嵌入的分布，称为基于变换器的文本嵌入深度（TTE深度），并介绍此深度在自然语言处理（NLP）管道中建模和分布推断的实际应用。我们首先定义了TTE深度以及相关的秩和检验，以确定两个语料库在嵌入空间中是否存在显著差异。然后，我们将TTE深度应用于情境学习提示选择任务，证明了与统计基线方法相比，这种方法在六个文本分类任务上可靠地提高了性能。最后，我们使用TTE深度及其相关秩和检验来表征合成和人类生成的语料库的分布，表明五个近期的合成数据增强过程会导致与相应人类生成文本可测量的分布偏移。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.557] DetectLLM: Leveraging Log Rank Information for Zero-Shot Detection of Machine-Generated Text
来源: EMNLP 2023
命中: rank
标题: DetectLLM：利用日志排名信息进行零样本检测机器生成文本
类别: 
作者: Jinyan Su;Terry Yue Zhuo;Di Wang;Preslav Nakov;
机构: Cornell University;Mohamed bin Zayed University of Artificial Intelligence;
类型: Long Findings
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=Dy2mbQIdMz
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.05540
英摘: With the rapid progress of large language models (LLMs) and the huge amount of text they generated, it becomes more and more impractical to manually distinguish whether a text is machine-generated. Given the growing use of LLMs in social media and education, it prompts us to develop methods to detect machine-generated text, preventing malicious usage such as plagiarism, misinformation, and propaganda. Previous work has studied several zero-shot methods, which require no training data. These methods achieve good performance, but there is still a lot of room for improvement. In this paper, we introduce two novel zero-shot methods for detecting machine-generated text by leveraging the log rank information. One is called DetectLLM-LRR, which is fast and efficient, and the other is called DetectLLM-NPR, which is more accurate, but slower due to the need for perturbations. Our experiments on three datasets and seven language models show that our proposed methods improve over the state of the art by 3.9 and 1.75 AUROC points absolute. Moreover, DetectLLM-NPR needs fewer perturbations than previous work to achieve the same level of performance, which makes it more practical for real-world use. We also investigate the efficiency--performance trade-off based on users preference on these two measures and we provide intuition for using them in practice effectively. We release the data and the code of both methods in https://github.com/mbzuai-nlp/DetectLLM
中摘: 随着大型语言模型（LLMs）的迅速发展及其生成的大量文本，人工判断文本是否由机器生成变得越来越不切实际。鉴于LLMs在社交媒体和教育领域的日益普及，这促使我们开发检测机器生成文本的方法，以防止诸如剽窃、错误信息和宣传等恶意使用。先前的研究已经探索了几种零样本方法，这些方法不需要训练数据。这些方法取得了良好的性能，但仍有很大的改进空间。在这篇论文中，我们提出了两种新颖的零样本方法，通过利用日志排名信息来检测机器生成的文本。一种被称为DetectLLM-LRR，它快速且高效；另一种被称为DetectLLM-NPR，虽然更准确，但由于需要扰动，速度较慢。我们在三个数据集和七个语言模型上的实验表明，我们提出的方法在绝对AUC-ROC分数上分别提高了3.9和1.75点。此外，与先前的工作相比，DetectLLM-NPR在达到相同性能水平时需要的扰动更少，这使其在现实世界的应用中更加实用。我们还根据用户对效率和性能偏好的权衡，研究了这两种方法的实际应用，并提供了有效使用的直观指导。我们已在https://github.com/mbzuai-nlp/DetectLLM上公开了这两种方法的数据和代码。

[No.558] Time-Considerable Dialogue Models via Reranking by Time Dependency

作者: Yuiko Tsunomori;Masakazu Ishihata;Hiroaki Sugiyama;

[No.559] SELFOOD: Self-Supervised Out-Of-Distribution Detection via Learning to Rank
来源: EMNLP 2023
命中: rank
标题: SELFOOD：通过学习排序进行自我监督的分布外检测
类别: 
作者: Dheeraj Mekala;Adithya Samavedhi;Chengyu Dong;Jingbo Shang;
机构: University of California, San Diego;
类型: Long Findings
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=L0SEfyrLsW
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.14696
英摘: Deep neural classifiers trained with cross-entropy loss (CE loss) often suffer from poor calibration, necessitating the task of out-of-distribution (OOD) detection. Traditional supervised OOD detection methods require expensive manual annotation of in-distribution and OOD samples. To address the annotation bottleneck, we introduce SELFOOD, a self-supervised OOD detection method that requires only in-distribution samples as supervision. We cast OOD detection as an inter-document intra-label (IDIL) ranking problem and train the classifier with our pairwise ranking loss, referred to as IDIL loss. Specifically, given a set of in-distribution documents and their labels, for each label, we train the classifier to rank the softmax scores of documents belonging to that label to be higher than the scores of documents that belong to other labels. Unlike CE loss, our IDIL loss function reaches zero when the desired confidence ranking is achieved and gradients are backpropagated to decrease probabilities associated with incorrect labels rather than continuously increasing the probability of the correct label. Extensive experiments with several classifiers on multiple classification datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our method in both coarse- and fine-grained settings.
中摘: 使用交叉熵损失（CE损失）训练的深度神经分类器往往存在校准不良的问题，这使得检测分布外（OOD）数据成为必要任务。传统的监督式OOD检测方法需要对分布内和OOD样本进行昂贵的手动标注。为了解决标注瓶颈问题，我们引入了SELFOOD，一种仅需分布内样本作为监督的自监督OOD检测方法。我们将OOD检测视为跨文档内部标签（IDIL）排序问题，并使用我们的配对排序损失，即IDIL损失，来训练分类器。具体来说，给定一组分布内文档及其标签，对于每个标签，我们训练分类器将属于该标签的文档的softmax得分排在高于属于其他标签的文档的得分之前。与CE损失不同，当达到期望的置信度排序时，我们的IDIL损失函数会达到零，反向传播的梯度会降低与错误标签相关的概率，而不是持续增加正确标签的概率。在多个分类数据集上使用几种分类器进行的广泛实验表明，在粗粒度和细粒度设置下，我们的方法均有效。
